### [1] [Subwavelength registration of skull-compensating acoustic holograms via nonlinear parametric array feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15730)
*Pradosh Pritam Dash,Costas D. Arvanitis*

Main category: physics.med-ph

TL;DR: 研究提出以参量阵（parametric array, PA）信号作为经颅超声全息对准的反馈替代信号，利用失配时颅骨内声能体积俘获增强非线性源这一机制，实现亚波长（sub-wavelength）精度的无创实时对准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化的声全息（acoustic holograms）用于经颅超声（transcranial ultrasound）等应用需亚波长对准精度（1 MHz 下优于 1.5 mm），而现有非 MRI 光学神经导航（optical neuro-navigation）方法难以达到该精度。

Method: 以两束高频主波非线性混频产生的高穿透、低频差频波——参量阵（PA）信号——作为对准指示量；通过仿真考察失配的颅骨补偿透镜（skull-compensating lens）对颅骨内声能的影响，并用二维弹性仿真与离体（ex vivo）脉冲测试检验剪切模式转换（shear-mode conversion）的作用，同时排查伪声（pseudo-sound）与微气泡伪影。

Result: 仿真显示失配会增强颅骨骨内的主声能量（体积能量俘获，volumetric energy trapping），从而强化 PA 信号的非线性源；失配下剪切型运动仅增加 3–5%，且离体透射脉冲未如剪切路径所要求的那样展宽；伪声与微气泡伪影不足以解释该信号，结果与 PA 信号源自骨组织本征非线性（intrinsic nonlinearity）一致。

Conclusion: 最小化该 PA 信号可构成无创、实时的反馈机制，达到亚波长对准精度并优于标准神经导航，为设计简易、经济且高性能的高精度神经介入经颅超声系统提供了稳健框架。

Abstract: Realizing the potential of optimized acoustic holograms for applications like transcranial ultrasound requires sub-wavelength alignment accuracy (less than 1.5 mm at 1 MHz), which is difficult to achieve with current non-MRI optical neuro-navigation methods. To address the critical challenge of alignment, we leverage the parametric array (PA) signal as an alignment surrogate, a highly penetrative, low-frequency difference wave generated by the nonlinear mixing of two primary high-frequency beams. Simulations show that a misaligned skull-compensating lens increases the primary acoustic energy within the skull bone (volumetric energy trapping), which strengthens the nonlinear source of the PA signal. We also tested whether shear-mode conversion in bone contributes: 2D elastic simulations showed only a small (3-5%) increase in shear-type motion with misalignment, and the transmitted ex vivo bursts did not lengthen as a shear path would require. Pseudo-sound and microbubble artifacts were examined and found unlikely to explain the signal, consistent with the PA signal arising from the intrinsic nonlinearity of the bone. Minimizing this PA signal yields a noninvasive real-time feedback mechanism that achieves sub-wavelength alignment accuracy, outperforming standard neuronavigation. Our findings present a robust framework that supports the design of simple, economical, and high-performance transcranial ultrasound systems for high-precision neuro-interventions.

</details>

### [2] [SunLightBots: Turning sunlight into self-sustained, high-power motion in soft robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14729)
*Yoo Jin Lee,Melanie Torres Crespo,Zhongxiao Deng,Andrew Tejada-Vega,Rebecca Kramer-Bottiglio,Wenzhong Yan,Yimin Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SunLightBots 是一类亚克量级（subgram）、完全由阳光驱动的无缆软体机器人，借助自复位突跳（self-resetting snapping, SRS）机制在近 1 Sun 光照下实现自主运动，峰值质量比机械功率达 89 W/kg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性材料软体机器人可自主利用环境能量，但环境能量采集功率低，而快速、高负载动作需要高机械输出功率，二者存在功率失配（power mismatch）；在亚克量级下负载能力有限，难以集成常规电源与控制硬件，因此亟需在材料与力学层面解决该矛盾。

Method: 提出将液晶弹性体（liquid crystal elastomers, LCEs）与突跳失稳（snap-through instability）集成的自复位突跳（SRS）机制：以低功率积累太阳能，再以快速高功率动作释放，并通过光热—力学反馈（photothermal-mechanical feedback）实现复位；同时协同优化 LCE 驱动温度、光热转换、驱动应力与结构能垒。

Result: 系统可在近 1 Sun（100 mW/cm²）下运行，峰值质量比机械功率为 89 W/kg；优化后的 LCE 在约 60 °C 下产生 0.1 MPa 驱动应力；机器人实现持续游泳、爬行与重复脉冲跳跃，在 1.5 Suns 下游泳者每周期前进超过三个体长（body lengths）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条材料—力学（materials-mechanics）路径，用于构建完全由环境能量驱动的自主软体机器人，缓解低功率采集与高功率输出之间的固有矛盾。

Abstract: Soft robots built from active materials can directly convert environmental stimuli into motion, offering a route to energy autonomy and embodied control. However, environmental energy is generally harvested at low power, whereas rapid, mechanically demanding actions require high mechanical output power. Reconciling this mismatch while sustaining self-regulated cyclic actuation is challenging, particularly at the subgram scale, where limited payload capacity constrains the integration of conventional power and control hardware. Here, we present SunLightBots, a class of subgram, sunlight-powered, untethered soft robots that achieve autonomous locomotion under continuous illumination near 1 Sun (100 mW/cm2). Their operation is enabled by a self-resetting snapping (SRS) mechanism that integrates liquid crystal elastomers (LCEs) with snap-through instability to accumulate solar energy supplied at low power, release it as rapid, high-power motion, and reset through photothermal-mechanical feedback. Through coordinated optimization of the LCE actuation temperature, photothermal conversion, actuation stress, and structural energy barrier, the system operates near 1 Sun and achieves a peak mass-specific mechanical power of 89 W/kg. At this irradiance, the optimized LCE generates an actuation stress of 0.1 MPa at approximately 60 deg C. SunLightBots demonstrate sustained swimming, crawling, and repeated impulsive jumping. Under 1.5 Suns, the swimmer advances more than three body lengths per cycle. This work provides a materials-mechanics route toward autonomous soft robots powered entirely by the environment.

</details>

### [3] [Learning to Calibrate Jet Energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14567)
*Bogdan Malaescu,Benjamin Nachman,Mukul Ram,Steven Schramm,Daniel Whiteson*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 该文提出一套原型机器学习流水线（machine-learning pipeline），用于强子喷注（hadronic jet）能量标定，将堆叠去除（pile-up removal）、绝对修正（absolute correction）与数据-蒙特卡洛修正（data-MC correction）统一处理，无需预设参数化函数形式，且性能在几乎每个阶段都达到或超过传统函数拟合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider, LHC）大量产生强子喷注，其能量精确重建对物理项目至关重要；现有标定依赖逐项拟合参数化函数形式（parametric functional forms），虽有效但脆弱且难以扩展。

Method: 构建原型机器学习流水线，依次完成堆叠依赖性去除、绝对修正与数据-MC 修正；不对修正施加任何函数形式约束，并允许便捷地加入额外特征（additional features）。

Result: 在几乎每一个标定阶段，该流水线的性能与传统函数形式方法相当或更优（matches or exceeds）。

Conclusion: 机器学习流水线为喷注能量标定提供了不依赖预设函数形式、更易扩展的替代方案，可作为传统函数拟合方法的有力补充或替代。

Abstract: Hadronic jets are produced copiously at the Large Hadron Collider, and are a vital element of its physics program. Accurate reconstruction of their energies is therefore crucial, including correction for pile-up, detector response and data-simulation differences. Currently, each correction is performed by fitting parametric functional forms, an approach which is effective but can be fragile and challenging to extend. We present a prototype machine-learning pipeline which performs pile-up dependency removal, absolute corrections and data-mc corrections. No functional form is imposed on the correction, additional features can be easily added, and the pipeline matches or exceeds the performance of the functional approach at nearly every stage.

</details>

### [4] [Uniform-in-time propagation of chaos for the Kuramoto model on the sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15015)
*Shi Chen,Zhengjiang Lin,Emre Parmaksiz*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文在任意维球面上推广经典 Kuramoto 模型，证明了对时间一致（uniform-in-time）的定量混沌传播（propagation of chaos）：初始独立且具有 L^p 密度（p>1）、非零均值向量的 n 个粒子，其经验分布与平均场（mean-field）分布之间的 Wasserstein 距离在全时间上确界具有显式高概率界；在足够高维时该界与球面上标准经验采样率同阶，从而最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Kuramoto 模型及其球面推广的混沌传播研究多局限于有限时间或特定维数，缺少对所有时间一致且具有明确收敛速率的定量结果。本文旨在建立任意维球面上推广 Kuramoto 模型的时间一致定量混沌传播界，并判定其速率的最优性。

Method: 证明由三部分组合而成：有限时间混沌传播（finite-time propagation of chaos）估计、平均场模型的定量同步（quantitative synchronization）结果，以及系统的后期稳定性（late-time stability）估计。初始假设为独立采样自具有 L^p 密度（p>1）且均值向量非零的概率测度。

Result: 得到经验分布与平均场分布之间 Wasserstein 距离在全时间上确界的显式高概率上界；在维数足够高时，该界与球面上标准经验采样率同阶，因而在速率意义下最优。

Conclusion: 所提方法在任意维球面推广 Kuramoto 模型中实现了时间一致的定量混沌传播，并在高维情形达到最优采样速率，表明有限时间估计、同步性与后期稳定性三者结合是分析此类平均场系统长期行为的有效途径。

Abstract: We establish quantitative uniform-in-time propagation of chaos for a generalization of the classical Kuramoto model on spheres of arbitrary dimension. For $n$ particles that are initialized independently from a probability measure with an $L^p$ density, $p>1$, and a nonzero mean vector, we obtain an explicit high-probability bound for the supremum over all times of the Wasserstein distance between the empirical and mean-field distributions. In sufficiently high dimension, this bound has the same order as the standard empirical sampling rate on the sphere and is therefore optimal. The proof combines a finite-time propagation of chaos estimate, a quantitative synchronization result for the mean-field model, and a late-time stability estimate of the system.

</details>


### [5] [Quantum unique ergodicity and the critical crossover for 1-d Gaussian band matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15085)
*Promit Ghosal,Guillaume Remy*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究一维带状高斯随机矩阵（Gaussian random band matrix）在临界带宽 W² ≍ N 处的联合本征值—本征向量极限定律，证明了本征向量的量子唯一遍历性（quantum unique ergodicity）并刻画了由 DMPK 方程支配的临界普适律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带状随机矩阵被预言在 W ∼ √N 处发生局域化—离域化转变（localization–delocalization transition），但临界点附近本征值与本征向量的联合统计长期缺乏严格结果；同时 Fyodorov–Mirlin 预测该联合律仅依赖 N/W²，需要数学证明。

Method: 采用 Yau–Yin 的周期分块形式：将 N 个格点划分为 n=N/W 个大小为 W 的块，仅块内与相邻块间矩阵元非零。通过分析带随机整函数（random entire function）的本征值零点与块质量分布（block-mass profiles），并结合酉类 DMPK 扩散（Dorokhov–Mello–Pereyra–Kumar diffusion）确定其极限律。

Result: 当 W²/N → ∞ 时，体本征向量（bulk eigenvectors）满足量子唯一遍历性，且无需 W² 与 N 之间的幂次或对数间隔。在阈值 N/W² → τ ∈ (0,∞) 时，标度化体本征值与块质量分布联合收敛到唯一极限律；该律下分布相对任意非平凡光滑检验函数具有严格正方差，故不集中均匀分布。当 τ→0 时方差消失并趋于正弦过程（sine process），τ→∞ 时趋于泊松过程（Poisson process）。

Conclusion: 临界族在离域与局域两 regime 之间插值，验证了 Fyodorov–Mirlin 关于 W² ≍ N 时联合律仅依赖 N/W² 的预测；该工作已由 AxiomProver 在 Lean4 中完成自动形式化（autoformalization）。

Abstract: We consider an $N\times N$ complex Gaussian random band matrix in one dimension with band width of order $W$, in the periodic block form of Yau and Yin. The $N$ sites are split into $n=N/W$ blocks of size $W$ on a ring, and the entries are nonzero only within a block and between adjacent blocks. The conjectured localization--delocalization transition occurs at $W\sim\sqrt N$. We prove quantum unique ergodicity for bulk eigenvectors at every deterministic rank whenever $W^2/N\to\infty$, with no power or logarithmic separation between $W^2$ and $N$. At the threshold $N/W^2\toτ\in(0,\infty)$, we show that the rescaled bulk eigenvalues and the block-mass profiles of their eigenvectors (the probability measures on the circle placing at $k/n$ the squared norm of the eigenvector on block $k$) converge jointly to a unique law. This law is encoded by a random entire function whose zeros give the limiting eigenvalues, and its derivatives there give the limiting profile integrals. The law of this function is determined by the Dorokhov--Mello--Pereyra--Kumar (DMPK) diffusion of the unitary class. In the critical law, the profiles have strictly positive variance against every nonconstant smooth test function, so they are not concentrated on the uniform profile. As $τ\to0$ this variance vanishes and the local spectrum tends to the sine process. As $τ\to\infty$ it tends to a Poisson process where both limits taken after the critical one. The critical family thus interpolates between the delocalized and localized regimes, confirming for this model the prediction of Fyodorov and Mirlin that at $W^2\asymp N$ the joint law of eigenvalues and eigenvectors depends only on $N/W^2$. The full content of this work has been autoformalized in Lean4 by AxiomProver.

</details>


### [6] [Secular Dynamics and the LFL for $β$ Corner Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15096)
*Zhigang Bao,Ruizhe Xu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究了边缘粒子收敛于 Tracy-Widom β 律的 Gaussian β 角过程（Gaussian β corner process），证明对任意固定 β>0，边缘粒子整条轨道的极值满足分数对数律（law of fractional logarithm, LFL），推广了此前 β=1,2 的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果仅在 β=1,2 情形下建立了角过程的 LFL，作者希望将其推广至任意固定 β>0，从而刻画边缘粒子整条轨迹极值的几乎必然（almost sure）行为。

Method: 证明依赖向上 Gamma 与向下 Dirichlet 长期方程（upward Gamma and downward Dirichlet secular equations），将其视为生成角过程的两个离散动力学；这些长期动力学同时用于相关性估计与去相关估计，其中在去相关部分被用作连续 Dyson 布朗运动（Dyson Brownian motion）的离散类比。

Result: 对每个固定 β>0，角过程满足分数对数律（LFL），即边缘粒子整条轨迹的极值具有相应的几乎必然极限行为，涵盖并扩展了 β=1,2 的已知结论。

Conclusion: 通过 Gamma/Dirichlet 长期方程的离散动力学框架，可将 Wigner 子矩阵过程（Wigner minor process）中证明 LFL 的相关性与去相关两步策略移植到 Gaussian β 角过程，从而在所有 β>0 下确立 LFL。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the Gaussian $β$ corner process, whose edge particles are known to converge to the Tracy-Widom $β$ law. We study the almost sure behaviour of the extrema of the whole trajectory of the edge particles and establish a law of fractional logarithm (LFL) for the corner process for every fixed $β>0$, extending the previous known results in the case $β=1,2$. Our proof relies on the upward Gamma and downward Dirichlet secular equations, which can be regarded as two discrete dynamics to form the corner processes. The secular dynamics are used in both the correlation estimate and the decorrelation estimate, which are known as two key steps of proving LFL in \cite{BCEHK-LFL} for the Wigner minor process. Especially, in the decorrelation part, they are used as discrete analogues of the continuous Dyson Brownian motion.

</details>


### [7] [The loop-ensemble scaling limits of near-critical percolation in the plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15209)
*Chang-Long Yao*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明了三角晶格（triangular lattice）上近临界位点渗流（near-critical site percolation）的全部团簇边界（cluster boundaries）在环系综空间（loop-ensemble space）中，当网格尺寸趋于零时存在唯一的标度极限（scaling limit），并由此推出圆盘内探索路径（exploration path）的分布收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作回应了 Camia、Fontes 和 Newman（2006）提出的问题，即近临界渗流的团簇边界集合是否存在环系综意义下的标度极限。此前已有近临界渗流的增强网络（enhanced networks）与四交叉标度极限（quad-crossing scaling limits）结果，但团簇边界整体的极限尚未解决。

Method: 核心思路是在近临界耦合（near-critical coupling）下构造截断环系综（cut-off loop ensembles），其构造基于 Garban、Pete 和 Schramm（2018）建立的增强网络与四交叉标度极限，并依赖对团簇链（cluster chains）几何性质的研究。

Result: 证明得到三角晶格近临界位点渗流团簇边界在网格尺寸趋于零时于环系综空间中唯一收敛；同时得到圆盘内近临界渗流探索路径依分布收敛到连续路径。极限环系综被证明具有旋转不变性（rotation invariance）、尺度协变性（scale covariance）与有限链接性（finite chaining）。

Conclusion: 论文解决了 Camia–Fontes–Newman 的公开问题，建立了近临界渗流团簇边界的环系综标度极限及探索路径的连续极限，并讨论了该极限结果与证明策略的若干应用。

Abstract: We prove that the collection of all cluster boundaries of near-critical site percolation (with a fixed parameter) on the triangular lattice has a unique scaling limit in the loop-ensemble space, as the mesh size tends to 0. This answers a problem proposed by Camia, Fontes and Newman (2006). Using this, we also prove that near-critical percolation exploration path in a disk converges in distribution to a continuous path as the mesh size goes to 0. The main idea of our proof is to construct the cut-off loop ensembles under the near-critical coupling, based on the enhanced networks and the quad-crossing scaling limits of near-critical percolation constructed by Garban, Pete and Schramm (2018); the proof also relies on the study of the geometry of cluster chains. Some properties of the limiting loop ensemble, including rotation invariance, scale covariance and finite chaining, are established. Moreover, a few applications of our scaling-limit result and proof strategy are discussed.

</details>

Abstract: We develop an iterated forward-backward stochastic differential equation (FBSDE) approach to the two-dimensional infinite-volume Abelian-Higgs model in Coulomb gauge. We prove tightness of the joint renormalized lattice gauge-Higgs laws, after continuum extension, as the lattice spacing tends to zero and the volume tends to infinity. We represent the lattice measure as the joint terminal law of the FBSDEs and establish uniform moment bounds for the corresponding terminal shifts. For the Higgs field, we combine a low-high frequency decomposition with covariant energy estimates to obtain bounds with sufficiently mild dependence on the gauge shift. For the gauge field, we establish high-frequency estimates for the effective force and combine them with quantitative diamagnetic control in the infrared.

The proof goes through three steps of a Tetris map, which lets cubes drop according to gravity in each of the three axes, yielding Solid-On-Solid (SOS), Half-Monotone SOS (HMSOS) and monotone surfaces (dimers). We establish GFF limits sequentially, in particular resolving the analogous problem for the $(2+1)$D SOS. Abstract: We present a new, simple and self-contained technique for proving a mass gap for Euclidean quantum field theories in the high-temperature regime using the stochastic quantisation approach. As an application, we establish exponential decay of spatial correlations for the fractional $Φ^4$ model on the plane in the Wick-ordering regime. The key idea is to analyse the propagation of perturbations using the linearised stochastic quantisation equation. Spatial propagation is controlled by an auxiliary random walk, while the potentially destabilising effect of the renormalised nonlinearity is controlled by combining dissipative energy estimates for the nonlinear dynamic with the Boué-Dupuis variational formula.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倾斜项 \(g_{x,r}\) 以 \(\exp(-C r)\) 随半径指数衰减，但半径 \(r\) 的球内含 \(\exp(c r^2)\) 个构型，使得标准团簇展开（cluster expansion）因构型数增长而失效；因此需要新的重求和（resummation）方法来处理此类多体倾斜。

Method: 将配分函数重求和为聚合物模型，其聚合物定义为若干球之连通并集的外边界（outer boundaries），并以沿边界的自旋进行装饰；证明仅依赖基础测度的域马尔可夫性（domain Markov property），并发展了作者近期在 Grassmann 变量重整化群（RG）分析中所用的重求和论证。

Result: 得到按边界长度指数衰减的聚合物权重；作为应用，给出高温与低温 Ising 模型多体倾斜的收敛团簇展开、无穷体积极限（infinite-volume limit）的存在性以及相关性的指数衰减（exponential decay of correlations）。

Abstract: We consider a nearest-neighbor Gibbs measure on a region in $\mathbb{Z}^2$ (e.g., the Ising model) tilted by $\sum_{x,r} g_{x,r}$, where $g_{x,r}$ is an arbitrary function of the spins in the ball of radius $r$ about $x$, with $\|g_{x,r}\|_\infty\leq \exp(-C r)$: an exponential decay in the radius of the ball rather than in its volume. Standard cluster expansions fail for such many-body tilts, as a ball of radius $r$ carries $\exp(c r^2)$ configurations. We show that the partition function can be resummed into a polymer model whose polymers are outer boundaries of connected unions of balls, decorated by the spins along them, with weights decaying exponentially in the boundary length. The proof uses only the domain Markov property of the base measure; it develops a version of this resummation argument that appeared in a recent work of the authors in the context of RG analysis of Grassmann variables. As applications, we show convergent cluster expansions for many-body tilts of Ising models at high and low temperatures; existence of infinite-volume limits; and exponential decay of correlations.

</details>


### [13] [The bias interval of the elephant random walk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14576)
*Arjun Pemmasani*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文证明了 Guérin 等关于超扩散大象随机游走（superdiffusive elephant random walk）的双峰极限律（bimodal limit law）猜想：双峰初始偏置构成以二分之一为中心的区间，并给出该区间非空的内存阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Guérin、Laulin、Raschel 与 Simon 猜想使双峰极限律出现的初始偏置（initial bias）构成以 1/2 为中心的区间，并将此问题视为具有挑战性；本文旨在证明这一猜想并刻画该区间。

Conclusion: 猜想得到完整证明，并补充了定量刻画：偏置边界（bias boundary）关于内存连续，且在内存趋于 1 时至少以二次速度趋于零。

Abstract: Guérin, Laulin, Raschel, and Simon conjectured that the initial biases giving the superdiffusive elephant random walk a bimodal limit law form an interval centered at one half, and they described the problem as challenging. We prove this. For every superdiffusive memory, strongly biased starts are unimodal, and the limit density has two local maxima exactly when the bias lies in a proper open interval. Outside it, including its endpoints, the density has a unique local maximum. The interval is nonempty exactly above a single memory threshold, numerically about $0.6688$, whose uniqueness follows from a positive-weight integral for the central curvature of the fair-start density. We control sign changes in the finite walk's first and second differences, including those created at the boundary, and pass them to the limit. The bias boundary is continuous and tends to zero at least quadratically fast as memory tends to one.

</details>

### [14] [Bayesian inference with a polymer probabilistic computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14782)
*Travis Wanless,Yuriy Bandera,Lubomir Kostal,Stephen H. Foulger*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该工作报道了基于溶液法共轭聚合物忆阻器（conjugated-polymer memristor）构建的概率计算机，四个聚合物概率比特（p-bit）在闭环吉布斯采样（Gibbs sampling）中在线跟踪漂移，无需针对目标拟合参数即可再现理想 p-bit 的分布与自相关统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）需要从后验分布采样，而确定性硬件只能通过模拟随机性实现；已有的 p-bit 硬件网络多采用无机器件、面向优化或学习任务，且未参照有限采样所预期的统计涨落进行评判。

Method: 用溶液加工的共轭聚合物忆阻器构建概率计算机，四个聚合物 p-bit 在线跟踪漂移并耦合于闭环吉布斯采样回路，对十六个状态上的竞争性、简并性与协同性后验分布进行采样，并通过软件重编程复现可逆 AND 门。

Result: 采样统计在分布与自相关两方面均落在理想 p-bit 的预期范围内，且未对目标拟合任何参数；该一致性延伸至事先预测结果的模拟传感器读数，包括指向错误答案的读数；复现 AND 门时温度亦为预先固定而非拟合。

Conclusion: 聚合物 p-bit 为可印刷、大面积、能与传感器协同进行推断的概率计算机提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Bayesian inference weighs competing explanations of noisy data by sampling from a posterior distribution, a task that deterministic hardware performs only by simulating randomness. Probabilistic bits (p-bits) supply this randomness physically, but the few hardware networks of p-bits built so far use inorganic devices, address optimization or learning, and are judged without reference to the spread expected from finite sampling. Here we report a probabilistic computer built from solution-processed conjugated-polymer memristors. Four polymer p-bits, tracked online for drift and coupled in a closed Gibbs-sampling loop, sample competing, degenerate and cooperative posteriors over sixteen states with statistics that fall within the range of ideal p-bits, both in their distribution and in their autocorrelation, without any parameter fitted to the target. This agreement extends to new simulated sensor readings whose outcome was predicted before each run, including readings that point away from the true answer. Reprogrammed in software, the same circuit and controller reproduces the invertible AND gate of earlier p-bit networks, with its temperature fixed in advance rather than fitted. Polymer p-bits thus open a route to printed, large-area probabilistic computers that infer alongside their sensors.

</details>

### [15] [Little-Parks Oscillations and Parity Effects in Mesoscopic Superconducting Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14597)
*Iogann Tolbatov,Luca Salasnich*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文用Ginzburg-Landau展开解析研究准一维介观环中的Little-Parks效应，将Tc(Φ)振荡分解为离散拓扑宇称项与连续质心动能项，并追踪二者从弱耦合BCS极限至中等耦合区间的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Little-Parks效应中拓扑宇称干涉与超流质心动能两种机制各自的角色及其随耦合强度的演化尚缺乏统一解析描述，尤其缺乏跨越弱耦合BCS极限到中等耦合区间的闭式结果。

Method: 采用含扭曲边界条件（twisted boundary conditions）的Ginzburg-Landau（GL）展开，同时纳入有限尺寸离散拓扑宇称干涉与超流引起的连续质心（center-of-mass, CM）动能移动，推导在GL框架内对可变相互作用强度成立的Tc(Φ)闭式表达式。

Result: LP响应包含两项：由关联长度比ξ/R控制的离散拓扑宇称效应，以及按1/R^2标度的连续动能项；耦合增强使ξ≪R时库珀对在几何上对边界约束失明，宇称调制被指数压制，但凝聚体仍需获得依赖磁通的宏观质心动量，故Tc保留由超流宏观动能主导的代数型LP调制。

Conclusion: 拓扑宇称与质心动能两种贡献随耦合强度此消彼长：宇称项指数衰减而动能项持续存在；理论适用性受限于平均场GL在强耦合极限附近、相位涨落主导之前的失效。

Abstract: We analytically investigate the Little-Parks (LP) effect, the periodic oscillation of the superconducting transition temperature $T_c$ driven by an applied magnetic flux $Φ$, from the weak-coupling Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) limit to the intermediate-coupling regime in a mesoscopic, quasi-one-dimensional ring. Utilizing a Ginzburg-Landau (GL) expansion with twisted boundary conditions, we correctly incorporate both the discrete finite-size topological parity interference and the continuous center-of-mass (CM) kinetic energy shift induced by the supercurrent. We derive a closed-form expression for $T_c(Φ)$ valid within the GL framework across variable interaction strengths. We demonstrate that the LP response comprises two distinct physical contributions: a discrete topological parity effect governed by the correlation length ratio $ξ/R$, and a continuous kinetic energy term scaling as $1/R^2$. As the coupling increases and the spatial correlation length of the pairs contracts ($ξ\ll R$), the constituent pairs become geometrically blind to the global boundary constraint, leading to an exponential suppression of the discrete parity modulation. However, the requirement that the condensate acquires a flux-dependent macroscopic CM momentum persists. This ensures that the critical temperature $T_c$ maintains an algebraic LP modulation driven predominantly by the macroscopic kinetic energy of the supercurrent. We bound our theoretical approach by discussing the breakdown of mean-field GL validity before phase fluctuations dominate near the strong-coupling limit.

</details>


### [16] [Symmetry-Enabled Rolling of Composite Bosons: A Route to Superconductivity from Strong Repulsion with $T_c$ Proportional to the Electron Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14615)
*Zhiyu Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作提出“滚动（rolling）”机制：在满足特定对称性要求的晶格上，由库仑斥力（Coulomb repulsion）形成的三电子一空穴（3e1h）玻色型复合体可通过单粒子跃迁移动而无须高能中间态，从而在强束缚极限下保持有限质量，使超导转变温度（Tc）可正比于电子带宽（electron bandwidth）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受强库仑斥力体系中发现的超导现象启发，作者追问这类斥力能否产生Tc仅受电子带宽限制的超导。常规图像下，强束缚使复合体只能经由高阶过程移动、质量变大，超流刚度（superfluid stiffness）受限，Tc远低于带宽，因此需要突破这一困境的机制。

Method: 作者从对称性出发论证可通过“滚动”运动绕开束缚-质量权衡，推导出滚动的判据，并据此构造出全部十四个支持此类滚动的最小晶格模型；随后证明可滚动的3e1h复合体可在基态中被稳定，并提出在双栅双层（dual-gate bilayer）体系中实现，用精确数值方法（exact numerics）计算Tc。

Result: 在受控区域（束缚远强于载流子动能）内，滚动机制使复合体质量在无限强束缚下仍保持有限，从而实现Tc与电子带宽成正比；数值结果表明，双栅双层方案中Tc可达电子跃迁（hopping）能量的百分之几。同时给出了滚动判据与全部十四个最小模型的分类构造。

Conclusion: 可滚动性（rollability）被确立为在强排斥电子体系中寻找与设计超导体的指导原则，所构造的最小模型可作为原型（prototypes）用于后续材料与器件设计。

Abstract: Motivated by superconductors discovered in systems with a strong Coulomb repulsion, we ask whether such a repulsion can give rise to superconductivity with a transition temperature $T_c$ bounded only by the electron bandwidth. Coulomb repulsion can produce pairing as it binds electrons to holes. If each hole binds three electrons into a three-electron-one-hole (3e1h) composite, the condensation of these bosonic composites can give rise to superconductivity. However, the resulting $T_c$ falls far below the electron bandwidth in the controlled regime where the binding is much stronger than the carriers' kinetic energy. This is because, on a lattice, a tightly bound composite usually has to move through a higher-order process where the intermediate state costs an energy of order the binding energy, so that the strong binding makes the composite heavy, limiting the superfluid stiffness and hence $T_c$. Here, we show that symmetry offers a way around this tradeoff. In lattices satisfying certain symmetry requirements, a composite can move by single-particle hops without any costly intermediate state, a motion we call rolling. We show that rollable 3e1h composites can indeed be stabilized in the ground state. We then propose to realize such superconductivity in a dual-gate bilayer, and use exact numerics to show that $T_c$ can reach a few percent of the electron hopping. These results establish rollability as a guiding principle in the search for and design of superconductors in strongly repelling electron systems, with the minimal models constructed here as prototypes.

Abstract: We study the interplay between pair density wave (PDW) and uniform superconductivity on the kagome lattice within a single-orbital $t$-$W$-$J$ model near the $p$-type van Hove filling, where $W$ and $J$ denote effective pairing attractions on lattice sites and nearest-neighbor bonds, respectively. While onsite attraction favors zero-momentum pairing through the conventional BCS instability, nearest-neighbor attraction naturally promotes finite-momentum pairing due to the kagome geometry and sublattice interference. The model therefore provides a framework for exploring the competition and coexistence between uniform superconductivity and PDW order. Self-consistent mean-field calculations in a $2\times2$ supercell reveal that the uniform component reorganizes both the amplitudes and phase structure of the three $F_1$ PDW components, giving rise to superconducting states absent in the pure bond-pairing limit. A Ginzburg--Landau analysis identifies symmetry-allowed couplings that can favor this reorganization. The resulting states exhibit distinct charge modulations and quasiparticle spectra, while the chiral PDW undergoes topological transitions as the onsite attraction increases. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习可预测候选超导体，但除临界温度（critical temperature）外缺乏经实验验证的训练数据，制约了面向下一代技术（如核聚变、无损耗电子学）的超导材料发现。

Result: 数据显示能隙比中位数由常规超导体的 3.6 升至铜氧化物（cuprates）的 6.4；28% 的上临界场超过泡利极限（Pauli limit）；临界温度存在与超流密度（superfluid density）成正比的上限。预测器在临界温度（R²_log = 0.83）与上临界场（R²_log = 0.69）上均取得较好精度，且迁移学习带来提升。

Conclusion: 在已知超导体系（superconducting families）内，仅凭组分信息即可对上述两个参数进行候选筛选；AutoSuperCon 为数千种超导体提供了物理洞察与数据驱动发现的统一基础。

Abstract: New superconductors could unlock next-generation technologies from nuclear fusion to lossless electronics. Machine learning can predict candidate superconductors but lacks validated experimental training data beyond the critical temperature. Here we present AutoSuperCon, a database of eight superconducting parameters, containing 20354 entries for 6793 superconductors. The data were extracted from over 47000 arXiv preprints using a large language model and validated against reference datasets ($R^2 = 0.96$ with NIMS SuperCon). Using AutoSuperCon, we show that median gap ratios rise from 3.6 in conventional superconductors to 6.4 in cuprates, 28% of upper critical fields exceed the Pauli limit, and critical temperatures lie below a ceiling proportional to the superfluid density. Further, we train composition-based predictors for the critical temperature ($R^2_{\log}=0.83$) and the upper critical field ($R^2_{\log}=0.69$), boosted by transfer learning. Within known superconducting families, composition alone is sufficient to screen candidates for both parameters. AutoSuperCon thus enables physical insight and data-driven discovery across thousands of superconductors.

</details>

*Vladislav Poliakov,Inti Sodemann Villadiego,T. Senthil*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在二维材料中发现手性超导体（chiral superconductors），促使作者考察能带几何非平庸时无序效应对超导电性的作用，并检验既有无序理论的适用性。

Method: 通过引入正常态布洛赫函数（Bloch functions）的结构来推广安德森定理与Abrikosov-Gorkov无序诱导配对破坏描述，并将结果应用于扭角MoTe2；同时分析随机势无序能否在手性时间反演破缺（time-reversal-breaking）超导体中成核涡旋。

Result: 对扭角MoTe2，BCS配对机制与实测正常态剩余电阻率（residual resistivity）存在冲突；对常规BCS机制稳定的超导体，弱无序不诱导涡旋玻璃相，这与由任意子液体（anyon fluid）形成的超导情形形成对比。

Conclusion: 能带几何会显著修改无序诱导的配对破坏图像；扭角MoTe2的配对机制可能需要超越简单BCS解释，且涡旋玻璃相的出现可作为区分不同超导形成机制（如BCS配对与任意子液体）的判据。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent discovery of chiral superconductors in 2d materials, we study the effects of disorder on superconductivity in materials with non-trivial band geometry. First, we show how Anderson's theorem and the conventional Abrikosov-Gorkov description of disorder-induced pair breaking are modified by the structure of the normal-state Bloch functions. We apply these results to twisted MoTe2 and show that a BCS pairing mechanism for the observed superconductor is in tension with the measured normal state residual resistivity. Second, we study whether random potential disorder can nucleate superconducting vortices in a chiral time- reversal-breaking superconductor. We show that, for a superconductor stabilized by the usual BCS mechanism, weak disorder does not induce a vortex glass phase, in contrast to superconductivity formed from an anyon fluid.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索非常规超导体 UTe$_2$ 的配对机制（pairing mechanism）及可能的拓扑性质（topological properties），通过考察这些性质对电感与电阻通道的影响来获得信息。

Method: 采用共振腔微扰技术（resonant cavity perturbation techniques）在微波频段对多种 UTe$_2$ 单晶测量复合表面阻抗（composite surface impedance），使电感与电阻通道处于可比且可等价处理的地位，并通过大量共振模式（resonant modes）研究复合电动力学（composite electrodynamics）的方向依赖性。

Result: 低温下穿透深度（penetration depth）与表面电阻（surface resistance）均呈现相似的幂律温度依赖，综合各模式后为二次且各向同性；同时残余损耗（residual loss）呈现明显且系统的各向异性。二次幂律结果与弱散射下费米面（Fermi surface）上单对点节点（point nodes）的情形不一致。

Conclusion: 作者提出拓扑超导（topological superconductivity）可作为同时解释各向同性幂律与各向异性残余损耗的一种可能诠释，但摘要未给出确定性结论。

Abstract: We explore the pairing mechanism and possible topological properties of the unconventional superconductor UTe$_2$ through their influence on both the inductive and resistive channels using resonant cavity perturbation techniques. We measure a composite surface impedance for a variety of UTe$_2$ single crystals at microwave frequencies, where the inductive and resistive channels are comparable and can be treated on equal footing. By studying the composite electrodynamics for a large number of resonant modes, we are able to provide a comprehensive picture of the directional dependence of the excitations out of the ground state. We find similar power-law temperature dependencies at low-temperature for both the penetration depth and surface resistance, which in aggregate across modes, are quadratic and isotropic. On the other hand, we find a distinct and systematic anisotropy of the residual loss. The power-law results are inconsistent with a single pair of point nodes on the Fermi surface under weak scattering. We discuss the possibility of topological superconductivity as an interpretation of these isotropic power-laws in conjunction with the anisotropic residual loss.

</details>


### [22] [Quantum geometry drives orbital paramagnetism in superconducting fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16387)
*Zi-Ting Sun,Ying-Ming Xie,Chunli Huang,Naoto Nagaosa*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文用高斯涨落理论研究多分量超导体在转变温度之上的轨道磁响应，发现超导序参量不同分量间的干涉（与配对量子几何相关）可产生顺磁贡献，并在特定kagome模型中克服常规抗磁贡献，形成净涨落顺磁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为超导体在转变温度之上因短暂形成的库珀对（Cooper pairs）而表现出随温度趋近转变点而发散的轨道抗磁磁化率（diamagnetic susceptibility）；本文旨在检验多分量超导体中涨落是否可能反而产生顺磁响应。

Method: 采用高斯涨落理论（Gaussian fluctuation theory）分析一般性多分量超导体在转变温度之上的行为，并应用于具有两个手性配对模式（chiral pairing modes）的kagome超导体模型。

Result: 识别出两种竞争贡献：库珀对质心运动带来的常规抗磁贡献，以及不同序参量分量间干涉产生的顺磁贡献，后者与配对量子几何（pair quantum geometry）相关。在时间反演对称（time-reversal symmetry）保持的母态下，顺磁贡献可超过抗磁贡献而呈净涨落顺磁性；当通过弱环流序（loop-current order）破坏轨道时间反演对称后，两手性模式简并解除，低温下低能模式主导涨落，单分量物理回归使抗磁性占优。

Conclusion: 研究提供了超导体中涨落顺磁性（fluctuation paramagnetism）的一种机制，并表明轨道磁响应可作为探测库珀对量子几何的手段。

Abstract: Superconductors expel magnetic fields through the Meissner effect. Even slightly above the superconducting transition temperature, electrons can briefly form Cooper pairs, leading to an orbital diamagnetic susceptibility that diverges as the transition is approached. In this work, we use Gaussian fluctuation theory to study a generic multicomponent superconductor above its transition temperature and show that fluctuations can instead produce a paramagnetic response. We identify two competing contributions: the usual diamagnetic contribution from the center-of-mass motion of Cooper pairs and a paramagnetic contribution from interference between different components of the superconducting order parameter. The latter is related to pair quantum geometry, which describes how the internal components of the Cooper pair change with momentum. We then apply our theory to a model of a kagome superconductor with two chiral pairing modes. When the parent state preserves time-reversal symmetry, the paramagnetic contribution can outweigh the conventional diamagnetic contribution, producing net fluctuation paramagnetism. However, when the orbital time-reversal symmetry is broken, e.g., by forming a weak loop-current order, the degeneracy between the two chiral modes is then lifted. Upon cooling toward the superconducting transition, the lower-energy mode dominates the fluctuations, and diamagnetism wins as single-component physics takes over. Our results provide a mechanism for fluctuation paramagnetism in superconductors and show that the orbital magnetic response can probe the quantum geometry of Cooper pairs.

</details>


### [23] [Noise-Protected Superconducting Qudits from Higher-Harmonic Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16563)
*Hamed Vakili*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文提出利用电流–相位关系的二次谐波，在由三个约瑟夫森结组成的超导环路中实现受强保护的 qutrit（三能级量子单元），并指出二次谐波的正负号可作为受保护超导 qudit 的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Qudit 相较 qubit 在每个电路元件中可存储更多信息，但其对噪声的敏感性仍是实现容错量子计算（fault-tolerant quantum computing）前必须克服的障碍，因此需要设计对弛豫与退相位具有保护的 qudit。

Method: 基于三结超导环路，利用电流–相位关系（current–phase relation）的二次谐波，针对正负号两种情形分别设计保护方案；并结合微观计算考察栅压对二次谐波符号的调控，提出交错磁约瑟夫森结（altermagnetic Josephson junctions）作为候选平台。

Result: 二次谐波为正时，结间存在小幅不对称的简单环路即可提供可调、对称性保护的 qutrit；为负时，需引入弱的对称性破缺项（如弱约瑟夫森二极管效应）才能实现受保护 qutrit。前者具有严格的电荷选择定则（charge selection rule），后者对电荷噪声近乎不敏感且准粒子误差可被纠正。微观计算表明栅压可选择二次谐波的符号，但栅压噪声随之为主要退相位来源。

Conclusion: 两种设计方案各有取舍，优选取决于所用材料，且均需可观的二次谐波；二次谐波的符号被确立为受保护超导 qudit 的设计原则。

Abstract: Qudits, which generalize qubits to $d$ levels, store more information per circuit element than qubits. As with qubits, however, their sensitivity to noise must be overcome before they can be used for fault-tolerant quantum computing. Here we show that, by exploiting the second harmonic of the current--phase relation, a superconducting loop of three Josephson junctions can host qutrits strongly protected against relaxation, while dephasing limits their coherence. When the second harmonic is positive, a simple loop with a small asymmetry between the junctions provides a tunable, symmetry-protected qutrit. When it is negative, a protected qutrit can be realized by adding a small symmetry-breaking term, such as a weak Josephson diode effect. The two designs involve different trade-offs: the first has an exact charge selection rule, whereas the second is nearly insensitive to charge noise and its quasiparticle errors can be corrected. The preferable choice therefore depends on the materials used. Both require a sizable second harmonic to begin with, and we identify altermagnetic Josephson junctions as a candidate platform. Finally, microscopic calculations show that a gate voltage selects the sign of the second harmonic in these junctions, although gate-voltage noise then becomes the leading source of dephasing. These findings establish the sign of the second harmonic as a design principle for protected superconducting qudits.

</details>


### [24] [Sign changing pairing in single layer FeSe/SrTiO3 revealed by nonmagnetic impurity bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16679)
*Huimin Zhang,Zhuozhi Ge,Michael Weinert,Lian Li*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过扫描隧道显微镜/谱（scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy, STM/STS）研究单层FeSe/SrTiO3中的Fe空位缺陷束缚态，发现其无磁场响应，支持变号（sign-changing）配对对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层FeSe/SrTiO3高温超导体的配对对称性存在争议：是常规的保持符号s波（sign-preserving s-wave），还是非常规的变号（sign-changing）配对，尚无定论。

Method: 采用分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy）生长单层FeSe/SrTiO3样品，利用扫描隧道显微镜/谱（STM/STS）探测Fe平面内非磁性Fe空位缺陷诱导的束缚态（bound states），并施加磁场观察其能量响应。

Result: 观测到稳健的能隙内束缚态（in-gap bound states），呈现强空间电子-空穴不对称性：空穴态为二重对称（two-fold symmetry），电子态为四重对称（four-fold symmetry）；束缚态在磁场下无能量移动或劈裂。

Conclusion: 非磁性缺陷束缚态无磁场响应与变号序参量（sign-changing order parameter）一致，为单层FeSe/SrTiO3非常规变号配对提供了证据。

Abstract: The discovery of high-temperature superconductivity in single layer FeSe epitaxially grown on SrTiO3(001) substrates has instigated extensive debate over whether its pairing symmetry is conventional sign-preserving s-wave or unconventional sign-changing. Here we probe the pairing state for single layer FeSe/SrTiO3 grown by molecular beam epitaxy using scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy. We observe robust in-gap bound states induced by non-magnetic Fe-vacancy defects within the Fe-plane, which exhibit strong spatial electron-hole asymmetry with two-fold symmetry in hole states and four-fold in electron states. The bound states exhibit no energy shift or splitting under an applied magnetic field, consistent with a sign-changing order parameter.

</details>

*N. K. Patra,Sneha Pradhan,P. K. Sahoo,Kai Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在对称远平行（symmetric teleparallel）$f(Q)$引力框架下，结合稠密物质状态方程（EOS）不确定性，对核物质与修改引力参数进行联合贝叶斯推断，并给出中子星（NS）观测量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在一致地处理稠密物质状态方程的不确定性，以量化核物理与修改引力之间的相互影响，并将对称远平行引力与核物理及多信使中子星观测进行系统对照。

Method: 聚焦线性$f(Q)$模型，采用核饱和密度附近的泰勒展开状态方程，结合核饱和性质、手性有效场论（chiral effective field theory）纯中子物质计算、重离子碰撞、经验核数据、GW170817潮汐形变后验及NICER质量—半径测量，对核物质参数与引力参数做联合贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）。

Result: 对典型$1.4\,M_\odot$中子星得$R_{1.4}=11.28^{+0.35}_{-0.31}\,\mathrm{km}$、$Λ_{1.4}=151.4^{+44.9}_{-31.2}$，最大质量$M_{\rm max}=2.22^{+0.29}_{-0.16}\,M_\odot$；修改引力参数与多数星体观测仅呈弱直接关联，而引力部分内部存在明显简并（degeneracy）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，计入状态方程不确定性对于刻画稠密物质物理与修改引力的相互作用至关重要，并提供了一套将对称远平行引力与核物理及多信使中子星观测相检验的系统框架。

Abstract: We investigate neutron star (NS) properties in symmetric teleparallel $f(Q)$ gravity while consistently accounting for uncertainties in the dense matter equation of state (EOS). We focus on the linear $f(Q)$ model and perform a joint Bayesian inference of the nuclear matter and modified gravity parameters using a Taylor expansion EOS around nuclear saturation density. The analysis incorporates complementary constraints from nuclear saturation properties, chiral effective field theory calculations of pure neutron matter, heavy-ion collision, and empirical nuclear data, the GW170817 tidal deformability posterior, and NICER mass--radius measurements. The resulting posterior distributions simultaneously constrain the dense matter and gravity sectors and quantify their correlations within a common framework. For a canonical $1.4\,M_\odot$ NS, we obtain $R_{1.4}=11.28^{+0.35}_{-0.31}\,\mathrm{km}$ and $Λ_{1.4}=151.4^{+44.9}_{-31.2}$, while the maximum mass is constrained to $M_{\rm max}=2.22^{+0.29}_{-0.16}\,M_\odot$. The modified gravity parameters exhibit only weak direct correlations with most stellar observables, while a pronounced degeneracy remains within the gravity sector. Our results show that accounting for EOS uncertainties is essential for quantifying the interplay between dense matter physics and modified gravity and provide a systematic framework for confronting symmetric teleparallel gravity with nuclear and multimessenger NS observations.

</details>

*Maxim Churilov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文提出一种由有向类光到达时间（directed null arrival times）与时空体积重建洛伦兹几何（Lorentzian geometry）的局域层析方案，在给定时钟分层与空间坐标规范下，静态数据可恢复光学度规，稳态情形可分离光学几何与位移，并给出有限方向采样所需测量数及误差上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一种可操作、规范条件化的层析定理（gauge-conditional tomography theorem），将洛伦兹几何的重建归结为可用标量到达测量与体积数据完成的局域逆问题，并明确其适用边界。

Method: 假设已给定时钟分层（clock foliation）与空间坐标规范，利用有向零到达时间与归一化体积密度进行局域重建；对含时 ADM 度规（ADM metric）推导精确的未来零指标线（null indicatrix）及其代数逆，并提出最少有限方向采样方案与半代数一致性检验（semialgebraic consistency test）。

Result: 静态到达数据可恢复光学度规（optical metric）$\bar h=N^{-2}h$，体积密度确定剩余共形尺度至整体常数；稳态下 Randers 到达函数分离光学几何与位移。$d$ 维空间需 $d(d+3)/2$ 次单向到达测量，且在该标量测量类内为稳定局域恢复所必需，并具备带认证鲁棒性与拒绝裕度的一致性检验。

Conclusion: 短时冻结估计与有限分辨率逆给出度规误差 $O(a/\varepsilon+\varepsilon+\zeta)$，优化后为 $O(\sqrt a+\zeta)$；代数与数值检验复现了反演与稳定性公式。该结果为规范条件化的操作层析定理，但不从无背景量子理论推断时钟或到达预言机（arrival oracle）。

Abstract: We formulate a local reconstruction of Lorentzian geometry from directed null arrival times and spacetime volume, assuming a supplied clock foliation and spatial coordinate gauge. Static arrival data recover the optical metric $\bar h=N^{-2}h$, while a normalized volume density fixes the remaining conformal scale up to a global constant. In the stationary case, the Randers arrival function separates optical geometry and shift. For time-dependent ADM metrics we derive the exact future null indicatrix, its algebraic inverse, and a minimal finite-direction sampling scheme: $d(d+3)/2$ one-way arrival measurements suffice in $d$ spatial dimensions, are necessary for stable local recovery within the specified scalar-measurement class, and admit an exact semialgebraic consistency test with certified robustness and rejection margins under bounded measurement error. A short-time freezing estimate and finite-resolution inverse give metric error $O(a/ε+ε+ζ)$ for arrival error $a$, probe scale $ε$, and volume error $ζ$; optimizing yields $O(\sqrt a+ζ)$. Algebraic and numerical checks reproduce the inversion and stability formulas. The result is an operational, gauge-conditional tomography theorem; it does not infer the clocks or arrival oracle from a background-free quantum theory.

</details>


### [27] [Energy exchange confined to the perturbations: the frozen dark sector confronted with Planck, DESI, supernovae and cosmic shear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14556)
*M. G. Richarte,L. F. Guimarães,S. J. Landau,M. Leizerovich*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对“冻结模型（frozen model）”给出宇宙学约束。该模型中暗物质与暗能量仅在扰动层次交换能量，背景膨胀历史与平坦ΛCDM（Lambda cold dark matter）一致。结果显示，耦合参数Σ的显著非零偏好主要来自Planck数据，可缓解与DESI的张力，但会加剧与弱透镜剪切振幅的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是检验一种背景与ΛCDM相同、但扰动层次存在暗物质—暗能量能量交换的模型，以考察该耦合能否解释Planck给出的低大尺度温度功率与透镜平滑现象，并缓解宇宙学数据集之间的张力。

Method: 采用无量纲耦合Σ（Σ<0增强、Σ>0抑制亚视界尺度暗物质密度反差增长）参数化模型，联合使用Planck 2018 CMB与透镜数据、DESI DR2重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillations, BAO）、DES五年超新星（Dovekie重校准）、KiDS-Legacy与DES Year 3宇宙剪切（cosmic shear）振幅，并对剪切振幅作模型增长修正。

Result: Planck单独给出Σ=-0.22±0.11；BAO与超新星不约束Σ。耦合使Planck–DESI张力由1.9σ降至1.0–1.5σ，Planck+超新星与DESI的张力由2.4–2.5σ降至1.7–2.1σ。联合Planck、DESI与超新星得Σ=-0.24±0.09、ln B=1.5，但排除最强耦合区域后该偏好消失。引入剪切后Σ=0.01±0.08、S_8=0.808±0.025、ln B=-1.3（倾向ΛCDM），且Planck–剪切张力由1.9σ升至2.5–2.7σ。H_0与宇宙年龄在各数据组合下均与ΛCDM一致，SH0ES差异仍为4.4σ。

Conclusion: 该耦合的偏好不稳健，且与弱透镜剪切振幅观测存在张力。无剪切情形下，耦合预言z=0.5处积分Sachs–Wolfe（integrated Sachs–Wolfe）振幅约为ΛCDM之半，并在最低层析bin中增强剪切信号；这些特征与星系团计数（cluster counts）的红移分布可在不依赖涨落振幅的情况下独立检验该耦合。

Abstract: We present cosmological constraints on the frozen model, in which dark matter and dark energy exchange energy only at the level of the perturbations. The expansion history is that of flat $Λ$CDM, while on sub-horizon scales a dimensionless coupling $Σ$ enhances ($Σ<0$) or suppresses ($Σ>0$) the growth of the dark-matter density contrast. We use Planck 2018 CMB and lensing data, DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO), the DES five-year supernovae (Dovekie recalibration), and the KiDS-Legacy and DES Year 3 cosmic-shear amplitudes, corrected for the model's growth. Planck alone gives $Σ=-0.22\pm0.11$, a preference driven by the lensing smoothing of the acoustic peaks and the low large-scale temperature power. Since BAO and supernovae do not constrain $Σ$, the coupling acts only through the Planck posterior, lowering the Planck--DESI tension from $1.9σ$ to $1.0$--$1.5σ$, and that between Planck plus supernovae and DESI from $2.4$--$2.5σ$ to $1.7$--$2.1σ$. Combining Planck, DESI and supernovae we find $Σ=-0.24\pm0.09$ and $\ln B=1.5$ relative to $Λ$CDM, a preference that vanishes when the strongest-coupling region is excluded. The enhanced growth, however, raises the Planck--shear tension from $1.9σ$ to $2.5$--$2.7σ$. With shear included, $Σ=0.01\pm0.08$ and $S_8=0.808\pm0.025$, while $\ln B=-1.3$ favors $Λ$CDM. $H_0$ and the age of the Universe agree with $Λ$CDM for every data combination, and the SH0ES discrepancy remains at $4.4σ$. Without shear, the favored coupling predicts half the $Λ$CDM integrated Sachs--Wolfe amplitude at $z=0.5$ and an enhanced shear signal in the lowest tomographic bins. These signatures and the redshift distribution of cluster counts test the coupling independently of the amplitude of the fluctuations.

</details>

*Filippo Camilloni,Konrad Topolski,Keneth Miler,Luciano Rezzolla*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文指出黑洞—中子星双星并合中，末态黑洞自旋随中子星自旋增大而减小，违反黑洞双星中“自旋随组分自旋单调增加”的直觉规律，机制为潮汐瓦解非线性动力学导致的角动量抛射损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨道角动量与自旋对齐的黑洞双星并合中，末态黑洞自旋随组分自旋单调增加是公认结论；本文旨在检验该直觉性规律是否适用于含中子星的混合双星系统。

Method: 采用大量具有不同自旋与质量比（mass ratio）的双星样本进行并合数值模拟分析，追踪末态黑洞自旋随中子星自旋的变化，并识别角动量损失的来源。

Result: 在黑洞—中子星双星（black hole–neutron star binaries）中，末态黑洞自旋随中子星自旋增大而减小；该反直觉行为源于潮汐瓦解（tidal disruption）的非线性动力学，其通过动力学抛射物（dynamical ejecta）造成显著角动量损失，部分情形下可与引力波（gravitational waves）辐射的角动量相当。

Conclusion: 恒星质量黑洞（stellar-mass black holes）在双星并合中的自旋演化不同于通常假设，更为复杂且更具研究价值，需重新审视相关自旋演化模型。

Abstract: It is well-known that in the merger of a black-hole binary with spins aligned with the orbital angular momentum, the spin of the final black hole always increases with the spin of the components. We show that this very intuitive behavior is violated in black hole--neutron star binaries, where the spin of the final black hole actually decreases as the spin of the neutron star increases. Using a large set of binaries with different spins and mass ratios, we identify the mechanism behind this surprising result in the nonlinear dynamics of the tidal disruption, which drives a substantial loss of angular momentum via dynamical ejecta, in some cases comparable to that radiated via gravitational waves. Our results highlight that the spin evolution of stellar-mass black holes involved in binary mergers is different from what normally assumed, far more complex, and interesting.

</details>


### [29] [Conic Tilings of Black Hole Images by Radial Closest Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14625)
*George N. Wong,Andrew Mummery*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文证明黑洞图像中某点的最短接近半径（closest-approach radius）等值线在标准 Bardeen 像坐标下呈圆锥曲线（conic section）之弧，并给出局部判据以筛选物理弧段；该结论覆盖 Kerr 度规与 Plebanski-Demianski（PD）族，且由 PD 场方程的系数恒等式保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞图像的每个像素对应一条光线，其回溯轨迹的最小径向坐标可衡量该光线对时空的探测深度；若这些等值线具有可解析的几何形状，则可脱离具体辐射模型，把返回图像直接转化为径向光子传播的几何地图。

Method: 将"径向速度为零"的条件作为完整圆锥曲线的定义，物理等值线取光线在遇到外侧转向点（outer turning point）之前即到达该半径的点集；对正 Killing 能量（Killing energy）与正质量情形，证明在径向度规函数为正、两端点运动均允许的连通正半径区域内，仅需对径向势（radial potential）斜率作局部检验即可选出物理弧段；该简化源于 PD 场方程施加的系数恒等式与主零方向（principal null directions）的对齐。

Result: 等值线在 Kerr 的标准 Bardeen 像坐标中、以及在 PD 族中有限半径观测者的自然局部像坐标中均为圆锥曲线弧；圆锥曲线族还给出一个单一多项式方程，可同时包含 Kerr 与 PD 中的临界曲线（critical curve）。文中另在跨越 r=0 的区域与可分（separable）的 Ovcharenko-Podolsky 延拓中给出反例，说明外侧转向点会使局部判据失效。

Conclusion: 返回图像因此可被理解为径向光子传播的几何映射，其结构不依赖于发射模型；局部斜率判据在满足设定条件的正半径区域内充分，但在跨 r=0 或存在外侧转向点的延拓中不成立。

Abstract: Each point in a black hole image corresponds to a light ray, whose minimum radial coordinate when traced backward from the observer measures how deeply it probes the spacetime. We show that contours of constant closest-approach radius form arcs of conic sections in standard Bardeen image coordinates for Kerr, and in natural local image coordinates for finite-radius observers in the broader Plebanski-Demianski (PD) family of Einstein-Maxwell solutions. At each radius, the condition of vanishing radial velocity defines a complete conic; the physical contour consists of those points whose rays reach that radius before encountering an outer turning point. For positive Killing energy and positive mass, we prove that throughout connected positive-radius regions with positive radial metric function and allowed motion at both endpoints, a local test of the radial potential's slope is sufficient to select the physical arcs. This simplification follows from a coefficient identity imposed by the PD field equations and alignment of the principal null directions. We give explicit counterexamples in a domain crossing r=0 and in the separable Ovcharenko-Podolsky extension, where an outer turning point invalidates the local test. The conic family also yields a single polynomial equation containing the critical curve in Kerr and PD. These results turn the returning image into a geometric map of radial photon propagation, independent of the emission model. (This abstract has been abridged to meet the arXiv character limit.)

</details>


### [30] [Post-adiabatic effects sourced by radiation reaction for inspiraling, eccentric binaries in post-Newtonian theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14627)
*Rohit S. Chandramouli,Nijaid Arredondo,Lidia J. Gomes Da Silva,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文利用重求和的多尺度分析（resummed multiple-scale analysis）从领头阶辐射反作用推导后绝热（post-adiabatic）修正，并揭示绝热近似（adiabatic approximation）的精度依赖于轨道初始相位，尤其对长寿命、高偏心率双星至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝热近似（轨道平均通量驱动偏心双星演化）在引力波（gravitational-wave）与天体物理建模中广泛使用，但其精度来源与适用条件缺乏系统刻画，尤其初始轨道相位对近似误差的影响尚不明确。

Method: 采用重求和的多尺度分析（与近恒等平均变换密切相关），从领头阶辐射反作用（radiation reaction）出发推导第一后绝热阶修正，并分析不同坐标下轨道要素的规范依赖性（gauge dependence）及波形极化形式的变化。

Result: 偏心率与半长轴的长期演化方程无显式一阶后绝热修正，其修正是通过初始条件进入解的；推导出可使同一物理事件在不同坐标下波形一致的引力波极化函数形式变化；在径向转折点（radial turning point）处，半长轴与偏心率的一阶后绝热振荡消失，密切能量与角动量与轨道平均值在二阶后绝热阶相符；在轨道其他位置初始化时绝热近似可积累显著退相位（dephasing），一阶后绝热近似可大幅降低，且两种退相位均可随参数空间变号。

Conclusion: 初始化位置是评估绝热及后绝热近似精度的关键因素，尤其对长寿命、高偏心率的双星系统；退相位随参数变号与相邻阶贡献的竞争相符，需在建模中显式考虑初始相位。

Abstract: The adiabatic approximation, in which eccentric binaries evolve under orbit-averaged fluxes, is widely used in gravitational-wave and astrophysical modeling. We use resummed multiple-scale analysis, which is closely related to near-identity averaging transformations, to derive post-adiabatic corrections from leading-order radiation reaction and explain how the accuracy of the adiabatic approximation depends on the initial orbital phase. While the secular evolution equations for the eccentricity and semi-major axis receive no explicit first post-adiabatic corrections, their solutions acquire such corrections through the initial conditions. We also derive the change in the functional form of the gravitational-wave polarizations that cancels the gauge dependence of the orbital elements at first post-adiabatic order, ensuring agreement between waveforms evaluated at the same physical event in different coordinates. At a radial turning point, the first post-adiabatic oscillations of the semi-major axis and eccentricity vanish, so the initial osculating energy and angular momentum agree with their orbit-averaged values up to second post-adiabatic corrections. When initialized elsewhere along the orbit, the adiabatic approximation can accumulate significant dephasing, which the first post-adiabatic approximation can substantially reduce. Both dephasings can change sign across parameter space, consistent with competing contributions from successive orders. Accounting for initialization is therefore essential for assessing the accuracy of these approximations, particularly for long-lived, highly eccentric binaries.

</details>


### [31] [Gravitational observables from a post-Carrollian expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14644)
*Max Niedermaier,Mahdi Sedighi Jafari*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文通过在Carroll引力中构造无穷多个精确的超局域Dirac可观测量，反驳了"爱因斯坦引力仅有非局域可观测量源于微分同胚不变性"这一归因，并给出向爱因斯坦引力推广的系统方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦引力（Einstein gravity）除少数例外仅允许非局域可观测量（nonlocal observables），学界通常将此归因于微分同胚不变性（diffeomorphism invariance），因引力场可被局域变换掉。作者意在检验并纠正这一归因。

Method: 在（速度主导型，即电型）Carroll引力（Carroll gravity）中构造无穷多个精确的超局域Dirac可观测量（ultralocal Dirac observables）。该理论描述爱因斯坦引力的超相对论极限（ultrarelativistic limit），具有完全微分同胚不变性。以此为基础，概述向爱因斯坦引力系统构造Dirac可观测量的方案，形成按空间梯度数或牛顿常数（Newton's constant）分数负幂次组织的"后Carroll展开"（post-Carrollian expansion）。

Result: 在完全微分同胚不变且可局域消除引力场的Carroll引力中仍存在无穷多精确超局域Dirac可观测量，表明微分同胚不变性并不必然导致可观测量非局域。后Carroll展开中每个部分和（partial sum）均为局域的。

Conclusion: 将爱因斯坦引力可观测量的非局域性归因于微分同胚不变性是错误的；Carroll引力的超局域可观测量为系统构建爱因斯坦引力的局域化Dirac观测量提供了起点。

Abstract: Einstein gravity, with a few exceptions, only admits nonlocal observables. This feature is often attributed to diffeomorphism invariance, since a gravitational field can thereby locally be transformed away. We show this attribution to be incorrect by constructing infinitely many exact ultralocal Dirac observables in (velocity dominated aka electric) Carroll gravity. The latter describes Einstein gravity's ultrarelativistic limit, is fully diffeomorphism invariant, and of independent interest in a variety of contexts. Starting from these Carroll observables a systematic construction of Dirac observables for Einstein gravity is outlined. The resulting "post-Carrollian" expansion is organized by the number of spatial gradients or fractional inverse powers of Newton's constant, where each partial sum is local.

</details>


### [32] [Binary black holes with non-orthogonal reference metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14647)
*Nishita Jadoo,Zachariah B. Etienne*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在协变移动穿刺（covariant moving-puncture）数值相对论框架下，首次实现并验证了非正交参考三维度规（non-orthogonal reference 3-metric），证明其可用于双黑洞（binary black hole, BBH）从旋近到铃宕的完整演化，且无病态现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前协变移动穿刺形式的数值相对论（numerical relativity, NR）模拟几乎仅采用正交参考度规（orthogonal reference metric）；作者希望突破这一限制，探索非正交参考度规的通用实现，以获得更大的网格设计自由度。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过参考度规调节结构化网格具有高度灵活性，支持将光滑去加密（smoothly derefined）的结构化网格用于双黑洞演化。

Abstract: To our knowledge, numerical relativity (NR) simulations in the covariant moving-puncture formalism have only been performed with orthogonal reference metrics. We first validate this reference metric implementation by numerically evolving the exact Kasner initial data with a fisheye-style reference 3-metric, demonstrating numerical results that converge to the exact solution with increasing grid resolution. Next, we validate our approach by evolving binary black holes (BBHs) in the moving-puncture formulation, from inspiral through merger and ringdown. Even with a large deformation in the reference geometry, introduced to allow a large change in grid refinement to enable the simulation to span the relevant length scales, from high resolutions near the BHs to low resolutions in the wave zone, no pathologies are observed. Smooth waveforms for up to $\ell=8$ are extracted on a grid without mesh refinement boundaries. We demonstrate the flexibility of tuning structured grids through the reference metric by employing spheroidal modifications of the spherical fisheye map to reduce the number of grid points in the $z$ direction when the orbit is in the $xy$-plane. These results support the use of smoothly derefined structured grids for BBH evolutions.

Conclusion: 结果表明，直接变分训练（direct variational training）对重构转动黑洞几何具有实用性，可作为求解稳态轴对称时空的可行途径。

Abstract: We use artificial neural networks to recover the Kerr solution by directly minimizing a regularized Ernst sigma-model action. Reduction along the axial Killing direction avoids the divergence at the ergosphere. Compactified coordinates cover the entire black-hole exterior, while the network parametrization enforces the asymptotic data and axis regularity. For dimensionless spins $0.1 \le a/M \le 0.99$, the median maximum relative error in the recovered metric component $g_{φφ}$ remains below $7.2\times10^{-4}$. When only the horizon rod length and angular momentum are prescribed, the networks also predict the mass with median relative errors below $1.8\times10^{-3}$. Abstract: We propose an effective framework relating correlation geometry to stochastic flows of correlation projectors. Assuming a smooth, spectrally separated correlation sector, local orthonormal frames define a Wilczek--Zee connection, while a Stratonovich gradient-flow ansatz preserves the projector constraints. In a local continuum approximation with massive fast modes, standard one-loop methods generate Yang--Mills-type terms and curvature terms of Einstein--Hilbert form. Along a smooth closed path, the trace of a cyclic product of projectors converges to the trace of the parallel-transport operator as the discretization is refined. We examine possible geometric, disordered, and topological regimes and motivate the stochastic dynamics through an effective open-system description. The first-order frame action defines a symplectic structure on the projector sector, providing a starting point for its relation to gravitational canonical variables. Method: 直接从诺特定理（Noether's theorem）计算量子场的涨落，并利用复标量场（complex scalar field）的时空平移对称性（spacetime translational symmetry），求出应力-能量张量（stress-energy tensor）的具体涨落形式。

Conclusion: 量子偏离经典路径导致的诺特流涨落，可通过应力-能量张量为宇宙学常数提供与场质量四次方成正比的贡献，结果与既有文献相符。

Abstract: When deriving the expression for the conserved Noether's current one obtains a term that is an integral over the Euler-Lagrange equation. This term vanishes vanishes if the system evolves along the classical path. However, quantum particles do not necessarily follow the classical path and, hence, for a quantum system, the Euler-Lagrange term does not vanish. This extra term corresponds to a quantum fluctuation of the Noether's current owing to the system deviating from the classical path. Here, we compute the fluctuation of a quantum field directly from Noether's Theorem and, by considering a spacetime translational symmetry of a complex scalar field, we find the specific fluctuations of the Stress-Energy tensor. As the Stress-Energy tensor is the source term for Einstein's equations of General Relativity, these fluctuations can be seen to be a contribution to the Cosmological Constant. The final expression for this contribution is proportional to the mass of the field to the fourth power which is consistent with previously derived expressions for the Cosmological Constant.

</details>

*Jie Wu,Mengfei Sun,Jin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在标量–高斯–博内（scalar–Gauss–Bonnet, sGB）引力中，通过近区与远区解的匹配，把孤立黑洞的曲率源标量场与双星有效动力学中的标量荷联系起来，并据此计算大质量标量场对双黑洞（binary black holes, BBHs）旋近动力学与引力波（gravitational waves, GWs）相位的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量标量场在sGB引力中引入有限相互作用范围，可改变双黑洞发出的引力波；但孤立黑洞的曲率源标量响应与双星有效动力学所用标量荷之间的关系尚需建立一致描述。

Abstract: Massive scalar fields introduce a finite interaction range in scalar--Gauss--Bonnet (sGB) gravity and can modify the gravitational waves (GWs) emitted by binary black holes (BBHs). We develop a matched description that relates the curvature-sourced scalar field of an isolated black hole (BH) to the scalar charge entering the effective binary dynamics. The massive field contains a distributed curvature-forced contribution and a homogeneous Yukawa sector. By matching the near- and far-zone solutions, we determine the local static coefficient and derive its finite conversion to a renormalized worldline coupling. The static coefficient has no linear scalar-mass correction, and its first correction is quadratic. We use the leading matched charges in the leading post-Newtonian dynamics and GW phasing of nonspinning, quasicircular BBHs while retaining the finite Yukawa range and massive-radiation kinematics. The scalar mass modifies the conservative dynamics, produces distinct dipole and quadrupole radiation thresholds, and changes the accumulated inspiral phase. We also construct the associated breathing and longitudinal tidal responses and treat dispersive massive propagation separately. These results connect the finite-mass BH scalar response to source and propagation observables for BBH inspirals.

</details>

*D. P. Tadas,S. V. Gore,S. P. Hatkar,S. D. Katore*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在超曲面均匀背景上对 $f(R,T)=R+2\mu T$ 引力中的磁化奇异夸克物质（magnetized strange quark matter）宇宙学模型给出精确与数值解，并用宇宙钟、Pantheon+ 超新星与 DESI DR2 重子声学振荡数据拟合，得到 $\mu=1.12^{+0.65}_{-0.58}$，但相对 $\Lambda$CDM 的观测偏好证据不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 $f(R,T)$ 引力下的磁化奇异夸克物质模型依赖运动学假设（kinematic ansatzes）且缺乏观测检验，而运动学假设与磁化源本身存在矛盾，需以物理闭合条件替代并引入数据约束。

Method: 采用含 LRS Bianchi I、Kantowski–Sachs 与 Bianchi III 的超曲面均匀背景，Maxwell 场按最小耦合精确处理；以袋模型状态方程（bag-model equation of state）与磁通量冻结（magnetic flux freezing）闭合场方程，并加入尘埃与辐射，在极限情形下精确求解、一般情况下数值求解；随后联合 32 个宇宙钟（cosmic chronometers）、1590 条 Pantheon+ 超新星光变曲线与 DESI DR2 重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillations）数据拟合。

Conclusion: 对 $\mu>0$ 的偏好依赖超新星样本与先验，常数耦合会移动物质—辐射相等时刻，且 $\mu$ 的符号取决于物质拉格朗日量，因此 $\mu$ 应视为有效的晚期参数（effective late-time parameter）而非基本常数。

Abstract: Cosmological models of magnetized strange quark matter in $f(R,T)$ gravity have so far relied on kinematic ansatzes and lacked observational tests. We revisit them for $f(R,T)=R+2μT$ on a hypersurface-homogeneous background containing the locally rotationally symmetric Bianchi I, Kantowski--Sachs and Bianchi III geometries. Treated exactly, the Maxwell field couples minimally. Because kinematic ansatzes contradict a magnetized source, we close the field equations with the bag-model equation of state and magnetic flux freezing and, adding dust and radiation, solve them exactly in limiting cases and numerically in general. Without primordial shear, the field-induced anisotropy keeps, as in general relativity, a nearly constant radiation-era level for $μ\ge0$, then decays as the model approaches de Sitter space; an assumed present field of 1 nG leaves $(σ/H)_0\simeq5.8\times10^{-11}$ today. A joint fit to 32 cosmic chronometers, 1590 Pantheon+ supernova light curves and baryon acoustic oscillations from the second data release of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument gives $μ=1.12^{+0.65}_{-0.58}$ and $H_0=69.1\pm3.2$~\kmsMpc, and the null, weak and dominant energy conditions hold throughout. Relative to the flat $Λ$ cold dark matter model, the Akaike (AIC) and Bayesian (BIC) information criteria give $Δ\mathrm{AIC}=-1.4$ and $Δ\mathrm{BIC}=+4.0$, and the Bayes factor is inconclusive. The fitted bag term, $B_c^{1/4}=2.15$ meV, is an effective vacuum energy, not the quantum chromodynamics bag constant. The preference for $μ>0$ depends on the supernova sample and the prior, a constant coupling would shift matter--radiation equality, and the sign of $μ$ depends on the matter Lagrangian; $μ$ is therefore an effective late-time parameter.

</details>

*Petar D. Dimitrov,Deyan P. Mihaylov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文计算了各向异性随机引力波背景（SGWB）在六种偏振模式下产生的天体测量相关函数（广义重叠约化函数），并在远源极限下给出闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距天体（恒星、类星体）的精密天体测量可探测纳赫兹引力波；现有SGWB上限来自甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）与Gaia，且超大质量双黑洞并合形成的天体物理背景预期显著各向异性，故需推广相关函数至各向异性情形。

Conclusion: 所得结果可用于未来天体测量中搜寻任意偏振的各向异性引力波背景，属于方法性与前瞻性讨论。

Abstract: Precise astrometric observations of distant objects such as stars and quasars can be used to probe gravitational wave signals at nanohertz frequencies. A stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB) produces correlated apparent motions of pairs of objects on the sky. That correlation as a function of their angular separation is the astrometric analog of the Hellings-Downs curve in the context of pulsar timing measurements. Upper limits on the SGWB have been obtained from very-long baseline interferometry and Gaia astrometry, and future Gaia data releases are expected to improve them. An astrophysical background from inspiraling supermassive black hole binaries is expected to be significantly anisotropic. In this work, we compute the astrometric correlation functions for an anisotropic SGWB, known as the generalized overlap reduction functions, for all six gravitational wave polarizations. We present closed-form expressions in all cases where the distant-light source limit can be applied, following the approach used for pulsar timing arrays. We find that the correlations differ qualitatively between the tensor, vector, and scalar polarizations. Finally, we discuss the applicability of these results in future astrometry searches for arbitrarily polarized anisotropic gravitational wave backgrounds.

</details>


### [39] [Quasinormal Modes of a Black Hole in a Dehnen-Type Dark Matter Halo with Radial Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15256)
*Guoyang Fu,Jiahao Zhou,Dan Zhang,Jian-Pin Wu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前对暗物质晕中黑洞QNM的研究多假设径向压为零，本文旨在考察径向压非零时Dehnen型暗物质晕对QNM的影响是否产生定性差异。

Conclusion: 非零径向压会改变暗物质晕中黑洞QNM对致密度的响应特征，γ 是调控该效应的主导参数，光子环角速度与李雅普诺夫指数可作为eikonal极限下红移偏差的有效描述量。

Abstract: We investigate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of scalar and (axial) gravitational perturbations of a black hole spacetime surrounded by a Dehnen-type dark matter (DM) halo with nonvanishing radial pressure. Our results reveal that the real and imaginary parts of the QNMs exhibit distinct redshift coefficients with respect to the DM halo compactness $M/a_0$, in contrast to the vanishing radial pressure case. The magnitude of this effect is dominated by the profile parameter $γ$, and the damping rate is more sensitive to the DM halo compactness than the oscillation frequency. In the eikonal limit, the redshift deviations are well captured by the light ring angular velocity $Ω_c$ and the Lyapunov exponent $λ$.

</details>


### [40] [Weak null singularities for the Einstein--Vlasov system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15318)
*Dawei Shen,Yuefeng Song,Jingbo Wan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在无对称性假设下证明大质量与无质量 Einstein–Vlasov 系统（massive and massless Einstein–Vlasov systems）解可形成稳定的弱零奇点（weak null singularity）：时空具奇异零边界但度规连续可延拓。


<details>

Our work constitutes the first example of eccentric population inference using only asymptotic constants of motion. Importantly, after the inference has been performed using this consistent reference, the results can be propagated to any preferred frequency for comparison with astrophysical predictions.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The non-Hermitian nature of the eigenvalue problem underlying black hole perturbations can induce the presence of exceptional points (EPs) in the quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum. EPs are points in parameter space where QNMs become degenerate. Near an EP, QNMs are resonantly excited, leaving imprints on the ringdown signal. In this work, we investigate the exceptional structure of scalar perturbations of the Kerr-Sen black hole, a rotating, charged solution of the low-energy limit of heterotic string theory, and compare it with the EP structure of the Kerr-Newman black hole in General Relativity. Starting from the EP associated with a massive scalar field in the Kerr limit, we trace exceptional lines in the parameter space spanned by the black hole spin, charge, and field mass. We locate these degeneracies directly by imposing double-root conditions on the QNM secular equation, thus avoiding the need for parameter space scans. We also investigate the hysteresis phenomenon, showing that encircling an EP in parameter space leads to an exchange of the associated QNMs.

### [56] [Machine learning and high accuracy: Neural preconditioners for computational gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16106)
*Thomas Osburn,Nils Vu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种优化的混合机器学习（hybrid ML）方法，将图神经网络（graph neural network, GNN）训练为稀疏线性系统的快速预条件子（preconditioner），在引力波与弹性波求解中把迭代次数降低2-15倍，同时保持甚至超过传统方法的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多机器学习技术（如神经网络）存在噪声下限（noise floor），难以满足高精度要求，因此引力波建模（gravitational wave modeling）等对灵敏度要求极高的领域此前对ML持谨慎态度。作者希望借助应用数学中的混合方法思路，在传统数值方案中嵌入快速但有噪声的ML机制，以同时满足高精度与速度需求。

Method: 针对非对称质量双黑洞（asymmetric-mass black hole binaries）引力波与弹性波（elastic waves）中带亥姆霍兹特征（Helmholtz features）的稀疏线性代数系统，训练GNN作为预条件子（preconditioner），并采用基于迭代算法真实残差（true residual）的新型损失函数进行优化；GNN直接作用于稀疏矩阵，仅需少量物理上下文。

Result: 在参数空间中最困难的区域，神经预条件子相比传统预条件子使迭代次数减少2-15倍。由于直接作用于稀疏矩阵且所需物理上下文有限，模型可泛化到训练域之外，并能通过重新训练而非重新设计来适配其他物理问题。

Conclusion: 此类混合ML方法为引力波建模及其他领域兼顾高精度与高速度需求提供了新的可行途径，表明ML可被嵌入传统数值框架以满足严格容差。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) developments have empowered valuable calculations in many areas of theoretical physics. Certain topics involving moderate-to-low accuracy requirements have been particularly fortunate because many ML techniques, such as neural networks, introduce a noise floor that would be difficult to penetrate with stricter tolerances. For this reason, topics requiring high accuracy can be hesitant to pursue ML. One prominent example is gravitational wave modeling, which relies on traditional methods that accommodate the high sensitivities of detectors such as LISA. However, recent progress in applied mathematics has identified hybrid methods where fast-but-noisy ML mechanisms are inserted into otherwise traditional numerical schemes to accelerate convergence in a way that ultimately meets or exceeds the accuracy of standard methods. We develop an optimized hybrid ML method to tackle a class of challenging PDEs; specifically, we demonstrate ML implementations for gravitational waves from asymmetric-mass black hole binaries and elastic waves. These require solving sparse linear algebra systems with Helmholtz features that are especially difficult for traditional iterative methods. Our new approach is to train a graph neural network (GNN) as a significantly faster preconditioner using a novel loss function based on the true residual of the iterative algorithm. For the most difficult regions of parameter space, our neural preconditioner decreases iteration counts by 2-15x compared to traditional preconditioners. Because the GNN operates directly on the sparse matrix and needs only modest physical context, it generalizes beyond its training domain and can be adapted to new problems across physics by retraining rather than redesign. Hybrid ML methods such as this offer a new and compelling avenue to handle high accuracy and speed requirements in gravitational wave modeling and beyond.

</details>

TL;DR: 该工作将中性大质量 Rarita-Schwinger 方程（Rarita-Schwinger equation）在有限转速的 Kerr 背景（Kerr background）上分离变量，得到二阶径向方程并重建场，用于计算准束缚谱（quasibound spectra）、灰体因子（greybody factors）与按螺旋度分辨的霍金辐射（helicity-resolved Hawking emission）。

### [61] [Tricritical Dynamics Reveal Black Hole Interior Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16564)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在所采样的最接近三临界性的平衡态上，重构的角向Kasner指数与独立向内积分所得结果吻合度约为0.5%。

Abstract: We reconstruct the interior Kasner exponents of a scalarized black-hole equilibrium from the growth rate of a radial perturbation outside the horizon. As the tricritical point is approached, the curvature vanishes as the fourth power of the horizon scalar amplitude. ### [62] [The uniqueness of smooth stationary black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16853)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞唯一性（black hole uniqueness）问题的核心在于：在尽量弱的正则性与物理假设下，确认旋转黑洞必为 Kerr 解。本文旨在去除解析性与小性假设这类较强限制。

Conclusion: 单旋转非退化黑洞外部时空在所述假设下被刚性地确认为亚极端 Kerr 时空，且无需解析性或小性假设，强化了稳态真空黑洞唯一性结论。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 固定时钟后，其标架场成为背景结构，时钟的重新取向表现为关系场空间上的矢量场而非时空微分同胚（spacetime diffeomorphisms）；这一协变性的丧失容许存在非平凡关系性哈密顿量，其与在约束面上为零的未修饰及修饰哈密顿约束（Hamiltonian constraints）均不同。作者给出了修饰方程重现原始约束动力学的条件，并指出关系性生成元即使关系时间平移不是修饰作用量的对称性时也能定义哈密顿流（Hamiltonian flow）。

Conclusion: 各经典描述相互兼容，但保留不同的变分原理（variational principles）与中间相空间（intermediate phase spaces）；其量子层面的等价性仍是未决问题。

Abstract: We compare covariant and canonical relational dressings with gauge-fixing reductions for the flat FLRW minisuperspace with a homogeneous massless scalar. The relational generator defines a Hamiltonian flow even when relational-time translations are not symmetries of the dressed action. In each clock frame, it corresponds to the canonical complete observable and reduced Hamiltonian on the appropriate solution space, through the Legendre map and relational evaluation. </details>

Abstract: We present the first systematic parameter-estimation analysis of 42 candidate gravitational-wave signals identified in GWTC-4.0 with a probability of astrophysical origin $p_\mathrm{astro} \geq 0.5$, but a false-alarm rate of at least $1\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$, which were therefore not selected for parameter estimation in the catalogue. We use a catalogue-style analysis workflow and publicly available strain data to obtain posterior samples for these candidates, of which 29 pass our validation criteria. Of the remaining 13 candidates, seven are consistent with noise or impacted by instrumental artefacts, while six yield results that cannot be reliably interpreted.
  We identify several interesting candidates: a possible neutron star-black hole merger, GW230920_064709, and two candidates with evidence for non-zero effective spin: GW230902_172430 and GW230823_142524. The latter also shows mild evidence of precession, although further analysis is needed to establish the robustness of this result. We quantify the information gained from these observations using the Kullback-Leibler divergence, finding that the inferred spin parameters are generally only weakly constrained.

</details>


### [65] [Spin to win: enhancing the quantum Otto cycle with the circular Unruh effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17027)

Main category: gr-qc

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨相对运动（特别是圆周运动）引起的局域温度效应如何改变量子热力学循环的运行条件与性能极限，从而检验标准卡诺界（Carnot bounds）在运动探测器情形下的适用性。

Conclusion: 在高温极限下，有效温度与在热浴中匀速平动的惯性探测器（inertial detector）结果一致；与小平隙极限比较表明，圆周运动可在比惯性情形更低的速率下突破标准卡诺界。

Abstract: We study a quantum Otto cycle whose working medium is a qubit Unruh-DeWitt detector undergoing uniform circular motion in $(3+1)$-dimensional Minkowski spacetime, interacting with a massless scalar field in a thermal state during the isochoric strokes. We compute the detector's transition rate and determine its energy gap-dependent effective temperature, showing that the effective temperature can be higher than the ambient bath temperature in the small energy gap limit and lower in the high bath temperature limit. We show that the engine efficiency and refrigerator performance coefficient can exceed their standard Carnot bounds based on the bath's rest-frame temperatures, while remaining bounded by generalised Carnot limits expressed in terms of the effective temperatures experienced by the detector. In the high-temperature limit, the effective temperature coincides with that of an inertial detector moving through a thermal bath, and a comparison with the small-gap limit reveals that circular motion allows the standard Carnot bounds to be exceeded at lower speeds than in the inertial case.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Multiparameter gravitational-wave tests of general relativity (GR) are limited by strong correlations among post-Newtonian (PN) phase deformations and the computational cost of Bayesian inference. We present DINGO-TGR, a neural posterior estimation framework that jointly infers binary-source parameters and six PN phase deformations within the TIGER parametrization, using IMRPhenomXPHM waveforms with spin precession and higher-order modes. Importance sampling reweights the neural proposal samples, while principal component analysis (PCA) identifies two well-constrained combinations of PN deformations. We validate the framework using 16 zero-noise GR injections and 12 beyond-GR injections, recovering GR-consistent posteriors in the former and leading-mode shifts consistent with the injected deformations in the latter. For GW150914 and GW250114, our results agree with independent \texttt{bilby-tgr} analyses using \texttt{dynesty}. After training, inference including importance sampling takes approximately 38 minutes and 4.4 hours, respectively, compared with 177 and 238 hours for conventional sampling. GW150914 exhibits a mild offset from GR in the leading PCA mode, whereas GW250114 is consistent with GR. To assess the significance of the GW150914 offset, we analyze 1000 GW150914-like GR injections with independent Gaussian-noise realizations and fixed detector power spectral densities. The resulting empirical upper-tail $p$-value is $11.5\%$, indicating that the observed offset does not reject the adopted GR null model at the 5\% significance level. ### [67] [Gauge aspects of teleparallel gravity and Noether's second theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17147)

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文从诺特定理第二定理（Noether's second theorem）出发考察广义相对论等价理论（Teleparallel Equivalent of General Relativity, TEGR）的规范结构，论证微分同胚（diffeomorphisms）与局部洛伦兹变换（local Lorentz transformations）是其仅有的规范对称性，并据此讨论物质耦合的确定性问题。

  <summary>Details</summary>
