Conclusion: TEGR的规范结构由微分同胚与局部洛伦兹变换界定，这对引力规范理论中翻译的地位及TEGR的物质耦合给出了明确约束，支持其与广义相对论在物质耦合上的等价性。

Abstract: We examine the gauge structure of the Teleparallel Equivalent of General Relativity (TEGR) from the perspective of Noether's second theorem. We show that diffeomorphisms and local Lorentz transformations are the gauge symmetries of TEGR, as these are the only variational symmetries that give rise to Noether identities. We then discuss the distinct roles of these two symmetries and their implications for the gauge-theoretic interpretation of gravity. Finally, we discuss consequences of these observations for the role of translations in gauge theories of gravity and the coupling of matter in TEGR. In particular, we demonstrate that the matter coupling prescription is uniquely determined and coincides with that of general relativity, including in the case of spinor fields.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 恢复效果取决于透镜放大与波形调制的竞争：较小碰撞参数（impact parameter）通常因更强放大而改善恢复，较高横向速度则通过更快调制部分抵消该增益；响应依赖本征信号强度，强信号呈小幅相对变化且部分配置出现净损失，弱信号失配分布更宽并延伸至更负值，显示更强增强。

Conclusion: 透镜效应不能仅靠放大率重标度搜索灵敏度来计入；在透镜假说下重新解释定向银心搜索的灵敏度与上限，需要在完整透镜参数空间上进行含完整透镜波形的注入研究。

Abstract: Continuous gravitational waves (CWs) from neutron stars beyond the Galactic Center can be lensed by the supermassive black hole in Sgr A$^*$. Since the signal lasts far longer than the lensing time delay, the two geometric-optics images overlap and interfere. Transverse source motion during the observation makes their magnifications and relative time delay evolve, producing evolving amplitude and phase modulations absent from standard CW templates. Using lensed and unlensed injections in Gaussian noise, we quantify their effect on a Hough-based semicoherent search through a semicoherent mismatch. Recovery is governed by the competition between lensing amplification and waveform modulation. Smaller impact parameters generally improve recovery through stronger magnification, while higher transverse velocities partially offset this gain through faster modulation, especially at small impact parameters and short source-lens distances, where image interference is appreciable. The response also depends on the intrinsic signal strength. Loud signals exhibit small fractional changes, with net losses in some configurations, whereas weaker signals yield broader mismatch distributions extending to more negative values, indicating greater enhancement. A population study, sampling lensing parameters from astrophysically motivated velocity and source-lens distance distributions, reproduces the strong dependence on signal strength. Differences between populations are modest, but high-velocity, short source-lens distance populations tend towards larger mismatches. Lensing effects therefore cannot be accounted for by rescaling search sensitivity with the magnification alone; reinterpreting directed Galactic Center search sensitivities and upper limits under the lensing hypothesis requires injection studies with the full lensed waveform across the lensing parameter space.

</details>

*Carlos Heredia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该摘要研究无穷阶（infinite-order）与非局部（nonlocal）拉格朗日力学（Lagrangian mechanics）中的全时间导数（total time derivative）项，指出非局部情形下"L=DW 精确形式"不足以保证非局部欧拉-拉格朗日表达式为零，并给出一个光滑卷积型反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准与形式无穷阶框架中，全时间导数项属于变分平凡（variationally trivial）项，不影响运动方程；摘要意在考察这一性质在非局部拉格朗日泛函中是否依然成立。

Conclusion: 无穷阶与非局部拉格朗日力学中全时间导数的变分平凡性存在本质差异；非局部框架需对"精确形式即变分平凡"这一经典直觉加以修正。

Abstract: We study total time derivatives in infinite-order and nonlocal Lagrangian mechanics. In the formal infinite-order setting, such terms remain variationally trivial. For nonlocal functionals on trajectories, however, exactness in the form $L=DW$ is not sufficient to ensure a vanishing nonlocal Euler-Lagrange expression. We exhibit a smooth convolution-type counterexample.

</details>

*Harold Erbin*

TL;DR: 本文借助 Hubbard–Stratonovich 变换绕过 Sonoda–Zwiebach 无解定理（no-go theorem），给出了闭玻色弦场论（closed bosonic string field theory）在所有阶数上的显式立方作用量构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭玻色弦场论作用量是非多项式的，且三十余年来 Sonoda–Zwiebach（1990）的无解定理排除了仅含单一弦场、对称顶点与标准传播子的立方表述，本文旨在突破这一限制。

Result: 所得作用量为立方型、对每个辅助场至多二次且呈三角结构；原规范对称性（gauge symmetry）无新增参数地提升至立方作用量，且在动能算符可逆的通用动量处辅助场不增加物理态。世界面（worldsheet）上 Σ_2 为纠缠的双弦态，经算子乘积展开（operator product expansion）表明四次顶点化为两个非对称立方顶点的粘合；弦顶点满足推广的亏格零 IBL_∞ 关系，当且仅当弦乘积构成循环 L_∞ 代数（cyclic L_∞ algebra）。

Abstract: The action of closed bosonic string field theory is non-polynomial, and for more than thirty years a no-go theorem of Sonoda and Zwiebach (1990) has excluded a cubic formulation with a single string field, a symmetric vertex and the canonical propagator. We evade it with a Hubbard--Stratonovich transformation and provide an explicit construction of a cubic closed string field theory at all orders. The transformation introduces an infinite tower of auxiliary $k$-string fields $Σ_k$, entering at order $g_s^{2 k - 2}$, which turn the vertices with four or more legs into kinetic terms and cubic couplings. The action is cubic, at most quadratic in each auxiliary field, and triangular: eliminating the auxiliary fields by Gaussian integrals recovers the original action order by order at tree level, under invertibility and kernel conditions on the auxiliary kinetic operators. The original gauge symmetry lifts to the cubic action with no new parameter, and we argue that the auxiliary fields add no physical state at generic momentum, wherever their kinetic operators are invertible. On the worldsheet, $Σ_2$ is an entangled two-string state: by an operator product expansion, the quartic form reduces to a gluing of two asymmetric cubic vertices dressed by the motion of the local coordinates and defined on states without level matching. We expect this picture to generalize to higher orders, defining a gluing of patches carrying $k$ punctures each. The string vertices, read as products with several outputs, satisfy an extended form of the genus-zero $\mathrm{IBL}_\infty$ relations, including operations with no input or no output, exactly when the string products form a cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra. Finally, we compare our action with the bar--cobar construction of Chiaffrino, Mamade and Zwiebach.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在全息重整化中采用渐近全局 AdS 参照系（asymptotically global AdS frame），将原用于七维的做法推广至五维和六维，并对具有 S^1×S^{d-2} 共形边界（conformal boundaries）的 AdS_d 黑洞计算重整化在壳作用；同时以背景减除作对照分析。

Background subtraction, in contrast, fails to reproduce this result in the same frame; the mismatch, which we identify exactly in terms of the Casimir term and the counterterms, demands a criterion for the cutoff surface yet to be established. The background-dressed relative determinant factorises exactly into effective, gradient-dependent strengths of the two plates, and the sign of each frequency channel of the interaction energy is fixed by their orientation. The interaction energy and the force conjugate to the separation neither share their sign everywhere nor change sign at the same positions: there are regions of negative energy and repulsive force, so attraction and repulsion must be read from the force. For such plates with $w\neq0$ the force is repulsive at short and attractive at large separation, and the interaction energy has a minimum at a separation that depends on the position of the pair. For $v=1.05$, $w=0.5$ and $d=1.5$ the energy changes sign at $c\simeq-0.44$ and $2.63$, and the force at $c\simeq-0.30$ and $2.00$.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 残数扩展框架统一了无源 ILW 与其带自洽源的推广，残数指标提供了孤子解的拓扑式分类；谱衰减率的消失为残数扇区之间的跃迁提供了直接的谱学特征。

Abstract: We study the analytic structure of scalar one-loop cosmological $n$-gon correlators using an integrand-level reduction and a Baikov representation adapted to linear tube-energy denominators. At spatial dimension $d=n$, we determine the algebraic factors that normalize their local leading singularities to unity and construct explicit $d\log$ forms for arbitrary $n$. For the complete box at $d=n=4$, we evaluate an external-energy discontinuity in a collinear kinematic family and find a nonvanishing complete elliptic integral of the second kind after summing all time orderings. We also compare successive tube-energy discontinuities in $d=3$ and $d=4$, and relate selected tube cuts to the cosmological dressing representation. These results illustrate the importance of integration domains in determining the analytic structure beyond local leading singularities.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于厘清"非局域动能项（kinetic term）"与"背景耦合（background coupling）"共同存在时典范量子化（canonical quantization）如何被修改，尤其是 λ 与 σ 两个参数在恢复局域/自由极限时的不同角色。

Result: 在正时间间隔 s 处，对易子等于 iħ 乘以该辛括号；ħ 表现为 s=σ 时对易子期望值的跃变（对平移变量而言即等时）。当 0<s<σ 时，任意 λ>0 的对易子均为零。

Conclusion: 固定 σ 令 λ→0 不能在该区间复现自由对易子；而固定 λ 令 σ→0 则在任意固定 s>0 处恢复局域对易子（local commutator），说明 σ 而非 λ 控制局域性恢复。

From the boundary term in the variation of the action, we construct a conserved symplectic form and derive the positive-time Poisson bracket. The canonical normalization $\hbar$ appears as a jump of the commutator expectation value at $s=σ$, which is equal time for shifted variables with a local kinetic term; for $0<s<σ$ the commutator vanishes for every $λ>0$. Hence $λ\to0$ at fixed $σ$ does not reproduce the free commutator on this interval, whereas $σ\to0$ at fixed $λ$ recovers the local commutator at every fixed $s>0$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高圈六点振幅通常因红外发散（infrared divergences）与积分复杂性而难以处理；本文旨在借助负几何方法探究三圈阶的结构，并检验共线反常维度（collinear anomalous dimension）与尖点（cusp）是否获得三圈修正。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了六点 ABJM 振幅出人意料的简洁性，并暗示其奇圈项与偶圈项之间存在迭代关系（iterative relation）。

Abstract: We compute the three-loop six-point amplitude in planar ABJM theory using negative geometries. At this order, only a single bipartite chain contributes to the logarithm of the amplitude, and we find that its dual-conformally invariant integrand is locally finite. The integrated chain is therefore infrared finite and, remarkably, equals exactly $-π^2$ times the one-loop amplitude. Hence the collinear anomalous dimension, like the cusp, receives no three-loop correction. Restoring the disconnected products of lower-loop contributions, which carry all the infrared divergences, we obtain the full three-loop amplitude, fixed entirely by lower-loop data and the coefficient $-π^2$. These results reveal an unexpected simplicity of the six-point ABJM amplitude and suggest an iterative relation between its odd- and even-loop terms.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶导数修正（higher-derivative corrections）在五维超引力中可能引入鬼（ghosts）与不稳定性，但其与超对称、辅助场（auxiliary fields）及全耦合（exact in couplings）结构的相互作用尚不清晰，且中心荷 $a$、$c$ 与谱结构的联系有待在完整 off-shell 框架下厘清。

Conclusion: 在 Gauss-Bonnet 扩展的 AdS$_3\times\Sigma_2$ 背景中，二维中心荷由 $c$-extremization 复现，但谱一般不幺正（non-unitary）并含能量在大 Kaluza-Klein 能级下保持有界的态塔；在部分参数空间，自旋-2 多重态塔具有正模，故谱无有限态密度（density of states）。

Abstract: We study the spectra and the central charges of off-shell gauged five-dimensional $\mathcal N=2$ supergravity extended by the Weyl squared, Ricci tensor squared and Ricci scalar squared invariants, and by their Gauss-Bonnet combination. Working exactly in the couplings and keeping all auxiliary fields, we organize the spectra around the maximally supersymmetric AdS$_5$ vacuum into supermultiplets. With the Ricci scalar squared invariant, the spectrum gains a long vector multiplet and is free of ghosts in a finite range of the coupling, while with the Weyl squared or Ricci tensor squared invariant, it gains a long spin-2 multiplet and always contains ghosts or instabilities. The Gauss-Bonnet combination adds no new modes: around its supersymmetric vacuum, the quadratic action is a rescaled copy of that of the two-derivative theory, and this fixes the spectrum of the Ricci tensor squared extension. We also compute the central charges $a$ and $c$ and the flavor central charge exactly in the couplings. The vacuum equation is $a$-maximization, and the kinetic terms of the massless graviton and vector multiplets are proportional to $c$ and to the flavor central charge, so that the critical points, where the massive spin-2 multiplet becomes degenerate with the graviton multiplet, are zeros of $c$. Finally, around supersymmetric AdS$_3\timesΣ_2$ backgrounds of the Gauss-Bonnet extension, the two-dimensional central charges are reproduced by $c$-extremization, while the spectrum is generically not unitary and contains towers of states whose energies stay bounded at large Kaluza-Klein levels. In part of the parameter space, the tower of spin-2 multiplets has positive norm, so that the spectrum has no finite density of states.

</details>


### [85] [Radiation from scattering on multiple shockwaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15468)
*Tim Adamo,Anton Ilderton,Hannah Kingham*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将电子在多个零电磁冲击波（null electromagnetic shockwaves）上的散射作为可精确求解的模型，计算弹性散射与单光子发射振幅，并据此研究独立散射与干涉散射区域中的辐射密度，重现并推广了 LPM 抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 兰道-波梅兰丘克-米格代尔（Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal, LPM）效应通常依赖近似建模：散射中心被线性处理（如屏蔽库仑势）、探针为高能、光子为软光子。作者希望构建一个可精确计算多次散射的场景，以检验并推广该效应。

Method: 考虑带电探针在多个零电磁冲击波上的散射，利用冲击波横向平面内的剖面精确确定任意运动学下的弹性散射与单光子发射振幅；再对冲击波横向位置与光前时间（lightfront time）做双重平均，计算独立散射和干涉散射两种区域下的辐射密度。

Result: 对于横向平面内径向对称的冲击波剖面（包括超助推库仑场），在更一般的运动学条件下重现了已知的 LPM 抑制；无源情形下，即在脉冲/超短平面波上的多次散射，发现在 LPM 区域出现更强的谱抑制。

Conclusion: 零电磁冲击波模型提供了可精确求解的多重散射框架，既适用于径向对称冲击波剖面，也适用于脉冲平面波，并表明更强抑制可在 LPM 区域（LPM regime）实现。

Abstract: It is well known that radiation produced by scattering an electron off multiple sources depends on the length of the mean free path between these scatterings. When this is sufficiently small, the spectrum is suppressed due to destructive interference, a famous result known as the Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal (LPM) effect. This is usually modelled approximately, with scattering centres treated linearly (e.g., as screened Coulomb potentials), high-energy probes and soft emitted photons. In this paper, we consider a scenario where multiple scatterings can be computed exactly: charged probe scattering on multiple null electromagnetic shockwaves. Here, the amplitudes for elastic scattering and single photon emission can be determined exactly, for arbitrary kinematics, in terms of the shock profiles in the transverse plane. We use these amplitudes to compute the radiation density (doubly-averaged over shock locations in the transverse plane and lightfront time) in the regimes of independent and interfering scatterings. For shock profiles which are radially symmetric in the transverse plane (including the ultraboosted Coulomb field), we recover the known LPM suppression, albeit with more general kinematics. In addition, our setup allows us to consider the source-free case of multiple scattering on impulsive/ultra-short plane waves, where we find much stronger suppression of the spectrum in the LPM regime.

</details>

Due to analytical difficulties, we approach the underlying thermodynamics through parallel programming simulations using CUDA codes. Abstract: We review recent progress on the conjectural relation between the resurgent structure of topological string free energies and BPS invariants of type II string theory compactified on the same Calabi-Yau threefold. In this picture, Borel singularities are located at integral periods associated with central charges of stable D-brane bound states, while Stokes constants encode their BPS indices, or generalized DT invariants. After introducing resurgence and perturbative topological strings, we explain how trans-series solutions of the holomorphic anomaly equations determine non-perturbative amplitudes and illustrate the proposed correspondence in local and compact geometries. We discuss the behavior under changes of polarization and moduli, and review supporting arguments based on exact WKB analysis, blowup equations, the Stokes automorphisms of flat coordinates and dual partition functions, and Kontsevich-Soibelman wall-crossing. We conclude with open questions concerning a general proof, the interpretation of non-perturbative amplitudes, and the construction of non-perturbative completions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为二维环面 Ising 模型构造全息对偶描述，借助拓扑量子场论与弦网（string-net）框架，寻找边界物理与体拓扑数据之间的对应关系。

Result: 态求和 ⟨D|Π|F⟩ 给出 core 缺陷态与物理边界融合图之间的关联；当缺陷 D 不存在时，2-skeleton P 终止于 bedding surface S，并被解释为弦网 F 的体世界面（bulk worldsheet）。

Conclusion: 该构造为二维 Ising 模型提供了以 Turaev-Viro TQFT 为体的全息对偶图像，并通过 core/sandwich 划分、bedding surface 与 2-skeleton 明确了缺陷、弦网与世界面之间的几何与代数关系。

Abstract: We propose a holographic dual of the 2d Ising model on a torus as the Turaev-Viro TQFT on a solid torus. The bulk spacetime is divided into the ``core'' region and the ``sandwich'' region. The core region prepares a defect state $|\mathcal{D}\rangle$ on the topological boundary $\mathbb{D}$. The sandwich region computes the Turaev-Viro state sum $\langle\mathcal{D}|Π|\mathcal{F}\rangle$ between the state $|\mathcal{D}\rangle$ on $\mathbb{D}$ and the state $|\mathcal{F}\rangle$ on the physical boundary $\mathbb{B}$. The fusion diagram (or string-net) $\mathcal{F}$ on $\mathbb{B}$ is given by the Aasen-Fendley-Mong construction. There is also a ``bedding surface'' $\mathbb{S}$ between $\mathbb{D}$ and $\mathbb{B}$, and the height variables (or Ising spins inside/outside of the domain wall) are naturally living on $\mathbb{S}$. The bulk spacetime is discretized by the 2-skeleton $P$ which interpolates $\mathcal{D}$ and $\mathcal{F}$. When the defect $\mathcal{D}$ is absent, the 2-skeleton $P$ ends on the bedding surface $\mathbb{S}$ and $P$ is interpreted as the bulk worldsheet of the string-net $\mathcal{F}$.

</details>


### [89] [1-loop partition function of 11d supergravity on $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_k$: matching $A(k)$ in ABJM free energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15510)
*Stefan A. Kurlyand,Arkady A. Tseytlin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出，对 $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb Z_k$ 上 11 维超引力的 1-圈配分函数，此前基于 $S^7$ 标准 KK 谱的计算遗漏了由欧氏 $\mathrm{AdS}_4$ 上平方可积调和 2-形式与 $S^7$ 模式张量构成的 3-形式场涨落贡献；补入该项并经 $\mathbb Z_k$ 投影与 zeta 函数正则化后，结果重现 ABJM 局域化给出的 $A(k)$，并推广至 3d ADHM 理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABJM 理论在 $S^3$ 上自由能大 $N$ 展开中的函数 $A(k)$ 可由局域化（localization）精确得到，但早期基于 $S^7$ 上标准 Kaluza–Klein（KK）谱的 11 维超引力 1-圈计算无法重现该结果，二者存在不一致，需查明缺失的贡献来源。

Conclusion: 标准 KK 谱并不完备，需额外计入 $\mathrm{AdS}_4$ 上非平凡平方可积调和形式所构造的涨落模式；补齐后超引力 1-圈计算与局域化结果在 ABJM 与 ADHM 两类理论中均一致，支持规范/引力对偶在 1-圈层面的精确匹配。

Abstract: The function $A(k)$ appearing in the large $N$ expansion of free energy of ABJM theory on $S^3$ is known exactly from localization. An earlier calculation of the 1-loop partition function of 11d supergravity on $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb Z_k$, based on the standard KK spectrum on $S^7$, did not reproduce it. We identify a missing 1-loop supergravity contribution as that of the 3-form field fluctuations built from square-integrable harmonic 2-forms on Euclidean $\mathrm{AdS}_4$ tensored with $S^7$ modes, which are not captured by the KK spectrum. Adding it to the KK tower contribution expressed in terms of superconformal characters and applying the $\mathbb{Z}_k$ projection, the total expression simplifies, and its evaluation in a zeta-function prescription reproduces the expression for $A(k)$. We also apply a similar approach to the 3d ADHM theory whose M-theory dual is the orbifold $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_p$, which has singularity along the fixed locus $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^3$, supporting a 7d $SU(p)$ vector multiplet. Adding the localized KK modes of this multiplet to the $\mathbb{Z}_p$-projected supergravity spectrum gives a 1-loop free energy that reproduces the ADHM localization counterpart of $A(k)$.

</details>

Abstract: We study pairwise and cross-horizon correlations of a charged scalar field in Rindler spacetime in the presence of parallel electric and magnetic fields. For a specified global vacuum in the negative-frequency sector, we obtain the outgoing four-mode Gaussian state and calculate logarithmic negativity and quantum mutual information for selected mode pairs. We find that the cross-wedge antiparticle-particle pair is entangled only when $q_2<\tanh r_U$. It can therefore become separable although its mutual information remains nonzero. The intra-wedge particle-antiparticle pair remains entangled for nonzero Schwinger squeezing. Using the Gaussian Schmidt decomposition, we show that the entanglement across the complete right--left wedge partition is carried by a pair of collective modes, even when the selected outgoing cross-wedge pair becomes separable. Using the Gaussian Schmidt decomposition, we show that the entanglement across the complete right--left wedge partition is carried by a pair of collective modes, even when the selected outgoing cross-wedge pair becomes separable. We discuss the Landau-level dependence and the limits of these mode-resolved conclusions, without identifying the vacuum-dependent mixing parameter with a detector temperature.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用带全背反的全息超导体模型进行数值比较，在固定末态能量与电荷下比较剩余视界熵（horizon entropy）的次序；并解析分析固定能量与电荷下临界慢支（critical slow branch）的视界位移与二次面积响应，考察视界正则性（horizon regularity）与均匀径向控制下的释放过程及协变标量通量（covariant scalar flux）。

Result: 在能量与电荷匹配时，边界驱动可逆转源移除后剩余视界熵的先后次序；部分方案即使计入驱动与等待时间，也能更早达到给定容差。临界慢支上视界位移抵消二次面积响应，对标量振幅 ε 留下 O(ε⁴) 的熵储备，该系数同时关联邻近超导平衡的熵优势与法向侧慢尾（slow tail）的熵权重。对压制主导视界场与外区电荷的释放，制备时间至少按 ε^{−2} 增长；电荷输运约束产生视界熵的同一协变标量通量，提高最小通量代价并强化时间下界。

Conclusion: 姆潘巴式次序逆转在计入制备时间后仍可能具有实际优势，但临界态有限的熵储备 O(ε⁴) 严格限制了慢场的移除速度；通量代价与时间下界表明制备代价由熵产生机制共同决定。

Abstract: We study Mpemba relaxation and its preparation cost in a holographic superconductor with full backreaction. At matched final energy and charge, boundary drives can reverse the ordering of the remaining horizon entropy after source removal. In separate numerical comparisons, some protocols reach a given tolerance earlier even when driving and waiting times are included. To examine the limits of preparation, we consider critical states that already carry a slow scalar field and exterior charge. Along the critical slow branch at fixed energy and charge, horizon displacement cancels the quadratic area response, leaving an entropy reserve of $O(\varepsilon^4)$ for an initial scalar amplitude $\varepsilon$. The same coefficient connects this reserve to the entropy advantage of the nearby superconducting equilibrium and the entropy weight of the slow tail on the normal side. This limited reserve constrains how quickly the existing field can be removed. For releases that suppress the leading horizon field and exterior charge, the preparation time grows at least as $\varepsilon^{-2}$ under horizon regularity and uniform radial control. Charge transport constrains the same covariant scalar flux that produces horizon entropy, raising the minimum flux cost and strengthening the time bound.

</details>


### [92] [Triple-Massive-Exchange Bispectrum of Quasi-Single-Field Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15660)
*Xingang Chen,Zhong-Zhi Xianyu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型的三重交换双谱自提出以来在整个参数空间上一直难以解析求解，尽管宇宙对撞机物理（cosmological collider physics）进展迅速，仍缺乏完整解析刻画。

Method: 将 Mellin-Barnes 表示（Mellin-Barnes representation）直接应用于无质量模式与有质量模式之间的混合传播子（mixed propagator），对一般质量和混合强度（mixing strength）求解，获得收敛的双重级数展开。

Result: 所得结果为精确（exact）且非微扰（non-perturbative）的，并具有物理透明性；适用于一般质量与混合强度参数。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了三重交换双谱在全参数空间缺乏解析结果的空白，且所提方法具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: The triple-exchange bispectrum of the scalar quasi-single-field inflation model has resisted analytic evaluation across its full parameter space since its advent, despite rapid progress in cosmological collider physics. In this Letter, we solve it for general mass and mixing strength as a convergent double series, by applying the Mellin-Barnes representation directly to the mixed propagator between the massless and massive modes. The result is exact, non-perturbative, and physically transparent; the method is broadly applicable.

</details>


### [93] [Quantum and classical thermal equilibrium for probabilistic classical fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15749)
*C. Wetterich*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文论证经典场概率分布的时间演化可用量子形式描述，自由经典场对应自由量子场论子系统；瑞利-金斯谱与普朗克谱的选择在无相互作用时并非基本概念问题，而在有相互作用时转化为动力学选择问题，并通过数值模拟加以判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在澄清经典热平衡（瑞利-金斯谱，Rayleigh-Jeans spectrum）与量子热平衡（普朗克谱，Planck spectrum）之间的选择依据，说明该选择在有相互作用时并非概念层面的问题，而是由渐近动力学决定的问题。

Method: 比较经典与量子平衡下经典能量及场关联（field correlations）的动量依赖关系；对含相互作用的概率经典场（probabilistic classical fields）进行数值模拟；对自由概率经典场构造经典概率分布与量子场论密度矩阵（density matrix）之间的一般“经典-量子映射（classical-quantum map）”。

Result: 建立了自由情形下经典概率分布与量子密度矩阵之间的对应映射；并给出可据动量依赖比较判定哪种平衡被渐近动力学选中的途径，含相互作用情形的结论由数值模拟得到。

Conclusion: 瑞利-金斯谱与普朗克谱的选择不是基本概念问题；一旦存在相互作用，它成为动力学选择问题，可通过比较动量依赖并结合数值模拟来确定所选平衡。

Abstract: The time evolution of the probability distribution for classical fields can be described by the quantum formalism. For free classical fields the free quantum field theory arises as a subsystem. Different probability distributions for classical fields correspond to the classical thermal equilibrium with the Rayleigh-Jeans divergence or the quantum thermal equilibrium with the Planck spectrum. The selection of the Rayleigh-Jeans or Planck spectrum is not a question of fundamental concepts. It becomes an issue of dynamical selection once interactions are present. We compare the momentum dependence of the classical energy and field correlations for the classical and quantum equilibrium. This permits to decide which equilibrium is selected as a result of asymptotic dynamics by numerical simulations of the probabilistic classical fields including interactions. For free probabilistic classical fields we construct a general ``classical-quantum map'' between the classical probability distribution and the density matrix of the quantum field theory.

</details>


### [94] [Harmonic superspace and Kaluza-Klein reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15782)
*Nikita Zaigraev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将 Kaluza-Klein 约化程序推广到调和超空间（harmonic superspace），通过调和导数（harmonic derivatives）的质量变形实现约化，从而在保持明显离壳超对称与 Grassmann 解析性的前提下，构造超对称规范多重态（supermultiplets）的离壳超场表述，并给出 5D 到 4D BPS 有质量矢量与超引力多重态的具体结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于把 Kaluza-Klein 约化（Kaluza-Klein reduction）引入调和超空间框架，以获得离壳（off-shell）超场形式的超对称规范多重态，尤其是 4D、\(\mathcal{N}=2\) 的 BPS 有质量多重态。

Method: 方法是对调和导数施加质量项变形来实现约化，同时确保明显离壳超对称（manifest off-shell supersymmetry）与 Grassmann 解析性（Grassmann analyticity）在各阶段均被保持；调和超空间中规范对称性由解析预势（analytic prepotentials）与调和导数自然编码，使该程序在技术上较为简洁。

Result: 作为首个应用，约化自由 \(5D,\mathcal{N}=1\) Maxwell 理论，得到 BPS \(4D,\mathcal{N}=2\) 有质量矢量超多重态的离壳超场描述，该表述自然包含解析 Stückelberg 型规范对称性；随后约化线性化 \(5D,\mathcal{N}=1\) Poincaré 超引力，导出 BPS \(4D,\mathcal{N}=2\) 有质量超引力多重态的无约束调和超空间作用量。

Conclusion: 该工作为有质量高自旋 \(4D,\mathcal{N}=2\) BPS 多重态的规范不变离壳超场表述提供直接途径，且所得理论可改写于 \(4D,\mathcal{N}=1\) 超空间，从而为 \(4D,\mathcal{N}=1\) 有质量高自旋理论（massive higher-spin theories）的规范不变离壳描述开辟道路。

Abstract: We extend the Kaluza-Klein reduction procedure to the framework of harmonic superspace and apply it to the construction of off-shell superfield formulations of supersymmetric gauge multiplets. The reduction is implemented by a deformation of the harmonic derivatives by mass terms, while ensuring that manifest off-shell supersymmetry and Grassmann analyticity are preserved at all stages. As a first application, we reduce the free \(5D,\mathcal{N}=1\) Maxwell theory and obtain an off-shell superfield description of the BPS \(4D,\mathcal{N}=2\) massive vector supermultiplet. The resulting formulation naturally incorporates an analytic Stückelberg-type gauge symmetry. We then reduce linearized \(5D,\mathcal{N}=1\) Poincaré supergravity and derive the unconstrained harmonic-superspace action for the BPS \(4D,\mathcal{N}=2\) massive supergravity multiplet. The technical simplicity of the procedure stems from the structure of gauge theories in harmonic superspace, where the gauge symmetry is naturally encoded in analytic prepotentials and harmonic derivatives. The approach presented in this paper opens a direct route to gauge-invariant off-shell superfield formulations of massive higher-spin $4D,\mathcal{N}=2$ BPS multiplets. Furthermore, the resulting theories can be reformulated in $4D,\mathcal{N}=1$ superspace, opening the way to gauge-invariant off-shell descriptions of $4D,\mathcal{N}=1$ massive higher-spin theories.

</details>


### [95] [How to find regular black-hole islands in the swamp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15795)
*Astrid Eichhorn,Pedro G. S. Fernandes,Verena Koeder*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文假设高曲率修正（higher-curvature corrections）逐阶渐进地改善黑洞奇异性，并将该假设翻译为度规函数偏离 Schwarzschild 解的单调性要求，由此给出约束有效场论（effective field theory）系数的条件，并在八阶构造出正则性条件构成的“岛屿”，进而检验弦论与渐近安全引力是否位于其中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无穷阶高曲率修正预期导致正则黑洞解（regular black-hole solutions），但正则行为如何随有效场论阶数逐步建立尚缺乏刻画；同时需要以单一奇异性解决能标为前提，对有效场论系数施加可操作的约束。

Method: 将“正则性渐进建立”与“单一新物理能标”假设转化为度规函数相对 Schwarzschild 黑洞偏离的单调性要求，据此在有效场论适用范围内构建追踪视界上及视界外“较弱奇异”行为增长的条件，用以约束有效场论系数；以五个具体条件为例，在八阶（order eight）求出对应的黑洞正则性条件岛屿（island）。

Result: 在八阶有效场论中给出了由黑洞正则性条件界定的参数岛屿；并进一步考察弦论（string theory）与渐近安全引力（asymptotically safe gravity）是否落入该岛屿。

Conclusion: 单调性假设可将正则黑洞的渐进形成转化为对有效场论系数的显式约束，并形成可用于判别具体引力理论（如弦论、渐近安全引力）是否与正则化要求相容的参数判据。

Abstract: An infinite tower of higher-curvature corrections is expected to lead to regular black-hole solutions. We assume that this regular behavior does not abruptly arise, but gradually builds up with each successive order of the effective field theory of gravity. In addition, we assume that the effective field theory comes with a single scale of singularity-resolving new physics. We translate this assumption into a monotonicity requirement for deviations of the metric functions from a Schwarzschild black hole. Under these assumptions, conditions can be formulated which trace the increase of ``less singular'' black-hole behavior on and outside of the black-hole horizon within the regime of validity of the effective field theory. These conditions constrain the effective field theory coefficients. To exemplify this construction, we motivate five specific conditions and find a corresponding island of black hole regularity conditions at order eight in the effective field theory. We also explore whether string theory as well as asymptotically safe gravity lie on this island.

</details>


### [96] [Swing Surfaces in AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15854)
*Jacqueline Caminiti,Sabrina Pasterski*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究 AdS₃/CFT₂ 平极限（flat limit）下的全息纠缠熵（holographic entanglement entropy），厘清 Ryu-Takayanagi 面与平空间全息所提“摆动面（swing surface）”的关系，并给出摆动面长度的信息论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平空间全息（flat-space holography）中纠缠熵的合适几何表述尚不明确，标准 RT 公式与 swing 面 prescription 之间的关系及其信息论含义有待澄清。

Method: 证明普通 RT 公式不存在良定义的平空间极限，并将其关联到平极限下态-算符映射（state-operator map）的微妙之处；随后构造 swing prescription 的 AdS₃ 提升（uplift），并从两条互补路径建立信息论解释。

Result: swing 面长度可刻画一类特殊纯 CFT 态中适当选取的近平零边界区间（nearly null boundary intervals）之间的互信息（mutual information），其引力侧对应 AdS₃ 体（bulk）深处的散射过程；此外，swing 面的信息论解释可归结为全息中黑洞内视界（black hole inner horizons）的信息论解释这一更一般问题。

Conclusion: 标准 RT 公式无法直接延拓至平空间极限，swing 面是更合适的候选；其信息论含义一方面体现为近平零区间的互信息，另一方面与内视界的信息论诠释深刻相关。

Abstract: We study holographic entanglement entropy in the flat limit of AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$, and clarify the relation between Ryu-Takayanagi surfaces and the swing surfaces proposed for flat-space holography. We show that the ordinary RT formula does not admit a well-defined flat-space limit and connect this to subtleties of the state-operator map in the flat limit. We then construct an AdS$_3$ uplift of the swing prescription and develop its corresponding information-theoretic interpretation through two complementary approaches. First, we show that the swing surface length computes the mutual information between suitably chosen nearly null boundary intervals in a special class of pure CFT states. The gravity interpretation of this result involves scattering processes which take place deep inside of the AdS$_3$ bulk. Second, we reduce the problem of finding an information-theoretic interpretation for swing surfaces to the more general problem of finding an information-theoretic interpretation of black hole inner horizons in holography.

</details>


### [97] [$α'^2$-corrections to the bosonic string from an action-level double copy map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15862)
*Eric Lescano,Agustina Pereyra Grau,Jesús A. Rodríguez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: The low-energy effective action of the bosonic string contains higher-derivative corrections of the form Riem$^2$ and Riem$^3$ to the Einstein--Hilbert action, together with the associated Kalb--Ramond couplings. We construct an action-level double copy description of these corrections up to cubic order in the fields. Two ingredients are required: a higher-derivative gauge-theory Lagrangian, which up to field redefinitions is controlled by a single $F^3$ coupling, and a deformation of the color-to-kinematics replacement of the structure constants by the corresponding three-momentum tensor. We show that no higher-derivative gauge-theory operator can produce the Riem$^3$ vertex with the standard replacement rule, and that the matching conditions for the gauge-theory coupling and the deformation of the map admit a unique solution, a double root, which renders the cubic action left--right symmetric, of the form $(\mathrm{YM}+F^3)\otimes(\mathrm{YM}+F^3)$. Without further input, the resulting map reproduces the cubic $\mathrm{Riem}\,H^2$ coupling of the bosonic string at order $α'$, the absence of six-derivative cubic Kalb--Ramond couplings at order $α'^2$, and the absence of interactions odd in the Kalb--Ramond field. The comparison is performed in transverse-traceless gauge, up to field redefinitions.

</details>


### [98] [Cosmological correlators and kinematic flow on complex Hartle-Hawking saddles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15868)
*Vikramaditya Mondal,Shinji Mukohyama*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究在正宇宙学常数的闭合宇宙中，洛伦兹引力路径积分由复数鞍点（如Hartle-Hawking鞍点）主导时，如何在无需求解体时空演化的前提下计算宇宙学关联函数及其对运动学变量移动（运动学流）的响应。作者以带立方相互作用的共形耦合标量检验场为例，将波函数系数表示为离散谱求和，并导出满足闭合差分方程的两点与四点关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹引力路径积分（Lorentzian gravitational path integral）在闭合宇宙中由复鞍点主导，但在此类复背景上计算宇宙学关联函数（cosmological correlators）以及理解其对运动学变量移动——即运动学流（kinematic flow）——的响应，尚缺乏不显式追踪体时间演化的方法，本文旨在填补这一空缺。

Method: 研究围绕Hartle-Hawking复鞍点附近一个带立方相互作用的共形耦合标量检验场（conformally coupled scalar test field）展开；将波函数系数写为离散谱求和，其求和项为爱因斯坦静态宇宙（Einstein static universe）中的对应系数（可类比平直时空的polytopes program获得），并与Srivastava-Daoust多变量超几何函数（multi-variable hypergeometric functions）对应。对一、两点Feynman-Witten树图推导运动学变量中的差分方程，得到由上三角有理变换矩阵描述、满足路径无关一致性条件的离散运动学流（discrete kinematic flow）。

Result: 波函数系数被表示为离散谱求和而非平直FLRW情形下的积分；所识别的系数依赖末态超曲面（通过由其尺度固定的相位），并被确认为Srivastava-Daoust多变量超几何函数，对任意格点数满足递推关系，使求和坍缩为有理函数。一、两点树图的差分方程在有限求和集合上闭合；变换矩阵为上三角且元素有理，并满足路径无关的一致性条件。由此得到两点、三点和四点宇宙学关联函数，并在晚期共形边界（late-time conformal boundary）导出四点函数的显式差分方程。

Conclusion: 在Hartle-Hawking复鞍点上，宇宙学关联函数可由离散谱求和及闭合的差分方程系统刻画，形成路径无关的离散运动学流；该方法无需显式追踪体时间演化即可计算关联函数，并在晚期共形边界给出四点函数的显式差分方程。

Abstract: The Lorentzian gravitational path integral over homogeneous and isotropic metrics in a closed universe with positive cosmological constant is dominated by complex saddle points, such as the Hartle-Hawking saddles. We ask how to compute cosmological correlators on such complex backgrounds, and how they respond to the shifts of the kinematic variables---the kinematic flow---without tracking the bulk time evolution explicitly. We, specifically, study a conformally coupled scalar test field with a cubic interaction around these saddles. We find that the wave function coefficients can be written as discrete spectral sums with the summands containing the corresponding coefficients in the Einstein static universe, which are obtainable in analogy with the polytopes program for flat spacetime. Whereas the wave function coefficients of the flat FLRW case are given by integrals over shifted kinematics, the discrete spectrum on the three-sphere for Hartle-Hawking saddles produces sums. We identify the coefficients, which depend on the final hypersurface through a phase fixed by its size, with Srivastava-Daoust multi-variable hypergeometric functions. They satisfy recursion relations for any number of sites, under which the sums collapse into rational functions. For the one- and two-site Feynman-Witten tree diagrams, we derive difference equations in the kinematic variables which close on a finite set of sums, giving a discrete kinematic flow. The transformation matrices are upper triangular with rational elements, and satisfy a consistency condition which makes the flow path independent. We then obtain the two-, three- and four-point cosmological correlators. At the late-time conformal boundary, we derive an explicit difference equation for the four-point function.

</details>


### [99] [Cosmological Dressing Rules for Massive Scalars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15870)
*Jonathan Gräfe,Matthias Nowinski,Ivo Sachs,Siva Pawan Venna*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文指出德西特（de Sitter）关联函数的几种Schwinger-Keldysh表述，实为同一Sturm-Liouville问题的等价谱表示，并由此导出任意质量标量场的dressing规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德西特关联函数的Schwinger-Keldysh表述存在多种形式（调和分析与着装平坦空间关联子），其相互关系和统一处理方式尚不清晰。

Method: 将上述表述识别为同一Sturm-Liouville问题的等价谱表示（spectral representation），从其中一种出发推导任意质量标量场的dressing规则，辅助传播子以Euler积分形式给出。

Result: 得到适用于任意质量标量场的dressing规则，其辅助传播子（auxiliary propagators）表示为Euler积分，从而可直接使用平坦空间（flat space）的图论工具。

Conclusion: 多种Schwinger-Keldysh表述在谱层面等价，该统一视角使德西特计算可借助平坦空间费曼图技术完成。

Abstract: The Schwinger-Keldysh formulation of de Sitter correlation functions comes in different guises including harmonic analysis and dressed flat space correlators. We observe that these are equivalent spectral representations of the same Sturm-Liouville problem. Starting from the latter we derive dressing rules for scalar fields of arbitrary mass with auxiliary propagators in the form of Euler integrals that are accessible to flat space diagrammatic tools.

</details>


### [100] [Subsystem Flow: Quantum Error Correction, Petz Recovery, and Ergodic Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15887)
*Yidong Chen,Nima Lashkari,Kwing Lam Leung*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文将量子纠错（quantum error correction, QEC）中"子系统的编码保护"问题重新表述为固定参考态下冯诺依曼代数包含（inclusion）的模动力学（modular dynamics）性质，提出"模自治（modular autonomy）"概念，并用"子系统流（subsystem flow）"度量其偏离程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域多体量子系统中，自然给出固定参考态下代数包含 $\mathcal{N}\subset\mathcal{M}$；判断该包含是否存在抵御补集擦除的参考保持编码，本质上等价于询问 $\mathcal{N}$ 是否在该态的模动力学下保持不变。论文旨在建立纠错与模理论之间的定量联系。

Method: 定义子系统流以刻画偏离纠错区域的程度：远离自治时，无穷小流量化相对熵（relative entropy）损失，并以历史代数（history algebra）与持久核（persistent core）描述模轨道生成与保留的观测量。子系统流的解析延拓给出 Petz 恢复映射（Petz recovery map）与一族正的包含母哈密顿量（parent Hamiltonian）；并构造由重复 Petz 恢复映射生成的耗散半群。

Result: 一般情形下，以包含母哈密顿量演化可使 $\mathcal{N}$ 逸出 $\mathcal{M}$，某些情形下甚至生成整个 $\mathcal{M}\vee\mathcal{M}'$。具有共同循环分离向量（cyclic and separating vector）的包含生成一切；因子标准包含（如 QFT 中嵌套子区域）是遍历的（ergodic）。$L^2$-核性（$L^2$-nuclearity）蕴含母哈密顿量谱离散；耗散半群的不动点代数即模持久核，且在 $L^2$-核性假设下谱有隙。

Conclusion: 论文提出以包含母哈密顿量的本征值与本能向量定义量子混沌（quantum chaos）的局域谱测度的猜想，将 QEC、模理论、Petz 恢复与量子混沌谱诊断统一在同一框架下。

Abstract: In quantum error correction (QEC), the goal is to engineer an encoding that protects logical information against the erasure of a physical subsystem. In local many-body quantum systems, by contrast, nature provides a subsystem inclusion $\mN\subset \mM$ in a fixed reference state $\ketΩ$. Asking whether the inclusion admits a reference-preserving code that protects subsystem $\mN$ against erasure of the complement is equivalent to asking whether $\mN$ is preserved under the modular dynamics of $\mM$ in this state (modular autonomy). We define the subsystem flow, which measures the departure from this error correction regime. Away from autonomy, the infinitesimal flow quantifies relative-entropy loss, while the history algebra and persistent core describe which observables the modular orbit generates and retains. We call an inclusion ergodic when modular orbits generate everything and retain nothing. Analytic continuation of subsystem flow yields Petz recovery maps and a family of positive inclusion parent Hamiltonians. We show that, in general, evolving with these parent Hamiltonians allows $\mN$ to leave $\mM$, and in some cases, it can generate the entire $\mM\vee\mM'$. Inclusions with a common cyclic and separating vector generate everything, and standard inclusions of factors, such as those of nested subregions in QFT, are ergodic. Moreover, the $L^2$-nuclearity property implies that the spectrum of parent Hamiltonians is discrete. We define a dissipative semigroup by repeating the Petz recovery map, whose fixed-point algebra is the modular persistent core and whose spectrum is gapped under the assumption of $L^2$-nuclearity. We conjecture that the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of inclusion parent Hamiltonians can be used to define local spectral measures of quantum chaos.

</details>


### [101] [Exact Infrared Triangle in Gravity with Long-Range Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15921)
*Sangmin Choi,Ameya Kadhe,Prahar Mitra,Andrea Puhm*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明在长程相互作用存在时，一种新的超旋转荷守恒等价于经典对数软引力子定理的无质量极限，并给出位移记忆（displacement memory）尾巴的对应公式；结果对引力耦合精确，表明长程引力 dressing 可重求和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无质量物质耦合的爱因斯坦引力在红外（infrared）具有普适性；已有工作处理的是引力耦合于有质量物质的情形，本文关注长程相互作用下无质量极限的行为及其与软定理、记忆效应的联系。

Method: 通过引入并证明一种新的超旋转荷守恒（superrotation charge conservation），将其与经典对数软引力子定理（classical logarithmic soft graviton theorem）的无质量极限相联系，并提出位移记忆（displacement memory）对应尾巴的公式；同时对已知超旋转荷计算高阶修正。

Result: 所得关系对引力耦合（gravitational coupling）精确，显示长程引力 dressing 可重求和（resum）；高阶修正对引力耦合精确，并对应 Cachazo-Strominger 次领头软引力子定理的 1-loop 精确修正。

Conclusion: 长程相互作用下无质量物质引力的红外结构中，超旋转荷守恒与对数软定理、位移记忆尾巴相互对应，且相关结果在引力耦合上精确、可重求和，扩展并定性改变了此前关于有质量物质的工作。

Abstract: Einstein gravity coupled to massless matter exhibits universality in the infrared. In the presence of long-range interactions we show that a novel superrotation charge conservation is equivalent to the massless limit of the classical logarithmic soft graviton theorem, and we propose a formula for the corresponding tail to the displacement memory. These results, which extend earlier work on gravity coupled to massive matter in a qualitatively different way, are exact in the gravitational coupling and reveal that long-range gravitational dressings resum. We also compute higher-order corrections to the known superrotation charge which are exact in the gravitational coupling and would correspond to a 1-loop exact correction to the Cachazo-Strominger subleading soft graviton theorem.

</details>


### [102] [Magnetic-slope crossovers in the QCD bulk-to-shear viscosity ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15929)
*Aalok Misra,Shivam S. Kushwah,Keshav Dasgupta,Charles Gale,Sangyong Jeon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个对归一化不敏感的符号预言：纵向体积粘度与剪切粘度之比 ζ_∥/η_∥ 的温度斜率在去禁闭伪临界温度 T_0 上下各反转一次符号，反转点满足 T_1<T_0<T_2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场如何改变夸克-胶子物质（quark–gluon matter）的耗散，几乎无法从第一性原理加以约束，因为实时输运（real-time transport）不能直接被格点模拟（lattice simulation）所处理。

Method: 采用受 MQGP 启发的自顶向下 M/弦论（M/string-theory）构造的类 QCD 热等离子体，结合指定单模准粒子截断（single-mode quasiparticle truncation）下的动理学理论恒等式，以及利用现有格点状态方程（equation-of-state）数据的相容性构造。

Result: 符号预言获得多重支持：具备相位适配载流子（T_0 以上为夸克-胶子等离子体、以下为手征π介子气体）的动理学恒等式重现同一模式；格点声速（speed-of-sound）数据亦与其一致；去禁闭相的 bottom-up VQCD 支持高温下斜率为负；但现有 ζ_∥/s、η_∥/s 图无法分辨该斜率交叉。

Conclusion: 纵向体积-剪切粘度比斜率的双重符号反转是可被提出的、对归一化不敏感的预言，其中较低反转点 T_1 以某一π介子气体速率不等式为条件；该斜率交叉目前尚无法从已有数据图版中解析出来。

Abstract: How a magnetic field alters dissipation in quark--gluon matter is almost unconstrained from first principles, since real-time transport is not directly accessible to lattice simulation. Using a top-down MQGP-inspired M/string-theory construction of a QCD-like thermal plasma, together with a kinetic-theory identity within a specified single-mode quasiparticle truncation and a compatibility construction using available lattice equation-of-state data, we propose a normalization-insensitive sign prediction that the field slope of the longitudinal bulk-to-shear viscosity ratio ζ_\parallel/η_\parallel reverses sign twice with temperature---below and above the deconfinement pseudo-critical temperature T_0, at T_1<T_0<T_2 (the lower reversal T_1 conditional on a stated pion-gas rate inequality). A kinetic-theory identity with phase-appropriate carriers, a quark--gluon plasma above T_0 and a chiral pion gas below, reproduces the same pattern, and lattice speed-of-sound data can be shown to be consistent as well. The bottom-up VQCD in the deconfined phase supports a negative slope for large temperatures; the slope crossover is not resolvable from the available ζ_\parallel/s, η_\parallel/s plots.

</details>


### [103] [Imaging the two-sided black hole interior with four-point correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15933)
*Joydeep Chakravarty*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出一种用波包涂抹（smeared with wavepackets）的边界四点函数（boundary four-point function），用于探测双侧 AdS 黑洞视界（horizon）背后的局域物理，并可对黑洞内部进行成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是寻找能够探测黑洞视界（horizon）背后局域物理的可观测量，并克服直接描述内部散射的困难。

Method: 利用算符（operator）的非平凡时间排序（time ordering），并将一个态依赖（state-dependent）算符插入移动半个热圆（thermal circle）；在高频下，指向内部体点的边界波包沿测地线（geodesic）传播并在该点附近局域散射。

Result: 在高频极限下，关联函数（correlator）因式分解为类平空间的散射振幅（flat-space like scattering amplitude）。

Conclusion: 该可观测量还能探测非平凡态特征：视界后的零冲击（null shocks）与物质壳层（matter shells）表现为信号跳变，从而实现对黑洞内部的成像。

Abstract: We propose a boundary four-point function smeared with wavepackets, which probes local physics behind the horizon of a two-sided AdS black hole. At high frequencies, boundary wavepackets aimed at an interior bulk point travel along geodesics and scatter locally about the bulk point, factorizing the correlator into a flat-space like scattering amplitude. To obtain this configuration, we utilize a non-trivial time ordering of operators, and also use one state-dependent operator insertion moved halfway across the thermal circle. An interesting property of this observable is that it also probes features of nontrivial states: null shocks and matter shells behind the horizon show up as jumps in the observed signal as the bulk point crosses them, thereby imaging the black hole interior.

</details>


### [104] [Extension of Poincare superalgebra via hidden symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15946)
*Ümit Ertem*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将庞加莱超代数（Poincare superalgebra）的奇部由Killing矢量场扩展为特殊奇Killing-Yano形式与特殊偶闭共形Killing-Yano形式，偶部仍对应Killing旋量（Killing spinors），并通过定义相应括号证明隐藏对称性与Killing旋量共同构成庞加莱超代数的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流形的等距（isometries）与Killing旋量原本对应庞加莱超代数结构，而Killing矢量场可推广为高阶反对称形式即Killing-Yano形式；作者希望把这一推广纳入超代数框架，以统一描述隐藏对称性（hidden symmetries）与Killing旋量。

Method: 考虑Killing矢量场到高阶形式的反对称推广（Killing-Yano形式），将庞加莱超代数的奇部从Killing矢量场空间及其旋转表示扩展为特殊奇Killing-Yano形式与特殊偶闭共形Killing-Yano形式（closed conformal Killing-Yano forms）构成的空间，偶部仍取Killing旋量空间，并定义相关的括号运算。

Result: 通过定义相应的括号（brackets），作者表明隐藏对称性与Killing旋量共同定义了一个庞加莱超代数的扩展（extension of the Poincare superalgebra）。

Conclusion: Killing-Yano形式可作为庞加莱超代数奇部的推广载体，从而把隐藏对称性与Killing旋量统一于同一超代数扩展结构之中。

Abstract: Isometries of a manifold and special types of spinors called Killing spinors which are related to Killing vector fields constitute a symmetry superalgebra structure corresponding to Poincare superalgebra. The antisymmetric generalization of Killing vector fields to higher degree forms which are called Killing-Yano forms are considered. The odd part of the Poincare superalgebra is extended from the space of Killing vector fields and their rotational representation to the space of special odd Killing-Yano forms and special even closed conformal Killing-Yano forms. The even part is again corresponds to the space of Killing spinors. By defining the relevant brackets, we show that hidden symmetries and Killing spinors together define an extension of the Poincare superalgebra.

</details>


### [105] [Self-Force and Bremsstrahlung in Finite Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15993)
*Kyungsun Lee,Piljin Yi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在有限自场与自能的非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）框架下，研究运动点电荷的自力（self-force）及其伴随的轫致辐射（Bremsstrahlung），以Born–Infeld理论为原型，论证局部自力与全局辐射耗散之间的相互一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 麦克斯韦理论中的自力问题历来受众多知名物理学家关注，作者在回顾Coleman关于麦克斯韦情形的清晰论述后，希望将同一问题推广到非线性电动力学中考察，以检验自力与辐射反作用在非线性情形下的表现。

Method: 以Born–Infeld理论作为原型，分别独立计算局部自力（local self-force）与全局辐射耗散（global radiative dissipation），并讨论文献中自力两种不同表现形式及其在本计算中的体现；附录进一步考察Born–Infeld ModMax作为推广情形。

Result: 局部自力与全局辐射耗散的计算结果相互一致；尽管非线性在渐近辐射场处失效，其残余效应仍通过运动电荷的有效多极矩（effective multipole moments）保留下来，从而使结果偏离麦克斯韦理论。

Conclusion: 非线性电动力学中自力与轫致辐射可在局部与全局两个层面得到自洽描述，非线性效应经由有效多极矩在辐射场中留下可观测痕迹，为区分Born–Infeld类理论与麦克斯韦理论提供了依据。

Abstract: We study the self-force and the accompanying Bremsstrahlung for a moving point charge in nonlinear electrodynamics with finite self-field and self-energy. The self-force in the Maxwell theory has been the subject of many studies, often by renowned physicists. After reviewing Coleman's lucid treatise for Maxwell among them, we wish to explore the same question in the Born--Infeld theory, our prototype. We independently compute the local self-force and the global radiative dissipation, and show how the two are mutually consistent. Despite the nonlinearity turning off for the asymptotic radiative field, its residual effect survives via the effective multipole moments of a moving charge, allowing deviation from Maxwell. We close with a detailed discussion about two different manifestations of the self-force in the literature and how these enter our computation. One of the appendices explores Born--Infeld ModMax as a generalization.

</details>


### [106] [Silent Heavy Fields: An Exclusion Rule for the Cosmological Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16029)
*Shuntaro Aoki,Zhehan Qin,Masahide Yamaguchi,Yuhang Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导出一条“排除规则（exclusion rule）”：在一大类图示中，宇宙对撞机（cosmological collider）信号严格为零。该规则覆盖 de Sitter 不变重扇区与单个外场在单一顶点共变耦合的两类图，先对重扇区两点函数媒介的交换图证至全圈阶，再通过将一圈多边形图的振荡贡献匹配到第一类而推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙对撞机利用原初关联子（primordial correlators）中的振荡特征探测暴胀期间的重场，但存在盲区（blind spots）：重场可以在时间上持续“滴答”却在关联子中保持沉默。因此需要明确信号在何种条件下消失，并重新审视落入这些类的既有过程。

Method: 先针对重扇区两点函数（two-point function）媒介的交换图，采用谱分解（spectral decomposition）后接场重定义（field redefinition），证明信号在全部圈阶（loop orders）消失；随后将一圈多边形图（one-loop polygons）的振荡贡献匹配到第一类图，从而推广排除规则。另构建一个由化学势（chemical potentials）启发的玩具模型（toy model）作为非零示例。

Result: 排除规则成立：对于所覆盖的两类图，宇宙对撞机信号为零；因此此前研究过的、落入这些类的过程其近似信号必须重新审视。作为非零（nonvanishing）示例，首次推导出化学势启发的玩具模型中三角圈（triangle loop）的领头信号。

Conclusion: 宇宙对撞机探测存在系统性盲区，可由一条结构性排除规则刻画；该规则为判定信号是否存在提供判据，并给出需重访的既有计算范围。同时通过一个显式非零案例说明信号在规则之外仍可出现。

Abstract: The cosmological collider uses oscillatory signatures in primordial correlators to probe heavy fields during inflation. This probe has blind spots, as heavy fields can keep ticking in time while remaining silent in the correlators. In this letter, we derive an exclusion rule under which cosmological collider signals vanish. The rule covers two classes of diagrams where a de Sitter invariant heavy sector couples covariantly to a single external field at one vertex. We first prove it for exchanges mediated by the two-point function of the heavy sector to all loop orders, using a spectral decomposition followed by a field redefinition. The result then extends to one-loop polygons by matching their oscillatory contributions onto those of the first class. The approximate signals of previously studied processes that fall into these classes must therefore be revisited. As a nonvanishing example, we derive the leading signal of a triangle loop for the first time in a toy model motivated by chemical potentials.

</details>


### [107] [Anti-periodic Gravitino and Dirac Effective Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16047)
*Karim Benakli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在保持 $U(1)_R$ 对称性（$U(1)_R$ symmetry）以禁止引力微子（gravitino）Majorana 质量、允许 Dirac 质量的框架下，于 $N=1$ 超空间（superspace）中构造了描述引力微子 Dirac 质量的有效算符，并在 $S^1/\mathbb Z_2$ 紧化的五维超引力中检验其结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引入守恒的 $U(1)_R$ 对称性可禁止引力微子 Majorana 质量而允许 Dirac 质量，但类比已知的 Dirac 规范微子（gaugino）质量，尚需在有效场论层面理解引力微子 Dirac 质量如何由 $N=1$ 超空间有效算符描述。

Method: 将自旋-3/2 双线性算符（spin-3/2 bilinear operator）用规范不变的引力微子曲率超场（gravitino curvature superfields）表示，通过含逆达朗贝尔算符（inverse d'Alembertian）的非局域核（nonlocal kernel）耦合；在 $S^1/\mathbb Z_2$ 紧化的最小五维超引力中，结合 Scherk-Schwarz twist 诱导的反周期边界条件分析两点函数，并分离物理模与规范模，得到 $16\times16$ 矩阵形式的费米子二次核。

Result: 物理 Kaluza-Klein Dirac 质量表现为与端点局域源相关联的曲率超场两点函数中的极点（poles）；该 $16\times16$ 矩阵核的矩阵元与 $N=1$ 超空间中两个不变量（invariants）的费米子分量相符，包括其旋量缩并（spinor contractions）与归一化。

Conclusion: 在具有非零 radion 辅助场（radion auxiliary field）的固定背景上，$N=1$ 超空间算符描述与五维紧化中物理 Kaluza-Klein Dirac 质量谱一致，支持以曲率超场有效算符刻画引力微子 Dirac 质量的构造。

Abstract: Introducing a preserved $U(1)_R$ symmetry forbids gravitini Majorana masses while allowing a Dirac mass. We investigate how a Dirac mass for gravitini can be described by an effective operator in $N=1$ superspace, in analogy with the well-known Dirac gaugino mass. We find that the corresponding spin-$3/2$ bilinear operator can be expressed in terms of gauge-invariant gravitino curvature superfields coupled through a nonlocal kernel involving an inverse d'Alembertian. We study this construction in minimal five-dimensional supergravity compactified on $S^1/\mathbb Z_2$. The gravitino satisfies anti-periodic boundary conditions induced by a Scherk-Schwarz twist. The physical Kaluza-Klein Dirac masses then appear as poles in the two-point functions of the curvature superfields associated with sources localized at the endpoints. Separation of physical and gauge modes relates these propagators to a quadratic fermionic kernel represented by a $16\times16$ matrix. Its elements match the fermionic components of two invariants written in $N=1$ superspace, including their spinor contractions and normalization. This matching is carried out on a fixed background with a nonzero radion auxiliary field.

</details>


### [108] [Holographic Wilsonian RG of Yang-Mills theory in AdS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16074)
*Soner Albayrak*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文把随机量子化（stochastic quantization）与Wilson重整化（Wilsonian renormalization）之间的平衡-演化对偶推广到反德西特（anti-de Sitter, AdS）空间中的非阿贝尔规范理论，以AdS径向坐标充当随机时间，并将Langevin过程识别为径向演化的基态（Doob）变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作建立了随机量子化与Wilson重整化之间的平衡-演化对偶，但此前主要限于较简单的情形；本文旨在将该对偶扩展至AdS空间中带非阿贝尔规范场的体理论（bulk theories），从而把全息Wilsonian框架与随机方法联系起来。

Method: 对无显式径向依赖的体理论，在所有场阶（to all orders in the fields）上证明截断ε=t处的Langevin分布等于紫外与红外地径向波函数之积，据此将该随机过程识别为径向演化的基态（Doob）变换；并通过显式计算扩展随机-重整化对偶词典，同时在树图层面（tree-level）于AdS₄中给出全息Wilsonian作用的三重迹与四重迹耦合（triple-trace and quadruple-trace couplings）的闭式表达式。

Result: 建立了随机-重整化对偶的扩展词典：体场的径向分量对应Zwanziger随机规范固定函数，径向规范对应Parisi-Wu量子化，体规范A_z=α⁻¹∂_iA_i对应Zwanziger规范；所得三重迹与四重迹耦合在红外极限下化为已知胶子关联函数（gluon correlators），在总能量极点处化为平直空间Yang-Mills振幅。

Conclusion: 随机量子化与全息Wilsonian重整化在AdS非阿贝尔规范理论中存在统一的演化对偶描述；虽然显式结果限于树图阶与AdS₄，文中亦讨论了德西特（de Sitter）情形的对应陈述以及圈修正（loop corrections）。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the equilibrium-evolution duality between stochastic quantization and Wilsonian renormalization to non-abelian gauge theories in anti-de Sitter space, for which the radial coordinate of AdS plays the role of the stochastic time. We show, to all orders in the fields for bulk theories without explicit radial dependence, that the Langevin distribution at time $t$ is the product of the ultraviolet and infrared radial wavefunctions at the cutoff $ε=t$. We therefore identify this Langevin process as the ground-state (Doob) transform of the radial evolution. By explicit calculation, we also extend the existing dictionary in the stochastic-renormalization duality: the radial component of the bulk field is Zwanziger's stochastic gauge-fixing function; radial gauge is Parisi-Wu quantization; and the bulk gauge $A_z=α^{-1}\partial_iA_i$ is Zwanziger's gauge. We also obtain the expressions for the triple-trace and quadruple-trace couplings of the holographic Wilsonian action at finite cutoff in closed form; in the infrared, they reduce to the known gluon correlators and, at the total-energy pole, to flat-space Yang-Mills amplitudes as expected. Although our explicit results are provided at tree-level and derived in AdS$_4$, we also comment on the de Sitter counterpart of these statements and on the loop corrections.

</details>


### [109] [Holographic Counterterms from Restricted Weyl Symmetry in Even-Dimensional Einstein-AdS Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16187)
*Giorgos Anastasiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出基于对称性的重整化方法，利用保持场方程渐近成立的无穷小体韦尔变换，构造偶数维Einstein-AdS引力的体协变完备化，并给出任意偶数维的闭式解与有限on-shell作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对偶数维Einstein-AdS引力中体维数发散与全息重整化的需求，寻求一种基于对称性原理、无需逐维构造的重整化方案。

Method: 要求Einstein-Hilbert密度在受限韦尔变换（参数Σ(X)）下变分至径向截断移除后积分有限的项，从而引入Euler密度与协变全导数层级两类补偿项；导出以平移拉普拉斯算子因式化表示的递推关系，得到任意偶数维闭式完备化，并与环境构造（ambient construction）的共形不变量比较。

Result: 在Einstein背景上（匹配归一化后）与环境构造结果一致；重整化体积恒等式在任意偶数维给出欧氏AlAdS Einstein几何的on-shell有限性；复现四、六、八维已知共形重整化作用量，并给出含至□^3的显式十维完备化；对具非共形平坦Einstein视界的八维与十维AdS黑洞，on-shell作用量有限且与全息重整化（Holographic Renormalization）一致。

Conclusion: 该对称性方法为偶数维Einstein-AdS引力提供了统一、闭式的重整化方案，并可与环境构造及全息重整化结果相互印证。

Abstract: We develop a symmetry-based approach to renormalization for even-dimensional Einstein-AdS gravity based on infinitesimal bulk Weyl transformations that preserve its field equations asymptotically. We determine a bulk-covariant completion of the Einstein-Hilbert density by requiring its variation under restricted Weyl transformations with parameter $Σ(X)$ to vanish modulo terms whose integrals remain finite as the radial cutoff is removed. This requirement induces two types of compensating terms with fixed couplings: the Euler density and a hierarchy of covariant total derivatives. The resulting recursive relations admit a factorized solution in terms of shifted Laplacians, yielding a closed-form completion in arbitrary even bulk dimensions. On Einstein backgrounds, these expressions agree, after matching normalizations, with those obtained through the ambient construction of conformal invariants. The associated renormalized-volume identity establishes on-shell finiteness for Euclidean AlAdS Einstein geometries in arbitrary even dimensions. The prescription reproduces the known conformally renormalized actions in four, six and eight dimensions and provides an explicit ten-dimensional completion involving powers of the Laplacian up to $\Box^3$. Applications to eight- and ten-dimensional AdS black holes with non-conformally-flat Einstein horizons yield finite on-shell actions in agreement with Holographic Renormalization.

</details>


### [110] [Multiparticle residues of open-string tachyon amplitudes from DDF sewing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16280)
*Dripto Biswas,Qu Cao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过在临界开玻色弦中求和全部物理中间态，计算了色序快子盘振幅（tachyon disk amplitudes）的最大同时留数（maximal simultaneous residues），并借助三次 DDF 顶点的缝合（sewing）将其约化为高斯收缩（Gaussian contractions）与行列式前因子（determinant prefactor），从而给出多点留数的显式矩阵表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为开玻色弦高点多点振幅的留数提供系统计算方法；四次点情形应与 Veneziano 极点系数（Veneziano pole coefficients）一致，并需与独立 Koba–Nielsen 展开对照，同时为联系 Grunsky 算子（Grunsky operators）与高阶 Koba–Nielsen 顶形式（top forms）建立基础。

Method: 将三次 Del Giudice–Di Vecchia–Fubini（DDF）顶点缝合，使任意平面三次树（planar cubic tree）上的求和化为高斯收缩（Gaussian contractions），每个内部能级独立指定并固定精确的有限模式截断（mode cutoff）；留数由缝合变量中的系数提取得到，形式为行列式前因子乘以源的二次型指数（determinant prefactor times exponential of a quadratic form）。

Result: 在一致的缝合约定下，局部坐标尺度（local-coordinate scales）在顶点与缝合度规（sewing metric）之间相消；四点情形利用三次局部映射与单玻色子应力张量 Ward 恒等式（one-boson stress-tensor Ward identity）给出全阶重求和（all-order resummation），复现 Veneziano 极点系数；并给出五点双留数（double residue）以及六点梳形（comb）与雪花形（snowflake）构型的显式矩阵，附带低能级检验。

Conclusion: 所得行列式为横向玻色子的真空缝合因子（vacuum sewing factor）；论文进一步表述了将该构造与 Grunsky 算子及完整高阶 Koba–Nielsen 顶形式相联系所需的算子、坐标映射与测度恒等式（operator, coordinate-map and measure identities）。

Abstract: We compute the maximal simultaneous residues of color-ordered tachyon disk amplitudes in the critical open bosonic string by summing all physical intermediate states. Sewing cubic Del Giudice--Di Vecchia--Fubini (DDF) vertices reduces this sum to Gaussian contractions for any planar cubic tree, with the internal levels specified independently. Each internal level fixes an exact finite mode cutoff. The residue then follows by coefficient extraction in the sewing variables from a determinant prefactor times the exponential of a quadratic form in the sources. With consistent sewing conventions, the local-coordinate scales cancel between the vertices and the sewing metric. At four points, cubic local maps and a one-boson stress-tensor Ward identity give an all-order resummation that recovers the Veneziano pole coefficients. The same construction gives explicit matrices for the five-point double residue and the six-point comb and snowflake, with low-level checks against independent Koba--Nielsen expansions. The determinant is the vacuum sewing factor of the transverse bosons. We formulate the operator, coordinate-map and measure identities needed to relate this construction to Grunsky operators and the full higher-point Koba--Nielsen top forms.

</details>


### [111] [Generalized Kosmann derivative and symmetry operators on Dirac structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16309)
*Ümit Ertem,Özgür Açık,Özgür Kelekçi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文在 $H$-twisted exact Courant algebroids 上为 metric Dirac structures 建立旋量对称框架，证明正旋量 Kosmann--Dorfman 作用与内蕴 Kosmann 导数的精确交织，并导出 KY/CKY 可积恒等式、Killing 旋量保持算子与 $S^2\times S^1$、$S^3$ 上的具体例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将广义等距（generalized isometries）、隐藏旋量对称（hidden spinorial symmetries）与通量敏感的 Lie algebroid 几何联系起来，为 metric Dirac structures 提供内蕴的旋量对称描述。

Method: 借助广义度量（generalized metric）赋予 Dirac structure 内蕴 Riemannian Lie-algebroid 几何，并与广义切丛正子丛等距等同；对保持广义度量的截面证明正旋量 Kosmann--Dorfman 作用与内蕴 Kosmann 导数的精确交织，该比较不依赖辅助相容广义联络；在内蕴几何中推导 KY/CKY 可积恒等式并由 Killing 旋量的 Hermitian 双线性构造相应形式。

Result: 得到 Killing--Yano（KY）与共形 Killing--Yano（CKY）可积恒等式，由奇特殊 KY 与偶特殊闭 CKY 形式构造保持 Killing 旋量的算子，并由虚 Killing 数的 Killing 旋量 Hermitian 双线性得到这些形式；在 $S^2\times S^1$ 上带非平凡 $H$-flux 类与秩二 anchor 的整体 Dirac structure 上给出显式 spin connection 与对称算子，Killing 旋量空间为横截族 $C^\infty(S^1,\mathbb C^2)$；内蕴 Killing 截面通过 Lie 代数表示作用于 Killing 旋量。

Conclusion: 内蕴 Killing 截面以 Lie 代数表示作用于 Killing 旋量；$S^3$ 上 graph Dirac structure 的 Hermitian-current bracket 奇 Jacobi 恒等式相差一个相位而失效，附加单个偶相位生成元后构成 Lie superalgebra，表明该框架统一刻画广义等距、隐藏旋量对称与通量敏感几何。

Abstract: We develop a spinorial symmetry framework for metric Dirac structures in $H$-twisted exact Courant algebroids. A generalized metric equips a Dirac structure with an intrinsic Riemannian Lie-algebroid geometry and identifies it isometrically with the positive subbundle of the generalized tangent bundle. For sections preserving the generalized metric, we prove an exact intertwining between the positive-spinor Kosmann--Dorfman action and the intrinsic Kosmann derivative. The comparison is independent of the auxiliary compatible generalized connection. Within the intrinsic geometry we derive Killing--Yano (KY) and conformal Killing--Yano (CKY) integrability identities, construct Killing-spinor-preserving operators from odd special KY and even special closed CKY forms, and obtain these forms from Hermitian bilinears of Killing spinors with imaginary Killing number. We illustrate the framework on a global Dirac structure over $S^2\times S^1$ with nontrivial $H$-flux class and rank-two anchor. Its spin connection and symmetry operators are explicit, and its Killing-spinor space is a transverse family $C^\infty(S^1,\mathbb C^2)$. Finally, intrinsic Killing sections act on Killing spinors by a Lie algebra representation. For a graph Dirac structure over $S^3$, the odd Jacobi identity of the Hermitian-current bracket fails by a phase, and adjoining a single even phase generator yields a Lie superalgebra. These results connect generalized isometries, hidden spinorial symmetries, and flux-sensitive Lie-algebroid geometry.

</details>


### [112] [Thermodynamics within and across AdS Gauss--Bonnet holographic CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16364)
*Limin Zeng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在五维AdS高斯–邦尼（Gauss–Bonnet）引力框架下，研究单个全息共形场论（CFT）内部以及不同反常系数（anomaly coefficient）理论之间的热竞争，并给出耦合区间端点理论间的一阶切换判据与广义Clapeyron关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息对偶中，不同反常系数的CFT如何在有限耦合区间内发生热力学选择，以及这种跨理论比较能否化为端点比较，缺乏系统判据；同时固定CFT内的Hawking–Page转变与带电小–大黑洞共存也需厘清。

Method: 从边界第一定律（first law）导出与无量纲耦合共轭的功项，使用共同边界clock在固定外部源下比较带电球对称鞍点（saddle）；在单位lapse柱面clock与零D型真空能下，利用固定半径处的严格凹性（strict concavity）将全局比较约化为两端点。

Result: 改变外部源驱动耦合区间端点CFT间的一阶切换，共存线（coexistence line）斜率与熵差通过广义Clapeyron关系联系；Einstein端点自身处于小–大共存时第三鞍点加入并使跨理论边界出现kink；有限能量平移下外部控制的补偿改变可保持鞍点序。固定CFT内确定了中性Hawking–Page线与带电小–大黑洞共存面，临界电荷在有限耦合处趋于零，熵隙以1/2指数闭合，临界电荷温度扫描下热容以2/3指数发散。

Conclusion: 跨CFT热竞争可借助端点比较与广义Clapeyron关系有效刻画，第三鞍点产生非解析kink；固定CFT内的相变临界行为呈非标准临界指数，耦合与外部源共同控制相结构。

Abstract: We study thermal competition within individual holographic conformal field theories (CFTs) and across theories with different anomaly coefficients in five-dimensional AdS Gauss--Bonnet gravity. We derive the work term conjugate to the dimensionless coupling from the boundary first law and use a common boundary clock to compare charged spherical saddles at fixed external source. Varying this source drives a first-order switch between the CFTs at the endpoints of the chosen coupling interval. In the unit-lapse cylinder clock with the zero-type-D vacuum energy, strict concavity at fixed radius reduces the global comparison to these two endpoints. The preferred coupling jumps across the coexistence line, whose slope is related to the entropy difference by a generalized Clapeyron relation. Where the Einstein endpoint itself undergoes small--large coexistence, a third saddle joins the competition and produces a kink in the cross-theory boundary. A compensating change of the external control preserves saddle ordering under finite energy shifts. Within fixed CFTs, we determine the neutral Hawking--Page line and the charged small--large black hole coexistence surface. The critical charge tends to zero at a finite coupling. The entropy gap closes with exponent one half, and the heat capacity diverges with exponent two thirds along a temperature sweep at the critical charge.

</details>


### [113] [Matching lattice and continuum chiral determinants at finite volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16544)
*Zhengwen Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在平直四环面上针对一个 SU(5) 代，证明了 Craig 的规范不变重叠测度与 Hsu–Witten 的连续 Weyl 行列式（相位由五维 η-不变量固定）在连续极限下沿光滑有隙路径的权重在固定归一化并减去局域 Yang–Mills 抵消项后一致，并给出了该抵消项系数与一致收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点手征规范理论（lattice chiral gauge theories）中复费米子权重的定义存在多种方案，其相互一致性问题尚未在有限振幅与一般背景下得到系统确立，本文旨在检验并量化两类定义的等价性。

Method: 比较 Craig 的规范不变重叠测度（gauge-invariant overlap measure）与 Hsu–Witten 基于重构规范联络的连续 Weyl 行列式（continuum Weyl determinant），采用真空展开与 Dirac 能隙论证，证明重构界并将 Hsu 的小场比较推广到有限振幅，同时给出对数变分的一致收敛速率与数值比较。

Result: 在有界光滑族上仅需一致的 Dirac 能隙（uniform Dirac gap），无需真空展开收敛；固定归一化并减去系数已确定的局域 Yang–Mills 抵消项（Yang-Mills counterterm）后，两者相对权重在连续极限下一致。对 Hsu 的反射正（reflection-positive）重构（平坦领口），相位变分仍匹配，但二次抵消项的对数系数被一个可计算、依赖剖面的因子增强。在真空展开发散的 hypercharge 背景中，数值结果支持相位的二次收敛，而模量对二次标度出现对数修正，其系数可由自由重叠真空极化（overlap vacuum polarization）解析给出。

Conclusion: 两类格点手征规范理论费米子权重定义在连续极限下可匹配，匹配条件为一致 Dirac 能隙与适当的局域抵消；反射正重构下需计入剖面依赖的系数增强，且模量的对数修正可由自由重叠真空极化解析预测，为格点手征规范理论的连续极限一致性提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Recent formulations of lattice chiral gauge theories define a complex fermion weight either through Craig's gauge-invariant overlap measure or, following Hsu and Witten, through a continuum Weyl determinant on a reconstructed gauge connection, with its phase fixed by the five-dimensional $η$-invariant. For one $SU(5)$ generation on a flat four-torus, we prove that their relative weights agree in the continuum limit along smooth gapped paths, after fixing a normalization and subtracting a local Yang-Mills counterterm, whose coefficient we determine. On bounded smooth families a uniform Dirac gap suffices: the vacuum expansion need not converge. We obtain explicit uniform convergence rates for the logarithmic variations and, for Craig's interpolation, prove the reconstruction bounds that the matching requires, extending Hsu's small-field comparison to finite amplitude. For Hsu's reflection-positive reconstruction with flat collars, the phase variations still match, while the logarithmic coefficient of the quadratic counterterm is enhanced by a calculable, profile-dependent factor. In a hypercharge background on which the vacuum expansion diverges, numerical comparisons at the input gauge field support quadratic convergence of the phase, whereas the modulus acquires a logarithmic correction to quadratic scaling, whose coefficient follows analytically from the free overlap vacuum polarization.

</details>


### [114] [Accelerating solitons in Rindler space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16568)
*Huiquan Li,Jianyong Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在二维闵可夫斯基时空中可积的相对论性非线性场论在林德勒空间中依然可积；本文研究林德勒空间中经典正弦-戈尔登（sine-Gordon, SG）孤子的性质，发现加速观测者只能看到部分孤子，且能量可流入流出林德勒视界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论性可积场论在弯曲时空（特别是林德勒空间）中的可积性与孤子行为尚需澄清，尤其是加速观测者对孤子的可观测部分及能量交换机制的刻画。

Method: 以二维闵可夫斯基时空中可积的相对论性非线性场论为出发点，将其置于林德勒空间中，考察经典正弦-戈尔登（sine-Gordon, SG）孤子解的结构与运动学，分析两个林德勒楔（Rindler wedge）中加速观测者的可观测区域。

Result: 每个林德勒楔内的加速观测者只能看到完整闵可夫斯基孤子的一部分，该部分自身构成一个携带部分拓扑荷（topological charge）的孤子；孤子解可解释为从视界入射、减速停于某点、随后回落并加速至光速飞向视界。能量可流入与流出林德勒视界（Rindler horizon），高能孤子从视界获得能量，低能孤子则失去能量。

Conclusion: 结果表明可积的相对论性非线性场论在林德勒空间中保持可积，孤子在该背景下的截断与能量交换具有明确的运动学与拓扑解释。

Abstract: Relativistic nonlinear field theories that are integrable in two-dimensional Minkowski spacetime remain integrable in Rindler space. We investigate the properties of classical sine-Gordon (SG) solitons in Rindler space. Accelerating observers in each of the two Rindler wedges can see only a portion of the full soliton in Minkowski spacetime; this portion itself forms a soliton carrying a partial topological charge. The soliton solution admits the following interpretation: a soliton incoming from the horizon decelerates to stop at some point, then falls back and accelerates to the speed of light toward the horizon. Energy can flow into and out of the Rindler horizon. The higher energy soliton gains energy from the horizon while the lower one loses energy.

</details>


### [115] [Is Spin Slow in Hot QCD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16684)
*Zi-Hao Liu,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 轻夸克自旋在夸克-胶子等离子体中并不缓慢弛豫，其阻尼速率为输运级 $g^4T\ln(1/g)$，与矢量流相当，因而将自旋提升为流体力学变量缺乏动力学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自旋（spin）提升为流体力学变量（hydrodynamic variable）需满足缓慢弛豫前提，而热 QCD 中质量压低的螺旋度翻转（helicity flips）似乎支持这一图像，本文旨在检验该前提是否成立。

Method: 采用完整的领头阶碰撞算符（leading-order collision operator）分析轻夸克自旋的动力学弛豫，考察螺旋度守恒的软、小角散射（small-angle scatterings）对自旋的转动作用。

Result: 轻夸克自旋以输运速率 $g^4T\ln(1/g)$ 阻尼，几乎与矢量流（vector currents）一样快；阻尼涉及多个动力学模式（kinetic modes）而非单一弛豫时间，整体旋转仅贡献可逆进动（reversible precession），耦合增强更不利于慢自旋模式。

Conclusion: 由于空间自旋与动量锁定，螺旋度守恒散射即可高效转动自旋，夸克-胶子等离子体中不存在缓慢的自旋模式，自旋流体力学假设受到质疑。

Abstract: Promoting spin to a hydrodynamic variable requires slow relaxation, as mass-suppressed helicity flips in hot QCD suggest. Using the full leading-order collision operator, we show instead that light-quark spin damps at the transport rate $g^4T\ln(1/g)$, nearly as fast as vector currents. Since spatial spin locks to momentum, helicity-conserving soft, small-angle scatterings readily rotate it. Damping involves multiple kinetic modes beyond a single relaxation time, global rotation adds only reversible precession, and stronger coupling further disfavors a slow spin mode in quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [116] [Discrete anomaly inflow from SymTFT of $U(1)$ global symmetry: the modified Villain resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16696)
*Napat Poovuttikul,K. Sapmee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文利用修改的 Villain 表述（modified Villain formulation）在格点上构造了带反常系数 k 的 U(1) 整体对称性的对称性拓扑场论（SymTFT），并用格点 Majorana 费米子显式实现奇 k 所需的旋量结构（spin structure）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是超越群上同调（group cohomology），从连续对称性的反常流入（anomaly inflow）出发推导整体反常的交点数据（junction data），而格点构造提供了不依赖玻色影（bosonic shadow）的直接途径。

Method: 在修改的 Villain 表述下构造格点 SymTFT；论证 Majorana 费米子位于对称线的交点处，即两个 U(1) 元素融合绕圆之处；从线与交点的格点代数导出体（bulk）及有能隙边界上的 R-符号与 F-符号（R-/F-symbols）。

Result: 对奇 k，边界 F-符号满足 U(1) 对称性缺陷的 Gu-Wen 关系，且无需经过玻色影；限制到 Z_2 ⊂ U(1) 时，从映射环面（mapping torus）与费米子堆叠规则（fermionic stacking rule）复现 mod-4 反常相位。

Conclusion: 作为该 SymTFT 的简单应用，确定了哪些子群 Z_d ⊂ U(1) 可以被规范化（gauged）及所得理论的对称性；该工作是从连续对称性反常流入推导超越群上同调的整体反常交点数据的第一步。

Abstract: We construct a lattice SymTFT for a $U(1)$ global symmetry with anomaly coefficient $k$ using the modified Villain formulation, in which the spin structure required for odd $k$ is realised explicitly by lattice Majorana fermions. We demonstrate that the same Majorana fermions sit at the junctions of the symmetry lines, exactly where the fusion of two $U(1)$ elements wraps around the circle. From the lattice algebra of lines and junctions we derive the $R$- and $F$-symbols in the bulk and on the gapped boundary, and show that for odd $k$ the boundary $F$-symbol obeys the Gu-Wen relation for the $U(1)$ symmetry defect, without passing through a bosonic shadow. Restricting to $\mathbb{Z}_2\subset U(1)$, we reproduce the mod-4 anomalous phase from a mapping torus and the fermionic stacking rule. As a simple application of this SymTFT, we also determine which subgroups $\mathbb{Z}_d\subset U(1)$ can be gauged and the symmetry of the resulting theories. This is a first step towards deriving, from the anomaly inflow of a continuous symmetry, the junction data of global anomalies beyond group cohomology.

</details>


### [117] [Finite cyclic monodromy in BMS3 gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16823)
*Evangelos Melas*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究三维渐近平坦 BMS 引力中带绕数（winding）的双曲 Virasoro 轨道上诱导数据的结构，重新导出其稳定子（stabilizer）并分析形式光滑等变截面（equivariant sections）在整体洛伦兹轨道上的限制，进而给出 Poincaré 螺旋度阶梯、选择定则及有限单值化轨道泛函的闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对带绕数、无常数超动量（supermomentum）的双曲 Virasoro 轨道，其诱导表示（inducing representation）的稳定子与等变结构尚缺乏系统刻画，且与有质量 BMS 粒子的情形存在差异，需澄清量子化标签的来源。

Method: 借助关联 Hill 算子（Hill operator）的发展映射（developing map）重导稳定子，并考察形式光滑等变截面到整体洛伦兹轨道（global Lorentz orbit）的限制；同时构造具有双曲洛伦兹和乐（Lorentz holonomy）的局部平坦绕数 BMS 几何，并分析近视界流模型。

Result: 在自旋双覆盖（spin double cover）中稳定子为一条直线与偶数阶循环群的直积，循环因子特征标构成独立于连续标签的离散诱导标签；在有限旋转对称点处，截面限制给出有限循环等变条件，并存在独立定义的酉整体分支实现相应有限差分方程；对类时投影动量导出 Poincaré 螺旋度阶梯，且投影静止能量不是轨道不变量；证明光滑实周期近视界流模型排除这些绕数轨道，并获得选择定则与有限单值化（monodromy）轨道泛函的闭式表达。

Conclusion: 研究提供了绕数双曲 BMS 轨道的稳定子、等变条件与轨道泛函的系统刻画，但未构造完整无限维轨道上的酉实现，循环扇区在量子引力中的解释仍属猜想性。

Abstract: We investigate inducing data for three dimensional asymptotically flat BMS gravity on hyperbolic Virasoro orbits with winding, which contain no constant supermomentum, and the restriction of formal smooth equivariant sections to global Lorentz orbits. Using the developing map of the associated Hill operator we rederive their stabilizer and show that, in the spin double cover, it is the direct product of a line with a cyclic group of even order. The character of the cyclic factor is therefore a discrete inducing label independent of the continuous one, unlike for the massive BMS particles, where it is fixed by the spin. At points with the corresponding finite rotational symmetry, restriction of formal smooth equivariant sections to a global Lorentz orbit yields the finite cyclic equivariance condition, while an independently defined unitary global branch realizes the associated finite difference equation. At points with timelike projected momentum we derive the resulting ladder of Poincare helicities, show that the projected rest energy is not an orbit invariant, construct locally flat winding BMS geometries with hyperbolic Lorentz holonomy, prove that the smooth real periodic near horizon current model excludes these winding orbits, and obtain selection rules and closed form expressions for finite monodromy resolved orbital functionals. A unitary realization on the full infinite dimensional orbit is not constructed, and the interpretation of the cyclic sectors in quantum gravity remains conjectural.

</details>


### [118] [Gauge Theory Bootstrap with varying $N_c$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16824)
*Rafael Cordoba,Yifei He,Martin Kruczenski,Derek Ping,Jignesh Mohanty,Emil Alegria*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文运用规范理论自举（Gauge Theory Bootstrap）方法研究随色数 N_c 变化的渐近自由规范理论，在 3≤N_c≤12 范围内提取低能观测量与共振参数，并检验其对 N_c 的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于通过调节微观参数 N_c 直接考察对应的低能强耦合动力学，并进一步检验规范理论自举方法对渐近自由规范理论的适用性。

Method: N_c 通过求和规则（sum rules）与渐近形状因子（asymptotic form factors）进入计算，从而可直接调节该微观参数；在 3≤N_c≤12 范围内提取散射长度、有效程（effective ranges）及低能常数 \bar{l}_i，并获得 σ、ρ、f_2 共振。

Result: ρ 与 f_2 的质量对 N_c 依赖很弱，其宽度随 N_c 增大而减小，符合常规 q\bar{q} 介子预期的 1/N_c 标度；相反，σ 介子的质量与宽度均随 N_c 增大，与此前的幺正化手征微扰理论（unitarized χPT）分析一致。

Conclusion: 上述结果为规范理论自举方法在渐近自由规范理论中的研究提供了进一步检验。

Abstract: We use the Gauge Theory Bootstrap to study asymptotically free gauge theories with a varying number of colors $N_c$. The parameter $N_c$ enters the computation through the sum rules and asymptotic form factors, allowing us to tune this microscopic parameter directly and examine the corresponding low-energy strongly coupled dynamics. For $3\le N_c\le 12$, we extract the scattering lengths, effective ranges, and low-energy constants $\bar{l}_i$, and study their $N_c$ dependence. We also obtain the $σ$, $ρ$, and $f_2$ resonances for the same range of $N_c$. The $ρ$ and $f_2$ masses depend only weakly on $N_c$, and their widths decrease as $N_c$ increases, consistent with the $1/N_c$ scaling expected for conventional $q\bar{q}$ mesons. In contrast, the $σ$ meson mass and width both increase with $N_c$, in agreement with a previous unitarized $χ$PT analysis. These results provide a further test of the Gauge Theory Bootstrap for studying asymptotically free gauge theories.

</details>


### [119] [Holographic viscous transport in scalar-coupled Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16936)
*Tao Ma,Song He,Li Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在五维 Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet 全息模型中，用完整边界推迟格林函数（retarded correlator）直接提取剪切与体粘滞系数，并与视界公式对比；结果显示常耦合下视界守恒流计算与 Kubo 结果一致，而标量依赖耦合下未修改的 Eling-Oz 公式仍能重现边界 ζ/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验视界公式（horizon formulas）给出的剪切粘滞 η 与体粘滞 ζ 是否与边界应力张量推迟关联函数直接提取的结果一致，并为视界方案提供直接的边界基准（boundary benchmark）。

Method: 采用标定至零重子密度格点 QCD 状态方程（lattice QCD equation of state）的五维 Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet 模型；引入所需的曲率与标量抵消项（counterterms）获得 UV 有限的 renormalized 关联函数，并在保留 Gauss-Bonnet 耦合 α 完整依赖的前提下求解涨落方程，不使用视界守恒律或熵产生公式。

Result: 常耦合情形下，视界守恒流计算在两个通道均重现边界 Kubo 结果；标量依赖耦合下，两种方法均给出 η/s 的极小值。体通道中一阶 α 截断的守恒流计算得到更低的 ζ/s 峰值高度，峰温与边界结果几乎不变；两种耦合下未修改的 Eling-Oz 公式 ζ/s=(4π)^{-1}(∂φ_h/∂ln s)^2 均重现边界 ζ/s。

Conclusion: 即使 Gauss-Bonnet 相互作用使 η/s 偏离 1/(4π)，前因子 1/(4π) 仍被保留；结果支持将未修改的 EO 公式推广至非常数耦合情形，并为视界处方提供边界基准。

Abstract: We test horizon formulas for the shear and bulk viscosities of a holographic plasma against viscosities extracted directly from retarded stress-tensor correlators. We work in a five-dimensional Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet model calibrated to the lattice QCD equation of state at zero baryon density.
