UV-finite renormalized correlators are obtained by including the required curvature and scalar counterterms, and the fluctuation equations are solved while retaining the full dependence on the Gauss-Bonnet coupling $α$, without using a horizon conservation law or an entropy-production formula. For constant coupling, the horizon conserved-current calculation reproduces the boundary Kubo results in both channels. For the scalar-dependent coupling studied here, both methods find a minimum in the shear-viscosity-to-entropy-density ratio $η/s$. In the bulk channel, the conserved-current calculation truncated at first order in $α$ yields a lower peak height in $ζ/s$, with the peak temperature nearly unchanged relative to the boundary result. For both couplings, the unmodified Eling-Oz (EO) formula, $ζ/s=(4π)^{-1}(\partialφ_h/\partial\ln s)^2$, reproduces the boundary result for $ζ/s$, where $φ_h$ is the horizon value of the scalar. The prefactor $1/(4π)$ is thus retained even when the Gauss-Bonnet interaction shifts $η/s$ from this value. These results provide a direct boundary benchmark for horizon prescriptions and support the unmodified EO formula beyond constant coupling.

</details>


### [120] [Infinite spin superparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17006)
*Eugenia Boffo,Martin Cederwall*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从超闵可夫斯基背景上的高阶导数超粒子作用量出发，在3、4、6、10维构造超庞加莱代数的无穷自旋表示，并通过超扭量变换导出一次量子化谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅暗示无穷自旋表示（infinite spin representations）的构造与超对称框架下的高阶导数超粒子（higher-derivative superparticle）描述相关。

Method: 在3、4、6、10维超闵可夫斯基（super-Minkowski）背景上引入高阶导数超粒子作用量，并执行超扭量变换（super-twistor transforms）以推导一次量子化谱（first-quantised spectra）。

Result: 获得了超庞加莱代数（super-Poincaré algebra）的无穷自旋表示，并借助超扭量变换得到相应的一次量子化谱。

Conclusion: 高阶导数超粒子作用量与超扭量变换相结合，可为3、4、6、10维超庞加莱代数提供无穷自旋表示及其一次量子化谱描述；摘要未给出进一步物理解释或推广结论。

Abstract: We obtain infinite spin representations of the super-Poincaré algebra from a higher-derivative superparticle action on a super-Minkowski background in 3, 4, 6 and 10 dimensions. Super-twistor transforms are performed, and used to deduce first-quantised spectra.

</details>


### [121] [De Sitter Gravity and Emergent Time from Fusion Matrix Product States and SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17045)
*Shinsei Ryu,Yu-ki Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文提出一个与二维德西特（dS）引力对偶的量子力学模型，以融合自旋的矩阵乘积态（MPS）定义的量子信道实现，并与无弦交叉的Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）模型极限相关，为二维dS/CFT对应提供具体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为二维德西特（de Sitter, dS）引力寻找具体的量子力学对偶描述，从而为dS/CFT对应（dS/CFT correspondence）提供可实现、可检验的模型。

Method: 构造由矩阵乘积态（matrix product state）定义的量子信道（quantum channel），每一步融合一个自旋；该构造关联于无弦交叉（without chord crossings）的SYK模型极限。其最简部分为具有涌现SL(2,R)对称性的随机游走（random walk），该对称性被等同于dS未来边界的对称性，并选取使游走服从Schwarzian作用量的时间变量。

Result: 该构造给出dS Jackiw-Teitelboim引力的圆盘振幅（disk amplitude）。在固定dS₂背景下的自由无质量标量场情形，Bunch-Davies真空中的两点函数（two-point function）与对偶描述一致，且对称性匹配。

Conclusion: 该构造为二维德西特引力的dS/CFT对应提供了一个具体模型，并暗示随机游走、Schwarzian作用量与dS边界对称性之间存在内在联系。

Abstract: We propose a quantum-mechanical model dual to two-dimensional de Sitter (dS) gravity. It is a quantum channel, defined by a matrix product state that fuses one spin at each step, and is related to a limit of the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model without chord crossings. Its simplest part is a random walk with an emergent $SL(2,\mathbb R)$ symmetry, which we identify with the symmetry of the future boundary of dS. It selects a time variable in which the walk obeys the Schwarzian action and yields the disk amplitude of dS Jackiw-Teitelboim gravity. As the simplest case, we consider free massless scalar fields in the fixed background of dS$_2$. We find the agreement of the two-point function in the Bunch-Davies vacuum and the matching of symmetry. Our construction provides a concrete model for the dS/CFT correspondence for two-dimensional de Sitter gravity.

</details>


### [122] [Covariant Wigner--Weyl calculus, non-dissipative transport and the chiral anomaly in inhomogeneous systems with nontrivial momentum-space topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17178)
*M. A. Zubkov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该综述系统化并扩展了非阿贝尔规范场中费米子的协变 Wigner–Weyl 演算（covariant Wigner–Weyl calculus），统一了坐标空间非均匀、动量空间具有非平凡拓扑的系统中非阿贝尔手征分离效应、量子霍尔效应、费米面不变量与手征反常分解等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于协变 Wigner–Weyl 演算及其拓扑应用的结果分散、约定不一、假设不清，需要统一梳理并为非阿贝尔手征分离效应、非椭圆模型等提供更一般且不依赖额外假设的表述。

Method: 以协变 Wigner–Weyl 演算为核心工具，将已有结果统一到共同约定并明确假设与定义，同时给出多项新结果，包括协变符号与规范场无关的算符刻画、积分反常的排序无关性、闭四维流形上的反常分解、Luttinger–Ward 恒等式与替代布里渊区（Brillouin zone）紧致性的连续条件、Moyal N3 与主符号纤维度的对应、非椭圆模型费米面不变量的留数求值，以及手征分离不变量的指标配对形式。

Result: 证明反常分解为瞬子数（instanton number）与 N3 在未扭曲指标（untwisted index）消失时精确成立；给出重整化速度流背后的 Luttinger–Ward 恒等式及非重整化定理所需的连续条件；将手征分离效应的 Moyal N3 等同于主符号的纤维度；在连续与格点上以留数求出非椭圆模型的费米面不变量，并指出其允许沿磁场方向的平衡手征磁效应（chiral magnetic effect），而球状费米面模型则以手征分离效应与单一 Weyl 反常替代之。

Conclusion: 协变 Wigner–Weyl 演算可为非均匀拓扑系统的各类不变量与反常提供统一的非交换几何框架，非椭圆模型的手征磁效应与手征分离效应/单一 Weyl 反常之间存在可相互转换的关系。

Abstract: We review, systematize and extend the covariant Wigner--Weyl calculus for fermions in non-Abelian gauge fields and its applications to systems inhomogeneous in coordinate space with nontrivial momentum-space topology: the non-Abelian chiral separation and quantum Hall effects, their stability under interactions, the Fermi-surface invariant of inhomogeneous systems and the non-elliptic models that carry it, with their equilibrium chiral magnetic effect, the factorization of the chiral anomaly into the instanton number and the momentum-space invariant $N_3$, and the noncommutative-geometric form of the invariants. Previous results are brought to common conventions, with hypotheses stated and notions defined. New results: the characterization of the operators whose covariant symbol is gauge-field independent and the ordering independence of the integrated anomaly; the anomaly factorization on a closed four-manifold without the homotopy hypothesis, the factorization into instanton number and $N_3$ holding exactly when the untwisted index vanishes; the Luttinger--Ward identity behind the renormalized-velocity current and the continuum conditions replacing the compactness of the Brillouin zone in the non-renormalization theorems; the identification of the Moyal $N_3$ of the chiral separation effect with the fibre degree of the principal symbol; the residue evaluation of the Fermi-surface invariant of the non-elliptic models, in the continuum and on a lattice, and the equilibrium current of the conserved charge along the magnetic field that it implies -- a chiral magnetic effect permitted by the non-Hamiltonian form of these models -- which the model with spherical Fermi surfaces trades for a chiral separation effect and a single-Weyl anomaly, and a modification of it restores at the price of the anomaly; and the index-pairing form of the chiral separation invariant.

</details>


### [123] [Love numbers for the Myers-Perry geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17261)
*Rhucha Deshpande,Oleg Lunin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在任意维度的 Myers-Perry 黑洞几何中分析标量场的 Love 数（Love numbers），认为潮汐响应由两个常数刻画，其中一个参数与视界结构无关，并给出了任意维度下的显式计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注黑洞潮汐响应（tidal response）的刻画问题，尤其是 Myers-Perry 几何中 Love 数与潜在的对数跑动（logarithmic running）参数的依赖性，旨在厘清这些常数是否依赖于视界（horizon）细节。

Method: 利用波动方程的渐近展开（asymptotic expansion）计算指定跑动的参数，对任意维度的 Myers-Perry 黑洞进行显式求解；Love 数则通过若干不同近似方法计算，因其依赖视界处边界条件（boundary conditions）的细节。

Result: 论证了刻画跑动的参数对视界结构不敏感，并在任意维度下显式给出该参数；Love 数依赖视界边界条件，通过多种近似得到计算。

Conclusion: 研究表明黑洞潮汐响应的两个常数具有不同性质：跑动参数对视界微观结构不敏感，而 Love 数依赖视界边界条件，需借助近似方法确定。

Abstract: We analyze Love numbers for scalar fields in the Myers-Perry geometry in arbitrary dimensions. The tidal responses of black holes are specified by two constants which encode a potential logarithmic running and the Love number. We argue that the parameter specifying the running is not sensitive to the structure of the horizon, and we explicitly compute this parameter for Myers-Perry black holes in arbitrary dimensions by utilizing the asymptotic expansion of the wave equation. The Love numbers rely on the details of the boundary conditions at the horizon, and we compute them using several distinct approximations.

</details>


### [124] [Twisting IKKT with branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17270)
*Fabian Hahner,Natalie M. Paquette*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文受IKKT矩阵模型与IIB型弦理论之间猜想性关系的启发，对矩阵模型的超对称扭曲（supersymmetric twists）进行系统分类，在无穷秩（infinite rank）情形下发现五个需要D-膜非交换背景的新扭曲类，并将其与拓扑弦理论（BCOV理论）及局部化计算相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于IKKT矩阵模型（IKKT matrix model）与IIB型弦理论（type IIB string theory）之间的猜想性对应关系，以及此前对有限N下仅存两种扭曲的研究局限，需在无穷秩情形下考察更丰富的扭曲结构，并与平坦空间IIB超引力的扭曲分类相对照。

Method: 在BV形式（BV formalism）下处理IKKT模型，允许动力学（dynamical）与运动学（kinematical）超荷，通过非交换背景（与非交换D-膜相关）展开，并借助经典弦场论的开闭对偶（open-closed duality）将各扭曲与BCOV理论（拓扑B-模型的闭弦场论）的形变相联系。

Result: 除任意有限N下已有的两种扭曲外，在无穷秩下找到五个额外扭曲类，共实现平坦空间IIB超引力中每个扭曲超荷等价类的代表，但轨道分层（orbit stratification）的具体形式不同，该差异与IIB超代数的更高中心扩张（higher central extension）相关。在非交换背景附近展开得到A-膜与B-膜乘积上的世界体理论（worldvolume theories），包括涌现D3-膜上的四维BF理论；并将具有Spin(7)对称性的一个扭曲经由BV形式重构Moore–Nekrasov–Shatashvili的局部化（localization）结果，其辅助场补全由二次反场项（quadratic antifield terms）编码。

Conclusion: IKKT矩阵模型的超对称扭曲在无穷秩下形成比平坦空间IIB超引力分类更丰富的结构，其差异归因于超代数的高中心扩张；BV形式为连接扭曲分类、D-膜世界体理论、BCOV理论形变及局部化计算提供了统一而自洽的框架。

Abstract: Motivated by the conjectural relationship between the IKKT matrix model and type IIB string theory, we classify supersymmetric twists of the matrix model, allowing for both dynamical and kinematical supercharges. Beyond the two twists available at any finite $N$, which we studied in our previous work, we find five additional classes at infinite rank which require non-commuting backgrounds associated with D-branes in IIB supergravity. Together, these twists realize representatives of every equivalence class of twisting supercharges in flat-space type IIB supergravity recently classified by the first author; however, the precise form of the orbit stratification differs. This difference is related to a higher central extension of the IIB superalgebra that appears in our treatment of the IKKT model in the BV formalism. Expanding around the non-commutative backgrounds gives worldvolume theories on products of A- and B-branes, including four-dimensional BF theory on an emergent D3-brane. We relate all these twists to deformations of BCOV theory (the closed string field theory of the topological B-model) using the open-closed duality of classical string field theory. For one of the twists with Spin$(7)$ symmetry, we also show the localization of the IKKT partition function by Moore, Nekrasov, and Shatashvili can be directly reconstructed with the BV formalism, with their auxiliary field completion encoded by quadratic antifield terms. Throughout, we provide self-contained treatments of the formal techniques we employ.

</details>


### [125] [A precision bound for gravitational transseries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17274)
*Ji Hoon Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出：在黑洞阈值之上，全息可观测量的 large $N$ transseries 存在一个由 D-brane instanton 数据决定的精度上限（precision bound）。作者发展了基于 Écalle 理论的大 $N$ resurgence 方法，用以刻画超越 $e^{-N}$ instanton 的 $e^{-N^2}$ 引力鞍点渐近行为，并以 $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM 的 $\frac{1}{16}$-BPS index 作为主要检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望回答：在有限 $N$ 下，large $N$ transseries 究竟能以多高精度确定一个全息可观测量。其出发点是黑洞阈值以上的全息观测量，并认为该精度限制由 D-brane instanton 扇区的数据设定。

Method: 作者基于 Écalle 的工作发展了 large $N$ resurgence 方法，捕获 $e^{-N^2}$ 量级的引力鞍点渐近行为，超出通常的 $e^{-N}$ instanton。在关于 D-brane instanton 求和在黑洞相中增长方式的假设下，作者分析了 transseries 重求和（resummation）的解析障碍。主要例子是 $\mathcal{N}=4$ 超杨-米尔斯（super Yang-Mills）的 $\frac{1}{16}$-BPS index 及其对应黑洞。

Result: 研究发现 large $N$ transseries 的重求和存在解析障碍（analytic obstruction），该障碍即设定精度上限。在 $\frac{1}{16}$-BPS index 中，作者推导出热 AdS 与黑洞上 D-brane instanton 数据之间的关系，其形式是一种交换两个背景上 D-brane instanton 数目的对偶（duality）；这些关系在 index 中重现了包裹黑洞的 D3-brane 的作用量。

Conclusion: 作者认为精度上限源于 D-brane instanton 扇区对 transseries 重求和的解析阻碍。文末给出对一般情形的预期，并讨论了其与双锥虫洞（double-cone wormhole）的关系。

Abstract: We propose that the large $N$ transseries of a holographic observable above the black hole threshold admits a precision bound, a limit on how precisely the transseries can determine the observable at finite $N$, set by the data of its D-brane instanton sectors. In doing so, we develop methods in large $N$ resurgence, based on the work of Écalle, that capture the $e^{-N^2}$ asymptotics of gravitational saddles beyond the $e^{-N}$ instantons. Under an assumption on the growth of the D-brane instanton sums that we expect in the black hole phase, we find an analytic obstruction to the resummation of the large $N$ transseries. This obstruction sets the precision bound. As our main example and consistency check, we study the $\frac{1}{16}$-BPS index of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills and its black hole. We derive relations between the D-brane instanton data on thermal AdS and on the black hole, in the form of a duality that exchanges the numbers of D-brane instantons on the two backgrounds. In the index, these relations reproduce the actions of D3-branes wrapped on the black hole. We conclude with our expectations for generic situations and their relation to the double-cone wormhole.

</details>


### [126] [The $(5,5)$ String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17275)
*Justin Kaidi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究一种新型 N=(2,1) 异弦（heterotic string），其所有态（含十六个超荷的无限塔）均无质量，并提出其经典靶空间理论为自对偶规范理论（self-dual gauge theory），其中弦的每个态都是规范场，引力被纳入规范代数，从而构成闭弦全部模式的纯三次场论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 N=(2,1) 弦需要零流（null current）且靶空间维度受限；本文旨在构造一种无需零流、靶空间仍为四维的 N=(2,1) 异弦，并寻找其经典靶理论的统一描述。

Method: 取右行子（right-movers）位于 R^{2,2}、左行子（left-movers）符号差为 (5,5)，通过附加格（lattice）II_{3,3} 构造该理论。对中性玻色子，论证 Kutasov-Martinec 行列式作用（determinant action）对偶于自对偶度规（self-dual metric）耦合自对偶 Maxwell 场的三次作用，并用单个超场（superfield）补全费米子；对带电荷态，给出其耦合为左行子物理态所成李超代数（Lie superalgebra）结构常数的证据。

Result: 该弦的全部态均为无质量态，形成含十六个超荷的无限塔；中性玻色子部分存在行列式作用与三次作用之间的对偶，费米子可由单一超场统一描述；带电态的耦合被证据支持为李超代数的结构常数。

Conclusion: 作者据此提出经典靶理论为该超代数的自对偶规范理论：弦的每一态对应一个规范场，引力是规范代数的一部分，从而得到闭弦所有模式的纯三次场论（purely cubic field theory）。

Abstract: We study an $N=(2,1)$ heterotic string with right-movers on $\mathbb R^{2,2}$ and left-movers of signature $(5,5)$, obtained by adjoining the lattice $\mathrm{II}_{3,3}$. Unlike the standard $N=(2,1)$ strings it requires no null current, and its target space remains four-dimensional. All of its states, an infinite tower with sixteen supercharges, are massless. For the neutral bosons, the Kutasov-Martinec determinant action is dual to a cubic action for a self-dual metric coupled to self-dual Maxwell fields, and we complete it with fermions using a single superfield. For the charged states we give evidence that the couplings are the structure constants of a Lie superalgebra formed by the physical states of the left-movers. We are thus led to propose that the classical target theory is a self-dual gauge theory for this superalgebra: every state of the string is a gauge field, and gravity is part of the gauge algebra. This would be a purely cubic field theory of all the modes of a closed string.

</details>


### [127] [Bound States of Two D0-Branes and the Three-Graviton Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17325)
*Sergio Sanjurjo,Themistocles Zikopoulos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对 M 理论（M-theory）的 SU(2) BFSS 矩阵模型（BFSS matrix model）基态进行数值求解，该基态对应两个 D0-膜（D0-branes）的零能束缚态，亦即沿 M 理论圆具有两单位动量的十一维引力子（graviton）。数值波函数重现了 Herderschee–Maldacena 给出的三引力子耦合（three-graviton coupling），并论证该耦合受超对称（supersymmetry）保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BFSS 猜想将矩阵模型基态与十一维引力子相联系，但该强耦合量子力学基态的具体波函数结构及其与引力相互作用的定量对应缺乏直接验证，尤其是三引力子耦合能否从矩阵模型中准确导出仍待检验。

Method: 对 SU(2) BFSS 矩阵模型的基态进行数值求解，分析波函数在强耦合核心区与 D0-膜彼此远离时的渐近幂律尾（power-law tail）；提取幂律衰减系数作为三引力子耦合，并与 Herderschee–Maldacena 的既有结果比对；同时借助超对称论证该耦合受保护，并由两个 D0-膜未重整化（non-renormalized）的单圈相互作用（one-loop interaction）导出其数值；最后给出关于近期提出的激发束缚态（excited bound states）的表示论（representation-theoretic）论证。

Result: 数值波函数呈现明显的强耦合核心及渐近幂律尾，其幂律衰减系数与 Herderschee–Maldacena 计算的三引力子耦合高度吻合；该耦合被证明在矩阵模型中受超对称保护，可由两 D0-膜的单圈相互作用非重整化性质确定其取值。

Conclusion: 数值结果在定量层面强化了 BFSS 猜想下矩阵模型基态与十一维引力子及引力耦合的对应关系，并将三引力子耦合的保护机制归结为超对称；对激发束缚态提供了表示论的简洁解释。

Abstract: We numerically solve for the ground state of the $SU(2)$ BFSS matrix model for M-theory. This state corresponds to the zero-energy bound state of two D0-branes or, by the BFSS conjecture, to the eleven-dimensional graviton with two units of momentum along the M-theory circle. The wavefunction exhibits a strongly coupled core, as well as an asymptotic power-law tail where the D0-branes are well separated. The coefficient of this power-law decay is the three-graviton coupling, previously computed by Herderschee and Maldacena, which our numerical solution reproduces with high accuracy. We also show that this coupling is protected by supersymmetry in the matrix model, and derive its value from the non-renormalized one-loop interaction of the two D0-branes. Lastly, we provide a simple representation-theoretic argument regarding the recently proposed excited bound states of the model.

</details>


### [128] [Decoupling Dynamics: A Tale of Two Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17327)
*Andrea Antinucci,Oscar Lewis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出，连续阿贝尔对称性（continuous Abelian symmetry）在动力学规范化（dynamical gauging）前后的不同 SymTFT，可视为同一 (d+1) 维动力学全息规范理论（holographic gauge theory）的不同拓扑极限，并给出基于希尔伯特空间与算符的完全量子定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SymTFT 在有限对称性规范化下不变，但在连续对称性动力学规范化下会改变，这一差异此前缺乏统一解释；本文旨在说明变化来源并给出量子层面的严格刻画。

Method: 将 (d+1) 维动力学全息规范理论与光子截断（photon truncation）结合，取合适的体（bulk）极限，构造 SymTFT 在希尔伯特空间与算符层面的量子定义；规范化通过改变边界条件（boundary condition）选取不同拓扑极限。

Result: 该构造阐明拓扑对称性缺陷（defects）、电荷与反常（anomalies）如何在电流动力学信息被丢弃后保留；给出 (2+1) 维体的非反常与反常完整量子分析，并在四维边界含混合轴矢反常（mixed axial-vector anomaly）的情形下复原规范化矢量对称性后残余的非可逆手征对称性（non-invertible chiral symmetry）的 SymTFT。

Conclusion: 不同 SymTFT 的差异被统一解释为同一动力学理论的不同拓扑极限，由此为规范化后对称性结构的存续提供了清晰的量子图像与示例验证。

Abstract: The symmetries of a $d$-dimensional QFT are known to be captured by a topological theory in one dimension higher, the Symmetry Topological Field Theory (SymTFT), which is invariant under gauging finite symmetries of the QFT, but not under dynamical gauging of continuous symmetries. We show that the distinct SymTFTs of a $d$-dimensional QFT before and after dynamical gauging of a continuous Abelian symmetry arise as different topological limits of the same dynamical holographic gauge theory in $(d+1)$-dimensions. Gauging changes the boundary condition of this dynamical theory and thereby selects a different topological limit, explaining why the associated SymTFT changes. We give a fully quantum definition of these SymTFTs at the level of Hilbert spaces and operators by combining suitable limits of the bulk holographic theory with photon truncation. This construction clarifies how topological symmetry defects, charges, and anomalies survive while the dynamical information carried by currents is discarded. We study non-anomalous and anomalous examples, providing a complete quantum analysis for $(2+1)$-dimensional bulks. For a four-dimensional boundary theory with a mixed axial-vector anomaly, we recover the SymTFT of the non-invertible chiral symmetry that remains after gauging the vector symmetry.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Quantitative Gaussian limits of periodic O(N) models at and below the critical temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14570)
*Weiqi Jiang,Pengcheng Hou,Zhiyi Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文在周期盒上为零场最近邻 $O(N)$ 模型（$O(N)$ model）的局部自旋坐标建立了体积一致的高斯近似（Gaussian approximation），误差以未归一化的 $1$-Wasserstein 距离度量，并给出误差趋于零的充要条件与若干情形下的尖锐阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 $d\ge3$、极限球模型（spherical model）临界温度及以下，需要刻画有限体积 $O(N)$ 模型局部自旋涨落的高斯性，且要求误差界对体积一致（volume-uniform），从而为有限尺寸效应与临界涨落提供定量控制。

Method: 对前 $p\le N$ 个坐标，以未归一化 $1$-Wasserstein 误差（unnormalized $1$-Wasserstein error）度量其与目标高斯律的偏差；目标为各坐标独立的场，每个场为标度化的各向异性高斯自由场（anisotropic Gaussian free field），临界点以下另含独立的空间常数高斯项。误差界由 $C\sqrt p\,(N^{-1/2}+s_M)$ 控制，其中 $s_M\to0$ 为有限体积平均球模型的谱移（spectral shift），并在正定向耦合与逆温度的紧集上一致。

Result: 在含最近邻星（nearest-neighbour star）的窗口上，误差当且仅当 $p=o(N)$ 且 $\sqrt p\,s_M\to0$ 时随 $M,N\to\infty$ 趋于零；对任意周期无穷体积累积态（accumulation states），任意非空固定窗口上误差当且仅当 $p=o(N)$ 时趋于零。$d\ge5$ 且逆温度超出临界值至少固定正量时，界改进为 $C(p/N+\sqrt p M^{-d})$；固定 $p$ 时，尖锐阶在含星窗口为 $N^{-1}+M^{-d}$，在周期累积态的任意非空固定窗口为 $N^{-1}$。

Conclusion: 结果给出体积一致的高斯近似及其在 $p$、$M$、$N$ 相对尺度上的阈值刻画，并以固定 $p$ 的尖锐阶表明所获误差界的阶数最优；深层（$d\ge5$、远离临界）情形下误差界进一步收紧。

Abstract: We prove a volume-uniform Gaussian approximation for local spin coordinates in the zero-field nearest-neighbour $O(N)$ model on periodic boxes of side $M$ in $d\ge3$, at and below the critical temperature of the limiting spherical model. On a fixed spatial window, for sufficiently large $M,N$, the unnormalized $1$-Wasserstein error for the first $p\le N$ coordinates is at most $C\sqrt p (N^{-1/2}+s_M)$, where $s_M\to0$ is the spectral shift of the finite-volume mean spherical model. The estimate is uniform on compact ranges of positive directional couplings and inverse temperatures. The target has independent coordinate fields, each a scaled anisotropic Gaussian free field with an independent spatially constant Gaussian term below criticality.
  On a window containing a nearest-neighbour star (a site and its $d$ positive-direction neighbours), the error tends to zero as $M,N\to\infty$ exactly when $p=o(N)$ and $\sqrt p s_M\to0$. For any choice of periodic infinite-volume accumulation states, the error on any nonempty fixed window tends to zero as $N\to\infty$ exactly when $p=o(N)$. In $d\ge5$, when the inverse temperature exceeds its critical value by at least a fixed positive amount, the bound improves to $C(p/N+\sqrt p M^{-d})$. For fixed $p$, the sharp order is $N^{-1}+M^{-d}$ on star-containing windows and $N^{-1}$ on every nonempty fixed window in periodic accumulation states.

</details>


### [130] [Rational layers in the low temperature expansion of the three-dimensional Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14577)
*Ran Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文将零场简单立方 Ising 模型按"超出最小壁面面积的多余量（excess）"分层，证明每一固定多余层均具有分母受限的有理双变量生成函数，并显式给出多余量 0 至 3 的完整层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低温展开（low-temperature expansion）按壁面面积（wall area）对 Ising 液滴排序，但横向跨度（transverse span）不同的液滴会落在同一阶，导致分层不唯一，需要一种更精细的组织方式。

Method: 以与两个给定跨度相容的最小壁面面积为基准，按超出该基准的多余量分层；证明中将重复的坐标切割压缩为有界装饰（bounded decorations），把每个无界区间归于一条单调链（monotone chain），再用有限控制器（finite controller）施加诱导接触与任意覆盖颜色复用。

Result: 证明每一固定多余层（含所有对数覆盖修正与 Euler 反演）都有有理双变量生成函数（rational bivariate generating function），分母因子限于 1−x、1−y 与 1−x−y；显式组装多余量 0 至 3 的全部宽度层，并将已知几何成分归因于既有 polyomino 计数；精确格点检验与公式一致。

Conclusion: 结果建立了精确的层结构；经认证的低温比较显示在某一采样点有适度混合精度增益但评估成本更高；对无界多余量求和仍是独立问题。

Abstract: A low-temperature expansion orders Ising droplets by wall area, but droplets with different transverse spans can occur at the same order. We instead organize the zero-field simple-cubic model by the excess above the minimum wall area compatible with two prescribed spans. We prove that every fixed excess layer, including all logarithmic cover corrections and Euler inversion, has a rational bivariate generating function with denominator factors restricted to 1 - x, 1 - y and 1 - x - y. The proof compresses repeated coordinate cuts into bounded decorations and assigns each unbounded interval to one monotone chain. A finite controller then enforces induced contacts and arbitrary cover-color reuse. We explicitly assemble the complete layers of excess zero through three over all widths, attributing their known geometric constituents to earlier polyomino enumeration. Exact lattice checks corroborate the formulas. A certified low-temperature comparison shows a modest hybrid accuracy gain at one sampling point and higher evaluation cost. The result establishes an exact layer structure; summation over unbounded excess remains a separate problem.

</details>


### [131] [Hidden universality of rare fluctuations and a central-limit structure at criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14621)
*Nikolaos G. Fytas,Víctor Martín-Mayor,Attilio L. Stella,Gianluca Teza,Alexandros Vasilopoulos,David Yllanes*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文在二维伊辛普适类（Ising universality class）中首次给出直接数值证据，证明临界涨落的极端尾部经归一化后坍缩至单一渐近规律，且由普适振幅控制，其数值提取与共形场论（conformal field theory）导出的猜想精确结果相容，指向临界涨落的广义中心极限定理（generalised central limit theorem）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心极限定理（central limit theorem）依赖弱关联随机变量假设，而临界点处存在长程关联（long-range correlations），使其失效；标度不变性（scale invariance）刻画典型临界涨落，但稀有涨落（rare fluctuations）概率极低、超出常规数值方法能力，长期未被探索，近期理论虽预言普适渐近律，却缺乏直接数值证据。

Method: 采用大规模系绳蒙特卡洛模拟（Tethered Monte Carlo simulations），在二维伊辛普适类中探测序参量涨落，覆盖低至 10^{-200} 的极小概率区间，并对不同微观模型的尾部按特征宽度归一化后进行比较。

Result: 归一化后，同一普适类中不同微观模型的远尾（far tails）坍缩至单一渐近规律；该坍缩由一普适振幅（universal amplitude）控制，其数值提取结果与作者从共形场论导出的猜想精确值相容。

Conclusion: 结果为临界处大偏差（large deviations）的普适渐近律提供了首个直接数值证据，并指向适用于临界涨落的广义中心极限定理。

Abstract: The central limit theorem is among the most fundamental principles in the natural sciences, governing the fluctuations of sums of weakly correlated random variables through Gaussian statistics. At critical points, however, long-range correlations invalidate this paradigm, raising a fundamental question: does any general limit theorem survive at criticality? Although scale invariance has long been recognised as the defining feature of typical critical fluctuations, the behaviour of rare fluctuations has remained largely unexplored, as their extraordinarily small probabilities place them beyond the reach of conventional numerical methods. Recent theoretical advances suggest that universal asymptotic laws {may govern large deviations in systems at criticality}, yet direct numerical evidence has been lacking. Here we provide the first such evidence in the two-dimensional Ising universality class. Using large-scale Tethered Monte Carlo simulations, we probe order-parameter fluctuations with probabilities as small as $10^{-200}$ and show that, once normalised by their characteristic width, the far tails collapse onto a single asymptotic law shared by distinct microscopic models in the same universality class. This collapse is governed by a universal amplitude, whose numerical extraction is compatible with the conjectured exact result we derive from conformal field theory, and points towards a generalised central limit theorem for critical fluctuations.

</details>


### [132] [Force-Dependent Change of Structural Behavior Upon Pulling a Grafted Flexible Polymer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14720)
*J. Matthew Smith,Michael Bachmann*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 采用广义系综蒙特卡罗模拟结合微正则拐点分析法，研究拉伸力对粗粒化柔性聚合物结构相变的影响，发现拉力增强会使螺旋-球状转变由二级变为一级并与液-固转变合并，且存在中间相与Gough-Joule效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借助光镊等实验手段操控聚合物链有助于理解大分子结构中分子力的效应，本文由此出发，用计算模拟系统考察拉伸力对柔性聚合物结构相的影响。

Method: 对柔性聚合物的粗粒化模型（coarse-grained model）施加拉伸力，采用广义系综蒙特卡罗方法（generalized-ensemble Monte Carlo）进行模拟，并运用新近提出的微正则拐点分析法（microcanonical inflection-point analysis）识别与分类结构相，配合特定序参量（order parameter）刻画转变行为。

Result: 随拉力增大，螺旋-球状转变（coil-globule transition）由二级转为一级，随后与液-固转变（liquid-solid transition）合并；在特定力值下识别出分隔螺旋相与球状相的中间相（intermediate phase）；并找到Gough-Joule效应的明确证据；当拉力超过某一阈值时转变完全不再发生。

Conclusion: 拉伸力可显著改变柔性聚合物的结构相图与相变性质，微正则拐点分析能有效识别和分类这些相变，且存在使相变消失的临界力阈值。

Abstract: Manipulating polymer chains by means of experimental instruments such as optical tweezers has helped understand better the effects of molecular forces in macromolecular structures. In this study, we simulate the influence of a pulling force on structural phases of a coarse-grained model for flexible polymers using generalized-ensemble Monte Carlo methodologies. The recently introduced microcanonical inflection-point analysis method is employed to identify and classify structural phases of the system. Specific order parameters help characterize the transition behavior in response to the present pulling force. We show that, upon increasing the pulling strength, the coil-globule transition turns from second to first order, then merges with the liquid-solid transition. An intermediate phase, separating the coil and globule phases, is identified for certain force strengths. Clear evidence of the Gough-Joule effect is found as well. Transitions do not occur at all if the pulling force exceeds a certain threshold value.

</details>


### [133] [Optimal Driving between Nonequilibrium Steady States under a Dissipation Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14891)
*Keisuke Sugie,Dimitri Loutchko,Tetsuya J. Kobayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究在固定“维持性熵产（housekeeping entropy production）”预算下，同时优化驱动协议与其时长，以最小化总熵产，并在慢驱动区间将其映射为加权测地线问题，给出近平衡下的几何下界与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平衡稳态（nonequilibrium steady state）之间驱动会产生两类热力学代价：控制参数变化导致的过剩熵产（excess entropy production）与维持稳态流所需的维持性熵产（housekeeping entropy production）；传统固定时长优化只最小化前者而忽略后者，故需在预算约束下统筹两者。

Method: 在固定的一阶维持性熵产预算约束下，对过剩熵产同时优化协议与持续时间；在慢驱动（slow-driving）区间，将累积维持性熵产视为“熵时间（entropic time）”，把问题转化为以稳态熵产率加权的摩擦度规（friction metric）下的测地线（geodesic）优化。

Result: 该基于资源的几何（resource-based geometry）确定了稳态下仍持续耗散系统的最优路径与调度，揭示维持性熵产与过剩熵产之间的内在权衡；近平衡情形下导出总熵产的几何下界，由修正的热力学长度（thermodynamic length）在最优预算处以均分（equipartition）达到。

Conclusion: 对ATP驱动钙泵三态模型的数值模拟显示，其最优协议与固定时长驱动明显不同，并验证了总熵产下界；表明将维持性熵产纳入预算约束会实质改变最优驱动策略。

Abstract: Driving between nonequilibrium steady states incurs two thermodynamic costs: excess entropy production (EP) resulting from changing control parameters, and housekeeping EP required to sustain steady currents. Conventional fixed-duration optimization minimizes the former while neglecting the latter. Here, we instead optimize excess EP over both the protocol and its duration subject to a fixed leading order of housekeeping-EP budget, thereby minimizing total EP for that budget constraint. In the slow-driving regime, accumulated housekeeping EP acts as an entropic time, mapping the problem to a geodesic optimization under a friction metric weighted by the steady-state EP rate. This resource-based geometry determines optimal paths and schedules for systems that continue to dissipate even in steady states, establishing an intrinsic trade-off between housekeeping and excess EPs. Near equilibrium, this framework further yields geometric lower bounds on total EP, attained with equipartition at the optimal budget given by a modified thermodynamic length. Numerical simulations of a three-state model for an ATP-driven calcium pump reveal distinct optimal protocols compared to fixed-duration driving, confirming the total-EP bounds.

</details>


### [134] [Eigenvalue Crossings and Discontinuous BBP Transitions in Spiked Euclidean Random Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15927)
*Pasquale Casaburi,Pierpaolo Vivo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究嵌入对称欧氏随机矩阵（Euclidean random matrix）中的秩一信号（rank-one signal）恢复问题；在缺乏旋转不变性（rotational invariance）的情形下，主特征向量恢复由噪声与信号主方向之间的竞争支配，并可出现不连续的BBP型相变（BBP-type transition）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有信号恢复理论多依赖矩阵条目的旋转不变性，而稠密且具有几何相关性的随机矩阵通常不具备该性质，因此需要刻画此类非旋转不变矩阵中主特征向量能否、以及何时检测到信号。

Method: 采用复制方法（replica method），将问题约化为Fredholm特征值方程（Fredholm eigenvalue equation），并以未扰动谱边缘（unperturbed spectral edge）、尖峰均值（spike mean）及主特征函数的空间平均表述恢复阈值判据。

Result: 给出最大特征值、特征向量分量分布与信号重叠（overlap）的解析极限；对二次距离核（quadratic distance kernel）与秩二核（rank-two kernel）求得显式解，并与数值对角化（numerical diagonalisation）高度吻合。

Conclusion: 信号可能先出现在次主特征方向（subleading eigendirection）中而不被主成分检测；随信号增强，竞争特征值交叉改变排序，使主特征向量由噪声主导突变为携带信号，导致重叠从零跳变至有限值。

Abstract: We study the recovery of a rank-one signal embedded in a symmetric Euclidean random matrix, whose dense and geometrically correlated entries generally lack rotational invariance. We show that signal recovery through the leading eigenvector is governed by a competition between the principal directions of the noise and the signal. In particular, the signal may already be encoded in a subleading eigendirection, while remaining undetected by the leading principal component. As the signal strength increases, a crossing between competing eigenvalues can change their ordering, causing the leading eigenvector to switch abruptly from a noise-dominated to a signal-bearing direction. This mechanism gives rise to a discontinuous BBP-type transition, in which the overlap with the signal jumps from zero to a finite value. Using the replica method, we reduce the problem to a Fredholm eigenvalue equation and derive general criteria for the existence of a positive recovery threshold, expressed in terms of the unperturbed spectral edge, the spike mean and the spatial averages of the principal eigenfunctions. We obtain analytically the limiting largest eigenvalue, eigenvector-component distribution and signal overlap, and illustrate the results with explicit solutions for quadratic distance and rank-two kernels, finding excellent agreement with numerical diagonalisation.

</details>


### [135] [Going beyond the standard quasiparticle picture: Entanglement spreading after quenching from highly entangled states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15409)
*Elisa Sabadini,Vincenzo Alba*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作研究XXZ自旋链从$n$-rainbow态（由越来越长程的Bell对构成的宏观均匀态）出发的量子淬火（quantum quench），超越传统"可积初态（integrable initial states）"假设；在非相互作用XX链中淬火产生$2n$类准粒子的纠缠多重态（entangled multiplets），种间关联虽随$1/n$衰减却强烈抑制纠缠熵增长，该行为由广义准粒子图像（generalized quasiparticle picture）刻画；而在相互作用XXZ链中tDMRG模拟显示纠缠熵按切割处热力学熵（thermodynamic entropy）交换速率增长，在所可达时间尺度内看不到多重态抑制效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准粒子图像（quasiparticle picture）虽可定量描述可积系统淬火后的纠缠扩散，但在相互作用模型中其成立依赖"淬火产生成对纠缠准粒子"的假设，而该假设仅对所谓可积初态成立。本文旨在突破这一限制，考察非可积初态（$n>1$的$n$-rainbow态）下的淬火行为。

Method: 构造由递增长程Bell对组成的宏观均匀$n$-rainbow态（$n>1$时非可积），解析推导淬火后局域稳态及其$1/n$阶修正；在非相互作用XX链中分析$2n$类准粒子多重态及种间关联，并用广义准粒子图像描述纠缠熵增长；在相互作用XXZ链中采用含时密度矩阵重整化群（time-dependent density matrix renormalization group, tDMRG）进行数值模拟，涵盖均匀淬火与$n$-rainbow态与无限温度态接合两类情形。

Result: 解析结果表明，淬火后局域稳态趋于无限温度态（infinite-temperature state），修正为$O(1/n)$量级。XX链中淬火产生$2n$类准粒子的纠缠多重态，种间关联以$1/n$衰减但显著抑制纠缠熵增长，使其增长率在$n\to\infty$时趋于零。XXZ链的tDMRG数据显示纠缠熵按切割处热力学熵交换速率增长，在可达时间尺度内未出现多重态抑制迹象；对$n$-rainbow态与无限温度态接合情形，作者提出von Neumann熵的猜想公式。

Conclusion: 研究表明准粒子图像的适用范围可推广至非可积初态：非相互作用情形需以广义准粒子图像纳入多重态关联；而相互作用情形下多重态抑制效应在tDMRG可达时间内不可见，纠缠增长改由热力学熵交换速率决定。

Abstract: The quasiparticle picture provides a quantitative description of entanglement spreading after quantum quenches in integrable systems. In interacting models, however, it relies on the assumption that the quench creates entangled pairs of quasiparticles, which is justified only for the so-called integrable initial states. Here we go beyond this setting by studying quenches in the XXZ spin chain from the $n$-rainbow states, a family of macroscopically homogeneous states formed by increasingly long-range Bell pairs, which are not integrable for $n>1$. The local stationary state after the quench approaches the infinite-temperature state as $n\to\infty$, with corrections of order $1/n$, as we show analytically. Nevertheless, in the noninteracting XX chain the quench produces entangled multiplets of $2n$ species of quasiparticles. Although interspecies correlations vanish as $1/n$, they strongly suppress the growth of the entanglement entropy, whose rate vanishes as $n\to\infty$. We capture this behaviour within a generalized quasiparticle picture. In the interacting XXZ chain, instead, time-dependent density matrix renormalization group (tDMRG) simulations show that the entanglement entropy grows at the rate at which thermodynamic entropy is exchanged across the cut. Within the time scales accessible to tDMRG, the data show no trace of the multiplet suppression. This holds both after homogeneous quenches and after joining the $n$-rainbow state with an infinite-temperature state, for which we conjecture a formula for the von Neumann entropy.

</details>


### [136] [Cumulant dynamics of the ferromagnetic dipolar XY model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15485)
*Jinxin Yang,Alberto R. Cavallar,Lode Pollet*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作以二阶截断累积量动力学（second-order truncated cumulant dynamics）研究方格子上偶极铁磁XY模型（dipolar ferromagnetic XY model）的动力学态制备，系统尺寸达L=60，揭示出有限尺度相干区与有限磁化在非绝热但足够缓慢的扫描下亦可形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解偶极XY模型中平衡物理相关的动力学态如何被制备，并为未来更大系统尺寸下探测偶极XY平衡物理的实验提出约束条件。

Method: 采用二阶截断累积量动力学方法，在方格子上对偶极铁磁XY模型进行数值模拟，线性尺寸至L=60，并考察不同扫描常数（ramp constant）、初始熵与时间演化。

Result: 结果显示形成一个具有有限空间范围和有限磁化的相干区（coherent region），其两个特征尺度均依赖于扫描常数、系统初始熵与时间；该相干区即使对非绝热（diabatic）但足够缓慢的扫描也能出现。

Conclusion: 研究给出清晰的物理图像，并对未来在大系统尺寸中研究偶极XY平衡物理的实验提出严格要求。

Abstract: We study the second-order truncated cumulant dynamics for the preparation of dynamical states in the dipolar ferromagnetic XY model on the square lattice for system sizes up to linear size L = 60. The observations lead to a transparent picture in which a coherent region of finite extent and finite magnetization forms, which is possible even for diabatic but sufficiently slow ramps. These two scales are functions of the ramp constant, the initial entropy of the system, and time. Our findings put strong requirements on future experiments addressing dipolar XY equilibrium physics in bigger system sizes.

</details>


### [137] [Probe Dynamics in Active Baths via Trajectory-Response Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15518)
*Chul-Ung Woo,Jiwon Choi,Heiko Rieger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文利用轨迹-响应关系（trajectory-response relations），研究弱耦合于理想活性浴（ideal active bath）的自由探针动力学，以速度空间势（velocity-space potential）刻画稳定静止态与运动态及其共存区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动探针在活性浴中会因依赖探针速度与相互作用几何的非平衡力（nonequilibrium forces）产生自发运动，需要建立无需直接测量浴响应的框架来预测其稳定状态与方向选择。

Method: 采用将浴对探针响应表达为自由粒子位移统计的轨迹-响应关系，分析平均力对探针速度的依赖，并讨论椭圆与四重（fourfold）相互作用几何；进一步将该关系推广到探针强度的高阶。

Result: 平均力定义的势的极小值给出稳定态；椭圆相互作用下静止态先沿窄轴失稳，在线性响应层面即出现方向选择；四重相互作用下线性摩擦各向同性，非线性平均力选择轴向或对角运动并产生方向多重稳定性（multistability）；高阶项（涉及多区间自由粒子轨迹统计）会移动高斯探针稳定运动态的速度。

Conclusion: 轨迹-响应关系可有效刻画活性浴中探针的静止/运动态与方向选择，并可系统推广至探针强度高阶，为预测活性浴中被动探针的定向运动提供理论工具。

Abstract: Passive probes in active baths can undergo spontaneous motion driven by nonequilibrium forces that depend on probe velocity and interaction geometry. Using trajectory-response relations that express the bath response to a probe in terms of free-particle displacement statistics, we investigate the dynamics of freely moving probes weakly coupled to ideal active baths. The mean force exerted by the bath defines a velocity-space potential whose minima describe the stable states. By analyzing how the mean force depends on the probe velocity, we identify stable resting and moving states and determine the parameter regime in which they coexist. For elliptical interactions, the resting state becomes unstable first along the narrow axis, resulting in direction selection already at the linear-response level. For fourfold interactions, linear friction remains isotropic, while the nonlinear dependence of the mean force on probe velocity selects axial or diagonal motion and produces directional multistability. We further show that the trajectory-response relations extend to higher orders in the probe strength, where multi-interval free-particle trajectory statistics become relevant. For a Gaussian probe, these higher-order terms shift the speed of the stable moving state.

</details>


### [138] [Spin Structure Factor of the classical Ising Model on the Centered Pyrochlore Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15677)
*L. Xu,T. Kreißel,N. Davier,L. D. C. Jaubert,L. Pollet*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作给出中心烧绿石晶格（centered pyrochlore lattice）上伊辛模型（Ising model）的自旋结构因子（spin structure factor），将 Henley 方法推广至中心自旋与顶点自旋不等价的情形，所得关联函数与蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）结果高度一致，并能描述有限温度下尖锐与展宽的收缩点（pinch points）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心烧绿石晶格中中心自旋与顶点自旋不等价，原有适用于等价自旋的 Henley 解析方法难以直接适用，需发展可处理非等价自旋并覆盖有限温度的描述框架。

Method: 将 Henley 的方法（Phys. Rev. B 71, 014424）推广到中心与顶点自旋不等价的情形，解析推导伊辛模型的自旋关联函数，并与蒙特卡罗模拟结果进行对照检验。

Result: 得到的自旋关联函数与蒙特卡罗结果符合得极好，且能够在有限温度下刻画尖锐的与展宽的收缩点特征。

Conclusion: 该推广方法对非等价中心—顶点自旋的烧绿石结构有效，并可进一步扩展至经典阻挫磁性（classical frustrated magnetism）中的其他中心型晶格结构。

Abstract: We present the spin structure factor of the Ising model on the centered pyrochlore lattice. Generalizing Henley's method [Phys. Rev. B 71, 014424] to the case of inequivalent center and vertex spins, we obtain spin correlation functions that are in excellent agreement with Monte Carlo results and can capture sharp and broadened pinch points, also at finite temperature. Our approach can be extended to other centered lattice structures in classical frustrated magnetism.

</details>


### [139] [Large deviations of spatial moments of the Kipnis-Marchioro-Presutti lattice gas following a localized energy release](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15739)
*Timo Schorlepp,Baruch Meerson,Pavel Sasorov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用宏观涨落理论（MFT）研究一维 Kipnis-Marchioro-Presutti（KMP）模型在单点瞬时释放能量后能量分布空间矩的长时统计，计算各阶矩的方差并解析刻画非高斯尾部分布，发现大矩尾部由单个绝热演化的"双峰子"主导，并预言在临界 M_2 处发生破坏反射对称性的动力学相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注一维 KMP 模型中能量空间矩 M_n(T) 的长时涨落统计，特别是其非高斯分布尾部与最优涨落路径的对称性结构，这一问题此前缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 采用宏观涨落理论（macroscopic fluctuation theory, MFT），对任意正整数 n 计算 M_n 的方差，并对 n=1、2 求非高斯尾部；建立 n=1 情形与确定性平坦初始密度下积分电流统计的精确对偶（exact duality），并通过直接最小化 MFT 作用量（action）的数值解检验解析预测。

Result: n=1 与 n=2 的大矩尾部由单一绝热演化的"双峰子"（doublon，即能量密度与共轭场梯度的一对快速传播窄孤脉冲）主导；小二阶矩尾部对应能量在 T 时刻于原点附近的聚集。比较单/双 doublon 历史，预言在临界 M_2 处最优路径破坏反射对称性 x→-x 的动力学相变（dynamical phase transition），数值解确认其为二阶相变；对任意 n 的尾部推广计算并在 n=4 时数值发现同类相变。

Conclusion: KMP 模型中能量空间矩的长时涨落由 doublon 型最优路径支配，并可出现由对称性破缺驱动的二阶动力学相变，该结论在 n=1、2 解析与数值上成立，并在 n=4 数值上得到推广验证。

Abstract: We study the long-time statistics of spatial moments of the time-dependent energy distribution in a one-dimensional Kipnis-Marchioro-Presutti model following an instantaneous release of energy on a single lattice site. The moments are $M_n(T)=\sum_i x_i^n u_i(T)$, where $u_i(T)$ is the energy at site $i$ at the observation time $T$. Employing macroscopic fluctuation theory (MFT), we calculate the variance of $M_n$ for every positive integer $n$ and determine the non-Gaussian distribution tails for the representative cases $n=1$ and $2$. For the first moment, we establish an exact duality, within MFT, to integrated-current statistics for a deterministic flat initial density. For both $n=1$ and $n=2$, the large-moment tails are dominated by a single adiabatically evolving doublon: a rapidly propagating pair of narrow solitary pulses of the energy density and of the gradient of the conjugate field. The small-second-moment tail is associated with concentration of the energy near the origin at time $T$. Comparing single- and two-doublon histories, we predict a dynamical phase transition at a critical value of $M_2$ at which the optimal path breaks reflection symmetry $x\to -x$. Numerical solutions obtained by direct minimization of the MFT action establish the second-order character of this transition and support our analytical predictions. Finally, we extend calculations of the large- and small-moment tails to arbitrary $n$ and numerically find a transition of the same type for $n=4$.

</details>


### [140] [Heterogeneous ice nucleation with TIP4P/Ice: the role of lattice mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15806)
*M. Camarillo,J. Oller-Iscar,M. M. Conde,J. Ramírez,S. Blazquez,E. Sanz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作通过全原子水分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）发现，基底晶格与冰晶格的失配（lattice mismatch）每拉伸1%，冰的成核温度约下降5–6 K；结合经典成核理论（Classical Nucleation Theory, CNT）可从非均相成核速率反推成核势垒、动力学前因子与接触角，且接触角与轨迹可视化结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰的形成主要通过固体基底上的非均相成核（heterogeneous nucleation）发生，对地球生命至关重要，但基底结构与诱导冰成核能力之间的关联仍认识不足，尤其缺乏对晶格失配（lattice mismatch）影响的定量刻画。

Method: 采用原子级水模型（atomistic model of water）进行分子动力学模拟，使用被拉伸的冰结构基底以明确量化失配度；将基底与过冷水（supercooled water）接触，测定模拟中冰自发成核的温度，并结合先前报道的均相成核速率估算非均相成核参数。

Result: 基底晶格相对冰每拉伸1%，成核温度下降约5–6 K，趋势与此前粗粒化水模型（coarse-grained water model）结果相似；基底诱导出波动的亚临界类冰团簇（subcritical ice-like clusters）网络，并最终随机涌现出球冠状超临界晶核（spherical-cap-shaped supercritical nucleus）；由此估算的成核势垒、动力学前因子与接触角（contact angle）与微观轨迹可视化一致。

Conclusion: 晶格失配显著削弱基底的成核能力，且经典成核理论可适用于描述非均相冰成核这一微观现象，其导出的接触角与轨迹观测相符。

Abstract: Ice formation, a crucial phase transition for life on Earth, mainly occurs through heterogeneous nucleation on a solid substrate. Despite the importance of this phenomenon, the correlation between the structure of the substrate and its ability to induce ice nucleation remains poorly understood. In this work, we use molecular dynamics simulations with an atomistic model of water to investigate how the lattice mismatch affects the ability of the substrate to nucleate ice. To unambiguously quantify the mismatch, we use substrates with a stretched ice structure. We put the substrate in contact with supercooled water and determine the temperature at which ice spontaneously nucleates during the course of the simulations. We find that the nucleation temperature decreases by $\sim$ 5-6 K for each percent by which the substrate lattice is stretched relative to that of ice. This trend is similar to that previously obtained with a coarse-grained water model [Camarillo et al., J. Chem. Phys. 160, 134505 (2024)]. \textcolor{black}{The substrate induces a fluctuating network of subcritical ice-like clusters, from which a spherical-cap-shaped supercritical nucleus eventually emerges stochastically.} Using Classical Nucleation Theory, combine previously reported homogeneous nucleation rates with heterogeneous nucleation rates computed in this work to estimate key heterogeneous nucleation parameters, including the nucleation barrier, the kinetic pre-factor, and the contact angle. The latter is consistent with a microscopic visualization of nucleation trajectories, supporting the applicability of Classical Nucleation Theory to describe a microscopic phenomenon such as heterogeneous ice nucleation.

</details>


### [141] [U(1)-Symmetric Spin SYK Models: Ordering, Chaos, and Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15896)
*Shiyong Guo,Enrico Rinaldi,Jad C. Halimeh,Brian Swingle*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文构建 U(1) 对称的 q-自旋随机相互作用模型作为复 SYK 模型的量子比特原生类比，验证其在共形与大 N、大 q 极限下复现鞍点结构，并用精确对角化与耦合簇链方法给出有限尺寸下的有序、量子混沌与磁化输运特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK 模型是强关联量子动力学与全息对偶（holography）的可解析对照平台，但其费米子表述在量子比特处理器上需要非局域编码，代价高昂；因此需要一种直接作用于量子比特希尔伯特空间、保持对称性的自旋类比模型。

Method: 采用随机且保持电荷的 q-自旋相互作用构造 U(1) 对称的自旋-SYK 模型；在共形与大 N、大 q 极限下解析求解鞍点结构（化学势对应磁场、电荷密度对应磁化强度）；并用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）研究有限 N、有限 q 区域，进而构建耦合簇链（coupled-cluster chains）模拟畴壁动力学。

Result: 大 N、大 q 极限下复现复费米子 SYK 的主导鞍点结构；有限尺寸下 U(1) 不变的有序化磁化率（ordering susceptibility）在 q=2、4 时随系统尺寸增大，在 q=6 时减小；体能谱统计在半填充处符合高斯正交系综（Gaussian orthogonal ensemble），在相邻磁化扇区符合高斯酉系综（Gaussian unitary ensemble）；小簇耦合簇链的畴壁动力学与扩散型磁化输运一致。

Conclusion: 研究为 U(1) 对称自旋-SYK 模型中的有序化、量子混沌（quantum chaos）与磁化输运提供了可解析的基准与有限尺寸判据，并指出低温、大系统区域难以由经典方法处理，从而为量子模拟提供了动机。

Abstract: The Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) model provides an analytically controlled setting for strongly interacting quantum dynamics and holography, but its fermionic formulation leads to nonlocal encoding overheads on qubit-based quantum processors. We study a U(1)-symmetric spin analogue of the complex SYK model, constructed from random charge-conserving $q$-spin interactions that are native to a qubit Hilbert space. In the large-$N$, large-$q$ limit, the model reproduces the leading saddle-point structure of complex fermionic SYK under the identification of chemical potential with magnetic field and charge density with magnetization. We investigate the finite-$N$, finite-$q$ regime using exact diagonalization. A U(1)-invariant ordering susceptibility increases with system size for $q = 2, 4$ but decreases for $q = 6$, while bulk-spectrum level statistics are consistent with the Gaussian orthogonal ensemble at half filling and the Gaussian unitary ensemble in the adjacent magnetization sector. We further construct coupled-cluster chains and obtain domain-wall dynamics consistent with diffusive magnetization transport in a small-cluster regime accessible to matrix-product-state simulations. These results establish analytical benchmarks and finite-size signatures of ordering, quantum chaos, and magnetization transport in U(1)-symmetric spin-SYK models, and they motivate quantum simulation of the low-temperature, large-system regime, which remains difficult for classical methods.

</details>


### [142] [Entropy production decomposition and fluctuation theorems under continuous feedback: From prescribed to stochastic protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16070)
*Long Him Cheung*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究反馈控制下熵产生（entropy production）的分解，将低通滤波（low-pass filtered）的测量信号视为随机协议，推导相应涨落定理（fluctuation theorem），并以谐振子模型给出自主热浴的物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明，当系统由预设协议驱动时，熵产生可分解为维持项与非自主项且各自满足涨落定理；但反馈控制下反馈参数依赖测量结果而非预设协议，该分解如何变化尚需澄清。

Method: 考虑过阻尼布朗粒子（overdamped Brownian particle），以低通滤波的测量信号 D 作为反馈参数，将 D 的每个实现视为随机协议，推导与 x 和 D 动力学相关的熵产生的涨落定理；在可解谐振子模型中把动力学映射为测量与滤波过程充当有效热浴（effective thermal bath）的自主系统（autonomous system）。

Result: 得到分别对应 x 与 D 动力学熵产生的涨落定理，并以信息流（information flow）加以解释；在谐振子模型中，反馈冷却（feedback cooling）对应热量从物理热浴经粒子传向更冷的有效热浴，信息流可解释为 x 与 D 之间热传导对应的熵。结果经数值模拟验证。

Conclusion: 反馈控制下熵产生的分解可借助随机协议视角与信息流表述，涨落定理依然成立，并可通过自主热浴映射获得清晰的热力学诠释。

Abstract: When a system is driven by a prescribed protocol, the entropy production can be decomposed into the housekeeping and non-autonomous contributions, each satisfying a fluctuation theorem. Under feedback control, however, the feedback parameter depends on measurement outcomes rather than following a prescribed protocol. To study how the decomposition changes, we consider an overdamped Brownian particle at position $x$ with a low-pass-filtered measurement signal $D$ as the feedback parameter. Regarding each realization of $D$ as a stochastic protocol, we derive fluctuation theorems for the entropy production associated with the dynamics of $x$ and of $D$, and interpret them in terms of the information flow. For a solvable harmonic model, we map the dynamics onto an autonomous system in which the measurement and filtering process acts as an effective thermal bath. Feedback cooling thus corresponds to heat conduction from the physical bath through the particle to this colder bath, and the information flow can be interpreted as the entropy associated with the heat conduction between $x$ and $D$. We verify these results by numerical simulations.

</details>


### [143] [Anti-Kibble-Zurek behavior driven by dichotomous Markov noise in a quantum Ising chain: Generalized stochastic resonance in the defect density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16237)
*Mingwei Yang,Peng Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究量子伊辛链（quantum Ising chain）中非高斯噪声——二分马尔可夫噪声（dichotomous Markov noise, DMN）对反Kibble-Zurek（anti-Kibble-Zurek, AKZ）缺陷密度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有AKZ行为研究多局限于高斯噪声；对高斯有色噪声（Gaussian colored noise, GCN），功率谱决定噪声诱导缺陷密度项随关联时间单调变化。作者希望考察非高斯有色噪声是否带来不同行为。

Method: 在量子伊辛链中引入DMN作为典型非高斯有色噪声，将噪声诱导缺陷密度视为响应函数（response function），分析其随噪声关联时间（correlation time）的变化。

Result: 噪声诱导缺陷密度呈现广义随机共振（generalized stochastic resonance）：随关联时间非单调变化，在快噪声区（fast-noise regime）增长、在慢噪声区（slow-noise regime）减小。该共振源于慢噪声区中DMN诱导趋势的非单调性，与GCN的单调情形形成对比，机制在于慢噪声区DMN实现的稀疏性（sparse realizations），而功率谱无法刻画该特征。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了量子伊辛链非平衡临界淬火动力学（nonequilibrium critical quench dynamics）中高斯噪声与非高斯噪声的基本区别。

Abstract: The anti-Kibble-Zurek behavior may arise under noise, adding a noise-induced contribution to the defect density beyond the conventional Kibble-Zurek term. Most investigations of such behavior have so far been restricted to Gaussian noise. For Gaussian colored noise (GCN), the power spectrum dictates a monotonic variation of this term with the correlation time. Here, we focus on dichotomous Markov noise (DMN), a prototypical non-Gaussian colored noise, in the quantum Ising chain. We show that the noise-induced defect density, taken as the response function, exhibits generalized stochastic resonance: it is nonmonotonic in the correlation time, growing in the fast-noise regime and decreasing in the slow-noise regime. This resonance stems from the anomalous AKZ behavior in the slow-noise regime, where the DMN-induced trend is nonmonotonic, in contrast to the monotonic GCN case. The underlying mechanism lies in the sparse realizations of DMN in the slow-noise regime, a feature not captured by the power spectrum. Our work thereby reveals a fundamental distinction between Gaussian and non-Gaussian noises in the nonequilibrium critical quench dynamics of the quantum Ising chain.

</details>


### [144] [Optimal staircase structure for bacterial adaptation in antibiotic landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16325)
*Kento Matsukawa,Shin-ichi Sasa,Namiko Mitarai*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文利用二维格点上的随机模型（物理空间×表型空间），在总范围固定、迁移与突变速率按端到端首次通过时间（first-passage time）缩放的前提下，发现步数增加会加速寻找下一个适应态、但提高重复种群饱和（population saturation）的累积代价，二者竞争可产生中间步数的最优值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗生素环境的空间异质性（spatial heterogeneity）驱动细菌抗性进化，但环境的离散结构（discrete structure）如何控制适应所需时间尚不清楚，本文针对此问题展开研究。

Method: 构建二维格点上的随机模型（stochastic model），两轴分别代表物理空间与表型空间（phenotype space）；在保持整体空间与浓度范围不变、并将迁移率与突变率按端到端首次通过时间进行缩放（scaling）的条件下，比较不同步数（number of steps）的情形，并用直接 Gillespie 模拟（Gillespie simulations）进行验证。

Result: 步数增加会加速对下一个适应态的搜索，但同时增加重复种群饱和的累积代价；两种效应的竞争可导致适应时间在中间步数处出现最优值。Gillespie 模拟再现了预测的适应时间对步数的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 离散环境结构对进化适应时间尺度（timescale）具有可预测的影响，研究结果为该机制提供了可检验的预测（testable predictions）。

Abstract: Spatial heterogeneity in antibiotic environments drives the evolution of bacterial resistance. However, how the discrete structure of these environments controls the time required for adaptation of bacteria remains unclear. We study this question using a stochastic model on a two-dimensional lattice whose axes represent physical space and phenotype space, comparing different numbers of steps while holding the overall spatial and concentration ranges fixed and scaling the migration and mutation rates by their end-to-end first-passage times. We find that increasing the number of steps accelerates the search for the next adapted state but increases the cumulative cost of repeated population saturation; this competition can produce an optimum at an intermediate number of steps. Direct Gillespie simulations reproduce the predicted dependence of the adaptation time on the number of steps, providing testable predictions for how discrete environmental structure shapes the timescale of evolutionary adaptation.

</details>


### [145] [Information Thermodynamics in Closed Quantum Many-Body Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16329)
*Yusuke O. Nakai,Shoki Sugimoto,Ryusuke Hamazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为初始处于微观热平衡（microscopic thermal equilibrium, MITE）的封闭量子多体系统建立了信息热力学（information thermodynamics）框架，并证明在局域测量与局域有限时间控制下，可提取功在热力学极限下与正则系综（canonical ensemble）一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息热力学已阐明麦克斯韦妖（Maxwell's demon）获取信息如何修正第二定律，但其在多体系统中的表述仍基本空白，本文旨在填补这一空缺。

Method: 在物理上合理的限制下（局域量子测量、由局域哈密顿量生成的有限时间控制）构建封闭量子多体系统的信息热力学框架，并额外假设每个局域反馈项仅依赖邻近测量结果，据此进行严格证明。

Result: 严格证明可提取功（extractable work）在热力学极限下与相应正则系综可提取功相等，并用量子-经典互信息（quantum-classical mutual information）及正则系综的 Holevo 信息等量子信息量表示该功；同时表明基于远距离测量结果的反馈可利用 MITE 中隐藏的长程关联（long-range correlations），使提取功相对正则系综获得广延（extensive）增加。

Conclusion: 局域测量与局域反馈下多体系统的信息热力学行为可由正则系综刻画，而远距离反馈则能借助 MITE 的长程关联突破该限制，实现广延量级的额外功提取。

Abstract: Although information thermodynamics explains how the second law is modified due to the information acquired by Maxwell's demon, its formulation in many-body systems remains largely open. Here, we develop an information-thermodynamic framework for closed quantum many-body systems initially in microscopic thermal equilibrium (MITE), under physically motivated restrictions: local quantum measurements and finite-time controls generated by local Hamiltonians. By additionally assuming that each local feedback term depends only on nearby measurement outcomes, we rigorously prove that the extractable work coincides in the thermodynamic limit with that extractable from the corresponding canonical ensemble. We further express this extractable work in terms of quantum-information quantities, including the quantum-classical mutual information and the Holevo information for the corresponding canonical ensemble. We also show that feedback based on distant measurement outcomes can exploit long-range correlations hidden in MITE, yielding an extensive increase in extracted work relative to the canonical ensemble.

</details>


### [146] [Continuous-Time Random Walks with Internal Stochastic Renewal Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16383)
*Boris Z. Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种基于结构化密度（structured density）的连续时间随机游走（continuous-time random walk, CTRW）框架：粒子内部状态在跳跃时“递增或重置为零”，按更新过程（renewal process）演化，由此导出多类积分-微分方程（integro-differential equations）及反常行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连续时间随机游走模型在刻画等待时间与跳跃分布如何产生各类非局部宏观方程方面缺乏统一、便于追踪粒子内部状态的构造，尤其是长等待时间与大步长相互作用导致的反常扩散（anomalous diffusion）机制需要更清晰的结构化建模。

Method: 通过跟踪粒子状态演化，构造结构化密度来描述过程；粒子每次跳跃时内部状态加一或重置为零，使内部状态服从更新过程。针对特定重置概率、等待时间和跳跃分布，显式推导依赖模式（mode-dependent）的记忆核（memory kernels）并分析其连续极限（continuum limits）。

Result: 该框架可恢复时间-空间分数阶 Fokker-Planck 方程（time-and-space-fractional Fokker-Planck equation）；得到一类通过几何递减等待时间序列在有限时间内收敛至平衡分布的伊辛过程；并构造引入自激发（self-excitation）与自抑制（self-inhibition）的等待时间机制，使均方位移满足与 t^ν 成正比、适用于任意 ν>0。

Conclusion: 上述示例表明，该统一框架能够把微观跳跃与重置机制同其非局部宏观控制方程（non-local macroscopic governing equations）联系起来，为构造和解释多种反常输运行为提供了结构化途径。

Abstract: We introduce a continuous-time random walk process via a structured density by tracking the state of the particle as the system evolves. Whenever the particle makes a jump, its internal state either increases by one or resets to zero, so that the internal state evolves according to a renewal process. A wide range of governing integro-differential equations may be obtained from this formulation, some of which exhibit various types of anomalous behaviour. For certain choices of reset probabilities, waiting times and jump distributions, we explicitly derive the mode-dependent memory kernels and investigate their continuum limits. The framework can recover the time-and-space-fractional Fokker-Planck equation, which traditionally models the interplay between long wait times and large jumps and exhibits anomalous diffusion. Furthermore, we obtain a process which converges toward the equilibrium distribution as time approaches some finite value through a sequence of waiting times which decrease geometrically. Finally, we investigate a process that incorporates self-excitation and self-inhibition into the waiting times for each state to produce a mean square displacement proportional to $t^ν$ for any $ν> 0$. Through these examples, we illustrate the link between the microscopic mechanisms in these processes and their non-local macroscopic governing equations.

</details>


### [147] [Freezing transition and shock-like transients in ballistic random walks under threshold resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16733)
*Rahul Das,Satya N Majumdar,Arnab Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维弹道随机游走者（ballistic random walker）在阈值重置（threshold resetting, TR）下的瞬态动力学，揭示稳态与速度分布无关，但弛豫过程强烈依赖速度分布，并出现空间依赖的弛豫率、冻结转变（freezing transition）、代数型慢弛豫与冲击式不连续等丰富非平衡现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对随机重置（stochastic resetting）的研究多关注稳态性质，而速度涨落如何影响瞬态空间结构尚不清楚。本文旨在刻画弹道运动与阈值重置耦合时的弛豫动力学，说明速度涨落即便不改变稳态，也可能深刻改变瞬态行为。

Method: 采用一维弹道随机游走模型：游走者自 \(x_0>0\) 出发，以从分布 \(φ(v)\) 抽取的速度向原点阈值运动，抵达阈值后以新抽样速度重置回 \(x_0\)。通过解析方法求解瞬态传播子与弛豫行为，并用蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）进行验证。

Result: 当到达阈值的平均首次通过时间（mean first-passage time）有限时，稳态在 \([0,x_0]\) 上均匀且与 \(φ(v)\) 无关；但弛豫率对一大类 \(φ(v)\) 显式依赖于空间坐标，并在超过临界位置后冻结为与位置无关的常数（冻结转变）。幂律速度分布下出现异常缓慢的代数弛豫（algebraic relaxation）；\(φ(v)\) 的锐利截断（sharp cutoff）则在传播子中产生向阈值传播、跳跃高度随时间衰减的类冲击不连续。

Conclusion: 结果表明，速度涨落虽不改变稳态分布，却能显著塑造瞬态空间结构与弛豫规律，展现阈值重置弹道系统的丰富非平衡行为；解析结果与蒙特卡洛模拟高度吻合。

Abstract: We investigate the transient dynamics of a one-dimensional ballistic random walker under threshold resetting (TR). The walker starts at $x_0>0$, moves towards a threshold at the origin with a velocity drawn from a distribution $φ(v)$, and on reaching the threshold is reset to $x_0$ with a new sampled velocity. Whenever the mean first-passage time to the threshold is finite, the walker reaches a steady state that is uniform on $[0,x_0]$ and independent of $φ(v)$. The relaxation towards it, in contrast, is highly sensitive to the behavior of $φ(v)$. For a large class of $φ(v)$-s, we find, rather remarkably, that the relaxation rate depends explicitly on the spatial coordinate, in contrast to conventional relaxation phenomena, where the rate is typically position-independent. Furthermore, this space-dependent relaxation rate eventually freezes to a constant, position-independent value beyond a critical position, a phenomenon we identify as \textit{freezing transition}. Additionally, for a power-law velocity distribution, the system exhibits an anomalously slow, algebraic relaxation towards the steady state. We also showed that a sharp cutoff in $φ(v)$ generates a shock-like discontinuity in the particle propagator that travels towards the threshold, with a jump height that decays with time. These results demonstrate how velocity fluctuations, despite leaving the steady state unchanged, can profoundly influence the transient spatial structures and showcase rich non-equilibrium behavior. Our analytical results are corroborated by Monte Carlo simulations, showing an excellent agreement.

</details>


### [148] [Work extraction from a single negative-temperature bath: a temperature gradient-driven heat flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16743)
*Wolfgang Rudolf Bauer*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出以大规模弱耦合工作系统组成的"虚拟集合"替代孤立机器，为从负绝对温度热库中"纯做功提取"（sole work extraction）提供与热力学第二定律相容的解释框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从热库纯提取功而不产生净状态变化违反第二定律（second law of thermodynamics）的开尔文-普朗克表述（Kelvin-Planck formulation），但负绝对温度（negative absolute temperature）热库被视为例外，其热力学稳定性因此存在争议，论文旨在调和这一矛盾。

Method: 考虑由大量弱耦合工作系统（weakly coupled working systems）构成的虚拟集合（virtual assembly）而非孤立机器，并基于其香农/信息熵（Shannon/information entropy）为其赋予正则温度（canonical temperature），使框架可自然推广至分子尺度机器（molecular-scale machines）。

Result: 给出范例表明该集合的正则温度低于负温熱库，因而纯做功提取等价于从高温系统（负温热库）向较冷系统（工作集合）的标准热流（standard heat flow），与第二定律完全一致；该信息与统计力学（statistical-mechanical）框架亦自然解释了"驴行为"（donkey behavior）——在负温热库中增加负载反而增强做功提取。

Conclusion: 通过信息熵定义的正则温度概念，可将负温热库中的纯做功提取纳入第二定律的自洽解释，并统一说明相关的反直觉现象，为负温系统热力学稳定性的争论提供新视角。

Abstract: Work dissipation into a thermal bath is standard, whereas the reverse process -- sole work extraction from a thermal bath without a net state change -- contradicts the Kelvin-Planck formulation of the second law of thermodynamics. However, this prohibition is bypassed for baths at negative absolute temperature, sparking ongoing debate regarding their thermodynamic stability. Here, we reconcile the second law with sole work extraction from a negative-temperature bath by considering a large virtual assembly of weakly coupled working systems rather than an isolated machine. By assigning this assembly a canonical temperature derived from its Shannon/information entropy, the framework naturally extends to molecular-scale machines. We demonstrate paradigmatic examples where the assembly's canonical temperature is cooler than the negative-temperature bath. Consequently, sole work extraction represents standard heat flow from a hot system (the negative-temperature bath) to a cooler system (the working assembly), rendering the process fully consistent with the second law. This informational and statistical-mechanical framework also naturally explains the counter-intuitive phenomenon of "donkey behavior", where increasing the workload in a negative-temperature bath leads to enhanced work extraction.

</details>


### [149] [Detecting non-Hermitian topology from long-time dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16745)
*Antonio D'Abbruzzo,Rosario Fazio*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种通过协方差矩阵（covariance matrix）弛豫测量手征对称开放高斯体系Lindbladian谱的非厄米拓扑不变量（non-Hermitian topological invariant）的方案，且在长时区间有效、不依赖边缘模式隔离，并在一维Kitaev链上验证其可分辨所有相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放高斯体系的Liouvillian谱可携带非厄米拓扑信息，但Liouvillian趋肤效应（Liouvillian skin effect）可能遮蔽边缘模式，使依赖边缘模式隔离的传统探测手段失效，故需一种不依赖边缘模式的测量方案。

Method: 从协方差矩阵的弛豫（relaxation）动力学中提取与手征对称（chiral symmetry）开放高斯体系Lindbladian谱相关的非厄米拓扑不变量，探测在长时区间进行；并以含库诱导非互易性（reservoir-induced nonreciprocity）的Kitaev链为具体模型展开分析。

Result: 首次给出该Kitaev链拓扑相图（topological phase diagram）的完全解析描述（此前未知）；所提方案能可靠区分全部不同相。

Conclusion: 协方差矩阵弛豫可作为探测开放高斯体系非厄米拓扑不变量的可行途径，即使在Liouvillian趋肤效应遮蔽边缘模式时仍适用，并在Kitaev链模型中得到了相图与相分辨能力的验证。

Abstract: We propose a protocol to measure the non-Hermitian topological invariant associated with the Lindbladian spectrum of chiral-symmetric open Gaussian systems from the relaxation of the covariance matrix. Importantly, the method works in the long-time regime and does not rely on isolating edge modes, so it remains applicable even when the Liouvillian skin effect may obscure them. We illustrate the technique on a Kitaev chain with reservoir-induced nonreciprocity, for which we first obtain a fully analytical and previously unknown description of its topological phase diagram. We then show that our protocol reliably distinguishes all the different phases.

</details>


### [150] [A deterministic theory of kinetic fluctuations in long-range interacting homogeneous systems without collective effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16893)
*Anwar El Rhirhayi,Jean-Baptiste Fouvry,Julien Barré*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文把长程相互作用系统有限-$N$涨落的统计问题，在无集体效应的均匀系统中约化为三个确定性动理学方程，从而用一次确定性积分替代大规模系综模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限-$N$涨落（finite-$N$ fluctuations）主导长程相互作用系统的慢弛豫，但获取其完整统计需要规模巨大且代价高昂的模拟系综，因此亟需一个低成本的确定性替代方案。

Method: 以Landau-Dean-Kawasaki方程（Landau-Dean-Kawasaki equation，即Landau方程大偏差原理的随机对应）为出发点，在$1/N$领头阶推导经验分布函数$n$阶矩（$n$-point moments）的无穷层级，并证明其可由准高斯闭合（quasi-Gaussian closure）精确求解，得到由Landau方程、两点矩的Lyapunov方程与三点矩的动理学方程构成的最小Landau涨落层级（minimal Landau fluctuation hierarchy）。

Result: 该层级精确保持碰撞不变量（collisional invariants）及其涨落，并可预测中心涨落区（central fluctuation regime）中可观测量的完整概率分布；与$N$体实现（$N$-body realizations）对比，均值、方差及高阶矩均被重现，且精度随$N$系统性提升。

Conclusion: 三个耦合偏微分方程的单次确定性时间积分即可在任意大$N$下替代大规模系综模拟，为长程相互作用系统的有限-$N$涨落统计提供了高效且有理论保证的计算框架。

Abstract: Finite-$N$ fluctuations govern the slow relaxation of long-range interacting systems, yet accessing their full statistics demands huge ensembles of costly simulations. We show that, in homogeneous systems without collective effects, this statistical problem collapses onto just three deterministic kinetic equations. Starting from the Landau-Dean-Kawasaki equation, i.e. the stochastic counterpart of the large deviation principle for the Landau equation, we derive the infinite hierarchy of $n$-point moments of the empirical distribution function at leading order in $1/N$. We prove that it is solved exactly by a quasi-Gaussian closure. The resulting minimal Landau fluctuation hierarchy is formed by the Landau equation, a Lyapunov equation for the two-point moment, and a kinetic equation for the three-point moment. From these three equations, all higher-order moments follow algebraically. The hierarchy exactly preserves the collisional invariants and their fluctuations, and predicts the full probability distribution of observables in the central fluctuation regime. Benchmarked against $N$-body realizations, it reproduces the mean, variance and higher-order moments with an accuracy that improves systematically with $N$. A single deterministic time-integration of three coupled partial differential equations thus replaces large ensembles of simulations, for any large $N$.

</details>


### [151] [Discrete Time Crystals from Anti-Unitary Floquet Driving and Dynamical Freezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17333)
*Anirban Das,Asmi Haldar,Arnab Das*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出利用反幺正周期驱动（anti-unitary periodic drive）通过动力学冻结（Dynamical Freezing, DF）直接稳定离散时间晶体（Discrete Time-Crystal），其中磁化强度表现为涌现守恒算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为离散时间晶体的稳定化提供一条直接路径，借助反幺正周期驱动与动力学冻结机制克服亚谐响应（subharmonic response）的衰减问题。

Method: 采用反幺正周期驱动结合动力学冻结框架，论证磁化强度成为涌现守恒算符，并指出两周期准能级（quasi-energies）的精确配对锁定亚谐响应；同时通过无限链（infinite-chain）数值模拟检验动力学行为。

Result: 动力学冻结保持有效哈密顿量（effective Hamiltonian）的准局域性（quasi-local）；无限链模拟显示，对任意有限磁化强度的典型态均无可探测的衰减，亚谐响应被稳定锁定。

Conclusion: 反幺正周期驱动可有效稳定离散时间晶体，并给出了该驱动的实验实现方案概述。

Abstract: We show anti-unitary periodic drive paves a direct path for stabilizing a Discrete Time-Crystal by Dynamical Freezing (DF). The magnetization appears as an emergent conserved operator, and an exact pairing of two-cycle quasi-energies pins the subharmonic response. DF keeps the effective Hamiltonian $\textit{quasi-local}$; infinite-chain simulations show no detectable decay for a generic state with any finite magnetization. We outline an experimental realization of the anti-unitary drive.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.other'></div>

# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [Carbon-made Lobachevsky surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14602)
*R. Gabbrielli,P. Pais,P. Castorina,T. P. C. Klaver,D. Legut,O. De Luca,R. G. Agostino,A. Iorio*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该综述以“平衡高斯–博内定理（balanced Gauss–Bonnet theorem）”为统一原理，梳理并扩展了碳质常负高斯曲率表面的构造，将曲率在向错缺陷、弹性变形六边形与边界之间分配，并借助SL(2,R)子群给出三类伪球面上的缺陷排布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳质常负高斯曲率表面可在实验室中实现罗巴切夫斯基平面（Lobachevsky plane）的局部，其动机涵盖类比引力（analogue gravity，其中椭圆、Beltrami与双曲伪球对应Bañados–Teitelboim–Zanelli黑洞的三个静态相）以及海水淡化等应用，因而需要统一理解其构造原理。

Method: 以平衡高斯–博内定理为核心原则进行综述与扩展：拓扑固定的曲率由向错缺陷（disclination defects）、弹性变形六边形与边界共同分担；并利用SL(2,R)的椭圆、抛物、双曲单参数子群构造三类伪球面上的缺陷排布（椭圆族为新建构，Beltrami情形复现已知结果，双曲伪球为提出方案）。同时以显式多面体构造说明仅由六边形构成的球面实为带孔球面。

Result: 无缺陷的曲率无法改变拓扑：仅含六边形的球面只能是穿孔球面；三类伪球面的缺陷排布分别源于SL(2,R)的三类单参数子群。作者将理想排布与设计获得及模拟纳米挤出（nano-extrusion）得到的碳结构进行比较。

Conclusion: 理想缺陷排布与实验/模拟碳结构可作对照，并推测无穷多Fuchsian群可能对应无穷多种罗巴切夫斯基曲面。

Abstract: Carbon-made surfaces of constant negative Gaussian curvature realize portions of the Lobachevsky plane in the laboratory, with motivations ranging from analogue gravity, where the elliptic, Beltrami and hyperbolic pseudospheres correspond to the three static phases of the Bañados--Teitelboim--Zanelli black hole, to water desalination. We review and extend their construction around a single principle, the balanced Gauss--Bonnet theorem: the curvature fixed by the topology is shared among disclination defects, elastically deformed hexagons and boundaries. Curvature without defects cannot change the topology: a sphere made of hexagons only is a punctured sphere, as an explicit polyhedral construction shows. The arrangements of the defects on the three pseudospheres stem from the elliptic, parabolic and hyperbolic one-parameter subgroups of $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$: we construct them for the elliptic family, recover the known one of the Beltrami pseudosphere, and propose one for the hyperbolic pseudosphere. We compare these ideal arrangements with carbon structures obtained by design and by simulated nano-extrusion, and suggest that the infinitely many Fuchsian groups may correspond to the infinitely many Lobachevsky surfaces.

</details>


### [153] [Certifying Majorana persistence through Hamiltonian learning from boundary measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16832)
*Tohid Farajollahpour,Alireza Qaiumzadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该工作将哈密顿量学习（Hamiltonian learning）与对易子界（commutator bounds）结合，利用链端有限次单发边界测量与局部键标定，在明确器件假设下给出局域马约拉纳算符（Majorana operators）持续性的与态无关下界，并在合成基准与IBM硬件记录上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链端的有限测量能否在不确定体拓扑（bulk topology）的前提下认证局域量子动力学，是亟待回答的问题；本文旨在仅凭边界信息为局域马约拉纳算符的持续性提供可认证的保证。

Method: 结合哈密顿量学习与对易子界，由时间分辨的单发边界测量（single-shot boundary measurements）与局部键标定（local bond calibrations）构造局域哈密顿量系数的联合置信集（joint confidence sets）；在给定失败概率下导出对称化自关联（symmetrized autocorrelations）的与态无关下界，且对局域系数（local coefficients）的所有相容延拓（compatible continuations）均成立。当第一键上跃迁与配对相等且无额外边界项时，利用精确恒等式可仅由末端量子比特推断，即使其余位置存在任意含时无关相互作用。

Result: 合成基准（synthetic benchmarks）在边界最优参数点（sweet spot）附近可将持续性认证到约60倍测量窗口；三比特与六比特IBM硬件数据强化了对所编程相干哈密顿量（coherent Hamiltonian）的条件性保证（conditional guarantees）。

Conclusion: 结果表明链端有限测量足以认证局域动力学与马约拉纳持续性，而无需确定体拓扑；对量子点模型（quantum-dot models）的应用将调控精度（tuning precision）与持续时间和失谐诱导劈裂（detuning-induced splitting）联系起来。

Abstract: Finite measurements at a chain end can certify local quantum dynamics without determining bulk topology. We combine Hamiltonian learning with commutator bounds to certify the persistence of localized Majorana operators in Kitaev chains. Time-resolved single-shot boundary measurements and local bond calibrations define joint confidence sets for local Hamiltonian coefficients. Under explicit device assumptions, these sets yield state-independent lower bounds on symmetrized autocorrelations with a prescribed failure probability, valid for all compatible continuations. With equal hopping and pairing on the first bond and no additional boundary terms, an exact identity enables inference from the end qubit alone, even with arbitrary time-independent interactions elsewhere. Synthetic benchmarks near a boundary sweet spot certify persistence for up to about 60 times the measurement window. Three- and six-qubit IBM records strengthen conditional guarantees for the programmed coherent Hamiltonian. Applications to quantum-dot models relate tuning precision to persistence times and detuning-induced splitting.

</details>


### [154] [High-pressure behavior of the low-frequency mode of water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17096)
*Maria Rescigno,Guillaume Radtke,Umbertoluca Ranieri,Richard Gaal,Luigi Paolasini,Andrea Piovano,Ferdinando Formisano,Paola Giura,Livia Eleonora Bove*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 利用高分辨率非弹性X射线散射（inelastic X-ray scattering, IXS）在0.1–1 GPa范围内研究H₂O与D₂O的集体动力学，发现低频（low-frequency, LF）模始终接近临界阻尼（critical damping），且频率随压力显著硬化（hardening），远超横声学模（transverse acoustic mode）的预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液态水THz谱中约10 meV的低频模微观起源长期存在争议，主要解释包括横声学类激发（transverse acoustic-like excitation）、横光学类模（transverse optic-like mode）与模-模耦合机制（mode-mode coupling），该问题尚未解决。

Method: 在室温、0.1至1 GPa压力下，采用能量分辨率1.6 meV的高分辨率IXS测量H₂O与D₂O的集体动力学，并将结果与Ice VI晶体的IXS测量及从头算模拟（ab initio simulations）进行对比。

Result: 低频模在所研究的整个压力范围内保持强阻尼、接近临界阻尼；其频率随压力明显硬化，幅度远大于横声学模的预期。与Ice VI的IXS及从头算模拟对比显示，同一能量区间存在一簇弱色散（weakly dispersive）模式。

Conclusion: 液态水的低频模并非单一明确激发，而具有混合特征（mixed character），源于多个分支的叠加（superposition of several branches），从而为压缩条件下水的反常THz动力学（anomalous THz dynamics）提供了自洽的微观图像。

Abstract: The low-frequency (LF) mode observed in the THz spectrum of liquid water near 10 meV has been known for decades, yet its microscopic origin remains debated. Leading interpretations assign it to a transverse acoustic-like excitation, a transverse optic-like mode, or a mode-mode coupling mechanism. We address this question by probing the collective dynamics of H$_2$O and D$_2$O at room temperature and pressures from 0.1 to 1 GPa using high-resolution (1.6 meV) inelastic X-ray scattering (IXS). The LF mode remains heavily damped, close to critical damping, throughout the explored range, while its frequency hardens markedly with pressure, much more than expected for a transverse acoustic mode. Comparison with IXS measurements and \textit{ab initio} simulations of crystalline Ice VI reveals a cluster of weakly dispersive modes in the same energy range, suggesting that the low-frequency mode in liquid water reflects a mixed character arising from the superposition of several branches rather than a single well-defined excitation, providing a coherent microscopic picture of water's anomalous THz dynamics under compression.

</details>

# math.FA [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Sampled resolvents of $C_0$-semigroups: aliasing and sharp rates on the Crandall-Pazy scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15563)
*Nicholas Castillo*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文研究用网格采样 Laplace 积分得到的预解式离散化 $Q_T=T(I-e^{-\lambda T}S_T)^{-1}$，把 Castillo–Costin–Costin 的二进制级数解释为矩形法则的伸缩求和，并给出由 Crandall–Pazy 类参数 $\alpha$ 刻画的尖锐误差率标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机来自 Castillo、Costin 与 Costin 关于自伴算子预解式的二进制级数：其部分和恰为 $Q_{2^{-N}}$，因而该级数可视为伸缩的矩形法则。由此需要理解此类采样离散化的误差结构与收敛速率。

Method: 对 $C_0$-半群（$C_0$-semigroup）的生成元 $B$ 在网格 $T\mathbb{N}$ 上采样 Laplace 积分，借助 Poisson 求和（Poisson summation）将误差表示为标量 $T/2$ 加上混叠点（alias points）$\lambda-2\pi i n/T$ 处预解式的对称极限；各二进制层按 $n$ 的 $2$-adic 赋值（$2$-adic valuation）消去混叠项。误差率以 Crandall–Pazy 类参数 $\alpha$ 刻画，即 $\|BS_t\|=O(t^{-1/\alpha})$。

Result: 得到梯形校正（trapezoid correction）$Q_T-\frac{T}{2}I$ 的尖锐速率标度：算子范数下误差为 $O(T^{\alpha})$，在 $X$ 与 $D(B)$ 之间的 $n$ 阶插值空间（interpolation space）上为 $O(T^{\alpha+\theta})$，在 $D(B)$ 上为 $O(T^{1+\alpha})$（后者仅需沿 $\mathrm{Re}\,z=\mathrm{Re}\,\lambda$ 的 $\alpha$ 阶预解衰减）。该结果在无界生成元的酉群（$\alpha=0$，范数下不收敛）与解析半群（$\alpha=1$）之间插值，且每个指数均可达到。

Conclusion: 对光滑数据，Euler–Maclaurin 展开为 Romberg 外推（Romberg extrapolation）提供依据；对自伴生成元给出各二进制层对 Stone 公式贡献的闭式；并将结果转化为由采样自相关计算 Green 函数（Green functions）的显式误差界与步长规则。数值实验（含半群属 Gevrey 类的结构阻尼弦）验证了所给速率。

Abstract: For a $C_0$-semigroup $(S_t)$ with generator $B$, sampling the Laplace integral $(λ-B)^{-1}=\int_0^\infty e^{-λt}S_t\,dt$ on the grid $T\mathbb{N}$ gives $Q_T=T(I-e^{-λT}S_T)^{-1}$, the impulse-invariant discretization of the resolvent. The motivation is a dyadic series of Castillo, Costin and Costin for the resolvent of a self-adjoint operator: its partial sums are exactly $Q_{2^{-N}}$, so the series is a telescoped rectangle rule. By Poisson summation, on $D(B)$ the error of $Q_T$ is the scalar $T/2$ plus the symmetric limit of the resolvents at the alias points $λ-2πin/T$, $n\ne0$, and the dyadic levels remove these aliases according to the $2$-adic valuation of $n$. Our main result is a sharp scale of rates for the trapezoid correction $Q_T-\frac T2I$. If $(S_t)$ belongs to the Crandall--Pazy class with parameter $α\in(0,1]$, that is $\|BS_t\|=O(t^{-1/α})$ as $t\to0$, the error is $O(T^α)$ in operator norm, $O(T^{α+θ})$ on the interpolation space of order $θ$ between $X$ and $D(B)$, and $O(T^{1+α})$ on $D(B)$, the last requiring only resolvent decay of order $α$ along the line $\mathrm{Re}\, z=\mathrm{Re}\,λ$. This interpolates between unitary groups with unbounded generator ($α=0$, no convergence in norm) and analytic semigroups ($α=1$), and every exponent is attained. For smooth data an Euler--Maclaurin expansion justifies Romberg extrapolation. For self-adjoint generators we give closed forms for the contribution of each dyadic level to Stone's formula, and we translate the results into an explicit error bound and step-size rule for Green functions computed from sampled autocorrelations. Experiments, including a structurally damped string whose semigroup is of Gevrey class, confirm the rates.

</details>
