### [156] [Axial von Mises Framework for Tensor-Dependent Second-Harmonic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16232)
*Sui Ip Ng,Yi-Ping Ho,Ken-Tye Yong*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究在一个轴向 von Mises 分布假设下，推导了投影纤维轴统计量与张量依赖的二次谐波产生（second-harmonic generation, SHG）谐波之间的闭式关系，并明确指出各角谐波的可识别条件与推断边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHG 角谐波常被用于推断纤维取向与张量性质，但谐波与投影纤维轴统计量之间缺乏显式的解析映射，容易导致对谐波零点与取向敏感性的误读。

Method: 采用单一轴向 von Mises 总体描述纤维取向，在域内相干发射、域间强度叠加的假定下，将每个角谐波分解为局部张量系数与轴向圆矩（circular moment）之积，分别推导平行分析器（second、fourth、sixth 谐波）与理想等权无分析器（second、fourth 谐波）检测情形。

Result: 公式给出几何特异的谐波零点、灵敏度损失与次级固定角比值的反演歧义，尤其指出无分析器四阶谐波在 $ρ=1/3$ 处的零点并不意味着取向敏感性完全丧失；制备角膜的正向/反向记录示范了经验无分析器谐波的提取，束状感兴趣区内二阶谐波两次扫描均可分辨但归一化幅度不同，四阶谐波未能分辨。

Conclusion: 贡献在于对 SHG 谐波提供解析解释及物理推断的明确条件；测量本身未确立绝对磁化率比 $ρ$、轴向浓度 $κ$，也未实现热无序与降解的实验区分，仅支持角测量的可行性而定量归一化形状一致性尚未建立。

Abstract: We derive closed-form relations between projected fibril-axis statistics and tensor-dependent second-harmonic generation (SHG) harmonics using a single axial von Mises population. Under coherent emission within domains and detected intensity addition across domains, each angular harmonic factors into a local tensor coefficient and an axial circular moment. Parallel-analyzer detection contains second, fourth and sixth harmonics; ideal equal-weight analyzer-free detection contains second and fourth harmonics. The resulting formulas identify geometry-specific harmonic zeros, sensitivity loss and ambiguous inversion of a secondary fixed-angle ratio. In particular, the analyzer-free fourth-harmonic zero at $ρ=1/3$ does not imply loss of all orientation sensitivity. Prepared-cornea forward/reverse recordings illustrate extraction of empirical analyzer-free harmonics. In the bundle ROI, second harmonics are resolved in both sweeps, while their normalized amplitudes differ and fourth harmonics remain unresolved. An exploratory central ROI from the same recordings yields a different forward/reverse profile. Within the same operator-identified tissue, low-signal space coverage and apparent fibre alignment vary slightly across recordings. These observations support angular-measurement feasibility while leaving quantitative normalized-shape agreement unestablished. The thermal extension illustrates consequences of assumed kinetics rather than calibrated tissue trajectories. The contribution is an analytical interpretation of SHG harmonics with explicit conditions for physical inference; the measurements do not establish absolute susceptibility ratio $ρ$, axial concentration $κ$, or experimental separation of thermal disorder from degradation.

</details>


### [157] [Asymmetric Frequency Conversion and Violation of Kirchhoff's Law of Thermal Radiation in Phased Floquet Metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17062)
*Yao-Ting Wang,Emanuele Galiffi,Sander A. Mann,Mauro Antezza,Andrea Alù*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究提出由三片近距导体薄片构成的系统，借助Floquet现象实现的非互易频率转换（nonreciprocal frequency conversion），使热辐射显著违反基尔霍夫定律（Kirchhoff's law）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在突破基尔霍夫定律对热辐射的约束，并探索无需磁场的非互易光子器件（magnet-free nonreciprocal photonic devices）途径。

Method: 构建三片紧密排列的导体薄片体系，对其中一个超表面（metasurface）施加时间调制（temporal modulation），利用表面模式转变（surface mode transitions）产生高度不对称的频率转换。

Result: 系统的热辐射显著违反基尔霍夫定律，并实现高度不对称的频率转换；该效应由Floquet现象驱动的非互易机制所支持。

Conclusion: 研究拓展了Floquet超表面（Floquet metasurfaces）在波转换与能量操控方面的能力，为无磁非互易光子器件及热管理、光子制冷（photonic refrigeration）应用提供可能。

Abstract: We study a system of three closely spaced conducting sheets, whose thermal emission largely violates Kirchhoff's law through nonreciprocal frequency conversion enabled by Floquet phenomena. We leverage surface mode transitions controlled by temporal modulation of one of the metasurfaces, resulting in largely asymmetric frequency conversion. Our findings broaden the capabilities of Floquet metasurfaces for wave conversion and energy manipulation, paving the way towards magnet-free nonreciprocal photonic devices with opportunities for thermal management and efficient photonic refrigeration.

</details>

### [158] [Slow Macroscopic Rotations of Neutron Stars With a Deformed Effective Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15591)
*A. A. Uleiev,A. G. Magner,S. P. Maydanyuk,A. Bonasera,H. Zheng,S. N. Fedotkin,A. I. Levon,U. V. Grygoriev,T. Depastas*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该文将有限理想流体中子星（neutron star, NS）宏观模型扩展至转动形变系统，通过有效表面（effective-surface, ES）方法在小频率线性扰动下推导出转动惯量（moment of inertia, MI）的解析表达式，并据此给出对NS半径的附加约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有NS宏观模型多针对静态球对称情形，需将其推广至缓慢转动的形变系统，以刻画强引力下转动惯量与半径的关系，并获得更严格的中子星半径约束。

Method: 在Schwarzschild度规附近对小于频率ω作线性扰动展开，采用球面坐标下的Kerr度规方法计算角动量I与转动惯量Θ，并在薄边界层参数a/R≪1的最低阶同时计入物态方程（Equation of State）能量密度的体项与梯度表面项。

Result: 得到解析宏观表达式Θ=dI/dω=widetildeΘ/(1−𝒯_{tφ})，其中tildeΘ为统计平均转动惯量、𝒯_{tφ}为时间—方位角(t,φ)关联，二者均由体积分与表面分组成；转动惯量Θ随有效半径R与形变参数显著变化，且(t,φ)关联、表面贡献及表面形变给出对NS半径更窄的约束。

Conclusion: 强引力下的形变与表面效应对NS转动惯量及半径约束有实质影响；文中还对若干具有强引力与较长转动周期的中子星检验了线性扰动理论适用的绝热条件（adiabaticity conditions）。

Abstract: The macroscopic model for a neutron star (NS) as a finite perfect fluid at the equilibrium is extended to rotating deformed systems by incorporating the linear perturbation expansion over a small frequency $ω$ near the Schwarzschild gravitational metric within the effective-surface (ES) approach. The NS angular momentum $I$ and moment of inertia (MI) for a slow stationary azimuthal rotation around the symmetry axis is calculated by using the Kerr metric approach in spheroidal coordinates, and compared with the spherical results. The gradient surface terms of the macroscopic NS energy density $\mathcal{E}(ρ)$ (Equation of State) are taken into account along with the volume ones at the leading order of the leptodermic parameter $a/R \ll 1$, where $a$ is the ES crust thickness and $R$ is the NS effective radius. The analytical macroscopic NS MI expressions, $Θ= dI/dω= \widetildeΘ/(1-\mathcal{T}_{t\varphi})$, have been obtained in terms of the statistically averaged MI, $\tildeΘ$, and its time and azimuthal-angle $t,\varphi$ correlation, $\mathcal{T}_{t\varphi}$, as sums of the volume and surface components. The MI $Θ$ is changed significantly as function of the effective radius $R$ and deformation parameter because of a strong gravity. We found the additional narrower constraints for the NS radius due mainly to the $t,\varphi$ correlations and surface contributions and NS surface deformation. The adiabaticity conditions for application of the linear perturbation theory is carried out for several neutron stars with a strong gravity and relatively large rotation periods.

</details>

### [159] [Emergent Anyons in a Weakly Interacting Gauged Chern Insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14658)
*Sven Bachmann,Leonardo Goller,Marcello Porta*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了费米子基态多体陈数（many-body Chern number）与 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 规范理论中磁通编织（braiding of fluxes）之间的微观关系：在能隙条件下，修饰后的通量环满足 $W_2W_1=e^{i\frac{\pi}{2}C}W_1W_2$。作者在弱相互作用 Qi-Wu-Zhang 绝缘体中通过规范化电荷宇称（gauging charge parity）实现 $C=1$，并证明所得相为带隙的拓扑有序相，具有四个局域不可区分基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将拓扑能带不变量（多体陈数）与非阿贝尔式通量编织统计建立直接微观联系，并在具体相互作用费米子模型中实现由此产生的拓扑有序相。

Method: 方法为解析推导基态扇区上通量环的对易关系，结合反射正性（reflection positivity）与棋盘估计（chessboard estimates）证明通量缺陷有质量；并在弱相互作用 Qi-Wu-Zhang 绝缘体中对电荷宇称做规范化以构造 $C=1$ 情形。

Result: 得到通量环对易关系 $W_2W_1=e^{i\frac{\pi}{2}C}W_1W_2$；在 Qi-Wu-Zhang 模型中实现 $C=1$，并证明通量缺陷有质量，系统处于带隙的拓扑有序相，基态四重简并且局域不可区分。

Conclusion: 多体陈数可作为 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 规范理论中通量编织相位（braiding phase）的微观来源，且弱相互作用拓扑绝缘体经规范化后可获得稳定的、带隙的拓扑有序相。

Abstract: We derive a microscopic relation between the many-body Chern number $C$ of the fermionic ground state and the braiding of fluxes in $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ gauge theory coupled to matter: under a spectral gap condition, dressed flux loops obey $W_2W_1=e^{i\fracπ{2}C}W_1W_2$ on the ground-state sector. We realize $C=1$ by gauging charge parity in a weakly interacting Qi-Wu-Zhang insulator. Reflection positivity and chessboard estimates show that flux defects are massive, which yields a gapped, topologically ordered phase with four locally indistinguishable ground states.

</details>


### [160] [Making Poisson σ again - an étude on groupoidal gauging, anomalies, gerbes, and defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14784)
*Rafał R. Suszek*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文将 Poisson σ-模型（Poisson σ-model, PSM）重新表述为主群胚丛（principaloid bundle）上的联络理论，推导其规范反常并给出反常抵消与向任意拓扑阴影丛（shadow bundle）推广的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为澄清 Poisson σ-模型中平坦方向与规范对称性的几何来源，并解决该场论的规范反常（gauge anomaly）问题，同时将模型推广至非平凡拓扑情形。

Method: 把 PSM 视为平凡主群胚丛 $P_0=\Sigma\times G\to\Sigma$ 上的联络理论，将平坦方向解释为 $P_0$ 自同构的线性化；提出通过将模型改写为 $F_0$ 上依赖联络的平凡 gerbe 全纯（holonomy），并与 $M$ 上具 $G$-等变结构的背景 gerbe 作张量积来实现反常抵消；进一步用局部乘积 gerbe 推广至任意拓扑的主群胚丛与阴影丛。

Result: 证明当 $\omega$ 可预量子化（prequantisable）时规范反常消失；证明 $G$-等变结构保证局部 gerbe 到 $F$ 上整体 gerbe 的 2-堆下降（2-stacky descent），并给出下降障碍的上同调讨论；最后将推广后的 PSM 重写为带拓扑规范对称性缺陷的场论。

Conclusion: 推广后的 PSM 由乘积 gerbe 的全纯定义，其规范化被理解为向 orbistack $M//G$ 的下降；$G$-等变结构与 $P$ 的过渡余循环共同构成拓扑缺陷的数据。

Abstract: The Poisson $σ$-model (PSM) over a closed worldsheet $Σ$ is reinterpreted as a theory of connections on a trivial principaloid bundle $P_0 = Σ\times G \to Σ$ with the symplectic structure groupoid $(G,ω)$ integrating the Poisson target $(M,Π)$, and that of sections of the shadow bundle $F_0 = Σ\times M \to Σ$ - as defined in 2503.09886 [math.DG]. The flat directions of the PSM are explained as linearisations of automorphisms of $P_0$, modelled on maps from $Σ$ into the group of lagrangean bisections of $G$, which induce Poisson automorphisms of $(M,Π)$. The gauge anomaly of the ensuing field theory is derived and demonstrated to vanish for prequantisable $ω$. A general anomaly-cancellation mechanism is proposed, which couples the bare model - rewritten as the holonomy of a connection-dependent trivial gerbe on $F_0$ - to a background gerbe over $M$ endowed with a $G$-equivariant structure. The coupling takes the form of the tensor product of the two gerbes and the extended PSM is defined as the holonomy of the product gerbe. An essential generalisation of the theory is then proposed - to shadows $F$ of principaloid bundles $P$ of arbitrary topology, in keeping with the interpretation of the gauging as descent to the orbistack $M//G$. The generalisation uses local product gerbes depending on local connections over trivialisations of $F$. The $G$-equivariant structure is proven to ensure a 2-stacky descent of the local gerbes to a global one over $F$. Its holonomy is taken as the definition of the generalised PSM. A cohomological discussion of obstructions against the descent is provided. Finally, a reformulation of the generalised PSM as a field theory with a topological gauge-symmetry defect is given, in which the $G$-equivariant structure is employed - alongside the transition cocycle of $P$ - as the datum of the defect.

</details>


### [161] [Cumulants of Operator Semigroups for Hierarchies of Many-Particle Evolution Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15304)
*V. I. Gerasimenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文综述量子多粒子系统演化方程数学理论的最新进展，指出算子半群的累积量（cumulants）概念为非微扰解展开提供基础，并证明关联（correlations）的演化支配有限与无限粒子系统的状态动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对量子多粒子系统基本演化方程（层级方程）的柯西问题（Cauchy problem），需要超越微扰论的非微扰解表示框架，以统一描述状态与可观测量的演化。

Method: 基于算子半群的累积量（cumulants of operator semigroups）概念，构造表示层级方程非微扰解的展开结构，涵盖约化密度算子（reduced density operators）的BBGKY层级与约化关联算子（reduced correlation operators）的非线性演化方程层级。

Result: 所构造的关联动力学可描述有限与无限粒子数情形下的状态演化；结果表明，粒子关联的行为在无限粒子量子系统中支配状态动力学。

Conclusion: 算子半群累积量方法为量子多粒子演化方程提供统一的非微扰表述，并揭示关联动力学是理解无穷粒子系统状态演化的核心。

Abstract: The paper presents a brief overview of recent advances in the mathematical theory of evolution equations for quantum many-particle systems.
  The structure of expansions representing non-perturbative solutions to the Cauchy problem for hierarchies of fundamental evolution equations for many quantum particles is outlined. It has been shown that the concept of cumulants of operator semigroups forms the basis for non-perturbative representation of solutions to hierarchies of evolution equations that describe the state and observables.
  It is proven that the constructed dynamics of correlations underlie the description of the evolution of the state, both for finitely and infinitely many particles, governed by the BBGKY hierarchy for reduced density operators or by the hierarchy of nonlinear evolution equations for reduced correlation operators. In fact, it is demonstrated that the behavior of particle correlations governs the dynamics of states in quantum systems with an infinite number of particles.

</details>


### [162] [On JT boundary algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15452)
*Feng Xu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了 Jackiw–Teitelboim（JT）引力耦合自由玻色物质时的正则边界代数（canonical boundary algebras），证明在最低权重大于 1/2 的有限或可数物种族下，两个边界代数为互为交换子（mutual commutants）的非超有限（nonhyperfinite）II∞ 型因子，其非零有限角为非超有限 II1 型因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是为 JT 引力耦合自由玻色物质建立严格的边界代数结构，并在不依赖路径积分（path-integral）论证的前提下获得忠实的正规半有限迹（faithful normal semifinite trace），同时厘清权重条件的作用与高能极限下的代数性质。

Method: 采用有界热正则化场（bounded heat-regularized fields）与 Hilbert 代数（Hilbert algebra）进行构造，从而直接得到忠实的正规半有限迹；并通过对单质量无质量标量的有界可观测量与正规态取高能极限进行分析。

Result: 当物种族非空、有限或可数且最低权重大于 1/2 时，两边界代数互为交换子且为非超有限 II∞ 型因子，其非零有限角为非超有限 II1 型因子；权重限制是尖锐的，当 0<d≤1/2 时基本点场在正热正则化后仍无界。对单个无质量标量，指定有界可观测量与正规态的高能极限由超有限连续核心（hyperfinite continuous core）描述。

Conclusion: 该权重条件不可放松，正热正则化不足以使低权重（0<d≤1/2）点场有界；高能极限得到的超有限连续核心并不保持精确代数的非超有限性，文中对此给出了解释。

Abstract: We construct the canonical boundary algebras of Jackiw--Teitelboim gravity coupled to free bosonic matter. For a nonempty finite or countable family of species with lowest weights greater than one half, the two boundary algebras are mutual commutants and nonhyperfinite type $\mathrm{II}_\infty$ factors. Their nonzero finite corners are nonhyperfinite type $\mathrm{II}_1$ factors. The construction uses bounded heat-regularized fields and a Hilbert algebra, which gives a faithful normal semifinite trace without a path-integral argument. The weight restriction is sharp: elementary point fields remain unbounded after positive heat regularization when $0<d\leq1/2$. For one massless scalar, specified bounded observables and normal states have a high energy limit described by a hyperfinite continuous core. We explain why this limit does not preserve the nonhyperfiniteness of the exact algebra.

</details>


### [163] [Metriplectic Lotka--Volterra systems with noise:interactions invisible at stationarity, visible in time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15632)
*J. A. Capitán,A. Megías,C. Sardón*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明：具内平衡点的 Lotka–Volterra 系统在 Volterra 耗散条件成立时，当且仅当具备以 Volterra 函数为能量、含环境熵变量的度规辛（metriplectic）结构，并显式构造之。经由随机 GENERIC 的涨落–耗散关系加噪后，平稳分布为独立 Gamma 分布的乘积型 Gibbs 测度，且该结论对非对称相互作用（含捕食–被捕食）同样成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微生物群落观测中常见的 Gamma 型、无相关丰度波动常被解读为物种间缺乏相互作用，该文旨在检验这一推断是否成立，并为随机 Lotka–Volterra 系统提供可解释的几何与统计力学框架。

Method: 采用度规辛（metriplectic）几何与对数规范（log-canonical）Poisson 双向量构造系统结构，并借助随机 GENERIC 的涨落–耗散（fluctuation–dissipation）关系引入乘性环境噪声；进一步用协方差恒等式与 Lyapunov 型代数关系刻画平稳性质与物种独立性。

Result: 平稳律仅由平衡点与 C/k 决定，维数为 n² 的开锥内任意相互作用矩阵（含任意 Poisson 部分）与其相容噪声产生与解耦随机 logistic 方程相同的分布；相互作用体现在时间维度：对数丰度协方差零延迟导数等于 A(C/k)⁻¹，其反对称部分即 Poisson 部分并承载全部平稳熵产生。对一般噪声，得到精确恒等式 AK+KᵀAᵀ+Q=0（K=Cov(x, log x)），并给出以 Q 到相容族距离表示的种间交叉协方差下界。

Conclusion: Gamma 型无相关丰度波动不能作为不存在相互作用的证据；相互作用可通过时间导数结构识别，其反对称部分对应熵产生。捕食–被捕食模型的模拟显示所给交叉协方差下界接近紧致。

Abstract: We show that a Lotka--Volterra system with an interior equilibrium admits a metriplectic structure, with a Volterra function $V_C$ as energy and an entropy variable for the environment, if and only if Volterra's dissipativity condition $CA+A^{\top}C\le0$ holds, and we construct it explicitly with a log-canonical Poisson bivector. Adding noise through the fluctuation--dissipation relation of stochastic GENERIC yields multiplicative environmental noise, with covariance fixed by the symmetric part of $AC^{-1}$, whose stationary law is an explicit Gibbs measure: a product of independent Gamma distributions. Unlike known Gibbs measures for stochastic Lotka--Volterra systems, this holds for nonsymmetric interactions, predator--prey included. The stationary law determines only the equilibrium and $C/k$: every interaction matrix in an open cone of dimension $n^2$, in particular any Poisson part, produces with its compatible noise the same law as uncoupled stochastic logistic equations. Gamma-distributed, uncorrelated abundance fluctuations, as observed in microbial communities, are therefore no evidence of absence of interactions. The interactions are, however, visible in time: the zero-lag derivative of the log-abundance covariance equals $A(C/k)^{-1}$, and its antisymmetric part is the Poisson part, which carries all the stationary entropy production, computed in closed form. Finally, for general noise we prove an exact Lyapunov-type identity $AK+K^\top A^\top+Q=0$ for $K=\mathrm{Cov}(x,\log x)$ at any stationary solution. It implies that the populations are independent if and only if the noise is metriplectically compatible, and gives an explicit lower bound on interspecies cross-covariances in terms of the distance of $Q$ to the compatible family. Simulations of a predator--prey model show that the bound is nearly sharp.

</details>


### [164] [Point symmetries and Lie reductions of Kadomtsev-Petviashvili equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15833)
*Yevhenii Yu. Chapovskyi,Serhii D. Koval*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用直接法与代数法计算 Kadomtsev-Petviashvili 方程（KP 方程）的点对称伪群（point symmetry pseudogroup），并重新审视其经典 Lie 对称分析，重点讨论隐藏无穷小对称与隐藏点对称，同时确定各余维一约化模型的点对称伪群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅表明对 KP 方程的对称性进行系统性的再考察与补充计算。

Method: 采用直接法（direct method）与代数法（algebraic method）两种途径计算点对称伪群，并对模型重新进行经典 Lie 对称分析（classical Lie symmetry analysis），聚焦隐藏无穷小对称（hidden infinitesimal symmetries）与隐藏点对称（hidden point symmetries）。

Result: 获得了 KP 方程的点对称伪群，识别出隐藏无穷小对称与隐藏点对称，并确定了各个余维一约化模型（codimension-one reduced models）的点对称伪群。

Conclusion: KP 方程的对称结构在直接法与代数法下得到一致刻画，隐藏对称性及约化模型的点对称伪群构成该方程对称性分析的重要补充。

Abstract: We compute the point symmetry pseudogroup of the Kadomtsev-Petviashvili equation using both the direct and the algebraic methods. We also revisit the classical Lie symmetry analysis of this model in detail, concentrating on hidden infinitesimal and hidden point symmetries. Additionally, we re-examine the symmetry properties of each codimension-one reduced model and determine their point symmetry pseudogroups.

</details>


### [165] [Fréchet structure over AF algebras I: The compact case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16021)
*Giuseppe De Nittis,Julián Gómez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文是两篇系列论文中的第一篇，研究 AF 代数上的空间 Fréchet 结构（spatial Fréchet structures）及其导子（derivations）。以固定正交基下几乎局部紧算子（almost-local compact operators）构成的 Fréchet 代数为例，重新刻画其连续导子并给出连续性判据，同时构造出一个不生成强连续单参数自同构群的对称导子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为 AF 代数上的空间 Fréchet 结构及其导子理论奠定基础，因此选择该结构中最简单的例子——与固定正交基相伴的几乎局部紧算子 Fréchet 代数——作为切入点进行系统考察。

Method: 方法上，借助无限矩阵及导子在速降向量（rapidly decreasing vectors）上的算子论实现来重新刻画连续导子；并引入量子多体系统（quantum many-body systems）理论中的 0-链（0-chains）语言重新表述该描述。

Result: 以空间增长（spatial growth）为条件，建立了导子连续性的一个充分条件；并构造出一个 Fréchet-连续的对称导子（symmetric derivation），该导子不生成关于 Fréchet 拓扑强连续的单参数自同构群（one-parameter group of automorphisms）。

Conclusion: 该 Fréchet 代数上存在 Fréchet-连续对称导子，但其生成的自同构群不具备强连续性，表明连续性假设不足以保证动力学生成的强连续性；这为后续论文中更一般的空间 Fréchet 结构研究提供了基础性反例与框架。

Abstract: This is the first of two papers devoted to spatial Fréchet structures on AF algebras and their derivations. Here, we study the simplest example of such a structure: the Fréchet algebra of almost-local compact operators associated with a fixed orthonormal basis. We revisit the characterization of its continuous derivations in terms of infinite matrices and their operator-theoretic implementation on rapidly decreasing vectors. Reformulating this description in the language of 0-chains, borrowed from the theory of quantum many-body systems, we establish a sufficient condition for continuity in terms of spatial growth. Finally, we construct a Fréchet-continuous symmetric derivation that does not generate a one-parameter group of automorphisms of this algebra that is strongly continuous with respect to its Fréchet topology.

</details>


### [166] [Baxter $\mathbb{Q}$-operator and Separation of Variables for BC-type open $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16105)
*I. Kotikov,P. Antonenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文为非紧致开边界 $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ BC 型自旋链构造双参数 Baxter $\mathbb{Q}$-算子，证明传递矩阵可分解为单参数 $Q$-算子之积，并用量子分离变量法（separation of variables, SoV）导出 Baxter 方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非紧致开边界 $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ BC 型自旋链（含一般边界相互作用）的可积结构，需要构造相应 Baxter $\mathbb{Q}$-算子并建立传递矩阵的本征值问题框架。

Method: 以主系列表示（principal series representation）的无穷维辅助空间上一般单值矩阵（monodromy matrix）之迹定义双参数 $\mathbb{Q}$-算子；用基于局域算子恒等式的代数推导与费曼图（Feynman diagram）图解两种方式证明分解关系；并对单边界传递矩阵应用量子分离变量法。

Result: 双参数 $\mathbb{Q}$-算子（$\mathbb{Q}$-operator）在特定参数值退化为更简单的单参数 Baxter 算子；一般传递矩阵（transfer matrix）可分解为这些单参数 $Q$-算子之积；SoV 给出本征函数的分离差分方程组，在核分解（kernel factorization）假设下化为一维差分方程，且与适当归一化单参数 Baxter $Q$-算子本征值的 Baxter 方程一致。

Conclusion: 结果为非紧致开边界 BC 型自旋链提供了 Baxter $\mathbb{Q}$-算子构造与传递矩阵分解的严格表述，并将谱问题纳入 SoV 与 Baxter 方程框架，为后续本征值求解奠定基础。

Abstract: We construct two-parameter Baxter $\mathbb{Q}$-operators for the transfer matrices of noncompact open $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ BC-type spin chains with general boundary interactions, treating both one-boundary and two-boundary models. The $\mathbb{Q}$-operators are defined as traces of general monodromy matrices over infinite-dimensional auxiliary spaces carrying principal series representations of $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$.
  At specific values of parameters the two-parameter $\mathbb{Q}$-operators degenerate into simpler one-parameter Baxter operators. We prove that the general transfer matrices factorize into products of these one-parameter $Q$-operators. We provide both an algebraic derivation based on local operator identities and a diagrammatic proof using Feynman diagrammatic techniques.
  Finally, we apply the quantum Separation of Variables (SoV) method to the spectral problem of the one-boundary transfer matrix. We derive the system of separated difference equations for the transfer matrix eigenfunctions and show that, under the assumption of kernel factorization, it reduces to a one-dimensional difference equation coinciding precisely with the Baxter equation for eigenvalues of appropriately normalized one-parameter Baxter $Q$-operator.

</details>


### [167] [A Probabilistic Characterization of the Ideals of Resolvent Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16133)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文借助Klein–Landau关于KMS系统与随机过程的对应，将预解代数（resolvent algebras）的理想（ideals）刻画为单粒子空间子空间对应的σ-代数，从而给出理想的概率论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预解代数理想此前主要通过单粒子空间的子空间来描述，缺少概率论层面的直观刻画；本文旨在为这些理想提供概率论特征化。

Method: 利用Klein与Landau建立的KMS系统与随机过程之间的对应（correspondence），把各向同性子空间（isotropic subspace）的场视为可交换随机变量，由此将子空间描述转为σ-代数（σ-algebras）描述。

Result: 由子空间Y之外的预解式生成的理想对应Y上场的σ-代数；理想之间的包含关系与σ-代数的包含关系相反；独立性（independence）由条件期望（conditional expectations）刻画。

Conclusion: 预解代数的理想结构可在概率论框架下等价转述为σ-代数结构，包含关系反转与独立性刻画均由条件期望等概率概念自然表述。

Abstract: We give a probabilistic characterization of the ideals of resolvent algebras. Through the correspondence of Klein and Landau between KMS systems and stochastic processes, the fields of an isotropic subspace become commuting random variables, and the description of ideals by subspaces of the one-particle space becomes a description by $σ$-algebras: the ideal generated by the resolvents outside a subspace $Y$ corresponds to the $σ$-algebra of the fields on $Y$, inclusions of the ideals reverse those of the $σ$-algebras, and independence is characterized by conditional expectations.

</details>


### [168] [Intrinsic Chiral HOTI Stabilized by a Single Diagonal Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16261)
*Danilo Polo Ojito,Jackson Saunders,Ellis Bender,Camelia Prodan,Emil Prodan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种理论判据，用以论证在对角反射点对称（point symmetry）与手性对称（chiral symmetry）共同约束下，四分之一平面几何中存在且仅存在一种本征高阶拓扑相；并给出最小紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）及其声学晶体（acoustic crystal）实现，通过COMSOL仿真验证角模（corner mode）的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶拓扑绝缘体与超导体（higher-order topological insulators and superconductors）在点对称与基本对称性联合下可支持高余维边界模式，如角模；但针对特定几何与对称性约束下本征高阶拓扑相的分类与唯一性尚缺乏明确的理论判据，以及可实现的物理平台。

Method: 构建一套理论工具，用于判定四分之一平面几何（quarter-plane geometry）、以角对角线反射为点对称且具手性对称的本征高阶拓扑相的唯一性；在此基础上构造最小紧束缚模型，并设计动力学等价于该模型的声学晶体，最后用COMSOL仿真考察角模的鲁棒性质。

Result: 理论上论证了该几何与对称性条件下存在“唯一”一个本征手性对称高阶拓扑相；给出了可描述该相的最小紧束缚模型，并设计了能够复现该模型动力学的声学晶体；COMSOL仿真显示角模具有鲁棒特性。

Conclusion: 该工作为手性对称高阶拓扑相提供了唯一性判据与最小模型，并借助声学晶体平台实现，表明角模可在实际声学系统中稳健存在，为后续理论与实验研究提供了参考框架。

Abstract: Higher-order topological insulators and superconductors, under combined point and fundamental symmetries, display topological modes supported by boundaries of higher codimensions, such as a corner mode. In this work, we explain a theoretical device that enables us to claim that there exists {\it one and only one} intrinsic chiral-symmetric higher-order topological phase for the quarter-plane geometry with point-symmetry given by reflection about the diagonal of the corner. Furthermore, we supply a minimal tight-binding model of this phase and design an acoustic crystal whose dynamics reproduce that of the model. The robust properties of the corner mode are investigated by COMSOL simulations.

</details>


### [169] [Physics-constrained differentiable causal discovery of constitutive mechanisms through boundary value problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16468)
*Michael A. Kraus*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 将数据驱动的本构模型发现重构为基于时间展开结构因果模型（time-unrolled structural causal models）的机制父集因果发现；无需测量应力或内变量。在含13个候选边、8个真实边的湿热黏弹性损伤板合成算例中，干预数据10次运行有7次精确恢复真实图且归因误差为零；纯观测数据（温湿相关-0.998）则完全失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据驱动本构发现依赖应力或内变量的测量，且仅用观测数据在强混淆（如温湿高度相关）下易得到错误机制归因。研究旨在把本构发现转化为因果发现问题，以干预数据和可识别性证书规避混淆偏差。

Method: 硬编码平衡律与本构骨架，候选图通过弱形式平衡残差（weak-form equilibrium gaps）对全场位移评分环境—机制与状态—状态边；用自适应lasso与NOTEARS型约束保证无环性和显式求值顺序，将气候箱协议视为干预；自动微分给出Gauss–Newton矩阵作为局部可识别性证书，驱动贪心D-最优试验设计。

Result: 干预数据（0.01px位移噪声）10次中7次精确恢复真实图，归因误差为零；观测数据因分数平坦250–380倍而完全失败。观测发现模型在训练气候下误差3.6%，但在高温高湿气候外推误差最高41.5%（平均16.9%），干预发现模型保持在3.2%以内；证书可预测归因误差，D-最优设计在126个候选试验集中排名第二或第三。

Conclusion: 干预式因果发现结合弱形式评分与可识别性证书，可在不测量应力与内变量的条件下可靠识别本构机制，并支持实验设计；弱形式评分的噪声偏差是主要局限，观测混淆会显著损害机制归因与外推能力。

Abstract: Data-driven constitutive model discovery is recast as causal discovery of mechanism parent sets using time-unrolled structural causal models. Hard-coding balance laws and constitutive skeletons, candidate graphs score environment--mechanism and state--state edges via weak-form equilibrium gaps evaluated from full-field displacements, avoiding the need to measure stresses or internal variables. An adaptive lasso and a NOTEARS-type constraint ensure graph acyclicity and an explicit evaluation order, treating climate-chamber protocols as interventions. Automatic differentiation yields the score's Gauss--Newton matrix as a local identifiability certificate, driving greedy D-optimal test design. In a synthetic study on a hygro-thermo-viscoelastic damaging plate (8 true edges among 13 candidates), interventional data recover the exact true graph in 7 of 10 runs (0.01px displacement noise) with zero attribution error. Conversely, observational data with a temperature--moisture correlation of $-0.998$ fail entirely because confounded scores are 250--380 times flatter. Observationally discovered models reproduce training climates (3.6% error) but mispredict hot and humid climates by up to 41.5% (16.9% average), whereas interventionally discovered models stay within 3.2%. A correlation sweep shows the certificate successfully predicts attribution errors, though weak-form score noise bias remains a primary limitation. Computed without knowledge of the truth, the D-optimal design selects test sets ranked second or third out of 126 candidates.

</details>


### [170] [Fluctuations of Local Patterns in Non-Periodic Uniquely Ergodic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16685)
*Jacek Miękisz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究正则图上符号构型的非周期系统，聚焦于基态构型在相互作用微扰下的稳定性，并提出刻画有限模式有界涨落的"严格边界性质（Strict Boundary Property）"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非周期结构（如镶嵌、替换系统、Sturmian系统）的唯一遍历性（unique ergodicity）其稳定性问题此前缺乏统一刻画，作者希望用能量极小化视角理解这类结构的刚性（rigidity）。

Method: 将符号视为粒子，研究使相互作用能量极小化的基态构型（ground-state configurations），并引入严格边界性质（Strict Boundary Property）以描述非周期构型有限模式的有界涨落，据此讨论稳定性。

Result: 论文讨论了严格边界性质与非周期结构稳定性之间的关联，并以Robinson镶嵌（Robinson's tilings）、Thue-Morse系统与Sturmian系统为例进行展示。

Conclusion: 严格边界性质被视为判断非周期唯一遍历系统稳定性的相关判据，作者据此提出了若干开放问题（open problems）。

Abstract: We study various families of non-periodic configurations of symbols assigned to vertices of regular graphs. We restrict ourselves to such systems where considered configurations support a uniquely ergodic measure. Symbols can be interpreted as particles and we are concerned with configurations which minimize the interaction energy of particles, the so-called ground-state configurations. One of the main questions then is whether ground-state configurations are stable with respect to small perturbations of interactions. Such stability may be interpreted as a sort of rigidity of non-periodic structures such as tilings, substitution systems, Sturmian systems, and other uniquely ergodic non-periodic systems. We introduce a property of bounded fluctuations of finite patterns of non-periodic configurations, the so-called Strict Boundary Property. We discuss how relevant this property is for stability of non-periodic structures. Examples of Robinson's tilings, Thue-Morse, and Sturmian systems are presented. Some open problems are formulated.

</details>


### [171] [Finite Bodies on Norton's Dome: Escape-Time Divergence and a Center-of-Mass Barrier Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16797)
*Guan-Chuan Chen,En-Jui Kuo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 将 Norton 穹顶（Norton's dome）中的质点替换为有限尺寸刚体后，在给定几何条件下顶点处的自发离开静止被排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Norton 穹顶说明牛顿力学初值问题可同时允许静止与延迟离开解，论文旨在考察质点理想化被正半径刚球及更一般有限体替代时，这种非唯一性（nonuniqueness）是否仍然成立。

Method: 对广义 Norton 穹顶族，证明任意固定正球半径均阻止顶点处自发离开；再对更一般物体给出两个充分条件：质心与顶点距离的几何约束，以及质心与初始接触点竖直对齐时的内部切球条件（interior tangent ball condition）；论证结合几何估计、能量不等式与 Grönwall 不等式。

Result: 固定正半径足以排除自发运动；两个充分条件同样保证不自发离开，其中第一条件亦适用于无可释放内能的被动可变形体（passive deformable bodies）。

Conclusion: 有限体几何在显式条件下排除了 Norton 质点模型所允许的自发运动，说明该非唯一性对质点理想化具有敏感性。

Abstract: Norton's dome illustrates how a Newtonian initial-value problem can admit both rest and delayed departure from the same initial state. We examine how this nonuniqueness changes when the point particle is replaced by a rigid sphere of positive radius. For a generalized family of Norton's domes, we show that any fixed positive sphere radius prevents spontaneous departure from rest at the apex. We then extend the analysis to more general bodies and establish two sufficient conditions: a geometric constraint on the distance between the center of mass and the apex, and an interior tangent ball condition for rigid bodies whose center of mass is vertically aligned with the initial contact point. Both arguments combine geometric estimates with an energy inequality and Grönwall's inequality. The first condition also applies to passive deformable bodies without releasable internal energy. These results identify explicit conditions under which finite-body geometry excludes the spontaneous motion allowed by Norton's point-particle model.

</details>


### [172] [Concentration of Quasimodes at Spherical Interface between 3D-Topological Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16809)
*Víctor Arnaiz,Éric Vacelet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在半经典狄拉克方程（semiclassical Dirac equation）的连续模型框架下，研究两种三维拓扑绝缘体（3D-topological insulators）球形界面上的边缘态（edge states），刻画界面处准模的半经典测度的集中性与不变性，并显式构造集中于球面特定周期测地线的准模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多关注平面或一般界面上的边缘态，而球形界面上的边缘态及其半经典测度行为缺乏系统描述；本文旨在从连续半经典狄拉克模型出发，刻画界面局域准模的谱与动力学性质。

Method: 采用半经典狄拉克方程的连续模型，利用与界面相切的半经典狄拉克算子（semiclassical Dirac operator tangent to the interface）的动力学与谱性质，并结合横向能量变量的朗道能级分层（Landau level stratification）进行分析。

Result: 给出了与界面局域准模相关的半经典测度（semiclassical measures）的若干集中性（concentration）与不变性（invariance）性质，并显式构造了满足其中部分性质、且集中于球面某些单一周期测地线（periodic geodesics）的准模。

Conclusion: 球形界面边缘态的半经典测度受切向狄拉克算子动力学与朗道能级分层的共同约束，且可由显式准模在特定周期测地线上实现集中，为界面边缘态的量子遍历性类问题提供了模型层面的刻画。

Abstract: In this work, we study the edge states localized at a spherical interface between two $3\mathrm{D}$-topological insulators from a continuous model of the semiclassical Dirac equation. Using the dynamical and spectral properties of the semiclassical Dirac operator tangent to the interface and the Landau level stratification of the energy in the transverse variable, we describe several concentration and invariance properties of the semiclassical measures associated with quasimodes of the system localized at the interface. Moreover, we construct explicit quasimodes which fulfill some of these properties and concentrate on some particular single periodic geodesics of the sphere.

</details>


### [173] [Spin-Boson Hamiltonians as Generators of Spin-Dressed Bogoliubov Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17025)
*Nathan Metraud*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文将 Bach–Lieb–Solovej 关于二次哈密顿量（quadratic Hamiltonians）的动力学刻画推广到广义自旋–玻色子哈密顿量（generalized spin–boson Hamiltonians），借助量子朗之万方程（quantum Langevin equation）与正则尺度（regularity scale）给出自旋修饰实现（spin-dressed implementation）的严格框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作将二次哈密顿量定义为实现 Bogoliubov 变换单参数群的酉群的生成元，但缺乏对自旋–玻色子系统的类似动力学刻画；单自旋能级与多自旋能级情形存在本质差异，需要新的实现概念。

Method: 对单自旋能级，回顾 Guichardet 与 Dereziński 的结果：van Hove 哈密顿量（含重整化版本）恰为不混合产生湮灭算符的仿射 Bogoliubov 动力学生成元；对多自旋能级，引入自旋修饰实现，即自由场流在有界算符值位移意义下实现，位移衰减速率由正则尺度衡量、其时间零处导数为耦合映射（coupling map），并满足量子朗之万方程。

Result: 单体哈密顿量 Υ 与耦合映射由哈密顿量决定；Fock 空间中最细尺度、形状因子（form factor）属于 D(Υ^{-1/2}) 的自旋修饰实现恰为广义自旋–玻色子哈密顿量，且唯一到自旋哈密顿量；最粗尺度下，具紫外奇异形状因子与任意隧穿（tunnelling）的两能级系统重整化自旋–玻色子哈密顿量是自旋修饰实现，且该定义本身在耦合映射可积的检验函数上给出渐近 Weyl 算符，标准假设下该集合稠密。

Conclusion: 自旋修饰实现为广义自旋–玻色子哈密顿量提供了统一且动力学化的刻画，可覆盖从最细到最粗正则尺度（包括重整化情形）的一族模型，并为渐近 Weyl 算符的存在性提供定义层面的依据。

Abstract: Following Bach, Lieb and Solovej, quadratic Hamiltonians can be defined as generators of unitary groups implementing one-parameter groups of Bogoliubov transformations. We develop an analogous dynamical definition of generalized spin--boson Hamiltonians. For a single spin level, we review results of Guichardet and Dereziński: the van Hove Hamiltonians, including the renormalized ones, are exactly the generators of affine Bogoliubov dynamics that do not mix creation and annihilation operators. For several spin levels, there is no analogue of this group, and we introduce spin-dressed implementations, that is to say the free flow of the fields is implemented up to bounded operator-valued displacements, which vanish at a rate measured by a so-called regularity scale and have a spin observable, the coupling map, as derivative at time zero. The one-particle Hamiltonian $Υ$ and the coupling map are determined by the Hamiltonian, and the field obeys a quantum Langevin equation. Spin-dressed implementations in Fock space, at the finest scale and for form factors in $D(Υ^{-1/2})$, are exactly the generalized spin--boson Hamiltonians, unique up to the spin Hamiltonian. At the roughest scale, the renormalized spin--boson Hamiltonian of a two-level system with ultraviolet-singular form factor and arbitrary tunnelling is a spin-dressed implementation, and the definition alone yields asymptotic Weyl operators on the test functions along which the coupling map is integrable, a dense set under standard assumptions.

</details>


### [174] [Generalized ladder operators and construction of families of superintegrable systems with Painlevé VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17117)
*Ian Marquette,Anthony J. L. Parr*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了以第六类 Painlevé 超越函数（Painlevé VI transcendent）为角向分量的量子哈密顿量的广义升降算符，并将其与径向移位算符组合，生成高阶积分与形变 Racah 型多项式代数，从而得到含 Painlevé VI 势的新一类 TTW/PW 型超可积模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的出发点是探索角向分量为一般 Painlevé VI 超越函数的量子哈密顿量是否仍具备可积结构，并推广 Tremblay-Turbiner-Winternitz（TTW）与 Post-Winternitz（PW）型高阶超可积框架。

Method: 对具有二次谱变量的一维 Schrödinger 算符构造广义升降算符（ladder operators）；对 Painlevé VI 角向哈密顿量，这些算符满足 [N, J±] = ±2kJ±（N = √M）并通过八次范数多项式闭合。再将角向移位与谐振子、Coulomb 及自由 Bessel 系统的径向移位算符结合，并借助 Gromak–Laine–Shimomura 收集的显式特解族分析特殊解扇区。

Result: 构造给出含 Painlevé VI 势、具有任意高阶积分的新 TTW/PW 型哈密顿量族，并可将三阶与四阶积分分类中的模型作为特例回收。对代表性谐振子扩展显式计算了 Casimir 量并由形变谐振子实现恢复有限维能谱；所得势被归入少量谱类，且明确区分升降范数的形式零点与物理自伴表示。正则非经典有理例子为三角 Pöschl–Teller 系统的有理 Darboux 形变，导致严格等谱的例外 Jacobi 扇区或含孤立角向能级的加态表示；奇异分支产生内部反平方势垒与分裂定义域问题。另对一正则例外扇区与两个加态扇区给出了多项式代数。

Conclusion: 以 Painlevé VI 超越函数作为角向分量可系统生成高阶超可积模型，并统一涵盖已知三、四阶积分模型；其特解扇区可组织为少数谱类，对应等谱例外 Jacobi 扇区、加态表示及分裂定义域问题，为后续谱代数分析提供框架。

Abstract: We study quantum Hamiltonians whose separated angular component is expressed through a generic Painlevé VI transcendent. We construct generalized ladder operators for one-dimensional Schrödinger operators with a quadratic spectral variable. For the Painlevé VI angular Hamiltonian these operators satisfy $[N,J_\pm]=\pm2kJ_\pm$, where $N=\sqrt M$, and close through an eighth-degree norm polynomial. Combining the angular shifts with the radial shift operators of the oscillator, Coulomb and free Bessel systems yields higher-order integrals and deformed Racah-type polynomial algebras for the two-dimensional superintegrable models. This construction provides new families of Tremblay-Turbiner-Winternitz (TTW)- and Post-Winternitz (PW)-like Hamiltonians with integrals of arbitrarily high order and Painlevé VI in the potential. This framework also recovers models from the classification of third- and fourth-order integrals as particular cases. For a representative oscillator extension we compute the Casimir explicitly and recover the finite-dimensional energy spectrum from a deformed-oscillator realization. We then analyze the special-solution sector using the explicit families collected by Gromak, Laine and Shimomura. The resulting potentials are organized into a small number of spectral classes, and the distinction between formal zeros of the ladder norm and physical self-adjoint representations is made explicit. The regular nonclassical rational examples are rational Darboux deformations of the trigonometric Pöschl--Teller system and lead to strictly isospectral exceptional-Jacobi sectors or to state-adding representations with an isolated angular level. A singular branch produces an interior inverse-square barrier and a split-domain problem. For one regular exceptional sector and two state-adding sectors we display the polynomial algebras.

</details>


### [175] [Existence of a fluid phase for the two-dimensional one-component plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17174)
*Jeanne Boursier,Thomas Leblé*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明在逆温度 β≤2 时二维单组分等离子体（two-dimensional one-component plasma）的关联函数呈指数衰减、Gibbs 态在涨落受控的点过程类中唯一，从而给出无限体积极限（推广的 Ginibre 点过程 Gin_β）的存在性及 Debye 尺度下两点截断函数的一阶 Debye–Hückel 展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学家预测二维单组分等离子体存在流体相（fluid phase），但其严格数学刻画（关联衰减、Gibbs 态唯一性、无限体积极限）此前尚未建立，作者旨在为这一预测提供部分严格证明。

Method: 研究对象为点电荷间呈对数排斥（logarithmic repulsion）、具均匀中和背景的二维点过程；在逆温度（inverse temperature）β≤2 的条件下，于涨落受控（controlled fluctuations）的点过程范围内证明关联的指数衰减（exponential decay）与 Gibbs 态的唯一性（uniqueness）。

Result: 证明 β≤2 时关联函数指数衰减且 Gibbs 态唯一；由此得到无限体积极限（infinite-volume limit）的存在性，该极限推广了 Ginibre 点过程（Ginibre point process），记为 Gin_β；并证明在 Debye 长度（Debye length）尺度上，其截断两点函数（truncated two-point function）在 β 的一阶近似下由 Debye–Hückel 近似给出。

Conclusion: 结果构成物理预测的流体相的一部分严格论证，确立了 β≤2 区间内二维单组分等离子体的良定义无限体积热力学极限与一阶屏蔽行为（Debye–Hückel 近似），并将 Ginibre 点过程纳入统一的 Gin_β 族。

Abstract: The two-dimensional one-component plasma is a system of point charges that repel each other logarithmically, in a uniform neutralizing background. When the inverse temperature $β$ is at most $2$, we prove that its correlations decay exponentially, and that its Gibbs state is unique among point processes whose fluctuations are controlled. This constitutes part of the fluid phase predicted by physicists. As a consequence, we obtain the existence of an infinite-volume limit, extending the Ginibre point process, which we call $\mathrm{Gin}_β$. We show that its truncated two-point function, at the scale of the Debye length, is given to first order in $β$ by the Debye-Hückel approximation.

</details>


<div id='math.DS'></div>

# math.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Visibility graphs of irregularly sampled two-dimensional fields via Voronoi tessellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16336)
*Francisco J. Muñoz,Juan Carlos Nuño*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该文将可见性图（visibility graph）推广到平面内任意不规则散点采样的标量数据，借助Voronoi胞元构造水平型与自然型Voronoi可见性图，并给出解析的连边概率、度分布及平均度结果，指出二维是各向同性水平可见性丧失局域性的临界维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的二维可见性图推广仅限于规则格点（regular lattices），可见性仅沿有限格点方向判定，无法处理实际中常见的不规则散点采样数据，因此需要一个适用于平面任意散点的定义。

Method: 为每个散点赋予其Voronoi胞元（Voronoi cell），使数据构成棱柱景观；当两点连线穿过胞元时视线不被遮挡则连边，由此定义水平Voronoi可见性图（HVGV）与自然Voronoi可见性图（NVGV），并可通过Delaunay三角剖分（Delaunay triangulation）上的精确行走算法构造。

Result: 该构造在点共线时精确退化为经典水平与自然可见性图，且包含点集的Gabriel图（Gabriel graph）。对不相关高度，水平图仅依赖数据秩，连边概率为2/[(M+1)(M+2)]（M为穿过的Voronoi胞元数）；对Poisson点得到无标度尾部P(k)≃(π³/4)k⁻²及随ln N增长的平均度(π³/8)ln N，与一维序列的⟨k⟩=4形成对比。

Conclusion: 二维是各向同性水平可见性由局域转向非局域的临界（marginal）情形；度分布与平均度的解析预测经最多N=1.28×10⁵个点的数值模拟验证，表明该框架可将问题化归为Voronoi剖分的几何性质。

Abstract: Visibility graphs map a time series into a network by linking two data whenever the straight line between them is not obstructed by intermediate data. Their extension to two-dimensional fields has so far been restricted to regular lattices, where visibility is checked along a finite number of lattice directions. We propose a definition for scalar data sampled at arbitrary, irregularly scattered points of the plane. Each point is assigned its Voronoi cell, so that the data become a landscape of prisms, and two points are linked when the line of sight between them clears the cells crossed by the connecting segment. This yields horizontal ($\HVGV$) and natural ($\NVGV$) Voronoi visibility graphs that reduce exactly to the classical horizontal and natural visibility graphs when the points are collinear, contain the Gabriel graph of the point set, and can be built by an exact walk through the Delaunay triangulation. For uncorrelated heights, the horizontal graph depends only on the ranks of the data, so its statistics are independent of the marginal distribution. The probability that two points see each other is $2/[(M+1)(M+2)]$, where $M$ is the number of Voronoi cells crossed, which reduces the problem to the geometry of the tessellation. For Poisson points this gives a degree distribution with a scale-free tail $P(k)\simeq(π^3/4)\,k^{-2}$ and a mean degree that grows as $(π^3/8)\ln N$, in contrast with the finite value $\langle k\rangle=4$ of one-dimensional series. Two dimensions are therefore the marginal case in which isotropic horizontal visibility ceases to be local. Numerical simulations with up to $N=1.28\times10^5$ points support these predictions.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [New Physics in Ultra-High-Energy Neutrino Flavor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14624)
*Samiur R. Mir,Carlos A. Argüelles,Vedran Brdar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次评估下一代超高能（UHE）中微子味组成测量对新物理的探测能力，以洛伦兹不变性破坏（LIV）为基准，发现其对四维及以上算符的LIV灵敏度较现有各类限制最多提升约二十个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子味测量可检验修改中微子产生、传播或探测的标准模型之外物理；现有IceCube测量仅覆盖至PeV能区，而LIV效应随中微子能量幂次增长，因此有必要评估延伸至UHE能区的新物理探测潜力。

Method: 结合冰内无线电探测（in-ice radio detection）与地球掠射陶中微子（Earth-skimming tau neutrinos），将味测量推广至100 PeV–10 EeV能区；以LIV为基准案例，计算投影的UHE味测量对新物理的灵敏度，并比较三味观测量与仅用地球掠射陶中微子的效果。

Result: UHE味比值是四维及以上维度算符LIV的最灵敏探针，较所有扇区的现有界限最多改善二十个数量级；三味观测量相对仅用地球掠射陶中微子可将灵敏度再提升最多一个数量级。

Conclusion: 下一代UHE中微子味测量有望显著拓展对新物理（尤其LIV）的探测范围，且充分利用三味信息对提升灵敏度至关重要。

Abstract: Neutrino flavor measurements test physics beyond the Standard Model that modifies neutrino production, propagation, or detection. IceCube has measured the flavor composition of the astrophysical neutrino flux up to PeV energies. Next-generation experiments will extend flavor measurements to the ultra-high-energy (UHE) regime, $100\,\mathrm{PeV}$-$10\,\mathrm{EeV}$, by combining in-ice radio detection with Earth-skimming tau neutrinos. We present the first study of the new-physics reach of these projected UHE flavor measurements. We take Lorentz invariance violation (LIV) as a benchmark, since its effects grow as a power of the neutrino energy. We find that UHE flavor ratios will be the most sensitive probe of LIV for operators of dimension four and higher, improving on current bounds from all sectors by up to twenty orders of magnitude. Three-flavor observables improve this sensitivity by up to an order of magnitude over Earth-skimming tau neutrinos alone.

</details>


### [178] [Quantum Computing Enhanced Search for Wavelike Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14629)
*Yunjia Bao,Yu Cheng,Liang Jiang,Lian-Tao Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于Grover搜索算法的量子计算增强探测方案，用于识别宽频带内的弱随机信号，并针对轴子（axion）暗物质给出超越现有天体物理限制的投影灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在寻找波状暗物质（wavelike dark matter）时，核心难题是从宽频带中识别微弱且随机的信号；传统方法受限于信噪比与积分时间，因此需要量子计算增强的传感手段。

Method: 采用基于Grover搜索算法的量子信号处理（quantum signal processing, QSP）增强传感；利用长积分时间稳定累积的传感相位以降低暗物质涨落引起的QSP误差，并令各oracle调用之间的QSP角度符号交替以抑制内禀QSP近似误差的相干累积；在transmon平台上进行基准测试。

Result: 在transmon基准下，投影灵敏度超过现有天体物理限制，可覆盖10–100 μeV质量范围内大量未探索参数空间；当使用10^6个纠缠传感量子比特时，投影灵敏度可达QCD轴子带（QCD axion band）。

Conclusion: 该协议为波状暗物质探测提供了加速发现通道，且虽以transmon为例演示，但可移植至其他实验平台。

Abstract: Identifying a weak, stochastic signal hidden within a broad frequency band is a central challenge in the search for wavelike dark matter. We explore quantum computing enhanced sensing based on the Grover search algorithm to address this problem and find that a long integration time stabilizes the accumulated sensing phase, reducing quantum signal processing (QSP) errors induced by dark matter fluctuations. We further propose alternating the signs of the QSP angles between oracle calls to suppress the coherent accumulation of the intrinsic QSP approximation errors during the search. Combining these strategies in a transmon-based benchmark, we obtain a projected sensitivity to axion dark matter beyond existing astrophysical limits. This opens access to substantial unexplored parameter space across the $10$-$100\,μ\mathrm{eV}$ mass range and offers an accelerated discovery channel for wavelike dark matter. With $10^6$ entangled sensing qubits, the projected sensitivity reaches the QCD axion band. Although illustrated with transmons, the protocol can be adapted to other experimental platforms for wavelike dark matter detection.

</details>


### [179] [A Grover-Accelerated Search for Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14641)
*Zachary Bogorad,Roni Harnik*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出利用类波暗物质（wave-like dark matter）在量子传感中充当可重复的量子相位门（quantum phase gate），并可借助 Grover 算法将多频段暗物质搜索加速约 $\sqrt{N_{\rm bins}}$ 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类波暗物质的相干时间（coherence time）$\tau$ 有限，传统上限制了单次测量内可积累的相干信号；作者希望说明在长于 $\tau$ 的积分时间内仍可实现相干探测，并寻找量子计算加速搜索的途径。

Method: 将类波暗物质经由色散耦合（dispersive coupling）在传感器上留下的 $O(1)$ 相干相位，视为可重复的量子相位门；把传感器制备于频率叠加态（superposition of frequencies），将该相位转移至量子比特寄存器（qubit register）并数字化，随后用量子计算处理，并采用 Grover 算法在 $N_{\rm bins}$ 个频率格中搜索。

Result: Grover 算法可使 $N_{\rm bins}$ 个频率格上的暗物质搜索获得 $\sqrt{N_{\rm bins}}$ 量级的加速；同时论证暗物质可作为相干门参与量子计算处理。

Conclusion: 尽管实际实现仍面临重大技术挑战，但结果表明，跨越多个相干时间、以量子计算技术加速类波暗物质搜索不存在根本性障碍（no fundamental obstruction）。

Abstract: We show that Grover's algorithm can be used to parametrically accelerate wave-like dark matter searches. Despite having a finite coherence time $τ$, wavelike dark matter can imprint a coherent $O(1)$ phase onto a sensor through a dispersive coupling over an integration time which significantly exceeds $τ$. As such, we show that dark matter can be used as a repeatable quantum phase gate in a quantum device. If the sensor is prepared in a superposition of frequencies, we show that this phase can be transferred to a qubit register and digitized. The qubits are thus available for quantum computational processing in which dark matter can take part as a coherent gate. We show that Grover's algorithm can be used to speed up a search for dark matter in $N_{\rm bins}$ frequency bins by a factor of $\sqrt{N_{\rm bins}}$. Although the technological challenges to implementing such a search remain substantial, this demonstrates that there is no fundamental obstruction to accelerating wave-like dark matter searches with quantum computational techniques over many coherence times.

</details>


### [180] [Earth-sized tests of long-range neutrino interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14642)
*Dyer F. Cardenas,Mauricio Bustamante*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文精确计算了地球中微子传播路径上由超轻中性玻色子（ultralight neutral boson）介导的味依赖势（flavor-dependent potential），并用于IceCube DeepCore 8年公开数据的分析，对14种$U(1)'$对称性中8种给出90%置信水平的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子可能感受一种区分其味（flavor）的新长程力，由新$U(1)'$规范对称性的超轻中性玻色子传递，源为普通物质的电子与核子。当力程约等于或小于一个地球半径时，该势沿中微子路径逐点变化，而此前的工作将其近似为路径常数或随局部物质密度变化，因而需要更精确的处理。

Method: 采用IceCube分析所用的地球模型，在每条路径的每一点精确计算该势，并考虑中微子产生高度的分布；据此分析IceCube DeepCore 8年公开样本，并纳入该分析的系統不确定度（systematic uncertainties）。

Result: 在介质子质量（mediator mass）$10^{-15}$至$10^{-9}$ eV、仅由地球提供势的区间，对14种$U(1)'$对称性中势在$\nu_\mu$与$\nu_\tau$之间不同的8种，给出90%置信水平（confidence level）的耦合上限。若将势固定在探测器处的取值，所得上限会放松约1至3倍。

Conclusion: 在约$10^{-11}$–$10^{-10}$ eV以下（依对称性而定），本文的上限强于等效原理（equivalence principle）检验所给出的限制。

Abstract: Neutrinos may experience a new long-range force that distinguishes their flavors, mediated by an ultralight neutral boson of a new $U(1)'$ gauge symmetry and sourced by the electrons and nucleons of ordinary matter. Atmospheric neutrinos that cross the Earth would then feel a flavor-dependent potential that alters their oscillations. When the range of the force is about one Earth radius or less, this potential varies from point to point along each neutrino's path, since it comes mostly from the matter near the neutrino. Earlier limits approximated it, either as constant along the path or as following the local density of matter. We compute it exactly at every point of each path, for the Earth model of the IceCube analysis and over the distribution of heights at which the neutrinos are produced, and use it to analyze the 8-yr public IceCube DeepCore sample with the systematic uncertainties of that analysis. For mediator masses from $10^{-15}$ to $10^{-9}$ eV, where the Earth alone sources the potential, we set upper limits at 90% confidence level on the coupling of eight of 14 $U(1)'$ symmetries, those whose potential differs between $ν_μ$ and $ν_τ$. A potential held at its value at the detector would weaken our limits by factors of about 1-3. Below about $10^{-11}$-$10^{-10}$ eV, depending on the symmetry, our limits are stronger than those from tests of the equivalence principle.

</details>


### [181] [A triple-threat challenge for $\mathit{T}$ and $\mathit{S}$ at two loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15807)
*Ulrich Haisch,Sara Martellini,Ben A. Stefanek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文对 $Z_2$ 对称实标量单态扩展标准模型中的斜参数（oblique parameters）$T$、$S$ 进行精确两圈（two-loop）计算，并用全理论、对数增强项推导与有效场论（effective field theory）三种方法相互验证，进而给出电弱精确测量（electroweak precision measurements）约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 $Z_2$ 对称的实单态标量扩展中，斜参数 $T$ 与 $S$ 的首个非零贡献出现在两圈阶，其精确预测存在困难，需要可靠的多方法交叉检验。

Method: 采用三重手段：一是在完整理论中进行精确两圈计算；二是基于单态标量重整化（rescales）希格斯与电弱规范玻色子耦合、破坏希格斯与戈德斯通（Goldstone）贡献间紫外发散（ultraviolet divergences）抵消的一般论证，导出对数增强项；三是在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）中复现结果，其中对数与质量依赖项来自修改希格斯动能项的量纲六算符（dimension-six operator）单圈矩阵元，剩余常数来自两圈匹配（matching）。

Result: 三种方法所得结果相互一致，并构成一类新物理情形的计算范式：即主导效应以保持 custodial 对称性（custodial symmetry）的方式修改希格斯耦合，使 custodial 对称性破坏及 $T$ 参数仅在单圈以上出现。基于当前及预期电弱精确测量给出的约束显示，对较轻单态标量而言，量纲六以上（beyond dimension six）的效应不可忽略。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了 $Z_2$ 实单态标量模型中斜参数的首个精确两圈预测，并可作为类似 custodial 对称性限制型新物理场景的模板；同时表明轻单态情形下高阶算符效应在电弱精确约束中具有实际意义。

Abstract: In the $Z_2$-symmetric real singlet-scalar extension of the Standard Model (SM), the oblique parameters $\mathit{T}$ and $\mathit{S}$ first receive contributions at two loops, making their precise prediction a challenge. We take on this challenge with a triple threat. First, we perform an exact two-loop calculation in the full theory. Second, we derive the logarithmically enhanced terms from a general argument, according to which the singlet rescales the Higgs couplings to the electroweak gauge bosons and thereby spoils the cancellation of ultraviolet divergences between Higgs and Goldstone contributions. Third, we reproduce the results in the SM effective field theory, where the logarithms and mass-dependent terms stem from one-loop matrix elements of the dimension-six operator that modifies the Higgs kinetic term, and the remaining constants from two-loop matching. Our calculations provide a blueprint for new-physics scenarios in which the leading effects modify the Higgs couplings in a custodially symmetric way, so that custodial-symmetry violation, and hence $\mathit{T}$, arises only beyond one loop. Finally, we derive constraints from current and projected electroweak precision measurements and show that effects beyond dimension six matter for light singlets.

</details>


### [182] [Dual-species atom interferometry for axion dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15874)
*Maike Reckermann,John Carlton,Michael Werner,Agnese Mariotti,Guillermo A. Perez-Lobato,Nathaniel Sherrill,Naceur Gaaloul,Dennis Schlippert,Elina Fuchs*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出双物种原子干涉测量序列，将重力梯度噪声（GGN）抑制近三个量级，并在10m基线上超越100m单物种梯度仪对轴子（axion）胶子耦合的灵敏度，100m基线的AICE可突破实验室限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子干涉仪对轴子暗物质（axion dark matter）引起的原子质量与跃迁频率振荡敏感，但主要受限于重力梯度噪声（gravity gradient noise, GGN）的灵敏度瓶颈，需在抑制噪声的同时增强信号响应。

Method: 设计双物种原子干涉测量序列（dual-species atom interferometry sequence），利用两类原子的质量差实现灵敏度增强，并结合对GGN的抑制机制；同时纳入全面的噪声分析（noise analysis）评估实际可行性。

Result: 在合理实验假设下，10m基线双物种干涉仪对轴子胶子耦合（axion gluon coupling）的灵敏度优于100m单物种梯度仪；汉诺威VLBAI（10m基线）可接近主流实验室限制水平，而100m基线AICE可将灵敏度再提升两个量级，在10^-19 eV/c^2 ≲ m_a ≲ 3×10^-15 eV/c^2 质量区间超越实验室约束。

Conclusion: 双物种原子干涉方案通过协同抑制GGN与利用质量差增强信号，显著提升轴子暗物质探测能力，并指明VLBAI与AICE等长基线装置在轴子参数空间中的竞争性探测前景。

Abstract: Atom interferometers are sensitive to oscillations of the masses and transition frequencies of atoms, which can be induced by axion dark matter. The main limitation is the sensitivity to gravity gradient noise (GGN). We propose a dual-species atom interferometry sequence that provides a suppression of GGN by up to three orders of magnitude and a sensitivity enhancement via the mass difference of the two atoms. We show that, under realistic experimental assumptions, a 10m-baseline dual-species atom interferometer is more sensitive to the axion gluon coupling than a 100m single-species gradiometer. Including a comprehensive noise analysis, we demonstrate that the Very Long Baseline Atom Interferometer (VLBAI) in Hannover with a baseline of 10m can probe the axion decay constant at levels almost competitive with leading laboratory constraints. The proposed 100m-baseline Atom Interferometer CERN Experiment (AICE) can extend this sensitivity by up to two orders of magnitude, surpassing laboratory constraints for axion masses of $10^{-19}\;\text{eV}/c^2 \lesssim m_a \lesssim 3\times10^{-15}\;\text{eV}/c^2$.

</details>


### [183] [The Curious Case of Anapole Dark Matter at LZ: Minimal, Elastic and Testable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15957)
*Sourav Bera,Suvam Maharana,Poulami Mondal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 马约拉纳费米子（Majorana fermion）因无电荷与偶极矩，其电磁相互作用由反常极矩（anapole moment）主导；该动量依赖的相互作用产生较硬的反冲谱，可解释 LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验记录到的单个高能核反冲事件，且同一耦合在约 300 GeV 质量处同时给出观测到的暗物质丰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定的马约拉纳费米子是电磁相互作用暗物质（dark matter）的最小实现，且 LZ 实验记录到一个几乎无本底预期区域内的单次高能核反冲事件，需要探索其可能的暗物质解释。

Method: 分析反常极矩相互作用（anapole interaction）的动量依赖性所导致的核反冲谱（recoil spectrum）硬化效应，并将其与 LZ 单事件数据、暗物质丰度、对撞机搜寻（collider searches）及间接探测（indirect detection）约束进行比对。

Result: 弹性散射的反常极矩暗物质可解释 LZ 的单个高能核反冲事件；拟合该数据的同一耦合在质量约 300 GeV 时给出观测到的暗物质丰度，并与对撞机搜寻和间接探测结果一致。

Conclusion: 该情景可检验：预言低能区将出现更多事件，完整 LZ 运行将予以探测，而 XLZD 等下一代探测器可决定性检验该解释。

Abstract: Majorana fermions can carry neither charge nor dipole moments, leaving the anapole moment as their leading electromagnetic interaction. A stable Majorana fermion is therefore a minimal realization of electromagnetically interacting dark matter. The momentum dependence of the anapole interaction produces a harder recoil spectrum than standard momentum-independent interactions. We show that this makes elastically scattering anapole dark matter a viable interpretation of the single high-energy nuclear-recoil event recorded by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment, in a region where almost no background is expected. Curiously, the same coupling that fits the LZ data also yields the observed dark matter abundance at a mass of about 300 GeV, and is consistent with collider searches and indirect detection. The scenario is testable, predicting further events at lower energies, which the full LZ run will probe and a next-generation detector such as XLZD will decisively test.

</details>


### [184] [Probing the internal structure of the $ΩΩ$ dibaryon via its weak decays and lifetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16508)
*Qi-Fang Lü,Deng-Wang Tang,Yu-Bing Dong,Peng-Nian Shen,Jia-Jun Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 采用有效拉氏量方法系统计算了ΩΩ双重子（dibaryon）的三体与四体弱衰变宽度及寿命，发现衰变模式随结合能显著变化，总寿命由10^-10 s量级延长至10^-9 s量级，可为RHIC与LHC实验搜寻提供理论参考并区分紧致六夸克态（hexaquark）与弱束缚分子态解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ΩΩ双重子的弱衰变性质对探测其内部结构、指导实验搜寻具有关键作用，而现有研究对其衰变模式与寿命缺乏系统计算。

Method: 基于有效拉氏量方法（effective Lagrangian approach），计算三体与四体末态衰变宽度，振幅包含单弱顶点与双弱顶点图（single- and double-weak-vertex diagrams）的贡献，并考察结合能从数MeV至100 MeV以上的依赖关系。

Result: 弱束缚情形（E_b=1、20 MeV）下，ΩΛK̄、ΩΞπ、ΞΞK̄三体道几乎耗尽全部宽度，主导道由E_b=1 MeV时的ΩΛK̄转为E_b=20 MeV时的ΩΞπ；深束缚情形（E_b=100、120 MeV）下ΩΛK̄道被禁戒，主导模式为ΩΞπ与ΞΞK̄，双弱顶点道ΛK̄ΛK̄可忽略；总寿命随结合能增大由10^-10 s量级延长至10^-9 s量级。

Conclusion: 衰变模式与寿命随结合能的显著差异为RHIC和LHC实验搜寻提供了有价值的理论参考值，并可清晰区分紧致六夸克态与弱束缚分子态两种解释。

Abstract: The weak decay properties of the $ΩΩ$ dibaryon play a crucial role in probing its internal structure and guiding experimental efforts. Using an effective Lagrangian approach, we systematically study its weak decays and lifetime by computing the widths for both three- and four-body final states, with amplitudes receiving contributions from single- and double-weak-vertex diagrams. We investigate the dependence of the decay properties on the binding energy ranging from a few MeV to over $100$ MeV. For weakly bound states ($E_b = 1$ and $20$ MeV), the three-body channels $ΩΛ\bar{K}$, $ΩΞπ$, and $ΞΞ\bar{K}$ exhaust essentially the entire width, with the largest channel shifting from $ΩΛ\bar{K}$ at $E_b=1$ MeV to $ΩΞπ$ at $E_b=20$ MeV. For deeply bound states ($E_b = 100$ and $120$ MeV), the $ΩΛ\bar{K}$ channel is forbidden, and the dominant modes are $ΩΞπ$ and $ΞΞ\bar{K}$, while the double-weak-vertex channel $Λ\bar{K} Λ\bar{K}$ is found to be negligibly small. As the binding energy increases, the total lifetime extends from the order of $10^{-10}~\mathrm{s}$ to $10^{-9}~\mathrm{s}$. These distinct differences in decay patterns and lifetimes provide valuable theoretical reference values for experimental searches at RHIC and the LHC, and offer a clear discriminator between the compact hexaquark and weakly bound molecular interpretations.

</details>


### [185] [Solar Neutrinos at the ESSνSB experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16807)
*ESSnuSB Collaboration,J. Aguilar,D. Barčot,E. Baussan,A. K. Bhattacharyya,M. Blennow,M. Bogomilov,B. Bolling,E. Bouquerel,F. Bramati,A. Branca,G. Brunetti,I. Bustinduy,C. J. Carlile,J. Cederkall,T. W. Choi,S. Choubey,P. Christiansen,I. Christodoulou,E. Cristaldo Morales,P. Cupiał,D. D'Ago,H. Danared,J. P. A. M. de André,E. A. Díaz,M. Dracos,I. Efthymiopoulos,T. Ekelöf,M. Eshraqi,G. Fanourakis,A. Farricker,E. Fasoula,T. Fukuda,S. Gago,N. Gazis,Th. Geralis,M. Ghosh,A. Giarnetti,G. Gokbulut,C. Hagner,L. Halić,S. G. Hernández,J. Hiegel,M. Hooft,K. E. Iversen,N. Jachowicz,M. Jakkapu,M. Jensen,I. Karakoulias,E. Kasimi,A. Kayis Topaksu,B. Kliček,K. Kordas,B. Kovač,A. Leisos,A. Longhin,M. López,C. Maiano,S. Marangoni,J. G. Marcos,C. Marrelli,D. Meloni,M. Mezzetto,N. Milas,J. L. Muñoz,K. Niewczas,M. Oglakci,T. Ohlsson,M. Olvegård,A. Opanasenko,M. Pari,D. Patrzalek,G. Petkov,Ch. Petridou,P. Poussot,A. Psallidas,F. Pupilli,M. L. Reguera,D. Saiang,E. Salehi,D. Sampsonidis,A. Scanu,C. Schwab,F. Sordo,G. Stavropoulos,M. Stipčević,R. Tarkeshian,F. Terranova,T. Tolba,M. Topp-Mugglestone,E. Trachanas,R. Tsenov,A. Tsirigotis,S. E. Tzamarias,M. Vanderpoorten,G. Vankova-Kirilova,N. Vassilopoulos,S. Vihonen,J. Wurtz,V. Zeter,O. Zormpa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估 ESSnuSB 大型地下水切伦科夫（water-Cherenkov, WC）探测器对太阳中微子两个关键特征的灵敏度：$^8$B 电子中微子存活概率 $P_{ee}(E_\nu)$ 中的 MSW 谱上翘（MSW spectral upturn）与稀有 hep 中微子通量的首次直接观测。结果表明，在 90% 散裂拒绝效率的现实情形下，残余本底使两信号在 20 年曝光内均无法达到 $5\sigma$；约 99% 的散裂拒绝效率才可实现发现级灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在检验 ESSnuSB 水切伦科夫探测器能否探测太阳中微子的 MSW 谱上翘与 hep 中微子通量这两个关键特征，并量化探测器系统误差与环境本底对发现潜力的限制。

Method: 采用以谱形为主导（shape-driven）的分析策略并设置浮动的整体归一化，使发现能力依赖谱畸变而非绝对事件率；分析纳入能量分辨率与通量归一化等探测器系统误差，并对宇宙线 μ 子散裂（muon spallation）与 $^{222}$Rn 污染等环境本底建模。

Result: 在散裂拒绝效率为 90%（参考 Super-Kamiokande 经验与近期 JUNO 分析）的现实情形下，残余散裂本底使两信号在 20 年曝光内均无法达到 $5\sigma$ 显著性；在所用阈值下，实现两信号的发现级灵敏度需约 99% 的散裂拒绝效率，且所需散裂否决效率与反冲能量阈值共同决定可达到的显著性。

Conclusion: hep 通量的发现与精度灵敏度得到详细研究，结果显示本底抑制（尤其是散裂否决）是决定 ESSnuSB 能否探测上述太阳中微子信号的关键因素。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of the ESSnuSB large underground water-Cherenkov (WC) detector to two key solar neutrino signatures: the MSW spectral upturn in the $^8$B electron neutrino survival probability $P_{ee}(E_ν)$, and the first direct observation of the rare hep neutrino flux. The analysis incorporates detector systematic uncertainties (energy resolution and flux normalization) and models environmental backgrounds from cosmic-ray muon spallation and $^{222}$Rn contamination. We adopt a shape-driven analysis strategy with a floating overall normalization, so that the discovery power relies on the spectral distortion rather than on the absolute event rate. In a realistic scenario with 90% spallation rejection efficiency, informed by the Super-Kamiokande experience and recent JUNO analyses, residual spallation backgrounds prevent either signal from reaching $5σ$ significance within 20 years of exposure. For the hep flux, we present a detailed sensitivity study to both its discovery and its precision, and quantify how the required spallation veto efficiency and recoil energy threshold jointly determine the achievable significance for both signals. At the adopted thresholds, discovery-level sensitivity for both signals requires approximately 99% spallation rejection over a 20-year exposure.

</details>


### [186] [Exploring the Pion Vector Form Factor Beyond the Physical Sheet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16974)
*Florian Herren,Fatemeh Nouri,Méril Reboud*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用解析性（analyticity）与幺正性（unitarity）对π介子矢量形状因子（pion vector form factor）在第一、第二黎曼面（Riemann sheets）上做模型无关参数化，并联合拟合τ衰变与类空数据，提取ρ±极点参数、π介子电荷半径及低能ππ散射参数，结果不依赖外部散射输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统提取ρ介子极点参数与低能ππ散射参数的方法往往依赖外部散射输入，作者希望建立一种不依赖外部散射输入的、模型无关的分析途径。

Method: 基于解析性与幺正性，在非弹性πω阈值以下对π介子矢量形状因子在第一和第二黎曼面上进行参数化，并将该参数化拟合到ALEPH、Belle、CLEO、OPAL的τ⁻→π⁻π⁰ν_τ数据以及NA7与JLab的类空（spacelike）数据。

Result: 提取出ρ±的极点位置与耦合、π介子电荷半径（pion charge radius）及低能ππ散射参数，结果与文献一致，其中M_{ρ±}=762.07±0.63 MeV、Γ_{ρ±}=143.62^{+0.79}_{-0.80} MeV；参数化已在EOS软件中公开提供。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型无关参数化不依赖外部散射输入即可一致地确定ρ±极点参数与相关低能量，并已通过EOS软件公开，便于后续复用与检验。

Abstract: Using analyticity and unitarity, we parametrise the pion vector form factor on the first and second Riemann sheets, below the inelastic $πω$ threshold. We fit this model-independent parametrisation to the available $τ^-\toπ^-π^0ν_τ$ data from ALEPH, Belle, CLEO and OPAL, together with spacelike data from NA7 and JLab, and extract the pole position and couplings of the $ρ^\pm$ along with the pion charge radius and low-energy $ππ$ scattering parameters. Our results are independent of external scattering input and agree with the literature; in particular, we find $M_{ρ^\pm} = 762.07\pm 0.63$ MeV and $Γ_{ρ^\pm} = 143.62^{+0.79}_{-0.80}$ MeV. The parametrisation is publicly available in the EOS software.

</details>


### [187] [Two-loop mixed QCD-EW corrections to bottom-pair production in electron-positron collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17171)
*Simone Devoto,Chinmoy Dey,Michele Dradi,Justin T. Fagnoni,Ziyang Huang,Xinchen Li,Michael J. Ramsey-Musolf,Alessandro Vicini,Jiang Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了电子-正电子对撞中产生大质量底-反底夸克对的双圈混合强-电弱虚修正，并采用半解析技术处理含三组不同质量、最多五条内禀大质量线的费曼积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升底夸克对产生过程的精度计算，需要完整的NNLO QCD-EW截面，其中双圈混合强-电弱虚修正（mixed strong-electroweak virtual corrections）是此前缺失的关键部分。

Method: 基于半解析技术（semi-analytical techniques）计算全部相关费曼积分（Feynman integrals），包括含最多五条内禀大质量线、三种不同质量值的积分，电弱规范玻色子采用复质量处理；结果经紫外重整化（ultraviolet-renormalized）与红外减除（infrared-subtracted）后得到有限修正。

Result: 所得到的有限修正贡献于底夸克对包容性产生的NNLO QCD-EW截面（NNLO QCD-EW cross section）。

Conclusion: 该方法可推广至完全电弱二阶修正（fully electroweak second-order corrections）的研究，服务于高亮度大型强子对撞机（High-Luminosity LHC）及未来轻子对撞机（future lepton colliders）的基准精度计算。

Abstract: We present the two-loop mixed strong-electroweak virtual corrections to the production of a massive bottom-antibottom quark pair in electron-positron collisions. The evaluation of all the relevant Feynman integrals, including those with up to five internal massive lines with three different mass values, has been worked out relying on semi-analytical techniques, using complex-valued masses for the electroweak gauge bosons. The ultraviolet-renormalized and infrared-subtracted finite correction contributes to the NNLO QCD-EW cross section of the inclusive production of a bottom-quark pair. The methodology adopted in this calculation can be extended to the study of fully electroweak second-order corrections to benchmark precision calculations relevant to both the high-luminosity phase of the Large Hadron Collider and prospective future lepton colliders.

</details>


### [188] [A Dark-Sector Mass Flip-Flop Mechanism for the ANITA-IV Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17328)
*Dibya S. Chattopadhyay,Carlos A. Argüelles,Vedran Brdar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出暗部门“质量翻转（mass flip-flop）”机制，用以调和ANITA-IV观测到的四个地平线下方向上空气簇射候选事件与IceCube在同能区无中微子事件的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ANITA-IV探测到四个接近地平线的上行空气簇射候选事件，与超高能τ中微子（ultra-high-energy ν_τ）一致；但曝光量远大于ANITA的IceCube（ice cube）在同能量段未探测到中微子事件，两者存在观测张力。论文旨在寻找可同时解释这两种结果的新物理机制。

Method: 考虑全天空、单能量的超高能N_1通量，N_1比其伙伴N_2更轻且在真空中稳定；引入与地球物质相互作用的超轻标量（ultralight scalar），在地球内部反转N_1与N_2的质量次序，使入射N_1在深地下衰变为N_2，N_2无阻碍穿出地球，随后在大气中N_2→N_1 Z'衰变在运动学上被允许，从而引发广延空气簇射（extensive air shower）。

Result: 该机制中，在IceCube深度处N_1→N_2 Z'与N_2→N_1 Z'在运动学上均不被允许，从而解释IceCube无类似事件；将N_1通量按ANITA-IV四个事件归一后，预测PUEO首飞期间将出现三个事件。

Conclusion: 暗部门质量翻转机制可自洽地同时解释ANITA-IV上行簇射候选事件与IceCube的零观测，并给出PUEO首飞的可检验预言。

Abstract: ANITA-IV has observed four upward-going air-shower candidates just below the horizon, consistent with ultra-high-energy $ν_τ$, yet IceCube, despite its much larger exposure, has detected no neutrino events at such energies. We propose a dark-sector mass flip-flop mechanism that reconciles these observations. We consider an all-sky, monoenergetic flux of ultra-high-energy $N_1$, that is lighter than its partner $N_2$ and stable in vacuum. In our scenario, an ultralight scalar mediating interactions with Earth matter inverts the mass ordering of $N_1$ and $N_2$, allowing an incident $N_1$ to decay into $N_2$ deep underground. The resulting $N_2$ propagates unimpeded through matter and exits the Earth, where the decay $N_2 \to N_1 Z'$ becomes kinematically allowed in the atmosphere, initiating an extensive air shower. At IceCube depths, neither $N_1\to N_2 Z'$ nor $N_2\to N_1 Z'$ is kinematically allowed, which explains the absence of analogous events at IceCube. Normalizing the $N_1$ flux to the four ANITA-IV events, we also predict three events during PUEO's first flight.

</details>


### [189] [SuSy and CoDy Radiation from Evaporating Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14563)
*Giacomo Cacciapaglia,Francesco Sannino,Paola Simone,Vania Vellucci*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文在BlackHawk中实现三种超对称（supersymmetry, SuSy）基准情形与一种复合动力学（composite dynamics, CoDy）情形，比较Schwarzschild黑洞与三种正则黑洞（polymerized、Hayward、Simpson–Visser）的蒸发光子与中微子谱。结果表明新物理与修改时空几何的效应在特定能区相互交织，光子与中微子谱可作为互补探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超出标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）的物理与修改时空几何（modified spacetime geometries）如何共同影响蒸发黑洞的光子与中微子谱，并检验两者效应是否可区分。

Method: 将三种代表性超对称基准与一个复合动力学情形植入BlackHawk黑洞蒸发计算框架，同时考虑Schwarzschild黑洞与polymerized、Hayward、Simpson–Visser三类正则黑洞（regular black holes）几何，计算瞬时次级光子与中微子谱。

Result: TeV尺度新自由度在对应黑洞质量约M_BH~10^10 g的霍金温度下开启；辐射衰变产生窄谱特征且相对幅度近似与温度无关，多步衰变级联产生随温度增强的宽谱增强，压缩质量谱则无局域特征；新物理与几何效应在特定能区相互交织并改变谱形。

Conclusion: 光子与中微子谱为新粒子物理与黑洞几何提供互补探针，预测的谱增强延伸至当前与未来高能天文台可及能区。

Abstract: We investigate the interplay between physics beyond the Standard Model and modified spacetime geometries in shaping the photon and neutrino spectra from evaporating black holes. We implement three representative supersymmetric (SuSy) benchmarks and a composite dynamics (CoDy) scenario in BlackHawk. We consider both Schwarzschild black holes and regular black holes geometries, namely polymerized, Hayward, and Simpson--Visser black holes. New degrees of freedom at the TeV scale become accessible at Hawking temperatures corresponding to black hole masses of approximately $M_{\rm BH}\sim 10^{10}\,\mathrm{g}$. We show that physics beyond the Standard Model produces distinctive signatures in both the instantaneous secondary-photon and neutrino spectra. Radiative decays generate narrow spectral features with approximately temperature-independent relative amplitudes, while multistep decay cascades produce broad enhancements that become increasingly pronounced at higher temperatures. In contrast, compressed mass spectra exhibit no localized features. Crucially, we show that the effects of new particle physics and modified spacetime geometries can become intertwined in specific energy regimes, altering the resulting spectral signatures. Our results demonstrate that photon and neutrino spectra provide complementary probes of new particle physics and black hole geometry, with predicted spectral enhancements extending into the energy ranges accessible to current and future high-energy observatories.

</details>


### [190] [The NANOGrav 15-year data set: A Limit on Continuous Spin Gravitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14613)
*Chinmay Ambasht,Olivia Bitcon,Tanner Trickle,the NANOGrav Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing arrays, PTA）的低频引力波灵敏度，对连续自旋引力子（continuous spin graviton）的自旋标度（spin scale）ρ_g 给出实验约束，NANOGrav 15 年数据集在 95% 可信水平下得到 ρ_g < 1.23×10⁻²³ eV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续自旋粒子（continuous spin particles, CSPs）是无质量粒子，其非零的有量纲自旋标度 ρ 会改变洛伦兹助推（Lorentz boost）下的螺旋度（helicity）；当 ρ→0 时退化为普通粒子，故光子或引力子可能实为 CSPs。因此确定实验允许的 ρ 取值具有重要性，而 CSPs 的物理效应在 ρ≪ω 时按 ρ/ω 的幂次标度，使低频探测尤为有利。

Method: 采用脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing arrays），其对纳赫兹（nanohertz）低频引力波具有优异灵敏度（近期已给出随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational wave background）的证据），并结合标准贝叶斯数据分析（Bayesian data analysis）技术，对连续自旋引力子的自旋标度施加约束。

Result: 基于 NANOGrav 15 年数据集，在 95% 可信水平（credible level）下将引力子自旋标度约束为 ρ_g < 1.23×10⁻²³ eV。

Conclusion: 脉冲星计时阵列因其低频灵敏度而特别适用于探测 CSPs 的低频效应，可作为约束连续自旋引力子自旋标度的有效手段，并给出了当前最强的量级约束之一。

Abstract: Continuous spin particles (CSPs) are massless particles characterized by a dimensionful spin scale, $ρ$, which, when non-zero, changes their helicity under Lorentz boosts. Since CSPs reduce to ordinary particles when $ρ\rightarrow 0$, the photon or graviton could be CSPs and only appear to behave as ordinary particles due to a small value of $ρ$. Therefore, it is important to determine what value of $ρ$ is experimentally allowed. Recent work has shown that the physical effects of CSPs scale as powers of $ρ/ω$ when $ρ\ll ω$, where $ω$ is a frequency scale, making low-frequency probes particularly beneficial for highlighting these phenomena. In this paper we use pulsar timing arrays to set constraints on the spin scale of the continuous spin graviton. Pulsar timing arrays are uniquely suited for this application as they have exceptional sensitivity to low, nanohertz frequency gravitational waves, as demonstrated by their recent evidence for a stochastic gravitational wave background. Using standard Bayesian data analysis techniques, we find that the NANOGrav 15-year data set constrains the graviton spin scale to $ρ_\text{g} < 1.23 \times 10^{-23} \, \text{eV} $ at the 95% credible level.

</details>


### [191] [Poles Are Not Enough: Zeros of Dyson-Schwinger Gluon Propagators in Yang-Mills Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16392)
*Marco Frasca,Anish Ghoshal,Stefan Groote*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 归一化两点函数（即使含多个极点）不足以确定无源热配分函数；对胶子场高斯测度，正留数有理传播子（rational propagator）的极点与交错零点共同贡献行列式，零点因子恢复了每个伴随特征值（adjoint eigenvalue）的两个紫外极化，从而使多极点完成（completion）能正确描述SU(3)一级相变（first-order transition）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭归一化两点函数无法唯一固定无源热配分函数（source-free thermal partition function），需要明确多极点传播子（propagator）的完成方式及其对热力学量的影响；文中还希望找到一个不依赖压强加性归一的紫外判据。

Method: 以原始胶子场的高斯测度（Gaussian measure）为出发点，将正留数有理传播子的极点行列式与极点间交错零点（zeros interlaced）的行列式一并纳入；并将该构造应用于椭圆型 Dyson-Schwinger 胶子核（elliptic Dyson-Schwinger gluon kernel）的背景协变完成（background-covariant completion）。以固定 holonomy 的自由能差作为紫外检验，与 Weiss 两极化结果对比。

Result: 单极点已呈现一级 SU(3) 相变；饱和六极点（six-pole）结果仅使 T_c/M_g 移动 1.48%（T_c 为共存温度，M_g 为最低极点质量）。在六极点下，零点行列式使 T_c/M_g 升高 2.31%、使 L/T_c^4 降低 12.4%（L 为潜热 latent heat，各方案用自身 T_c）；固定 M_g 时量纲潜热下降 4.0%。固定 holonomy 的自由能差的渐近值：保留零点时与两极化 Weiss 结果一致，忽略零点（poles-only）时为其 (3P-1)/2 倍。

Conclusion: 零点因子是区分“独立极点场气体”与正确完成的关键，可在任意有限截断（finite truncation）下恢复每个伴随特征值的两个紫外极化；忽略零点会系统性高估紫外自由能差并错估 T_c 与潜热。

Abstract: A normalized two-point function with several poles does not, on its own, fix the source-free thermal partition function. For the Gaussian measure of the original gluon field, a positive-residue rational propagator contributes determinants from its poles and from zeros interlaced between them. The zero factors distinguish this completion from a gas of independent pole fields and restore two ultraviolet polarizations per adjoint eigenvalue at every finite truncation. We apply the construction to a background-covariant completion of the elliptic Dyson-Schwinger gluon kernel. The first-order SU(3) transition is already present with a single pole, and the saturated six-pole result shifts $T_c/M_g$ by only $1.48\%$, where $T_c$ is the coexistence temperature and $M_g$ is the lowest pole mass. At six poles, the zero determinants raise $T_c/M_g$ by $2.31\%$ and lower $L/T_c^4$ by $12.4\%$, where $L$ is the latent heat and each prescription uses its own $T_c$. At fixed $M_g$ the dimensional latent heat decreases by $4.0\%$. The fixed-holonomy free-energy difference gives an ultraviolet test independent of the additive pressure normalization. At fixed pole number $P$, its asymptotic value agrees with the two-polarization Weiss result when the zeros are retained and is $(3P-1)/2$ times as large in the poles-only prescription.

</details>


### [192] [Rapidly Spinning Primordial Black Holes from Confinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15169)
*Michael Zantedeschi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 禁闭（confinement）可将相距遥远的粒子转化为高速自旋的原初黑洞（primordial black holes）；失配对可束缚、辐射并最终坍缩为近极端黑洞，产生随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational-wave background），其自旋分布特征可供引力波观测检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨禁闭机制下重夸克—反夸克对及磁单极—反磁单极对在弦（flux tube/string）牵引下相互接近并形成黑洞的可能性，并考察宇宙学潮汐与弦弯曲造成的失配对黑洞形成及自旋分布的影响。

Method: 基于通量管（flux tube）将储存能量转化为相对论性运动的图像，并在磁性对偶（magnetic dual）的希格斯相（Higgs phase）中作平行分析；结合数值弦演化（numerical string evolutions）验证所得到的自旋分布规律。

Result: 黑洞形成要求来自平滑、宽广相遇分布的稀有近距离通过；由此得到的初始自旋密度（natal spin density）线性上升至极端性（extremality），平均无量纲自旋为三分之二，数值弦演化重现该规律。失配的对可保持束缚，经辐射后坍缩为更轻的近极端黑洞。

Conclusion: 该机制产生随机引力波背景；尽管丰度（abundance）仍依赖模型，禁闭留下了可由引力波观测检验的独特自旋印记（spin signature）。

Abstract: Confinement can turn widely separated particles into rapidly spinning primordial black holes. A flux tube pulls a heavy quark--antiquark pair together, converting its stored energy into relativistic motion. The same mechanism is possible in the magnetic dual, where a Higgs phase confines magnetic flux into a string joining a monopole--antimonopole pair. As the particles approach, cosmological tides and string bends can deflect their motion and make them miss. These disturbances can originate during inflation or be generated later. Black-hole formation then requires rare close passages from a smooth, broad encounter distribution. The resulting natal spin density therefore rises linearly to extremality, with mean dimensionless spin two thirds. Numerical string evolutions recover this law. Pairs that miss can remain bound, radiate and later collapse into lighter, near-extremal holes, sourcing a stochastic gravitational-wave background. Although the abundance remains model dependent, confinement leaves a unique spin signature that gravitational-wave observations can test.

</details>


### [193] [A unified mean-field description of dense hadron matter constrained from $χ$EFT and lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15388)
*Md. Emanuel Hoque,Arunava Mukherjee,Sayantan Sharma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文构建了一个强子相对论平均场理论（RMFT），结合两阶段贝叶斯分析，在宽温区与宽重子密度范围内描述QCD的禁闭相，并给出约束良好的重子—介子耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为QCD禁闭相（confined phase）在宽温度与宽重子密度范围内提供一个有效描述框架，并利用中子物质与格点QCD（lattice QCD）输入对重子—介子耦合给出可靠约束。

Method: 构建包含标量介子与矢量介子同重子及质量至3 GeV共振态相互作用的相对论平均场理论，并纳入夸克模型与格点QCD预言但尚未观测的共振态；采用两阶段贝叶斯分析（Bayesian analysis）：第一阶段用六核经验参数约束六个耦合，这些参数由手征有效场论（chiral effective field theory）对纯中子物质每核子能量的预言导出（密度范围n_B∈[0.08,0.32] fm^-3），并使用高斯过程似然（Gaussian-process likelihood）处理输入数据相关性；第二阶段将该后验作为先验，利用格点QCD输入约束其他重子—介子耦合。

Result: 所得后验显著改善了对各重子—介子耦合及非线性介子自相互作用（non-linear meson self-interactions）的约束，且与当前中子星质量、半径及潮汐形变参数（tidal deformability parameters）的天体观测相一致。

Conclusion: 两阶段贝叶斯策略可有效贯通核物理与格点QCD输入，为宽温度、宽密度条件下描述QCD禁闭相的RMFT提供约束良好且与天文观测相容的参数化。

Abstract: We construct a relativistic mean field theory (RMFT) of hadrons in an attempt to describe the confined phase of QCD across a wide range of temperatures and baryon densities. This includes the scalar and vector mesons interacting with baryons and resonances up to $3$ GeV, including those which are not yet discovered but predicted within the quark model and lattice QCD. The baryon--meson couplings are constrained through a two-stage Bayesian analysis. In the first stage, we constrain six different couplings in our theory using six nuclear empirical parameters inferred from the chiral effective field theory predictions of the energy per nucleon for pure neutron matter over baryon densities $n_B \in [0.08, 0.32]~\mathrm{fm}^{-3}$, employing a Gaussian-process likelihood to account for correlations in the input data. In the second stage, this posterior is set as the prior for constraining different baryon--meson couplings in our RMFT construction, using lattice QCD input.
