The resulting posterior provides substantially improved constraints for different baryon--meson couplings and non-linear meson self-interactions, which are consistent with the current astrophysical observations of neutron star masses, radii and tidal deformability parameters.

</details>


### [194] [Radiative stability of species-separated Kaluza--Klein spectra in warped geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15753)
*M. A. Anacleto,F. A. Brito,E. Passos,Joao R. L. Santos,J. A. V. Campos,I. E. L. M. Santana*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文检验 Randall–Riojas 提出的"物种相关第五方向光锥（fifth-direction lightcones）"能否在量子修正下维持规范玻色子与费米子 Kaluza–Klein（KK）能标分离：色单圈（quark loops）不超越裸速度区间，但胶子交换会使夸克速度向胶子速度靠拢，使"轻胶子"基准的质量排序失去微扰保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 Randall 与 Riojas 表明，由标量梯度算符（scalar-gradient operators）生成的物种相关第五方向光锥可在翘曲额外维（warped extra dimension）中分离规范场与费米子的 KK 能标。本文动机是检验这一速度（光锥）分离在量子修正（quantum corrections）下是否依然成立。

Method: 采用微扰量子场论方法计算单圈（one-loop）修正：分析夸克圈（quark loops）对胶子速度的影响，以及胶子交换（gluon exchange）对夸克速度的拉动，并给出当胶子为慢物种时被第五分量交换（fifth-component exchange）显著增强的单圈系数；随后在有效理论（effective theory）框架内以强耦合（strong-coupling）估计对轻胶子基准设定效应量级。

Result: 夸克圈只能把胶子速度移动在裸速度所张成的区间内；胶子交换将夸克速度拉向胶子速度。对轻胶子基准（胶子 5.5 TeV 低于 10.74 TeV 的夸克伙伴），KK 模对夸克速度给出量级为一（order one）的修正，该修正比质量排序所能吸收的量大数倍。反之，慢夸克加重胶子的反向基准，其排序稳健性约高十倍。

Conclusion: 轻胶子排序不具备微扰保护（no perturbative protection），量子修正可能破坏规范与费米子 KK 能标的分离；而慢夸克、重胶子的排序相对更稳健（约十倍）。因此物种相关光锥方案的可行性依赖于具体的质量/速度排序选择。

Abstract: Species-dependent fifth-direction lightcones, generated by scalar-gradient operators, can separate gauge and fermion Kaluza--Klein (KK) scales in a warped extra dimension, as recently shown by Randall and Riojas. In this work, we study whether this separation survives quantum corrections. Quark loops can only move the gluon speed within the interval spanned by the bare speeds. Gluon exchange pulls the quark speed toward the gluon speed, with a one-loop coefficient strongly enhanced by fifth-component exchange when the gluon is the slow species. We set the size of this effect using the strong-coupling estimates for a light-gluon benchmark, where a $5.5$~TeV gluon sits below quark partners at $10.74$~TeV. Within the effective theory, the Kaluza--Klein (KK) modes then correct the quark speed at order one. That correction is several times larger than the mass ordering can absorb, so the light-gluon ordering has no perturbative protection. The reverse benchmark, with slow quarks and a heavy gluon, keeps its ordering roughly ten times more robust.

</details>


### [195] [Cosmological Magnetic Fluctuations: Positivity Bounds and QCD Contributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14595)
*Ahmadullah Zahed*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文以两种互补途径研究热宇宙学磁涨落（thermal cosmological magnetic fluctuations）：一是不依赖谱形的平衡磁功率谱（magnetic power spectrum）上下界，二是QCD强子贡献的领头阶计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁功率谱的谱形通常需要模型假设，故有必要建立不依赖具体谱形的严格界；同时需评估强子（hadronic）QCD对宇宙学磁涨落的贡献。

Method: 利用涨落-耗散关系（fluctuation–dissipation relation）、谱正定性（spectral positivity）以及热核（thermal kernels）的凸性与凹性，以两个谱矩（spectral moments）给出平衡磁功率谱的界；并按领头电磁阶（leading electromagnetic order）计算强子QCD贡献，其中估计了主导时间类强子贡献的ρ共振（ρ-resonance）项。

Result: 所得界无需谱形模型，亦可借助各自的矩应用于单个电流贡献，且已知频率阈值可强化上界；在以QCD、电子-正电子等离子体（electron–positron plasma）与冷碰撞等离子体（cold collisional plasma）为例的检验中，这些界均得到验证。

Conclusion: 所得到的谱描述特定宇宙学时期（cosmological epoch）的涨落，为宇宙学磁涨落提供了不依赖谱形的约束框架与QCD强子层面的定量贡献。

Abstract: We study thermal cosmological magnetic fluctuations in two complementary ways. First, we bound the equilibrium magnetic power spectrum in terms of two spectral moments. The proof uses the fluctuation--dissipation relation, spectral positivity, and the convexity and concavity of the thermal kernels. The bounds do not require a model for the spectral shape. They also apply to individual current contributions, using their own moments, and a known frequency threshold strengthens the upper bound. Second, we calculate a hadronic QCD contribution to cosmological magnetic fluctuations at leading electromagnetic order. We estimate the \(ρ\)-resonance contribution, which dominates the timelike hadronic contribution in our comparison. Examples from QCD, an electron--positron plasma and a cold collisional plasma test the bounds. The spectra describe fluctuations at the chosen cosmological epoch.

</details>


### [196] [Toward the minimal set of states in axion superradiance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15865)
*Enrico Cannizzaro,Thomas F. M. Spieksma,Samuel J. Witte*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将自相互作用轴子（axion）云的研究扩展至近300个耦合准束缚态（quasi-bound states），针对10、10^4与10^8太阳质量黑洞进行长期演化，识别出描述黑洞演化与云形态所需的最小态集合，并揭示共振、引力波跃迁与环境相互作用的丰富效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞超辐射（black hole superradiance）的观测搜寻需在天体物理时间尺度上预测准束缚态的增长与演化，但轴子自相互作用在短时间内将能量与角动量从增长最快的态重新分配至高阶弱束缚态，导致大参数空间内基本可观测量的计算困难。

Method: 遵循近300个耦合准束缚态的演化，对10太阳质量黑洞演化一个Salpeter时间尺度，对10^4与10^8太阳质量黑洞演化1 Gyr，并针对每个轴子质量确定描述黑洞演化与超辐射云形状所需的最小态集合。

Result: 多数情形下演化随弱束缚态的增加而收敛，至少203个不同准束缚态参与建立收敛；但在小参数区域内不存在收敛解，宽共振特征与近简并散射率将质量重新分配至主量子数n≳45的束缚态。云可延伸至比通常假设大若干数量级半径，引力波能级跃迁可阻止超辐射态增长。

Conclusion: 引力波谱（gravitational-wave spectrum）及与环境相互作用远比此前认识丰富；自相互作用轴子云的多态耦合演化在大参数空间内可收敛，但存在需更高激发态才能描述的非收敛区域。

Abstract: Observational searches for black hole superradiance require predicting the growth and evolution of quasi-bound states on astrophysical timescales. For axions (and most light spin-0 particles), self-interactions efficiently redistribute energy and angular momentum from the fastest-growing superradiant states into higher-order, more weakly bound states, and do so on short timescales. This has made it difficult to compute even the most mundane of observables across large regions of parameter space. In this work we extend the study of self-interacting systems, following the evolution of nearly 300 coupled quasi-bound states. We evolve a $10\,M_\odot$ black hole over a Salpeter timescale, and $10^4$ and $10^8\,M_\odot$ black holes over a Gyr, identifying for each axion mass the minimal set of states required to describe the evolution of the black hole and the shape of the superradiant cloud. In most cases the evolution converges as increasingly weakly bound states are added; we identify no less than 203 distinct quasi-bound states that play a role in establishing this convergence. In small regions of parameter space, however, we find no converged solution, and show that broad resonant features and nearly degenerate scattering rates redistribute mass to bound states with principal quantum numbers $n \gtrsim 45$. We further show that the cloud can extend to radii orders of magnitude larger than usually assumed, that gravitational-wave level transitions can prevent the growth of superradiant states, and that both the gravitational-wave spectrum and the interaction with the environment are far richer than previously appreciated.

</details>


### [197] [Six-Loop Renormalization in Quantum Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15366)
*Zeyu Li,Ming-xing Luo,Tong-Zhi Yang,Hua Xing Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文在 $\MSbar$ 方案下解析计算了量子电动力学（quantum electrodynamics, QED）中电荷、费米子质量与费米子场标度演化的六圈（six-loop）修正，适用于任意数量的等电荷费米子，将 QED 重整化推进一个圈阶，并为高阶梯形展开行为提供新检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 QED 重整化计算止于较低圈阶，需扩展一个圈阶以检验微扰论（perturbation theory）在高阶下的行为，并为量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics, QCD）的类似计算铺路。

Method: 采用解析方法，在 $\MSbar$ 重整化方案（$\MSbar$ scheme）下计算电荷、费米子质量与费米子场标度演化的六圈修正，结果对任意数量的等电荷费米子成立。

Result: 给出了上述三个量的六圈修正解析结果，将 QED 重整化提升一个圈阶，并提供对高阶梯形展开行为的新检验途径。

Conclusion: 所发展的方法为量子色动力学中重整化的六圈计算开辟了道路，扩展了高阶微扰计算的可及范围。

Abstract: We analytically compute the six-loop corrections to the scale evolution of the electric charge, fermion mass, and fermion field in quantum electrodynamics. The results are obtained in the $\MSbar$ scheme for an arbitrary number of equally charged fermions. They extend renormalization in quantum electrodynamics by one loop order and allow new tests of the behavior of perturbation theory at high orders. The methods developed here also open the way to six-loop computations of renormalization in quantum chromodynamics.

</details>


### [198] [The three-loop QCD helicity amplitudes for $V$+jet production at hadron colliders in full color](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15836)
*Thomas Gehrmann,Petr Jakubčík,Andreas von Manteuffel,Cesare Carlo Mella,Lorenzo Tancredi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文计算了夸克-反夸克湮灭（quark-antiquark annihilation）及其运动学交叉道中矢量玻色子（V=Z, W±, γ*）与喷流（jet）产生的三圈量子色动力学（QCD）修正，并保留对色数（number of colors）的完整依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量玻色子加喷流是强子对撞机上的关键过程，其高精度理论预言需要高阶QCD修正；三圈振幅是迈向该精度的重要一步。

Method: 将红外有限（infrared-finite）的剩余部分用小规模的有理函数与特殊函数（special functions）表示，并借助数值传输（numerical transport）实现全运动学区域内的快速可靠求值。

Result: 发现振幅所处的函数空间比相应完整费曼积分（Feynman integrals）空间受到强得多的限制，且该表示可在所有运动学区域高效、稳定地求值。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了矢量玻色子加喷流过程三圈QCD振幅的紧凑函数表示与通用数值求值方案，为更高精度的对撞机理论预言提供基础。

Abstract: We compute the three-loop Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) corrections to the production of a vector boson $V=Z,W^\pm,γ^*$ and a jet in quark-antiquark annihilation and its kinematic crossings, retaining full dependence on the number of colors. By expressing the infrared-finite remainders in terms of a small set of rational and special functions, we find that the amplitudes live in a strongly restricted function space, compared to the full space of the related Feynman integrals. We provide fast and reliable evaluation in all kinematic regions using numerical transport.

</details>


### [199] [Scalar and pseudoscalar glueballonia: do they bind?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15873)
*Narmin Nasibova,Francesco Giacosa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在纯Yang-Mills（Yang-Mills）理论中，借助非相对论势模型结合Dilaton有效理论（dilaton effective theory）与格点输入，发现标量胶子球素（glueballonium）与赝标量胶子球素均能形成，且在YM真空中稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨纯Yang-Mills理论中是否存在由两个胶子球（glueball）组成的分子型束缚态，即胶子球素（glueballonia），重点关注标量与赝标量通道。

Method: 在束缚态刚出现的近阈区采用非相对论（nonrelativistic）框架，使用吸引的标量交换Yukawa势（Yukawa potential）加涂抹接触项（smeared contact term），并以标量Dilaton有效理论确定势参数：t、u道单胶子球交换给出吸引，s道湮灭与接触相互作用给出排斥；参数由格点Yang-Mills（lattice-YM）输入固定。

Result: 标量胶子球素可以束缚，且结果对参数变化相当稳健；在最小标量-赝标量扩展中，t道标量交换与交叉u道赝标量交换的吸引局域贡献使赝标量胶子球素同样束缚；两者在YM真空中均稳定。赝标量-赝标量、标量-张量、张量-张量通道亦呈吸引并可成束，但因衰变为两个较轻胶子球而在纯YM中不稳定。作为交叉检验，该形式化确认Higgs-Higgs束缚态不会形成。

Conclusion: 纯规范场中可能存在稳定的标量与赝标量胶子球素，而其他通道的候选束缚态因衰变而不稳定；该框架亦能正确排除Higgs-Higgs束缚态。

Abstract: We investigate the existence of molecular two-glueball bound states, so-called glueballonia, in pure Yang-Mills (YM) theory. We mainly concentrate on the scalar and pseudoscalar channels, thus studying scalar-scalar and scalar-pseudoscalar bound states, respectively. Near the onset of binding, when a bound state first appears just below threshold, a nonrelativistic framework is justified. We employ an attractive scalar-exchange Yukawa potential together with a smeared contact term contribution and determine the critical attraction required for binding. For two scalar glueballs, we use the scalar dilaton effective theory to determine the parameters of the non-relativistic potential: the Yukawa interaction is generated by t- and u-channels involving the exchange of one scalar glueball, while the repulsion emerges by the s-channel annihilation and contact interaction. Using lattice-YM input to fix the dilaton parameters, we find that the attraction is strong enough: the scalar glueballonium binds, and the result is quite robust with respect to parameter variations. In a minimal scalar-pseudoscalar extension, the Yukawa attraction is again given by the t-channel scalar glueball, while the crossed u-channel pseudoscalar exchange induces an attractive local contribution, making the pseudoscalar glueballonium also bind in pure gauge. Importantly, both the scalar and pseudoscalar glueballonia are stable in the YM vacuum. Next, we briefly comment on the pseudoscalar-pseudoscalar, scalar-tensor, and tensor-tensor channels: all these channels are attractive too and could form bound states, which, however, would be unstable in pure YM due to decay into two lighter glueballs. Finally, as a cross-check, our formalism confirms that Higgs-Higgs bound state does not form.

</details>


### [200] [Probing the Molecular Nature of $D_{sJ}(2860)$ and $D_{s3}(2860)$ through Their Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16276)
*Qing Lu,Dan Dan Feng,Cai Cheng,Yin Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有效拉格朗日量方法下研究DsJ(2860)与Ds3(2860)的强衰变，通过拟合LHCb角分布与衰变宽度确定分子态耦合常数与截断参数，结果倾向于两者均为P波D*K*分子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DsJ(2860)与Ds3(2860)的分子态结构归属尚不明确，需要借助强衰变与分支比检验不同分子构型假设。

Method: 采用有效拉格朗日量方法（effective Lagrangian approach），对DsJ(2860)考虑S波D1K分子态与P波D*K*分子态两种构型，Ds3(2860)视为P波D*K*分子态；通过拟合LHCb在B_s^0→D̄^0K^-π^+中测量的角分布与衰变宽度，确定耦合常数与截断参数Λ。

Result: 对DsJ(2860)，S波D1K构型对总宽度的描述略优于P波D*K*；但将DsJ(2860)、Ds3(2860)分别取D1K与D*K*的组合假设被Ds3(2860)的R预言（超出实验上限）所否定；两者均取P波D*K*时与Ds3(2860)的R符合良好，并预言R_DsJ(2860)≃1.26–1.29。

Conclusion: 结果支持DsJ(2860)与Ds3(2860)均为P波D*K*分子态（molecular interpretation），该判断可通过未来对R_DsJ(2860)的测量进一步检验。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the molecular interpretations of $D_{sJ}(2860)$ and $D_{s3}(2860)$ by studying their strong decays within the effective Lagrangian approach. We consider two possible configurations for $D_{sJ}(2860)$: an $S$-wave $D_1K$ molecule and a $P$-wave $D^*K^*$ molecule, while treating $D_{s3}(2860)$ as a $P$-wave $D^*K^*$ molecule. By fitting the LHCb-measured angular distribution and decay widths in $B_s^0\to\bar{D}^0K^-π^+$, we determine the molecular coupling constants and cutoff parameter $Λ$. Using these parameters, we calculate the total decay widths and the ratios $R$ of the partial widths into the $D^*K$ and $DK$ channels. For $D_{sJ}(2860)$, the $S$-wave $D_1K$ assignment provides a slightly better description of the measured total width than the $P$-wave $D^*K^*$ assignment. However, the combined $D_1K$ and $D^*K^*$ assignments for $D_{sJ}(2860)$ and $D_{s3}(2860)$, respectively, are disfavored by the predicted $R_{D_{s3}(2860)}$, which exceeds the experimental upper limit. In contrast, assigning both states as $P$-wave $D^*K^*$ molecules yields good agreement with the measured $R_{D_{s3}(2860)}$ and predicts $R_{D_{sJ}(2860)}\simeq 1.26$--$1.29$. These results favor the $P$-wave $D^*K^*$ molecular interpretation for both states, which can be further tested through future measurements of $R_{D_{sJ}(2860)}$.

</details>


### [201] [Proton Spin Fluctuation Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17032)
*Tomoya Uji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出质子自旋（proton spin）的涨落分解（fluctuation decomposition），以夸克（quarks）与胶子（gluons）的自旋及轨道角动量的方差（variances）与协方差（covariances）表述，并给出其单部分子与双部分子分布（single- and double-parton distributions）表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统质子自旋分解（conventional proton spin decomposition）无法刻画自旋与轨道贡献之间的关联与涨落，本文旨在建立一个能显式揭示此类关联的框架。

Method: 将质子自旋按夸克与胶子的自旋、轨道角动量的方差和协方差进行分解，并推导其在单部分子分布与双部分子分布下的表示；同时利用质子自旋确定（definite proton spin）所施加的求和规则（sum rules）。

Result: 求和规则要求自旋与轨道贡献的方差必须由其协方差加以补偿，从而使总自旋保持确定值。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了传统自旋分解所不可见的关联与涨落（correlations and fluctuations），为质子自旋结构研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: We formulate a fluctuation decomposition of proton spin in terms of the variances and covariances of the spin and orbital angular momentum of quarks and gluons. We derive their representations in terms of single- and double-parton distributions. The definite proton spin imposes sum rules requiring the variances of its spin and orbital contributions to be compensated by their covariances. This framework reveals correlations and fluctuations that are invisible to the conventional proton spin decomposition.

</details>


### [202] [Monopole catalysis with massive fermions: the fate of the twisted sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17253)
*Valentin V. Khoze*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在无质量极限下，最低分波中费米子与最小 SU(5) 磁单极散射会产生各味费米子数均为半整数的扭曲态（twisted state），可理解为附着于拓扑线（topological line）的普通费米子；在标准模型中该线被费米子质量与禁闭截断，导致扭曲态衰变为整数费米子数的物理渐近态。研究给出该衰变的截面、能量分配、衰变道与分支比（branching ratio）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解磁单极散射中半整数费米子数扭曲态的物理命运：标准模型的费米子质量与禁闭在超过逆质量标度的距离上破坏拓扑线，使扭曲态必须衰变为可观测的整数费米子数末态，因此需要定量刻画该衰变过程（截面、能量分配、分支比），并澄清 Callan 对分数态的统计解释。

Method: 针对单一代的入射轻子或夸克，分析最低分波散射后扭曲态的衰变：计算截面并与幺正性界限（unitarity bound）比较；用宽分布的 Dirichlet 分布描述出射能量在四种费米子味间的分配，并据此确定阈值与运动学；再依据守恒律枚举允许的衰变道，分别在两个极限下确定分支比，并对中间能区建立能量依赖的模型。

Result: 该分波截面饱和幺正性界限，σ=π/p²；出射态能量以宽 Dirichlet 分布分配到四种费米子味。守恒律对每个入射费米子仅允许两个衰变道：螺旋度翻转（helicity flip）及其破坏 (B+L) 的伙伴道。入射电子时，(B+L) 破坏道为反核子合成（anti-nucleon synthesis）：高能下两道等概率，低能（低于反核子阈值）仅剩螺旋度翻转，中间能区给出建模的能量依赖。入射慢质子中的夸克时，(B+L) 破坏道为催化的质子衰变（catalysed proton decay），无阈值且发生概率接近 1。

Conclusion: Callan 对分数态的统计解释以更清晰的形式出现：该态并非两个末态以相等权重的叠加，而是以能量依赖概率衰变到这两个末态的单一态，仅在高能时两概率相等。

Abstract: In the massless limit a fermion scattering on the minimal $SU(5)$ monopole in the lowest partial wave produces an outgoing state with half-integer fermion number in every flavour. This state lives in a twisted sector of the Hilbert space and is now understood as an ordinary fermion attached to a topological line running back to the monopole. In the Standard Model fermion masses and confinement destroy the line at distances greater than the inverse mass scales, causing the twisted state to decay into physical asymptotic states with integer fermion numbers. We study this decay for an incoming lepton or quark of one generation. The cross-section in this partial wave saturates the unitarity bound, $σ=π/p^2$, and the energy of the outgoing state is shared among the four fermion flavours with a broad Dirichlet distribution, which sets the thresholds and the kinematics. Conservation laws allow exactly two decay channels for each incoming fermion, the helicity flip and its $(B+L)$-violating partner. For an incoming electron the $(B+L)$-violating channel is anti-nucleon synthesis, and we determine the branching ratios in two limits: the two channels are equally likely at high energy, and only the helicity flip remains at low energy, below the anti-nucleon threshold. In between we model the energy dependence. For a quark in a slow proton the $(B+L)$-violating channel is catalysed proton decay, which has no threshold and occurs with a probability close to one. Callan's statistical interpretation of the fractional state emerges in a sharper form: the state is not a superposition of two final states with equal weights, but a single state that decays into them with energy-dependent probabilities, equal only at high energy.

</details>

### [203] [Development and Experimental Characterization of a Compact $^{228}$Th--D$_2$O Photoneutron Source for Low-Energy Nuclear Recoil Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16415)
*Yueqiang Tian,Yuxin Cui,Xunan Guo,Zichao Guo,Pingquan Wang,Hui Zhang,Chen Cheng,Xiaopeng Zhou*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该工作研制并表征了一种基于²²⁸Th–D₂O的光致中子源（photoneutron source），通过D(γ,n)p反应将²⁰⁸Tl的2.615 MeV γ射线转换为亚MeV中子，用于液氙探测器（liquid xenon detector）的keV量级核反冲响应刻度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标定液氙探测器在几keV核反冲（nuclear-recoil）能区的响应需要低能中子源，而现有源在该能区存在不足。

Method: 优化D₂O几何结构以平衡中子产生与逃逸效率，并通过实验验证所选构型；利用Bonner球（Bonner sphere）数据展开中子能谱。

Result: 前向发射率为1734 ± 76（统计）± 65（系统）h⁻¹，展开谱集中于0.17–0.22 MeV；在PandaX-20T的220 h曝光中预计可获得约1000个筛选后的核反冲事件。

Conclusion: 结果表明长寿命²²⁸Th–D₂O源适用于大型液氙探测器的低能中子刻度。

Abstract: Calibrating the few-keV nuclear-recoil response of liquid xenon detectors calls for a low-energy neutron source. We have developed and characterized a $^{228}$Th--D$_2$O photoneutron source that converts the 2.615~MeV $γ$ rays from $^{208}$Tl into sub-MeV neutrons through the D($γ$,n)p reaction. After optimizing the D$_2$O geometry to balance neutron production against escape efficiency, we validated the chosen configuration experimentally. The forward emission rate is $1734 \pm 76~(\mathrm{stat.}) \pm 65~(\mathrm{sys.})~\mathrm{h}^{-1}$, and the spectrum unfolded from Bonner sphere data is concentrated at $0.17$--$0.22~\mathrm{MeV}$. In a 220~h exposure of PandaX-20T, the same configuration should provide about 1000 selected nuclear-recoil events. These results support the use of a long-lived $^{228}$Th--D$_2$O source for low-energy neutron calibration of large liquid xenon detectors.

</details>


### [204] [Progress on the MALTA CMOS Sensor: Edge-TCT Measurements for Parametric Pixel Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16552)
*Lucian Fasselt,Prafulla Behera,Dumitru Vlad Berlea,Daniela Bortoletto,Craig Buttar,Theertha Chembakan,Valerio Dao,Ganapati Dash,Yuji Enari,Sebastian Haberl,Tomohiro Inada,Fuat Kerem Isik,Cigdem Issever,Karol Krizka,Long Li,Xuan Li,Heinz Pernegger,Petra Riedler,Walter Snoeys,Carlos Solans Sánchez,Anna Swoboda,Ilkay Turk Cakir,Milou van Rijnbach,Anusree Vijay,Steven Worm*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文总结了单片有源像素传感器（monolithic active pixel sensor, MAPS）MALTA2 在表征与仿真方面的最新进展：利用数字读出与边缘瞬态电流技术（Edge Transient Current Technique, Edge-TCT）实现全像素矩阵边缘的二维电荷收集参数化，并将其作为参数化像素仿真的输入，仿真结果经测试束数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MALTA2 面向高计数率环境下的抗辐照应用，需要在芯片设计与系统级研究中准确描述电荷收集行为；以往工作仅能借助矩阵边缘特设的模拟输出像素进行研究，覆盖范围受限，因而需要一种可获取整个像素矩阵边缘信息的表征手段与配套仿真工具。

Method: 采用 Edge-TCT 研究外延硅（epitaxial silicon）传感器的电荷收集，使用数字读出并仅由二值命中数据（binary hit data）重建电荷量；通过对相邻像素进行激光扫描（laser scans）获得电荷收集的二维参数化，再将该参数化作为输入构建参数化像素仿真（parametric pixel simulation）。

Result: 数字读出使测量得以覆盖完整的像素矩阵边缘，激光扫描实现了电荷收集的二维参数化；所建立的参数化像素仿真可用于芯片设计优化与系统级研究，其仿真结果与测试束（test beam）数据相符。

Conclusion: 基于数字读出的 Edge-TCT 表征与参数化像素仿真相结合，为 MALTA2 的芯片设计优化和系统级研究提供了经实验验证的可行途径。

Abstract: MALTA2 is a monolithic active pixel sensor, developed in the 180 nm CMOS imaging technology for radiation-hard applications in high-rate environments. This contribution summarizes recent developments in the characterization and simulation of MALTA2. The charge collection of a sensor on epitaxial silicon is studied with the Edge Transient Current Technique (Edge-TCT), using a digital readout, where the charge is reconstructed solely from binary hit data. In previous works, only specially designed analog output pixels at the matrix periphery were investigated. In contrast, the digital readout gives access to the full edge of the pixel matrix. Laser scans across neighboring pixels enable a two-dimensional parameterization of the charge collection. This parameterization serves as input to a parametric pixel simulation, which has been developed for chip design optimization and system-level studies. The simulation results are validated with test beam data.

</details>


<div id='math.GT'></div>

# math.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Gluing Geometric Chains Of Manifolds And Orbifolds With Corners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15551)
*Hao Yu*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 本文用粘合关系（gluing relation）替代原有的退化链消灭关系（relation D），证明几何链复形在紧多面体与无边光滑流形上于整系数与有理系数下均计算奇异同调（singular homology），且该关系仅由链的源（sources）定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有结果中关系 D 需通过消灭退化链定义，其定义不单依赖源空间，导致诸如 d 重覆盖与基链 d 倍的等价、Morse 复形链映射的修正项等在模 D 意义下不成立或退化为零，故需要一个更自然且仅由源决定的关系。

Method: 以粘合关系替代 D：当源沿整条余维一的面（codimension-one faces）由有限多块粘合而成时，将链等同于其各块限制之和。证明采用公理化方式：单射性来自积分或对偶胞腔（dual cells），满射性来自沿正则水平的切割（切割后仍是粘合关系），并用到从属于角层（corner strata）的带角流形 Whitehead 三角剖分。

Result: 在该关系下，从奇异同调到几何链复形的比较映射（comparison map）对紧多面体与无边光滑流形在所有次数上为同构，且对有理系数与整系数均成立；粘合关系使覆盖的 d 倍恒等式与 Morse 复形链映射的修正项成为粘合恒等式。

Conclusion: 粘合关系可作为 D 的有效替代，并在底层空间上读出时进一步适用于 orbifold 源（整系数，模去带定向反转自同构的类）与有效 orbifold 靶（effective orbifold targets）。

Abstract: Geometric chains are formal combinations of smooth maps from compact oriented manifolds or orbifolds with corners. In earlier work the author showed that, modulo a relation D that kills degenerate chains, they compute rational singular homology. We show that D can be replaced by a gluing relation, which identifies a chain with the sum of its restrictions to finitely many pieces whenever its source is glued from these pieces along whole codimension-one faces. With this relation the comparison map from singular homology is an isomorphism in all degrees for compact polyhedra and smooth manifolds without boundary, with rational and with integer coefficients. Unlike D, the gluing relation is defined through the sources alone, and chain-level identities such as the equivalence of a degree d covering with d times its base, which fails modulo D, and the correction terms of the chain map from a Morse complex, which are degenerate modulo D, become gluing relations. The proof is axiomatic; injectivity comes from integration or dual cells, surjectivity from cutting along regular levels, where the cut of a gluing relation is again one, and at the point we use Whitehead triangulations of manifolds with corners subordinate to their corner strata. The gluing relation, read on underlying spaces, also suffices for orbifold sources, with integer coefficients modulo classes with an orientation reversing automorphism, and for effective orbifold targets.

</details>

### [206] [Hands-on fabrication of 2D material field-effect transistors for the classroom using off-the-shelf ionic gel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14565)
*Yigit Sozen,Tayfun Kutlu,Juan J. Riquelme,Andres Castellanos-Gomez*

Main category: physics.ed-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种低成本、无需洁净室的手工实验方法，利用离子凝胶门控（ionic-gel gating）与机械剥离的MoS2薄片制备范德华半导体场效应晶体管（FET），适用于本科与早期研究生教学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统范德华材料FET制备依赖洁净室（cleanroom）、真空沉积（vacuum deposition）与定制电解质，门槛高，难以在常规教学实验室中开展。

Method: 采用商用 hydrogel 导体作为离子凝胶门控介质，使用商用薄膜叉指电极阵列（interdigitated electrode arrays），并以高通量机械剥离（mechanical exfoliation）制备的MoS2薄片作为半导体沟道，实现FET的制备与电学表征。

Result: 代表性电学测量显示沟道电导（channel conductance）受栅极电压调控，并呈现离子门控晶体管（ionic-gated transistor）工作的典型特征。

Conclusion: 该平台为学生在不依赖传统半导体代工厂（semiconductor foundry）的条件下自主制备、表征与分析场效应器件提供了可行路径，可作为电子学与固体物理课程的实践补充。

Abstract: We introduce a hands-on experimental method that enables students to fabricate and operate van der Waals semiconductor-based field-effect transistors (FETs) using readily available materials and minimal infrastructure. The approach is based on ionic-gel gating using commercially available hydrogel conductors originally developed for muscle electrostimulation devices. For device fabrication we rely on commercially available thin-film interdigitated electrode arrays and for the semiconductor channel, we deposited MoS2 flakes produced by high-throughput mechanical exfoliation. By avoiding the need for cleanroom processing, vacuum deposition, or custom electrolytes, this method allows functional FETs based on van der Waals materials to be realized in a standard teaching laboratory. The experiment is well suited for undergraduate and early graduate courses and is intended to complement concepts introduced in electronics and solid-state physics by providing students with first-hand experience in the fabrication and electrical characterization of emerging electronic devices. Representative electrical measurements demonstrate gate-dependent modulation of the channel conductance and illustrate characteristic features of ionic-gated transistor operation. Overall, the proposed platform provides an accessible route for students to fabricate, characterize, and analyze field-effect devices without access to conventional semiconductor foundry.

</details>

### [207] [Reinforcement learning to design near-perfect acoustic absorbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15422)
*M. Novelli,R. Arnau,L. M. Garcia-Raffi,V. Romero-García*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 将强化学习（reinforcement learning）用于亥姆霍兹共振器（Helmholtz resonator）的多目标设计，结合传递矩阵法建模、有限元全波仿真与3D打印阻抗管实验，实现近完美吸声的紧凑吸声体自动设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亥姆霍兹共振器设计需在体积、共振频率与声反射等多个相互竞争的目标间权衡，传统设计流程依赖经验与反复试算，缺乏自动化的多目标优化手段。

Method: 把共振器设计表述为序贯决策问题（sequential decision-making），几何参数迭代更新，采用基于神经网络（neural network）的Q学习（Q-learning）探索设计空间并获取非支配解（non-dominated solutions）构成帕累托前沿（Pareto front）；声学响应由含黏热损耗（viscothermal losses）与声学末端修正（acoustic end corrections）的传递矩阵法（transfer matrix method）评估。

Result: 三个代表性设计经全波有限元（finite element）仿真验证，吸收与反射谱与降阶模型吻合良好；其中一个优化共振器经三维打印（3D printing）并在阻抗管（impedance tube）中实测，共振频率附近吸收系数约为95%，与数值预测一致。

Conclusion: 结果证明强化学习可用于紧凑吸声体的自动化设计，并建立了融合多目标优化、数值建模与实验验证的高计算效率框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is proposed as a strategy for the multi-objective design of Helmholtz resonators with near-perfect acoustic absorption. The approach formulates resonator design as a sequential decision-making problem in which geometrical parameters are iteratively updated to balance competing objectives, including resonator volume, resonance frequency, and acoustic reflection. A Q-learning algorithm based on a neural network is developed to explore the design space and identify non-dominated solutions, providing a Pareto front of optimal configurations. The acoustic response is evaluated using a transfer matrix method that incorporates viscothermal losses and acoustic end corrections. Three representative designs are subsequently investigated using full-wave finite element simulations, showing good agreement in their absorption and reflection spectra and validating the accuracy of the reduced-order model. Finally, one optimized resonator is fabricated by three-dimensional printing and experimentally characterized in an impedance tube. The measured absorption coefficient reaches approximately 95\% near the resonance frequency, in agreement with the numerical predictions. These results demonstrate the potential of reinforcement learning for the automated design of compact acoustic absorbers and establish a computationally efficient framework combining multi-objective optimization, numerical modelling, and experimental validation.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Randomly growing uniform hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14824)
*P. L. Krapivsky*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究了超边为 $(k-1)$-单纯形的不断增长的均匀随机超图（uniform random hypergraph），发现其渗流转变属于 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless 型，巨连通分量在 $p<p_c=1-(2k-1)^{-2}$ 时出现且占比按 $\exp[-C/\sqrt{p_c-p}]$ 标度，并刻画了巨分量的拓扑不变量（Euler 示性数与 Betti 数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关注超图（hypergraph）这一超越成对相互作用的网络结构，探究其增长过程中渗流（percolation）行为与拓扑性质，填补单纯形构建的随机超图在相变与同调特征方面的认识空白。

Method: 构造增长模型：每步以概率 $p$ 添加新顶点，以概率 $1-p$ 添加由随机选择的互异顶点构成的 $k$ 顶点单纯形（simplex）。先详细分析三元超图（$k=3$）再推广至一般 $k$，考察巨分量占比、簇尺寸分布及 Euler 示性数（Euler characteristic）与 Betti 数。

Result: 巨分量占比呈 $\exp[-C/\sqrt{p_c-p}]$ 的非解析标度，是 BKT 相变的标志；非渗流相中簇尺寸分布为指数连续变化的代数尾（algebraic tail），临界点处出现对数修正。巨分量的 Euler 示性数与第一 Betti 数随系统尺寸线性增长，高阶 Betti 数在实现间涨落但保持有限。

Conclusion: 由于高阶 Betti 数有界，巨分量的归一化 Euler 示性数与第一 Betti 数在渐近意义上相等（至多相差一个符号），说明渗流超图在临界附近的拓扑结构主要由一维同调性质决定。

Abstract: We investigate growing uniform random hypergraphs whose hyperedges are $(k-1)$-simplices. At each step, one of two processes occurs: (i) a new vertex is added with probability $p$; (ii) a simplex $\langle v_1,\ldots,v_k\rangle$ with randomly chosen distinct vertices is added with probability $1-p$. Emerging random uniform hypergraphs exhibit a percolation transition. A giant component is formed when $p<p_c=1-(2k-1)^{-2}$. The fraction of vertices in the giant component scales as $\exp[-C/\sqrt{p_c-p}]$, a signature of the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless phase transition. In the non-percolating phase, the cluster size distribution has an algebraic tail with a continuously varying exponent, while at $p=p_c$, it acquires a logarithmic correction. We analyze triadic hypergraphs ($k=3$) in detail and then generalize to higher $k$. We show that the Euler characteristic and the first Betti number of the giant component scale linearly with system size. Higher Betti numbers fluctuate between realizations but remain finite, and hence the normalized Euler characteristic and the first Betti number of the giant component are asymptotically equal, up to a sign.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Irreversible dynamics for ideal fluids on Riemannian manifolds: metriplectic energy-conserving alternatives to Navier-Stokes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17049)
*Erwin Luesink,Archishman Saha,Tanya Schmah,Cristina Stoica*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文基于度规辛（metriplectic/GENERIC）框架，通过在哈密顿向量场上叠加由对称括号（由黎曼度规构造）生成的耗散项，构造了能量守恒而广义熵单调演化的不可逆动力学，并应用于紧黎曼流形上的理想流体，在二维重现了能量约束的Navier-Stokes方程，在三维导出新的不可逆模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈密顿（Hamiltonian）框架在建模摩擦、扩散、粘性等耗散现象时存在关键局限：一旦卡西米尔不变量（Casimir invariants）固定，哈密顿流保持相空间体积，无法描述向平衡态的弛豫。因此，刻画耗散需要修改哈密顿结构。

Method: 采用度规辛（metriplectic，等价于GENERIC）方法，在哈密顿向量场上添加由对称括号生成的耗散项，该括号由黎曼度规（Riemannian metric）构造；所得流保持能量及可选的其他不变量，同时单调驱动选定的广义熵。将该构造应用于紧黎曼流形（compact Riemannian manifolds）上的理想流体，以李-泊松括号（Lie-Poisson bracket）的卡西米尔量作为熵。

Result: 二维情形下，耗散拟能（enstrophy）得到已知的能量约束Navier-Stokes方程，其中包含恢复能量平衡的非局部项。三维情形下拟能不再是卡西米尔量，由此导出新的不可逆模型：对携带平流标量的不可压缩与可压缩流体，耗散势拟能（potential enstrophy）或更高阶势涡度矩会产生非线性各向异性扩散（anisotropic diffusion），使标量梯度与势涡度梯度趋于对齐；对纯不可压缩流体，以螺旋度（helicity）为熵得到一阶非光滑化耗散项，其定常态为Beltrami场。

Conclusion: 当熵在能量水平集上沿其被驱动方向有界时，它充当Lyapunov泛函，从而保证不可逆性。该构造统一了已知的二维耗散流体模型并系统给出三维新的不可逆流体模型，展示了度规辛方法在耗散现象建模中的适用性。

Abstract: For modelling dissipative phenomena such as friction, diffusion and viscosity, the Hamiltonian framework has a key limitation: once the Casimir invariants are fixed, Hamiltonian flows preserve phase-space volume, which rules out relaxation to equilibrium. Thus modelling dissipation requires modifying the Hamiltonian structure. Following the metriplectic (equivalently, GENERIC) approach, we add to the Hamiltonian vector field a dissipative term generated by a symmetric bracket, here constructed from a Riemannian metric. The resulting flow conserves the energy, and optionally further invariants, while driving a chosen generalised entropy monotonically, which makes the dynamics irreversible; when the entropy is bounded on energy level sets in the direction in which it is driven, it acts as a Lyapunov functional. We apply this construction to ideal fluids on compact Riemannian manifolds, choosing Casimirs of the Lie-Poisson bracket as entropies. In two dimensions, dissipating enstrophy yields the already-known energy-constrained Navier-Stokes equations, which include a nonlocal term restoring energy balance. In three dimensions, where enstrophy is no longer a Casimir, we derive new irreversible models. For incompressible and compressible fluids carrying an advected scalar, dissipating potential enstrophy or higher potential-vorticity moments gives a nonlinear, anisotropic diffusion that acts to align the gradients of the scalar and of the potential vorticity. For a purely incompressible fluid, taking helicity as the entropy gives a first-order, non-smoothing dissipative term whose stationary states are Beltrami fields.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.IM'></div>

# astro-ph.IM [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [MAGI (Multivariate Autoencoder for particle Generative Inference): a generative Machine Learning framework for accelerated multi-stage particle simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14710)
*Francesco Monastra,Marco Pietrosanto,Giuseppe Angora,Stefano Cavuoti,Francesco Fenu,Simone Lotti,Matteo Perri*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: MAGI 是一种条件变分自编码器（conditional variational autoencoder），用于学习粒子穿越闭合表面时的能量、位置与方向联合分布，并将其重采样注入小型质量模型，从而规避大质量包围小灵敏体积时的粒子输运计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛粒子模拟（Monte Carlo particle simulations）的主要瓶颈在于粒子输运（particle transport）的计算成本，当大质量结构包围小灵敏体积时尤为突出。

Method: MAGI 在多阶段模拟中学习粒子穿越闭合表面的能量、位置和方向联合分布，生成描述后续粒子特征的穿越变量（crossings），再注入同一个 Geant4 质量模型，并与完整模拟在探测器层面的能谱与计数进行对比验证。

Result: 在基准模型上，每次注入穿越的率与 1 在约 1σ 内一致；统计放大因子（statistical amplification）1/χ 从高统计宇宙射线（cosmic rays）的 150 提升至稀有放射性衰变（radioactive decays）的 12000，计算加速比（speed-up）S 依源类型与质量模型不同介于 2.1 至近 240 之间。

Conclusion: 上述首批结果验证了发布于 GitHub 的 MAGI v1.0.0 的有效性。

Abstract: Monte Carlo particle simulations are hindered by the computational cost of particle transport. This is especially true when large mass surrounds a small sensitive volume. MAGI is a conditional variational autoencoder that learns the joint energy, position and direction distribution of particles crossing a closed surface in a multi-stage simulation, to be resampled and re-injected in a smaller mass model. We validated it generating crossings, variables describing particle features later in the simulation, injecting them into the same Geant4 mass model and comparing the spectrum and counts at detector level with full simulation. Rates per injected crossing agree with unity at $\sim1σ$ on our benchmark models. We measure the gain of MAGI with statistical amplification $1/χ$ and computational speed-up $S$: the first goes from 150 for high-statistics cosmic rays to 12000 for rare radioactive decays, while the latter ranges from 2.1 to almost 240, depending on source type and mass model. These first results validate MAGI v1.0.0 released on GitHub.

</details>


### [211] [Accurate calibration method of the Back-linked Fabry Perot interferometer using null channel for space-based gravitational wave antenna](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14788)
*Yusuke Okuma,Shoki Iwaguchi,Ryosuke Sugimoto,Yutaro Enomoto,Kiwamu Izumi*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文针对后向链接法布里-珀罗干涉仪（Back-linked Fabry-Perot Interferometer, BLFPI）提出一种基于零通道（null channel）的腔响应标定方法，利用三角激光链路三个干涉仪输出构建零通道，通过最小化零通道积分功率来估计腔响应参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLFPI 利用法布里-珀罗腔（Fabry-Perot cavity）实现高引力波（gravitational-wave, GW）灵敏度，但残余标定误差会直接限制激光频率噪声的抑制，而目前尚无实用的腔响应标定策略。

Method: 由三角激光链路配置的三个干涉仪输出构建零通道，使 GW 信号相互抵消，残余激光频率噪声即反映腔响应估计误差；通过优化响应参数最小化零通道积分功率来估计腔响应，并解析推导技术噪声引起的估计偏差，再进行数值性能评估。

Result: 标定后的残余激光频率噪声在整个观测频段内低于 BLFPI 设计灵敏度一个数量级以上，表明该方法达到了有效扣除激光频率噪声所需的精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的零通道标定方法可满足 BLFPI 中激光频率噪声有效扣除的精度要求，为腔响应的实用标定提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate calibration of the detector response is essential for precision gravitational-wave (GW) observations. In the Back-linked Fabry-Perot Interferometer (BLFPI), which employs Fabry-Perot cavities to achieve high GW sensitivity, accurate calibration of the cavity response is particularly important because residual calibration errors directly limit the suppression of laser frequency noise. However, no practical calibration strategy for the cavity response has yet been established. In this paper, we propose a method for calibrating the cavity response using a null channel constructed from the three interferometer outputs of the triangular laser-link configuration. In null channel, GW signals are canceled and residual laser frequency noise indicates errors in the cavity response estimates. Therefore we can estimate the cavity response by optimizing the response parameters to minimize the integrated power of the null channel. We derive the estimation bias introduced by technical noise analytically and evaluate the performance of the proposed method numerically. The results show that the residual laser frequency noise after calibration is more than an order of magnitude below the designed BLFPI sensitivity throughout the observation band. This demonstrates that the proposed null-channel calibration method achieves the accuracy required for effective subtraction of laser frequency noise.

</details>

### [212] [Search for gravitational wave coincident neutrinos in DEAP-3600](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15821)
*P. Adhikari,P. -A. Amaudruz,D. J. Auty,M. Batygov,B. Beltran,S. Belayneh Habtemariam,M. A. Bigentini,C. E. Bina,W. Bonivento,M. G. Boulay,J. Brachman,B. Broerman,J. F. Bueno,M. Cadeddu,B. Cai,M. Cárdenas-Montes,N. Cargioli,S. Cavuoti,S. Choudhary,B. T. Cleveland,R. Crampton,R. Curtis,S. Daugherty,P. Di Stefano,G. Dolganov,L. Doria,F. A. Duncan,M. Dunford,E. Ellingwood,A. Erlandson,S. S. Farahani,N. Fatemighomi,L. Ferro,G. Fiorillo,R. J. Ford,A. Garai,P. García Abia,S. Garg,P. Giampa,D. Goeldi,P. Gorel,K. Graham,A. L. Hallin,M. Hamstra,S. Haskins,T. Hoyte,J. Hu,J. Hucker,D. Huff,T. Hugues,A. Ilyasov,B. Jigmeddorj,C. J. Jillings,G. Kaur,A. Kemp,M. Khoshraftar Yazdi,G. Killaire,M. Kuźniak,F. La Zia,M. Lai,S. Langrock,B. Lehnert,M. Lissia,L. Luzzi,I. Machulin,A. Maru,J. Mason,A. B. McDonald,T. McElroy,J. B. McLaughlin,C. Mielnichuk,S. Mishra,S. Mohanty,A. Moharana,J. Monroe,A. Murray,M. Needs,C. Ng,G. Nieradka,G. Oliviéro,M. Olszewski,S. Pal,D. Papi,B. Park,R. Pavarani,M. Perry,V. Pesudo,T. R. Pollmann,F. Rad,C. Rethmeier,F. Retière,L. Roszkowski,R. Santorelli,F. G. Schuckman,M. Sestu,S. Seth,V. Shalamova,P. Skensved,T. Smirnova,K. Sobotkiewich,T. Sonley,J. Sosiak,J. Soukup,R. Stainforth,M. Stringer,J. Tang,P. Taylor,C. Tierney,S. Tullio,R. Turcotte-Tardif,E. Vázquez-Jáuregui,G. Vera Díaz,S. Viel,B. Vyas,J. Walding,M. Ward,S. Westerdale,R. Wormington*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于DEAP-3600液氩探测器2016—2020年数据，对LVK观测到的7次含中子星致密天体并合事件搜寻时间符合的中微子信号，未发现统计显著证据，并给出电子中微子注量（fluence）排除上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星并合预期产生中微子（neutrino），但迄今从未被探测到；利用引力波（gravitational wave）事件提供的时空指向与时标，可开展符合搜寻（coincident search）。DEAP-3600 近期通过 $^{40}$Ar 靶上的带电电流中微子吸收（charged current neutrino absorption）对电子中微子具备灵敏度。

Method: 在2016—2020年间DEAP-3600记录的数据中，针对LVK（LIGO、Virgo、KAGRA）引力波观测到的7次含中子星并合事件，开展时间符合的中微子信号搜寻。

Result: 对所分析的全部并合事件均无统计显著的中微子证据；据此设定电子中微子注量上限，例如GW170817在单能模型（mono-energetic model）下，10 MeV中微子为$6.8\times10^{12}~\text{cm}^{-2}$，100 MeV为$1.8\times10^{10}~\text{cm}^{-2}$。

Conclusion: 结果不支持所分析并合事件存在可探测的中微子发射，并以注量排除上限形式对相关模型给出约束。

Abstract: We searched DEAP-3600 data for neutrino signals coincident with gravitational waves from compact object mergers observed by the LIGO, Virgo, and KAGRA collaborations (LVK). While mergers involving neutron stars are expected to produce neutrinos, these have never been detected. The DEAP-3600 experiment is sensitive to electron neutrinos as recently observed through charged current neutrino absorption on its $^{40}$Ar target. Between 2016 and 2020, the detector recorded data during seven compact merger events involving neutron stars that were observed gravitationally by LVK. Upon examination, there is no statistically significant evidence of neutrinos from any of the mergers analyzed. Exclusion limits are set on the electron neutrino fluence, e.g. from GW170817 in the mono-energetic model at $6.8\times10^{12}~\text{cm$^{-2}$}$ ($1.8\times10^{10}~\text{cm$^{-2}$}$) for 10 MeV (100 MeV) neutrinos.

</details>


### [213] [Impact of dynamical, quadratic, and higher-order tidal effects on gravitational-wave observations of binary neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14628)
*Andrea Valletta,Francesco Crescimbeni,Takuya Katagiri,Francesco Pannarale,Paolo Pani*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文将动力学潮汐（dynamical tides）与二次Love数（quadratic Love numbers）的非线性贡献纳入后牛顿（post-Newtonian）波形，最高至8PN阶，并评估其对中子星双星引力波信号及潮汐形变率（tidal deformability）推断的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星双星是探测超核密度核物质性质的重要引力波源，但需精确波形模型刻画晚期旋近（late inspiral）阶段的潮汐效应；现有低阶PN潮汐项可能不足以支撑精密测量。

Method: 在post-Newtonian旋近波形中引入最先进的潮汐修正，包括动力学潮汐和quadratic Love numbers的非线性贡献，扩展至8PN阶；采用贝叶斯模型比较（Bayesian model comparison），并以类GW170817双星及第三代探测器（如Einstein Telescope）为场景进行评估。

Result: 这些修正可显著影响引力波信号；在亚太阳质量（subsolar-mass）区间，8PN潮汐响应与低阶PN潮汐贡献相当。对第三代探测器观测的极亮类GW170817双星，高阶潮汐效应可以高统计显著性被区分；忽略它们会给潮汐形变率推断及中子星状态方程（equation of state）约束带来显著偏差。亚太阳质量双星因组分致密度（compactness）较低而潮汐效应增强，即使在当前探测器下也可能重要。

Conclusion: 高阶潮汐修正对致密核物质的精密引力波测量具有重要性，应在波形建模与参数推断中予以纳入。

Abstract: Neutron-star binaries are prime gravitational-wave sources for current and next-generation detectors, and provide a unique probe for the behaviour of nuclear matter at supranuclear densities. Extracting information about this requires accurate waveform models that capture tidal effects during the late inspiral. We incorporate state-of-the-art tidal corrections into a post-Newtonian inspiral waveform, including dynamical tides and nonlinear contributions from quadratic Love numbers through eighth post-Newtonian (8PN) order. Using Bayesian model comparison, we find that these corrections can significantly affect the gravitational-wave signal, with the 8PN tidal response becoming comparable to lower-PN tidal contributions in the subsolar-mass regime. For exceptionally loud GW170817-like binaries observed by third-generation detectors such as the Einstein Telescope, higher-order tidal effects can be distinguished with high statistical significance, while neglecting them can introduce substantial biases in the inferred tidal deformabilities and, consequently, in constraints on the neutron-star equation of state. These corrections are particularly relevant for putative subsolar-mass binaries: the lower compactness of their constituents enhances tidal effects and makes them potentially important even for current detectors. Our results highlight the importance of higher-order tidal corrections for precision gravitational-wave measurements of dense nuclear matter.

</details>


### [214] [Magnetically Deformed Neutron Stars and Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15345)
*Remo Ruffini,Sang Pyo Kim,Yu Wang,Shurui Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在牛顿引力框架下研究毫秒至秒级自转、磁场达 $10^{12}$–$10^{18}$ G 的中子星与磁星几何形状，解析给出轴对称情形下的扁球体以及磁轴与自转轴不平行时的三轴椭球体形状、倾角与连续引力波振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星与强磁场磁星的形状（扁率、三轴性）直接影响其四极矩、连续引力波辐射与自转减速，而磁场接近 Chandrasekhar-Fermi 位力标度时现有弱场近似不再适用，需要任意偏心率和任意磁倾角的解析描述。

Method: 在牛顿理论中取均匀不可压缩流体、内部无电流均匀磁场经面电流匹配外部偶极场；对对齐轴情形，在固定质量、体积、角动量与冻结磁通量下最小化总引力、转动与磁能；对错轴情形引入四极形状张量（quadrupolar shape tensor）并对其对角化，用扁球调和函数求磁能的闭合形式。

Result: 对齐情形得到扁球体，其椭率为转动与磁贡献之和，弱场下回到 Ferraro 极限，大形变时磁权重依赖偏心率；错轴情形得到主轴被磁压倾斜的三轴椭球，倾角与偏心率以转动、磁偏心率及错位角的解析函数给出，弱场形变正比于 $P_2(\cos\alpha)$，并推广为转动主导的 Ferraro-Maclaurin 关系。

Conclusion: 弱场下三轴偏心率由 Ferraro 偏心率与转动、磁偏心率二阶调和平均决定；所得形变参数给出四极矩，从而确定该三轴位形的连续引力波振幅、光度和自转减速力矩，为强磁场磁星的引力波探测提供解析模型。

Abstract: We study the geometric figures of neutron stars and magnetars with spin periods from milliseconds to seconds and magnetic fields from $10^{12}$-$10^{15}$ G to hypothetical values near the Chandrasekhar-Fermi virial scale, $10^{18}$ G. In Newtonian theory, we find the equilibrium figure of a rotating incompressible fluid with a current-free uniform interior magnetic field matched to an exterior dipole field by surface currents. For aligned axes, an oblate spheroid is obtained by minimizing the total gravitational, rotational and magnetic energy at fixed mass, volume, angular momentum and frozen magnetic flux. Its ellipticity is the sum of rotational and magnetic contributions, recovers the Ferraro limit for a weak magnetic field, and has a magnetic weight that depends on the eccentricity for large deformation. For misaligned magnetic and spin axes, we introduce a quadrupolar shape tensor and diagonalize it to find a triaxial ellipsoid whose principal axes are tilted by magnetic pressure. The tilt angle and eccentricities are computed analytically as functions of the rotational and magnetic eccentricities and the misalignment angle. The magnetic energy of the misaligned configuration is obtained in closed form in oblate spheroidal harmonics for arbitrary eccentricity and obliquity. Its weak-field deformation, up to $10^{15}$ G, is proportional to $P_2(\cosα)$, which fixes the magnetic forcing tensor, and its finite-eccentricity form gives a generalized Ferraro-Maclaurin relation for rotation-dominated misaligned stars. In the weak-field regime, the triaxial eccentricity is determined by the Ferraro eccentricity and the harmonic mean of order 2 of the rotational and magnetic eccentricities. The deformation parameters give the quadrupole moments and hence the continuous gravitational-wave amplitudes, luminosity and spin-down torque of the triaxial configuration.

</details>


### [215] [Residual ellipticity driven by intense magnetic stress in the hot neutron star era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16636)
*Yasufumi Kojima,Akira Dohi,Kotaro Fujisawa,Shota Kisaka*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究磁致中子星（neutron star）壳层椭率（ellipticity）在凝固前后的演化效应，通过比较凝固前后两个平衡态模型，估计残留椭率约为 \(10^{-8}(B_0/10^{13}\mathrm{G})^2\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非轴对称质量分布的中子星持续辐射引力波（gravitational waves），其振幅取决于形变大小，对引力波天文学（gravitational-wave astronomy）至关重要；磁诱导椭率的长时标演化难以直接模拟，需寻找可处理的估计途径。

Method: 假设凝固前高温阶段由磁流体力学（magneto-hydrodynamics）过程产生大形变，随后因电流衰减与重新分布导致作用力减弱而演化；不模拟长时标形变演化，而是比较凝固前后两个平衡模型（equilibrium models）来估计残留椭率。

Result: 对表面偶极场强度（dipole field strength）\(B_0\)，残留椭率量级为 \(\sim 10^{-8}(B_0/10^{13}\mathrm{G})^2\)；该值主要由后期磁场决定，对初始大形变依赖较弱。

Conclusion: 凝固前状态的记忆（memory）虽有保留，但影响很小，因为弹力（elastic force）对平衡态的贡献有限。

Abstract: A neutron star with a nonaxisymmetric mass distribution continuously radiates gravitational waves. The amplitude depends on the magnitude of the deformation, and is crucial for gravitational-wave astronomy. We studied an evolutionary effect on the magnetically induced ellipticity in the neutron star crust. A large deformation at the hot era before solidification is assumed to be produced magneto-hydrodynamically, and the fate at later time follows owing to the decrease in the force associated with the decay and redistribution of the current. Instead of simulating the evolution of deformation with the magnetic field over a long timescale, we estimate the ellipticity that remains after the formation of the solid crust by comparing two equilibrium models before and after solidification. The relic ellipticity is $\sim 10^{-8} (B_0/10^{13}{\rm{G}})^2$ in the magnitude for dipole field strength $B_{0}$ at the surface. This value was primarily determined by the magnetic field at the later time, with a weak dependence on the initial large deformation. The memory of the presolidification state was retained, but was small because the elastic force contributes little to the equilibrium.

</details>


### [216] [The $w$-mode instability of strangeon ergostars and its impact on energy extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16836)
*Haojia Xia,Hong-Bo Li,Enping Zhou,Ren-Xin Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作研究快速旋转奇异子星（strangeon ergostar）的 $w$-模不稳定性（$w$-mode instability），发现在所考察的能量提取路径上相关模式始终为阻尼态，未提供增长型引力波通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速旋转致密奇异子星被视为短伽马射线暴（short gamma-ray bursts）的替代中心引擎，但非轴对称不稳定性驱动的引力波辐射等竞争性能量损失通道可能削减可供相对论电磁外流使用的功率，故需评估其可行性。

Method: 在Hilbert规范下用谱方法（spectral method）求解耦合的时空与流体微扰方程，不依赖慢旋转近似（slow-rotation approximation），计算十二个代表性奇异子物态方程沿最大能量提取路径上源于非旋转星基频 $l=m=2$ 模的轴向主导与极向主导 $w$-模分支复本征值。

Result: 尽管存在能层（ergoregion），两分支在所有路径上均保持阻尼；通过改变恒星角速度确定不稳定阈值，凡识别出阈值的情形，路径端点角速度均低于临界值，最大比值为 0.926。

Conclusion: 所考察的 $w$-模不稳定性不构成沿能量提取路径增长的引力波辐射通道，支持此前估算的转动能储库可供电磁提取之用。

Abstract: Rapidly rotating compact ergostars have been proposed as alternative central engines for short gamma-ray bursts. However, competing energy-loss channels, such as gravitational-wave emission driven by non-axisymmetric instabilities, could curtail the power available for relativistic electromagnetic outflows. In this work, we investigate the $w$-mode instability of rapidly rotating strangeon ergostars by solving the coupled spacetime and fluid perturbation equations in the Hilbert gauge using a spectral method, without relying on a slow-rotation approximation. We calculate the complex eigenvalues of the axial-led and polar-led $w$-mode branches originating from the fundamental $l=m=2$ modes of nonrotating stars along the maximum-energy-extraction paths of twelve representative strangeon equations of state. We find that both branches remain damped throughout these paths, despite the presence of ergoregions. We further vary the stellar angular velocity to determine the instability thresholds. In every case with an identified threshold, the angular velocity at the corresponding extraction-path endpoint remains below the critical value, with the largest ratio being $0.926$. Our results indicate that the $w$-mode instabilities examined here do not provide a growing gravitational-wave emission channel along the considered energy-extraction paths, supporting the availability of the previously estimated rotational energy reservoirs for electromagnetic extraction.

</details>


### [217] [Lense-Thirring induced repeating blast trains in continuous gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16914)
*Kailib Ryan Doney,Souvik Jana,Otto A. Hannuksela,Paul Martens*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文刻画了绕自旋超大质量黑洞运行的连续引力波源（continuous gravitational-wave source）因Lense-Thirring效应（Lense-Thirring effect）产生的引力透镜（gravitational lensing）现象，预言信号表现为周期性重复的短时引力波"爆发"（blast）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对自旋黑洞周围轨道运动源可能产生的Lense-Thirring致透镜效应，给出该情形的信号特征描述，以识别此类此前未被系统刻画的可观测现象。

Method: 基于Lense-Thirring效应的解析刻画：假设源轨道倾角与黑洞自旋轴不平行，且Lense-Thirring时标短于观测窗口（observational window），据此推导透镜导致的信号时间结构。

Result: 信号表现为持续时间 \(T_{\rm blast}\) 的短促引力波爆发，在每个轨道周期 \(T_{\rm orb}\) 后重复，并集中出现在有限时间窗 \(T_{\rm train}\) 内；爆发强度取决于系统几何构型而非黑洞质量；在一个完整Lense-Thirring进动周期内信号会两次静默并重新出现。

Conclusion: Lense-Thirring致透镜可产生特征鲜明的重复型爆发信号，其发生条件与信号特征可用于判别该物理场景，并为相关引力波观测提供理论依据。

Abstract: We characterise gravitational lensing induced by the Lense-Thirring effect in a continuous gravitational-wave source orbiting a spinning supermassive black hole. This scenario is very likely to generate lensing events, as long as the source's orbital inclination is not aligned with the black hole's spin axis and the Lense-Thirring timescale is shorter than the observational window. The signal appears as short-duration ($T_{\rm blast}$) gravitational-wave "blasts" that repeat after every orbit ($T_{\rm orb}$) within a limited time window ($T_{\rm train}$). The blasts' signal strength depends on the system's geometry and not on the black hole mass. The signal will go silent and renew twice in a full Lense-Thirring precession period.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Controlled preparation and unconditional transport on a fluctuating quantum graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14588)
*Xianbin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在指定方形候选格点的一探针模型上，结合广义 Rokhsar--Kivelson 母态、谱约化、绝热制备与局域动力学估计，证明可无条件以至少 5/8 的概率将信号送达指定接收区域，并在局域误差界下保持 9/16 的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lieb--Robinson 界（Lieb--Robinson bounds）只限制量子信息传播速度，并不能保证给定输入以可观概率被制备并传出；对边涨落的量子图（quantum graphs），还需控制图相干性、有限边界与制备误差。

Method: 在指定方形候选格点上构建一探针模型（one-probe model），组合广义 Rokhsar--Kivelson 母态（generalized Rokhsar--Kivelson parent）、谱约化（spectral reduction）、绝热制备（adiabatic preparation）与局域动力学估计；在给定相干图基态前提下构造有限源波包与跃迁斜坡（hopping ramp），填充群速度严格为正的孤立支。

Result: 证明在足够大的有限开放方形中，指定接收区域的无条件到达概率至少为 5/8；显式的局域联合态与哈密顿量误差界使下界保持 9/16，且无需全局多体态接近或对图测量做后选择（postselection）。另有精确算术证书与小系统数值诊断支持该定量分析。

Conclusion: 该构造为涨落量子网络中的输运与读出提供模型特定的基准，将传播机制与可观测下界相连，并给出保持该下界所需的局域误差预算；区分了“抑制不良影响”与“保证信号投递”。资源估计偏保守，假设图态可由外部提供，且结果不确立无背景几何或引力动力学。

Abstract: Lieb--Robinson bounds limit the spread of quantum information, but do not establish that a prescribed input can be prepared and transmitted with appreciable probability. For quantum graphs with fluctuating edges, connecting spectral propagation to unconditional readout also requires control of graph coherence, finite boundaries, and preparation errors. We address this problem in a one-probe model on a prescribed square candidate lattice, combining a generalized Rokhsar--Kivelson parent, spectral reduction, adiabatic preparation, and local dynamical estimates. Assuming a supplied coherent graph ground state, we construct a finite-source wave packet and a hopping ramp that populate an isolated branch with strictly positive group velocity. We prove an unconditional arrival probability of at least $5/8$ in a specified receiver region of a sufficiently large finite open square. Explicit bounds on local joint-state and Hamiltonian errors retain a lower probability of $9/16$, without requiring global many-body state closeness or postselection on graph measurements. Exact arithmetic certificates and a separate small-system numerical diagnostic support the quantitative analysis. The construction provides a model-specific benchmark for transport and readout in fluctuating quantum networks, linking a propagation mechanism to an observable lower bound and identifying the local error budgets required to preserve it. It distinguishes suppression of unwanted influence from guaranteed signal delivery, a useful separation for quantum-network and emergent-geometry models. The resource estimates are conservative, graph-state supply is assumed, and the result does not establish background-free geometry or gravitational dynamics.

</details>


### [219] [Local enhancement of finite-strength code preparation: recoverability and Wilson coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14967)
*Tong Xu,Yuanchen Zhao,Dong E. Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究有限测量强度下稳定子测量（stabilizer measurements）导致的编码映射相干形变问题，指出非局域长程序信号不足以保证编码信息受保护，并提出局域性增强测量方案的修复条件与维度区分判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子测量是制备量子纠错码（quantum error-correcting codes）与长程纠缠态的直接途径，但实际中有限测量强度会使编码映射偏离投影极限；已有的非局域长程序特征并不足以保证编码量子信息受到保护，需明确其失效机制。

Method: 在固定测量强度下分析局域逻辑泄漏（local logical leakage）沿逻辑算符支撑的累积效应；进而构造仅在选定逻辑支撑附近增强消失比例校验（checks）的局域方案，并在环面寄存器（toric registers）与弱局域随机误差下证明恢复性；最后在三维解禁闭相（deconfined phase）与二维情形中比较宏观分离的逻辑代表元的等价性。

Result: 固定强度下局域逻辑泄漏阻碍渐近忠实恢复（asymptotically faithful recovery），其累积迫使相关测量强度随码距（code distance）对数增长；仅增强选定逻辑支撑附近消失比例的校验即可恢复可恢复性，而体区域（bulk）仍保持有限测量强度；三维解禁闭相中增强管（enhanced tubes）可恢复宏观分离代表元的等价性，二维中可恢复性本身不足以恢复该等价性。

Conclusion: 有限测量强度下的编码保护不能由长程序信号单独保证，局域逻辑泄漏构成本质障碍，但可通过局域稀疏的测量增强予以消除；逻辑操作的等价性存在维度区分：三维中增强管可实现渐近等价的受控逻辑作用（controlled logical action），二维中即使两个编码均可恢复，代表元仍可能不等价。

Abstract: Stabilizer measurements provide a direct route to preparing quantum error-correcting codes and long-range entangled states. In realistic implementations, however, finite measurement strength can coherently deform the encoding map away from the projective limit. Certain nonlocal signatures associated with long-range order can survive such a deformation, but this alone does not guarantee that the resulting state protects encoded quantum information. We show that, at fixed strength, local logical leakage obstructs asymptotically faithful recovery; along logical operators' supports, its accumulation forces the relevant measurement strength to grow logarithmically with code distance. We then prove that this obstruction can be removed locally: for fixed toric registers under sufficiently weak local-stochastic errors, enhancing only a vanishing fraction of checks near selected logical supports restores recovery while the bulk remains at finite measurement strength. With recoverability restored, we prove a further dimensional distinction in logical operations. In the strict three-dimensional deconfined phase, suitable enhanced tubes restore the equivalence of macroscopically separated representatives, so either representative asymptotically implements the same controlled logical action on a fixed encoding. In two dimensions, recoverability alone does not restore this equivalence: the representatives can remain inequivalent even when both associated encodings are recoverable.

</details>


### [220] [Subadditivity and the failure of strong subadditivity in the Nichols--Wootters family of entropies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15431)
*Lin Zhang,Wenlin Zhang,Haobo Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明 Nichols–Wootters 熵族（von Neumann 熵与 subentropy 之间的插值）在共同环境维度下满足次可加性（subadditivity），并完全刻画等号条件；同时证明除 von Neumann 熵外的所有成员均不满足强次可加性（strong subadditivity），并给出其缺陷的定量分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决关于 subentropy 的次可加性猜想（subadditivity conjecture），并厘清该熵族在强次可加性等基本性质上的行为。

Method: 在同一环境维度下统一评价各谱；通过对压缩矩阵熵的 Haar 平均（Haar averages of compressed matrix entropies）构造正表示，得到 underlying 的 pinching 不等式；并结合秩约束下的优化分析。

Result: 证明次可加性，且除 von Neumann 熵外等号成立当且仅当至少一个边缘态为纯态；证明除 von Neumann 熵外均违反强次可加性（在两量子比特最大纠缠态与混合量子比特张量积上即可失败），其强次可加性缺陷在量子 Markov 态上可取正负两种符号；对纯双分态加辅助态识别缺陷为可分离凹乘积缺口并给出精确最大值；对任意双分态给出以边缘纯度表示的下界；对两量子比特态确定各环境维度下最优系数（subentropy 为 1/3）。

Conclusion: 该熵族在次可加性上表现良好但一般不满足强次可加性，其缺陷可由 pinching 不等式与乘积缺口精确刻画；结果还给出 unital 量子信道的熵增益界，并将各熵解释为指定各向同性部分投影测量后保留的经典通信容量。

Abstract: We prove subadditivity for the Nichols--Wootters family of entropies interpolating between the von Neumann entropy and the subentropy, with all spectra evaluated in one common ambient dimension. Beyond the von Neumann entropy, equality holds exactly when at least one marginal is pure. This settles the subadditivity conjecture concerning subentropy. Every member except the von Neumann entropy fails strong subadditivity, already on a maximally entangled two-qubit state tensored with a mixed qubit. Its strong-subadditivity defect takes both signs on quantum Markov states. For a pure bipartite state tensored with an ancillary state, we identify the defect with a separately concave product gap and determine its exact maximum under rank constraints. For arbitrary bipartite states, we obtain an explicit lower bound in the marginal purities. For two-qubit states, we determine the optimal coefficient multiplying the product of the smaller marginal eigenvalues in every common ambient dimension, including the subentropy coefficient $1/3$. A positive representation by Haar averages of compressed matrix entropies gives the pinching inequality underlying these results and also yields entropy-gain bounds for unital quantum channels.As an application, each entropy equals the classical communication capacity retained after a specified isotropic partial projective measurement, at a prescribed average input state, even when entangled block encodings are allowed.

</details>


### [221] [Critical excited states in quantum metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15683)
*Marek Kuchař,Michal Macek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究强耦合量子比特-玻色子系统在基态与激发态量子相变附近的临界性增强传感，结合有限尺寸效应设计驱动协议以提升参数估计信噪比，并证明激发态临界性可通过单模玻色压缩（single-mode bosonic squeezing）规避"绝热性消亡"，从而获得超越传统基态临界量子计量（quantum metrology）的计量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基态临界量子计量受制于"绝热性消亡（death of adiabaticity）"问题，限制了其实际计量性能；因此需要探索激发态量子相变（excited-state quantum phase transitions）是否能提供新的计量资源。

Method: 在强耦合量子比特-玻色子（qubit-boson）系统中同时考察基态与激发态量子相变；计入有限尺寸效应（finite-size effects），设计利用发散磁化率（diverging susceptibilities）的驱动协议（driving protocols），并采用实验上相关的生成元（generators）进行参数估计。

Result: 激发态临界性在可规避"绝热性消亡"的参数区间内，通过单模玻色压缩提供信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）与参数估计上的计量优势。

Conclusion: 激发态临界性构成一种可行的临界计量资源，其单模玻色压缩机制可突破常规基态临界量子计量的绝热限制。

Abstract: We investigate criticality-enhanced sensing near ground- and excited-state quantum phase transitions in a strongly coupled qubit-boson system. Accounting for finite-size effects, we design driving protocols that exploit diverging susceptibilities to enhance signal-to-noise ratios in parameter estimation using experimentally relevant generators. We show that the excited-state criticality provides a metrological advantage through single-mode bosonic squeezing in regimes that circumvent the 'death of adiabaticity', limiting conventional critical quantum metrology with ground-state criticality.

</details>


### [222] [Effect of atomic interactions on quantum communication protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16519)
*Zixian Hong,John C. Schotland*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文建立原子介质中光子量子通信的第一性原理框架，从实空间电磁场量子化出发推导偏振编码单光子有效模型，并应用于超密编码与BB84协议，表明通信效率与安全性随原子-光场耦合增强而单调下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子通信协议分析多基于理想信道假设，缺乏从原子介质微观物理出发的通信性能刻画，因此需要一个连接实空间光场量子化与协议层面效率、安全性评估的第一性原理框架。

Method: 基于与二能级原子系综耦合的电磁场实空间量子化（real-space quantization），推导偏振编码单光子态的有效模型；将其用于超密编码（superdense coding）的协议容量评估，以及构造丢弃擦除结果并利用微观模型估计内禀损耗率的修正BB84协议并推导渐近密钥率。

Result: 在擦除（erasure）、退相位（dephasing）与退极化（depolarizing）信道下，固定Bell态字母表的超密编码协议容量随原子-光场耦合增强而单调下降；修正BB84协议的渐近密钥率同样随耦合强度单调下降，并在超过有限临界耦合后降为零。

Conclusion: 原子介质中的光场耦合会系统性地削弱光子量子通信的容量与安全性，且存在使密钥率归零的临界耦合；该框架为从微观模型定量评估量子通信协议性能提供了统一途径。

Abstract: We develop a first-principles framework for analyzing photonic quantum communication in an atomic medium. Starting from a real-space quantization of the electromagnetic field coupled to an ensemble of two-level atoms, we derive an effective model for polarization-encoded single-photon states. We apply this framework to superdense coding to study communication efficiency and to the BB84 quantum key distribution protocol for communication security. For superdense coding, we evaluate the protocol capacity of the fixed Bell-state alphabet under erasure, dephasing, and depolarizing channels and show that it decreases monotonically with increasing atom-field coupling. For BB84, we formulate a modified protocol that discards erasure outcomes and uses the microscopic model to estimate the intrinsic loss rate. We derive the corresponding asymptotic key rate and show that it decreases monotonically with coupling strength and vanishes beyond a finite critical coupling.

</details>


### [223] [Thermodynamically Consistent Quantum-Classical Hybrid Dynamics: A Metriplectic Approach to Generalized Lindblad and Bloch-Torrey Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16708)
*Azeddine Zaidni,Ayoub Arraji,Philip J. Morrison*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子—经典混合动力学的度规辛框架，通过能量守恒与非负熵产生保证热力学一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子—经典混合动力学描述在热力学一致性（thermodynamic consistency）方面缺乏统一保证，需要同时满足能量守恒与非负熵产生。

Method: 构建混合泊松括号（hybrid Poisson bracket）与度规辛四括号（metriplectic 4-bracket）相结合的形式，量子与经典子系统之间仅通过耗散过程（dissipative processes）耦合。

Result: 在该混合表述下，得到林德布拉德主方程（Lindblad master equations）与布洛赫—托里方程（Bloch–Torrey equations）的热力学一致推广。

Conclusion: 实现了量子弛豫（quantum relaxation）与经典扩散（classical diffusion）的统一描述，并给出熵产生（entropy production）的清晰解释。

Abstract: We develop a metriplectic framework for quantum--classical hybrid dynamics that ensures thermodynamic consistency through conservation of energy and non-negative entropy production. The approach combines a hybrid Poisson bracket with a metriplectic $4$-bracket, with coupling between quantum and classical subsystems arising solely through dissipative processes. Within this hybrid formulation, we produce thermodynamically consistent generalizations of the Lindblad master equations and the Bloch--Torrey equations for open quantum systems. This provides a unified description of quantum relaxation and classical diffusion, together with a clear interpretation of entropy production.

</details>


### [224] [Probabilistic distillation of superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16719)
*Gökhan Torun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究确定了在“无叠加（superposition-free）”操作下，将一个非正交态叠加（如光学猫态）转化为另一个叠加态的最优概率，并据此给出对称源态的完整可达性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非正交态叠加（如光学猫态）作为量子资源的价值取决于其各分量之间的干涉方式，因此需要刻画在保持资源性质的自由操作下，不同叠加态之间可转换性的定量规律。

Method: 以“无叠加操作”为自由操作集合，即不能确定性地让叠加分量产生更强破坏性干涉且保留全部分量；在此原则下求解最优转换概率。对相干态参考态，证明凡将每个参考态映射为相干态的操作（如光子损耗、光子减除）均属无叠加操作，并对两个参考态情形完全求解。

Result: 等模系数叠加（相干理论中最大相干态的对应物）构成不等价资源：经归一化光子减除，奇猫态可确定性地变为偶猫态，而偶猫态转奇猫态的概率在振幅小时趋于零；偶猫态与Yurke–Stoler猫态双向均不能确定性互转。多拷贝集体操作优于逐个转换，但相干蒸馏除外。对三个及以上等正重叠参考态，无任何态可确定性到达所有其他态；干涉最具破坏性的等模叠加可确定性得到最大相干态，其可达性不劣于该类态但最坏情况下不更优。

Conclusion: 无叠加操作下的最优转换概率由“不能确定性增强破坏性干涉”这一原则唯一确定（对分量置换对称的源），由此揭示等模叠加态之间的资源不等价性，并界定集体操作与多参考态情形下确定性转换的极限。

Abstract: How well a superposition of nonorthogonal states, such as an optical cat state, serves as a resource depends on how its components interfere. Here we determine the optimal probability of converting one such superposition into another by superposition-free operations. These operations cannot make the components of a superposition interfere more destructively with certainty while keeping all of them, and this principle fixes the optimal probability for every source that is symmetric under permutations of its components. When the reference states are coherent states, as in a cat state, every operation that maps each of them to a coherent state, such as photon loss or photon subtraction, is superposition-free. For two reference states we solve the problem completely. Superpositions with coefficients of equal modulus, the counterparts of the maximally coherent states of coherence theory, are therefore inequivalent resources: an odd cat state becomes even with certainty by a normalized photon subtraction, whereas an even one becomes odd only with a probability that vanishes for small amplitudes, and the even and the Yurke$-$Stoler cat states cannot be converted into each other with certainty in either direction. Collective operations on several copies can do better than converting them one at a time, except in the distillation of coherence, which gains nothing from them. For three or more reference states with equal positive overlaps, no state reaches every other state with certainty; the equal-modulus superpositions that interfere most destructively still yield a maximally coherent state with certainty and reach every superposition of all reference states at least as well as such a state does, but in the worst case no better.

</details>


### [225] [When can local unitaries generate a complete orthogonal basis?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16921)
*Julian Eisfeld,Hermann Kampermann,Nikolai Wyderka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明了 3×3 两 qutrit 系统的普适局域编码性（universal local encodability），并在一类双分体维度（如 d×2、d×3 及含无限维子系统的情形）中构造出局域编码，同时给出非 ULE 的必要条件与操作解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域可编码性（local encodability）问题表述简单，但其答案依赖系统的局域维度，此前 3×3 情形仅为猜想，四量子比特及以上已被证否，亟需给出确定结论与更一般的判据。

Method: 通过理论与构造性证明解决 3×3 猜想；在多个双分体维度中显式构造普适局域编码（universal local encodability）；并将启发式参数计数（heuristic parameter count）严格化为普适性的必要条件。

Result: 证明了两 qutrit（two qutrits）的普适性；构造出 d×2（d≥2）、d×3（d=3,4 及 d≥6）以及任一含一或两个无限维（infinite-dimensional）子系统的双分体系统的普适局域编码；给出排除无限多种情形的必要条件。

Conclusion: 非普适局域编码（non-ULE）等价于在检测未知随机局域幺正旋转（local unitary rotations）的判别任务中，一般测量（general measurements）相对可投影模拟测量（projectively simulable ones）具有优势，从而赋予 ULE 操作层面的意义。

Abstract: The question of local encodability asks: can a given, multipartite pure quantum state be completed to an orthonormal basis using only local unitary rotations? If any such state in a given system has this property, it is called universally locally encodable (ULE), a feature, which has applications in quantum information theory, engineering and math. Despite its easy formulation, the answer to this question depends nontrivially on the local dimensions of the system: While universal local encodability is known for $2\times 2$, $4\times 4$ and $8\times 8$-dimensional systems, it was so-far only conjectured to hold in $3\times 3$, and non-ULE was recently proved for systems of four and more qubits by Pauwels and Gühne.
  We resolve the open conjecture by proving universality for two qutrits. Furthermore, we construct (among others) universal local encodings in dimensions $d\times2$ for $d\ge2$, in dimensions $d\times3$ for $d=3,4$ and $d\ge6$, and in every bipartite system with one or two infinite-dimensional subsystems. For finite-dimensional multipartite systems, we turn a heuristic parameter count into a necessary condition for universality, which excludes universal local encodability in infinitely many cases. Finally, we give universal local encodability an operational interpretation by showing that non-ULE is equivalent to whether general measurements yield an advantage over projectively simulable ones in discrimination tasks where unknown, random local unitary rotations have to be detected.

</details>


### [226] [Near-Optimal Multiparameter Pure-State Metrology with a Constant Number of Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17281)
*Bowen Xiao,Zhiyao Yang,Huangjun Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以“Fisher floor”（费舍尔底线，即量子费舍尔信息 QFI 在所有纯态与参数方向上保留的最小比例）刻画均匀灵敏度，证明常数个固定正交基即可实现与希尔伯特空间维数 d 无关的近似最优 Fisher floor。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数纯态计量（multiparameter pure-state metrology）受限于不同参数各自最优测量之间的不相容性（incompatibility），需寻找能对任意参数方向都保持稳定灵敏度的测量策略。

Method: 引入 Fisher floor 作为均匀灵敏度的量化指标，并证明固定正交基数量为常数时即可达到近似最优；采用随机 Haar 基（Haar bases）分析其高概率性能，并与相互无偏基（mutually unbiased bases, MUB）方案进行比较。

Result: 五个独立 Haar 基以高概率给出正且与维数无关的 floor；更大的常数基族可实现近似普适 Fisher 对称（universal Fisher symmetry），在每个方向保留近一半 QFI，即测量不相容性所允许的最大均匀比例；所需基数目在逆精度对数因子意义下最优。相反，d+1 个 MUB 虽可最小化平均 Fisher 各向异性，其 Fisher floor 至多为 1/(d+1)。

Conclusion: 常数规模的固定基集合足以实现维数无关的均匀多参数灵敏度；最优平均平衡（optimal average balance）并不能保证均匀灵敏度，二者存在本质差异。

Abstract: Multiparameter pure-state metrology is limited by the incompatibility of measurements optimized for different parameters. We characterize uniform sensitivity by the Fisher floor, the smallest fraction of the quantum Fisher information (QFI) retained over all pure states and parameter directions. We prove that a constant number of fixed orthonormal bases suffices for a nearly optimal Fisher floor, independently of the Hilbert-space dimension $d$. With high probability, five independent Haar bases already achieve a positive, dimension-independent floor, and larger constant families achieve approximate universal Fisher symmetry: they retain nearly one half of the QFI in every direction, the largest uniform fraction permitted by measurement incompatibility. The required number of bases is optimal up to a logarithmic factor in inverse accuracy. In contrast, $d+1$ mutually unbiased bases, when they exist, can minimize mean Fisher anisotropy among families of $d+1$ bases yet have a Fisher floor of at most $1/(d+1)$, showing that optimal average balance does not ensure uniform sensitivity.

</details>


### [227] [Quantum Metrology and Quantum Battery in a Hyperfine-Coupled Spin System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17310)
*Omar Bachain,Mohamed Amazioug,Rachid Ahl Laamara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究基于氢原子超精细结构构建有效电子—质子自旋系统，同时考察其多参数量子度量学性能与能量学性质。通过量子Fisher信息矩阵与量子Cramér–Rao界评估磁场与超精细耦合的联合估计精度，并在幺正充电协议下表征遍历功等能量特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在综合刻画超精细自旋量子系统在量子传感（quantum sensing）与储能（energy storage）两方面的潜力，弥补对同一物理系统度量学与能量学性质统一认识的不足。

Method: 将外磁场（magnetic field）与超精细耦合强度（hyperfine coupling strength）视为未知参数，推导量子Fisher信息矩阵（quantum Fisher information matrix）并建立联合估计的量子Cramér–Rao界（quantum Cramér–Rao bound）；同时分析参数相关性与弱对易条件（weak-commutativity condition）。另在幺正充电协议（unitary charging protocol）下计算遍历功（ergotropy）、反遍历功（anti-ergotropy）、储能容量（storage capacity）与能量基相干性（energy-basis coherence）。

Result: 尽管对称对数导数（symmetric logarithmic derivatives）非对易，系统仍满足弱对易条件；上述能量学量（遍历功、反遍历功、储能容量与能量基相干性）均依赖于磁场、超精细耦合、温度与充电时间。

Conclusion: 该工作对基于超精细自旋的量子系统的度量学性质与能量性能给出全面刻画，凸显其作为量子传感与能量存储平台的潜力。

Abstract: We investigate multiparameter quantum metrology and the energetic properties of an effective electron--proton spin system inspired by the hyperfine structure of the hydrogen atom. Considering the external magnetic field $\mathcal B$ and the hyperfine coupling strength $Λ$ as unknown parameters, we derive the quantum Fisher information matrix and establish the corresponding quantum Cramér--Rao bounds for their simultaneous estimation. We analyze the estimation precision and the correlations between the two parameters, showing that the weak-commutativity condition is satisfied despite the noncommutativity of the associated symmetric logarithmic derivatives. Independently, we explore the energetic properties of the system under a unitary charging protocol, characterizing its ergotropy, anti-ergotropy, storage capacity, and energy-basis coherence. We investigate the dependence of these quantities on the magnetic field, hyperfine coupling, temperature, and charging time. Our results provide a comprehensive characterization of the metrological properties and energetic performance of a hyperfine spin-based quantum system, highlighting its potential as a platform for quantum sensing and energy storage.

</details>


### [228] [Lorentz Boosts and Governing Equations of the Quantum Geometric Tensor in 4D Energy-Momentum Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16967)
*Yiming Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将量子几何张量（quantum geometric tensor, QGT）推广至四维能量-动量空间（4D energy-momentum space, (ω/c, k)），把频率与动量视为独立坐标，从而在量子几何框架下统一描述类引力度规（gravitational-like metric）与类电磁规范场（electromagnetic-like gauge fields）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固体中，QGT 的虚部主导反常速度与量子化霍尔输运（quantized Hall transport），实部决定量子权重及平带超流刚度与介电响应的几何贡献；作者希望突破这一限于空间动量的框架，把频率纳入几何描述以统一引力式度规与电磁式规范场。

Method: 以实空时平面波相位的不变性固定能量-动量空间中的洛伦兹变换（Lorentz transformation），对静态 Dirac 与 Weyl 系统施加洛伦兹助推（Lorentz boost），生成频率度规、四极型频率-动量剪切度规（quadrupolar frequency-momentum shear metrics）与类电 Berry-Maxwell 场；并识别五个洛伦兹不变缩并（Lorentz-invariant contractions），由静态 Weyl 几何与洛伦兹协变性推导场方程与流守恒律。

Result: 获得了量子度规与 Berry 曲率（Berry curvature）的支配场方程与流守恒律，并将 QGT 的高斯谱振幅（Gaussian spectral amplitudes）与四维空时中的定域高斯事件相联系；同时讨论了在空时晶体（spacetime crystals）或时变介质（time-varying media）中的实验实现途径。

Conclusion: 所提出的四维量子几何为类引力度规与类电磁规范场提供了统一描述，并把几何自洽性（geometric self-consistency）与物理事件动力学（physical event dynamics）联系起来。

Abstract: In solids, the quantum geometric tensor (QGT), $Q_{μν}=g_{μν}-\frac{i}{2} Ω_{μν}$, combines the quantum metric and Berry curvature. Its imaginary part governs anomalous velocity and quantized Hall transport, while its real part determines the quantum weight and geometric contributions to flat-band superfluid stiffness and dielectric response. Here, we extend the QGT to four-dimensional (4D) energy-momentum space ($ω/c,k$), treating frequency and momentum as independent coordinates. Invariance of the real-spacetime plane-wave phase fixes their Lorentz transformation in energy-momentum space. Lorentz boosts of static Dirac and Weyl systems then generate frequency metrics, quadrupolar frequency-momentum shear metrics, and electric-like Berry-Maxwell fields. We identify five Lorentz-invariant contractions and derive the governing field equations and flow conservation laws for both the quantum metric and Berry curvature from static Weyl geometry and Lorentz covariance. We further connect Gaussian spectral amplitudes of the QGT to localized Gaussian events in 4D spacetime. Finally, we discuss experimental realizations in spacetime crystals or time-varying media and clarify how this unified quantum geometry of a gravitational-like metric and electromagnetic-like gauge fields connects geometric self-consistency with physical event dynamics.

</details>


### [229] [Non-interferometric tests of gravitational decoherence models with mechanical oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17260)
*Sandro Donadi,Iarley P. Lobo,Matteo Fadel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子引力诱导退相干（quantum-gravity-induced decoherence）可在非干涉测量中显现：三类唯象模型（变形相对论对称性、随机度规涨落、随机广义不确定原理）均导致动量基退相干，并预言各具特征的加热与位移噪声谱，可用于对普朗克尺度参数给出实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有量子引力退相干研究多依赖干涉测量（interferometric measurements），本文旨在表明唯象模型也能在非干涉实验中留下可观测效应，从而拓展探测途径。

Method: 研究三个模型：基于变形相对论对称性（deformed relativistic symmetries）、随机度规涨落（stochastic metric fluctuations）与随机广义不确定原理（stochastic generalized uncertainty principle）；分别分析自由粒子与谐振子（harmonic oscillator）的动量分布、能量、位置扩散与退相干，并推导稳态位置功率谱密度（power spectral density），与力学平台实验对比。

Result: 自由粒子：动量分布与平均能量守恒，但出现位置扩散与模型相关的动量基退相干。谐振子：坍缩算符与哈密顿量不对易导致反常加热（anomalous heating），三模型能量动力学定性不同——恒定速率、正比于动能（可指数增长）、随激发数立方增长。弱噪声下三者给出相同领头阶过量线型（excess lineshape），不同于力噪声。

Conclusion: 位移噪声测量已将第一模型涨落时间约束至普朗克时间（Planck time）以下；占据数测量可将第二模型界限较悬浮粒子基态加热改善至多十四个数量级；低温实验余热释放对第一、第二模型分别给出远低于及约等于普朗克时间的约束。加热与连续测量可成为互补的非干涉探针。

Abstract: Phenomenological models of quantum-gravity-induced decoherence can leave observable effects even in experiments that do not rely on interferometric measurements. We study three such models, based respectively on deformed relativistic symmetries, stochastic metric fluctuations, and a stochastic generalized uncertainty principle, all of which induce decoherence in the momentum basis. For a free particle, the models preserve momentum distribution and mean energy but produce position diffusion and momentum-space decoherence with model-specific momentum dependences. For a harmonic oscillator, the noncommutativity of the collapse operators with the oscillator Hamiltonian results in anomalous heating, with the three models leading to qualitatively different energy dynamics. The first gives heating at a constant rate, the second at a rate proportional to the kinetic energy, which can lead to exponential growth, and the third at a rate growing cubically with excitation. We further derive the stationary position power spectral density and show that all three models produce the same leading-order excess lineshape in the weak-noise regime, distinct from that of force noise.
