Comparing with existing mechanical platforms, we find that displacement-noise measurements can already constrain the fluctuation time of the first model below the Planck time, while occupation measurements could improve the bounds on the second model by up to fourteen orders of magnitude over those from ground-state heating of levitated particles. Summing the heating over all phonon modes of a solid, the residual heat release of low-temperature experiments constrains the first model far below the Planck time and the second at the Planck time. Our results establish heating and continuous measurements as complementary non-interferometric probes of gravity-related decoherence.

</details>


### [230] [Higher-Group Statistics on Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16962)
*Chen Yang,Yu-An Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出格点框架，将高群（higher group）统计定义为闭合跳跃算符序列的Berry相位，并证明d维球面上统计过程由$H_{d+2}(B\mathbb G,\mathbb Z)$分类，为耦合缺陷提供新不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穿过畴壁可置换任意子（anyon），相交弦线可产生粒子，这类不同维度缺陷间的耦合由高群刻画，但缺乏系统性统计描述与计算手段。

Method: 以闭合跳跃算符序列的Berry相位定义高群统计，要求其在算符局部修改下不变；将Smith标准形算法推广至更高群，并直接从同调类构造统计过程以规避指数级缺陷构型枚举。

Result: 构造出相位为$+i$的环翻转过程，细化通常的$\pm1$不变量；在二维中区分两个$\mathbb Z_4$环面码（toric code），其任意子统计相同但规范理论分别为$D_4$与$Q_8$的量子双重；证明$d$维球面统计过程由$H_{d+2}(B\mathbb G,\mathbb Z)$分类；可检测1、2、3维的2-群反常。

Conclusion: 高群统计为刻画量子多体系统中耦合缺陷提供了新的不变量，并可用于探测高群对称保护拓扑相（SPT）的边界反常。

Abstract: Crossing a domain wall can permute anyons, and intersecting strings can create a particle. These couplings between defects of various dimensions are specified by higher groups. We develop a lattice framework defining higher-group statistics as Berry phases of closed sequences of hopping operators, invariant under local modifications of the operators. Our central example is a closed Majorana chain, a 1-dimensional topological superconductor, embedded in 3 dimensions. We construct a loop-flip process with phase $+i$, refining the usual $\pm 1$ loop-flip invariant through intermediate transfer of fermion parity between the chain and physical fermions. In two dimensions, charge-wall statistics distinguish two $\mathbb Z_4$ toric codes enriched by $\mathbb Z_2$ charge-conjugation symmetry, with identical anyon statistics but distinct gauged theories: the quantum doubles of the dihedral group $D_4$ and the quaternion group $Q_8$. We provide two complementary constructions of statistical processes. The first extends the Smith normal form algorithm in [Phys. Rev. X $\textbf{16}$, 011010 (2026), arXiv:2412.01886] to higher groups, generating processes from lattice geometry and prescribed local moves. For a finite higher group $\mathbb G$, we prove that statistical processes on a $d$-sphere are classified by $H_{d+2}(B\mathbb G,\mathbb Z)$. The second constructs processes directly from homology classes without examining exponentially many defect configurations, enabling us to study statistics for complex higher-group structures where the previous method is computationally prohibitive. These processes detect boundary anomalies of higher-group symmetry-protected topological phases. For example, we distinguish 2-group anomalies in 1, 2, and 3 dimensions. Higher-group statistics provide new invariants for characterizing coupled defects in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [231] [Probing Krylov Dynamics in Driven Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14611)
*András Grabarits,Adolfo del Campo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文精确求解了含时多体Krylov问题，提出驱动动力学在正交Krylov子空间中的闭式描述，并给出可由现有量子模拟平台直接测量的扩展复杂度方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov复杂度（Krylov complexity）是研究算符增长、扰乱（scrambling）与量子混沌的核心探针，但实验研究受限于一个基本事实：实验室量子系统通常被含时驱动，而Krylov构造假定生成元固定，一旦哈密顿量随时间变化便失效。

Method: 针对现有量子模拟与控制平台中实现的标度不变（scale-invariant）与旋转不变（rotation-invariant）系统，对含时多体Krylov问题给出精确闭式解；建立将驱动动力学映射到相互正交Krylov子空间的关系式，并将构造推广到Liouville空间以处理含时密度矩阵Krylov问题。

Result: 扩散复杂度（spread complexity）具有闭式表达，仅由初始哈密顿量的谱性质与驱动时序决定；初始热态的Krylov动力学由此具备实验可及性。在驱动Lieb-Liniger模型、Tonks-Girardeau气体与Calogero-Sutherland模型等标度不变量子气体中，复杂度由原子云宽度及其变化率决定，可通过原位（in situ）与飞行时间（time-of-flight）成像测量；在Haldane-Shastry与中心自旋（central-spin）等旋转不变自旋系统中，复杂度由集体自旋进动支配，可在囚禁离子、腔QED与电路QED平台探测和读出。

Conclusion: 该工作消除了Krylov复杂度实验研究中的含时驱动障碍，把驱动诱导的复杂度与可观测的谱性质、驱动时序及原子云宽度或集体自旋进动等实验量直接联系，为在多类量子模拟平台上测量算符增长与量子混沌提供可行路径。

Abstract: Krylov complexity has become a central probe of operator growth, scrambling, and quantum chaos. Yet, a basic fact hinders its experimental study: laboratory quantum systems are routinely driven in time, whereas the Krylov construction assumes a fixed generator and breaks down once the Hamiltonian varies. Here, we solve the time-dependent many-body Krylov problem exactly and in closed form for scale- and rotation-invariant systems realized in current quantum-simulation and control platforms. We find exact relations that map the driven dynamics onto mutually orthogonal Krylov subspaces and yield a closed-form spread complexity, determined solely by the spectral properties of the initial Hamiltonian and the driving schedule. The construction extends to Liouville space and solves the time-dependent density-matrix Krylov problem, which makes the Krylov dynamics of initial thermal states experimentally accessible. In scale-invariant quantum gases, such as the driven Lieb-Liniger model, the Tonks-Girardeau gas, and the Calogero-Sutherland model, the complexity is set by the width of the atomic cloud and its rate of change, directly accessible via in situ and time-of-flight imaging. In rotation-invariant spin systems such as the Haldane-Shastry and central-spin models, it is governed by collective spin precession that can be probed and read out in trapped-ion, cavity-QED, and circuit-QED platforms.

</details>


### [232] [Squeezed Light Generation by Cooper Pair Recombination in a Superconductor-Semiconductor Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14622)
*Sima Buchbinder,Avi Koriat,Alex Hayat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于超导体-半导体混合结构中库珀对复合（Cooper-pair recombination）的非线性光-物质相互作用，理论证明了一种紧凑、电驱动的压缩光（squeezed light）产生方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩光产生方法在紧凑性与电驱动集成方面存在局限，本文旨在探索利用库珀对复合过程实现电泵浦压缩光的可行性，以推动紧凑集成结构中的压缩光实现。

Method: 基于二阶微扰理论（second-order perturbation theory）构建有效哈密顿量（effective Hamiltonian），将相互作用的电子部分表述为光子耦合强度系数，从而建立描述库珀对复合对光子态正交压缩（quadrature squeezing）效应的模型。

Result: 压缩强度与超导能隙（superconducting energy gap）的平方及器件长度成正比，并随外加电压增大而增强；在实际器件长度、材料与偏压条件下且工作于临界温度（critical temperature）以下时，计算所得压缩值可与现有压缩光产生方法相当。

Conclusion: 该理论方案证明了电泵浦压缩光产生的可行性，可为在紧凑集成结构中实现压缩光铺平道路。

Abstract: We theoretically demonstrate squeezed light generation in a compact, electrically driven device utilizing the nonlinear light-matter interaction of Cooper-pair recombination in a hybrid superconductorsemiconductor structure. On the basis of second-order perturbation theory, we developed an effective Hamiltonian which incorporates the electronic component of the interaction as a photon coupling strength coefficient, enabling a model to describe the quadrature squeezing effect of Cooper-pair recombination on the photonic state. We find the squeezing strength to be proportional to the superconducting energy gap squared and device length, as well as increasing with applied voltage. For practical device length, materials and voltage bias, we calculated squeezing values comparable to those of existing squeezed light generation methods when operating below the critical temperature. This theoretically developed concept shows viability for electrically pumped squeezed light generation and can pave the way towards realization of squeezing in compact integrated structures.

</details>


### [233] [Quantum synchronization in XX chains under relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14657)
*Kübra Sümer,Ege Yiğit Aldırmaz,Steve Campbell,Barış Çakmak,Göktuğ Karpat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将均匀量子比特XX链的稳定同步结果推广至频率与交换耦合均随格点依赖的非均匀情形，在多重局域振幅阻尼噪声下给出稳定同步的充要条件：由阻尼格点分隔的各未阻尼段须恰好共享一个非零本征值（即暗模能量），并据此证明同步边缘比特对的渐近纠缠保持非零恒定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Çakmak 等，Phys. Rev. A 114, L030202 2026）仅处理均匀链的稳定同步，而实际量子网络中的比特链往往存在格点依赖的频率与交换耦合；本文旨在检验非均匀性如何改变同步判据，并厘清同步与纠缠之间的联系。

Method: 在多重局域振幅阻尼噪声（multi-local amplitude damping noise）下研究具有格点依赖频率与交换耦合的量子比特XX链，通过谱分析确定暗模（dark mode）能量，即以阻尼格点分隔的各未阻尼段（undamped segments）所共享的本征值；结合解析算例与非均匀性机制分析，并计算渐近成对共生纠缠度（pairwise concurrence）。

Result: 证明边缘比特的通用稳定同步（generic stable synchronization）当且仅当各段恰好共享一个本征值且该值非零；此时暗模支撑上所有比特同步，锁定常数与初始制备无关，而各段与阻尼格点的耦合决定相对振荡幅度。解析算例表明，非均匀性可产生均匀链中不存在的同步，并将依赖制备的锁定转变为通用同步。单暗模情形下，参与同步振荡的每一对量子比特都保持非零且恒定的共生纠缠度。

Conclusion: 同步的出现可由谱匹配条件（spectral matching condition）直接刻画，并用以筛选支持同步的哈密顿量参数；非均匀性是产生鲁棒同步与持续纠缠的可设计资源，为耗散量子网络中的同步与纠缠协同提供理论依据。

Abstract: We study stable synchronization in qubit XX chains with site-dependent frequencies and exchange couplings under multi-local amplitude damping noise, extending the uniform-chain results of B. Çakmak et al., [Phys. Rev. A 114, L030202 2026] in several directions. We establish that the dark-mode energies are precisely the eigenvalues shared by all undamped segments separated by the damped sites. Generic stable synchronization of the edge qubits occurs if and only if these segments share exactly one eigenvalue and this eigenvalue is nonzero, in which case every qubit on the support of the corresponding dark mode is synchronized with locking constants independent of the initial preparation. This spectral matching condition identifies the Hamiltonian parameters that support synchronization, while the couplings to the damped sites control the relative oscillation amplitudes. We present analytical examples which show how inhomogeneity can create synchronization absent in uniform chains and turn preparation-dependent locking into generic synchronization. We also calculate the asymptotic pairwise concurrence and prove that, in the single-dark-mode regime, every pair participating in synchronized oscillations remains entangled with a nonzero, constant concurrence.

</details>


### [234] [Gatemon qubits based on in-situ ternary InAsSb/Al nanowire Josephson junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16595)
*Zhenhai Sun,David Feldstein-Bofill,Sangeeth Kallatt,Sabbir A. Khan,Morten Kjaergaard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在半导体内外延超导异质结（epitaxial superconductivity）平台上，通过单次生长过程中的阴影限定原位InAsSb/Al约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction），实现了门控超导量子比特（gatemon qubit），并展示其相干操控与弛豫特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三元InAsSb/Al纳米线兼具可组分调控的半导体性质与外延邻近超导性，适合混合量子电路与介观超导研究；但需验证基于原位结的门控量子比特是否具备相干可调性。

Method: 采用阴影限定（shadow-defined）原位InAsSb/Al约瑟夫森结、单次生长工艺制备gatemon；利用门控依赖的微波谱学（microwave spectroscopy）表征跃迁频率调控，并以Rabi振荡与Ramsey干涉评估相干性。

Result: 量子比特跃迁频率可通过静电门控在数GHz范围内调节；测得代表性T1 = 0.79 μs、T2* = 0.91 μs；三块芯片上五个gatemon的弛豫时间约0.2–1 μs，且随比特频率升高而下降，量级与向电磁环境自发辐射的电路估计相符。

Conclusion: 原位InAsSb/Al结可作为相干、门控可调的电路元件；电路量子电动力学（cQED）架构可作为探测三元弱连接（weak link）动力学的微波手段。

Abstract: Ternary InAsSb/Al nanowires combine compositionally engineerable semiconductor properties with epitaxial proximity superconductivity, making them attractive for hybrid quantum circuits and mesoscopic superconductivity. Here, we realize gatemon qubits based on shadow-defined in-situ InAsSb/Al Josephson junctions in a single growth process. Gate-dependent microwave spectroscopy demonstrates electrostatic tuning of the qubit transition over several gigahertz. Rabi oscillations and Ramsey interference show coherent qubit control, with representative values T1 = 0.79 us and T2* = 0.91 us. Across five gatemons distributed over three chips, the measured relaxation times range from approximately 0.2 us to 1 us and decrease with increasing qubit frequency. Their magnitude is comparable to a circuit estimate of spontaneous emission into the electromagnetic environment. These results establish in-situ InAsSb/Al junctions as coherent, gate-tunable circuit elements and introduce cQED architecture as a microwave probe of the dynamics of the ternary weak link.

</details>


### [235] [Quantum tunneling of a measurement-driven active particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16626)
*Tobias Nadolny,Matteo Brunelli,Christoph Bruder,Michel Fruchart*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作展示了自旋-轨道耦合与阻尼将量子粒子的运动锁定于自旋取向，并借助量子芝诺效应（quantum Zeno effect）通过连续测量使取向持续化，从而使单条量子轨迹呈现主动的“跑-翻滚”（run-and-tumble）运动，并改变粒子在势垒处的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动粒子（active particles）的核心特征是取向决定持续运动，但这一性质此前主要在经典体系中讨论；该研究旨在将持久运动这一主动物质关键性质推广到量子层面。

Method: 利用自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）与阻尼使粒子运动与自旋取向锁定，并通过基于量子芝诺效应的连续测量（continuous measurement）维持自旋取向的持久性，进而考察粒子与势垒的相互作用。

Result: 单个量子轨迹表现出主动的跑-翻滚运动；粒子撞击势垒时，其持久速度导致在势垒附近积累，与自由量子粒子的反射行为形成对照；量子隧穿（quantum tunneling）使主动粒子的透射率高于类比经典粒子。

Conclusion: 量子主动粒子的持久运动可通过自旋-轨道耦合、阻尼与连续测量实现，并在势垒散射中产生积累效应与增强的隧穿透射，显示出量子主动物质区别于经典主动粒子与自由量子粒子的行为特征。

Abstract: Active particles can exhibit persistent motion set by their orientation. We demonstrate this key property for a quantum active particle: The particle's motion is locked to the orientation of its spin due to spin-orbit coupling and damping, and the orientation is made persistent through continuous measurement via the quantum Zeno effect. As a result, individual quantum trajectories feature an active run-and-tumble motion. When the particle impinges on a potential barrier, its persistent velocity leads to accumulation near the barrier, in contrast with the reflection of a free quantum particle. We show that quantum tunneling enhances the active particle's transmission rate compared to that of an analogous classical particle.

</details>


### [236] [Geometric Theory of Quasiperiodically Driven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16683)
*Chengzhi Ye,Marin Bukov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于绝热捷径（counterdiabatic driving）构建通用框架，用于识别准周期驱动（quasiperiodic drives）中的动力学不变空间，并给出准周期 Floquet 理论的非阿贝尔推广与遍历性新判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期驱动系统在非平衡动力学与哈密顿量工程中应用广泛，但与周期驱动（Floquet）系统不同，缺乏用有效含时无关哈密顿量及其本征态描述演化的通用理论。

Method: 以绝热捷径为基础，借助精确有效平均能量算符（effective average-energy operator）与规范势（gauge potential）分解幺正演化，并在频率晶格算符空间（frequency-lattice operator space）中用变分原理求解。

Result: 依据动力学不变空间的几何与拓扑，将准周期驱动分为（非）阿贝尔与（非）扭曲四类；阿贝尔非扭曲类可视为 Floquet 定理向不可公约驱动的直接推广，并进一步导出 Floquet 理论的非阿贝尔推广。

Conclusion: 提出以动力学不变本征空间合并为标志的遍历性（ergodicity）概念，类比混合经典相空间中遍历性的出现；所给数学框架、变分原理与非阿贝尔遍历性可推广至准周期驱动之外。

Abstract: Quasiperiodically driven systems give rise to a rich set of physical phenomena in contemporary quantum physics, with applications ranging from nonequilibrium dynamics to Hamiltonian engineering and control. However, unlike periodically driven (Floquet) systems, their study is hindered by the absence of a generic theory to describe their evolution using an effective time-independent Hamiltonian and its eigenstates. Based on counterdiabatic driving, we construct a general framework to identify and determine the dynamically invariant spaces of arbitrary quasiperiodic drives (i.e., the generalization of Floquet states). We decompose their unitary evolution using the exact effective average-energy operator Æ and the corresponding gauge potential, which we compute via a variational principle in frequency-lattice operator space. We then formulate a four-fold taxonomy of quasiperiodic drives based on the geometry and topology of their dynamically invariant spaces into (non)abelian and (un)twisted classes. The abelian untwisted class admits a direct extension of Floquet's theorem to incommensurate quasiperiodic drives; going beyond, we derive a nonabelian generalization of Floquet theory. Finally, we propose a concept of ergodicity whose onset is marked by coalescing dynamically invariant eigenspaces, in direct analogy to the emergence of ergodicity in a mixed classical phase space. The effective mathematical framework, variational principle, and nonabelian ergodicity we put forward apply beyond quasiperiodic drives.

</details>


### [237] [Symmetry-Controlled Criticality and Zeno Localization in a Monitored Quasiperiodic Superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16912)
*Pinaki Singha,Marcin Szyniszewski,Nilanjan Roy,Auditya Sharma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究带配对（pairing）的自由费米子在连续监测下，准周期性（quasiperiodicity）如何同时调控局域化与监测场论的对称类，从而决定测量诱导相变（measurement-induced transition）的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由费米子的测量诱导相变强烈依赖对称性，但其与准周期局域化（quasiperiodic localization）及超导配对（superconducting pairing）的相互作用尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 构建带配对的自由费米子连续监测模型，以拓扑纠缠熵（topological entanglement entropy）作为非局域关联探针，在量子态扩散模拟（quantum state diffusion simulations）中刻画相变；并在深度 Zeno 区推导单条轨迹的有效非厄米 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 描述。

Result: 识别出 von Neumann 纠缠熵按 (ln L)^2 标度的临界相与面积律（area-law）相之间的相变；干净极限（clean limit）下无相变，与 DIII 和 D 型非线性 sigma 模型（nonlinear sigma model）一致。相变线表现出强多重分形性（multifractality），并随准周期性呈非单调依赖，归因于对称类交叉；深度 Zeno 区得到的闭式局域化长度（localization length）与数值结果定量吻合。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对称性、准周期局域化与测量反作用（measurement backaction）共同决定了被监测超导体中的临界纠缠行为与微观轨迹局域化。

Abstract: Measurement-induced transitions of free fermions depend strongly on symmetry, but their interplay with quasiperiodic localization and superconducting pairing remains largely unexplored. We study a continuously monitored model of free fermions with pairing, where quasiperiodicity both controls localization and changes the symmetry class of the monitored field theory. Using topological entanglement entropy as a probe of nonlocal correlations in quantum state diffusion simulations, we identify a transition between a critical phase whose von Neumann entanglement entropy scales as $(\ln L)^2$ and an area-law phase, whereas no transition occurs in the clean limit, consistent with the corresponding DIII and D nonlinear sigma models. The transition line exhibits strong multifractality and depends nonmonotonically on quasiperiodicity, which we associate with the symmetry crossover. Deep in the Zeno regime, we derive an effective non-Hermitian Bogoliubov-de Gennes description of individual trajectories and obtain a closed-form localization length that quantitatively agrees with numerics. Our results reveal how symmetry, quasiperiodic localization, and measurement backaction combine to determine both critical entanglement and microscopic trajectory localization in monitored superconductors.

</details>


### [238] [Unified master equation for driven multilevel quantum systems: Derivation, dynamics, and spectral signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16952)
*Sagarika Basak,D. Blume*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于Lewis-Riesenfeld动力学不变量（Lewis-Riesenfeld dynamical invariants）推导出统一主方程（unified master equation），可在Born-Markov理论框架内处理任意驱动下的多能级量子系统耗散，并揭示周期驱动三能级Λ系统的荧光光谱中被常规描述遗漏的谱学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规主方程（conventional master equations）通常无法准确刻画多能级量子系统（multilevel quantum systems）中由驱动修正的耗散（drive-modified dissipation），因此需要建立能涵盖任意驱动形式的统一描述。

Method: 利用Lewis-Riesenfeld动力学不变量，在Born-Markov理论内推导统一主方程，并将其应用于恒定Rabi耦合（continuous-wave）、周期调制Rabi耦合以及非周期（斜坡与啁啾）驱动协议；以周期驱动三能级Λ系统的荧光光谱（fluorescence spectra）作为检验手段。

Result: 荧光光谱显示存在常规描述遗漏的谱学特征。常规方法的局限被归因于浴频率采样（bath-frequency sampling）不准确以及非久期耗散耦合（nonsecular dissipative couplings）被忽略，并给出了需要统一处理的参数区间。

Conclusion: 荧光光谱提供了实验可及的谱学判据（spectroscopic guide），可用于耗散过程的可靠建模，并服务于量子控制、量子模拟、量子传感与量子信息处理。

Abstract: Drive-modified dissipation in multilevel quantum systems is not, in general, accurately captured by conventional master equations. We derive a unified master equation using Lewis-Riesenfeld dynamical invariants, accommodating arbitrary driving within Born-Markov theory. We demonstrate the applicability of the unified master equation to constant-Rabi-coupling (continuous-wave), periodically-modulated-Rabi-coupling, and nonperiodic (ramped and chirped) driving protocols. Fluorescence spectra of a periodically driven three-level $Λ$ system reveal signatures missed by conventional descriptions. We trace the limitations of the conventional descriptions to inaccurate bath-frequency sampling and the neglect of nonsecular dissipative couplings, and map out the parameter regimes that require a unified treatment. The fluorescence spectra provide experimentally accessible signatures, which serve as a spectroscopic guide to reliable modeling of dissipative processes that can inform quantum control, simulation, sensing, and information processing.

</details>


### [239] [Ergodicity breaking for data classification in a dissipative Ising model with competing two- and four-body interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17036)
*Simon Kochsiek,Mario Boneberg,Igor Lesanovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文探讨数据处理与相互作用多体系统动力学之间的紧密联系，提出可通过设计耗散量子伊辛模型（dissipative quantum Ising model）的稳态流形（stationary state manifold）来实现多类分类（multi-class classification）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 某些量子神经网络（quantum neural networks）可映射为以林德布拉德主方程（Lindblad master equation）描述的耗散量子伊辛模型，其中涌现的非线性（emergent nonlinearities）导致非遍历动力学（non-ergodic dynamics），使不同吸引域（basins of attraction）中的初态演化至可区分的稳态，这一机制正是量子数据分类的基础。

Method: 受上述联系启发，研究考察具有相互竞争的两体与四体相互作用（two- and four-body interactions）的伊辛模型，采用平均场计算（mean-field calculations）与精确小尺度数值模拟（exact small-scale numerics）相结合的方法进行分析。

Result: 结果表明，稳态流形可被设计以支持多类分类；对随之形成的吸引域结构的考察进一步显示，这些系统能够实现具有非线性决策边界（nonlinear decision boundaries）的量子态分类。

Conclusion: 相互作用多体系统的稳态与吸引域结构可作为量子数据分类的可设计资源，为实现非线性决策边界的量子态分类提供了机制层面的依据。

Abstract: We explore the close relationship between the processing of data and the dynamics of interacting many-body systems. For example, certain classes of quantum neural networks can be mapped onto dissipative quantum Ising models whose evolution is described by a Lindblad master equation. In these architectures, emergent nonlinearities lead to non-ergodic dynamics under which initial states located in different basins of attraction evolve towards distinguishable stationary states. This mechanism underlies the classification of quantum data. Motivated by this connection, we consider an Ising model with competing two- and four-body interactions. Using a combination of mean-field calculations and exact small-scale numerics, we show that the stationary state manifold can be designed to enable multi-class classification. We also investigate the structure of the ensuing basins of attraction, thereby showing that these systems allow for the classification of quantum states with nonlinear decision boundaries.

</details>


### [240] [Multi-particle quantum walk search on a two-dimensional lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17061)
*Gonçalo Meneses,D. O. Oriekhov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多粒子量子行走搜索算法（multi-particle quantum walk search algorithm）的问题表述与效率，聚焦二维网格上的局域相互作用模型，分析相互作用强度与最大纠缠度对渐近成功时间与概率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单粒子量子行走搜索已被广泛研究，但多粒子情形下粒子间局域相互作用如何影响搜索效率尚缺乏系统分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 每个粒子的离散时间演化采用 Grover 量子行走演化算符（Grover's quantum walk evolution operator）；考虑仅当两个及以上粒子处于同一网格点时状态才发生改变的局域相互作用模型（local interactions models），并主要针对二维网格（two-dimensional grid）展开分析。

Result: 相互作用与非相互作用的两粒子、三粒子搜索算法相较单粒子版本均获得常数因子改进（constant factor improvement）；同时给出了搜索渐近成功时间与概率同相互作用强度及粒子间最大纠缠（maximal entanglement）之间依赖关系的分析。

Conclusion: 多粒子量子行走可对单粒子搜索实现常数因子加速，并提出了一个新的多粒子量子行走广义相互作用模型（generalized model of interactions）。

Abstract: In the present paper we study the formulation of a search problem and the efficiency of multi-particle quantum walk search algorithms. The discrete-time evolution of each particle in space is treated according to Grover's quantum walk evolution operator. We focus on the local interactions models, where the particles' states are modified only when two or more of them appear on the same grid site. The main focus is given to the two-dimensional grid in space. We study the dependence of the search algorithm asymptotic success time and probability on the interaction strength and the generated maximal entanglement between particles. We show that the interacting and non-interacting two- and three-particle search algorithm attains a constant factor improvement over the single-particle counterpart. In addition, we provide a new generalized model of interactions for multi-particle quantum walks.

</details>


### [241] [Ensemble phase transitions in measurement-unitary hybrid quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17322)
*Saptarshi Mandal,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究混合量子电路（unitary 演化与投影测量交替）中辅助量子比特的深遍历性破缺，发现轨迹系综（TE）与投影系综（PE）在测量诱导纯化转变的临界点发生非解析的系综相变，且一阶矩始终为完全极大混合而对相变"失明"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子动力学生成的状态系综的普适性质为希尔伯特空间遍历性（Hilbert-space ergodicity）与深度热化（deep thermalisation）提供了更精细的刻画视角，而深遍历性破缺转变此前缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 考察与系统纠缠的辅助量子比特在混合量子电路（hybrid quantum circuits）下的行为，分析两类系综：以测量结果为条件的轨迹系综（trajectory ensemble, TE），以及在末时刻边界对系统追加投影测量生成的投影系综（projected ensemble, PE）；并分别在 Haar 随机电路与 Clifford 电路中考察其统计性质。

Result: 两类系综均在测量诱导纯化转变（measurement-induced purification transition）临界点呈现非解析变化，即系综相变；但一阶矩保持完全极大混合，无法察觉该转变。Haar 随机电路中 TE 从平凡单位系综变为均匀 Haar 系综，PE 则从 Haar 系综变为平凡的单点纯态；Clifford 电路中非稳定子性（non-stabiliserness）缺失使 PE 限于 1-design，而 TE 形成 k ≤ 3 的 k-design。

Conclusion: 这些真正的系综相变为测量诱导纠缠/纯化转变提供了清晰的物理机制，说明遍历性引导量子子系统的能力在混合相中源于时间边界测量，而在纯相中转向体轨迹测量。

Abstract: The universal properties of state ensembles generated by quantum dynamics provide a refined lens into Hilbert-space ergodicity and deep thermalisation, or lack thereof. We investigate deep-ergodicity breaking transitions of an ancillary qubit entangled with a system undergoing dynamics via hybrid quantum circuits, where unitary evolution is interspersed with projective measurements. Specifically, we analyse the statistical properties of two distinct state ensembles: the trajectory ensemble (TE) conditioned on the measurement outcomes of the hybrid circuit, and the projected ensemble (PE) generated by further projective measurements of the system at the final temporal boundary. We find that both ensembles undergo non-analytic changes -- ensemble phase transitions -- at the critical point of the measurement-induced purification transition, despite their first moments remaining completely maximally mixed and blind to the transition. For Haar-random circuits, the TE transitions from a trivial identity ensemble to the uniform Haar ensemble, while the PE transitions from the Haar ensemble to a trivial singleton pure state. In Clifford circuits, absence of non-stabiliserness limits the PE to a $1$-design, while the TE forms a $k$-design for $k \leq 3$. We demonstrate that these genuine ensemble transitions offer a clear physical mechanism for the measurement-induced entanglement or purification transitions, illustrating how the capacity to ergodically steer a quantum subsystem shifts from the temporal boundary measurements in the mixed phase to the bulk trajectory measurements in the pure phase.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [242] [Fatigue crack growth without plasticity under cyclically reversed shear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14722)
*E. A. Jagla*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文展示了一种无需塑性、摩擦或时间相关过程的本质性循环疲劳（cyclic fatigue）机制：在低于静态破坏阈值的循环剪切下，预先存在的缺陷（flaw）可逐周期扩展数个晶格间距；其成立需两个条件——键在某一剪切方向下更易断裂，且循环中反向剪切幅度足以破坏尚未断裂的键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统疲劳解释通常依赖塑性（plasticity）、摩擦或时间相关过程，本文旨在探究这些机制均不存在时疲劳是否仍可发生，即寻找无塑性疲劳的最小条件。

Method: 建立表征两个弹性体之间弱界面（weak interface）的模型，界面受剪切加载，元 ele 素在达到不可逆破坏阈值前保持弹性；对低于静态破坏阈值的循环载荷进行模拟，观察预存缺陷的扩展行为。

Result: 在足够大的、低于静态阈值的循环剪切下，预存缺陷逐步推进，每周期前进约几个晶格间距（lattice spacings）；该行为需满足两个条件：键在某一剪切方向（sense of shear）下更易失效，且循环中的反向剪切量需足以破坏尚未失效的键。

Conclusion: 该模型识别出本质性、无塑性疲劳机制的最小条件；此类条件可能在承受强烈反向剪切的界面与弱面（weak planes）处得到满足，例如微动磨损（fretting）、滚动接触（rolling contact）、反向扭转（reversed torsion），以及胶结颗粒材料（cemented granular materials）的循环剪切。

Abstract: We show that cyclic fatigue can occur in a system whose elements behave elastically up to an irreversible failure threshold, without plasticity, friction, or any time-dependent process. The model represents a weak interface between two elastic bodies loaded in shear. Under sufficiently large cyclic loading below the static failure threshold, a pre-existing flaw advances progressively, by a few lattice spacings per cycle. Two conditions are needed for this behavior: each bond fails more easily under one sense of the shear than the opposite, and the shear must be reversed during the cycle by an amount large enough to break bonds that have not yet failed. The model thus identifies the minimal conditions for an intrinsic, plasticity-free fatigue mechanism. Such conditions may be met at interfaces and weak planes subjected to strongly reversed shear, as in fretting, rolling contact, and reversed torsion, or in cyclic shear of cemented granular materials.

</details>


### [243] [Soft material mechanical property degradation at high strain rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14872)
*Victor Sanchez,Joseph Beckett,Aryan Shah,Sicong Wang,Jin Yang,Jonathan B. Estrada,Mauro Rodriguez*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出一种惯性微空化流变（Inertial Microcavitation Rheometry, IMR）框架，利用归一化Sigmoid历史函数从空化气泡半径-时间曲线中反演随时间变化的有效材料属性（剪切模量、黏度），并经合成测试与聚丙烯酰胺、明胶实验数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高应变率下软组织类材料可能表现损伤、断裂与塑性等历史相关行为，而传统IMR分析假设空化过程中材料属性恒定，难以捕捉这些变化。

Method: 构建IMR框架，使剪切模量（shear modulus）、黏度（viscosity）或二者均随事件演化，采用归一化Sigmoid历史（normalized sigmoid histories）描述其时间历程，并从实测空化半径历史中识别有效材料属性历史。

Result: 合成测试可恢复预设的恒定与随时间变化的材料历史；应用于聚丙烯酰胺（polyacrylamide）与明胶（gelatin）数据集，得到不同配方与实验条件下的有效材料属性历史。各数据集黏度均下降，剪切模量降低程度随材料与配方而异；两者均下降时，其降低可发生在空化不同阶段。

Conclusion: 材料力学属性退化具有属性依赖性，并取决于其在高应变事件中的演化过程；该框架拓展了IMR对历史相关材料行为的表征能力。

Abstract: The rheometry of soft, tissue-like materials at ultra-high strain rates is critical to biomechanics, materials science, and medical engineering. One approach for characterizing high-rate material response is Inertial Microcavitation Rheometry (IMR), which infers viscoelastic constitutive behavior from the measured radius-time history of a laser-induced cavitation bubble. At these rates, materials may exhibit history-dependent behavior, including damage, fracture, and plasticity. However, conventional IMR analyses typically assume constant material properties throughout the cavitation event, limiting their ability to capture such changes. We present an IMR framework for identifying effective time varying material properties from measured cavitation radius histories. The framework allows the shear modulus, viscosity, or both to evolve during the event using normalized sigmoid histories. Synthetic tests recover prescribed constant and time varying material histories. Application to polyacrylamide and gelatin datasets yields effective material-property histories across formulations and experimental conditions. Viscosity decreased across the datasets, while the extent of shear modulus reduction varied with material and formulation. When both properties decreased, their reductions could occur at different stages of cavitation. This distinction in mechanical property degradation depends on both the property and its evolution during high-strain events.

</details>


### [244] [Structural Relaxation in Supercooled Water: Hydrogen-Bond Exchange, Defect Propagation, and Power-Law Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14915)
*Biman Bagchi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出反应-输运理论（reaction–transport theory），解释深过冷水中结构弛豫时间与三配位氢键缺陷浓度之间的幂律关系，并说明有效指数（effective exponent）为何偏离平均场值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子模拟显示深过冷水（deeply supercooled water）中结构弛豫时间（structural relaxation time）与特定三配位氢键缺陷（three-coordinated hydrogen-bond defect）浓度呈幂律关系，但其微观动力学起源及非整数指数的来源尚缺乏理论解释。

Method: 构建反应-输运理论：活性缺陷（active defect）通过协同氢键重排（concerted hydrogen-bond rearrangements）传播——不饱和供体氢成新键形成瞬时五配位中间体（five-coordinated intermediate），随后断键将缺陷转移至邻位分子；模型包含传播、自衰减（self-decay）及与五配位缺陷的相遇湮灭（encounter-induced annihilation），并在四连接网络（four-connected network）上进行解析与示例计算。

Result: 独立作用的缺陷给出平均场（mean-field）标度 τα ∝ c3′^{-1}；而浓度依赖的传播、缺陷相互作用及连续网络重排之间的关联可导致更大的非整数有效指数 αD。

Conclusion: 基元交换动力学（exchange kinetics）、有限缺陷寿命（finite defect lifetimes）与对局域环境的重复探索共同改变弛豫效率（relaxation efficiency），从而修饰所观测的幂律形式。

Abstract: Molecular simulations of deeply supercooled water reveal an intriguing power-law relationship between structural relaxation time and the concentration of a specific three-coordinated hydrogen-bond defect, $3'=H^1O^2$. We develop a reaction--transport theory in which active $3'$ defects propagate through concerted hydrogen-bond rearrangements. An unsatisfied donor hydrogen forms a new bond, creating a transient five-coordinated intermediate; subsequent breaking of another bond transfers the defect to a neighboring molecule. The dynamics includes propagation, self-decay, and encounter-induced annihilation with five-coordinated defects. Independently acting defects yield the mean-field result $τ_α\propto c_{3'}^{-1}$. By contrast, concentration-dependent propagation, defect interactions, and correlations between successive network rearrangements can generate a larger, noninteger effective exponent $α_D$. Analytical results and illustrative calculations on a four-connected network show how elementary exchange kinetics, finite defect lifetimes, and repeated exploration of local environments modify the relaxation efficiency and hence the observed power law.

</details>


### [245] [The metastability of icosahedral quasicrystals assembled from one-component systems with isotropic interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14921)
*Cosmin A. Dicu-Gohoreanu,Jonathan P. K. Doye,Eva G. Noya*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过直接共存模拟（direct coexistence simulations）估算振荡势（oscillatory potentials）体系中单组分二十面体准晶（icosahedral quasicrystals, IQCs）的熔化线，发现其在所探索参数空间内始终热力学不稳定，被周期性晶体相竞争取代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单组分IQC可在模拟中通过振荡势稳健自组装，但其热力学稳定性（thermodynamic stability）仍是未解问题，需定量判定其是否为平衡相。

Method: 针对一类振荡势，采用直接共存模拟（direct coexistence simulations）估算竞争固相之间的熔化线，比较IQC与周期性晶相的相对稳定性。

Result: 在所探索参数空间内IQC从未热力学稳定，总被周期性晶相压制；这种亚稳性（metastability）主要源于IQC相对竞争固相更高的内能（internal energy）。

Conclusion: 模拟中观察到的单组分IQC形成很可能是动力学现象（kinetic phenomenon）而非热力学现象，即IQC为亚稳相。

Abstract: Icosahedral quasicrystals (IQCs) are long-range ordered materials that lack translational periodicity. While they have only been observed in multi-component metallic systems, simulations have shown that one-component IQCs can robustly self-assemble using oscillatory potentials. However, their thermodynamic stability remains an open question. Here, we employ direct coexistence simulations to estimate the melting line of the competing solids for one such family of potentials. We find that the IQCs are never thermodynamically stable within the explored parameter space, being consistently outcompeted by periodic crystalline phases. This metastability is primarily driven by the higher internal energy of the IQC relative to the competing solids. Our findings suggest that the observed formation of these single-component IQCs in simulations is likely a kinetic phenomenon rather than a thermodynamic one.

</details>


### [246] [Polymer rigidity controls conformational response to deformation-dependent nonreciprocal interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15183)
*Shalabh K. Anand*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文以最小模型研究非互易相互作用（nonreciprocal interactions）如何重塑聚合物构象，发现弯曲刚度（bending rigidity）是决定响应方向的关键控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易相互作用违反作用-反作用对称性（action-reaction symmetry），是活性与驱动系统中产生非平衡力的通用机制，但其如何影响聚合物构象尚不明确。

Method: 构建最小模型，使聚合物主链（backbone）上的局域应变（local strain）产生非互易力，并考察柔性与半柔性聚合物在不同弯曲刚度下的构象响应。

Result: 柔性聚合物在活性下收缩，半柔性聚合物呈非单调响应并最终伸长；这些大尺度构象变化伴随的局域结构统计变化很弱，活性主要通过重新分配沿轮廓的键取向关联（bond-orientational correlations）起作用——削弱柔性链的取向记忆、增强半柔性链的取向记忆。

Conclusion: 弯曲刚度决定形变依赖的非互易活性如何跨尺度传递，为调控活性丝状物质（active filamentous matter）的结构与力学提供机制。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal interactions, which violate action-reaction symmetry, constitute a broadly applicable mechanism for generating nonequilibrium forces in active and driven systems. Here, we explore how such interactions remodel polymer conformations using a minimal model in which local strain along the backbone gives rise to nonreciprocal forces. We find that bending rigidity is the decisive control parameter: flexible polymers contract under activity, whereas semiflexible polymers display a non-monotonic response that ultimately yields extension. These large-scale conformational changes arise despite only weak shifts in local structural statistics. Instead, activity reassigns bond-orientational correlations along the contour, damping orientational memory in flexible chains while amplifying it in semiflexible ones. Collectively, our results show that bending rigidity governs how deformation-dependent nonreciprocal activity transmits across length scales, offering a mechanism to regulate the structure and mechanics of active filamentous matter.

</details>


### [247] [States, localization, and oxidation dynamics in gene segments with CAG repeats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15594)
*Gerasimos Papadopoulos,Constantinos Simserides*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 基于紧束缚导线模型（tight-binding wire model）在碱基对层面研究含CAG三核苷酸重复（CAG triplet repeats）基因的相干量子性质，发现周期性CAG区促进空穴离域（hole delocalization），而侧翼引物破坏周期性从而增强局域化（localization），且转移路径依赖于氧化位点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAG重复序列长度异常与多种神经退行性疾病相关，文献将其归因于DNA复制、修复与转录错误导致的重复扩增；本文旨在从纯相干量子力学角度考察这些基因片段的电荷输运特性。

Method: 在紧束缚导线模型框架下、以碱基对分辨率分析亨廷顿病（Huntington's disease）、肯尼迪病（Kennedy's disease）、脊髓小脑性共济失调6型与7型（Spinocerebellar Ataxia 6/7）相关基因的致病与非致病序列，计算本征态、态密度（density of states）、各站点空穴占据概率的时间平均、平均转移速率（mean transfer rates）、参与比（participation ratios）及逆参与比（inverse participation ratios）。

Result: 周期性CAG区促进空穴离域，而引物（primers）破坏周期性并增强局域化；该效应源于CAG重复的周期结构及引物处周期性的破坏，属纯相干量子力学起源，无法用热激活跳跃（thermally activated hopping）解释。此外，转移取决于氧化位点：在C-G或G-C位点产生的空穴主要经由其他C-G和G-C碱基对转移，经由A-T位点的转移概率显著较低。

Conclusion: 含CAG重复基因片段的空穴输运呈现由序列周期性主导的相干量子特征，周期性重复区与侧翼引物分别对应离域与局域两种行为；转运路径的选择性依赖于初始氧化位点的碱基对类型。

Abstract: We study coherent quantum-mechanical properties of gene segments with CAG repeats: eigenstates including density of states, mean over time probabilities of finding the hole at each site after initial oxidation at a particular site, mean transfer rates between sites, participation ratios, and inverse participation ratios. The genes are analyzed at the base-pair level within the framework of the Tight-Binding Wire Model. All selected genes share a common feature: a region consisting of multiple repeats of the CAG triplet (referring to a strand in the 5'-3' direction). For each gene, there exists a range of CAG repeat numbers for which the gene remains functional, while values outside this range are associated with pathogenicity. We examine the genes associated with (a) Huntington's disease, (b) Kennedy's disease, (c) Spinocerebellar Ataxia 6, and (d) Spinocerebellar Ataxia 7. These genes differ in the sequences flanking the CAG repeats, as well as in the thresholds separating non-pathogenic and pathogenic repeat lengths. In the literature, the underlying cause of these disorders is attributed to errors in DNA replication, repair and transcription leading to expansion of the triplet repeats. We model both pathogenic and non-pathogenic sequences. We find that periodic CAG regions promote hole delocalization, while primers break periodicity, enhancing localization. This is of purely coherent quantum-mechanical origin, arising from the periodic structure of the CAG repeats and the disruption of periodicity at the primers, and cannot be explained by thermally activated hopping. Finally, transfer depends on oxidation site, e.g., a hole generated at a C-G or G-C site is mainly transferred through other C-G and G-C base pairs, while transfer through A-T sites is much less probable.

</details>


### [248] [Phase Transitions of Polar Nematic Liquid Crystals Driven by Electrostatics and Flexoelectricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16035)
*Lincoln Paik,Jonathan V. Selinger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于挠性电（flexoelectricity）与静电（electrostatics）相互竞争的理论，解释极性液晶（polar liquid crystals）从高温向列相到中间温度调制向列（反铁电）相、再到低温均匀极性（铁电）相的相行为，并通过数值求解描述指向矢、极性序与电势空间变化的微分方程，给出反铁电相结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极性液晶可呈现从高温常规向列相（nematic phase）、中间温度调制向列（反铁电，antiferroelectric）相到低温均匀极性（铁电，ferroelectric）相的多重相与相变，但缺乏统一的理论描述。

Method: 构建理论模型，核心为挠性电（倾向于在调制相中产生展曲，splay）与静电（对展曲施加能量惩罚）之间的竞争；通过自由能最小化导出描述指向矢（director）、极性序（polar order）与电势（electric potential）空间变化的微分方程，并进行数值求解；同时与相变附近的简化模型进行比较。

Result: 数值求解确定了反铁电相的结构：高温下调制结构呈正弦形式（sinusoidal）；较低温度下转变为由窄畴壁（domain walls）分隔的宽畴（wide domains）结构。

Conclusion: 挠性电与静电的竞争可统一解释极性液晶的相序列与反铁电相结构演化，数值解可与相变附近适用的简化模型相互对照。

Abstract: Polar liquid crystals can exhibit a series of phases and phase transitions, from the conventional nematic phase at high temperature to a modulated nematic (antiferroelectric) phase at intermediate temperature, and then to a uniform polar (ferroelectric) phase at lower temperature. Here, we develop a theory for this phase behavior. The theory is based on a competition between flexoelectricity (which favors splay in the modulated phase) and electrostatics (which provides an energy penalty for splay). Minimizing the free energy leads to differential equations that describe the spatial variation of the director, the polar order, and the electric potential. By solving the differential equations numerically, we determine the structure of the antiferroelectric phase. At high temperature, the modulated structure is sinusoidal. At lower temperature, it crosses over to a structure of wide domains separated by narrow domain walls. These numerical solutions can be compared with simplified models that apply near the phase transitions.

</details>


### [249] [Folding of multishape metamaterials guided by plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16045)
*Wenfeng Liu,Jean-Baptiste Chombart,Bernard Ennis,Corentin Coulais*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种单轴折叠策略（uniaxial folding strategy），利用塑性（plasticity）与加载历史（loading history），将剪纸结构（kirigami）转化为具备可调刚度（tunable rigidity）的多形态超材料（multishape metamaterials）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多形态超材料在可调光学、可穿戴电子、软体机器人与航空航天等领域前景广阔，但其驱动通常依赖复杂的多轴或多物理场加载（multiaxial or multiphysics loading），限制了应用的可扩展性。

Method: 采用带切口图案的二维剪纸片材（kirigami），通过单轴拉伸与压缩循环，借助塑性变形与加载历史引导出多种可调且彼此不同的形状变化，实现自引导塑性折叠（self-guided plastic folding）。

Result: 所展开的形态展现出跨越三个数量级的宽范围力学性能，并具备可调刚度。

Conclusion: 自引导塑性折叠为从微米尺度到工业尺度部署承载型超材料（load-bearing metamaterials）提供了独特的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Multishape metamaterials can morph into several different shapes and offer exciting prospects in areas as diverse as tunable optics, wearable electronics, soft robotics, and aerospace. Yet, multishape metamaterials are typically actuated with complex multiaxial or multiphysics loading. Here, we introduce a uniaxial folding strategy that turns kirigami, 2D sheets patterned with cuts, into multishape metamaterials with tunable rigidity. Our folding approach leverages plasticity and loading history to guide multiple and tunable distinct shape changes upon cycles of stretching and compression. The deployed shapes exhibit a wide range of mechanical properties spanning three orders of magnitude. Self-guided plastic folding provides a uniquely scalable route to deploy load-bearing metamaterials from the micrometer up to the industrial scale.

</details>


### [250] [Elasto-inertial force exerted on a sphere oscillating in a fluid near a soft interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16575)
*Axel Batalha,Harshit Joshi,Yacine Amarouchene,Thomas Salez*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文在润滑（lubrication）区内理论研究浸没球体在弹性壁附近的高频振荡，先通过内层润滑流与外层流的一致匹配（matching）厘清刚性壁附加质量（added mass）的既有分歧，再以刚性壁问题为参考做壁面变形的微扰分析（perturbative analysis），导出流体力学力的首阶弹性-惯性修正（elasto-inertial correction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限流动中即使雷诺数（Reynolds number）很低、对流惯性可忽略，流体惯性（fluid inertia）仍可能重要；但其与界面可变形性（interface deformability）的耦合尚属开放问题。

Method: 将内层润滑流与外层流进行一致匹配以统一刚性壁附加质量的预测，并以此刚性壁问题为基准，对壁面变形开展微扰分析，求取流体力学力的首阶弹性-惯性修正。

Result: 结果表明，界面柔软性与流体惯性的耦合产生一个主导的非耗散（non-dissipative）力贡献，在高频极限下按频率的四次方（power four of the frequency）标度，对应球体位置的第四阶时间导数。

Conclusion: 该发现可能对生物与软物质（soft-matter）体系中可变形界面附近胶体颗粒（colloidal particles）的短时动力学（short-time dynamics）具有启示意义。

Abstract: Fluid inertia can remain important in confined flows, even at low Reynolds number when convective inertia is negligible; yet its coupling with interface deformability is an open problem. Here, we theoretically investigate the high-frequency oscillations of an immersed sphere near an elastic wall in the lubrication regime. We first reconcile previous predictions for the added mass near a rigid wall by consistently matching the inner lubrication flow with the outer flow. Using this rigid-wall problem as a reference, we then develop a perturbative analysis of wall deformation and derive the leading elasto-inertial correction to the hydrodynamic force. Our results reveal that the coupling between interface softness and fluid inertia produces a dominant non-dissipative force contribution scaling as the power four of the frequency in the high-frequency limit, thus corresponding to the fourth time derivative of the sphere's position. This finding may have implications regarding the short-time dynamics of colloidal particles near deformable interfaces in biological and soft-matter settings.

</details>


### [251] [Small-angle scattering reveals heterogeneous nanoparticle size distributions through Bayesian inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16587)
*Songhao Yin,Yushiro Mori,Yui Hayashi,Zuolin Meng,Lester Canque Geonzon,Hiroyoshi Nakano,Hiroshi Noguchi,Masato Okada,Koichi Mayumi,Kumiko Hayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究结合小角中子散射（SANS）与小角X射线散射（SAXS），在贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）框架下构建多峰粒径分布模型，用于解析纳米颗粒（nanoparticles）的非均一粒径分布，并强调模型选择（model selection）对准确估计的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米颗粒在生物医学、催化、传感与环境修复等领域应用广泛，表面功能化（surface functionalization）进一步拓展其功能；但在高离子强度条件下易聚集形成团簇，生物来源纳米颗粒本身粒径也可能不均一，因此需要可靠手段表征其异质粒径分布（heterogeneous particle-size distribution）。

Method: 采用小角中子散射（small-angle neutron scattering, SANS）与小角X射线散射（small-angle X-ray scattering, SAXS）探测静态散射强度（static scattering intensity）随波矢（wavevector）的变化，并在贝叶斯框架内构建多峰粒径分布（multimodal particle-size distribution）模型进行分析。

Result: 由于SAXS与SANS对散射强度的波矢依赖具有敏感性，即使大颗粒仅占很小比例（< 10%）并与小颗粒混合，也能分辨出两种不同粒径；同时表明模型选择对准确估计非均一粒径分布具有关键作用。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯散射分析方法可广泛用于跨应用领域的纳米颗粒表征（nanoparticle characterization）。

Abstract: Nanoparticles have attracted considerable attention owing to their broad range of applications. Surface functionalization further expands their functionality, making them useful in fields such as biomedicine, catalysis, sensing, and environmental remediation. In such applications, nanoparticles may form clusters through aggregation under high ionic-strength conditions, while biologically derived nanoparticles can be intrinsically heterogeneous in size. In this study, we apply small-angle neutron scattering (SANS) and small-angle X-ray scattering (SAXS) to nanoparticles and use Bayesian inference to analyze their heterogeneous particle-size distributions. Because SAXS and SANS probe the wavevector dependence of the static scattering intensity, they can sensitively resolve two distinct particle sizes even when a small fraction (< 10%) of larger particles is mixed with smaller particles. We construct multimodal particle-size distribution models within a Bayesian framework and demonstrate the importance of model selection for accurately estimating heterogeneous particle-size distributions. This approach should be broadly applicable to nanoparticle characterization across a wide range of applications.

</details>


### [252] [Complex nematic dynamics driven by steady Kolmogorov flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16857)
*Rahil N Valani,Sumesh P Thampi,Julia M Yeomans*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 在被动向列相中施加稳态但空间非均匀的Kolmogorov流，可在无速度涨落与流体反馈条件下，仅凭向列序的非线性动力学驱动从定常到周期再到不规则时空动力学的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂向列流体动力学（nematohydrodynamics）与不规则缺陷动力学通常与含时流动（如主动湍流）相伴，但尚不清楚时间复杂性是否必须依赖流体反馈。本文考察稳态空间变化流场能否单独诱发时间依赖动力学。

Method: 构建平面向列相受时间恒定、空间周期变化Kolmogorov流作用的简化模型，并消除速度涨落与流体动力学反馈；结合分岔分析（bifurcation analysis）、相空间分析与降阶模型（reduced-order models），包括两模态模型与五模态Galerkin截断（Galerkin truncation）。

Result: 随流场强度增大，系统由定常态转为周期振荡，并在更强驱动下进入不规则时空动力学；时间依赖态涉及局域向列序抑制与指向矢重排。两模态模型通过鞍结无穷周期分岔（saddle-node infinite-period bifurcation）刻画周期运动的发生，五模态Galerkin截断再现定常—周期—不规则的全过程。

Conclusion: 固定的空间非均匀速度场可通过向列序自身的非线性动力学产生时间复杂性，说明流体反馈并非此类复杂动力学的必要条件；时间依赖行为源于空间变化剪切与向列弛豫之间的竞争。

Abstract: Complex nematohydrodynamics, including irregular defect dynamics, is commonly associated with time-dependent flows, most notably in active turbulence. Here we consider a simple model of a planar nematic subjected to a temporally steady but spatially varying flow and investigate the onset of time-dependent dynamics. Subjecting a passive nematic to steady Kolmogorov flow, while eliminating velocity fluctuations and hydrodynamic feedback, we find that increasing the flow strength drives a transition from stationary to periodic and, at stronger forcing, irregular spatiotemporal dynamics. The time-dependent states involve repeated local suppression of nematic order and director reorganisation, arising from the competition between spatially varying shear and nematic relaxation. Bifurcation and phase-space analyses characterise this progression, while reduced-order models identify the dynamical origins. A two-mode model captures the onset of periodic motion through a saddle-node infinite-period bifurcation. A five-mode Galerkin truncation further recovers the progression from stationary to periodic and irregular dynamics. These results allow us to explain how a fixed, spatially heterogeneous velocity field can generate temporal complexity through the nonlinear dynamics of nematic order alone.

</details>


### [253] [Interfacial Structure and Dynamics of Active-Passive Fluid Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16973)
*Linnea Heitmeier*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 采用分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulation）研究活性布朗粒子（active Brownian particles）与被动Lennard-Jones流体界面的静态与动态性质，发现两种表面张力（surface tension）测量方法在活性开启后系统性不一致，并识别出三个活性区间及不可逆混合阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究活性流体（active fluid）与被动流体（passive fluid）界面这一尚未充分理解的问题，特别是活性如何改变界面张力与界面动力学。

Method: 对与被动Lennard-Jones流体接触的活性布朗粒子体系进行分子动力学模拟；通过压强各向异性（pressure anisotropy）与毛细波分析（capillary-wave analysis）两条独立路径计算表面张力，并分析静态密度/取向分布及界面动力学。

Result: 两种表面张力计算方法在活性开启后出现系统性分歧；静态密度与取向剖面显示三个不同的活性区间（activity regimes），粒子倾向于朝界面取向；界面动力学中存在一个使混合变为不可逆的活性阈值，并与混合区间的起始相吻合，同时界面宽度出现向稳态弛豫前的瞬态过冲（transient overshoot）。

Conclusion: 活性显著改变液-液界面行为：表面张力的测量路径依赖性、粒子界面取向偏好以及不可逆混合阈值共同刻画了活性-被动界面的物理特征。

Abstract: We investigate the interface between an active and a passive fluid using molecular dynamics simulations of active Brownian particles in contact with a passive Lennard-Jones fluid. We compute the surface tension via two independent routes, pressure anisotropy and capillary-wave analysis, and find that they disagree systematically once activity is switched on. Static density and orientation profiles reveal three distinct activity regimes, with particles preferentially oriented toward the interface. Going beyond these static properties, we characterize the interface dynamics: a threshold activity above which mixing becomes irreversible, coinciding with the onset of the mixing regime, and a transient overshoot in interfacial width before relaxation to steady state.

</details>


### [254] [Sedimentation of flexible fibers through an ordered array of pillars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17023)
*Ursy Makanga,Camille Duprat,Blaise Delmotte*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过数值模拟揭示，周期柱阵列中柔性纤维的沉降轨迹在长时间下收敛为"之字形（zigzag）"与"位移（displacement）"两种迁移模式，由短时纤维-柱相互作用散射动力学决定，并可用极简理论模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在既有工作（Makanga et al., Physical Review Fluids 8, 044303 (2023)）基础上，进一步考察周期柱阵列对黏性流体中柔性纤维沉降动力学的影响，以理解细长颗粒在柱阵列中的长期输运行为。

Method: 采用数值模拟（numerical simulations）研究柔性纤维在周期柱阵列中的沉降，并结合一个仅含最小要素的理论模型（theoretical model with minimal ingredients）对长期行为进行预测。

Result: 纤维轨迹在长时间下进入两种迁移模式之一：之字形模式下相对重力方向无平均位移，质心近似沿准直线运动；位移模式下存在相对重力方向的净位移，质心沿周期性偏斜-凸起路径（cyclical skew-bumping path）运动。模式由纤维-柱相互作用引发的短时散射动力学（scattering dynamics）决定。

Conclusion: 上述发现及纤维的长期输运性质可为后续实验提供依据，并有助于设计基于重力的确定性侧向位移（deterministic lateral displacement, DLD）器件，以按几何和/或力学性质分选细长颗粒。

Abstract: In this study, we extend our previous work [Makanga \textit{et al.}, Physical Review Fluids 8, 044303 (2023)] to investigate the effects of a periodic array of pillars on the settling dynamics of a flexible fiber in a viscous fluid. By means of numerical simulations, we show that the fiber trajectory falls at long times into one of two migration modes, ``zigzag" and ``displacement", which are determined by the short-time scattering dynamics induced by fiber-pillar interactions. In the zigzag mode, there is no average displacement relative to the direction of gravity; the center of mass of the fiber follows a quasi-straight line. Conversely, in the displacement mode, there is a net displacement relative to the direction of gravity; the fiber center of mass follows a cyclical skew-bumping path. We show how this long-time behavior can be predicted using a theoretical model with minimal ingredients. These findings, together with the long-time transport properties of the fiber, provide insights into future experiments, as well as the design of gravity-based deterministic lateral displacement (DLD) devices to sort elongated particles based on their geometric and/or mechanical properties.

</details>


### [255] [Time reparametrization across kinetic rules in a glass-forming liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17092)
*Leonardo dos Santos Vitoria*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 在三维多分散硬球液体中，将平面约束规则（$f_P$）与限制交换参与的纯 $f_R$ 规则调至可比的结构弛豫时间后，消除时间变量，发现集体参数关系近乎坍缩（near-collapse），但存在虽小却可分辨的残余，且该残余与规则相关的位移统计及取向性迁移率信号共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究提示，结合交换移动（swap moves）与动力学约束的蒙特卡洛算法对液体弛豫路径的改动，大部分可被吸收进时间重参数化（time reparametrization），表现为不同空间尺度重叠量（overlaps）间参数关系的近乎坍缩。本文旨在检验这一对应关系是否完备，即是否存在无法被时间重参数化吸收的规则依赖残余。

Method: 采用三维多分散硬球液体（polydisperse hard-sphere liquid），对比两种规则：将部分粒子平移限制在随机取向平面上的平面 $f_P$ 规则，与限制交换参与的纯 $f_R$ 规则。在每个堆积分数（packing fraction）下将两规则调至可比的结构弛豫时间并显式消去时间，考察 $0.58 \leq \phi \leq 0.62$ 范围内的参数关系；辅以自—异分解（self–distinct decomposition）与"幽灵"对照（ghost control）分析。

Result: 在 $0.58 \leq \phi \leq 0.62$ 区间观察到近乎坍缩，同时存在小幅但可分辨的残余，且该残余在将直接受约束粒子排除出观测量后依然存在。自—异分解显示个体位移统计可再现该残余的大部分量级；在平移自由的粒子中，当指向受约束邻居的相对方向与其禁戒法向（forbidden normals）越对齐时，大位移概率下降，即使固定受约束邻居数目亦然；该关联在幽灵对照中与零一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果补充而非否定时间重参数化图像：集体参数关系的近乎坍缩可以与规则相关的位移统计、以及未被直接约束粒子迁移率中的取向性特征（orientational signature）共存，表明时间重参数化未能完全吸收算法规则带来的动力学差异。

Abstract: Monte Carlo algorithms combining swap moves with kinetic constraints allow the relaxation pathways of a liquid to be modified without changing its equilibrium measure. Recent work suggests that much of this change can be absorbed into a time reparametrization, yielding a near-collapse of parametric relations between overlaps measured at different spatial scales. We examine this correspondence in a three-dimensional polydisperse hard-sphere liquid by comparing a planar $f_P$ rule that confines the translation of a fraction of particles to randomly oriented planes with a pure-$f_R$ rule that restricts swap participation. At each packing fraction, the two rules are tuned to comparable structural relaxation times, and time is eliminated explicitly. For $0.58 \leq φ\leq 0.62$, we find a near-collapse accompanied by a small but resolved residual that persists when directly confined particles are excluded from the observable. A self--distinct decomposition shows that the statistics of individual displacements reproduce most of the scale of this residual. Among translationally free particles, the probability of large displacements decreases as the relative directions to constrained neighbors become more aligned with their forbidden normals, even when the number of constrained neighbors is held fixed. This association is consistent with zero in a ghost control, where the same normals impose no translational restriction. Our findings complement the time-reparametrization picture by showing that a near-collapse of collective parametric relations can coexist with rule-dependent displacement statistics and an orientational signature in the mobility of particles that are not directly constrained.

</details>


### [256] [Inter-Nanoparticle Repulsion Effect on Self-Organization in Polymer Nanocomposites: A Langevin Dynamics Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17254)
*Paawan Chandrakanth,Nikos Ch. Karayiannis,Subhalaxmi Das,Supriya Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过朗之万动力学（Langevin Dynamics）模拟，系统研究强受限条件下纳米颗粒间排斥强度对聚合物纳米复合材料（PNCs）自组装形貌的调控作用，识别出四种形貌区间并揭示颗粒有序与聚合物向列有序的反相关关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米颗粒聚集与液-液相分离（liquid-liquid phase separation）相关，涉及生物分子区室化、弹性网络中的液体等场景；混合-去混合行为受粒径分布、维度、分子间相互作用与相对浓度等因素影响，而纳米颗粒间排斥对极端受限下自组装的作用尚需系统考察。

Method: 采用朗之万动力学模拟，将纳米颗粒组装体（致密液滴）置于半柔性棒状链的聚合物基体中，在固定其他相互作用的前提下仅改变纳米颗粒-纳米颗粒排斥强度；使用基于团簇（cluster-based）的分析、结晶度与向列有序度的精细描述符分别量化局域与全局有序。

Result: 随排斥强度变化识别出四种形貌区间：液滴（droplet）、平板（slab）、晶态液滴（crystalline droplet）与分散（dispersed）。平板区间纳米颗粒结晶可忽略但聚合物向列有序最强；晶态液滴因呈菱形（rhomboid）而具广泛三角密堆积晶畴且聚合物向列有序显著降低；分散区间颗粒碎裂促进高效混合与聚合物的局域近晶型（smectic-like）有序。

Conclusion: 精细调节纳米颗粒间排斥可控制自组织及最终形貌，为具有定制功能的聚合物纳米复合材料提供设计思路。

Abstract: Demixing through liquid-liquid phase separation is relevant to nanoparticle (NP) aggregation in polymer nanocomposites (PNCs), biomolecular compartmentalization, and liquids embedded in elastic networks. Mixing-demixing phase behavior depends on factors, such as particle size distribution, system dimen-sionality, intermolecular interactions, and relative concentrations. Here, using Langevin Dynamics simu-lations, we systematically investigate the effect of inter-nanoparticle repulsion on the self-assembly of PNCs under extreme confinement. A nanoparticle assembly, in the form of a compact droplet, is immersed in a polymer matrix of semiflexible, rod-like chains. By varying the nanoparticle-nanoparticle repulsion strength, while fixing all other interactions, we identify four distinct regimes with respect to the self-assembled NP morphologies: droplet, slab, crystalline droplet, and dispersed. Cluster-based analysis reveals a progressive loss of nanoparticle droplet integrity, leading to fragmentation within the dispersed regime. Local order of the nanoparticle assembly and global order of polymer chains are gauged through refined descriptors, quantifying the degrees of crystallinity and of nematic order, respectively. Interest-ingly, the slab regime exhibits negligible nanoparticle crystallization yet demonstrates the strongest polymer nematic ordering. In contrast, the crystalline droplet displays extensive triangular close-packed crystallites accompanied by a significant reduction in polymer nematic order, owing to its rhomboid shape. In the dispersed regime, nanoparticle fragmentation facilitates efficient mixing and local smectic-like ordering of the polymer chains. These findings demonstrate that fine-tuning inter-NP repulsions can control self-organization and the resulting morphologies, providing insights into PNCs with tailored functionality.

</details>


### [257] [Interactions from memory footprints: Cage-hopping, mnemotaxis & nonreciprocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17257)
*Jyoti Prasad Banerjee,Sambodh Sinha,Shashi Thutupalli,Rituparno Mandal,Deb Sankar Banerjee*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文通过主体（agent-based）模拟，构建统一框架，将共享记忆足迹（memory footprints）介导的单细胞动力学与群体自组织（collective organization）联系起来，并揭示其产生的有效成对相互作用（effective pairwise interactions）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞通过重塑环境留下共享、长寿命的物理化学轨迹（记忆足迹）并据此主动导向自身轨迹，这种趋激反（stigmergic responses）也影响微菌落形成等大规模自组织。然而，连接单细胞动力学与群体组织的统一框架仍难以确立，尤其是通过有效成对相互作用（effective pairwise interactions）这一桥梁。

Method: 对一类显式考虑足迹沉积（deposition）与衰减（decay）的相互作用随机游走模型（interacting random walk model）进行大规模主体模拟（agent-based simulations）。

Result: 在孤立细胞中观察到显著的笼跳跃（cage-hopping）动力学，并揭示细胞对外部施加的记忆梯度（memory gradients）表现出复杂的记忆趋性反应（mnemotactic responses）；共享空间记忆场还产生非平凡的有效成对相互作用，其既可为互易（reciprocal）也可为非互易（nonreciprocal），并塑造细胞群体的集体组织。

Conclusion: 研究在单细胞与集体尺度之间建立了统一图景，结论亦可推广至群体机器人（swarm robots）等合成活性物质（synthetic active matter）的集体行为。

Abstract: Cellular motility is integral to biological processes ranging from foraging to immune responses and cancer metastasis. While cells often navigate by sensing pre-existing environmental cues, many actively steer their future trajectories by remodeling their surroundings via shared, long-lived physicochemical trails called memory footprints. These stigmergic responses to shared footprints also influence large-scale self-organization, such as the early stages of microcolony formation. Despite recent progress, a unified framework connecting single-cell dynamics and collective organization through effective pairwise interactions remains elusive. To bridge this gap, we perform extensive agent-based simulations of an interacting random walk model that explicitly accounts for footprint deposition and decay. Our approach reveals distinct cage-hopping dynamics in solitary cells, as well as complex mnemotactic responses to externally imposed memory gradients. Furthermore, we demonstrate that shared spatial memory fields generate nontrivial effective pairwise interactions, both reciprocal and nonreciprocal, that shape the collective organization of cell populations. Beyond multiscale cellular dynamics, our findings have broader implications for the collective behavior of synthetic active matter, such as swarm robots.

</details>


<div id='math.QA'></div>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [A Max-Flow Min-Cut Theorem for Quantum Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15508)
*Gian Luca Spitzer,Ion Nechita*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文为量子图（quantum graph）提出了割（cut）与流（flow）的自然定义，并证明其满足最大流最小割定理（max-flow min-cut theorem）：最大 S-T 流值等于最小广义 S-T 割值，而最小普通 S-T 割值可能更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典图上的最大流最小割定理已成熟，但量子图上尚无对应的割与流定义，需要建立与之相容的概念框架。

Method: 提出两类割：将顶点集二分的普通割（ordinary cut），以及作为二元划分函数连续松弛（continuous relaxation）的广义割（generalised cut）；并据此定义量子图上的 S-T 流。

Result: 对任意不相交的终端集（terminal sets）S 与 T，最大 S-T 流值等于最小广义 S-T 割值，而最小普通 S-T 割值可能严格更大；在经典图上两者一致，从而复原经典定理。

Conclusion: 将流视为广义割的对偶（dual）比视为普通割的对偶更为自然。

Abstract: We propose natural definitions of cuts and flows for quantum graphs. For cuts we consider two notions: ordinary cuts, which partition the vertex set into two parts; and generalised cuts, defined as a continuous relaxation of the binary partition function. We show that our definitions satisfy a max-flow min-cut theorem: For any disjoint terminal sets $S$ and $T$, the maximum value of an $S$-$T$-flow of a quantum graph equals the minimum value of a generalised $S$-$T$ cut, while the minimum value of an ordinary $S$-$T$ cut can be larger. While for classical graphs the values of the minimum generalised cut and the minimum ordinary cut coincide---recovering the classical max-flow min-cut theorem---we also show how it is more natural to think of flows as duals of generalised cuts instead of ordinary cuts.

</details>


### [259] [Quantization of Betti Mapping Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16137)
*John Francis,Owen Gwilliam,Rajiv Kaipa*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该文提出一种针对以移位泊松（shifted Poisson）目标为取值空间的Betti映射叠（Betti mapping stacks）的量子化（quantization）程序，其核心是将因子化同调（factorization homology）与形式几何（formal geometry）相结合，并应用于低维流形局部系统（local systems）的导出叠（derived stacks）之量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅指出所处理的对象为移位泊松目标下的Betti映射叠及低维流形局部系统的导出叠，可推断目标是为此类对象建立量子化框架。

Method: 方法上组合使用因子化同调与形式几何两种工具，构造Betti映射叠在移位泊松目标情形下的量子化程序。

Result: 作者声称得到一种Betti映射叠的量子化程序，并可对低维流形局部系统的导出叠实现量子化；摘要未给出具体定理或计算实例。

Conclusion: 因子化同调与形式几何的结合可为移位泊松目标下的Betti映射叠提供统一的量子化途径，适用于低维流形局部系统的导出叠。

Abstract: We describe a quantization procedure for Betti mapping stacks with shifted Poisson targets by combining factorization homology and formal geometry. We quantize derived stacks of local systems of low-dimensional manifolds.

</details>


### [260] [Braided module categories from small braidings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16900)
*Tyler Franke,Ingo Runkel,Volker Schomerus*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文引入幺半范畴包含（inclusion of monoidal categories）$\mathcal{C}\hookrightarrow\mathcal{D}$ 上的“小编织（small braiding）”，使 $\mathcal{C}$ 中对象可与 $\mathcal{D}$ 中对象编织，并据此将 $\mathcal{D}$ 及其子范畴构造为 $\mathcal{C}$ 上的编织模范畴（braided module category）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于编织模范畴的重构结果（reconstruction results）多依赖有限性假设（finiteness assumptions），本文旨在给出不依赖此类假设的推广版本，并补充来自量子群（quantum groups）的新实例。

Method: 定义幺半范畴包含 $\mathcal{C}\hookrightarrow\mathcal{D}$ 上的小编织，该结构使 $\mathcal{C}$ 成为编织幺半范畴（braided monoidal category），并使 $\mathcal{D}$ 及 $\mathcal{D}$ 的某些子范畴成为 $\mathcal{C}$ 上的编织模范畴；在此基础上证明相应重构定理的版本，并构造量子群相关例子及其量子矩映射（quantum moment maps）。

Result: 证明了不依赖有限性假设的编织模范畴重构结果版本，并构造了若干源自量子群的新例子，以及与之关联的量子矩映射。

Conclusion: 小编织为在更一般（无需有限性假设）的框架下研究编织模范畴与重构提供了统一工具，并通过量子群的实例展示了该框架的适用性。

Abstract: We introduce small braidings on inclusions of monoidal categories $\mathcal{C}\hookrightarrow\mathcal{D}$, which allow one to braid objects in $\mathcal{C}$ with objects in $\mathcal{D}$. A small braiding makes $\mathcal{C}$ a braided monoidal category, and turns $\mathcal{D}$, as well as certain subcategories of $\mathcal{D}$, into braided module categories over $\mathcal{C}$. We prove versions of previously known reconstruction results for braided module categories which do not rely on finiteness assumptions. We construct a number of new examples coming from quantum groups, as well as the associated quantum moment maps.

</details>
