# math.SG [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Toward the abelian category of positive Lagrangians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17282)
*Asaf Kapota,Jake P. Solomon*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 该文构造一类以单参数特殊拉格朗日子流形族为基础的几何过程，用以刻画Calabi-Yau流形导出Fukaya范畴中正拉格朗日膜（positive Lagrangian branes）间态射的核与余核；若过程总终止，则正拉格朗日膜全子范畴为阿贝尔范畴，并在split-generation假设下给出稳定对象对应特殊拉格朗日膜的Bridgeland稳定性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是验证Joyce关于正拉格朗日膜子范畴构成阿贝尔范畴（abelian category）的猜想，并在Thomas-Yau猜想框架下把稳定对象与特殊拉格朗日膜（special Lagrangian branes）联系起来。

Method: 方法基于带边特殊拉格朗日子流形的单参数族：边界一部分位于态射定义域所对应的拉格朗日子流形上，另一部分位于余定义域所对应的拉格朗日子流形上；当族遇到奇点或障碍墙（obstruction walls）时执行手术（surgery），并以该构造的终止配置确定态射的核（kernel）与余核（cokernel）。

Result: 若构造总是终止，则正拉格朗日膜的全子范畴为阿贝尔范畴，且该阿贝尔范畴关于全纯体积形式定义的中心荷（central charge）容许Harder-Narasimhan filtrations；在适当的split-generation假设下，可构造稳定对象同构于特殊拉格朗日膜的Bridgeland稳定性条件（Bridgeland stability condition）。构造在双环面（two-torus）上被证明总终止，并给出高维示例。

Conclusion: 结论是核与余核的几何刻画为Joyce猜想提供了条件性支持，并将Harder-Narasimhan filtrations与Thomas-Yau猜想的稳定性预测纳入同一框架；终止性目前仅在双环面得到证明，高维情形仅有示例。

Abstract: We introduce a geometric construction whose terminal configuration determines the kernel and cokernel of a morphism between positive Lagrangian branes in the derived Fukaya category of a Calabi-Yau manifold. Thus, if the construction always terminates, the full subcategory of positive Lagrangian branes is abelian, as conjectured by Joyce. We show that this abelian category admits Harder-Narasimhan filtrations with respect to the central charge defined by the holomorphic volume form. Under appropriate split-generation assumptions, we construct a Bridgeland stability condition whose stable objects are isomorphic to special Lagrangian branes, as predicted by the Thomas-Yau conjecture.
  The geometric construction is based on a one-parameter family of special Lagrangian submanifolds with boundary. One part of the boundary is contained in the Lagrangian underlying the domain of the morphism and the other in the Lagrangian underlying the codomain. Surgeries occur as the family encounters singularities or obstruction walls. We prove that the construction always terminates for the two-torus and provide higher dimensional examples.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [262] [Monopole crystal and effective theory for a monopole-enhanced 3D U(1) gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15922)
*Alessandro Mariani*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 在立方晶格 U(1) 格点规范理论（lattice gauge theory）中引入磁单极增强项（monopole enhancement term）后，当增强足够强时磁单极不再稀疏，而形成反铁磁的 Néel 有序基态；稀薄激发叠加于稠密基态之上仍给出定性类似标准理论的 sine-Gordon 有效模型，并预测固定规范耦合下随磁单极化学势变化的一阶相变及随规范耦合变化的 Ising 普适类相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期文献提出了带磁单极增强项的立方晶格 U(1) 格点规范理论版本，本文旨在加深对该理论的理解，特别是磁单极增强对真空结构、有效描述及相图的影响。

Method: 采用解析与数值相结合的方法（analytical and numerical methods）：解析上论证强增强下磁单极的稠密反铁磁 Néel 有序基态，并借鉴 Polyakov 论证构造稠密基态上稀薄激发的有效 sine-Gordon 模型，同时解析预测相变；数值上则用于研究相图并检验部分预测。

Result: 当磁单极增强足够大时，磁单极不再稀薄（no longer dilute），而排列为反铁磁 Néel 有序基态；基于该稠密基态的稀薄激发变体 Polyakov 论证给出与标准理论定性相似的有效 sine-Gordon 模型。解析上预测：固定规范耦合（gauge coupling）而改变磁单极化学势（monopole chemical potential）时出现一阶相变（first-order transitions），对应主导磁单极电荷的改变；改变规范耦合时出现属于 Ising 普适类（Ising universality class）的相变。数值结果用于检验上述部分预测。

Conclusion: 磁单极增强项显著改变 U(1) 格点规范理论的基态结构（由稀薄转向稠密 Néel 有序），但其有效场论描述仍与标准理论定性一致，且理论相图包含一阶与 Ising 普适类相变；数值研究对部分解析预测提供了检验。

Abstract: Recently, a version of U(1) lattice gauge theory on the cubic lattice with a monopole enhancement term was considered in the literature. In this work, we further our understanding of this theory with a mixture of analytical and numerical methods. We show that for sufficiently large monopole enhancement, the monopoles are no longer dilute and they arrange themselves into an antiferromagnetic, Néel-ordered ground state. Nonetheless, a variant of Polyakov's argument for the dilute excitations over the dense ground state leads to an effective sine-Gordon model which is qualitatively similar to that of the standard theory. We also predict analytically the presence of first-order transitions as the monopole chemical potential is varied at fixed gauge coupling, corresponding to a change in the dominant monopole charge. Similarly, we predict a transition in the Ising universality class as the gauge coupling is varied. We then use numerical methods to study the phase diagram of the theory and check some of our predictions.

</details>


### [263] [Critical behavior of the bosonized Schwinger model with uniform matrix product states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16092)
*Takuya Okuda*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该工作采用玻色子化（bosonization）模型的哈密顿格点离散化，结合变分均匀矩阵乘积态（VUMPS）方法与受Symanzik有效理论约束的连续极限外推，确定二维量子电动力学（Schwinger模型）在θ=π处的临界质量 (m/g)_c = 0.333 555(5)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schwinger模型（二维量子电动力学）在θ=π处的临界质量是受关注的非微扰量，但已有基于交错费米子（staggered fermion）的确定结果需要独立方法交叉检验；玻色子化对偶表述此前在该问题上的格点应用尚不充分。

Method: 基于非紧致玻色场（non-compact boson field）的对偶表述做哈密顿格点离散化（Hamiltonian lattice discretization），在无限长链上用变分均匀矩阵乘积态（variational uniform matrix product state, VUMPS）算法数值求解基态，并严格保持电荷共轭（charge conjugation）对称性。

Result: 局域希尔伯特空间采用对角化自由极限下单点约化密度矩阵的谐振子基（oscillator basis）作为截断基；连续极限外推的拟合函数由格点修正的Symanzik有效理论约束。最终得到临界质量 (m/g)_c = 0.333 555(5)，与近期交错费米子结果在可比精度内一致。

Conclusion: 玻色子化格点表述配合VUMPS与对称性保持及有效理论约束的外推，可对Schwinger模型临界质量给出与费米子方法精度相当且相互印证的独立测定。

Abstract: Quantum electrodynamics in two spacetime dimensions, also known as the Schwinger model, admits a dual formulation in terms of a non-compact boson field. Using a Hamiltonian lattice discretization of the bosonized model, we compute the critical mass of the continuum Schwinger model at $θ=π$. We numerically obtain the ground state on an infinite chain by the variational uniform matrix product state (VUMPS) algorithm, preserving charge conjugation exactly. As a truncated basis for the local Hilbert space, we employ the oscillator basis that diagonalizes the one-site reduced density matrix of the free limit. We constrain the fitting functions for the continuum extrapolation using the Symanzik effective theory for lattice corrections. Our result for the critical mass, $(m/g)_c = 0.333\,555(5)$, agrees with recent staggered-fermion determinations at comparable precision.

</details>


### [264] [Numerical tests of chiral fermion weights with $η$-invariant phases in the 3450 model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16876)
*Tatsuhiro Misumi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 数值检验了将连续手征费米子与插值格点规范场耦合的方案：费米子权重取Pauli-Villars正则化模长，相位由高一维的Atiyah-Patodi-Singer η不变量给出。在无反常二维U(1)理论中，Polyakov圈与精确值吻合至0.04%，相因子缺失则严重偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续手征费米子（chiral fermions）与格点规范场耦合缺乏严格表述，近期提出的以PV模长与APS η不变量相位构造权重的方法需数值验证，尤其需检验APS相在恢复规范不变性与正确拓扑响应中的作用。

Method: 选用无反常（anomaly-free）二维U(1)理论（左手荷3、4，右手荷5、0，环面行列式已知精确解），将格点链上的规范场插值为连续场。零磁通时在16×16平坦联络网格上计算Polyakov圈并与精确值比较；单位磁通时在柱面上施加广义APS边界条件计算十二点关联函数（correlator），并考察规范场延拓至第三维对相位的影响。

Result: 零磁通下Polyakov圈与精确值偏差在0.04%以内；去掉APS相位后降至精确值约3%。真空极化响应给出诱导质量m/e=2.82089，接近5/√π=2.82095。单位磁通下三个光滑背景的模长偏差小于0.8%；相位偏差在含η不变量时为0.2–0.5 mrad，不含时为1.8–3.0 mrad。单费米子相位随第三维延拓方式变化15–46 mrad，但在无反常组合下相消至0.2 mrad以下。

Conclusion: 数值结果支持该APS η不变量相位方案能准确再现无反常手征规范理论的精确行列式与关联函数，且其延拓依赖性在无反常组合中被抑制，验证了方案的可行性与规范不变性。

Abstract: We test numerically a recently proposed prescription for coupling continuum chiral fermions to interpolated lattice gauge fields, in which the fermion weight has a Pauli-Villars (PV) regularized magnitude and a phase given by an Atiyah-Patodi-Singer (APS) $η$-invariant in one higher dimension. We use the anomaly-free two-dimensional $U(1)$ theory with left-handed charges $3,4$ and right-handed charges $5,0$, whose torus determinant is known exactly. At zero magnetic flux, with gauge fields interpolated from lattice links, the Polyakov loops evaluated on a $16\times16$ grid of flat connections agree with the exact values within $0.04\%$, whereas omitting the APS phase substantially reduces them to about $3\%$ of the exact result. The vacuum-polarization response yields an induced mass $m/e=2.82089$, close to $5/\sqrtπ=2.82095$. At unit flux, where zero modes make the determinant vanish, we calculate a twelve-point correlator on a cylinder with the generalized APS boundary condition. For three smooth backgrounds its magnitude agrees with the exact result within $0.8\%$. Without the $η$-invariant its phase deviates by $1.8$-$3.0$ mrad, and with it by $0.2$-$0.5$ mrad. The phase of each charged fermion changes by $15$-$46$ mrad when the gauge field is extended differently into the third dimension, but the change cancels to below $0.2$ mrad in the anomaly-free combination.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [Higher-Order Exceptional Point Sensing with Rydberg Micro-Ensemble Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14893)
*Jun Zhang,Ya-Jun Wang,Zi-Han Li,Jin-Hang Fang,Luan-Yi Wei,Qing Li,Guang-Can Guo,Bang Liu,Li-Hua Zhang,Dong-Sheng Ding,Bao-Sen Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作利用冷里德堡气体中空间结构化的微系综阵列（microensemble arrays），借助长程相互作用与空间连通性提升有效非厄米维度，实现并调控高阶奇异点（higher-order exceptional points），并获得超越标准量子极限的微波传感灵敏度 0.47(10) nV cm⁻¹ Hz⁻¹ᐟ²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶奇异点源于多个本征值与本征态的合并（coalescence），可增强对弱扰动的谱响应；但在相互作用多体系统中，由于多个相互作用本征态之间耦合复杂，实现与调控此类高阶简并仍具挑战。

Method: 在冷里德堡气体（cold Rydberg gas）中构建空间结构化的微系综阵列，利用长程相互作用（long-range interaction）与空间连通性（spatial connectivity）扩大有效非厄米维度（effective non-Hermitian dimensionality）；通过改变格点空间排布实现不同阶数的非厄米奇异点，并调节测量时间与原子数。

Result: 里德堡激发格点间的相互作用可提升量子费舍尔信息（quantum Fisher information），从而改善传感灵敏度；高阶奇异点呈现逐渐增强的非线性相边界（phase-boundary）响应。通过追踪相边界移动，获得 0.47(10) nV cm⁻¹ Hz⁻¹ᐟ² 的灵敏度，超越标准量子极限（standard quantum limit）。

Conclusion: 结果确立里德堡微系综阵列为连接高阶非厄米临界性（higher-order non-Hermitian criticality）、多体动力学（many-body dynamics）与微波传感（microwave sensing）的平台。

Abstract: Higher-order exceptional points arise from the coalescence of multiple eigenvalues and eigenstates, leading to enhanced spectral responses to weak perturbations. However, realizing and controlling such higher-order degeneracies in interacting many-body systems remains challenging due to the complex coupling among multiple interacting eigenstates. Here, we construct spatially structured microensemble arrays in cold Rydberg gas, where long-range interaction and spatial connectivity enlarge the effective non-Hermitian dimensionality, enabling higher-order exceptional points. By varying the spatial arrangement of these sites, we realize different orders of non-Hermitian exceptional points. We further show that interactions between Rydberg excitation sites can enhance the quantum Fisher information, thereby improving sensing sensitivity. The resulting higher-order exceptional points exhibit increasingly nonlinear phase-boundary responses, which can be further tuned by varying the measurement time and atom number. By tracking shifts of these phase boundaries, we achieve a sensitivity of 0.47(10) nV cm-1 Hz-1/2, surpassing the standard quantum limit. Our results establish Rydberg microensemble arrays as a platform connecting higher-order non-Hermitian criticality, many-body dynamics, and microwave sensing.

</details>


### [266] [Observing Autler--Townes splitting of an rf-dressed Feshbach resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15016)
*Samyajit Gayen,Matthew Chilcott,Chongwu Xie,Niels Kjærgaard*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 实验实现了Ding等人提出的利用射频缀饰场（rf dressing field）控制磁Feshbach共振附近散射长度（scattering length）的方案，在偶极阱中87Rb的1007 G共振上观测到Autler–Townes分裂，并演示了射频切换碰撞响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验并实验实现此前理论提出的方案：通过射频场将准束缚分子态（quasibound molecular state）与更深的、不同自旋投影的束缚态耦合，从而在磁Feshbach共振（magnetic Feshbach resonance）附近调控散射长度。

Method: 在偶极阱（dipole trap）中的87Rb上，用射频缀饰场耦合1007 G Feshbach共振对应的准束缚分子态与更深束缚态；以原子损耗（atom loss）为观测量，并在偏离裸共振的固定磁场下进行射频切换实验。

Result: 观测到1007 G Feshbach共振的Autler–Townes分裂，并实现碰撞响应的射频切换；原子损耗行为与散射长度实部幅值增大导致的三体复合（three-body recombination）增强相一致。

Conclusion: Autler–Townes分裂的观测证实了产生足够分子缀饰所需强射频磁场的可行性，为进一步实验检验射频控制相互作用所预测的弹性特性（elastic nature）奠定了基础。

Abstract: We experimentally implement the scheme introduced by Ding, D'Incao, and Greene [Phys. Rev. A {\bf 95}, 022709 (2017)] for controlling the scattering length near a magnetic Feshbach resonance using an rf dressing field. Specifically, we observe Autler--Townes splitting of the 1007 G Feshbach resonance in dipole-trapped $^{87}\mathrm{Rb}$ by coupling the associated quasibound molecular state to a deeper-lying bound state with a different spin projection. At a fixed magnetic field away from the bare resonance, we further demonstrate rf switching of the collisional response. Our experimental observable is atom loss, which is consistent with enhanced three-body recombination arising from an increase in the magnitude of the real part of the scattering length. Our observation of Autler--Townes splitting establishes the feasibility of generating the strong rf magnetic fields required for sufficient molecular dressing and provides an important basis for further experimental tests of the predicted elastic nature of the rf-controlled interactions.

</details>


### [267] [Engineering many-body tunneling dynamics of one-dimensional bosons with finite-core power-law interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15504)
*Abhik Kumar Saha,Cesare Vianello,Andrea Trombettoni,Barnali Chakrabarti*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文利用MCTDHB方法研究一维Bose-Josephson结中有限核幂律相互作用（finite-core power-law interactions）的量子多体隧穿动力学，揭示相互作用空间结构、势垒与初始布居不平衡对相干、退相位与复相等隧穿行为的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有Bose-Josephson结研究多聚焦于接触或固定形式相互作用，而相互作用核（interaction kernel）的空间结构如何影响多体隧穿动力学尚不清楚，且Rydberg dressing提供了可调控软核相互作用的实验途径，因而有必要系统探究相互作用轮廓与强度等因素的独立作用。

Method: 针对含中心势垒的盒状势中超冷玻色子构成的一维Bose-Josephson结，采用玻色子多组态含时Hartree方法（multiconfigurational time-dependent Hartree method for bosons, MCTDHB）求解多体含时动力学，并辅以两模扩展Bose-Hubbard模型（two-mode extended Bose-Hubbard model）进行对比分析，相互作用形式为V_α(r)=g/(r^α+d^α)。

Result: 弱相互作用下，增大α使系统从Josephson振荡（Josephson oscillations）过渡到退相位（dephasing）与复相（recurrent rephasing）；较高势垒抑制复相，较大初始不平衡促进向近零布居不平衡的弛豫；增强相互作用强度逐步抑制布居转移涨落，且在较大α时更明显。两模模型仅在每个阱的最低轨道主导时有效，强相互作用下多体空间结构发展并需多个自然轨道（natural orbitals）才能收敛，超出两模描述。

Conclusion: 相互作用轮廓与强度、势垒高度和初始态可作为相互独立的控制参数，用于调控一维玻色子的相干、退相位、复相及受抑制的多体隧穿；借助Rydberg dressing定制软核相互作用（soft-core interactions）为实验实现相互作用工程化隧穿（interaction-engineered tunneling）提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We investigate the many-body dynamics of a one-dimensional Bose-Josephson junction of ultracold bosons in a box-like potential with a central barrier, interacting via finite-core power-law interactions $V_α(r)=g/(r^α+d^α)$, where $d$ sets the short-distance scale and $α$ controls the spatial dependence. Using the multiconfigurational time-dependent Hartree method for bosons (MCTDHB), we explore how the interaction profile and strength, barrier, and initial population imbalance shape tunneling dynamics. At weak interaction, increasing $α$ drives a crossover from Josephson oscillations to dephasing and recurrent rephasing, demonstrating the importance of the interaction kernel's spatial structure. A higher barrier suppresses recurrences, whereas a larger initial imbalance promotes relaxation toward nearly vanishing population imbalance. Increasing interaction strength progressively suppresses population-transfer fluctuations, particularly at larger $α$. The two-mode extended Bose-Hubbard model captures the dynamics when the lowest orbital in each well dominates, but loses accuracy as additional orbitals become populated. In the strongly interacting regime, many-body spatial structures develop and several natural orbitals are needed for converged dynamics, placing the system beyond a two-mode description. Our results establish the interaction profile and strength, barrier, and initial state as distinct control parameters for coherent, dephasing, recurrent, and suppressed many-body tunneling of one-dimensional bosons. The ability to tailor soft-core interactions via Rydberg dressing offers a promising route to exploring interaction-engineered tunneling in ultracold atoms.

</details>


### [268] [Quantum Cost of Emptiness in a One-Dimensional Bose Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16247)
*Bin Huang,Ziyan Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 研究了排斥型Lieb–Liniger气体基态中空区间概率 $P_0(R)$ 的远尾渐近，将 $\ell=\rho R\to\infty$ 时的指数代价展开到常数量级，并给出椭圆积分形式的 $f(\gamma)$ 与普适的 $-1/4$ 对数系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空区间概率（empty interval probability）探测密度涨落的远尾，是刻画热力学基态长程涨落结构的核心观测量；此前缺乏对排斥Lieb–Liniger气体中该量在 $\ell\to\infty$ 时的完整渐近展开与系数来源的解析理解。

Method: 对辅助场行列式（auxiliary-field determinant）作精确高斯补全（Gaussian completion），把领头集体响应约化为静电狭缝问题（electrostatic slit problem）；再由偶极极化尾（dipole polarization tail）驱动的辅助散射返回、完整返回谱与占据快度端点（occupied-rapidity endpoints）确定各阶系数，并借助接触恒等式（contact identity）分析边界愈合尺度。

Result: 在固定 $\gamma=c/\rho>0$、$\ell=\rho R\to\infty$ 下，$-\log P_0=f(\gamma)\ell^2+B(\gamma)\sqrt{\ell}-\tfrac{1}{4}\log\ell+O_\gamma(1)$，无线性项，且 $B=2\zeta(3/2)\sqrt{f/(\pi\gamma)}$；$f(\gamma)$ 以椭圆积分（elliptic integrals）表出。

Conclusion: 平方根边界代价源于局域密度饱和而活跃快度范围按 $\sqrt{R}$ 扩张；展宽空洞在边界处探测到致密愈合尺度，其特征占据数的增长支持在强相互作用体能中出现涌现的经典场描述（emergent classical-field description）。

Abstract: The probability $P_0(R)$ of an empty interval of radius $R$ probes the far tail of density fluctuations in the thermodynamic ground state of the repulsive Lieb--Liniger gas. Here $ρ$ is the bulk density and $c$ the repulsion. As $\ell = ρR \to \infty$ at fixed $γ= c/ρ> 0$, we find $-\log P_0 = f(γ)\ell^2 + B(γ)\sqrt{\ell} - \frac{1}{4}\log\ell + O_γ(1)$, with no linear term and $B = 2ζ(3/2)\sqrt{f/(πγ)}$. Exact Gaussian completion of the auxiliary-field determinant reduces the leading collective response to an electrostatic slit problem, yielding $f(γ)$ in elliptic integrals. Its dipole polarization tail drives auxiliary scattering returns whose local density saturates while the active rapidity range expands as $\sqrt{R}$, producing the square-root boundary cost. The full return spectrum and the occupied-rapidity endpoints together fix the $-1/4$ logarithmic coefficient. Through the contact identity, widening the void probes a dense healing scale at the boundary whose growing characteristic occupation supports an emergent classical-field description even in a strongly interacting bulk.

</details>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [Poisson and Jacobi structures on quadratic Lie algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15800)
*Alessandro Arsie,Fabricio Valencia*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文将广义双重扩张（generalized double extension）理论用于二次（quadratic）Poisson 与 Jacobi 结构，给出保持标记各向同性理想（isotropic ideal）的斜对称算子之充要重构判据，并借助经典 Yang–Baxter 方程（classical Yang–Baxter equation）实现 Poisson 结构的重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是与有限维李代数（Lie algebra，特征零域上）上二次结构相容的 Poisson 与 Jacobi 结构，并推广已有的二次 Poisson 扩张理论至含退化算子、非交换二次商（nonabelian quadratic quotient）与完整广义扩张数据的更一般情形。

Method: 基于广义双重扩张理论给出斜对称算子保持标记各向同性理想的充要重构判据；结合 Kostant–Meyer 的环境括积重构与预设辛叶（symplectic leaf）数据重构 Poisson 结构，并以不变形式（invariant form）表达标准代数 Jacobi 方程；以统一的余链（cochain）框架刻画剩余标量障碍。

Result: 得到非退化特征子代数下 Poisson 与 Jacobi 情形的类似重构结果，并特别处理接触（contact）与局部共形辛（locally conformally symplectic）情形；剩余标量障碍分别落在 Poisson 情形的第三上同调（third cohomology）与 Euler 元属于标记理想的 Jacobi 分支中，后者系数取一维模。

Conclusion: 重构结果给出由 n 维二次 Poisson 结构系统构造 n+2k 维结构的流程，并在 Poisson 算子可逆时退化恢复已有的二次辛扩张公式；算例涵盖振子代数（oscillator algebras）、五维李代数 A_{5,3} 及与双不变度量（bi-invariant metric）曲率张量相关的 Poisson 结构。

Abstract: We study Poisson and Jacobi structures compatible with quadratic structures on finite-dimensional Lie algebras, mostly over fields of characteristic zero. Building on the theory of generalized double extensions, we give necessary and sufficient reconstruction criteria for the skew operators preserving a marked isotropic ideal, allowing degenerate operators, a nonabelian quadratic quotient, and the full generalized extension data. Recalling the characterization of quadratic Poisson structures in terms of skew-symmetric solutions of the classical Yang--Baxter equation, we combine the ambient bracket reconstruction of Kostant and Meyer with prescribed symplectic leaf data to reconstruct Poisson structures whose characteristic subalgebra is nondegenerate for the quadratic form. We express the standard algebraic Jacobi equations through the invariant form and obtain analogous reconstruction results for nondegenerate characteristic subalgebras. Particular attention is given to the contact and locally conformally symplectic cases. A common cochain framework identifies the remaining scalar obstruction in third cohomology in the Poisson case and in the Jacobi branch whose Euler element belongs to the marked ideal, with coefficients in a one dimensional module in the latter case. Our reconstruction results yield systematic procedures for constructing quadratic Poisson structures in dimension $n+2k$ from those in dimension $n$, and recover the earlier quadratic symplectic extension formulas when the Poisson operator is invertible. Explicit examples illustrate the applicability of the theory, including oscillator algebras, the five-dimensional Lie algebra $A_{5,3}$, and Poisson structures associated with the curvature tensor of a bi-invariant metric.

</details>


### [270] [Determinant Identities and Branch Selection in the Stationary Multi-Horizon Balance Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15637)
*Daheng Min*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 论文研究 Hennig-Neugebauer 族稳态轴对称真空多视界（multi-horizon）候选解，利用对称轴（symmetry axis）正则性条件导出的二次行列式关系处理符号分支歧义，并在非退化假设下证明其中一个分支不可能，从而得到刻画平衡态的显式行列式方程组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hennig-Neugebauer 族中存在由对称轴正则性条件产生的二次行列式关系，其在两个符号分支（sign branches）间留有表观歧义，需澄清哪一分支物理上可行，以正确刻画多视界平衡解。

Method: 在明确的非退化假设（non-degeneracy assumptions）下，对由轴对称正则性导出的二次行列式关系的两个符号分支进行分析，证明其中一支不可能成立，实现分支选择（branch selection）。

Result: 证明了两个符号分支中有一支不可能，从而消除了表观歧义；该分支选择给出了一组显式行列式方程（explicit determinant equations），用于刻画该族内的平衡（equilibrium）条件。

Conclusion: 对称轴正则性结合非退化假设足以唯一筛选出可行分支，所得显式行列式方程组可作为研究 Hennig-Neugebauer 族多视界平衡构型的系统化表述基础。

Abstract: We study the Hennig-Neugebauer family of stationary and axisymmetric vacuum multi-horizon candidates. The regularity conditions on the symmetry axis lead to quadratic determinant relations which leave an apparent ambiguity between two sign branches. Under explicit non-degeneracy assumptions, we prove that one of these branches is impossible. This branch selection yields a system of explicit determinant equations characterizing equilibrium within the family.

</details>


### [271] [Entire boosted hypersurfaces of vanishing mean curvature in Schwarzschild spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15912)
*Albachiara Cogo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明了在最大延拓史瓦西时空（maximally extended Schwarzschild spacetime）的两个渐近平直端中存在整极大超曲面（entire maximal hypersurfaces），其渐近行为趋近于依赖坐标的助推（coordinate-dependent boosts），并由此在外延史瓦西区域导出极大超曲面叶状结构（foliation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于构造与任意助推参数（boost parameter）相对应的几何时间函数，从而为史瓦西时空中的极大超曲面叶层化提供几何依据。

Method: 证明的关键步骤是引入最大延拓史瓦西时空中一个新的旋转对称时间函数（rotationally symmetric time function），该函数具有柯西性（Cauchy）及其他独立有价值的性质；随后据此构造渐近于依赖坐标助推的整极大超曲面。

Result: 成功证明了所述整极大超曲面的存在性，并作为推论得到外延史瓦西（exterior Schwarzschild）区域中极大超曲面的叶状结构，对应任意助推参数下的时间函数几何选取。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了极大超曲面与助推参数之间的几何联系，所引入的旋转对称柯西时间函数本身亦具有独立意义，可用于进一步研究史瓦西时空的叶层化与时间函数选取。

Abstract: We prove the existence of entire maximal hypersurfaces, asymptotic to suitable coordinate-dependent boosts in the two asymptotically flat ends of maximally extended Schwarzschild spacetime. As a corollary, this generates a foliation of maximal hypersurfaces in exterior Schwarzschild, which corresponds to a geometric choice of time function associated with any boost parameter. As a crucial step in the proof, we introduce a novel rotationally symmetric time function in maximally extended Schwarzschild, which is Cauchy and has properties of independent interest.

</details>


### [272] [The Cauchy problem for skew-torsion-parallel spinors with applications to supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17319)
*C. S. Shahbazi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究整体双曲洛伦兹四维流形上关于带斜对称挠率度规联络的不可约实旋量平行旋量的柯西问题，证明其模一处显式障碍的适定性，并给出超对称 Neveu-Schwarz 系统的适定性与刚性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决带斜对称挠率（skew-symmetric torsion）的度规联络下平行旋量（parallel spinor）的柯西问题（Cauchy problem），并厘清四维 Neveu-Schwarz 超引力演化流与旋量演化流的相容性。

Method: 借助旋量形式（spinorial forms）理论，通过柯西超曲面 Σ 上向量场生成流的旋转拉回作用显式积分旋量与度规的耦合流；将约束方程刻画为外微分系统（exterior differential system），并用接触结构（contact structures）与平坦叶状结构（flat foliations）描述其解。

Result: 证明柯西问题模显式障碍适定并给出初始数据刻画；在紧致情形下对约束方程完全分类，得出每个定向紧致三维流形均可作为携带斜挠率平行旋量的整体双曲洛伦兹四维流形的柯西超曲面；并证明四维 Neveu-Schwarz 超引力演化流与旋量演化流适定且相容。

Conclusion: 确立了四维超对称 Neveu-Schwarz 系统的适定性并给出超对称的初始数据刻画；进一步得到刚性结果：任何具紧致柯西超曲面的整体双曲超对称 Neveu-Schwarz 解均为平坦且丛-gerbe（bundle gerbe）曲率消失。

Abstract: We solve the Cauchy problem for an irreducible real spinor parallel with respect to a metric connection with skew-symmetric torsion on a globally hyperbolic Lorentzian four-manifold, proving that it is well-posed modulo an obstruction that we identify explicitly, and obtaining an initial-data characterization of such spinors. Our proof relies on the theory of spinorial forms, which we use to explicitly integrate the coupled flow for the spinor and underlying metric via a rotated pull-back action of the flow generated by a vector field on the Cauchy hypersurface $Σ$. We characterize the corresponding constraint equations as an explicit exterior differential system and describe all its solutions in terms of contact structures and flat foliations, obtaining a complete classification in the compact case. In particular, we conclude that every oriented compact three-manifold arises as the Cauchy hypersurface of a globally hyperbolic Lorentzian four-manifold equipped with a skew-torsion parallel spinor. Building on these results, we study the interaction between the four-dimensional Neveu-Schwarz supergravity evolution flow on an abelian bundle gerbe and the spinorial evolution flow determined by its supersymmetric conditions, which we prove to be well-posed and compatible. This establishes the well-posedness of the supersymmetric Neveu-Schwarz system in four dimensions and provides an initial-data characterization of supersymmetry. Furthermore, we obtain a strong rigidity result stating that every globally hyperbolic supersymmetric Neveu-Schwarz solution with a compact Cauchy hypersurface is flat and has vanishing bundle-gerbe curvature.

</details>


### [273] [A constructive computer-assisted existence proof for a Calabi--Yau metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16908)
*Michael R. Douglas,Javier Gómez-Serrano,Fabian Lehmann,Daniel Platt,Yidi Qi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文构造性地严格证明了Fermat四次超曲面（Fermat quartic hypersurface）上存在Ricci平坦Kähler度量（Ricci-flat Kähler metric），且不依赖Yau定理，并给出显式解析近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在不依赖Yau著名定理的前提下，独立验证Fermat四次超曲面上Ricci平坦Kähler度量的存在性，同时获得显式且精确的解析近似；方法具一般性，可推广至其他流形。

Method: 结合数值技术获得近似Ricci平坦的度量、用不动点定理（fixed point theorem）证明存在性，并以可验证数值（verified numerics）给出不动点定理所需的界；摘要还讨论了将该方法应用于其他流形时的计算问题。

Result: 给出Ricci平坦Kähler度量存在性的严格构造性证明（独立于Yau定理），并提供显式、精确的解析近似度量。

Conclusion: 该工作表明数值近似、不动点定理与可验证数值相结合可形成严格的构造性存在性证明框架，具有推广到其他流形的潜力。

Abstract: We give a rigorous, constructive proof that there exists a Ricci-flat Kähler metric on the Fermat quartic hypersurface, independently of Yau's famous theorem, along with an explicit and accurate analytic approximation to the metric. The proof combines numerical techniques to obtain the approximately Ricci flat metric, a fixed point theorem to prove existence, and verified numerics to get the bounds used by the fixed point theorem. The method is general and we discuss the computational issues in applying it to other manifolds.

</details>


### [274] [Spinorial geometry of Bismut--Hermitian--Einstein manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17195)
*Beatrice Brienza,Matteo Kevin Crisafio,Anna Fino,Dario Martelli*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出一对推广 I 型超引力（Type I supergravity）内部 Killing 旋量方程的旋量方程，证明其刻画六维黎曼自旋流形（six-dimensional Riemannian spin manifolds）上的若干类 Bismut-Hermitian-Einstein 结构，并在不假设紧致性的前提下给出到四维横截 Hermitian 几何的约化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以旋量方程统一刻画六维几何结构，并把 I 型超引力的内部 Killing 旋量条件与 Bismut-Hermitian-Einstein 结构、横截四维 Hermitian 几何联系起来，从而理解紧致与非紧致解（包括与 Hull-Strominger 系统及五维 Gauntlett-Kim 几何的关系）。

Method: 构造一对旋量方程，将内部 Killing 旋量方程推广至若干结构类；利用 Killing 旋量的双线性（bilinears）得到两个典范的、相互交换的、Bismut-平行（Bismut-parallel）Killing 向量场，实现无需紧致性假设的四维横截约化，并讨论各类变体及其嵌入。

Result: 其中一类结构的横截几何为共形 Kähler（conformally Kähler），且同时存在紧致与非紧致解；另一些变体不允许紧致解，其中之一具有 hyper-Hermitian 横截几何。文中还给出与闭三形式 Hull-Strominger 系统及五维 Gauntlett-Kim 几何的联系，并显式嵌入 I 型、IIB 型与 massive IIA 型超引力及广义超引力（generalized supergravity）。

Conclusion: 所提出的旋量方程框架统一描述多类六维 Bismut-Hermitian-Einstein 结构及其四维横截约化，并通过紧致与非紧致实例、超引力嵌入展示其适用性与与相关几何及通量紧致化系统的关联。

Abstract: We present a pair of spinorial equations, generalizing the internal Killing spinor equations of Type I supergravity, and show that they characterize several classes of Bismut-Hermitian-Einstein structures on six-dimensional Riemannian spin manifolds. We show that in this framework the local reduction to a four-dimensional transverse Hermitian geometry is provided by two canonical, commuting, Bismut-parallel Killing vector fields arising as bilinears in the Killing spinors, without assuming compactness. In one class, the transverse geometry is conformally Kahler and both compact and non-compact solutions exist. We also discuss variants that do not admit compact solutions, one of which has hyper-Hermitian transverse geometry. We discuss the relation of these structures to the Hull-Strominger system with closed three-form and to five-dimensional Gauntlett-Kim geometries and provide explicit embeddings in Type I, Type IIB and massive Type IIA supergravity, as well as in generalized supergravity. We illustrate our results with a number of examples, both compact and non-compact.

</details>

### [275] [A Positive Turn: Coupled Quintessence and Cosmological Neutrino Masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14584)
*Tian-Nuo Li,William Giarè,Guo-Hong Du,Eleonora Di Valentino,Luis A. Anchordoqui,Shao-Jiang Wang,Xin Zhang,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 在ΛCDM框架下中微子质量总和的宇宙学上限与振荡实验下限存在张力，允许负有效质量时分析倾向负值。本文检验暗物质与标量场通过指数型质量耦合的耦合精质（CQ）模型能否缓解该张力，并与其他相互作用暗能量模型比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ΛCDM中CMB与DESI BAO给出的中微子质量总上限与振荡实验下限相矛盾，且允许负有效质量的分析偏好负值，提示需引入超出ΛCDM的物理以调和观测与实验室约束。

Method: 构建耦合精质（coupled quintessence, CQ）模型：暗物质与正则标量场φ通过指数型质量耦合m_c(φ)∝e^{-βφ/M_Pl}相互作用；联合CMB、DESI BAO与超新星（supernova）数据进行参数约束，并与CPL及其他相互作用暗能量模型对比。

Result: CQ给出的中微子质量约束与CPL相当，且在正常序（normal ordering）下与振荡实验在2σ内一致，无需标量场状态方程（equation of state）穿越幽灵分界（phantom divide）；相较之下，CQ比那些偏好更负有效质量的替代相互作用暗能量模型更有效地缓解张力。

Conclusion: 暗部门（dark sector）相互作用能否放宽中微子质量上限，最终取决于其对原初CMB各向异性（primary CMB anisotropies）、几何（geometry）、增长（growth）与透镜效应（lensing）等效应之间的相互权衡。

Abstract: In $Λ$CDM, the CMB and DESI BAO upper limits on the total neutrino mass are in tension with the lower bounds from oscillation experiments, and analyses allowing negative effective masses favor negative values. We investigate whether a coupled quintessence (CQ) model in which dark matter interacts with a canonical scalar field $φ$ through an exponential mass coupling $m_{\rm c}(φ)\propto e^{-βφ/M_{\rm Pl}}$ can alleviate this tension. Combining CMB, DESI, and supernova data, we find that CQ yields neutrino-mass constraints comparable to those obtained in CPL and consistent with oscillation experiments within $2σ$ for normal ordering, without requiring the scalar-field equation of state to cross the phantom divide. We further show that it alleviates the tension more effectively than alternative interacting dark energy models that instead favor more negative effective masses. This comparison shows that whether dark sector interactions can relax neutrino mass bounds ultimately depends on the interplay of their effects on primary CMB anisotropies, geometry, growth, and lensing.

</details>


### [276] [A consistent formulation of stochastic inflation II: Reassessing its role in nonlinear cosmological perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15050)
*Diego Cruces,Tomotaka Kuroda*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文质疑传统随机暴胀（stochastic inflation）表述能否定量可靠地描述宇宙学扰动，并将随机方法推广至非线性阶以检验其精度。结果表明非马尔可夫效应与非白噪声项对定量准确性不可或缺，传统框架至多在线性阶得到定量支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者重新审视随机暴胀方法在研究宇宙学扰动（cosmological perturbations）中的作用与动机，将其与该方法在重求和红外长期发散（IR secular divergences）方面确有充分依据的动机相区分。核心疑问在于：传统随机暴胀表述是否能定量可靠地描述宇宙学扰动。

Method: 为提供定量检验基础，将随机暴胀推广至超越线性阶（beyond the linear regime）：引入由红外-紫外相互作用（infrared-ultraviolet interactions）产生的非马尔可夫效应（non-Markovian effects），并纳入紫外涨落的二次项（产生非白噪声，non-white noise）。随后针对 de Sitter 空间中的旁观者标量场（spectator scalar field）以及含度规扰动的慢滚暴胀子（slow-roll inflaton），推导三点关联函数（three-point correlation functions）。

Result: 推导所得三点关联函数与 in-in 量子场论（in-in quantum-field-theoretic）对超视界非线性演化（superhorizon nonlinear evolution）的预言精确一致。结果进一步显示，非马尔可夫效应与二次（高阶）噪声项对定量准确性均为必要。

Conclusion: 传统随机暴胀框架的定量合理性至多在线性阶（linear order）成立，其作为非线性扰动的定量描述工具受到实质性质疑。

Abstract: We question whether the conventional formulation of stochastic inflation can provide a quantitatively reliable description of cosmological perturbations. More fundamentally, we reassess the role and motivations of the stochastic approach to study cosmological perturbations, distinct from its well-founded motivation for resumming IR secular divergences. To provide a quantitative basis for this reassessment, we extend stochastic inflation beyond the linear regime by incorporating non-Markovian effects arising from infrared-ultraviolet interactions and terms quadratic in ultraviolet fluctuations, which generate non-white noise. We derive three-point correlation functions for a spectator scalar field in de Sitter space and a slow-roll inflaton including metric perturbations, demonstrating exact agreement with in-in quantum-field-theoretic predictions for superhorizon nonlinear evolution. Our results show that both non-Markovian effects and quadratic (higher order) noise terms are essential for quantitative accuracy, implying that the conventional stochastic-inflation framework is, at best, quantitatively justified only at linear order.

</details>


### [277] [Non-Gaussian matter fluctuations from primordial magnetic fields and galaxy luminosity function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15188)
*Teppei Minoda,Yi Mao,Shohei Saga,Hayato Shimabukuro*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 原初磁场（primordial magnetic fields, PMFs）经由洛伦兹力（Lorentz force）产生的密度扰动，即便在PMFs本身统计高斯（statistically Gaussian）的情形下，其一点统计也呈现本征非高斯（non-Gaussian）的概率分布函数（PDF），高密度尾部增强逾两个数量级。基于Press-Schechter形式，该非高斯性显著提升稀有天体（尤其是大质量晕与高红移晕）的丰度，并可在无需额外增强恒星形成效率的前提下解释JWST观测到的z≳12亮端星系过量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究主要以物质功率谱（matter power spectrum）这一两点统计（two-point statistic）刻画PMFs对宇宙结构形成的印记，而一点统计（one-point statistics）所携带的非高斯信息尚未被系统利用。论文旨在考察PMF诱导密度扰动PDF的非高斯性及其对稀有天体丰度与高红移星系观测的影响。

Method: 论文证明PMF诱导密度涨落的一点统计PDF具有本征非高斯性并定量给出高密度尾部的增强幅度；随后采用Press-Schechter形式（Press-Schechter formalism）计算稀有天体丰度，并进一步计算z=6、12、16处的星系紫外光度函数（ultraviolet luminosity function），与JWST近期观测结果进行对比。

Result: PDF高密度尾部相对高斯情形增强超过两个数量级；该非高斯性显著提升稀有天体丰度，尤其是大质量晕与高红移晕。在总密度场继承该PDF的极限情形下，与JWST观测比较显示PMF来源的非高斯密度涨落可解释z≳12处亮端（M_UV≲−18）的星系过量。

Conclusion: PMF诱导的非高斯密度涨落为高红移亮星系过量提供了一种无需额外天体物理增强恒星形成效率的解释路径，表明一点统计是刻画PMF宇宙学印记的重要补充手段。需注意该结论在总密度场完全继承该PDF的极限假设下成立。

Abstract: Primordial magnetic fields (PMFs) represent a promising candidate for the origin of large-scale magnetic fields observed in astrophysical environments. Through the Lorentz force, PMFs generate matter density perturbations, leaving distinct imprints on the formation of cosmic structures. While previous studies have primarily characterized these imprints via the matter power spectrum --- a two-point statistic --- we demonstrate that the one-point statistics of PMF-induced density fluctuations reveal an intrinsically non-Gaussian probability distribution function (PDF), even when the PMFs themselves are statistically Gaussian. In particular, we find that the high-density tail of the PDF is enhanced by over two orders of magnitude relative to the Gaussian case. Adopting the Press-Schechter formalism, we show that this non-Gaussianity significantly boosts the abundance of rare objects, especially massive halos and those at high redshifts. We further compute the ultraviolet luminosity function of galaxies at $z=6$, $12$, and $16$, and compare our predictions with recent James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) observations that have reported an excess of high-redshift galaxies at $z\sim 12$ -- $16$ relative to standard $Λ$CDM expectations. Intriguingly, in the limiting case where the total density field inherits this PDF, our results suggest that the non-Gaussian density fluctuations sourced by PMFs can explain the bright-end ($M_\mathrm{UV}\lesssim -18$) excess at $z\gtrsim 12$, without requiring additional astrophysical enhancements to star-formation efficiency.

</details>


### [278] [Constraining Dark Matter Gravitational Production with the 21cm Signal at the Cosmic Dawn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15454)
*André A. Costa,Valdir B. Bezerra*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文探讨利用宇宙黎明时期21cm亮温度限制暗物质的引力产生机制，并据此对暗物质质量给出上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质的本性与起源仍是现代宇宙学（cosmology）的重大未解问题；直接探测、间接探测与加速器产生实验均未发现暗物质与标准模型（standard model）粒子的确证相互作用，提示暗物质可能仅通过引力相互作用产生。

Method: 基于宇宙黎明（cosmic dawn）的21cm亮温度（21cm brightness temperature），仅考虑吸收轮廓（absorption profile）出现的时间段，对暗物质的引力产生（gravitational production）施加约束。

Result: 在仅考虑吸收轮廓出现时段的条件下，可对暗物质质量（dark matter's mass）给出上限（upper limits）。

Conclusion: 未来观测有望扩展当前约束，并有助于理解暗物质的本性。

Abstract: The origin of dark matter is one of the major problems in modern cosmology. We can infer its existence from the gravitational potential wells around astrophysical objects, but there is no convincing explanation for what its nature and origin are. Moreover, experiments for direct detection, indirect detection, or particle production in accelerators have not shown, until now, any conclusive interaction between dark matter and particles in the standard model. This may imply that dark matter does not interact with ordinary matter and originates from pure gravitational interaction. In this manuscript, we investigate the possibility to constrain the gravitational production of dark matter from the 21cm brightness temperature at the cosmic dawn. Considering only the time when the absorption profile occurs, we can impose upper limits on the dark matter's mass. Future measurements have potential to extend current constraints and help understanding the nature of dark matter.

</details>


### [279] [Enhanced Early Structure from Axiodilatons & 21cm Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15647)
*Chan Youn Weon,Carsten van de Bruck,C. P. Burgess,Jeong Han Kim,Adam Smith*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: Axiodilaton 模型在解释暗物质与暗能量的同时，会自然地增强早期结构形成，从而暗示 21cm 信号可作为区别于 ΛCDM 的可观测判据；作者预测 SKA1-Low 可在 2σ 水平上探测到小至约 10⁻² 量级的 dilaton 耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Axiodilaton 模型凭借偶然近似对称性（accidental approximate symmetries）以技术自然的方式统一描述暗扇区（Dark Sector），并"顺带"缓解哈勃张力（Hubble tension）与演化暗能量（evolving Dark Energy）等 ΛCDM 现象学难题；此外该模型倾向增强早期结构形成，与观测到的结构形成早于预期的证据相呼应，值得深入检验。

Method: 作者在与其他宇宙学观测吻合的相同参数选择下，详细计算 axiodilaton 模型预期的 21cm 信号，并完整推导相关宇宙学观测量对质量的依赖关系，包括 dilaton 依赖的重子与电子质量如何影响超精细跃迁（hyperfine transition）、莱曼α耦合（Lyα coupling）、X 射线加热与电离，以及宇宙黎明（cosmic dawn）和再电离时期（epoch of reionization）；同时给出辐射转移（radiative transfer）、原子物理及星系际介质热与电离态方程。

Result: 在固定天体物理参数的前提下，预测 SKA1-Low 能在 2σ 显著性下将小至数倍 10⁻² 的 dilaton 耦合与 ΛCDM 区分开，其主要来源是结构的更早形成。

Conclusion: 早期结构增长由于最为清晰可测，成为检验 axiodilaton 模型的优良探针；21cm 观测有望为区分该模型与 ΛCDM 提供可行途径。摘要未进一步给出其他结论。

Abstract: Axiodilatons provide a promising unified model of the Dark Sector whose properties are motivated by the use of accidental approximate symmetries to embed the light fields needed for observations into well-motivated UV physics in a technically natural way. These models turn out to help ameliorate many current puzzles in $Λ$CDM phenomenology (e.g. the Hubble tension and the evidence for evolving Dark Energy) `for free' despite not being designed for that purpose. We here explore in more detail another serendipitous feature of these models: their tendency to enhance early structure formation, in a way that seems suggestive given the accumulating evidence for structure arising earlier than expected in $Λ$CDM. Since early structure growth is the cleanest to study we explore in detail the expected 21cm signal produced by axiodilaton models, using the same parameter choices that improve agreement with observations elsewhere in cosmology. For fixed astrophysics, we forecast that SKA1-Low could distinguish dilaton couplings as small as a few times $10^{-2}$ from $Λ$CDM at $2σ$, mainly because of the earlier formation of structure. We provide complete derivations of the mass dependence of the relevant cosmological observables, including how dilaton-dependent baryon and electron masses affect the hyperfine transition, the Ly$α$ coupling, X-ray heating and ionization during cosmic dawn and the epoch of reionization. We provide details of radiative transfer, atomic physics and the equations governing the thermal and ionization state of the intergalactic medium.

</details>


### [280] [Improved constraints on axion inflation with the new code AxioGen: an efficient generator of curvature perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15841)
*Matthew Reinhard,Zachary Slepian,Atsushi Taruya*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该工作针对轴子暴胀（axion inflation）模型中谱功率与双谱的标定函数 $f_2$、$f_3$ 给出更精确的拟合函数，并据此更新对模型参数 $\xi$ 的约束与耦合能标下界，同时发布弱反作用区间曲率扰动实现的公开代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对 $f_2$ 与 $f_3$ 的计算依赖规范场模式函数的近似解，该近似仅在模型参数 $\xi$ 较大时较准确（$\mathcal{O}(10\%)$），在 $\xi$ 较小时误差显著，因此需要更精确的标定。

Method: 基于近期工作指出的近似解缺陷，构建新的 $f_2$、$f_3$ 拟合函数，并将其用于对 $\xi$ 施加新约束、对 $\phi^{1/3}$ 与 $\phi^{2/3}$ 型暴胀势下轴子—规范场耦合能标给出下界；此外开发并公开用于生成弱反作用（weak backreaction）区间原初曲率扰动实现的代码。

Result: 新拟合函数在精度上显著优于先前所用函数，并据此得到对 $\xi$ 的新约束及两种暴胀势下耦合能标的下界；公开代码可高效生成曲率扰动实现，支持高阶关联函数（higher order correlators）的数值计算。

Conclusion: 该研究提升了轴子暴胀中非高斯性（non-Gaussianity）描述的精度，收紧了对耦合参数与能标的限制，并为大尺度结构形成（large-scale structure formation）的初始条件提供了一条新途径。

Abstract: Axion inflation, in which a pseudoscalar inflaton couples to a $U(1)$ gauge field via a Chern-Simons interaction, has garnered significant interest due to its rich phenomenology. The power spectrum and bispectrum for this model are often parametrized by functions known as $f_2$ and $f_3$, respectively. Previous work computed $f_2$ and $f_3$ using an approximation for the gauge field mode functions. While these approximate solutions are fairly accurate ($\mathcal{O}(10\%)$) for large values of the model parameter $ξ$, which controls gauge field particle production, our recent work has shown them to be quite inaccurate for smaller values of $ξ$. We present new fitting functions for $f_2$ and $f_3$ that are significantly more accurate than those previously used. We use these functions to place new constraints on $ξ$ and also set lower bounds for the energy scale of the axion gauge field coupling for $φ^{1/3}$ and $φ^{2/3}$ inflationary potentials. Additionally, we present a new public code to efficiently generate realizations of the primordial curvature perturbation for axion inflation in the weak backreaction regime. This code enables numerical computation of higher order correlators and provides a novel path to initial conditions for large-scale structure formation.

</details>


### [281] [Shear, vorticity, and $f(R)$ theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16026)
*Antonino Del Popolo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 论文用含剪切、涡度与变色龙屏蔽的扩展球坍缩模型（ESCM）计算 Hu-Sawicki f(R) 模型中的坍缩阈值、 turnaround 密度与半径及晕质量函数，指出周转半径因观测误差过大无法用于检验，而晕丰度（尤其高红移）具备区分 f(R) 与广义相对论的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准球坍缩模型（SSCM）忽略剪切、涡度与屏蔽效应，可能使 f(R) 修改引力的可观测预言失真；研究旨在检验更完备的 ESCM 下各坍缩量与晕质量函数能否区分 Hu-Sawicki f(R) 与广义相对论（General Relativity, GR）。

Method: 在 Hu-Sawicki f(R) 模型中构建包含剪切（shear）、涡度（vorticity）与变色龙屏蔽（chameleon screening）的扩展球坍缩模型，得到依赖质量、红移与 |f_R0| 的 δ_c，并结合游程集（excursion set）方法计算晕质量函数（halo mass function）。

Result: ESCM 下 GR 与大场极限（LF）的 δ_c 相差数个百分点，而 GR（ESCM）与 LF（SSCM）相差约 20%；δ_t 相差约 27-29%，R_t 约 6.5%。考虑屏蔽后，F4 的 δ_c 差异为 2.5%、F6 为零；δ_t 差异分别为 >20%（F4）、约 10%（F5）、0（F6）；R_t 约 6.5%（F4）、3%（F5）、0（F6）。

Conclusion: F4 已被观测排除、F6 与 GR 几乎不可区分，仅 F5 有判别潜力；但 turnaround 半径约 20% 的观测不确定度使 R_t 及相关量无法作为 f(R) 检验手段，相比之下 f(R) 预言的晕丰度（尤其高红移）可区分 f(R) 与 GR。

Abstract: We study how an extended spherical collapse model (ESCM), including shear, vorticity and chameleon screening, modifies the collapse threshold $δ_c(z)$ and $δ_c(M)$, the turnaround density $δ_t$, the turnaround radius $R_t$ and the halo mass function in the Hu-Sawicki $f(R)$ model, and whether these quantities can distinguish $f(R)$ from General Relativity (GR). Unlike the standard spherical collapse model (SSCM), in the ESCM $δ_c$ depends on mass, redshift and $|f_{R0}|$; we use it to compute the halo mass function with the excursion set approach. Between GR and the large-field (LF) limit of $f(R)$, $δ_c$ differs by a few percent within the ESCM and by about 20% between GR in the ESCM and the LF limit in the SSCM; $δ_t$ differs by about 27-29% and $R_t$ by about 6.5%. With screening, the difference between GR and screened $δ_c$ is 2.5% for $|f_{R0}|=10^{-4}$ (F4) and vanishes for $|f_{R0}|=10^{-6}$ (F6); for $δ_t$ it is larger than 20% (F4), about 10% (F5) and 0 (F6); for $R_t$ about 6.5% (F4), 3% (F5) and 0 (F6). Since observations exclude F4 and F6 is practically identical to GR, only F5 could discriminate, but the ~20% uncertainty on measured turnaround radii rules out $R_t$ and related quantities as tests of $f(R)$. The halo abundance predicted by $f(R)$, especially at high redshift, can instead distinguish $f(R)$ from GR.

</details>


### [282] [Minimal vacuum histories beyond $Λ$CDM: model comparison within VCDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16407)
*Antonio De Felice,Eleonora Di Valentino,Luis Escamilla Torres,Jack Zidich*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文在 VCDM 框架下比较三种仅增加一个宇宙学参数（cosmological parameter）的演化暗能量（evolving dark energy）模型（F42、Ag、Qui），联合 CMB（SPT、Planck、ACT）与 DESI DR2 距离测量（含/不含 DESY5 Dovekie 超新星）进行拟合。三者均优于 ΛCDM 的最佳拟合似然，但信息准则与贝叶斯证据（Bayesian evidence）尚不能给出稳健偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期宇宙学观测（cosmological observations）促使研究者寻找超越 ΛCDM、具有演化暗能量的替代模型；需在同等参数复杂度下比较物理图像不同的暗能量历史，检验拟合改进是否具有判别力。

Method: 在 VCDM 中实现三个模型：F42（状态方程 equation of state 为 w(a)=w₀/√a）以及两个光滑、可变号的有效暗能量轮廓（effective dark-energy profile）Ag 与 Qui；VCDM 可在不引入额外传播引力自由度（propagating gravitational degree of freedom）的情况下确定背景与线性扰动（linear perturbations）。数据组合为 SPT、Planck、ACT 的 CMB 观测加 DESI DR2 距离测量，并分别在有/无 DESY5 Dovekie 超新星样本下分析。

Result: 三种替代模型的最佳拟合似然（best-fit likelihood）均优于 ΛCDM。纳入超新星数据时，赤池信息准则（Akaike information criterion, AIC）偏向 F42，而 Ag 与 Qui 在该准则下与 ΛCDM 几乎不可区分；贝叶斯证据（Bayesian evidence）未能在三者之间建立稳健偏好。

Conclusion: 当前观测的拟合改进可来自物理上不同的暗能量演化历史，因而模型区分能力有限；需借助对膨胀（expansion）与结构增长（structure growth）的互补测量（complementary measurements）加以辨别。

Abstract: Recent cosmological observations motivate alternatives to $Λ$CDM with evolving dark energy. We compare three such models, each introducing one additional cosmological parameter: F42, with equation of state $w(a)=w_0/\sqrt{a}$, and two smooth sign-changing effective dark-energy profiles, Ag and Qui. All models are implemented within VCDM, which determines the background and linear perturbations without introducing an additional propagating gravitational degree of freedom. We use CMB observations from SPT, Planck, and ACT, combined with DESI DR2 distance measurements, with and without the DESY5 Dovekie supernova sample. All three alternatives improve the best-fit likelihood relative to $Λ$CDM. When supernovae are included, the Akaike information criterion favors F42, while Ag and Qui remain nearly indistinguishable from $Λ$CDM on this criterion. The Bayesian evidence, however, does not establish a robust preference among the three alternatives. Our results show that improved fits to current observations can arise from physically different dark-energy histories, motivating complementary measurements of expansion and structure growth to distinguish them.

</details>


### [283] [Kinessence, phantom crossing and sign-switching dark energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16629)
*Philippe Brax,Tom Canaguier,Andrei Lazanu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文提出Kinessence模型，将K-mouflage（K-屏蔽）与quintessence（精质）结合，在Yukawa耦合下实现暗能量状态方程的phantom-crossing（幽灵穿越）与暗能量密度符号翻转，并系统分析背景与微扰层级的宇宙学观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在借助结合K-essence动力学项与精质势的暗标量部门，构造可产生phantom-crossing及暗能量密度符号切换的模型，并探讨其是否能缓解H0、S8与中微子质量求和等宇宙学张力。

Method: 在Jordan frame（约当框架）下于背景与微扰层级研究模型动力学，重点计算有效状态方程与物质分数，并分析CMB谱、线性功率谱及由fσ8描述的结构增长；标量场介导的第五力由K-mouflage机制屏蔽。

Result: 精质势在低红移驱动加速且状态方程大于-1，Yukawa耦合强度决定phantom-crossing行为；K-mouflage屏蔽使β~0.1的较大耦合仍通过太阳系检验，并在z~0.5穿越phantom divide。结构增长相对LCDM被抑制，物质分数偏离标准模型数个百分点，低红移Hubble率更高，CMB谱仅有小幅偏离，暗能量分数在z>2时变为负值。

Conclusion: 上述特征有望缓解H0偏大、S8增长偏低及中微子质量求和上限等张力；CMB lensing（引力透镜）谱相对LCDM的偏离可能为这类模型提供可探测的观测窗口。

Abstract: We introduce Kinessence models where combining K-mouflage and quintessence leads to phantom-crossing of the dark energy equation of state and sign-switching of the dark energy density. This is realised by joining a dark scalar sector comprising K-essence kinetic terms and a quintessence potential together with a Yukawa coupling to matter. We investigate the dynamics of these models in the Jordan frame at the background and perturbative levels. We concentrate on the effective equation of state and the matter fraction for the background. The CMB spectra, the linear power spectrum and the growth of structure via $fσ_8$ are also analysed at the perturbative level. The quintessence potential drives the acceleration of the Universe at low redshift with an equation of state greater than -1. The Yukawa coupling to matter leads to a phantom crossing behaviour depending on its strength. The potential fifth force mediated by the scalar field is screened by the K-mouflage mechanism triggered by the K-essence kinetic terms and passes Solar System tests. This allows for relatively large Yukawa couplings to matter $β\sim 0.1$, large enough to lead to the crossing of the phantom divide at $z\sim 0.5$. Despite a significant scalar interaction on large scales, we find that the growth of structure is reduced compared to LCDM. The matter fraction deviates from the standard model by a few percent and the Hubble rate is larger at low redshift than at recombination whilst the CMB spectra show small deviations from LCDM. The dark energy fraction becomes negative at redshifts greater than 2. All these features should alleviate the various tensions such as the larger value of $H_0$ at small redshift, a possible reduction of growth as measured by $S_8$ and the bound on the sum of the neutrino masses. Deviations from LCDM of the CMB lensing spectrum may provide a detectable window for these models.

</details>


### [284] [Efficient Standard Siren Cosmological Inference with Posterior Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16697)
*Alexander Papadopoulos,Michael J. Williams,Christian E. A. Chapman-Bird,Rachel Gray,Christopher Messenger*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文将 ASPIRE 采样器应用于暗标准汽笛（dark siren）的宇宙学与族群参数联合估计，利用已有后验样本快速更新至新假设下的后验，在 GWTC-5.0 案例中结果与嵌套采样（nested sampling）一致，并在复用旧后验时获得 6-28 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准汽笛宇宙学（standard siren cosmology）依赖源族群、星系目录与探测选择函数（selection function）等建模假设，量化每种选择的影响通常需完整独立分析；且引力波目录（gravitational-wave catalogs）逐步增长，大量算力被用于重复分析高度重叠的数据集。

Method: 采用新近提出的 ASPIRE（Accelerated Sequential Posterior Inference via Reuse）采样器，将已有后验样本更新为新假设下的后验而无需从先验（prior）重新启动；该方法结合归一化流（normalising flows）与序贯蒙特卡洛（sequential Monte Carlo, SMC）方案，输出精确后验与无偏证据估计（evidence estimates）。

Result: 在基于 GWTC-5.0 的多个暗汽笛分析案例中，所得后验与嵌套采样结果一致；在使用已有后验的一组分析中，相对嵌套采样取得约 6-28 倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: ASPIRE 支持对建模选择进行高效探索，并可在目录增长时快速更新结果，避免重复已有的计算投入。

Abstract: Standard siren cosmology depends on several modelling assumptions, including the underlying source population, galaxy catalogue, and detection selection function. Quantifying the effect of each choice typically requires a full, independent analysis. As gravitational-wave catalogs grow incrementally, much computational effort is spent re-analysing largely overlapping datasets. We apply the recently developed ASPIRE (Accelerated Sequential Posterior Inference via Reuse) sampler to the joint estimation of cosmological and population parameters with dark sirens. ASPIRE updates existing posterior samples into the posterior under a new hypothesis without restarting from the prior. It combines normalising flows with a sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) scheme to produce accurate posteriors and unbiased evidence estimates. Applying ASPIRE to a number of dark siren analysis cases with GWTC-5.0, we recover posteriors consistent with nested sampling, while recording a performance improvement of between 6-28 times over the set of analyses when using an existing posterior. This enables efficient exploration of modelling choices and rapid updates as catalogs grow, without duplicating existing computational effort.

</details>


### [285] [Localization of gravitational-wave source behind galaxy clusters by gravitational-wave lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16916)
*Brian H. Y. Cheng,Laura Uronen,Souvik Jana,Samuel Lange,Thomas E. Collett,Otto A. Hannuksela*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文提出利用星系团强引力波透镜效应精确定位引力波源，基于哈勃前沿场已知星系团透镜样本模拟地面探测器观测，发现同模型族下约90%事件可正确识别透镜，源位置可达亚角秒精度并可估计源红移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波与电磁对应体联合观测需要精确的源定位以证认宿主星系并开展后续研究（如确定哈勃常数）。已有工作仅考虑星系透镜，星系团透镜因质量结构复杂而尚未被研究。

Method: 采用哈勃前沿场（Hubble Frontier Fields）中已知星系团透镜列表，基于其质量模型重建模拟引力波透镜效应，并模拟地面引力波探测器（ground-based gravitational-wave detectors）的观测，进而开展透镜识别与源定位测试，包括对不同透镜模型进行边缘化处理。

Result: 当模拟与定位使用同一模型族时，约90%的模拟事件可正确识别透镜；源位置可定位至亚角秒（sub-arcsecond）精度并估计源红移。对全部可能透镜模型边缘化后，只要正确透镜模型包含在可用模型中，透镜识别与定位仍然有效；若正确模型被排除，多数定位尝试在初始化阶段失败。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了理想化条件下星系团强引力波透镜定位的概念可行性（proof of concept），但需进一步研究星系团透镜建模差异与重建偏差以充分验证该方法。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave observations have opened new opportunities to study massive object events like merging binary black holes. When combined with electromagnetic counterparts, accurate localization of gravitational-wave sources could allow us to identify their host galaxies and carry out follow-up studies, such as study the environment of merging black holes and determine the Hubble constant. One of the possible methods for precise localization is strong gravitational-wave lensing. Previous studies have considered strong gravitational-wave lensing with galaxy lenses. However, galaxy clusters have yet to be investigated due to the complexity of their mass structures. Here we take the list of known cluster lenses from the Hubble Frontier Fields, simulate lensing properties of gravitational waves from their reconstructions as they would be observed by the ground-based gravitational-wave detectors. When same model family is used in simulation and localization, we find that the correct lens is identified for around 90\% of the simulated events. We also show that the source positions within the cluster can be pinpointed at sub-arcsecond precision with an estimate on source redshift. When we marginalize over all possible lens models for the clusters, the lens identification and localizations remain valid as long as the correct lens model is contained in the available cluster lens models. However, most localization attempts fail at initialization if the correct cluster model is excluded. These tests establish a proof of concept of localization under idealized conditions, while more work is needed to understand the cluster lens modeling discrepancies and reconstruction bias to validate the approach further.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [Interpretable machine learning diagnosis of degradation mechanisms in lead-free $RbSn_{0.5}Ge_{0.5}I_{3}$ solar cells from temperature- and illumination-dependent electrical measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16750)
*Nabanita Sarker,Hafiz Imtiaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作以约42,000次SCAPS-1D仿真，同时研究无铅RbSn0.5Ge0.5I3太阳能电池的正向性能预测与逆向退化机制识别；指出因子网格训练的诊断模型会严重高估精度，改用拉丁超立方设计可恢复泛化能力，多标签分类器识别六种机制的宏F1达0.938。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习与器件仿真耦合已广泛用于薄膜太阳能电池优化，但几乎只用于正向方向（从器件参数到预测性能）；器件制备后识别限制性能的机制这一逆向问题（inverse problem）仍依赖对表征数据的人工检查。

Method: 基于约42,000次SCAPS-1D仿真构建数据集，研究RbSn0.5Ge0.5I3电池在CuI与Spiro-OMeTAD空穴传输层（hole transport layer）下的效率差异；对比因子参数网格（factorial parameter grid）与拉丁超立方设计（Latin hypercube design）训练的代理模型（surrogate model）泛化表现；并用多标签分类器（multi-label classifier）从模拟的温度与光照依赖开路电压测量中识别六种退化机制。

Result: 用CuI替代Spiro-OMeTAD使模拟功率转换效率（power conversion efficiency）从19.6%提升至21.6%，几乎完全来自填充因子（fill factor）的改善。XGBoost回归器在留出网格点上R²=0.9997，但在独立采样器件上仅0.58；在同等规模的拉丁超立方设计上训练可恢复R²=0.995。逆向任务中多标签分类器宏F1（macro F1）为0.938，优于校准物理规则基线（calibrated physics-rule baseline）的0.687。

Conclusion: 基于因子网格训练的代理模型会严重高估预测精度，采样设计对泛化能力至关重要；数据驱动方法在退化机制识别上显著优于人工物理规则，但分类器无法泛化到训练集中未出现的机制组合。

Abstract: Machine learning coupled with device simulation is widely used to optimize thin-film solar cells, but almost exclusively in the forward direction, from device parameters to predicted performance. The inverse problem encountered after fabrication, namely identifying the mechanism that limits a given device, is still addressed by manual inspection of characterization data. In this work, both directions are examined for lead-free $RbSn_{0.5}Ge_{0.5}I_{3}$ solar cells using approximately 42,000 SCAPS-1D simulations. Replacing the Spiro-OMeTAD hole transport layer with CuI increases the simulated power conversion efficiency from 19.6\% to 21.6\%, almost entirely through the fill factor. Surrogate models trained and tested on a factorial parameter grid substantially overstate their predictive accuracy. An XGBoost regressor attains $R^2 = 0.9997$ on held-out grid points but only 0.58 on independently sampled devices, whereas training on a Latin hypercube design of comparable size restores $R^2 = 0.995$. For the inverse problem, a multi-label classifier identifies which of six degradation mechanisms are present from simulated temperature- and illumination-dependent open-circuit-voltage measurements. It achieves a macro $F_1$ score of 0.938, compared to 0.687 for a calibrated physics-rule baseline. The classifier does not, however, generalize to combinations of mechanisms absent from the training set.

</details>


### [287] [ISLA: A Coordinate-Free, Measure-Weighted Transformer for Boundary-Driven PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17141)
*Peter Sharpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ISLA（Invariant Slice Attention），一种面向稳态、边界驱动 PDE 的切片注意力 Transformer（slice-attention transformer），通过去除学习算子对坐标的依赖，实现刚体运动与反射等变、单位不变及网格细化一致，并在 AeroZoo 与 AirfRANS 基准上显著降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理基本要求是坐标系无关性：PDE 系统行为不依赖原点、朝向、单位或网格，代理模型（surrogate model）也应如此。但当前空气动力学基准上误差最低的神经代理模型缺乏这些不变性，在平移旋转或重采样边界等简单变换下严重失效（表面压力误差从 4% 升至 100% 以上，边界重采样后约 30%）。

Method: ISLA 采用切片注意力机制：任何可学习操作均不接收坐标输入，边界上的求和均以数值积分（quadrature）实现；位置仅以到学习得到的切片锚点（slice anchors）的距离与相对角度进入模型，长度以由边界计算的单位度量；内部点通过树加速的学习核（tree-accelerated learned kernel）查询边界，且各查询相互独立。每个边界单元作为一个 token，并配合精确的边界读取（exact boundary read）。

Result: 在 AeroZoo 的 2,816 个流场（形状、尺寸、速度与边界条件各异）上，其参考尺度归一化的速度与压力误差分别至少比任意基线低 3.1 倍和 3.3 倍；在 AirfRANS 上误差仅次于边界积分模型（boundary-integral model）GLOBE，且至少比 Transolver 与 GeoTransolver 低 1.6 倍。

Conclusion: 将对称性（等变/不变性）作为架构设计约束嵌入切片注意力，可在保持查询独立性的同时获得对刚体运动、反射、单位与网格细化的稳健性，并在多个气动基准上取得领先精度。

Abstract: A foundational principle of physics is that all coordinate systems are equally valid: the behavior of a PDE-governed system does not depend on the origin or orientation of the frame, nor on the units or mesh used to write it down, and neither should a surrogate model. Yet the neural surrogates with the lowest errors on today's aerodynamics benchmarks lack these invariances and fail under simple transformations of the same problem: for models trained on PDEs of cars in one pose (i.e., translation and rotation) and from one mesher, rotating the full PDE raises the surface-pressure error from 4% to more than 100%, and re-sampling its boundary raises it to about 30%. We introduce ISLA (Invariant Slice Attention), a slice-attention transformer for steady, boundary-driven PDEs in which no learned operation receives a coordinate and every sum over the boundary is a quadrature. Positions enter only as distances and relative angles to learned slice anchors, lengths are measured in a unit computed from the boundary, and interior points query the boundary through a tree-accelerated learned kernel without influencing one another. With every boundary cell a token and an exact boundary read, ISLA is equivariant to rigid motions and reflections, invariant to the units of its declared data, and consistent under mesh refinement, while at every setting each query is independent of all other queries. On AeroZoo, 2,816 flows that vary in shape, size, speed, and boundary conditions, its mean reference-scaled velocity and pressure errors are at least 3.1 and 3.3 times lower than any baseline's. On AirfRANS its errors are second only to those of a boundary-integral model (GLOBE) and at least 1.6 times lower than those of Transolver and GeoTransolver.

</details>


### [288] [Fast Accurate Quantum Monte Carlo without Metropolis Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10187)
*Reuben Cohn-Gordon,Gabriel Pescia,Sumner N. Hearth,Jakob Robnik,Uroš Seljak,Peter Lunts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将计算统计学中“未调整”（unadjusted）的哈密顿蒙特卡洛（Hamiltonian Monte Carlo, HMC）方法迁移至量子蒙特卡洛（Quantum Monte Carlo, QMC）场景，在一个高维自旋—费米子（spin-fermion）量子相变模型上验证其效率优于经 Metropolis-Hastings（MH）调整的标准 HMC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统的物理量可表示为概率分布的期望值，从而可用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov Chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）估计；标准做法是用 MH 准则“调整”HMC 核以保证无偏收敛，但近期计算统计学研究表明未调整 HMC 核可能更高效且偏差相对有限样本方差误差可忽略。作者意在检验该结论能否迁移到 QMC 场景。

Method: 采用哈密顿动力学设计马尔可夫核的 HMC 方法，去除 MH 调整步骤，得到未调整 HMC 核，并将其应用于一个具有挑战性的高维自旋—费米子量子相变模型的 QMC 计算，与标准调整型 HMC 进行对照。

Result: 以每次梯度调用（gradient call）的有效样本量（effective sample size）为指标，未调整方法优于调整型 HMC，同时结果保持准确。

Conclusion: 未调整 HMC 可作为 QMC 中更高效的采样方案，在可接受的渐近偏差下提升采样效率；该结论在高维自旋—费米子量子相变这一困难模型上得到支持。

Abstract: In quantum systems, physical quantities of interest can be expressed as expectation values of a probability distribution, which allows their estimation by Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods. This approach, an instance of Quantum Monte Carlo (QMC), plays a central role in obtaining predictions of system properties relevant to condensed matter and high energy physics. The use of Hamiltonian dynamics to design a Markov kernel, known as Hybrid or Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC), is widespread in quantum applications. In this context, it is standard to use an HMC kernel that is ``adjusted'' with the Metropolis-Hastings (MH) criterion, so that estimates of expectations converge to their true value in the limit of infinite sample size. However, recent work in computational statistics suggests that an \emph{unadjusted} HMC kernel can be significantly more efficient, while safely maintaining an asymptotic bias which is small relative to the error arising from the variance of the finite sample size. We adapt this approach to the QMC setting, focusing on a challenging and high-dimensional \emph{spin-fermion} model of a quantum phase transition. We find that unadjusted methods outperform adjusted HMC in terms of effective sample size per gradient call, while retaining accurate results.

</details>


### [289] [Physically Interpretable Basin Selection in Discrete-Variable Origami Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17196)
*Lucy Liu,Leon M. Kamp,Katia Bertoldi,L. Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过折纸（origami）设计基准问题，揭示连续松弛（continuous relaxation）优化后离散化会落入不利盆地（basins），并提出简单初始化偏置与损失惩罚两种改进显著提升离散解成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动微分（automatic differentiation）推动了连续变量机械设计，但基于梯度的优化难以处理离散变量；常规做法是先连续松弛优化、最后取最近离散设计，而该过程为何落入不利盆地缺乏理解，且离散化间隙（discretization gap）成因不明。

Method: 构建一个受折纸启发的可解释设计问题：每张折纸片几何固定，采用双层优化（bilevel optimization）框架在四类离散铰链（hinge）类型中选择每个折痕的配置，并用自动微分设计具有目标功能行为的形状。在此基础上，比较基线梯度设计、偏向刚性平坦铰链选项的均匀初始化先验（initialization prior），以及在外层损失中直接惩罚挫败度（frustration）与松垮度（floppiness）的效果。

Result: 基线方法存在较大离散化间隙：松弛优化在97%的运行中求解成功，但最终离散解仅约一半成功（49%）。均匀偏向刚性平坦铰链的初始化先验将最终求解比例从49%提升至69%，且不增加计算量、不改变优化过程；相比基线，所得折纸片具有更好的几何兼容性（geometric compatibility）与机械刚度（mechanical stiffness）。直接惩罚挫败度与松垮度效果更强，求解比例提升至78%，表明这些性质有助于区分不利盆地。

Conclusion: 机械设计的损失景观（loss landscape）中存在物理意义明确但无法稳健承受离散化的盆地，从而导致求解失败；针对这些盆地的物理特征进行干预（即使仅是初始化偏置）即可有效规避不利盆地。

Abstract: Automatic differentiation has enabled rapid progress on continuous-variable mechanical design problems, but gradient-based optimization still struggles with discrete variables. The standard approach is to optimize using a continuous relaxation of the choices, then commit to the nearest discrete design at the end. Why this procedure selects unfavorable basins is poorly understood. Here, we introduce a practical, interpretable design problem inspired by origami, in which automatic differentiation is used to design shapes with a target functional behavior. Each origami sheet has a fixed geometry, and a bilevel optimization framework tunes which one of four discrete hinge types to place at each fold. Baseline gradient-based design settles into basins with a large discretization gap: relaxed optimization solves 97% of runs, but the final, discrete solutions succeed only half the time. We find that the gap can be relieved with a surprisingly simple modification. An initialization prior uniformly biased toward the stiff, flat hinge option boosts the final solve fraction from 49% to 69%. Applying the prior adds no additional compute and leaves the optimization process unchanged. Compared to the baseline, sheets produced with the prior have greater geometric compatibility and mechanical stiffness. Directly penalizing frustration and floppiness in the outer loss function produces an even greater improvement than the prior does, raising the solve fraction to 78% and indicating that these properties help distinguish the unfavorable basins. Our results demonstrate that loss landscapes for mechanical design can have physically meaningful basins that are not robust to discretization and cause solves to fail. Targeting these basins' physical signatures, even with just an initialization bias, helps avoid them.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [290] [Distilling an atomistic foundation model is all you need for large-scale molecular dynamics of semiconductor interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14948)
*Feiyu Zhou,Wei Qiu,Fei Shuang,Yilun Liu,Shengying Yue,Penghua Ying*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文检验能否将原子尺度基础模型（atomistic foundation models）的知识直接蒸馏为紧凑势函数（compact potentials），并在异质半导体界面（heterogeneous semiconductor interfaces）的热学与力学响应上保持精度；结果表明直接蒸馏可保留关键界面响应，同时将模拟速度与可达体系规模提升数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进原子尺度基础模型虽具广泛可迁移的能量与力预测能力，但直接用于大规模分子动力学（molecular dynamics）计算代价过高，因此需要将其知识压缩至紧凑势函数中。

Method: 从22个模型家族的128个预训练模型中选定固定的EquiformerV3教师模型，在不进行目标特定教师微调的前提下，训练原子团簇展开（atomic cluster expansion, ACE）与神经演化势（neuroevolution potential, NEP）学生模型，并以匹配的训练配置与架构同基于密度泛函理论（density-functional-theory, DFT）标签训练的学生模型直接对比；另构建四模型委员会（committee）用于误差配置筛选。

Result: 在79,920原子的BAs/GaN界面上，直接蒸馏保留了终止依赖的热导（thermal conductance）与主要谱特征，与DFT训练对应模型的电导差异不超过6.6%；在500 K加载的29,792原子Cu/diamond试样中，抗拉强度与加载功分别相差2.8%与1.5%；探索性SiC/a-C/SiC模拟保留了主要热瓶颈与退火响应方向。四模型委员会在10%筛选预算下检索到42-56%的高误差教师构型并改善高误差力拟合，但未稳定改善下游性质。

Conclusion: 直接蒸馏能够在紧凑原子间势中保留异质界面的关键热学与力学响应，为异质界面的大规模、高通量模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: State-of-the-art atomistic foundation models provide broadly transferable energy and force predictions, but their direct deployment in large-scale molecular dynamics remains computationally costly. Here we examine whether their knowledge can be distilled into compact potentials while retaining the physical responses of heterogeneous semiconductor interfaces. From 128 pretrained models spanning 22 model families, we select a fixed EquiformerV3 teacher and train atomic cluster expansion (ACE) and neuroevolution potential (NEP) students without target-specific teacher fine-tuning, with both simulation speed and accessible system size improved by several orders of magnitude relative to the teacher model. Matched training configurations and architectures enable direct comparisons with students trained on density-functional-theory (DFT) reference labels. For 79,920-atom BAs/GaN interfaces, direct distillation preserves termination-dependent thermal conductance and dominant spectral features, with conductance differing by at most 6.6% from DFT-trained counterparts. In 29,792-atom Cu/diamond specimens loaded at 500 K, tensile strength and loading work differ by 2.8% and 1.5%, respectively. Exploratory SiC/a-C/SiC simulations retain the principal thermal bottleneck and the direction of the annealing response. Furthermore, a four-model committee retrieves 42-56% of the highest-error teacher configurations with a 10% selection budget and improves high-error force fitting, but does not consistently improve downstream properties. These findings demonstrate that direct distillation can preserve key interfacial thermal and mechanical responses in compact interatomic potentials, opening a path towards large-scale, high-throughput simulations of heterogeneous interfaces.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [291] [Dynamical mean-field theory of latching Potts networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16999)
*Anindita Basu,Carlo Orientale Caputo,Fernando Lucas Metz,Alessandro Treves*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文研究随机联想网络（random associative networks）中记忆间自发转换所需的最小表征结构。通过对带慢适应阈值（slow adaptive thresholds）的 Potts 联想网络进行动态平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory）分析，发现多状态（S>1）表征对结构化记忆转换是必要的，而二元网络（binary units, S=1）强烈抑制此类转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部生成认知（如走神、自由联想）中观察到的记忆间自发转换，需要神经动力学能够使一个吸引记忆状态失稳并转向另一状态；作者由此追问在随机联想网络中实现这种转换所需的最小表征结构。

Method: 研究每个单元具有 S 个活动状态的 Potts 联想网络（Potts associative networks），配以慢适应阈值（慢适应阈值）；采用动态平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory），分别考虑有限负载（finite-load）与扩展负载（extensive-load）情形，后者中存储模式数随网络规模与连接线性增长，并聚焦于连接矩阵中被单列出的两个凝聚记忆（condensed memories）之间的转换。

Result: 有限负载下，转换由有效驱动项（effective drive term）支配，其符号与时间演化区分出高锁存（high-latching，结构化）、低锁存（low-latching，随机）与非锁存（non-latching）三种机制；相图（phase diagram）表明仅靠适应不足，转换只在两记忆重叠度的特定范围内发生。S=1 时无单元能在两个记忆中处于不同 Potts 状态，从而强烈抑制 S>1 所具备的高锁存结构化转换。扩展负载下，非凝聚记忆通过有效记忆核（effective memory kernel）产生有色涨落（colored fluctuations）与延迟自相互作用（retarded self-interactions），锁存轨迹改变该核的有效时间尺度。

Conclusion: 结果表明多状态 Potts 式表征（multi-state Potts-like representations）对结构化的记忆间转换（memory-to-memory transitions）至关重要，而这是自发思维（spontaneous thought）的标志性特征。

Abstract: Spontaneous transitions between memories, as observed in internally generated cognition such as mind-wandering and free association, require neural dynamics capable of destabilizing one attractive memory state and moving towards another. We ask what minimal representational structure is required for such transitions to emerge in random associative networks. To address this question, we study Potts associative networks with $S$ active states per unit and slow adaptive thresholds. The $S=1$ case can model a standard Hopfield-like network of binary units. We employ dynamical mean-field theory in the extensive-load regime, where the number of stored patterns scales linearly with network size and connectivity, and we focus on transitions between two condensed memories, which are singled out in the connectivity matrix. In the finite-load regime, we show that transitions are governed by an effective drive term, whose sign and temporal evolution distinguish high-latching (structured), low-latching (random), and non-latching regimes. The resulting phase diagram shows that adaptation alone is not sufficient: transitions only occur within certain ranges of the overlap between the two memories. In the binary case $S=1$, in particular, no unit can be active in both memories but in different Potts states, thus strongly supressing the high-latching, structured transitions that exist for $S>1$. In the extensive-load regime, non-condensed memories generate colored fluctuations and retarded self-interactions through an effective memory kernel. We find that latching trajectories change the effective timescale of this kernel, a dynamical signature of transitions in the extensive-load regime. These results indicate that multi-state Potts-like representations are essential for structured memory-to-memory transitions, a hallmark of spontaneous thought.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [292] [Correlation-Consistent Gaussian Basis Sets for Periodic Solids. I. Hydrogen and p-block Elements from Boron to Chlorine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14768)
*Jincheng Yu,Hong-Zhou Ye*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出面向固体的相关一致高斯基组系列 cc-pVXZ-s（X = D、T、Q），覆盖氢及硼至氯的 p 区元素，在平均场与相关波函数计算中验证了精度、可迁移性与数值稳定性，并支持向完备基组（CBS）极限的可靠外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子体系的 cc-pVXZ 系列已成熟，但周期性固体计算缺乏质量一致、可系统收敛的高斯基组；标准原子优化在固体参考环境中易产生过于弥散的基函数，导致数值不稳定。

Method: 将 Dunning 分子 cc-pVXZ 的构造思路与改进的材料约束原子优化（MCAO）算法相结合；MCAO 对标准原子优化做正则化处理，抑制过度弥散函数，并在参考固体集合中保证数值稳定性。基组覆盖全电子势及多种赝势/有效核势。

Result: 与分子标准 cc-pVXZ 计算及固体平面波计算的基准对比表明，该系列在平均场（mean-field）与相关波函数（correlated wavefunction）计算中均具备精度、可迁移性与数值稳定性，并确认可可靠外推至完备基组（CBS）极限。

Conclusion: 应用全电子 cc-pVXZ-s 结合至 CCSD(T) 的相关波函数方法，对 7 种体相半导体的晶格常数、体模量与内聚能实现基组收敛计算；体模量和内聚能系统性趋近实验值，晶格常数存在小幅系统性高估，提示有限尺寸近似处理可能存在局限，为周期计算中系统收敛高斯基组的后续发展奠定基础。

Abstract: We present the cc-pVXZ-s series (X = D, T, and Q) of correlation-consistent Gaussian basis sets for solids, covering hydrogen and the p-block elements from boron to chlorine. The series includes basis sets of consistent quality for the all-electron potential and for various pseudopotentials and effective core potentials commonly used in periodic calculations. Our design combines Dunning's construction of the molecular cc-pVXZ series with a revised material-constrained atomic optimization (MCAO) algorithm for improved numerical performance. MCAO regularizes standard atomic optimization to suppress overly diffuse basis functions and ensure numerical stability in a set of reference solids. Benchmarks against standard cc-pVXZ calculations for molecules and plane-wave calculations for periodic solids establish the accuracy, transferability, and numerical stability of cc-pVXZ-s in both mean-field and correlated wavefunction calculations and confirm reliable extrapolation to the complete-basis-set (CBS) limit. As an application, we use all-electron cc-pVXZ-s to compute basis-set-converged lattice constants, bulk moduli, and cohesive energies for 7 bulk semiconductors. Using a hierarchy of correlated wavefunction methods up to local coupled cluster with singles, doubles, and perturbative triples [CCSD(T)], we find systematic convergence toward experimental bulk moduli and cohesive energies, while a small systematic overestimation of lattice constants suggests potential limitations in the approximate finite-size treatment. This work provides a foundation for the further development of systematically convergent Gaussian basis sets for periodic calculations.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [293] [Susceptibility Bottlenecks in Equilibrium Propagation Using Oscillator Ising Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14727)
*Malihe Farasat,Oshada Rathnayake,Nikhil Shukla*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究剖析振荡器伊辛机（oscillator Ising machine, OIM）中平衡传播（equilibrium propagation, EP）学习信号的传输机制，指出隐藏层响应可分解为输出端微扰力（nudging force）与跨层磁化率（susceptibility）两个因子，并表明仅实现目标能量景观不足以保证有效物理学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡传播（EP）依赖自由平衡态与弱扰动平衡态之间的位移实现物理系统学习，但振荡器动力学如何决定学习信号向隐藏层的传输尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一机制性空白。

Method: 构建相互作用可在相位差耦合（phase-difference coupling）与相位和耦合（phase-sum coupling）之间插值的二部振荡器伊辛机（bipartite OIM），并约束所有耦合形式在二值锁相态下对应同一伊辛哈密顿量（Ising Hamiltonian），据此分别考察微扰力与磁化率两个因子。

Result: 二次谐波注入（second-harmonic injection）主要通过相位二值化（phase binarization）影响微扰力；耦合形式则通过能量景观的混合曲率（mixed curvature）控制跨层磁化率。在等量混合时，混合曲率在二值流形（binary manifold）上消失，显著抑制隐藏层学习信号；数值仿真验证了上述机制及其对分类性能的影响。

Conclusion: 实现理想的能量景观本身不足以保证有效的物理学习，系统还须支持学习所需扰动的传输；耦合形式与动力学结构是物理学习可行性的独立约束条件。

Abstract: Equilibrium propagation (EP) enables learning in physical systems by exploiting the displacement between free and weakly perturbed equilibrium states. Here, we investigate how the underlying oscillator dynamics govern the transmission of learning signals in oscillator Ising machines (OIMs). We show that the hidden-layer response can be decomposed into two distinct factors: the nudging force applied at the output and the susceptibility governing its transmission to the hidden layer. Using a bipartite OIM with interactions interpolating between phase-difference and phase-sum coupling, we investigate these factors while ensuring that all coupling forms realize the same Ising Hamiltonian at binary phase-locked states. We demonstrate that second-harmonic injection primarily influences the nudging force through phase binarization, whereas the coupling form controls the cross-layer susceptibility through the mixed curvature of the energy landscape. At equal mixing, the mixed curvature vanishes on the binary manifold, strongly suppressing hidden-layer learning signals. Numerical simulations validate these mechanisms and reveal their consequences for classification performance. Our findings establish that realizing a desired energy landscape alone is insufficient to ensure effective physical learning; the system must also support the transmission of perturbations required for learning.

</details>


### [294] [Beyond Ground States: Learning Many-Body Hamiltonians for p-Bit Invertible Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14744)
*Tian Feng,Xutang Fan,Yiyun Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文将概率比特（probabilistic-bit, p-bit）可逆逻辑的哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）设计重构为逆统计力学（inverse statistical mechanics）问题，通过约束最大似然（constrained maximum likelihood）拟合使吉布斯分布（Gibbs law）在各钳制（clamp）情形的合法态上趋于均匀，并在有限时间采样下显著优于手工编码与间隙最大化（gap-maximizing）拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统构造仅以基态归属和宽最小能隙（minimum energy gap）为目标，但有限时间采样器会经过激发态（excited states），导致实际求解效率未得到优化。

Method: 将哈密顿量设计表述为逆统计力学：在保持每个非法态能量高于所有合法态的能隙下界（gap floor）约束下，拟合最高四次交互项，使各钳制情形合法态上的吉布斯律趋于均匀；采用带剪枝与重拟合的约束最大似然从固定项池中选项，并在所有情形上精确审计每个能量；以九种调优采样器重启下到达已验证解的时间为评分标准。

Result: 欠定模式下，学习到的乘子编码在 4x4 至 7x7 上仅为手工编码更新量的 0.55、0.44、0.52、0.46 [0.38, 0.56]，7x7 以下各尺寸族均更快且无统计可分辨的工作量代价；学习加法器在 8 比特反向模式为 0.71，24 与 32 比特约四分之一，32 比特工作量 0.55。5x5 等池、预算与能隙下，间隙最大化拟合达到手工编码更新量的 0.79 [0.73, 0.88] 与 1.26 倍工作量，而似然拟合仅需其 0.55 [0.46, 0.58] 更新与 0.58 [0.48, 0.61] 工作量。FPGA 上系数取最窄精确整数词时与软件位精确一致，学习乘子在 4x4 处为手工编码周期数的 0.41，7x7 处为 0.057 [0.045, 0.068]，查找表最多为其 2.4 倍。

Conclusion: 该方仅需电路的合法赋值；但在确定模式（determined modes）下，手工编码工作量更少，且加法器从 12 比特起手工编码更新次数更少，说明方法优势集中于欠定模式与中低比特规模。

Abstract: Probabilistic-bit (p-bit) invertible logic encodes a Boolean relation as an energy whose ground states are its legal assignments, computing in any direction by sampling. Traditional constructions target ground-state membership and a wide minimum energy gap, yet finite-time samplers traverse excited states. We cast Hamiltonian design as inverse statistical mechanics: fit an energy with interactions up to order four whose Gibbs law approaches uniformity on each clamp case's legal states, under a gap floor keeping every legal state below every illegal one. Constrained maximum likelihood with pruning and refitting fits terms from a fixed pool, and every delivered energy is audited exactly on all cases. Each energy is scored by time to a verified solution under restarts with its best of nine tuned samplers.
