In underdetermined modes, learned multipliers factor in 0.55, 0.44, 0.52 and 0.46 [0.38, 0.56] of the hand-written encodings' updates from 4x4 to 7x7, faster within every family below 7x7 at no resolved work penalty; learned adders solve the reverse mode in 0.71 of the updates at 8 bits and about a quarter at 24 and 32 bits, at 0.55 of the work at 32 bits. At 5x5 under equal pool, budget and floor, a gap-maximizing fit reaches 0.79 [0.73, 0.88] in updates and 1.26 in work against the hand-written encoding, while likelihood fitting takes 0.55 [0.46, 0.58] and 0.58 [0.48, 0.61] of the gap fit's updates and work. Rounding coefficients to the narrowest exact integer words tested, we run both energies at each pair's common word on one FPGA color-sweep SSA core, bit-exact with software: learned multipliers take 0.41 of the hand-written encoding's cycles at 4x4 and 0.057 [0.045, 0.068] at 7x7, at up to 2.4 times its lookup tables. The method requires only a circuit's legal assignments; in determined modes, hand-written encodings take less work, and from 12 bits on the adder fewer updates.

</details>


### [295] [Zeroth persistent homology of ground-state correlations at clean and disordered quantum transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14797)
*Francisco Belchí,Nahuel Statuto*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 论文考察交错基态关联的零维持久同调（zeroth persistent homology）能否为横纵场反铁磁Ising链的常规响应量增添新信息，发现其持续性景观速度极大值定义的无阈值伪临界点可精确定位干净临界点和混合阶转变线，并在回归有序区与强无序重整化群簇识别中展现独特价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横纵场反铁磁Ising链包含连续临界点、混合阶转变线以及淬火无序下的无限无序不动点和回归有序（reentrant order）；既有研究关注常规响应量，而拓扑数据分析（尤其零维持久同调）能否补充或超越传统探针尚不明确，故作者系统检验其附加信息。

Method: 采用精确自由费米子解（exact free-fermion solutions）与密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）；提取交错基态关联的零维持久同调条形码，记录单链接（single-linkage）层级的合并尺度，并以持续性景观速度（persistence-landscape velocity）的最大值定义无阈值伪临界点，与常规响应量对比。

Result: 该伪临界点定位干净临界点和混合阶线的精度与常规响应量相当；在回归有序区，全局速度误判进入点，仅当把过滤限制在有序尺度（ordering scale）时才能恢复，而所有测试估计量均无法定位退出点；边界条件强制的畴壁（domain wall）压制无序平均的回归叶。对单个无序实现，带标签合并树（labelled merge tree）可恢复多数强无序重整化群簇，包括格点受限层级遗漏的非连续簇，且无序越强吻合越好。

Conclusion: 相关与拓扑信息存在于完整关联矩阵中，而零维持久同调提供了一种稳定、无阈值的层级结构，可作为常规响应的补充探针；但其在回归有序区定位进入/退出点方面仍有局限。

Abstract: The antiferromagnetic Ising chain in transverse and longitudinal fields exhibits a continuous critical point, a mixed-order transition line and, with quenched disorder, an infinite-disorder fixed point and reentrant order. Using exact free-fermion solutions and the density-matrix renormalization group, we examine what the zeroth persistent homology of staggered ground-state correlations adds to conventional responses. Its barcode records the merge scales of the single-linkage hierarchy, and the maximum of its persistence-landscape velocity defines a threshold-free pseudocritical point. This point locates the clean critical point and the mixed-order line with an accuracy comparable to conventional responses. In the reentrant regime, the global velocity misplaces the entry, which is recovered only by restricting the filtration to the ordering scale, and none of the tested estimators locates the exit. A domain wall forced by the boundary conditions suppresses the disorder-averaged reentrant lobe. For individual disordered realizations, the labelled merge tree recovers most strong-disorder renormalization-group clusters, including noncontiguous ones missed by lattice-restricted hierarchies, with increasing agreement at stronger disorder. This information resides in the full correlation matrix, while zeroth homology provides a stable, threshold-free hierarchy.

</details>


### [296] [Direct imaging of Su-Schrieffer-Heeger edge modes in a disordered suspended mechanical oscillator chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14826)
*Defeng Lu,Rohan Modi,Nicolas Delnour,Michael Hilke*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作以一个由交替质量块和相同压缩弹簧构成的悬挂链，实现了可重构的宏观力学 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）模型，并以键坐标（bond coordinate）基重构键分辨谱与模态分布，直接观测到局域于边界的带隙中模式（mid-gap modes）及其受质量扰动影响的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSH 模型是拓扑物理的典型范式，但多集中于量子或波动系统；本文旨在构建一个可直接成像、参数可调的经典力学平台，以研究拓扑简正模式（topological normal modes）及无序效应。

Method: 采用交替质量由相同压缩弹簧耦合的悬挂链；以相邻质量块间的相对纵向位移为 SSH 自由度，在键坐标基下对力学动力学矩阵（mechanical dynamical matrix）作常数平移与重标度，将 N 个质量块的链映射为 (N−1) 格点的 SSH 链；通过视频追踪（video tracking）重建键分辨谱与模态分布，并与理想有限 SSH 链的理论预言比较。

Result: 拓扑性终止（topological termination）支持局域于边界的带隙中模式，并呈现有效偶数长度 SSH 链所预期的交替子晶格（alternating-sublattice）结构；质量变化使边缘模式频率发生劈裂，并产生相对于无无序预言的反对称模态分布。

Conclusion: 该可直接成像的平台可用于研究经典力学系统中的拓扑简正模式与无序效应，为宏观力学体系中拓扑现象的实验探索提供了可重构途径。

Abstract: We report a reconfigurable macroscopic mechanical realization of the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model: a suspended chain of alternating masses coupled by identical compression springs. The relevant SSH degree of freedom is the relative longitudinal displacement between neighboring masses. In this bond-coordinate basis, applying a constant shift and rescaling of the mechanical dynamical matrix maps a chain of $N$ blocks onto an (N-1)-site SSH chain. Using video tracking, we reconstruct bond-resolved spectra and mode profiles and compare them with predictions for an ideal finite SSH chain. The topological termination supports mid-gap modes localized near the boundaries, with the alternating-sublattice structure expected for an effective even-length SSH chain. Mass variations split the edge-mode frequencies and produce asymmetric mode profiles relative to the non-disordered predictions. This directly imageable platform enables studies of topological normal modes and disorder effects in a classical mechanical system.

</details>


### [297] [Physical interpretation of spin-glass models with correlated disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16765)
*Hidetoshi Nishimori*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文评述将一类具有关联键无序（correlated bond disorder）的 Ising 自旋玻璃模型与铁磁序联系起来，说明其铁磁相本质上是"伪装的"自旋玻璃相，并借此获得关于温度混沌与磁场混沌的新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究利用特定类型的关联键无序，把自旋玻璃相的性质（如温度混沌（temperature chaos）与磁场混沌（magnetic-field chaos））与铁磁序关联起来，需要一个清晰的物理图像来解释其可行性与意义。

Method: 该关联无序的构造为：从对称 ±J 分布的爱德华兹-安德森模型（Edwards-Anderson model）中抽取一个平衡自旋构型作为参考构型，再在使该参考构型成为全上态的规范（gauge）下写出模型；此构造与 Mattis 模型（Mattis model）互为角色颠倒。

Result: 关联无序模型的阻挫统计（frustration statistics）与对称 ±J 模型完全一致；在引入额外参数 γ 的推广中，规范等价类（gauge equivalence classes）的概率仅由 γ 决定，而抽取参考构型时的逆温度只决定类内分布。该模型的磁化强度即与参考构型的重叠（overlap）。

Conclusion: 通过对该模型铁磁相的研究，得到了爱德华兹-安德森模型自旋玻璃相中温度混沌与磁场混沌的意外结果；关联无序模型为自旋玻璃问题提供了新视角，并成为产生高度非平凡结果的有效途径。

Abstract: Ising spin-glass models with a specific type of correlated bond disorder have recently been used to relate properties of spin-glass phases, such as temperature chaos and magnetic-field chaos, to ferromagnetic order. This correlated disorder has a simple physical interpretation, which underlies the sampling procedure introduced by Oshima, Arai, and Hukushima for numerical simulations: the bond configuration is obtained from the Edwards-Anderson model with the symmetric $\pm J$ distribution by drawing one of its equilibrium spin configurations and writing the model in the gauge in which this reference configuration becomes the all-up state. The construction is analogous to the Mattis model, with the roles of the ferromagnet and the spin glass reversed: whereas the Mattis model is a ferromagnet in disguise, the ferromagnetic phase of the correlated model is essentially a spin-glass phase in disguise. The frustration statistics of the correlated-disorder model are exactly those of the symmetric $\pm J$ model, and, in a generalization with an additional parameter $γ$, the probabilities of the gauge equivalence classes are determined by $γ$ alone, whereas the inverse temperature at which the reference configuration is drawn determines only the distribution within each class. The magnetization of this model is the overlap with the reference configuration. Through this correspondence, studies of its ferromagnetic phase have led to unexpected results on temperature chaos and magnetic-field chaos in the spin-glass phase of the Edwards-Anderson model. Models with correlated disorder have thus provided a new perspective on spin glasses and have proved fertile ground for highly nontrivial results on the spin-glass problem.

</details>


### [298] [Low-temperature vibrational anomalies and thermal transport in polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16848)
*V. Sokolenko,A. I. Krivchikov,A. Jeżowski,V. Boiko,O. A. Korolyuk,D. Szewczyk*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究通过低温热容、低频拉曼光谱与热导率测量，系统比较六种多环芳烃（polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon, PAH）晶体的低频振动与热输运行为，表明类玻璃态（glass-like）的热学与振动特征并非非晶材料独有，而与非晶性之外的结构与堆积差异相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色峰（boson peak）通常被视为结构无序（structural disorder）的标志，但有序分子晶体中也存在类似的熱容异常，其与分子结构的关系及与热输运的联系尚不清楚。

Method: 选取芘、并四苯、苝、并五苯、晕苯与碗烯六种PAH晶体，开展低温热容测量、低频拉曼光谱（low-frequency Raman spectroscopy）与热导率（thermal conductivity）测量，并对热容过量部分进行标度函数拟合。

Result: 对可分辨极大值的化合物，热容过量上升侧符合无形状可调参数的升余弦（raised-cosine）标度函数，衰减侧则依化合物而异；可分辨的晶格振动拉曼带数目与宽度在系列中变化，但其指认受光谱背景与分辨率限制；热导率覆盖从类晶体到类玻璃态的范围，五种平面分子中分辨出激活型贡献，而碗烯数据在当前灵敏度下无需该贡献。

Conclusion: 类玻璃态热学与振动特征并非非晶材料所独有，而与分子结构和晶体堆积（crystal packing）差异相关；但结果本身并不能将堆积确认为唯一因果变量。

Abstract: The boson peak is an excess of low-frequency vibrational states over the Debye prediction and is commonly associated with structural disorder. Similar heat-capacity anomalies also occur in ordered molecular crystals, but their dependence on molecular structure and their relation to heat transport remain incompletely understood. Here we compare six polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon (PAH) crystals -- pyrene, tetracene, perylene, pentacene, coronene and corannulene -- using low-temperature heat capacity, low-frequency Raman spectroscopy and thermal conductivity measurements. For compounds with well-resolved maxima, the rising side of the heat-capacity excess is consistent with a raised-cosine scaling function with no adjustable shape parameter, whereas the decay is compound-dependent. The number and widths of resolved lattice-mode Raman bands vary across the series, although their assignment is constrained by spectral background and resolution. Thermal conductivity ranges from crystal-like to glass-like behaviour. An activated contribution is resolved in the five planar compounds but is not required to describe the corannulene data within the sensitivity of the present measurements. These results show that glass-like thermal and vibrational signatures are not unique to amorphous materials and are associated with differences in molecular structure and crystal packing; they do not, by themselves, isolate packing as the sole causal variable.

</details>


### [299] [Cup and cap synchronization: higher-order node dynamics from topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16979)
*Runyue Wang,Yuanzhao Zhang,Ginestra Bianconi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文借助代数拓扑（algebraic topology）与非线性动力学（nonlinear dynamics），提出 Cup and Cap Synchronization 统一框架，将高阶网络（higher-order network）的拓扑组织自然纳入基于节点的高阶同步动力学，并揭示四体相互作用（four-body interactions）可从根本上改变同步转变的临界性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于节点的高阶同步研究缺乏能囊括已有模型、并揭示三角形（triangles）之外高阶相互作用作用的统一理论框架；此前认为不同高阶 Kuramoto 类模型的临界行为源自不同机制。

Method: 结合代数拓扑与非线性动力学，引入 Cup and Cap Synchronization 框架，以自然刻画底层高阶网络的拓扑组织，并与既有的高阶 Kuramoto 类模型进行临界行为对比。

Result: Cup and Cap Synchronization 可展现属于两种既有高阶 Kuramoto 类模型普适类（universality classes）的临界行为（critical behavior），并呈现此前模型未能捕捉的额外现象；其中四体相互作用的引入能从根本上改变同步转变的临界性质，产生仅含边（edges）与三角形介导相互作用的模型无法观测到的集体现象。

Conclusion: 研究强调超越通常研究的三体项（three-body terms）的重要性，并确立 Cup and Cap Synchronization 作为理解高阶网络集体动力学（collective dynamics）的统一理论框架。

Abstract: Higher-order node-based synchronization provides distinct mechanisms for discontinuous synchronization transitions. However, a comprehensive theoretical framework capable of encompassing existing models and revealing the role of higher-order interactions beyond triangles is still missing. Here, combining algebraic topology and nonlinear dynamics, we introduce Cup and Cap Synchronization, a unifying theoretical framework for higher-order node-based dynamics that naturally incorporates the topological organization of the underlying higher-order network. We show that Cup and Cap Synchronization can display critical behavior belonging to the universality classes of two existing higher-order Kuramoto-like models, whose distinct critical behaviors were thought to originate from different mechanisms. Remarkably, we demonstrate that Cup and Cap Synchronization displays additional phenomenology not captured by the previously proposed models of higher-order node-based synchronization. Most relevantly, the inclusion of four-body interactions can fundamentally alter the critical properties of the synchronization transition, giving rise to collective phenomena that cannot be observed in models including only interactions mediated by edges and triangles. These findings reveal the importance of going beyond the commonly studied three-body terms and establish Cup and Cap Synchronization as a unifying theoretical framework for understanding collective dynamics in higher-order networks.

</details>


### [300] [Statistics of Fock-space correlation lengths in the many-body localised phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17306)
*Bibek Saha,David E. Logan,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文针对无序量子系统多体局域化（many-body localisation）相中Fock空间本征态振幅的强非均匀性，建立以两点Fock空间关联函数为核心的微观定量理论，揭示典型与平均关联行为的本质差异及其几何起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体局域化相中存在长程、多体共振（many-body resonances），其在Fock空间表现为本征态振幅的强非均匀性，但缺乏定量、微观层面的理论描述。

Method: 构建基于两点Fock空间关联函数（two-point Fock-space correlation functions）的微观理论，并对无序倾斜场Ising链（disordered tilted-field Ising chain）进行精确对角化（exact diagonalisation）以检验理论预言。

Result: 典型关联（typical correlations）呈完美指数衰减，而平均关联（average correlations）呈亚指数衰减并带有系统性尺寸依赖，源于稀有共振节点；该行为来自Fock空间关联长度分布，其方差与系统尺寸成反比，并与超立方Fock空间图（hypercubic Fock-space graph）的高维几何共同作用。

Conclusion: 理论可导出多体局域化本征态的奇异谱（singularity spectrum），并从理论上确立量子多重分形性（quantum multifractality）的反常对称性破缺为Fock空间几何的内禀结果。

Abstract: The presence of long-ranged, many-body resonances in the many-body localised phase of disordered quantum systems is manifest in strong inhomogeneities of eigenstate amplitudes on the Fock space. We develop a quantitative, microscopic theory for this, centred on two-point Fock-space correlation functions. Exact diagonalisation of the disordered tilted-field Ising chain uncovers a stark contrast: typical correlations decay perfectly exponentially, whereas average correlations exhibit subexponential decay with systematic system-size dependence driven by rare resonant nodes. We show that this behaviour stems from an underlying distribution of Fock-space correlation lengths with a variance that scales inversely with system size. Crucially, the interplay of this distribution with the high-dimensional geometry of the hypercubic Fock-space graph, leads to the qualitative difference between the typical and average behaviours. The theory also allows us to derive the singularity spectrum of the many-body localised eigenstates, and to establish theoretically the anomalous symmetry violation of quantum multifractality as an inherent consequence of the Fock-space geometry.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [301] [A Construction of Cauchy Data for Trapped Surface Formation in the Massive Einstein--Klein--Gordon System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14315)
*Xueyi Yu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 在巨大爱因斯坦–克莱因–戈登系统（massive Einstein–Klein–Gordon system）中构造了一类光滑、球对称、渐近平坦的柯西初值（Cauchy data）：初值曲面处处非陷获（untrapped），但其极大柯西发展（maximal Cauchy development）中会出现陷获面（trapped surface）形成，且初始紧致度（compactness）$2m/r$ 可任意小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注引力坍缩与陷获面形成是否能在极弱场（紧致度任意小）条件下发生，以检验"大紧致度才导致坍缩"的直觉，并考察带质量物质场情形下的弱宇宙监督（weak cosmic censorship）相关问题。

Method: 采用构造性方法：在内部球上取收缩的 Friedmann–Lemaître–Robertson–Walker（FLRW）初值，在更大的球外取 Schwarzschild 初值，中间以光滑过渡拼接，从而得到球对称、渐近平坦的初值；再分析其极大柯西发展中的陷获面形成。

Result: 所得初值光滑、球对称、渐近平坦且初始处处非陷获，但其极大柯西发展中确实形成陷获面；同时可使整个初始柯西超曲面上的比值 $2m/r$（$m$ 为 Hawking 质量，$r$ 为面积半径）任意小。

Conclusion: 陷获面形成不要求初始紧致度大，在紧致度任意小的初值中亦可发生，说明黑洞形成在弱场初始条件下同样可能，为相关坍缩与宇宙监督问题提供反直觉的构造性例证。

Abstract: We construct a class of smooth, spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat Cauchy data for the massive Einstein--Klein--Gordon system that are initially untrapped but give rise to trapped surface formation in their maximal Cauchy developments. These data coincide with contracting Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker (FLRW) data on an interior ball and with Schwarzschild data outside a larger ball. The data can also be made to have arbitrarily small compactness: the ratio $2m/r$, where $m$ is the Hawking mass and $r$ is the area radius, can be made arbitrarily small throughout the initial Cauchy hypersurface.

</details>


### [302] [Off-diagonal decay of capacitance matrices via Brownian motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16978)
*Habib Ammari,Clemens Thalhammer*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究链状、屏幕状与晶体状几何中无限球谐振子系统的电容矩阵非对角元衰减，给出上下界：链状为 d^{-1}log^{-2}(2+d)，屏幕状为 d^{-3}，三维相对稠密构型为指数衰减，且不假设周期性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对无限系统中电容矩阵（capacitance matrix）非对角元随距离衰减速率缺乏定量刻画的问题，在链、屏幕、晶体三类几何下建立上下界。

Method: 基于 Dirichlet 问题（Dirichlet problem）与布朗运动（Brownian motion）之间的经典联系，将衰减估计转化为布朗运动的击中概率（hitting probabilities）问题。

Result: 链状（圆柱内、轴向间隙有界）衰减为 d^{-1}log^{-2}(2+d) 量级；屏幕状（板层内、切向相对稠密）为 d^{-3}；三维相对稠密构型获得指数上下界；未假设周期性。

Conclusion: 三类几何下电容矩阵非对角元的衰减速率获得匹配的上下界，且结果不依赖周期性，表明布朗运动击中概率方法是研究此类衰减问题的有效途径。

Abstract: We establish upper and lower bounds for the off-diagonal entries of the capacitance matrix of infinite systems of spherical resonators in chain, screen and crystal geometries. Let $d$ denote the distance between two resonator centres. For chains contained in a cylinder of bounded radius and with bounded axial gaps, the decay is of order $d^{-1}\log^{-2}(2+d)$. For screens contained in a slab and relatively dense in the tangential directions, it is of order $d^{-3}$. For relatively dense three-dimensional configurations, we obtain exponential upper and lower bounds. No periodicity is assumed. Our proofs are based on the classical connection between the Dirichlet problem and Brownian motion, which enables us to relate the decay estimates to hitting probabilities of Brownian motion.

</details>


### [303] [How to arrange four points in the plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16990)
*Senping Luo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该摘要研究平面上四个点在广义 Lennard-Jones 势下的相互作用能量最小构型分类，得到一个由参数 p、q 决定的阈值曲线，据此判定极小构型为正方形或特定角度的菱形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点排布看似初等几何问题，却与统计物理（statistical physics）深度关联；作者以四点构型作为玩具模型（toy model），希望完全刻画一般 Lennard-Jones 势（Lennard-Jones potentials）下的极小构型。

Method: 考虑 R^2 上四点相互作用能量 min Σ_{i<j}(|x_i-x_j|^{-p} − b|x_i-x_j|^{-q})，其中 p>q>0、b∈R，对一般 Lennard-Jones 势 V(r)=r^{-p}−br^{-q} 的极小构型做完全分类。

Result: 给出阈值曲线 D_{p,q}=2^{-(p−q)/2}(1+(p−q)/(q+2)(1+2^{-(q+2)/2})) 以决定极小形状：当 b≤0 时不存在极小元；当 b>0 时极小元在旋转与平移下唯一，且 D_{p,q}≥1 时为正方形，D_{p,q}<1 时为夹角 θ∈(π/3,π/2) 的菱形。

Conclusion: 四点问题的极小构型可完全由参数 b 的符号与阈值 D_{p,q} 与 1 的相对大小刻画，正方形与菱形之间的转变由该阈值曲线控制。

Abstract: Arranging points in the plane appears to be an elementary geometric problem, yet it is deeply rooted in statistical physics. As a toy model, we consider the configuration of four points. We completely classify the minimal configurations of four points interaction energy in $\R^2$ under the general Lennard-Jones potentials $V(r)=r^{-p}-br^{-q}$, where $p>q>0$ and $b\in\R$. We find that there is a threshold curve \[
  D_{p,q}=2^{-(p-q)/2}\Bigl(1+\frac{p-q}{q+2}\bigl(1+2^{-(q+2)/2}\bigr)\Bigr) \] to determine the minimal shapes. The main results can be stated concisely as follows: consider $\min\sum_{1\le i<j\le4}\bigl(|x_i-x_j|^{-p}-b|x_i-x_j|^{-q}\bigr)$. For $b\le0$, the minimizer does not exist. For $b>0$, the minimizer is unique up to rotation and translation. Furthermore, the minimizer is \[
  \begin{cases}
  \text{a square},&\hbox{if}\;\; D_{p,q}\ge1,
  \text{a rhombus with angle }θ\in(π/3,π/2),&\hbox{if}\;\; D_{p,q}<1.
  \end{cases} \]

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [304] [Singlet-triplet superconductivity from repulsive interactions in a $p$-wave magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14609)
*Hanbit Oh,Laura Classen,Daniel F. Agterberg*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于p波磁体（p-wave magnets）中奇宇称自旋劈裂（odd-parity spin splitting）的对称性，采用parquet重整化群（pRG）研究Van Hove奇点附近电子在排斥相互作用下的失稳竞争，发现超导（superconductivity）在整个相图中始终是主导失稳，且配对态一般为自旋单态与三重态的混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期提出的p波磁体具有奇宇称自旋劈裂，其对称性如何支配电子关联系统中的失稳竞争尚不清楚，本文旨在厘清该机制。

Method: 对单子格p波磁体模型采用pRG分析，并将分析推广至更真实的双子格（two-sublattice）模型；考虑Van Hove奇点附近的电子与局域排斥相互作用。

Result: 在纯排斥相互作用下，超导在单子格模型的整个相图中均为领先失稳；其配对态一般为自旋单态与自旋三重态的混合，当Tc远小于每个Van Hove电子与其反演伙伴的能量偏移时趋于等权重混合。双子格模型中超导仍为主导，但子格自由度导致多种单-三重态配对通道竞争，甚至出现有限动量的等自旋配对密度波（pair-density wave）。

Conclusion: 该机制广泛适用于奇宇称磁体，并为实现节点拓扑超导（nodal topological superconductivity）提供了自然途径。

Abstract: Motivated by recent proposals of $p$-wave magnets, which host odd-parity spin splitting, we study electrons near Van Hove singularities with local repulsive interactions. We demonstrate how the symmetry of the $p$-wave spin splitting governs the competition among instabilities. Using a parquet renormalization group (pRG) analysis of a simple one-sublattice model of a $p$-wave magnet, we find that superconductivity is the leading instability over the entire phase diagram for purely repulsive interactions. The resulting superconducting state is generically a mixture of spin-singlet and spin-triplet pairing and approaches an equal-weight mixture when $T_c$ is small compared with the energy offset between each Van Hove electron and its inversion partner. Extending the pRG analysis to a more realistic two-sublattice model, we find that superconductivity remains the leading instability, while the sublattice degree of freedom leads to a competition among various singlet-triplet pairing channels and even a finite-momentum equal-spin pair-density wave. Our mechanism applies broadly to odd-parity magnets and opens a natural route to nodal topological superconductivity.

</details>


### [305] [Superlattice Stabilization of the Topological Quantum Hall Ferromagnet in Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14679)
*Tiago Cecchi,Ahmed Khalifa,Shubhayu Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 可调超晶格势（superlattice potential）能够重构石墨烯中自旋与谷序（spin and valley orders）的竞争，在无需强面内磁场或强介电抑制库仑相互作用的情况下，于电荷中性点稳定铁磁量子霍尔拓扑绝缘体（ferromagnetic quantum Hall topological insulator）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯中自旋序与谷序的竞争使其成为在量子霍尔（quantum Hall）区间调控关联拓扑相的通用平台，但通常稳定铁磁量子霍尔态需依赖大面内磁场或介电屏蔽来抑制库仑相互作用，寻找更可控的调控手段是本文动机。

Method: 先用计入超晶格势的广义重整化群（renormalization group）程序计算决定竞争态选择的相互作用各向异性（interaction anisotropies），再结合重整化各向异性开展自洽的动量空间 Hartree-Fock 计算，确定自旋极化铁磁绝缘态的稳定区域，并检验其与量子霍尔铁磁体的绝热连通性。

Result: 超晶格势抑制了与自旋极化相竞争的各向异性，但同时引入色散（dispersion），后者偏好部分自旋极化的补偿半金属（compensated semi-metal）；通过自洽计算确定了绝缘自旋极化铁磁体的稳定区间，并验证该铁磁体与量子霍尔铁磁体绝热连通，从而确认其拓扑性质。

Conclusion: 超晶格势可作为调控相互作用石墨烯相图（phase diagram）的手段，并可用于进入其螺旋量子霍尔（helical quantum Hall）区间。

Abstract: Competition between spin and valley orders makes graphene a versatile platform for engineering correlated topological phases in the quantum Hall regime. We show that a tunable superlattice potential reorganizes this competition, and stabilizes the ferromagnetic quantum Hall topological insulator at charge neutrality without requiring a large in-plane magnetic field or strong dielectric suppression of Coulomb interactions. The interaction anisotropies responsible for choosing between different competing states are first computed by a generalized renormalization group procedure that accounts for the superlattice potential. We find that the potential suppresses anisotropies that compete with spin-polarization. At the same time, the potential introduces dispersion which prefers a partly spin-polarized compensated semi-metal. Therefore, we carry out a self-consistent momentum-space Hartree-Fock computation with renormalized aniostropies to establish the regime of stability of the insulating spin-polarized ferromagnet. Finally, we verify that this ferromagnet is adiabatically connected to the quantum Hall ferromagnet, thereby confirming its topological character. Our results establish superlattice potentials as a means of controlling the phase diagram of interacting graphene and accessing its helical quantum Hall regime.

</details>


### [306] [Termination-selective Kondo hybridization of topological surface states in CeCuAs$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15022)
*Bo Chen,Chen Zhang,Hao Liu,Qi-Yi Wu,Zhong-Tuo Fu,Ying Zhou,Chun-Hui Lyu,Yang Luo,Zi-Jie Xu,Hai-Long Deng,Zheng-Tai Liu,Mao Ye,Zhen-Hua Chen,Yao-Bo Huang,Sheng-Tao Cui,Zhe Sun,Da-Wei Shen,Jun He,Yu-Xia Duan,Jian-Qiao Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究结合终止面分辨（termination-resolved）角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）、Ce 4d→4f 共振光电子能谱（resonant photoemission）与表面电子结构计算，在近藤晶格（Kondo lattice）化合物 CeCuAs₂ 中观察到终止面选择性的 f-Dirac 杂化（f-Dirac hybridization）及拓扑表面态中 4f 特征的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近藤晶格中的强关联拓扑态（strongly correlated topological states）是凝聚态物理前沿，边界电子与局域 4f 态的耦合可重塑低能电子结构，但其微观调控手段与谱学证据仍待明确。

Method: 采用终止面分辨 ARPES、Ce 4d→4f 共振光电子能谱及表面电子结构计算三者结合，通过特征费米面（Fermi surface）几何与终止面特异性计算，将两种表面电子结构分别归属为 As1 与 CeAs2 终止面。

Result: 在 CeAs2 终止面上，与计算所得非平庸体拓扑（nontrivial bulk topology）相关的狄拉克表面态（Dirac surface state）与费米能级附近弱色散的 Ce 4f 能带共存；共振光电子能谱确认了低能 4f 特征并揭示狄拉克分支能量范围内的晶体场（crystal-field）精细结构。降温时 4f 与狄拉克区域谱重均增加，而参考导带区域变化明显较小。

Conclusion: 结果提供了终止面选择性 f-Dirac 杂化及拓扑表面态中 4f 特征发展的谱学证据，表明表面终止面（surface termination）可作为调控近藤关联（Kondo correlations）与拓扑边界电子相互作用的微观手段。

Abstract: Strongly correlated topological states in Kondo lattices represent a frontier in condensed matter physics, where the coupling between boundary electrons and localized f states can reshape the low-energy electronic structure. Here, we combine termination-resolved angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), Ce 4$d \rightarrow 4f$ resonant photoemission, and surface electronic structure calculations to investigate the Kondo lattice compound CeCuAs$_2$. Two distinct surface electronic structures are assigned to the As1 and CeAs2 terminations through their characteristic Fermi-surface geometries and termination-specific calculations. On CeAs2, a Dirac surface state associated with the calculated nontrivial bulk topology coexists with a weakly dispersive Ce 4$f$ band near $E_F$. Resonant photoemission identifies the low-energy 4$f$ character and reveals crystal-field-related fine structure in the energy range traversed by the Dirac branch. On cooling, the 4$f$ and Dirac regions both gain spectral weight, whereas a reference conduction-band region changes much less. Together, these observations provide spectroscopic evidence for termination-selective $f$-Dirac hybridization and the development of 4$f$ character in a topological surface state. Our results identify surface termination as a microscopic means of controlling the interplay between Kondo correlations and topological boundary electrons.

</details>


### [307] [Maximally Chiral Eigenstate from Inverse Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15081)
*Tigran A. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作表明有序量子态可支持单向有限速度、反向指数小能量的非常规谱，并据此反构非局域哈密顿量，得到具有发散磁化率、对数熵与可调束缚对等奇异性质的基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有序量子态能否同时容纳方向性动力学（单向有限速度传播）与异常热力学行为，并建立相互作用设计与这些现象之间的对应关系。

Method: 为自旋-1/2 海森堡（Heisenberg）本征态指定目标谱，反构非局域哈密顿量（nonlocal Hamiltonian），并通过热力学构造使该有序载流态成为基态；辅以自旋响应计算，并引入额外交换作用与更平滑的模式选择。

Result: 该基态磁矩有限但弱场响应斜率发散；每自旋熵仅随温度对数倒数趋于零，系数精确含黄金比例（golden ratio）；自旋响应揭示能量可调而强度不变的束缚对；加入交换与平滑模式选择后最低能量恢复二次型激发。

Conclusion: 结果将相互作用设计与方向性动力学及奇异热力学联系起来，并提出动量分辨自旋谱学（momentum-resolved spin spectroscopy）、弱场磁强计（weak-field magnetometry）与低温熵测量作为实验检验手段。

Abstract: We show that an ordered quantum state can support waves with a finite speed in one direction and exponentially small energies in the other. We prescribe this spectrum for a spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg eigenstate and reconstruct a nonlocal Hamiltonian. The thermodynamic construction selects the ordered, current-carrying state as a ground state. Its magnetic moment remains finite while its response to a weak field has a divergent slope. The entropy per spin vanishes only inversely with a logarithm of temperature, with an exact coefficient containing the golden ratio. Our spin-response calculations reveal a bound pair whose energy can be tuned without changing its intensity. Added exchange and smoother mode selection restore quadratic excitations at the lowest energies. These results connect the design of interactions to directional dynamics and unusual thermodynamics. We propose momentum-resolved spin spectroscopy, weak-field magnetometry, and low-temperature entropy measurements to probe these counterintuitive signatures.

</details>


### [308] [Higher-order topology induced by altermagnetism in quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15139)
*Xingmin Huo,Xingchuan Zhu,Xingyu Ma,Shiping Feng,Huaiming Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Thue-Morse与Ammann-Beenker准晶中，由d波准晶交变磁序与二维拓扑绝缘体耦合驱动的高阶拓扑相（HOTPs）涌现；发现d_{x²-y²}波交变磁序可打开边缘能隙并诱导零能高阶拓扑角态，从而作为识别非周期体系中交变磁序的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁（AM）具有零净磁化与动量依赖自旋劈裂，近期被推广至准晶，但在缺乏平移对称性时其动量依赖自旋劈裂的理解不足；本文旨在探究准晶交变磁序如何驱动高阶拓扑相。

Method: 以Thue-Morse和Ammann-Beenker准晶为平台，将d波准晶交变磁序与二维拓扑绝缘体耦合，考察不同对称性（d_{x²-y²}波交变磁、常规反铁磁、d_{xy}波交变磁）构型下的能隙与角态；分析交变磁对称性及其与晶格角取向的匹配关系。

Result: d_{x²-y²}波交变磁在打开边缘能隙与诱导零能高阶拓扑角态方面表现出类铁磁特征；常规反铁磁与d_{xy}波交变磁构型无法诱导高阶拓扑能隙。

Conclusion: 交变磁对称性及其与晶格角的对齐在实现HOTPs中起关键作用；HOTPs的涌现可作为在非周期体系中识别交变磁序的特征信号。

Abstract: Altermagnetism (AM), a novel magnetic phase featuring zero net magnetization and momentum-dependent spin splitting, provides a compelling platform for exploring exotic quantum phenomena. While recent studies have extended the concept of AM to quasicrystals, the momentum-dependent spin splitting in the absence of translational symmetry remains inadequately understood. Here, we investigate the emergence of higher-order topological phases (HOTPs) in Thue-Morse and Ammann-Beenker quasicrystals, driven by the coupling between $d$-wave quasicrystal AM orders and a two-dimensional topological insulator. We reveal that the $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave AM exhibits a ferromagnetism-like aspect in opening edge gaps and inducing zero-energy higher-order topological corner states. In contrast, conventional antiferromagnetic and $d_{xy}$-wave AM configurations are unable to induce the higher-order topological gaps. These findings highlight the pivotal role of altermagnetic symmetry and its alignment with lattice corners in the realization of HOTPs and establish that the emergence of HOTPs can serve as a signature for identifying altermagnetic orders in aperiodic systems.

</details>


### [309] [Magnetism on emergent molecular-orbital lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15141)
*Zhan Liu,Chenrui Wang,Chen Fang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在红宝石晶格（ruby lattice）紧束缚 Hubbard 模型中证明：当跳跃参数（hopping parameters）改变使涌现的分子轨道（molecular orbital）晶格由三角晶格转为蜂窝晶格时，同一原子晶格、同一填充下可出现阻挫与非阻挫反铁磁性；分子形状因子（molecular form factor）的零点会在低能动态结构因子（dynamic structure factor）中抑制谱权重，可用于实验检验分子轨道磁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体中邻近原子轨道强杂化形成空间上覆盖一簇原子的分子轨道（MOs），其中心构成不同于原子晶格的涌现晶格（emergent lattice）。对于部分填充的分子轨道，电子相互作用可能在该涌现晶格上诱导局域磁矩来自分子轨道而非单个原子的新型磁性，此机制尚需理论刻画与实验判据。

Method: 构建红宝石晶格紧束缚 Hubbard 模型（ruby-lattice tight-binding Hubbard model），通过调节跳跃参数改变涌现分子轨道晶格的几何（三角↔蜂窝），并结合随机相位近似（random phase approximation, RPA）计算低能动态结构因子与分子形状因子。

Result: 在分子轨道晶格由三角变为蜂窝时，同一原子晶格与同一填充下可分别出现阻挫（frustrated）与非阻挫（unfrustrated）反铁磁序。RPA 计算显示：低能积分结构因子在分子形状因子零点附近出现极小值，且不同布里渊区（Brillouin zone）选定等价动量之间的比值与形状因子平方之比一致。

Conclusion: 分子轨道磁性可由低能动态结构因子中的分子形状因子调制效应加以实验验证：形状因子零点不依赖分子磁矩所形成的磁序类型而抑制低能谱权重，为识别涌现晶格上的新型磁性提供了可测量判据。

Abstract: In solids, strong hybridization between nearby atomic orbitals forms molecular orbitals (MOs), the range of which covers a cluster of atoms. The centers of molecular orbitals form an emergent lattice generally different from the atomic lattice. For partially filled MOs, electron interaction may induce a new type of magnetism on this emergent lattice where the local moments come from the molecular orbitals instead of single atoms. In this work, we show in a ruby-lattice tight-binding Hubbard model that frustrated and unfrustrated antiferromagnetism may appear on the same atomic lattice and at the same filling, as the emergent molecular-orbital lattice changes from triangular to honeycomb upon changing the hopping parameters. Moreover, we show that general molecular orbital magnetism may be experimentally verified in the low-energy dynamic structure factor: the molecular form factors, as the Fourier transforms of the molecular spin-density, modulate the low-energy dynamic structure factor across different Brillouin zones. Zeros of these form factors suppress low energy spectral weight irrespective of the magnetic order formed by the molecular moments. This is exemplified by random phase approximation calculations for the ruby-lattice model: the structure factor integrated over low energies exhibits minima around molecular form-factor zeros, while its ratios between selected equivalent momenta in different Brillouin zones agree with ratios of squared form factors.

</details>


### [310] [Kitaev Exchange and Altermagnetism in the Double Perovskite Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$: An Ab Initio Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15152)
*Alexey V. Ushakov,Sergey V. Strelsov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于第一性原理（ab initio）密度泛函理论计算，Sr₂CoTeO₆被识别为具有中等强度铁磁Kitaev交换作用、显著交换阻挫以及交变磁性（altermagnetism）的候选材料，可产生横向压磁效应（transverse piezomagnetic effect）与非常规磁光响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Co²⁺基双钙钛矿（double perovskite）Sr₂CoTeO₆因其非平庸磁性受到关注，需要从微观交换作用层面阐明其磁性来源与材料分类归属。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论（density functional theory）计算，考察最近邻Co²⁺之间的交换作用，并结合面心立方（face-centered cubic）晶格几何分析阻挫效应。

Result: 计算发现最近邻间存在可观的铁磁Kitaev交换（|K|~J/3）；与面心立方晶格结合后导致显著的交换阻挫（exchange frustration），使该材料处于Kitaev相互作用强度中等的Kitaev材料之列。同时Sr₂CoTeO₆被判定为交变磁体（altermagnet），具有可观的非相对论性自旋劈裂（non-relativistic spin splitting）。

Conclusion: Sr₂CoTeO₆集中等强度Kitaev物理与交变磁性于一体，其非相对论性自旋劈裂可能带来横向压磁效应和非平庸磁光响应，为相关功能磁性研究提供新平台。

Abstract: The Co$^{2+}$-based double perovskite Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$ attracts attention due to its nontrivial magnetic properties. \textit{Ab initio} density functional theory calculations reveal a substantial ferromagnetic Kitaev exchange $|K|\sim J/3$ between nearest neighbors that, combined with the face-centered cubic lattice, leads to pronounced exchange frustration and places Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$ among Kitaev materials with moderate Kitaev interaction strength. In addition, Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$ is found to be altermagnetic with a sizable non-relativistic spin splitting, which can give rise to a transverse piezomagnetic effect and a nontrivial magneto-optical response.

</details>


### [311] [Multifractal collective order : Application to multifractal superconductivity in dodecagonal quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15446)
*SeHyun Park,Junmo Jeon,SungBin Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出确定性非周期几何（deterministic aperiodic geometry）可稳定多重分形集体序（multifractal collective order），并建立一个判别多重分形序参量的通用诊断框架，在十二边形氧化物准晶（dodecagonal oxide quasicrystal）的Ti网络上验证了超导序满足全部三条判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集体有序描述通常假设空间均匀的序参量（order parameter），但底层电子态可能存在强烈空间不均匀性或多重分形性（multifractality），因此需要识别几何驱动的非常规集体序并给出可检验的判据。

Method: 构建适用于超导、磁性与电荷通道的通用诊断方法，将序参量的直接标度分析（scaling analysis）与三条判据结合：空间层级须由底层几何决定、须出现在主导失稳模式中、须在单一平均场有序起始（mean-field ordering onset）以下的自洽有序态中持续存在；并以实验解析的十二边形氧化物准晶Ti网络为对象进行检验。

Result: 该网络上的超导性满足全部三条判据；所得凝聚体在已解析的空间尺度范围内呈多重分形标度，并在整个有序相中保持层级结构，同时使磁通诱导的平衡电流（flux-induced equilibrium currents）沿几何锁定的环路流动。

Conclusion: 确定性非周期几何能够稳定多重分形集体序，序参量的层级可与几何结构锁定并影响电流输运，表明准晶等非周期体系是研究非常规集体序的可行平台。

Abstract: Conventional descriptions of collective ordering often assume spatially uniform order parameters, although the underlying electronic states can exhibit strong spatial inhomogeneity or multifractality. Here, we demonstrate that deterministic aperiodic geometry can instead stabilize multifractal collective order. We formulate a general diagnostic applicable to superconducting, magnetic, and charge channels, combining direct scaling analysis of the order parameter with three criteria: its spatial hierarchy must be dictated by the underlying geometry, emerge in the leading ordering instability, and persist in the self-consistent ordered state below a single mean-field ordering onset. We demonstrate that superconductivity on the experimentally resolved Ti network of a dodecagonal oxide quasicrystal satisfies all three criteria. The resulting condensate exhibits multifractal scaling over the resolved range of spatial scales, retaining its hierarchical structure throughout the ordered phase and channeling flux-induced equilibrium currents along geometry-locked rings.

</details>


### [312] [Charge Pumping Leptogenesis from the Symmetric Mass Generation Phase on a Four-Leg Ladder Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42103)
*Runze Chi,Meng Zeng,Tao Xiang,Yi-Zhuang You,Bertrand I. Halperin,Juven Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）研究准二维四腿梯形结构中 3-4-5-0 手征费米子模型单边带六费米子相互作用时的量子化电荷泵浦（quantized charge pumping），发现弱、强耦合下边缘无能隙，中等耦合下边缘可处于完全能隙的对称质量生成（SMG）相，且三种边界相均保持每磁通量子相同的泵浦电荷，仅空间分布不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔体系中量子化电荷泵浦通常表现为无能隙边缘的手征模式通过磁通插入产生谱流，而无反常边界可被对称质量生成（symmetric mass generation, SMG）相互作用对称地打开能隙；但相互作用能隙边界对量子化电荷泵浦的影响此前并不清楚，本文旨在厘清该问题。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）数值模拟，构建 (1+1) 维 3-4-5-0 格点手征费米子模型的四腿梯形准 (2+1) 维实现，并在其中一条边缘引入六费米子（six-fermion）相互作用。

Result: 弱耦合与强耦合下相互作用边缘附近均存在无能隙模式，而中等耦合时边缘可进入完全能隙的 SMG 相；超过临界相互作用强度还出现模型 Z2 对称性的自发破缺。三种边界相下均观测到每磁通量子相同的量子化泵浦电荷，但泵浦电荷的空间分布随相而异；在 SMG 相中，注入单个费米子在开边界条件下会于能隙边缘端点产生相互作用诱导的味混合电流（flavor-mixing current），弱、强耦合极限下则不存在。

Conclusion: 量子化电荷泵浦在相互作用能隙边界下依然保持量子化，但其空间分布与边缘电荷再分配机制依赖于边界相，SMG 相具有独特的电荷再分配机制。作者进一步将该模型的电荷泵浦类比于 (3+1) 维标准模型中的拓扑轻子生成（topological leptogenesis）情景：3-4-5-0 模型的无反常手征 U(1) 对称性类似于标准模型的重子减轻子（baryon-minus-lepton）对称性，二者均表现为沿经典定义但量子反常方向的连续形变伴随电荷泵浦，同时某一离散子群仍为精确量子对称性。

Abstract: Quantized charge pumping in quantum Hall systems is a bulk topological property, typically manifesting on gapless boundaries as spectral flow through chiral edge modes upon flux insertion. When the boundary is anomaly-free, it can be symmetrically gapped by symmetric mass generation (SMG) interactions. However, the effects on quantized charge pumping at an interacting gapped boundary are unclear. We address this using density-matrix renormalization group simulations of a four-leg ladder quasi-(2+1)d realization of the (1+1)d 3-4-5-0 lattice chiral fermion model with six-fermion interactions at one edge. We find gapless modes near the interacting edge for both weak and strong couplings, but the edge can be in a fully gapped SMG phase at intermediate coupling. We also identify spontaneous breaking of the model's $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry above a critical interaction strength. Across all three boundary phases, we find quantized charge pumping with the same pumped charge per flux quantum. The spatial distribution of the pumped charge varies between phases. Besides charge pumping, we study how a single injected fermion propagates under open boundary conditions. In the SMG phase, an interaction-induced flavor-mixing current develops at the endpoints of the gapped edge, which is absent in the weak- and strong-coupling limits. This current reveals a charge-redistribution mechanism unique to the SMG phase. Additionally, we propose an analogy between charge pumping in this model and a topological leptogenesis scenario in the (3+1)d Standard Model. The anomaly-free chiral U(1) symmetries of the 3-4-5-0 model play a role analogous to the baryon-minus-lepton symmetry of the Standard Model. In both settings, charge pumping can be associated with a continuous deformation along a classically defined but quantum-anomalous direction, while a discrete subgroup remains an exact quantum symmetry.

</details>


### [313] [Toward Self-Driving Multi-reference Calculations: Belief-Aware Orbital Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15682)
*Chen Li,Kaifan Pan,Xinyu Sun,Yinxuan Song,Lianhua He,Teng Lu,Haibo Ma,Zhenggang Lan,Yingjin Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将活性轨道选择（active-orbital selection）重构为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），并与二次无约束二值优化（QUBO）耦合，实现类似专家的、随计算进程持续自适应（belief-aware）的活性空间选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统活性空间选择多依赖一次性的阈值过滤（one-shot threshold-based filtering），缺乏随计算进展的自适应能力，且难以在复杂化学反应全程中保持与专家判断相当的选择质量。

Method: 以POMDP建模选择过程，用QUBO在固定电子数与轨道数下构造完备候选空间，并利用占据数（occupation numbers）、轨道组成（orbital composition）与轨道纠缠（orbital entanglements）三类轨道信息（可单独或联合使用），通过逐次计算更新信念以校正和细化活性轨道。

Result: 在小分子的价空间（valence-space）构建、金属化合物TiF$_3$的光谱计算以及1,2-二氧杂环丁酮（1,2-dioxetanone）在圆锥交叉（conical intersections）附近的活性空间细化中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该方法直接向目标分子的计算学习，无需预先构建训练集，因而适用于跟踪复杂化学反应的整个过程，实现连续的活性空间选择。

Abstract: We reformulate active-orbital selection as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), analogous to decision-making in autonomous driving and robot control, and couple it with quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) to emulate expert-like selection of active orbitals. The three popular types of orbital information, i.e. occupation numbers, orbital composition, and orbital entanglements, can be used separately or jointly. QUBO constructs complete candidate spaces under fixed electron and orbital counts, while the POMDP selects successive calculations, updates its belief from each completed result, and thereby corrects and refines the active orbitals. This belief-aware sequential procedure enables continuous active-space selection rather than one-shot threshold-based filtering, so the space can adapt as the calculation proceeds. We demonstrate the approach for valence-space construction in small molecules, spectral calculation of metal compound TiF$_3$, and active-space refinement of 1,2-dioxetanone around conical intersections. Because selection learns from calculations on the molecule of interest without requiring a pre-existing training set, it is suited to following the entire course of complex chemical reactions.

</details>


### [314] [$^{55}$Mn-NMR/NQR study on the kagome metal Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$: Re-examining the possibility of spin-singlet paired state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15693)
*Qing-Ping Ding,Yongbin Lee,Charuni Dissanayake,Yasuyuki Nakajima,Yuji Furukawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过$^{55}$Mn核磁共振（NMR）与核四极共振（NQR）结合DFT计算研究kagome金属Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$的低能磁性与电子性质，未发现此前报道的约10 K自旋单态配对迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$被提出接近铁磁失稳（ferromagnetic instability），且近期$^{55}$Mn NMR研究提示约10 K以下出现自旋单态配对态（spin-singlet paired state），需澄清其低能磁性与电子性质。

Method: 对单晶样品开展$^{55}$Mn NMR与NQR谱测量，并结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，确定Mn位点的四极频率$ν_{\rm Q}$、不对称参数$η$及电场梯度（EFG）主轴；分析Knight shift（$K$）、自旋-晶格弛豫率（$1/T_1T$）与线宽的温度依赖。

Result: 得到$ν_{\rm Q}$=4.6~4.8 MHz、$η$=0.45~0.48；$K$与直流磁化率$χ$在约50 K以上趋势相似，低温下$K$近乎恒定而$χ$继续增大，表明$χ$的低温升高非本征；$1/T_1T$近乎恒定，证实金属态；10 K附近$1/T_1T$无峰，$K$与线宽均无降低。

Conclusion: 结果未显示Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$在约10 K存在自旋单态配对的清晰证据。

Abstract: Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$ is a Mn-based kagome metal that has been proposed to be close to a ferromagnetic instability. Recent $^{55}$Mn nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) study suggested the emergence of a spin-singlet paired state below $\sim$10 K. To clarify its low-energy magnetic and electronic properties, we performed $^{55}$Mn NMR and nuclear quadrupole resonance (NQR) measurements on single-crystalline Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$. From the detailed NMR/NQR spectrum measurements and the DFT calculations, a quadrupolar frequency of $ν_{\rm Q}$ = 4.6$\sim$4.8 MHz and an asymmetry parameter of $η$ = 0.45$\sim$0.48 as well as the principal axes of electric field gradient EFG for the Mn site were determined. The temperature dependence of Knight shift ($K$) shows a similar temperature dependence of the DC magnetic susceptibility $χ$ except for the low-temperature region below $\sim$ 50 K where $K$ is almost constant while $χ$ keeps increasing, which suggests that the increase in $χ$ at low temperatures is not intrinsic. $^{55}$Mn spin-lattice relaxation rate divided by temperature ($1/T_1T$) is found to be nearly constant, confirming the metallic state of Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$ from a microscopic point of view. Furthermore, no peak in $1/T_1T$ is observed near 10 K, and neither the Knight shift nor the linewidth shows any reductions at low temperatures. These results suggest no clear signature for the spin-singlet pairing around 10 K in Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$.

</details>


### [315] [Potts Order Preempts Emergence of 2-Form U(1) Spin Liquidity in Three-Dimensional Spin Vorticity Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15845)
*Michael D. Burke,Mohid U. Farooqi,Kristian Tyn Kai Chung,Michel J. P. Gingras*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作指出三维自旋涡度模型（spin vorticity models, SVMs）中2-形式U(1)经典自旋液体（classical spin liquid, CSL）会被熵驱动的高度锁定（height locking）不稳定性所抢先占据，从而无法实现临界库仑基态系综。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前数值研究发现烧绿石晶格SVM虽呈现U(1)自旋液体特征的捏点（pinch points），却同时出现微弱全进全出（all-in--all-out, AIAO）序，构成一个未解之谜，需要从约束与熵的角度解释其根源。

Method: 采用高度映射（height mapping）解析基态约束，并将其与3态Potts反铁磁体（3-state Potts antiferromagnet）建立对应关系；进一步把SVM推广到共角八面体与立方体晶格，并在粗粒化高度理论（coarse-grained height theory）框架下分析锁定势与相变。

Result: 烧绿石中的弱AIAO序被识别为置换对称子晶格（permutationally symmetric sublattice, PSS）序，但锁定强度极弱；推广晶格中则出现隐藏的破缺子晶格对称（broken-sublattice-symmetry, BSS）序，其无磁布拉格峰（magnetic Bragg peaks）并与集体顺磁性共存；三个模型均经历与对称性允许锁定势一致的3D伊辛相变（3D Ising transition）。

Conclusion: 自旋液体仅存在于中间长度与温度尺度，临界库仑基态系综（critical Coulomb ground state ensemble）因有限锁定长度（locking length）截断关联而未能实现；结果表明微观约束可决定阻挫自旋系统中涌现规范理论（emergent gauge theories）的最终稳定性。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) spin vorticity models (SVMs) have been proposed to realize an exotic 2-form U(1) classical spin liquid (CSL), characterized by a zero-curl constraint, string excitations, and deconfined fluctuating membranes. A previous numerical study [PRB 111, 064417 (2025)] uncovered a puzzle in the pyrochlore lattice SVM: despite exhibiting pinch points characteristic of a U(1) spin liquid, it also develops weak all-in--all-out (AIAO) order. Here, we show that the emergence of the 2-form CSL in 3D SVMs is preempted by an intrinsic instability towards entropy-driven height locking. We establish this by resolving the ground state constraints using a height mapping, which we further relate to a 3-state Potts antiferromagnet. This identifies the pyrochlore's weak AIAO order as permutationally symmetric sublattice (PSS) order, albeit with an exceptionally weak locking strength. We extend the SVM to lattices of corner-sharing octahedra and cubes, where we identify the alternative broken-sublattice-symmetry (BSS) order, a hidden order without magnetic Bragg peaks coexisting with collective paramagnetism. All three models undergo a 3D Ising transition consistent with a symmetry-allowed locking potential in the coarse-grained height theory. The spin liquid persists over intermediate length and temperature scales, but a critical Coulomb ground state ensemble fails to materialize, its correlations cut off by the finite locking length. These findings illustrate how microscopic constraints can determine the ultimate stability of emergent gauge theories in frustrated spin systems.

</details>


### [316] [Universal Theory of Strange and Bad Metals from Spatially Random Interactions with Underdamped Quantum-Critical Bosons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15851)
*Aaron Kleger,Rufus Boyack*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个(2+1)维理论：费米子通过空间无序相互作用与欠阻尼量子临界玻色子耦合，并通过对能标分离（separation of scales）的恰当设定使玻色子保持欠阻尼，从而解释奇异金属（strange metals）中电阻率随温度线性变化的长期难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚态物理（condensed matter physics）的核心挑战之一是理解奇异金属（strange metals）的宏观性质，尤其是其电阻率（resistivity）在数十个数量级温度范围内保持线性（$T$-linear）的成因，此前缺乏令人满意的理论解释。

Method: 构建(2+1)维费米子与量子临界玻色子（quantum-critical bosons）通过空间无序相互作用（spatially disordered interaction）耦合的理论；其关键创新在于确立合适的能标分离（separation of scales），以保证玻色子处于欠阻尼（underdamped）状态。

Result: 得到两种费米子自能（self-energy）形式：共形极限解（conformal-limit solution）$Σ\sim\sqrt{ω}$，与边缘费米液体解（marginal Fermi-liquid solution）$Σ\simω\log(1/ω)$，分别对应Mott-Ioffe-Regel极限之上与之下的区域。这些解给出普朗克散射（Planckian scattering）、精确的$T$线性电阻率、光电导（optical conductivity）中的$ω/T$标度（scaling），以及低温下$\sim T\log(1/T)$的热容（heat capacity）。

Conclusion: 该理论在单一框架内统一了奇异金属（strange-metal）与坏金属（bad-metal）现象，作者认为这标志着理解强关联物质（strongly correlated matter）物理的新纪元正在到来。

Abstract: One of the most important challenges in condensed matter physics is understanding the macroscopic properties of strange metals, particularly why their resistivity remains linear in temperature over several decades. We propose that a (2+1)d theory of fermions coupled to underdamped quantum-critical bosons through a spatially disordered interaction provides this long-sought-after theoretical explanation. The key novelty of our work is to establish the appropriate separation of scales so that the bosons are underdamped. Using this framework, we obtain two distinct forms for the fermionic self-energy: a conformal-limit solution, $Σ\sim\sqrtω$, and a marginal Fermi-liquid solution, $Σ\simω\log(1/ω)$, corresponding to regimes above and below the Mott-Ioffe-Regel limit, respectively. These solutions produce Planckian scattering, exhibit exact $T$-linear resistivity, $ω/T$ scaling in the optical conductivity, as well as $\sim T\log(1/T)$ heat capacity at low temperatures. Moreover, our theory naturally unifies strange- and bad-metal phenomena within a single framework, suggesting that we are now approaching a new epoch in understanding the physics of strongly correlated matter.

</details>


### [317] [Crystalline Equivalence Principle for Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15857)
*Ching-Yu Yao,Ryohei Kobayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在(1+1)与(2+1)维玻色子晶格系统中，对由晶体对称性与有限幺正内部对称性组合而成的微观对称性反常（symmetry anomaly）进行分类，构造反常指数并给出上同调取值与阻碍结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有反常分类多依赖连续场论描述或涌现洛伦兹不变性，缺乏不借助连续极限的微观刻画；本文旨在建立玻色反常的微观层面晶体等价原理（crystalline equivalence principle）。

Method: 构造对称性算符的反常指数（anomaly indices）并推导其上链条件（cocycle conditions）与上边界歧义（coboundary ambiguities）；对结合格点希尔伯特空间置换与量子元胞自动机（quantum cellular automata）的广义晶体对称性，确定指数取值于 $H^3(B\widetilde Γ,U(1)_r)$ 与 $H^4(B\widetilde Γ,U(1)_r)$，其中系数扭曲 $r$ 刻画空间取向反转。

Result: 当晶体对称性仅置换格点希尔伯特空间时，反常类限制到空间子群上平凡；总反常指数非平凡会阻碍任何对称可逆相（symmetric invertible phase）；框架可纳入内禀对称性被空间对称性置换的调制对称性（modulated symmetries），并重现 LSM 与 SPT–LSM 约束，后者表明谱序列微分（spectral-sequence differentials）可平凡化候选的 LSM 反常指数。

Conclusion: 结果在微观层面确立了玻色反常的晶体等价原理，无需连续描述或涌现洛伦兹不变性假设，并统一涵盖 LSM 与 SPT–LSM 类型的约束。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic characterization of crystalline symmetry anomalies in bosonic lattice systems in (1+1) and (2+1) dimensions with a combination of crystalline and finite unitary internal symmetry. We construct anomaly indices of symmetry operators and derive their cocycle conditions and coboundary ambiguities. For generalized crystalline symmetries combining permutations of onsite Hilbert spaces with quantum cellular automata, anomaly indices take values in $H^3(B\widetilde Γ,U(1)_r)$ and $H^4(B\widetilde Γ,U(1)_r)$ respectively, where $\widetilde Γ$ is the total group of crystalline and internal symmetries, and the coefficient twist $r$ accounts for spatial orientation reversal. When crystalline symmetries act solely by permuting onsite Hilbert spaces, the anomaly classes restrict trivially to the spatial subgroup. These results establish a crystalline equivalence principle for bosonic anomalies at the microscopic level, without assuming a continuum description or emergent Lorentz invariance. We prove that a nontrivial total anomaly index obstructs any symmetric invertible phase. The framework incorporates modulated symmetries where spatial symmetries permute internal symmetries. Our results reproduce LSM and SPT--LSM constraints, with the latter illustrating how spectral-sequence differentials can trivialize candidate LSM anomaly indices.

</details>


### [318] [Learning Quantum Hamiltonians from the Dynamical Structure Factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15859)
*Nisarga Paul,Thomas Schuster,Jason Alicea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究从动力学结构因子（dynamical structure factor, DSF）中学习量子哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）的问题，证明DSF包含足以高效重建多类量子哈密顿量的信息，并提出三种互补的哈密顿量层析（Hamiltonian tomography）方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别支配量子系统的微观相互作用对建立可预测的材料理论及验证量子技术至关重要，但从真实实验数据中学习哈密顿量仍是难题；研究受中子散射（neutron scattering）与量子模拟（quantum simulation）驱动，聚焦量子磁体中由DSF所捕获的两点关联数据（two-point correlation data）。

Method: 利用DSF低阶谱矩（low-order spectral moments）与微观耦合之间的简单关系，构建三种互补的哈密顿量层析协议，分别基于高温展开（high-temperature expansion）、强场极化（strong-field polarization）与涨落耗散定理（fluctuation-dissipation theorem）。

Result: 理论分析表明DSF编码了足以高效重建广泛类别量子哈密顿量的信息，三种协议可从常见实验数据中实现哈密顿量学习。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架建立了从常规实验数据出发的实用且稳健的哈密顿量学习途径，并可能直接应用于中子散射实验。

Abstract: Identifying the microscopic interactions governing a quantum system is essential for developing predictive theories of materials and validating quantum technologies. Despite considerable progress, learning Hamiltonians from realistic experimental data remains a challenge. Motivated by neutron scattering and quantum simulations, we study the problem of learning a quantum Hamiltonian from the dynamical structure factor (DSF), which captures two-point correlation data in quantum magnets. We show that the DSF encodes sufficient information to efficiently reconstruct broad classes of quantum Hamiltonians. Our approach exploits simple relations between low-order spectral moments of the DSF and microscopic couplings, giving rise to three complementary Hamiltonian tomography protocols based on the high-temperature expansion, strong-field polarization, and the fluctuation-dissipation theorem, respectively. The framework we introduce establishes practical and robust Hamiltonian learning from common experimental data, with potentially immediate application to neutron scattering experiments.

</details>


### [319] [What Hall Measures in Strongly Correlated Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15940)
*Emily Z. Zhang,Thomas P. Devereaux*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于Liouvillian框架将霍尔电导率（Hall conductivity）分解为多体干涉算符，并用Hubbard模型的Schrieffer-Wolff变换揭示投影电荷运动与虚跃迁如何贡献绕磁通的有向轨迹，为量子蒙特卡罗中观测到的整数幂律提供微观解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 霍尔效应（Hall effect）是探测强关联金属中电子关联的灵敏探针，但超出准粒子输运（quasiparticle transport）层面的微观解释仍不清楚，尤其是横向输运与多体相干动力学的关系尚待阐明。

Method: 构建霍尔电导率的Liouvillian表述，将响应组织为不同类别的磁通敏感多体干涉算符（flux-sensitive many-body interference operators）；对Hubbard模型施加Schrieffer-Wolff变换，并结合高温展开（high-temperature expansion）分析各项贡献的温度层级。

Result: 高温展开给出与各干涉过程对应的逆温度贡献层级，可对行列式量子蒙特卡罗（determinant quantum Monte Carlo）此前观测到的整数幂律（integer power laws）给出可能的微观解释，尽管这些幂律出现在形式上受控温区之外。

Conclusion: 结果表明霍尔输运对相干多体动力学高度敏感，并建立了晶格几何（lattice geometry）、电子关联与横向电荷响应之间的联系。

Abstract: The Hall effect provides a sensitive probe of electronic correlations in strongly interacting metals, yet its microscopic interpretation beyond quasiparticle transport remains poorly understood. Here, we develop a Liouvillian formulation of the Hall conductivity that organizes the response into different classes of flux-sensitive many-body interference operators. Using a Schrieffer-Wolff transformation of the Hubbard model, we identify how projected charge motion and virtual hopping processes contribute to oriented trajectories enclosing magnetic flux. A high-temperature expansion reveals a hierarchy of inverse-temperature contributions associated with these interference processes. This framework provides a possible microscopic interpretation of the integer power laws previously observed in determinant quantum Monte Carlo calculations, which emerge at temperatures below the regime where the expansion is formally controlled. Our results highlight the sensitivity of Hall transport to coherent many-body dynamics and establish a connection between lattice geometry, electronic correlations, and transverse charge response.

</details>


### [320] [Elastic tricriticality at the collapse of magnetic order in a valence fluctuating Eu-based compound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15984)
*Yassine Agarmani,Arif Ata,Lars Franke,Jan Zimmermann,Ulrich Tutsch,Marius Peters,Michelle Ocker,Kristin Kliemt,Cornelius Krellner,Bernd Wolf,Markus Garst,Michael Lang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作指出，与电子关联相关的三临界点（tricritical point, TCP）上，晶格弹性耦合本质上是非微扰的，会改变三临界普适类并产生“弹性三临界性（elastic tricriticality）”；在 EuPd$_2$(Si$_{1-x}$Ge$_x$)$_2$ 单晶中，通过热膨胀与氦气压力下的弹性响应测量确认了该行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋与电荷涨落是理解强关联电子材料性质的关键，但晶格耦合通常被认为弱且可微扰处理。本文质疑该假设，探讨电子关联驱动的三临界点处晶格弹性的作用。

Method: 采用理论论证与实验结合：先论证三临界点处弹性耦合的非微扰性质及其对普适类的修正；再以 EuPd$_2$(Si$_{1-x}$Ge$_x$)$_2$ 单晶为对象，利用互补探针（尤其温度与氦气压力控制下的相对长度变化测量）定位 $x\approx0.1$ 处的三临界点，并测量弹性响应。

Result: 在 $x\approx0.1$ 处通过互补探针确认了三临界点（TCP）的存在；弹性响应测量揭示出弹性三临界性的明确特征，特别是压缩率（compressibility）的显著增强。

Conclusion: 在三临界点处，晶格弹性耦合不可忽略且非微扰，会将体系推向新的“弹性三临界”普适类，其压缩率随关联长度（correlation length）以普适方式发散；EuPd$_2$(Si$_{1-x}$Ge$_x$)$_2$ 的实验支持这一图像。

Abstract: Spin and charge fluctuations are key for understanding the properties of materials with strongly correlated electrons, but their coupling to the atomic crystal lattice is often assumed to be weak and perturbative. In this work, we demonstrate that at tricritical points (TCPs), that are associated with electronic correlations, a coupling to crystal elasticity is generically non-perturbative. It will modify the tricritical universality class leading to elastic tricriticality, characterized by a compressibility that diverges in a universal manner with the correlation length. To test this scenario experimentally, we use single crystals of EuPd$_2$(Si$_{1-x}$Ge$_x$)$_2$, where, depending on $x$ and pressure, magnetic order collapses due to strong valence fluctuations. First, we confirm the presence of a TCP for $x \approx 0.1$ by using complementary probes, in particular, measurements of relative length changes under control of temperature and He-gas pressure. Second, by studying the elastic response, we uncover clear signatures of elastic tricriticality, notably a drastically enhanced compressibility.

</details>


### [321] [Mott transitions in Dirac nodal-line semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16033)
*Andressa R. Medeiros-Silva,Lucas O. Lima,Marcello B. da Silva Neto,Fakher F. Assaad,Natanael C. Costa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文结合平均场理论、行列式量子蒙特卡洛与弱耦合重整化群分析，比较了立方晶格上节点点（nodal-point）与节点线（nodal-line）半金属中相互作用驱动的反铁磁相变与能带不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 节点流形（nodal manifold）的几何结构如何影响相互作用驱动的量子相变尚不清楚，节点线半金属为研究这一问题提供了自然平台。

Method: 在立方晶格哈伯德模型（Hubbard model）上，采用平均场理论、行列式量子蒙特卡洛（determinant quantum Monte Carlo）模拟以及弱耦合重整化群（renormalization group, RG）分析，对比立方π通量节点点模型与节点线半金属态。

Result: 两种情形下反铁磁序（antiferromagnetic order）均需有限相互作用强度才能出现，节点线体系因低能态密度增强而临界耦合更小、有限尺寸效应更强；节点点模型的量子临界行为与(3+1)维Gross–Neveu平均场标度相符，而弱耦合RG显示节点线在二阶自能产生的U²cos k_z相关项下重构为各向异性的孤立节点点。

Conclusion: 弱耦合下的节点线重构区别于强耦合反铁磁转变，凸显了节点流形几何在相互作用半金属中的关键作用。

Abstract: Nodal-line semimetals provide a natural setting to investigate how the geometry of a nodal manifold affects interaction-driven quantum phase transitions. Here, we study the Hubbard model on a cubic lattice supporting either nodal-point (cubic $π$-flux model) or nodal-line semimetallic states using mean-field theory, determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations, and a weak-coupling renormalization-group (RG) analysis. In both cases, antiferromagnetic order develops only above a finite interaction strength, with the nodal-line system exhibiting a smaller critical coupling due to its enhanced low-energy density of states and markedly stronger finite-size effects. For the nodal-point model, the quantum critical behavior is compatible with $(3+1)$-dimensional Gross--Neveu mean-field scaling. In contrast, the weak-coupling RG reveals that the nodal line itself is unstable against interactions: although the Hubbard interaction is perturbatively irrelevant, its second-order self-energy generates a symmetry-allowed relevant term proportional to $U^2\cos k_z$, reconstructing the nodal line into isolated, strongly anisotropic nodal points. This weak-coupling reconstruction is distinct from the strong-coupling antiferromagnetic transition and highlights the role of nodal-manifold geometry in interacting semimetals.

</details>


### [322] [Lattice response theory for subsystem symmetry-protected topological phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17182)
*Yuki Furukawa,Takuya Okuda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文以格点响应理论（lattice response theory）为框架研究子系统对称保护拓扑（SSPT）相，系统构建了从微观 X-型对称作用到时空格点规范场的对应，并通过纠缠器（entangler）实现响应作用所描述的 SSPT 相的堆叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为 SSPT 相提供统一的响应理论描述，将全局对称、子系统对称及更一般的调制对称（modulated symmetries）纳入同一构造框架，并刻画超出交配对（intersection pairing）形式的 SSPT 相。

Method: 从 qudit 系统上一般 X-型对称作用出发系统构造时空格点规范场，再由规范不变响应作用构造相应纠缠器，证明其实现与响应作用对应的 SSPT 相堆叠；并借助交配对与 Bockstein 同态的 type-II 群上闭链（group cocycle）子系统类比进行分类刻画。

Result: 证明一大类 SSPT 相可由交配对描述并由簇纠缠器（cluster entangler）实现；构造了一个由两个 2-叶层状平面子系统对称与一个全局 0-型对称共同保护的 3+1 维新 SSPT 相，其响应作用为 type-III 上闭链的子系统类比。

Conclusion: 该 3+1 维新相边界上 0-型对称通过堆叠 2+1 维线性 SSPT 相起作用，表明存在混合 't Hooft 反常（mixed 't Hooft anomaly），说明响应理论框架可统一刻画交配对型与高阶上闭链型 SSPT 相及其边界反常。

Abstract: We develop a framework to study subsystem symmetry-protected topological (SSPT) phases using lattice response theory. We begin with a systematic construction of spacetime lattice gauge fields from general microscopic $X$-type symmetry actions on qudit systems, including global symmetries, subsystem symmetries, and more general modulated symmetries. Given a gauge-invariant response action, we then construct a corresponding entangler and explicitly show that it implements stacking with the SSPT phase described by the response action. We show that a broad class of SSPT phases can be described by intersection pairings and realized by cluster entanglers. We also study SSPT phases beyond intersection pairings, including those described by subsystem analogues of type-II group cocycles involving the Bockstein homomorphism. We construct a new 3+1d SSPT phase protected by two 2-foliated planar subsystem symmetries and a global 0-form symmetry, whose response action is a subsystem analogue of a type-III cocycle. On its boundary, the 0-form symmetry acts by stacking with a 2+1d linear SSPT phase, signaling a mixed 't Hooft anomaly.

</details>


### [323] [Noninvertible Lieb-Schultz-Mattis Constraints and Deconfined Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16249)
*Linhao Li,Zhen Bi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文为一维量子比特（qudit）系统推导了由双线性相位映射算符（bilinear phase map operators）实现的内禀非可逆对称性（internal noninvertible symmetries）的 Lieb-Schultz-Mattis 定理，并给出一个 Z4 模型实例与数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 LSM 约束（Lieb-Schultz-Mattis theorem）多基于射影表示（projective representations），本文旨在将约束推广到由双线性相位映射算符实现的非可逆内禀对称性情形。

Method: 为一类由双线性相位映射算符实现内禀非可逆对称性的一维 qudit 系统推导 LSM 定理；并以一个 Z4 qudit 模型进行说明与数值模拟。

Result: 证明同时保持该对称性与平移（translation）或反演（inversion）对称性的哈密顿量必须无能隙（gapless）或具有简并基态；此类约束来源于内禀对称性与晶体对称性之间的非平凡代数关系，而非射影表示。Z4 模型包含两个分别自发破缺内禀非可逆对称性与反演对称性的竞争相，数值模拟显示二者之间存在直接连续相变，实现稳定的、涉及内禀非可逆对称性的解禁量子临界点（deconfined quantum critical point）。

Conclusion: 非可逆内禀对称性与晶体对称性的代数关系可产生新型 LSM 型约束，并可在具体 qudit 模型中支持稳定的解禁量子临界点。

Abstract: We derive Lieb-Schultz-Mattis theorems for one-dimensional qudit systems with a class of internal noninvertible symmetries implemented by bilinear phase map operators. We show that any Hamiltonian preserving these symmetries together with translation or inversion symmetry must either be gapless or have degenerate ground states. Unlike conventional LSM constraints based on projective representations, these new constraints arise from nontrivial algebraic relations between the internal and crystalline symmetries. We illustrate this mechanism in a $\mathbb{Z}_4$ qudit model hosting two competing phases that spontaneously break the internal noninvertible symmetry and inversion symmetry, respectively. Numerical simulations reveal a direct continuous transition between these phases, realizing a stable deconfined quantum critical point involving an internal noninvertible symmetry.

</details>


### [324] [Why "quantum spin liquids'' are quantum singlet liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16428)
*Sergey Syzranov,Arthur P. Ramirez*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文主张：在无杂质极限下，低温不发生磁序的几何阻挫（geometrically frustrated, GF）磁性材料会形成“量子单态液体（quantum singlet liquid）”，即由非磁性激发构成的无序态，并给出实验与理论两方面的支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid, QSL）已投入大量理论与实验努力，但实验验证困难：QSL 特征具有间接性，且材料中的杂质可能破坏或掩盖 QSL；同时理论界对最简单几何阻挫晶格上量子自旋基态仍缺共识。

Method: 采用实验证据梳理与理论论证相结合的方式：总结无杂质极限下、低温不发育磁序的现有 GF 磁性材料的实验表现，并以理论论证确认该单态液体及其无能隙（gapless）性质。

Result: 无杂质极限下、低温不磁序化的 GF 磁性材料将形成量子单态液体，即非磁性激发的无序态；该单态液体被理论论证确认为存在且具无能隙特征。

Conclusion: 该单态液体体现于几何阻挫磁体的热力学（thermodynamics）中，而在低温下不呈现磁性特征；这为在 GF 磁性材料中识别 QSL 相关行为提供了判据视角。

Abstract: The last couple of decades have seen enormous theoretical and experimental research efforts aimed at finding quantum spin liquids (QSLs) in geometrically frustrated (GF) magnetic materials. Experimental demonstrations of QSLs are challenging due to the indirect character of possible QSL signatures and the presence of impurities in materials, which may destroy or obscure a QSL. At the same time, theoretical literature often lacks consensus about the ground states of quantum spins on even the simplest GF magnetic lattices. We summarize experimental evidence that in the impurity-free limit, available GF magnetic materials that do not develop magnetic order at low temperatures would form {\it a quantum singlet liquid}, i.e. a disordered state of non-magnetic excitations. We further confirm the existence of such a singlet liquid and its gapless character with theoretical arguments. Such a liquid manifests itself in the thermodynamics of GF magnets while having no magnetic features at low temperatures.

</details>


### [325] [Thermal Hall sign reversal from competing magnons and magnon pairs in pyrochlore spin-nematic magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16440)
*Tetsuya Sato,Emika Takata,Masahiro Sato*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在磁场诱导的全极化相中，磁振子对（magnon pair）与单磁振子（single magnon）携带符号相反的贝里曲率（Berry curvature），其竞争可导致热霍尔电导率（thermal Hall conductivity）随磁场和温度发生符号反转，从而为区分束缚磁振子对提供输运探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 束缚磁振子对（bound magnon pairs）是自旋向列序（spin-nematic order）的核心，但实验上难以将其与单磁振子区分，因而需要一种可辨识的特征信号。

Method: 针对具有铁磁双二次交换（ferromagnetic biquadratic exchange）的自旋-3/2烧绿石反铁磁体（pyrochlore antiferromagnet），在磁场诱导的全极化相中构建单磁振子带与磁振子对带，引入Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya（DM）相互作用，并计算其本征热霍尔电导率。

Result: 最低磁振子带与磁振子对带具有相反符号的贝里曲率；由于两类准粒子的能隙对磁场响应不同，其竞争贡献可驱动磁场诱导与温度诱导的热霍尔电导率符号反转。对铬尖晶石氧化物（chromium spinel oxides）的估算表明该效应可接近实验可及量级，且DM相互作用引起的单、双磁振子扇区杂化仍保留该定性特征。

Conclusion: 同时具有磁振子与磁振子对的一类阻挫磁体（frustrated magnets）为调控热霍尔电导率符号及通过热输运实验探测束缚磁振子对提供了可能途径。

Abstract: Bound magnon pairs are central to spin-nematic order, yet experimentally distinguishing them from single magnons remains challenging. We show that their Berry-curvature-driven heat transport provides a characteristic signature in the thermal Hall response. For a spin-$3/2$ pyrochlore antiferromagnet with ferromagnetic biquadratic exchange in the magnetic-field-induced fully polarized phase, we construct single-magnon bands together with magnon-pair bands, include the Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya (DM) interaction, and evaluate their intrinsic thermal Hall conductivities. The lowest magnon and magnon-pair bands carry Berry curvatures of opposite sign. Because the gaps of the two quasiparticles respond differently to magnetic field, their competing contributions can drive both magnetic-field- and temperature-induced sign reversals of the thermal Hall conductivity. Estimates for chromium spinel oxides indicate that the effect can approach experimentally accessible magnitudes. We also find that the hybridization between the one- and two-magnon sectors induced by the DM interaction preserves this qualitative signature. These results suggest that a broad class of frustrated magnets with both magnons and magnon pairs provides a possible route to tuning the sign of the thermal Hall conductivity and probing bound magnon pairs through thermal transport experiments.

</details>


### [326] [Probing the Dirac Quantum Geometry in Quantum Magnets from Polarized Raman Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16491)
*Yuki Yamazaki,Doh Young Kim,Takahiro Morimoto,Bohm-Jung Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于对称性的拉曼提取原理，用于从二维量子磁体的双带准粒子激发中直接读出量子几何量。通过圆偏振谱分离量子度规与贝里曲率的收缩，并在C3对称大质量狄拉克谷中用谱比消去未知拉曼强度与强度归一化。对反演相关的大质量狄拉克锥对，阈值附近的归一化反向圆偏振拉曼不对称由陈数固定并呈普适频率标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性拉曼光谱（magnetic Raman spectroscopy）对准粒子量子几何敏感，但微观拉曼顶点（Raman vertex）通常阻碍对几何量的直接解释。作者希望建立无需绝对强度校准或频率积分的局域谱学读出方案。

Method: 针对具有双带准粒子激发的二维量子磁体建立基于对称性（symmetry-based）的提取原理：利用相反圆偏振谱分离顶点加权的量子度规（quantum metric）与贝里曲率（Berry curvature）收缩；在C3对称大质量狄拉克谷（massive Dirac valley）中由对称性固定拉曼与速度双重态至共同标度，使未知拉曼强度与强度归一化在谱比中抵消；并分析反演相关狄拉克锥对的阈值行为。

Result: 对反演相关的大质量狄拉克锥对，频率阈值Ω_th处的归一化反向圆偏振拉曼不对称（counter-circular Raman asymmetry）由陈数（Chern number）固定，并遵循普适的近阈值频率标度（near-threshold frequency scaling）。该原理在场诱导蜂窝Kitaev自旋液体中的马约拉纳费米子（Majorana fermions）与蜂窝铁磁体中的拓扑磁振子（topological magnons）上得到演示，并给出了实验分辨所需条件。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种无需绝对强度校准、无需频率积分的局域光谱读出量子几何的方法，并指出可在场诱导Kitaev自旋液体与拓扑磁振子体系中实验检验。

Abstract: Magnetic Raman spectroscopy is sensitive to quasiparticle quantum geometry, yet microscopic Raman vertices generally prevent a direct geometric interpretation. Here we establish a symmetry-based extraction principle for two-dimensional quantum magnets with two-band quasiparticle excitations. Opposite circular-polarization spectra separate vertex-weighted quantum-metric and Berry-curvature contractions. Near a $C_3$-symmetric massive Dirac valley, symmetry fixes the leading Raman and velocity doublets up to a common scale, allowing the unknown Raman strength and intensity normalization to cancel from their spectral ratio. For an inversion-related pair of massive Dirac cones, the normalized counter-circular Raman asymmetry at frequency threshold $Ω_{\rm th}$ is fixed by the Chern number and follows a universal near-threshold frequency scaling. This yields a local spectroscopic readout without absolute-intensity calibration or frequency integration. We demonstrate the principle for Majorana fermions in the field-induced honeycomb Kitaev spin liquid and for topological magnons in a honeycomb ferromagnet, and identify the conditions for resolving it experimentally.

</details>


### [327] [Thermal and dynamical behaviors of the spin-nematic phases in a spin-1 triangular lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16532)
*Lei Xu,Zhentao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该理论研究自旋-1三角晶格模型（spin-1 triangular lattice model）的有限温度性质与动力学，以解释Na₂BaNi(PO₄)₂中自旋向列（spin-nematic）物理的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Na₂BaNi(PO₄)₂被确认是实现自旋向列物理的强候选材料，需要理论模型阐明其有限温度行为与低能动力学。

Method: 采用SU(3)相干态半经典方法、半经典蒙特卡洛（semiclassical Monte Carlo）、低能有效模型的量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）以及自旋波（spin wave）方法。

Result: 小的双二次相互作用（biquadratic interaction）可稳定自旋向列相；有限温度相变与半涡旋（half vortices）增殖导致的BKT转变（BKT transition）相关；量子蒙特卡洛给出的相界与半经典分析定性一致；低能自旋动力学可由自旋-1模型或有效自旋-1/2模型等价描述。

Conclusion: 零场下半经典方法的偶然简并（accidental degeneracy）通过显式解析参数化被揭示，并与自旋动力学中观测到的赝戈德斯通模（pseudo-Goldstone mode）相关联。

Abstract: The triangular lattice compound Na$_2$BaNi(PO$_4$)$_2$ has been recently established as a strong candidate for realizing the spin-nematic physics. In this theoretical work, we study the finite-temperature properties and the dynamics of a spin-1 triangular lattice model, which reproduces the spin-nematic physics and provides insights relevant to the compound. We show that a small biquadratic interaction can be used for stabilizing the SN phases in the semiclassical approach with SU(3) coherent states, which allows for a mathematical parameterization of the spin-nematic wave functions. With a semiclassical Monte Carlo calculation, the finite temperature transitions into the spin-nematic phases are shown to be related to BKT transitions associated with the proliferation of half vortices in the original spin-1 model. Using quantum Monte Carlo calculations with a low-energy effective model, the phase boundaries are determined and found to agree qualitatively with the semiclassical analysis. Within the spin wave approach, the low-energy spin dynamics can be captured equally well by either the spin-1 model with a small biquadratic term, or the effective spin-$1/2$ model. The accidental degeneracy of the semiclassical approach at zero field is revealed by explicit analytical parameterization, and is shown to be associated with the pseudo-Goldstone mode observed in the spin dynamics.

</details>


### [328] [Learning to control and predict light-driven electron-phonon dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16780)
*Matteo Puviani*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作在一个双格点电子-声子模型（two-site electron-phonon model）中构建了结合场优化与神经网络预测的可微框架，用于通过驱动晶格振动调控电子双占位（double occupation），并证明其作为非平衡材料控制概念验证的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动晶格振动可通过非线性电子-声子耦合（nonlinear electron-phonon coupling）直接操控电子关联，但寻找合适驱动场需要超越单纯选择脉冲振幅与频率、对耦合动力学进行更精细的控制。

Method: 建立可微优化（differentiable）框架，比较分段常数（piecewise-constant）、B样条（B-spline）、前馈网络（feed-forward）与循环网络（recurrent）四类控制参数化，以最大化累积电子双占位为目标；同时用独立的循环网络由历史可观测量与外加场预测下一步双占位与声子可观测量。

Result: 在无耗散、Hubbard相互作用为零的模型中，所有方法均提升双占位，其中B样条参数化使平均总双占位提高约30%；循环网络亦能从先前值与外加场预测下一步的双占位与声子可观测量。

Conclusion: 结果构成通过声子驱动塑造局域电子关联的计算概念验证（proof of concept），并指出了通向非平衡材料控制学习模型的有前景路径。

Abstract: Driving lattice vibrations provides a direct route to manipulating electronic correlations through nonlinear electron-phonon coupling. Finding suitable driving fields, however, requires control over the coupled dynamics beyond a simple choice of pulse amplitude and frequency. Here we develop a differentiable framework that combines field optimization with neural-network prediction in a two-site electron-phonon model. We compare piecewise-constant, B-spline, feed-forward, and recurrent control parameterizations, optimized to increase the accumulated electronic double occupation. For the dissipation-free model at zero Hubbard interaction, all the methods increase double occupation, with the B-spline raising the mean total double occupation of approximately 30%. Alongside control optimization, a separate recurrent network learns to predict the next-step double occupation and phonon observables from their preceding values and the applied field. These results establish a computational proof of concept for shaping local electronic correlations through a phonon drive and identify a promising route toward learned models for nonequilibrium materials control.

</details>


### [329] [Two Unconventional Hall Effects in the Kagome Magnet TmMn4Cr$_2$Sn$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16841)
*Mohamed El Gazzah,Fehmi Sami Yasin,Bishal Thapa,Sk Jamaluddin,Abhijeet Nayak,Resham Babu Regmi,Mary Kate Carroll,Igor I. Mazin,Nirmal J. Ghimire*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在笼目磁体（kagome magnet）TmMn$_4$Cr$_2$Sn$_6$中识别出两种霍尔异常（Hall anomaly）：高场异常源于不连续自旋重取向（spin reorientation）及不同子晶格（sublattice）对霍尔输运与磁化的贡献差异，低场异常表现为霍尔滞后（Hall hysteresis）在磁滞可忽略时仍持续存在，提示与手性（chirality）相关的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清笼目磁体（kagome magnet）中磁性转变与非规霍尔信号（unconventional Hall signal）之间的混淆来源，并检验基于磁化的霍尔分解（magnetization-based Hall decomposition）方法的适用边界。

Method: 基于对TmMn$_4$Cr$_2$Sn$_6$中两种霍尔异常的识别，结合高场与低场两种区间的霍尔输运与磁化行为对比分析；摘要未提供具体实验手段与定量细节。

Result: 高场异常可归因于不连续自旋重取向（spin reorientation）与不同子晶格（sublattice）贡献的差异；低场下霍尔滞后（Hall hysteresis）在磁滞近乎可忽略时仍存在，暗示手性（chirality）相关贡献，但其微观起源尚未解决。

Conclusion: 两类对比区制（regime）表明磁性转变可模拟非规霍尔信号（unconventional Hall signal），并揭示以磁化为基准进行霍尔分解的局限性。

Abstract: We identify two Hall anomalies in the kagome magnet TmMn$_4$Cr$_2$Sn$_6$. We propose that the high-field anomaly reflects a discontinuous spin reorientation and differing sublattice contributions to Hall transport and magnetization. At low fields, Hall hysteresis persists despite negligible magnetic hysteresis, suggesting a chirality-related contribution, although its microscopic origin remains unresolved. These contrasting regimes show how magnetic transitions can mimic unconventional Hall signals and highlight the limits of magnetization-based Hall decomposition.

</details>


### [330] [Symmetry-preserving gapped Dirac state revealed by site-selective $^{13}$C NMR in $α$-(BETS)$_2$I$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16939)
*Takahiko Sekine,Kazuya Miyagawa,Koichi Hiraki,Reizo Kato,Kazushi Kanoda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用位点选择性的 ¹³C 核磁共振（NMR）证明 α-(BETS)₂I₃ 低温绝缘相同时保持空间反演对称性与时间反演对称性，其基态为对称性保持的能隙型狄拉克态（gapped Dirac state）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-(BETS)₂I₃ 在约 50 K 发生金属-绝缘体转变（metal-insulator transition）或尖锐交叉，并被提出为自旋轨道耦合诱导的狄拉克绝缘体候选体系，但其绝缘基态性质尚未确定，需要微观层面的对称性判据。

Method: 采用位点选择性 ¹³C 核磁共振（site-selective ¹³C nuclear magnetic resonance）测量谱线分裂结构与核自旋-晶格弛豫率，并结合奈特位移（Knight shift）分析各分子位点的自旋磁化率。

Result: NMR 谱在低至 2 K 均无额外分裂，可完全由一个四重峰与两个二重峰（对应 A(=A′)、B、C 三类分子位点）描述，表明反演对称性保持；未观察到异常谱线展宽或弛豫率的临界增强，排除磁有序与时间反演对称性破缺。奈特位移给出位点分辨的自旋磁化率满足 χ_C > χ_A > χ_B，为倾斜狄拉克锥（tilted Dirac cone）电子态特征，但 α-(BEDT-TTF)₂I₃ 中源于无能隙倾斜狄拉克锥的微弱场致类亚铁磁自旋激发在 BETS 体系缺失。

Conclusion: α-(BETS)₂I₃ 的绝缘基态是对称性保持的能隙型狄拉克态，为该分子狄拉克体系中相关拓扑相的候选理论提供了微观约束。

Abstract: We report evidence that the low-temperature insulating phase of $α$-(BETS)$_2$I$_3$ preserves both inversion and time-reversal symmetries using site-selective $^{13}$C nuclear magnetic resonance. $α$-(BETS)$_2$I$_3$ undergoes a metal-insulator transition or sharp crossover at approximately 50 K and has been proposed as a candidate spin-orbit-coupling-induced Dirac insulator, yet the nature of its insulating ground state remains unresolved. The NMR spectra exhibit no additional splitting down to 2 K and remain fully described by one quartet and two doublets associated with the three types of molecular sites, A (=A$^{\prime}$), B and C sites, demonstrating the preservation of inversion symmetry. Furthermore, no anomalous line broadening or critical enhancement of the nuclear spin-lattice relaxation rate is observed, indicating the absence of magnetic ordering and time-reversal-symmetry breaking. Analysis of the Knight shifts yields site-specific spin susceptibilities satisfying $χ_{\mathrm{C}} > χ_{\mathrm{A}} > χ_{\mathrm{B}}$, a characteristic feature of tilted Dirac-cone electronic states as observed in $α$-(BEDT-TTF)$_2$I$_3$ under pressure. However, the faint field-induced ferrimagnetic-like spin excitations observed in $α$-(BEDT-TTF)$_2$I$_3$, which are ascribed to the gapless tilted Dirac cones, are absent in $α$-(BETS)$_2$I$_3$. These results demonstrate that the insulating ground state of $α$-(BETS)$_2$I$_3$ is a symmetry-preserving gapped Dirac state and provide microscopic constraints on proposed correlated topological phases in this molecular Dirac system.

</details>


### [331] [Unconstrained $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory on a triangular lattice: deconfined fermions in a vison crystal and superfluidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16988)
*Welberth Kennedy,João C. Inácio,Fakher F. Assaad*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在三角晶格半满条件下，研究无约束 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 规范场与费米子耦合模型，发现弱横场下存在去禁闭的 vison 晶体相，费米子形成 Dirac 半金属，并在有限温度下给出完整 $T\times h$ 相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角几何可诱导对 plaquette 通量（plaquette fluxes）起 Ising 作用的整体 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 对称性；研究其能否改变该模型的相与临界行为，并与方晶格情形对比。

Method: 采用辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（auxiliary field quantum Monte Carlo）模拟，在三角晶格半满条件下研究无约束 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 规范场与费米子耦合系统，并扩展至有限温度。
