Result: 弱横场 $h$ 下该对称性自发破缺，稳定 vison 晶体；费米子在新兴 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 规范场下于有序 $\pi$-通量背景中传播并形成 Dirac 半金属（Dirac semimetal）。增大 $h$ 触发向禁闭 $s$-波超流的一阶量子相变（first-order quantum phase transition），禁闭与整体对称破缺同时发生，与方晶格的连续相变形成对比。有限温度下超流经 Kosterlitz–Thouless 转变融化，vison 晶体经历热 Ising 转变。

Conclusion: 结果确立了 $T\times h$ 相图，并表明三角几何在相与临界性两方面带来定性变化。

Abstract: We investigate an unconstrained $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory coupled to fermions on the triangular lattice at half filling, using auxiliary field quantum Monte Carlo simulations. The triangular geometry gives rise to a global $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry that acts as an Ising symmetry on the plaquette fluxes. At weak transverse field $h$, this symmetry is spontaneously broken, stabilizing a vison crystal. We argue that this phase is deconfined: fermions, charged under an emergent $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge field, propagate in an ordered $π$-flux background and form a Dirac semimetal. Increasing the transverse field triggers a first-order quantum phase transition to a confined $s$-wave superfluid, at which confinement and global symmetry breaking occur simultaneously. This contrasts with the square lattice, where the corresponding transition is continuous. We extend our analysis to finite temperature and determine the thermal phase boundaries of both ordered phases. The superfluid melts through a Kosterlitz--Thouless transition, whereas the vison crystal undergoes a thermal Ising transition. Together, our results establish the $T \times h$ phase diagram and demonstrate how the triangular geometry leads to qualitative changes in both the phases and the criticality of the model.

</details>


### [332] [Theory of critical behavior at non-polar structural phase transitions from functional renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17081)
*Max Fornoville,Kilian Fraboulet,Michael M. Scherer,Laura Classen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文通过泛函重整化群（FRG）研究含额外动力学项 $h(\partial_i\phi_i)^2$ 的 $O(d)$ 模型，处理热相变与量子相变；结论为该耦合在 Wilson-Fisher 不动点处为无关（irrelevant），但具有不可忽略的 corrections-to-scaling 修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 $O(N)$ 模型无法描述由内部对称性与空间对称性"交锁"（interlocked）生成的序参量，这类序参量出现在偶极反铁磁体、自旋轨道耦合磁体、非极性铁性体及部分向列系统中，需扩展有效理论框架。

Method: 构建含额外对称性允许动力学项 $h(\partial_i\phi_i)^2$ 的 $O(d)$ 有效理论，该耦合改变纵向与横向涨落的相对刚度；采用泛函重整化群（functional renormalization group, FRG）推导并分析流动方程，重点关注两点函数（two-point function）的张量结构以一致地提取动力学项的流动。

Result: 内部与空间对称性的交锁使空间导数与虚时导数被区分，量子模型不能由热模型经维度扩展 $d\rightarrow d+1$ 直接得到。分析表明，额外的动力学耦合在热与量子两种情形下于 $O(d)$ Wilson-Fisher 不动点（Wilson-Fisher fixed point）处均为无关（irrelevant），量子-经典对应（quantum-to-classical correspondence）因而重新显现。

Conclusion: 该耦合的 corrections-to-scaling 指数虽小但量级可观，导致临界点附近出现显著标度修正；因此尽管普适类不变，有限温度或有限尺度实验数据拟合时需考虑该修正。

Abstract: $O(N)$ models provide a versatile framework for describing continuous phase transitions in a wide variety of physical systems. We investigate an extension of these models arising from order parameters that transform under interlocked internal and spatial symmetries which describe transitions towards non-polar structural order. Such order occurs, for instance, in dipolar antiferromagnets, spin-orbit-coupled magnets, non-polar ferroics, and certain nematic systems. The resulting effective theory is an $O(d)$ model with an additional symmetry-allowed kinetic term $h(\partial_iφ_i)^2$, which modifies the relative stiffness of longitudinal and transverse fluctuations. We study both thermal and quantum phase transitions within this $O(d)$ model using the functional renormalization group (FRG). In contrast to conventional $O(N)$ models, the interlocking of internal and spatial symmetries distinguishes spatial and imaginary-time derivatives, such that the quantum model is not simply obtained from the thermal one by a dimensional extension $d\rightarrow d+1$. We derive and analyze the corresponding FRG flow equations, paying particular attention to the tensor structure of the two-point function, which is essential for consistently extracting the flow of the kinetic terms. Our analysis shows that the additional kinetic coupling is irrelevant at the $O(d)$ Wilson-Fisher fixed point in both thermal and quantum cases, where quantum-to-classical correspondence thus re-emerges. However, its small but sizable correction-to-scaling exponent gives rise to substantial corrections to scaling in the vicinity of the critical point.

</details>


### [333] [A neural quantum state variational Monte Carlo study of Aharonov-Casher bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17144)
*Conor Smith,Nicolás Morales-Durán,Jingtian Shi,Andrew J. Millis,Miguel A. Morales,Shiwei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用基于神经网络的变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo）方法，在Aharonov-Casher连续模型（摩尔材料模型）中研究分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator）与广义维格纳晶体（generalized Wigner crystal）之间的竞争，并给出强能带混合下的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子陈绝缘体与广义维格纳晶体在摩尔材料（moiré materials）中的竞争关系尚不清楚，尤其在强能带混合（band mixing）以及现有方法难以处理的体系尺寸下，需要新的数值手段来判定基态相。

Method: 采用基于神经网络的变分蒙特卡洛方法，构造受磁通附着（flux attachment）启发的变分波函数以刻画分数化相，并与刻画晶态相的Slater–Jastrow拟设比较能量，从而确定分数填充下的相图。

Result: 离散平移对称性（编码于非均匀量子几何（quantum geometry））对填充因子ν=1/3与ν=2/3的分数化态影响不同；能带混合可稳定非公度（incommensurate）晶态基态，并揭示拓扑相与非拓扑晶态相之间的精细竞争。

Conclusion: 在Aharonov-Casher模型中，拓扑分数化相与晶态相存在微妙竞争，其胜负依赖填充因子、量子几何与能带混合强度；神经网络变分方法可扩展至其他方法难以达到的体系尺寸与强能带混合区间。

Abstract: We use neural-network-based variational Monte Carlo to investigate the competition between fractional Chern insulators and generalized Wigner crystals in the Aharonov-Casher Hamiltonian, a continuum model for moiré materials. We introduce a variational wave function inspired by flux attachment that captures fractionalized phases and, by comparing its energy with that of a Slater--Jastrow ansatz that captures crystalline phases, we determine the phase diagram at fractional fillings in regimes of strong band mixing and for system sizes beyond the reach of other methods. We find that discrete translation symmetry, encoded in a nonuniform quantum geometry, affects fractionalized states at filling fractions $ν=1/3$ and $ν=2/3$ differently, and that band mixing can stabilize incommensurate crystalline ground states. Our results further reveal the delicate competition between topological and non-topological crystalline phases.

</details>


### [334] [Reentrant Nematicity from Landau Quantization near a Pomeranchuk Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17150)
*Luís E. Leite Macêdo,Daniel G. Barci*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在零磁场下各向同性的二维费米液体中，朗道量子化可诱导出向列序；磁场变化会产生向列区与各向同性区交替出现，并在德哈斯–范阿尔芬振荡中留下可区分的实验特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在说明朗道量子化（Landau quantization）如何在原本各向同性的二维费米液体中诱导向列序（nematic order），并寻找可用于实验探测的量子振荡特征。

Method: 在四极型波梅兰丘克不稳定性（quadrupolar Pomeranchuk instability）附近采用平均场理论（mean-field theory）分析磁场驱动的向列转变及其对费米面形变的影响。

Result: 随磁场变化体系呈现向列区与各向同性区的交替，转变既可为连续型也可为一级；德哈斯–范阿尔芬（de Haas–van Alphen）振荡中，固定化学势时频率可显著改变而回旋质量（cyclotron mass）变化较小，固定粒子密度时费米面面积守恒使主频保持不变，但回旋质量与谐波振幅仍对向列序敏感。

Conclusion: 这些对比行为提供了场诱导向列性（field-induced nematicity）的实验相关特征，并表明热力学约束（thermodynamic constraints）在量子振荡测量中具有重要性。

Abstract: Landau quantization can induce nematic order in a two-dimensional Fermi liquid that is isotropic at zero magnetic field. Using a mean-field theory near a quadrupolar Pomeranchuk instability, we show that varying the magnetic field produces alternating nematic and isotropic regions, with transitions that can be either continuous or first order. The resulting nematic state also leaves distinctive signatures in the de Haas--van Alphen oscillations. At fixed chemical potential, the field-dependent deformation of the Fermi surface can produce substantial changes in the oscillation frequency, even when the cyclotron mass varies only modestly. At fixed particle density, conservation of the Fermi-surface area preserves the leading oscillation frequency, while the cyclotron mass and harmonic amplitudes remain sensitive to nematic order. These contrasting behaviors provide experimentally relevant signatures of field-induced nematicity and reveal the importance of thermodynamic constraints in quantum-oscillation measurements.

</details>


### [335] [The de Haas--van Alphen effect in a two-dimensional nematic Fermi fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17166)
*Luís E. Leite Macêdo,Daniel G. Barci*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在垂直磁场下用均匀平均场近似研究可产生向列相（nematic）序的二维相互作用电子模型，揭示朗道量子化（Landau quantization）与向列序的双向耦合：磁场可促进或抑制向列序，而向列序又改变 de Haas–van Alphen 振荡（de Haas–van Alphen oscillations）的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子振荡（quantum oscillations）常用于探测金属费米面（Fermi surface），但当电子相互作用可自发使费米面形变时其行为变得复杂；本文旨在厘清量子振荡与电子序之间的相互作用机制。

Method: 采用垂直磁场下的均匀平均场近似（uniform mean-field approximation），研究一个具备向列序（nematic order）能力的二维相互作用电子模型，并分析其与 Pomeranchuk 不稳定性（Pomeranchuk instability）的关系。

Result: 随磁场变化，朗道量子化可促进或抑制向列序，在 Pomeranchuk 不稳定性附近产生交替的各向同性（isotropic）与向列相区域，并伴随连续与一阶相变（first-order transitions）。向列序反过来改变 de Haas–van Alphen 振荡：固定化学势（chemical potential）时振荡频率可显著变化而回旋质量（cyclotron mass）仅小幅改变；固定粒子密度时费米面面积守恒保持主导的 Onsager 频率，但回旋质量与谐波振幅（harmonic amplitudes）仍对向列序敏感；磁化强度（magnetization）与磁化率（magnetic susceptibility）提供额外特征信号。

Conclusion: 量子振荡既能揭示也能影响电子序，本文结果为此类强关联金属（strongly correlated metals）实验的解释提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Quantum oscillations are widely used to probe the Fermi surface of metals, but their behavior becomes more complex when electronic interactions can spontaneously deform that surface. We investigate this interplay in a two-dimensional model of interacting electrons capable of developing nematic order, using a uniform mean-field approximation in a perpendicular magnetic field. We show that Landau quantization can favor or suppress nematic order as the field varies, producing alternating isotropic and nematic regions near a Pomeranchuk instability, with both continuous and first-order transitions. Conversely, the evolving nematic order modifies the de Haas--van Alphen oscillations in ways that depend on the thermodynamic conditions. At fixed chemical potential, the oscillation frequency can change significantly even when the cyclotron mass varies only modestly. At fixed particle density, conservation of the Fermi-surface area preserves the leading Onsager frequency, while the cyclotron mass and harmonic amplitudes remain sensitive to nematic order. The magnetization and magnetic susceptibility provide additional signatures of the nematic transitions. These results show how quantum oscillations can both reveal and influence electronic order, offering a framework for interpreting experiments in strongly correlated metals.

</details>


### [336] [Optimizing Chern Bands for Moore-Read States: A Parent Hamiltonian Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17188)
*Bishoy M. Kousa,Yung-Chun Chao,Jung-Jung Su,Allan H. MacDonald*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一套通过优化相互作用以稳定零磁场下非阿贝尔摩尔-里德（Moore-Read）态的框架，以带状投影三粒子谱与理想摩尔-里德母哈密顿量的距离定义代价函数，并应用于朗道能级与扭转MoTe$_2$的陈带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩尔材料中分数量子反常霍尔态（fractional quantum anomalous Hall states）的实现，使在无磁场条件下稳定非阿贝尔拓扑序（non-Abelian topological order）成为可能且具有研究动机。

Method: 将带状投影的三粒子谱（band-projected three-particle spectra）与由三体相互作用定义（three-body interaction）的摩尔-里德母哈密顿量（Moore-Read parent Hamiltonian）谱进行对比，并定义代价函数（cost function）量化体系接近理想摩尔-里德极限的程度。

Result: 该框架被应用于朗道能级（Landau levels）以及扭转MoTe$_2$的陈带（Chern bands），用于评估各体系中相互作用偏离理想摩尔-里德极限的程度。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种以代价函数为判据优化相互作用、从而趋向非阿贝尔摩尔-里德态的通用途径，并可在真实摩尔材料陈带中实施。

Abstract: The recent realization of fractional quantum anomalous Hall states in moiré materials motivates efforts to stabilize non-Abelian topological order in the absence of a magnetic field. Here, we propose a framework for optimizing interactions to favor the non-Abelian Moore-Read state. Our approach compares band-projected three-particle spectra to that of the Moore-Read parent Hamiltonian, which is defined by a three-body interaction, and quantifies the comparison by defining a cost function that estimates proximity to the ideal Moore-Read limit. We apply our approach to Landau levels and to the Chern bands of twisted MoTe$_2$.

</details>


### [337] [Cubic, Non-Fermi Liquid Exchange Couplings for Doublet Impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17192)
*Anna I. Tóth,Davide Marenduzzo,Andrew D. Huxley*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文构建了立方金属中双重态杂质（doublet impurities）的非费米液体（non-Fermi liquid, NFL）量子杂质模型，涉及总角动量 $J$ 为半整数的传导电子，并指出仅两种受晶体对称性保护的NFL杂质模型，同时构造了两种非对称性保护的立方模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于系统厘清立方金属环境中双重态杂质所对应的非费米液体量子杂质模型，并区分哪些NFL行为受晶体对称性保护、哪些不受保护，从而为后续求解新模型奠定基础。

Method: 采用构建量子杂质模型的方法：考虑半整数总角动量 $J$ 的传导电子，识别受晶体对称性保护的NFL模型，并显式验证四极Kondo模型（quadrupolar Kondo model）保持时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）；另外构造两个同时由 $\Gamma_8$ 与 $\Gamma_6$ 或 $\Gamma_7$ 传导电子屏蔽杂质的立方模型。

Result: 确认仅存在两种受晶体对称性保护的NFL量子杂质模型：自旋半杂质与 $J=3/2$ 传导电子的Kondo模型，以及源自四极Kondo模型的二通道Kondo模型（two-channel Kondo model）；所构造的两个立方模型中，一个对应非Kramers双重态（non-Kramers doublet），另一个对应Kramers双重态（Kramers doublet）杂质基态，后者据作者所知此前未被考虑。

Conclusion: 作者预期Kramers双重态模型可实现一个新的NFL不动点（fixed point），但其求解留待未来工作；此外推导了驱动非对称性保护系统偏离其NFL标度区（scaling regime）的相关立方微扰（cubic perturbation）。

Abstract: We construct non-Fermi liquid (NFL) quantum impurity models for doublet impurities in a cubic metal, involving conduction electrons with half-integer total angular momentum, $J$. We highlight the only two crystal-symmetry-protected NFL quantum impurity models: the spin-half impurity, $J=3/2$ conduction electron Kondo model, and the two-channel Kondo model originally derived in the context of the quadrupolar Kondo model. We show explicitly that the quadrupolar Kondo model preserves time-reversal symmetry. We also construct two cubic quantum impurity models in which NFL behavior is not symmetry-protected. Each has $Γ_8$ and either $Γ_{6}$ or $Γ_{7}$ conduction electrons screening the impurity simultaneously, one model for a non-Kramers doublet and the other for a Kramers doublet impurity ground state. To our knowledge, the latter model has not been considered before; we expect it to realize a new NFL fixed point, but its solution is left for future work. Finally, we derive the relevant cubic perturbation that drives the non-symmetry-protected systems away from their NFL scaling regimes.

</details>


### [338] [Integer and Fractional Chern Plateaus Shaped by Layer-Dependent Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17217)
*Ziyue Qi,Zhan Wang,Hongming Weng,Kun Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文以扭转双层 MoTe₂ 的三轨道真实模型为平台，研究层依赖无序（layer-dependent disorder）如何通过布洛赫波函数（Bloch wave functions）的层组分影响整数与分数量子陈绝缘体的霍尔平台宽度与转变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩尔（moiré）体系中整数与分数量子陈绝缘体（Chern insulators）的实现，为探究布洛赫波函数结构如何影响拓扑与无序（disorder）的相互作用提供了晶格平台；但无序强度之外的层分辨结构所起作用尚需澄清。

Method: 采用扭转双层 MoTe₂ 的真实三轨道模型（three-orbital model），比较在位势（onsite-potential）、位移场（displacement-field）与扭转角（twist-angle）三类无序下霍尔输运与体态输运，并借助复合费米子平均场理论（composite-fermion mean-field theory）分析分数量子化。

Result: 在相同裸无序强度下，在位势无序给出宽整数平台且转变近乎直接；位移场无序则产生由导电区隔开的窄平台并伴随显著霍尔电阻凹陷。差异源于布洛赫态的层组分：位移场无序下不同层的贡献部分抵消，削弱体态局域化（bulk localization）。复合费米子理论进一步在 ν_h=2/3 与 3/5 给出分数量子平台，其平台宽度呈现相同的无序依赖。

Conclusion: 层依赖无序是决定摩尔体系中陈平台（Chern plateau）稳定性的重要机制，其作用不能仅由裸无序强度刻画。

Abstract: The realization of integer and fractional Chern insulators in moiré systems provides a lattice setting for exploring how the structure of Bloch wave functions influences the interplay between topology and disorder. Here we investigate how layer-dependent disorder shapes Hall plateau widths and transitions. Using a realistic three-orbital model of twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$, we compare Hall and bulk transport under onsite-potential, displacement-field, and twist-angle disorder. At equal bare disorder strength, onsite-potential disorder produces broad integer plateaus with nearly direct transitions, whereas displacement-field disorder yields narrow plateaus separated by conducting regions with pronounced Hall-resistance dips. These contrasting behaviors arise from the layer composition of the Bloch states: contributions from different layer under displacement-field disorder partially cancel, weakening the bulk localization. Composite-fermion mean-field theory further yields fractional plateaus at $ν_h=2/3$ and $3/5$ with the same disorder dependence of plateau widths. These findings identify layer-dependent disorder as an important mechanism shaping Chern plateau stability in moiré systems beyond bare disorder strength alone.

</details>


### [339] [Long-lived light-driven bimagnons at finite momentum in NiO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17335)
*Xian P. Yang,Christopher T. Parzyck,Martin Cross,Ella Blake,Octave Duros,Yinchuan Lv,Hari Paudyal,Che-Min Lin,Sharon S. Philip,Lingjia Shen,Daniel Jost,Giacomo Coslovich,Joshua J. Turner,Choongwon Seo,Elyse Schriber,Michael E. Flatté,Xiaoqin Li,Yuri Suzuki,Durga Paudyal,Harold Y. Hwang,Georgi L. Dakovski,Matthias C. Hoffmann,Wei-Sheng Lee,Alexander H. Reid*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用中红外脉冲共振泵浦NiO的声子辅助双磁振子吸收，并通过时间分辨共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）在有限动量下探测磁性动力学，发现光诱导的双磁振子信号寿命达28±11 ps，比此前报道的双磁振子退相位时间长两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体中光可产生关联磁振子对（双磁振子，bimagnon），但相较单磁振子阻尼已有细致测量与建模，双磁振子的寿命、相互作用与衰变仍知之甚少；已有双磁振子测量显示线宽宽或相干在200 fs内退相位。

Method: 以中红外脉冲（远低于带隙）泵浦NiO的声子辅助双磁振子吸收，并用时间分辨共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）在有限动量下探测光激发后的磁动力学。

Result: 光激发后单磁振子线宽与能量基本不变，表明长程磁序在无光载流子诱导退磁的情况下得以保持；谱能量增益（反斯托克斯，anti-Stokes）侧出现泵浦诱导的双磁振子信号，其谱权重在泵浦后4 ps达峰（远超0.5 ps时间分辨率），提示磁振子对在布里渊区内动量重分布导致延迟增长；该信号以28±11 ps时间常数衰减。

Conclusion: 结果确立了超越单磁振子态的长寿命光驱动双磁振子关联，类似超冷气体中测得的高阶关联，表明光可在超快时间尺度上操控磁序及其激发。

Abstract: Light can manipulate magnetic order and its excitations on ultrafast timescales, including through resonantly driven phonons. In antiferromagnets, light can also create correlated magnon pairs, or bimagnons. Unlike single magnons, whose damping has been measured and modeled in detail, little is known about the lifetime, interactions and decay of such correlated pairs: prior two-magnon measurements show broad linewidths or coherences that dephase within 200 fs. Here we pump the phonon-assisted two-magnon absorption of NiO with mid-infrared pulses, far below its bandgap, and probe the resulting magnetic dynamics at finite momentum with time-resolved resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS). Upon photoexcitation, the one-magnon linewidth and energy are essentially unchanged, indicating that the long-range magnetic order is preserved without the photocarrier-induced demagnetization characteristic of above-bandgap excitation. A pump-induced two-magnon signal appears on the energy-gain (anti-Stokes) side of the spectrum. Its spectral weight peaks 4 ps after the pump, well beyond our 0.5 ps time resolution, indicative of a delayed growth likely due to momentum redistribution of the light-induced magnon pairs across the Brillouin zone. This two-magnon signal decays with a time constant of $28\pm11$ ps, exceeding the previously reported two-magnon dephasing time by more than two orders of magnitude. These results establish long-lived light-driven bimagnon correlations beyond the one-magnon state, akin to the higher-order correlations measured in ultracold gases.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [340] [Regularization Independence in the One-Dimensional Schrödinger Equation with Delta Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14843)
*Alex Czemerinski,Jonathan Nunes,Sergei V. Shabanov*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对一类正则化（regularization）$\{\delta_\varepsilon\}$ 证明：在固定能量下，Hamiltonian $H_\varepsilon=-\frac{d^2}{dx^2}-\lambda\delta_\varepsilon$ 的透射系数（transmission coefficient）与反射系数（reflection coefficient）收敛于 delta 相互作用对应的值；在 $\delta_\varepsilon$ 有界时给出负本征值（negative eigenvalues）的显式收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于尺度化单一固定剖面（scaling a single fixed profile）所得正则化族的散射矩阵收敛结果，而 Brasche–Figari–Teta 的范数预解收敛（norm resolvent convergence）本身不足以推出散射矩阵收敛，因此需要把结论推广到更一般的正则化类。

Method: 所研究的正则化类仅要求一致 $L^1$ 界（uniform $L^1$ bound）、归一化 $\int\delta_\varepsilon\to1$ 以及截断的一阶矩条件（truncated first-moment condition），对符号、形状、支集（support）与标度不作限制。对透射/反射系数证明收敛，并在有界性附加假设下证明束缚态（bound state）收敛，均借助 $\delta_\varepsilon$ 的两个可直接计算的积分给出显式速率。

Result: 固定能量下透射与反射系数收敛于 delta 相互作用的值。当每个 $\delta_\varepsilon$ 有界且 $\lambda>0$ 时，任何与零有正距离的负本征值族收敛到 $-\lambda^2/4$；当 $\lambda<0$ 时不存在此类族。两类收敛均带显式速率。

Conclusion: 本文对严格更大的正则化类证明了散射矩阵收敛，扩大了此前仅适用于单剖面尺度化族的结论；束缚态收敛部分则给出基于 Brasche–Figari–Teta 结果的初等证明及显式速率。

Abstract: We consider a class of regularizations $\{δ_\varepsilon\}$ of the Dirac delta defined by a uniform $L^1$ bound, the normalization $\intδ_\varepsilon\to1$, and a truncated first-moment condition, with no restriction on sign, shape, support, or scaling. For this class we prove that the transmission and reflection coefficients for the Hamiltonian operator $H_\varepsilon=-\frac{d^2}{dx^2}-λδ_\varepsilon$ at fixed energy converge to those of the delta interaction. Under the additional assumption that each $δ_\varepsilon$ is bounded, we show that any family of negative eigenvalues of $H_\varepsilon$ bounded away from zero converges to $-λ^2/4$ when $λ>0$, and that no such family exists when $λ<0$. Both convergences are obtained with an explicit rate, expressed through two integrals of $δ_\varepsilon$, directly computable for a given regularization. The bound-state convergence follows from the work of Brasche, Figari, and Teta; here, our contribution is an elementary proof with an explicit rate. Their norm resolvent convergence, however, does not by itself imply convergence of the scattering matrix, which was previously known for families obtained by scaling a single fixed profile; we prove it for a strictly larger class.

</details>


### [341] [Counting eigenvalues below the lowest Landau level Robin Dirichlet boundary conditions in an annulus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15775)
*Marwa Nasrallah,Iman Almoustafa*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 研究环形区域（annulus）中被强均匀磁场约束的带电量子粒子，在内边界施加狄利克雷条件（Dirichlet condition）、外边界施加罗宾条件（Robin condition），在半经典情形下给出低于最低朗道能级（lowest Landau level）的束缚态数目的渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关注低于平面最低朗道能级的束缚态计数问题，并刻画该计数如何依赖环形区域几何（外边界长度与曲率）以及罗宾参数强弱，同时区分排斥与吸引两类罗宾条件的影响。

Method: 将问题约化为外边界附近的有效一维模型（effective one-dimensional model），并借助角动量分离变量（separation of variables in angular momentum）完成证明。

Result: 在半经典情形（semiclassical regime）得到束缚态数目的渐近公式，显式体现外边界长度、曲率以及罗宾参数强度的影响；并说明吸引情形如何改变产生这些低能态的边界行为。

Conclusion: 低能束缚态计数由环形区域外边界几何与罗宾参数共同决定，排斥与吸引情形下边界机制不同；证明框架基于外边界附近的一维有效模型与角动量分离变量。

Abstract: We study a charged quantum particle confined to an annulus and subject to a strong uniform magnetic field. A Dirichlet condition is imposed on the inner boundary and a Robin condition on the outer boundary. We are interested in the number of bound states whose energy lies below the lowest Landau level, the threshold set by the magnetic field in the whole plane. In the semiclassical regime, we derive an asymptotic formula for this number. It shows how the count depends on the geometry of the annulus, through the length and curvature of the outer boundary, and on the strength of the Robin parameter. We treat both the repulsive and the attractive cases of the Robin condition and describe how the attractive case changes the boundary behavior that produces these low-lying states. The proof reduces the problem to an effective one-dimensional model near the outer boundary and relies on separation of variables in angular momentum.

</details>


<div id='stat.OT'></div>

# stat.OT [[Back]](#toc)

### [342] [Exact power spectra and effective dimensionality in critical block-GOE networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16427)
*Konstantinos Grammenos,Dionissios Hristopulos*

Main category: stat.OT

TL;DR: 该工作针对块高斯正交系综（block-GOE）矩阵建模的振荡神经网络，推导出网络平均功率谱、网络自协方差与归一化有效维度的精确解析解，为理解振荡网络在临界性附近的信息处理特性提供了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界性（criticality）对神经网络最优信息处理至关重要，而近期研究已从静态临界性转向频率依赖行为；但针对block-GOE矩阵所描述振荡系统，预测功率谱（power spectrum）与有效维度（effective dimensionality）等谱性质的解析工具仍然匮乏。

Method: 在块高斯正交系综（block-Gaussian orthogonal ensemble, block-GOE）框架下进行解析推导，给出网络平均功率谱 $\operatorname{Tr}_{N}[\mathbf{S}(\omega)]$、网络自协方差（autocovariance）$\operatorname{Tr}_{N}[\mathbf{C}(\tau)]$ 以及归一化有效维度 $D_N(\omega)$ 的精确解。

Result: 获得上述三个谱量的精确解析表达式，构成理解振荡网络在临界性附近运行方式的系统性框架，并揭示大规模神经系统中稳定性（stability）与灵活性（flexibility）之间的动态平衡。

Conclusion: 所推导的精确解为振荡神经网络的频率依赖临界性研究提供了可用的解析工具，有助于刻画大规模神经系统的动态平衡机制。

Abstract: Criticality in neural networks is crucial for optimal information processing. Recent studies have shifted focus from static criticality to frequency-dependent behavior, particularly in oscillatory systems modeled by block-Gaussian orthogonal ensemble (block-GOE) matrices. However, analytical tools to predict spectral properties such as power spectrum and effective dimensionality remain scarce. In this work, we derive exact solutions for the network-averaged power spectrum $\operatorname{Tr}_{N}[\mathbf{S}(ω)]$, the network autocovariance $\operatorname{Tr}_{N}[\mathbf{C}(τ)]$, and the normalized effective dimensionality $D_N(ω)$ in block-GOE ensembles. These results provide a comprehensive framework for understanding how oscillatory networks operate near criticality, offering insights into the dynamic balance between stability and flexibility in large-scale neural systems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [343] [High-Fidelity In Silico Dataset of Ordered Binary and Ternary MXenes with Predictive Synthesis Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14687)
*Ahmed H. Biby,Charles B. Musgrave*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究基于 r²SCAN 密度泛函理论构建了 1040 个有序二元与三元裸层剥离 MXene 的高保真数据集，计算形成/分解吉布斯能、分解产物、层对称群与凸包上能量，系统评估其热力学稳定性并识别可合成候选与合成条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型 MXene 的定向合成受限于其庞大的结构与组分空间，且缺乏能考虑合成条件的可靠稳定性描述符（stability descriptors），阻碍了数据驱动的材料发现。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）结合 r²SCAN 泛函，构建涵盖单过渡金属、面内有序双过渡金属、面内有序金属-双空位及面外有序双过渡金属四类 MXene 的 1040 个结构数据集，并计算形成与分解吉布斯能（Gibbs energies）、分解产物、层对称群（layer symmetry groups）及凸包上能量（energy above the hull）。

Result: 所有 MXene 在平衡条件下均热力学不可及；但在 500–2000 K 高温下有 115 个以碳化物为主的 MXene 实现热力学稳定，10 GPa 高压下有 23 个稳定，Mn–Fe–C 化学空间中有 3 个在实验可行的碳化学势区间内稳定。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了 MXene 空间的稳定性趋势并提出可指导实验的合成策略，数据集为开发机器学习稳定性描述符奠定基础，并通过给出可合成候选及其合成条件，弥合数据驱动 MXene 发现中的关键缺口，为未来自主实验流程提供支撑。

Abstract: Despite their promise, the targeted synthesis of novel MXenes remains challenging due to their vast structural and compositional space and the lack of reliable stability descriptors that account for synthesis conditions. To address this gap, we utilized density functional theory (DFT) with the r$^2$SCAN functional to develop a high-fidelity dataset of 1,040 ordered binary and ternary bare delaminated MXenes, including mono-transition metal MXenes, in-plane ordered double-transition metal MXenes, in-plane ordered metal-divacancy MXenes, and out-of-plane ordered double-transition-metal MXenes. For each structure, we calculated formation and decomposition Gibbs energies, decomposition products, layer symmetry groups, and energies above the hull. We find that all MXenes are thermodynamically inaccessible under equilibrium conditions. However, 115 predominantly carbide MXenes become thermodynamically stabilized at elevated temperatures (500--2000 K), and 23 are stabilized under high pressure (10 GPa). Additionally, three MXenes in the Mn--Fe--C chemical space are stabilized within an experimentally feasible range of carbon chemical potentials. These findings reveal stability trends across the MXene space and identify synthesis strategies that can guide experimental efforts. Our dataset provides a robust foundation for developing machine-learned stability descriptors and for guiding experimental efforts toward novel MXene discovery. By identifying synthesizable candidates along with their synthesis conditions, this work bridges a critical gap in data-driven approaches to MXene discovery and lays the foundation for future autonomous experimental workflows.

</details>


### [344] [Improving Nonequilibrium Thermal Conduction in CaF2 by Breaking the Phonon Bottleneck Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14957)
*Mingde Qu,Minghuan Song,Yuxing Wang,Wudi Wang,Chenbo Zhang,Jiamin Quan,Jun Xu,Zhongwei Zhang,Jie Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出通过选择性声子激发打破CaF2中由弱传播纵光学声子引起的非平衡声子瓶颈，从而改善激光光学材料的散热。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光光学材料的散热不仅取决于热导率，还取决于吸收能量在各声子模式间的再分配；CaF2中热电子向弱传播纵光学声子（LO phonons）的快速传能会形成非平衡声子瓶颈效应，限制高效排热。

Method: 结合电子-声子散射计算、声子玻尔兹曼输运分析与非平衡分子动力学模拟，并借助三声子散射相空间分析指导选择性激发横声学模式（TA modes）。

Result: LO声子被识别为主要优先接收热载流子能量的模式并因输运与弛豫效率低而能量积累；选择性激发TA模式可加速LO弛豫并向载热声学模式传能。持续LO激发使热导率下降约12%，而额外TA激发相比LO瓶颈受限状态使热传导提升约21%，并超过未激发值。

Conclusion: 控制支间能量再分配可抵消声子瓶颈导致的热积累，为激光光学材料中通过选择性调控声子布居实现主动热管理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Heat dissipation in laser optical materials depends not only on their thermal conductivity but also on how absorbed energy is redistributed among various phonon modes. In calcium fluoride (CaF2), rapid energy transfer from hot electrons to weakly propagating longitudinal optical (LO) phonons can create a nonequilibrium phonon bottleneck effect that limits efficient heat removal. Here, we propose selective phonon excitation as an effective strategy to break this bottleneck. Combining electron-phonon scattering calculations, phonon Boltzmann transport analysis, and nonequilibrium molecular dynamics simulations, we identify LO phonons as the primary modes that preferentially receive energy from hot carriers, leading to energy accumulation due to their inefficient transport and relaxation. Guided by three-phonon scattering phase-space analysis, selective excitation of transverse acoustic (TA) modes can accelerate LO relaxation and promote energy transfer toward heat-carrying acoustic modes. While sustained LO-phonon excitation reduces the thermal conductivity by approximately 12%, additional TA-mode excitation improves heat conduction by approximately 21% compared to the state limited by the LO-phonon bottleneck, surpassing the unexcited value. These findings demonstrate that controlling interbranch energy redistribution can counteract phonon-bottleneck-induced heat accumulation and reveal a working route for active thermal management in laser optical materials through selective control of phonon populations.

</details>


### [345] [Reversal of the Anomalous Hall Effect by Directional In-Plane Electric Field in Strained Bilayer MoO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14982)
*Wenpeng Wang,hang Shi,Xiangru Kong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在双轴应变下的双层 MoO 中，面内电场方向可选择性调控单谷自旋劈裂并反转反常霍尔电导，同时 AC1 堆垛可呈现金属—拓扑绝缘体转变及压磁等应变驱动效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状磁性材料兼具可调谷与自旋自由度，有望用于多功能自旋电子学器件，因此需要一种以外场（应变与电场）协同调控谷选择性自旋劈裂与霍尔响应的手段。

Method: 基于第一性原理类计算（摘要未明示具体方法），考察能量与动力学稳定的 AC2 与 AC1 两种堆垛双层 MoO，分析双轴应变诱导的类狄拉克能带交叉及面内电场对 X、Y 谷自旋简并和贝里曲率分布的影响。

Result: 沿 a 与 b 方向的面内电场分别选择性解除 X 与 Y 谷的自旋简并而保持另一谷不变，并伴随反号贝里曲率重分布，产生可逆的反常霍尔响应；AC1 堆垛则出现从金属到拓扑绝缘体的转变（自旋霍尔电导量子化），以及谷极化与压磁性。

Conclusion: 应变与电场的联合效应可实现谷选择性自旋劈裂与反常霍尔响应的调控，双层 MoO 被视为多功能自旋电子学与谷电子学器件的候选平台。

Abstract: Layered magnetic materials with tunable valley and spin degrees of freedom hold promise for multifunctional spintronic devices. Here, we demonstrate that the orientation of an in-plane electric field enables selective switching of single-valley spin splitting and reversal of the anomalous Hall conductivity in strained bilayer MoO. In particular, electric fields along the a and b directions selectively lift the spin degeneracy at the X and Y valleys, respectively, while preserving it at the other valley. This valley-selective switching is accompanied by a redistribution of Berry curvature with opposite signs, giving rise to a reversible anomalous Hall response. These effects are realized in the energetically and dynamically stable AC2 stacking configuration, where biaxial strain induces Dirac-like band crossings that enable subsequent electric-field control. Meanwhile, the AC1 stacking configuration, which is also energetically and dynamically stable, exhibits rich strain-driven phenomena, including a transition from metal to topological insulator with quantized spin Hall conductivity, as well as valley polarization and piezomagnetism. Our findings reveal a combined effect of strain and electric fields for controlling valley-selective spin splitting and anomalous Hall responses, highlighting bilayer MoO as a promising platform for multifunctional spintronic and valleytronic devices.

</details>


### [346] [Depth determination from core-loss 4D-STEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15037)
*Michael Deimetry*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出利用动量分辨（momentum-resolved）核心损失（core-loss）四维扫描透射电子显微镜（4D-STEM）数据确定掺杂剂（dopant）深度的方法，并借助贝叶斯框架（Bayesian framework）在低计数条件下实现纳米至亚纳米深度精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越传统样品投影显微图像（specimen-projected micrographs）、获取掺杂剂的三维分布，对材料表征与材料开发具有关键意义。

Method: 在玻恩近似（Born approximation）下对探针位置作傅里叶变换（Fourier transformation），由条纹频率编码掺杂剂到焦平面的距离；针对动力学散射（dynamical scattering）导致的条纹对比度下降，采用偏离主晶带轴（major zone axes）采集加以部分缓解；针对核心损失剂量受限，引入贝叶斯框架。

Result: 模拟中仅需100至1000个核心损失计数（core-loss counts），即可达到纳米至亚纳米（nanometre to sub-nanometre）的深度精度，具体取决于信噪比（signal-to-background）。

Conclusion: 该方法为低剂量条件下掺杂剂深度的三维表征提供了可行途径，但深度精度受信噪比与动力学散射影响，需通过离轴采集与贝叶斯推断加以调控。

Abstract: Moving beyond specimen-projected micrographs in scanning transmission electron microscopy to determine the three-dimensional distribution of dopants is of vital importance for materials characterization and development. We present an approach for dopant depth determination using momentum-resolved core-loss four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy data. Within the Born approximation, Fourier transformation over probe position reveals fringes whose frequency encodes the dopant's distance from the focal plane. Two principal challenges arise in extending this approach to realistic experimental conditions. First, dynamical scattering degrades fringe contrast which can be partially mitigated by acquiring off major zone axes. Second, because core-loss doses are punishing, we use a Bayesian framework which, in simulation, achieves nanometre to sub-nanometre depth precision from only 100 to 1000 core-loss counts, depending on the signal-to-background.

</details>


### [347] [Defective $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15060)
*Jorge Vega Bazantes,Timo Lebeda,Zhiqiang Mao,Jianwei Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用 meta-GGA 密度泛函理论研究块体 α-In₂Se₃ 的电子结构及中性点缺陷（空位、反位）在富 Se 与富 In 条件下的形成能，发现中间层 Se 空位在两种化学环境下均具有出人意料的低形成能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-In₂Se₃ 是具铁电应用前景的二维范德华材料，但本征缺陷（native defects）会显著影响其结构与电子性质，因此需明确缺陷形成的规律与微观机制。

Method: 使用密度泛函理论（density functional theory）结合先进的 meta-GGA 交换关联泛函（exchange-correlation functional），计算块体 α-In₂Se₃ 的电子结构，以及 Se 富和 In 富生长条件下中性空位（vacancy）与反位（antisite）缺陷的形成能。

Result: 计算复现了本征材料的实验结构参数；中间层 Se 空位在两种化学环境下形成能异常低，结构分析显示相邻四面体配位 In 原子沿三重轴（three-fold axis）发生显著局域弛豫，配位数由四降至三的同时仍保持 C₃ᵥ 位点对称性。

Conclusion: 该局域弛豫与对称性保持为中间层 Se 空位的异常稳定性提供了微观解释，揭示了缺陷结构与稳定性之间的内在联系。

Abstract: $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ is a two-dimensional van der Waals material with promising applications involving ferroelectricity. However, the presence of native defects can significantly influence its structural and electronic properties. In this work, we employ density functional theory with a state-of-the-art meta-generalized gradient approximation (meta-GGA) exchange-correlation functional to investigate the electronic structure of bulk $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ and the formation of neutral point defects, including vacancies and antisites, under Se-rich and In-rich growth conditions. Our calculations reproduce the experimental structural parameters of the pristine material and reveal that the Se vacancy in the middle layer exhibits an unexpectedly low formation energy under both chemical environments. Structural analysis shows that the neighboring tetrahedrally coordinated In atom undergoes a substantial local relaxation along the three-fold axis, which preserves its $C_{3v}$ site symmetry even as its coordination is reduced from four to three, providing a microscopic explanation for the remarkable stability of this defect.

</details>


### [348] [High-Throughput Surface Screening Reveals Energetically Competitive Low-Symmetry Facets in Wurtzite Semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15138)
*Gustavo Madrigal,Cibrán López,Jordi Llorca,Claudio Cazorla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结合自动化表面生成、机器学习原子间势筛选与密度泛函理论，系统考察纤锌矿GaN、LaN、ZnO的226个表面模型，发现高指数棱柱面在能量上可与常规低指数面竞争，但其形成能接近并不意味着能带对齐相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往第一性原理研究受计算成本与建模复杂度限制，多集中于高对称低指数晶面，而实际中表面形成能由平面原子密度与断裂键强度决定，高密勒指数表面未必不具能量竞争力，因此需系统评估真实可能出现的晶面。

Method: 构建自动化表面生成流程，用机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potential）进行预筛选，再以密度泛函理论（density functional theory）计算；覆盖|h|,|k|,|l|≤3的全部密勒取向及非等价终止，共226个洁净化学计量表面模型；并对关键表面采用杂化泛函（hybrid-functional）计算能带边位置。

Result: 十五个最低能非极性表面中有九个为高指数面；各化合物五个最佳候选面与最小表面能仅差2.5–5.3%。但能量接近未对应电子对齐相似：GaN中最稳定的两个表面形成能仅差0.08 J m⁻²，真空参考带边位置却相差近0.8 eV；LaN中近简并的m面与a面相差0.16 eV。

Conclusion: 形成能相近的纤锌矿晶面可能具有显著不同的电离势与电子亲和能，故需对稳定性与能带对齐进行联合评估；该工作提供了可实际出现晶面的候选图谱，支撑纳米结构与异质界面的理性设计。

Abstract: Surfaces and interfaces govern the performance of wurtzite semiconductors in electronics, sensing, and solar-fuel conversion. To date, first-principles studies have focused mostly on high-symmetry low-index facets, owing to the computational cost and complexity of modelling low-symmetry high-index surfaces. In practice, however, surface formation energy is determined by the planar atomic density and the strength of the bonds severed upon cleavage, neither of which inherently excludes high-Miller-index surfaces from being energetically competitive. Here, we combine automated surface generation, machine-learning interatomic potential screening, and density functional theory to explore 226 clean, stoichiometric surface models of wurtzite GaN, LaN, and ZnO, spanning all Miller orientations with $|h|,|k|,|l| \le 3$ and their inequivalent terminations. We find that low-symmetry high-index prismatic facets are energetically competitive with respect to the conventional $m$- and $a$-planes: nine of the fifteen lowest-energy nonpolar surfaces are high-index, and the five best candidates of each compound lie within only 2.5-5.3% of the minimum surface energy. Hybrid-functional calculations reveal, however, that this energetic proximity does not translate into similar electronic alignment. In GaN, the two most stable surfaces differ by just 0.08 J m$^{-2}$ in formation energy but by almost 0.8 eV in vacuum-referenced band-edge positions, whereas in LaN the nearly degenerate $m$- and $a$-planes are separated by 0.16 eV. Wurtzite facets with similar formation energies may therefore exhibit very different ionization potentials and electron affinities. Our results enable the joint, systematic assessment of stability and band alignment across the facets that can actually occur in wurtzites, and provide a map of candidate surfaces for the rational design of related nanostructure and heterointerfaces.

</details>


### [349] [Probing Unknown Luttinger-Like Parameters and Band Structure in BAs: A 24-Band k.p and DFT Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15186)
*Mohsen Jemai,Nouha Mastour,Omar Zitouni,Tarek Barhoumi,Said Ridene*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作采用24带k·p方法与DFT理论计算并分析了砷化硼（BAs）p型导带的电子能带结构及未知的Luttinger类参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 砷化硼（BAs）p型导带相关的Luttinger类参数尚未确定，且需要一种比DFT更高效的方法来模拟基于BAs的复杂纳米结构与量子阱。

Method: 使用24带k·p理论，并结合基于CP2K代码的高斯基与平面波基密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，对哈密顿量参数进行调整与验证。

Result: 与DFT及已有实验结果对比表明，24带k·p模型能精确再现BAs的电子能带结构与关键能带参数，有效质量等参数与已发表理论研究一致。

Conclusion: k·p方法在计算效率上优于DFT，适合用于基于BAs半导体的纳米结构与量子阱建模。

Abstract: In this work, the electronic band structure and the unknown Luttinger-like parameters associated with the p-type conduction bands (CBs) of boron arsenide (BAs) are calculated and analyzed using both 24-band $k.p$ and density functional (DFT) theories. For the DFT study, Gaussian and plane-wave basis sets implemented in the CP2K code were used. Comparison with DFT and available experimental results shows that the 24-band k.p model accurately reproduces the known electronic properties of BAs. These results demonstrate that the 24-band k.p model accurately reproduces the electronic band structure and key band parameters over the investigated energy range. In addition, the 24-band $k.p$ Hamiltonian parameters are adjusted and we show that the effective masses and the band parameters are validated against previously published theoretical studies. These findings suggest that the $k.p$ method is computationally more efficient than DFT, making it suitable for modeling complex nanostructures and quantum wells based on BAs semiconductor.

</details>


### [350] [Competing Octahedral Tilting and $B$-Site Off-Centering Govern the Phase Diagram of CsGe$_{1-x}$Sn$_x$Br$_3$ Perovskite Alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15200)
*Apinya Ngoipala,Erik Fransson,Paul Erhart,Julia Wiktor*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用基于杂化泛函数据训练的电量感知机器学习原子间势（charge-aware machine-learned interatomic potential）开展大规模分子动力学模拟，绘制了 CsGe$_{1-x}$Sn$_x$Br$_3$ 全组分相图，揭示 Ge 孤对电子驱动的极性偏心与 Sn 诱导的八面体倾转之间的竞争，并在低温柔性相界处发现新中间相与带隙跃变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无铅卤化物钙钛矿（lead-free halide perovskites）以 Ge、Sn 替代 Pb 可规避毒性，但两种阳离子倾向不同的结构畸变模式，其竞争机制在全组分范围内尚不清晰，制约了对极性序与旋转序的调控。

Method: 采用在杂化泛函（hybrid-functional）数据上训练的电量感知机器学习原子间势，对 CsGe$_{1-x}$Sn$_x$Br$_3$ 全组分范围进行大规模分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟，构建成分—温度相图并计算带隙。

Result: 低温下合金在 $x=0.60$ 至 $0.65$ 之间呈现柔性相界（morphotropic phase boundary），分隔铁电单斜 $Cc$ 相与兼具反极性与极性偏心的单斜 $P2_1$ 相，倾转模式与偏心序均发生不连续变化；相图重现了两端元的已知转变序列与温度，并在相界附近揭示出两端元均不具备的中间四方 $I4/mcm$ 相；转变温度以上 $B$ 位阳离子仍保持局域偏心但取向无序。

Conclusion: 相界处的结构转变导致低温带隙跃变，而 350 K 时带隙随 Sn 含量平滑下降；结果表明 $B$ 位混合（$B$-site mixing）可作为调控无铅卤化物钙钛矿中极性序与旋转序的有效手段。

Abstract: Lead-free halide perovskites based on Ge and Sn avoid the toxicity of Pb, but the two cations favor different structural distortions. The stereochemically active Ge $4s^2$ lone pair drives polar $B$-site off-centering, whereas the larger Sn$^{2+}$ cation favors octahedral tilting. Here, we map how these two instabilities compete across the full composition range of CsGe$_{1-x}$Sn$_x$Br$_3$ using large-scale molecular dynamics simulations with a charge-aware machine-learned interatomic potential trained on hybrid-functional data. At low temperature the alloy exhibits a morphotropic phase boundary between $x=0.60$ and $0.65$ that separates a ferroelectric monoclinic $Cc$ phase from a monoclinic $P2_1$ phase with mixed antipolar and polar off-centering, with both the tilt pattern and the off-centering order changing discontinuously. The composition-temperature phase diagram reproduces the known transition sequences and temperatures of both end members and reveals an intermediate tetragonal $I4/mcm$ phase near the boundary that has no counterpart in either parent compound. Above the transition temperatures the $B$ cations remain locally off-centered but orientationally disordered. The structural transition at the boundary causes a jump in the band gap at low temperature, whereas at $350$ K the gap decreases smoothly with Sn content. These results establish $B$-site mixing as a means to tune polar and rotational order in lead-free halide perovskites.

</details>


### [351] [Periodic Trends in Oxygen Vacancy Formation Energies Across Binary Main-Group Metal Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15297)
*Manish Kumar,Bridget S. Ferris,Avital Isakov,Robert B. Wexler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文对39种二元主族金属氧化物多晶型的中性氧空位形成能进行了系统的r²SCAN密度泛函理论（density functional theory）调研，发现形成能跨度约8 eV，并随族序下降、随氧化态升高而降低；仅用晶体还原电位与带隙两个描述符即可在留出集上以0.31 eV的平均绝对误差复现形成能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三元钙钛矿（perovskite）的氧空位（oxygen vacancy）形成能已有系统标定，但作为其概念与热化学构建单元的二元氧化物（binary oxides）数据分散、计算方法不一，缺乏可比基准，因而需要统一方法下的系统研究。

Method: 采用r²SCAN密度泛函理论，对涵盖第1、2、13、14、15族的39种二元主族金属氧化物多晶型计算中性氧空位形成能，并用ShakeNBreak方法定位重构后的缺陷基态；随后拟合一个输入为完整体相性质的可解释晶体特征模型（crystal feature model），并进行完整Born-Haber循环拟合。

Result: 形成能跨度达8 eV，沿族向下递减，且在多数多价阳离子中随氧化态升高而降低，两种趋势均与阳离子还原性（reducibility）一致；仅保留晶体还原电位（crystal reduction potential）与带隙两个主导描述符时，模型留出集平均绝对误差为0.31 eV，优于过渡金属钙钛矿的四描述符母模型；完整Born-Haber拟合中的键断裂项与稳定性项在二元氧化物中被证明统计上不显著。

Conclusion: 该数据集为主族氧化物的机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potentials）提供基准；所建模型可仅凭体相性质估算氧空位形成能，最大残差则标示出电子容纳偏离“阳离子还原”图像的材料。

Abstract: The function of oxide materials, from solid-oxide fuel cells to solar thermochemical fuel production, depends on their oxygen vacancies. Their formation energetics have been mapped systematically for ternary perovskites, but reported values for the binary oxides, their conceptual and thermochemical building blocks, remain scattered across studies with differing computational methods. Here we report a systematic r$^2$SCAN density functional theory survey of neutral oxygen vacancy formation energies across 39 binary main-group metal oxide polymorphs spanning groups 1, 2, 13, 14, and 15, using the ShakeNBreak method to locate reconstructed defect ground states. The formation energies span 8 eV and decrease down each group and, for most multivalent cations, with increasing oxidation state. Both trends follow the reducibility of the cation. An interpretable crystal feature model is fit to these energies; its inputs are properties of the pristine bulk, with the reduction potential alternatively taken from tabulated thermochemistry. Retaining only its two dominant descriptors, the crystal reduction potential and the band gap, the model reproduces the formation energies with a held-out mean absolute error of 0.31 eV, lower than that of its four-descriptor parent model for transition-metal perovskites. The bond-breaking and stability terms of the full Born-Haber fit prove statistically null for the binary oxides, and the largest residuals identify the hosts where electron accommodation departs from the cation-reduction picture that the descriptors encode. The dataset provides a benchmark for machine-learning interatomic potentials, and the model estimates vacancy formation energies of main-group oxides from bulk properties alone.

</details>


### [352] [Anisotropy driven critical phenomena in a van der Waals ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15380)
*Neesha Yadav,Harish Chandr Chauhan,Pintu Das*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了范德华铁磁体（van der Waals ferromagnet）\(\mathrm{Fe_3GeTe_2}\) 的场依赖临界行为（field-dependent critical behavior），揭示磁场方向、磁晶各向异性（magnetocrystalline anisotropy）与竞争性能量尺度共同调制临界指数（critical exponents）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在阐明外磁场（\(B\)）、强磁晶各向异性及新出现的其他能量尺度如何调制 \(\mathrm{Fe_3GeTe_2}\) 的临界行为，并说明在非平庸范德华磁体中开展场依赖临界行为研究的必要性，以超越传统固定框架的分析方式。

Method: 采用新近发展的归一化斜率平均法（average of normalized slope, ANS）分析临界行为，在最高至 \(7\,\mathrm{T}\) 的不同磁场窗口内评估临界指数 \(β\)、\(γ\) 与 \(δ\)，并分别考察磁场平行易轴（\(B\parallel c\)）与面内难轴（\(B\perp c\)）两种构型。

Result: 沿易轴加场时，体系表现出由双交换（double-exchange）与巡游 RKKY 机制竞争主导的长程相互作用，该行为随 \(B\) 增大被系统性抑制并趋近长程三维伊辛普适类（3D Ising universality class，\(β\sim0.330\)、\(γ\sim1.00\)）；沿面内难轴加场时，在测量磁场范围内出现 \(γ<1\) 的亚单位（sub-unity）区域，归因于外场塞曼能（Zeeman energy）与强面外磁晶各向异性的强烈竞争。

Conclusion: 非平庸范德华磁体的临界行为本质上受磁场取向与磁晶各向异性强度调制，表明相较于传统框架，有必要开展场依赖的临界行为研究。

Abstract: We report a detailed investigation into the field-dependent critical behavior of the van der Waals ferromagnet $\mathrm{Fe_3GeTe_2}$ to elucidate how applied magnetic fields ($B$), strong magnetocrystalline anisotropy, and emerging other energy scales modulate its critical behavior. Utilizing the recently developed method, viz., the average of normalized slope (ANS), for analysis of critical behavior, we evaluate the critical exponents ($β$, $γ$, and $δ$) across distinct field windows up to $\rm{7\,T}$. For $B$ applied along the crystal's easy axis ($B\parallel c$), the system exhibits long-range interactions dominated by competing double-exchange and itinerant Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) mechanisms, which is systematically suppressed with increasing $B$ to converge toward the long-range 3D Ising universality class ($β\sim0.330$, $γ\sim1.00$). Conversely, under an in-plane hard-axis configuration ($B\perp c$), the critical behavior study exhibits a sub-unity regime ($γ<1$) across the measured range of $B$. This behavior is attributed to the intense competitive interplay between the external Zeeman energy and strong out-of-plane magnetocrystalline anisotropy. Our findings demonstrate that critical behavior in nontrivial van der Waals magnets is fundamentally modulated by the orientation of $B$ and the strength of magnetocrystalline anisotropy, demonstrating the necessity of $B$-dependent critical behavior investigation over conventional frameworks.

</details>


### [353] [Materials research with a general-purpose agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15451)
*Paulo R. Pires,Théo Cavignac,Tiago F. T. Cerqueira,Hai-Chen Wang,Silvana Botti,Miguel A. L. Marques*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将约1500万条密度泛函（density-functional）计算、生成模型、通用原子间势、图神经网络与反应图整合给通用语言模型智能体（language-model agent），使智能体能从自然语言问题出发，自主完成材料筛选、候选剔除与实验方案设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望验证：将大规模材料数据库、训练好的模型与反应图（reaction graph）交给通用智能体后，能否完成任何单一组件无法独立完成的任务，即从一句话问题走到实验室可执行的实验。

Method: 构建并开源Alexandria数据库（含PBE、PBEsol、SCAN、TB-mBJ结果，最高150 GPa弛豫结构、介电张量、约14.5万条电子-声子计算及超4亿条机器学习势弛豫结构），据此训练晶体结构生成模型、通用原子间势与十余种性质的图神经网络，并由凸包（convex hull）计算17.4亿个反应的图；随后让通用语言模型智能体无人值守地调用这些资源。

Result: 智能体自主完成太阳能吸收材料筛选，剔除合成放能过低的候选，并给出含前驱体、坩埚与气氛的合成方案；在Ba-Zr-S体系中发现有未报道的硫化物并设计验证实验；应要求设计空气稳定的替代材料并用生成模型的新结构进行检验。仅靠网络搜索时，同一智能体只能复述文献已有讨论。

Conclusion: 数据、模型与反应图的组合使通用智能体具备单一组件不具备的能力，可实现从自然语言提问到实验室可运行、可检验实验的端到端闭环，表明智能体可作为材料发现的集成式研究工具。

Abstract: Over the last decade we have built Alexandria, an open database of about 15 million density-functional calculations of crystals. It includes PBE, PBEsol, SCAN and TB-mBJ results, structures relaxed at pressures up to 150 GPa, dielectric tensors and about 145,000 electron-phonon calculations, together with more than 400 million structures relaxed with machine-learned potentials. On these data we trained a generative model for crystal structures, a universal interatomic potential and graph neural networks for more than ten properties, from band gaps to superconducting critical temperatures. From the convex hull we computed a reaction graph of the 17.4 billion reactions between its 193,416 stable compounds. Here we give all of this to a general-purpose language-model agent. Working unattended, the agent screens the database for a solar absorber, drops a candidate whose synthesis releases too little energy, and writes a synthesis plan with precursors, crucible and atmosphere. Asked about the well-studied Ba-Zr-S system, it finds an unreported sulfide and designs the experiment that would confirm or rule it out. Asked for an air-stable alternative, it designs one and tests it against new structures from the generative model. With web search alone, the same agent proposes what the literature already discusses. Put together, the data, the models and the reaction graph let a general agent do what none of them does alone: go from a question in plain words to an experiment that a laboratory can run and test.

</details>


### [354] [Defect Topology Governs the Optical Response of Doped Amorphous Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15478)
*Ridwan Hussein,Chinonso Ugwumadu,Louise A. M. Rosset,Roxanne M. Tutchton*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用混合泛函密度泛函理论（hybrid-functional density functional theory）与线性响应（linear-response）计算，考察四种结构不同的连续无规网络（continuous random networks）在硼、磷替位掺杂下的光学响应，指出缺陷拓扑（defect topology）而非仅掺杂浓度决定掺杂敏感性与介电扰动特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶硅（amorphous silicon, a-Si）中缺陷拓扑与化学掺杂（chemical doping）对光学响应的影响此前尚未厘清，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用混合泛函密度泛函理论（hybrid-functional density functional theory）和线性响应（linear-response）计算，构建四种结构上不同的连续无规网络（continuous random networks），并分别进行硼（boron）与磷（phosphorus）的替位掺杂（substitutional doping）比较。

Result: 计算结果再现了关键实验趋势，表明掺杂敏感性不仅取决于缺陷浓度，还取决于缺陷的性质与空间组织；完全伸展的四配位（4-fold coordination）构型对掺杂的介电扰动最强，而含3配位与5配位缺陷（总缺陷分数7.4%）的网络则反转了通常的硼主导响应，对磷更敏感。

Conclusion: Tauc分析与光学求和规则（optical sum-rule）分析表明网络拓扑决定整体光学图景，掺杂主要影响带隙附近态并重新分配谱权重；据此确立缺陷拓扑（defect topology）作为连接非晶结构、掺杂活性与光学响应的微观控制参数。

Abstract: The influence of defect topology and chemical doping on the optical response of amorphous silicon (a-Si) remains unresolved. In this study, hybrid-functional density functional theory and linear-response calculations are employed to examine four structurally distinct continuous random networks with substitutional boron and phosphorus doping. The calculated optical properties reproduce key experimental trends and demonstrate that dopant sensitivity is determined not only by defect concentration, but also by the character and spatial organization of defects. A configuration with fully extended 4-fold coordination shows the strongest dielectric perturbation to doping, while dopant effects in networks with pre-existing defect states are more varied. For most networks, the dielectric response is more strongly perturbed by boron than phosphorus. Notably, a network having both 3- and 5-fold defects with a total defect fraction of 7.4% reverses the typical boron-dominant response of other cases, becoming more sensitive to phosphorus. Tauc and optical sum-rule analyses further indicate that network topology defines the broader optical landscape, whereas doping primarily affects states near the gap and redistributes spectral weight. These findings establish defect topology as a microscopic control parameter that links amorphous structure, dopant activity, and optical response in amorphous silicon.

</details>


### [355] [Competing lattice instabilities in epitaxially strained BaSnO3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15550)
*Benyao Sun,Anna Grünebohm*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文利用密度泛函理论（density functional theory）绘制双轴压应变下BaSnO3的晶格动力学图景，揭示其相稳定性由多种晶格失稳模式的竞争及非谐耦合共同决定，而非仅由极性模软化推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规做法常以极性模（polar mode）软化判断应变下钙钛矿相稳定性，但该判据可能不足；研究旨在厘清BaSnO3在双轴压应变下各类结构失稳的层级关系。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论计算双轴压应变下BaSnO3的晶格动力学，并进行双模能量面（two-mode energy-surface）分析，考察极性序参与转动序参量之间的相互作用及非谐耦合（anharmonic coupling）与结构弛豫的影响。

Result: A点和M点的八面体转动（octahedral rotations）在中等应变下先于区中心极性模失稳并主导谐波响应，但其对应无谐波晶格耦合的连续相变；大应变可诱发向超四方极性态（supertetragonal polar state）的一级相变，其极化强度与铁电能谷深度可比于压应变下的BaTiO3；极性模与转动序参量以竞争性相互作用为主。

Conclusion: 应变BaSnO3的相稳定性不能用单一极性模软化推断，须考虑多模式竞争与非谐耦合；电场或电子掺杂（electron doping）可改变各模式间的平衡，在更低的临界应变下促进超四方结构而非反极性（antipolar）与转动构型。

Abstract: We use density functional theory to map the lattice-dynamical landscape of BaSnO3 under biaxial compressive strain and uncover the hierarchy of structural instabilities. Our results demonstrate that the phase stability of strained BaSnO3 cannot be inferred from the softening of the polar mode alone. Instead it is governed by the competition among lattice instabilities and their anharmonic coupling to structural relaxation. Two-mode energy-surface analyses show that the polar and rotational order parameters interact predominantly competitively. While A- and M-point octahedral rotations become unstable before the zone-center polar mode and dominate the harmonic response for moderate values of strain, they are related to a continuous transition without anharmonic lattice coupling and a moderate gain of energy with strain. Large strain may induce a first order transition to a supertetragonal polar state. Once this state is stabilized, the magnitude of polarization and ferroelectric energy well depth are comparable to BaTiO3 under compressive strain. Furthermore, we discuss how an electric field or electron doping may tip the balance between the different modes and favor of the supertetragonal structure over antipolar and rotational alternatives at a reduced critical strain.

</details>


### [356] [Cation-intermixing driven evolution from a narrow avoided crossing to an enlarged inverted gap in Ge$_2$Bi$_2$Te$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15631)
*Alexander S. Frolov,Alexander V. Fedorov,Nadezhda V. Vladimirova,Sergey M. Aksenov,Mark A. Naumov,Savely V. Domenko,Artem A. Minaev,Vasily S. Stolyarov,Denis V. Vyalikh,Lada V. Yashina*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过结合Bi₂Te₃与GeTe的结构基元合成Ge₂Bi₂Te₅，并证明阳离子互混（cation intermixing）可驱动电子结构由狭窄的避免交叉演化为显著增大的反转带隙，从而稳定拓扑电子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑绝缘体（topological insulator）Bi₂Te₃与铁电Rashba半导体（ferroelectric Rashba semiconductor）GeTe各自具备独特的结构与电子性质，将二者结构基元组合有望获得可调控的拓扑电子结构，而阳离子无序在其中可能扮演的角色尚待阐明。

Method: 合成并全面表征了存在显著阳离子互混的Ge₂Bi₂Te₅化合物，采用动量分辨光电子能谱（momentum-resolved photoemission spectroscopy）与第一性原理计算（ab initio calculations）相结合的手段研究其电子结构。

Result: 实验与计算表明，阳离子互混使电子结构从狭窄的避免交叉（avoided crossing）演变为大幅增大的反转带隙（inverted band gap）；其微观机制在于阳离子亚晶格中Ge与Bi原子的重新分布，该分布提高了价带顶附近Bi来源态的贡献并增强了自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的有效作用。

Conclusion: 阳离子无序（cation disorder）可在晶体生长过程中加以控制，为拓扑电子结构的工程设计提供了额外自由度，并为功能性量子材料（quantum materials）的开发开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Combining the building blocks and structural motifs of the topological insulator Bi$_2$Te$_3$ and the ferroelectric Rashba semiconductor GeTe, we synthesized and comprehensively characterized the resulting Ge$_2$Bi$_2$Te$_5$ compound, which exhibits pronounced cation intermixing. Using momentum-resolved photoemission spectroscopy and ab initio calculations, we demonstrate how cation intermixing drives the evolution of its electronic structure from a narrow avoided crossing to a substantially enlarged inverted band gap. We establish the microscopic origin of this behavior in the redistribution of Ge and Bi atoms within the cation sublattice, which increases the contribution of Bi-derived states near the valence-band maximum and enhances the effective contribution of spin-orbit coupling, thereby stabilizing the topological electronic structure. Our findings demonstrate that cation disorder, which can be controlled during crystal growth, provides an additional degree of freedom for engineering topological electronic structures, opening new opportunities for the development of functional quantum materials.

</details>


### [357] [Brute-force molecular dynamics simulation of aging in amorphous Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15664)
*Elisa Petroni,Dario Baratella,Omar Abou El Kheir,Andrea Redaelli,Marco Bernasconi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potential）进行分子动力学模拟，揭示非晶态Ge₂Sb₂Te₅在玻璃转变温度以下时效（aging）过程中的结构弛豫机制：轴向键的Peierls畸变增强、原子迁移率下降与键寿命延长，并关联到带隙展宽；但同极键（homopolar bonds）在约100 ns尺度内未见减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硫系合金（chalcogenide alloys）如Ge₂Sb₂Te₅因晶态与非晶态间快速可逆转变而用于相变电子存储器（phase change electronic memory）；但非晶相时效会导致电阻升高，损害器件工作，因此需明确时效背后的结构弛豫来源。

Method: 采用机器学习原子间势驱动分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations），在略低于玻璃转变温度的条件下研究非晶Ge₂Sb₂Te₅的时效：先在400 K下平衡24 ns，再进行约100 ns的时效模拟；并在较小的时效非晶模型上用密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory）计算电子带隙。

Result: 时效过程中观察到轴向键的Peierls畸变（Peierls distortion）增强，该特征与DFT计算所得带隙（band gap）展宽相关；同时原子迁移率（atomic mobility）显著下降、键寿命（bond lifetimes）延长；但同极键（homopolar bonds）未出现减少。

Conclusion: 时效引起的结构弛豫主要通过Peierls畸变增强与动力学减缓体现，并与带隙展宽相联系；理想老化玻璃中本应缺失的同极键的消除需要远长于模拟时长的时间尺度，故在约100 ns内无法观测。

Abstract: Chalcogenide alloys such as the prototypical Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$ compound are used in phase change electronic memories due to their ability to undergo a fast and reversible transition between the amorphous and crystalline phases upon heating. The amorphous phase is subject to aging that leads to an increase in the electrical resistance, which is detrimental to the operation of the devices. Using a machine learning interatomic potential, we have investigated the structural relaxations responsible for aging in amorphous Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$ in molecular dynamics simulations at temperatures slightly below the glass transition. The aging simulation, lasting about 100 ns, follows an equilibration of 24 ns at 400 K. During aging we observed an enhancement of the Peierls distortion in axial bonds which is a typical feature of these alloys that we relate to the widening of the electronic band gap computed within Density Functional Theory in smaller aged amorphous models. We also observed a marked reduction of the atomic mobility with an increase of the bond lifetimes during aging. However, we do not see a reduction in homopolar bonds, which are expected to be absent in the ideal aged glass and which should be removed on a much longer time scale.

</details>


### [358] [Zero-Field Optical Readout of Quintet States in Through-Space Coupled Tetracene Oligomers at Room Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15713)
*Jeannine Grüne,Maciej Majdecki,William K. Myers,Przemysław Gaweł,Hannah L. Stern*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将零场光探测磁共振（zero-field ODMR）与瞬态及脉冲电子顺磁共振（EPR）结合，在构象柔性、空间耦合的四并苯（tetracene）寡聚体中实现室温零场五重态（quintet state）光学读出，并测得446 ns的室温相位记忆时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温、无外加磁场下对分子自旋态的光学读出是实现分子量子技术实用化的关键一步；由单线态裂分（singlet fission）产生的五重态是有前景的多能级量子比特（qudit）候选，但其在零场与弱场下的本征自旋跃迁尚未被充分研究，且室温五重态相干性此前主要在结构受限的分子体系中得到证实。

Method: 以构象柔性、通过空间耦合（through-space coupled）的四并苯二聚体与六聚体为对象，结合零场光探测磁共振与瞬态、脉冲电子顺磁共振，考察五重态的自旋性质及其对弱磁场的依赖关系。

Result: 在四并苯二聚体与六聚体中均实现了室温零场五重态光学读出，并绘制了光探测跃迁对弱外磁场的响应；EPR测量支持五重态经由随机交换涨落（stochastic exchange fluctuations）形成，并给出446 ns的室温相位记忆时间（phase-memory time）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，促成高自旋态形成的构象动力学与可观的室温自旋相干性可以共存；该化学可调制、空间耦合的分子架构同时集成了无场光学读出、弱场响应与室温自旋相干，拓展了可光学寻址分子量子比特与量子传感器的设计空间。

Abstract: Optically addressable electron spins are promising platforms for quantum technologies, with molecular systems offering chemical control over their spin and optical properties. Achieving optical readout of molecular spin states at room temperature and without an applied magnetic field is an important step towards their practical implementation. Quintet states generated via singlet fission are promising optically addressable multilevel qudits, but their intrinsic spin transitions at zero and weak magnetic fields remain largely unexplored, while substantial room-temperature quintet coherence has predominantly been demonstrated in structurally constrained molecular architectures. Here, we combine zero-field optically detected magnetic resonance (zero-field ODMR) with transient and pulsed electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) to investigate the magnetic-field dependence and coherent spin properties of quintet states in conformationally flexible, through-space coupled tetracene oligomers. We demonstrate zero-field optical readout of quintet states at room temperature in both tetracene dimers and hexamers, and map the response of optically detected transitions to weak applied magnetic fields. Complementary EPR measurements support quintet formation through stochastic exchange fluctuations and demonstrate a room-temperature phase-memory time of 446 ns, showing that the conformational dynamics enabling high-spin formation remain compatible with substantial spin coherence. Together, these results integrate field-free optical readout, weak-field response and room-temperature spin coherence within a chemically modular, through-space coupled molecular architecture, expanding the design space for optically addressable molecular qudits and quantum sensors.

</details>


### [359] [Chiral-phonon-induced anomalous Hall effect in bilayer MoSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15761)
*Omer Yaniv,Swati Chaudhary,Carolina Paiva,Dominik M. Juraschek*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文证明光驱动的手性声子（chiral phonons）可在非磁性、中心对称材料中诱导反常霍尔响应（anomalous Hall response）。以双层 MoSe$_2$ 为对象，圆偏振激发 $E_u$ 声子产生圆形动力学 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合（dynamical Rashba spin-orbit coupling），使两个谷（valleys）的 Floquet 能带携带同号贝里曲率（Berry curvature），霍尔响应符号随声子手性（handedness）而变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索在无磁性、中心对称体系中实现并超快调控贝里曲率与霍尔输运的机制，以突破传统上依赖磁性或对称性破缺的限制，并考察手性声子作为调控手段的可能性。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）参数化双层 MoSe$_2$ 的有效 $\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}$ 哈密顿量（Hamiltonian），并在此基础上分析圆偏振驱动 $E_u$ 声子所诱导的 Floquet 能带与输运性质。

Result: 圆偏振激发 $E_u$ 声子生成圆形动力学 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合，所得 Floquet 能带在两个谷处具有同号贝里曲率，从而产生符号由声子手性决定的霍尔响应；在实验可及的光脉冲条件下，霍尔电导（Hall conductivity）范围为 $\sigma_{xy}\sim10^{-2}-0.21\ \frac{e^2}{2\pi\hbar}$。

Conclusion: 结果表明，手性声子为在超快时间尺度（ultrafast time scales）上控制电子贝里曲率与输运提供了一条可行途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that optically driven chiral phonons induce an anomalous Hall response in a nonmagnetic, centrosymmetric material. Using first-principles calculations to parameterize an effective $\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}$ Hamiltonian for bilayer MoSe$_2$, we show that circular excitation of an $E_u$ phonon generates a circular dynamical Rashba spin-orbit coupling. The resulting Floquet bands carry same-sign Berry curvature at the two valleys, producing a Hall response whose sign follows the phonon handedness. We obtain a range of $σ_{xy}\sim10^{-2}-0.21$ $\frac{e^2}{2π\hbar}$ for experimentally accessible light pulses. Our results show that chiral phonons provide a route to controlling electronic Berry curvature and transport on ultrafast time scales.

</details>


### [360] [A Hierarchical Harmonic-to-Anharmonic Framework for ab initio Screening and Mechanistic Analysis of Negative Thermal Expansion in ZrV2O7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15767)
*J. Bustamante,A. Miliute,N. Jaykhedkar,A. A. Naik,J. Grandel,F. Emmerling,T. M. Stawski,J. George*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究针对ZrV2O7的负热膨胀（negative thermal expansion, NTE）建立了准简谐近似（QHA）、温度依赖有效势（TDEP）与机器学习加速从头算分子动力学（ML-MD）相结合的分层计算策略，并用X射线衍射（XRD）验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨架材料中的负热膨胀源于复杂晶格动力学，传统理论方法难以准确描述，因此需要发展兼顾效率与精度的分层计算方案来揭示其微观机制。

Method: 构建QHA—TDEP—ML-MD的分层策略：QHA用于以负Grüneisen参数的低频声子模作为低成本筛选描述符，TDEP引入有限温度非谐性，ML-MD进一步揭示驱动晶格收缩的温度依赖结构畸变，并与XRD测量相互印证。

Result: 计算揭示了驱动晶格收缩的温度依赖结构畸变；横向位移（transverse displacement, uT）随温度同步增大，为高温下NTE的准刚性单元模式（quasi-rigid-unit-mode, QRUM）机制提供了微观证据，且与ZrW2O7等骨架材料的既有报道一致。

Conclusion: 低成本晶格动力学描述符可用于筛选潜在NTE材料，而更高层级的非谐模拟能够在原子尺度解析其结构机制，形成从筛选到机理阐释的可行工作流。

Abstract: Negative thermal expansion (NTE) in framework materials arises from complex lattice dynamics that are challenging to capture using conventional theoretical approaches. Here, we establish a hierarchical computational strategy for investigating NTE in ZrV2O7, integrating the quasiharmonic approximation (QHA), temperature-dependent effective potentials (TDEP), and machine-learning-accelerated ab initio molecular dynamics (ML-MD). Quasi-harmonic lattice-dynamical calculations identify low-frequency phonon modes with negative Grüneisen parameters as inexpensive screening descriptors, while TDEP incorporates finite-temperature anharmonicity and ML-MD further reveals temperature-dependent structural distortions that drive the lattice contraction. The observations are further corroborated with X-ray diffraction (XRD) measurements. The concurrent increase in transverse displacement (uT) provides microscopic evidence for a quasi-rigid-unit-mode (QRUM) mechanism underlying the NTE behaviour at elevated temperatures. This is consistent with previous reports on other framework materials, including ZrW2O7, where collective polyhedral rotations and transverse atomic motions have been linked to NTE. Overall, our results demonstrate how relatively inexpensive lattice-dynamical descriptors can identify potential NTE materials, while higher-level anharmonic simulations can then resolve the underlying structural mechanisms at the atomic scale.

</details>


### [361] [Evidential Deep Regression for Universal Density Functional Theory Hamiltonian Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15786)
*Jiaxuan Liu,Yan Zhang,Caiyuan Ye,Tiannian Zhu,Zhong Fang,Hongming Weng,Quansheng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 EviDTB，一种基于证据深度回归（evidential deep regression）的紧束缚密度泛函理论（DFT）哈密顿量预测方法，可在单次前向传播中同时给出哈密顿量预测与不确定度估计，并用于筛选候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管跨化学与结构空间训练的通用 DFT 哈密顿量模型可高效由原子结构映射到电子性质，但在快速给出哈密顿量预测的同时估计预测不确定度（prediction uncertainty）仍是难题。

Method: 采用证据深度回归（evidential deep regression）对紧束缚 DFT 哈密顿量建模，实现单次前向传播的不确定度量化（uncertainty quantification），并与深度集成（deep ensembles）、蒙特卡洛 dropout（Monte Carlo dropout）及均值-方差估计（mean-variance estimation, MVE）进行对比。

Result: 在 5,478 个留出测试结构（held-out test structures）上，全自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit-coupled, SOC）哈密顿量矩阵元的平均绝对误差为 0.622 meV；每原子平均不确定度与平均局部平方预测误差相关（Spearman ρ=0.812）；剔除最不确定的 15% 结构后，该误差的 99 分位数由 16.63 降至 3.72 eV²，降幅 78%。

Conclusion: EviDTB 在计算成本与不确定度量化性能之间取得更优平衡，该不确定度评分可用于优先筛选待第一性原理（first-principles）验证的候选材料，有望加速目标电子性质材料的搜索。

Abstract: Universal density functional theory (DFT) Hamiltonian models trained across broad chemical and structural spaces offer an efficient route from atomic structure to electronic properties. However, estimating prediction uncertainty alongside fast Hamiltonian predictions remains a challenge. In this work, we present EviDTB, an evidential deep regression approach for tight-binding DFT Hamiltonians that effectively estimates prediction uncertainty in a single forward pass. Compared with deep ensembles, Monte Carlo dropout, and mean-variance estimation (MVE), EviDTB achieves a better balance between computational cost and uncertainty-quantification performance. On 5,478 held-out test structures, EviDTB achieves a full spin--orbit-coupled (SOC) Hamiltonian matrix-element mean absolute error of 0.622meV. Its mean uncertainty per atom correlates with the mean local squared prediction error (Spearman $ρ=0.812$); removing the 15% most uncertain structures reduces the 99th percentile of this error by 78%, from 16.63 to 3.72eV$^2$. This score offers a practical way to prioritize candidates for first-principles verification. We hope that combining fast Hamiltonian prediction with uncertainty-guided screening will accelerate the search for materials with desirable electronic properties.

</details>


### [362] [Lithium redistribution and electrode degradation in commercial NMC/Graphite and LCO/Graphite cells under high-rate cycling conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15818)
*Naisargi Kanabar,Nicholas Fusco,Seiichiro Higashiya,Devendra Sadana,Stephen Bedell,Haralabos Efstathiadis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 商用NMC811/石墨与LCO/石墨扣式电池在0.3C–3C倍率下循环100圈，两者均在1C与2C之间出现循环稳定性转变；核反应分析（NRA）显示2C时石墨负极锂累积量最大，表明高倍率性能衰减与负极侧退化及充电协议密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快充会加速锂离子电池（lithium-ion batteries）退化，但正极化学（cathode chemistry）与充电协议（charging protocol）的影响难以分离，故需在同条件下对两种正极体系进行比较研究。

Method: 对商用NMC811/石墨（200 mAh）与LCO/石墨（45 mAh）扣式电池（coin cells）在0.3C、1C、2C、3C下循环100圈，随后采用扫描电子显微镜（SEM）、X射线衍射（XRD）与核反应分析（NRA）表征。

Result: NMC811/石墨在0.3C与1C下容量保持约99.7%，2C降至72.3%、3C降至63.5%；LCO/石墨在2C仅保持11.1%。NRA显示两种化学体系均在2C时石墨负极锂累积量最大，SEM观察到颗粒状表面沉积物，XRD显示正极(003)与石墨(002)衍射峰随倍率移动。

Conclusion: 3C下锂累积较低与电荷吞吐量（charge throughput）减少相关，LCO/石墨的3C条件受其充电协议限制；解读商用锂离子电池高倍率循环性能时需重视负极侧退化与充电协议的作用。

Abstract: Fast charging accelerates degradation in lithium-ion batteries, but the effects of cathode chemistry and charging protocol remain difficult to separate. Here, commercial NMC811/graphite (200 mAh) and LCO/graphite (45 mAh) coin cells were cycled for 100 cycles at 0.3C, 1C, 2C, and 3C, followed by scanning electron microscopy (SEM), X-ray diffraction (XRD), and nuclear reaction analysis (NRA). Both cell types exhibited a transition in cycling stability between 1C and 2C. NMC811/graphite retained approximately 99.7% of its initial discharge capacity at 0.3C and 1C, decreasing to 72.3% at 2C and 63.5% at 3C, while LCO/graphite retained only 11.1% at 2C. NRA revealed maximum lithium accumulation in graphite anodes at 2C for both chemistries, supported by SEM observations of granular surface deposits. XRD showed rate-dependent shifts in cathode (003) and graphite (002) reflections, indicating structural changes during cycling. The lower lithium accumulation at 3C was associated with reduced charge throughput, while the LCO/graphite 3C condition was limited by its charging protocol. These findings highlight the importance of anode-side degradation and charging protocols in interpreting high-rate cycling performance of commercial lithium-ion batteries.

</details>


### [363] [A New High $γ$-$γ'$ Fraction Polycrystalline Co/Ni Superalloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15969)
*Cameron Crabb,Mark C. Hardy,Michelle S. Conroy,David Dye*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种可铸锭加工的 Co/Ni 基 γ/γ' 高温合金（superalloy），经均匀化、热轧与热处理后获得 50% γ' 相与 142 μm 晶粒，兼具高温强度、抗蠕变与抗氧化性能，适用于航空发动机高温部件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向航空发动机锻件（wrought parts）需求，需要一种既便于铸锭加工（ingot-processable）又能在高温下保持强度与抗氧化性的 Co/Ni 基 γ/γ' 合金，以补充现有变形镍基合金的不足。

Method: 采用 8.5 kg 真空感应熔炼（VIM）铸锭，经均匀化（homogenisation）与热轧至真应变 1，并开发热处理工艺以获得 50% γ' 与 142 μm 晶粒；随后开展高温拉伸、蠕变（creep）、800 ℃/100 h 氧化及 800 ℃/8000 h 长期时效实验。

Result: 高温拉伸屈服强度为 700 ℃/820 MPa、800 ℃/785 MPa、850 ℃/783 MPa；750 ℃/600 MPa 与 800 ℃/300 MPa 蠕变测试显示最小蠕变速率降低、断裂寿命延长；800 ℃/100 h 氧化形成约 1 μm 的铬/铝双相氧化层（chromia/alumina scale），亚表层损伤轻微；800 ℃/8000 h 长期暴露下 γ' 粗化有限，且未出现有害金属间化合物或晶界相。

Conclusion: 该合金在可加工性、强度与抗氧化性之间取得良好平衡，具备用于高温静止与转动部件（static and rotating components）的潜力。

Abstract: An ingot-processable Co/Ni-based $γ/γ'$ superalloy has been developed for wrought parts in aircraft engines. An 8.5 kg VIM ingot was homogenised and hot rolled to a true strain of 1. A heat treatment was then developed to produce 50% $γ'$ and a grain size of 142 $μ$m. High-temperature tensile testing showed yield strengths of 820 MPa at 700 $^\circ$C, 785 MPa at 800 $^\circ$C and 783 MPa at 850 $^\circ$C. Creep tests at 750 $^\circ$C/600 MPa and 800 $^\circ$C/300 MPa demonstrated reduced minimum creep rates and extended rupture lives relative to current wrought Ni-base alloys. Oxidation at 800 $^\circ$C for 100 h formed a 1 $μ$m dual chromia/alumina scale with minimal subsurface degradation. Long-term exposure at 800 $^\circ$C for 8000 h showed limited $γ'$ coarsening and no deleterious intermetallic or grain boundary phases. The alloy therefore offers a promising balance of processability, strength and oxidation resistance for high-temperature static and rotating components.or grain boundary phases. The alloy therefore offers a promising balance of processability, strength and oxidation resistance for high-temperature static and rotating components.

</details>


### [364] [Quantifying sample heating by ablation-plasma x-rays in laser-driven shock experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16054)
*R. F. Smith,I. K. OCampo,S. Singh,T. M. Hutchinson,T. E. Lockard,J. H. Eggert,C. P. McGuire,A. L. Coleman,J. M. McNaney,S. Pandolfi,B. Nagler,D. McGonegle,H. J. Lee,D. Khaghani,N. Czapla,A. Krygier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过“先加热、后加压”的实验设计，定量分离并测量了烧蚀等离子体（ablation plasma）X射线对样品的预加热（preheat）效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光驱动动态压缩（dynamic compression）实验中，烧蚀等离子体发射的X射线会造成样品预加热，且该加热与压力驱动同时发生、难以区分，从而干扰对主雨贡纽（principal Hugoniot）等明确热力学状态的判定。

Method: 采用独特实验设计使样品在压力波到达前先被加热，利用原位X射线衍射（in situ x-ray diffraction）将1 μm厚Au样品的晶格膨胀与温度关联，考察25 μm与75 μm厚Kapton-B（聚酰亚胺）烧蚀体在不同激光辐照度下的表现，并测试0.2 μm Al闪光镀层的影响。

Result: 25 μm厚聚酰亚胺烧蚀体下观察到足以熔化Au的加热（ΔT约1050 K），加0.2 μm Al镀层后加热显著增强；而75 μm厚无Al镀层Kapton-B的加热被强烈抑制，2.5 μm厚Ti与1 μm厚Fe样品在辐照度达1.2×10^13 W/cm^2时无可测加热。

Conclusion: 烧蚀体厚度与Al闪光镀层显著影响X射线预加热程度，厚且无镀层的Kapton-B可有效抑制预加热，为动态压缩实验中热力学状态的更清晰解释提供依据。

Abstract: Sample preheat due to absorption of x-rays emitted by an ablation plasma has long been a source of uncertainty in laser-driven dynamic compression experiments. This unknown heating complicates interpretation of experiments designed to reach well-defined thermodynamic states, such as the principal Hugoniot, particularly because the pressure drive and sample heating occur simultaneously and are difficult to disentangle. Here, we quantify sample heating from x-rays using a unique experimental design in which the sample is heated prior to the arrival of the pressure wave. Using in situ x-ray diffraction, we relate lattice expansion to temperature within a 1-um-thick Au sample for commonly used 25-um and 75-um-thick Kapton-B (polyimide) ablators over a range of laser irradiances. Heating, sufficient to melt Au (Delta T ~ 1050 K), is observed for 25-um-thick polyimide ablators, which is significantly enhanced by the addition of a 0.2-um Al flash coating. In contrast, heating is strongly suppressed for 75-um-thick, non-Al-coated Kapton-B, with no measurable heating observed in 2.5-um-thick Ti or 1-um-thick Fe samples at laser irradiances up to 1.2 x 10^13 W/cm^2. .

</details>


### [365] [Tunable Long-Range Magnetic Exchange in Adatom-Decorated Transition Metal Dichalcogenide Monolayers: An ab initio Embedding Model Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16085)
*Xiaohang Jia,Ryan Weiss,Yusong Bai,Brenda Rubenstein*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文以单层金属性过渡金属硫族化合物（transition-metal dichalcogenides, TMDs）TaS₂上的Fe吸附原子为平台，结合DFT+U与第一性原理嵌入模型，揭示中距离区间交换耦合的原子尺度振荡及其应变可调性，并确认RKKY耦合为主导机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料中的磁性相互作用工程是实现可编程磁相与可重构自旋电子学（spintronics）功能的重要途径，因此需要明确金属性单层TMD上吸附原子间间接交换耦合的机制与可调手段。

Method: 采用DFT+U计算考察单层TaS₂上两个Fe吸附原子在中距离区间的交换耦合，并构建第一性原理嵌入模型（embedding model）以复现DFT给出的对交换结果；通过受控对比分析RKKY贡献。

Result: DFT+U显示交换耦合存在原子尺度振荡，且双轴应变（biaxial strain）可调控该耦合；嵌入模型复现了DFT的对交换并表明RKKY机制有显著贡献，受控对比进一步确认RKKY介导耦合是应变可调性的主要来源。

Conclusion: 吸附原子排布与应变的结合可作为在金属性单层TMD中工程化可重构磁相互作用的手段。

Abstract: Engineering magnetic interactions in two-dimensional materials offers a promising route toward programmable magnetic phases and reconfigurable spintronic functionalities. Here, we investigate magnetic adatoms on metallic monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) as a platform for tunable indirect exchange, focusing on Fe adatoms on monolayer TaS$_2$. In the intermediate-distance regime, DFT+U calculations reveal atomic-scale oscillations in the exchange coupling between two Fe adatoms and show that biaxial strain tunes the coupling. We develop a first-principles embedding model that reproduces the DFT-calculated pair exchange and establishes a substantial RKKY contribution to the exchange; controlled comparisons further identify RKKY-mediated coupling as the primary origin of the exchange's strain tunability. These results point to combining adatom arrangement with strain as a means of engineering reconfigurable magnetic interactions in metallic monolayer TMDs.

</details>


### [366] [Uncertainty Aware AFM-IR Hyperspectral Cube Reconstruction via Convolutional-Attentive Conditional Neural Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16118)
*William F. Kent,Roberto does Reis,Gajendra Shekhawat,Emma Alexander,Vinayak P. Dravid*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出卷积-注意力条件神经过程（CACNP），仅用5%稀疏光谱与少量结构图像即可重建AFM-IR高光谱立方体，推断时间低于0.1 s，并提供预测不确定性以支持自适应采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子力显微镜-红外光谱（AFM-IR）可在纳米尺度空间分辨下映射化学组成，但逐点采集光谱耗时过长，成为高光谱成像（hyperspectral imaging）的瓶颈。

Method: 采用卷积-注意力条件神经过程（convolutional-attentive conditional neural process, CACNP）架构，从稀疏采样光谱与少量在选定波数下获取的结构图像重建高光谱立方体；模型完全在由实验红外光谱生成的合成立方体上训练，同时预测光谱强度及其不确定性。

Result: 在嵌入环氧树脂的聚甲基丙烯酸甲酯（poly(methyl methacrylate)）与聚苯乙烯（polystyrene）颗粒实验数据集上验证，仅用5%可用光谱即实现准确重建，性能与其他方法具竞争力；128×128立方体推断耗时不足0.1 s，预测不确定性为局部化学特征的自适应采样提供依据。

Conclusion: 该方法可推动自主、高通量AFM-IR工作流，并为加速其他高光谱成像方法提供了一种通用架构。

Abstract: Atomic force microscopy-infrared spectroscopy (AFM-IR) maps chemical composition at nanoscale spatial resolution, but the time required to acquire individual spectra limits hyperspectral imaging. Here, we introduce a convolutional-attentive conditional neural process (CACNP), a neural process architecture for reconstructing AFM-IR hyperspectral cubes from sparsely sampled spectra and a small set of structure images, each acquired at a selected wavenumber. The model is trained entirely on synthetic cubes generated from experimental infrared spectra and predicts both spectral intensities and their associated uncertainty. Validation on experimental datasets of poly(methyl methacrylate) and polystyrene particles embedded in epoxy demonstrates accurate reconstruction using only 5% of the available spectra, with competitive performance when compared to other methods.
