Inference takes less than 0.1 s on 128 X 128 hyperspectral cubes, supporting rapid reconstruction updates, while predictive uncertainty provides a basis for adaptive sampling of localized chemical features. This approach advances autonomous, high-throughput AFM-IR workflows and offers an architecture for accelerating other hyperspectral imaging methods.

</details>


### [367] [Direct imaging of plasmonic coupling between gold nanoparticles and one-dimensional van der Waals materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16132)
*Ibrahim Tanriover,Arindom Nag,Jong-Ho Lee,Huy Hoang,Haihua Liu,Thang Pham*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究报道了金纳米颗粒（Au nanoparticles）与一维（one-dimensional, 1D）范德华（van der Waals, vdW）材料 NbS3 等离激元耦合（plasmonic coupling），并利用光子诱导近场电子显微镜（photon-induced near-field electron microscopy, PINEM）直接测量 Au-NbS3 接触处的近场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等离激元金属/范德华杂化体系研究局限于二维层状材料，且金属/vdW 界面近场多由远场测量间接推断，缺乏对一维 vdW 体系及界面近场的直接表征。该工作旨在将此类杂化体系拓展至一维 vdW 晶体，并建立界面原子结构与纳米尺度等离激元响应的关联方法。

Method: 采用盐辅助气-液-固（salt-assisted vapor-liquid-solid）法在石墨烯覆盖的透射电镜载网上生长 NbS3 纳米线与纳米带，经原子分辨电子显微镜确认原子级锐利的 Au/NbS3 界面；在 1030 nm 激发下用光子诱导近场电子显微镜（PINEM）直接测量接触处近场，并以有限差分时域（finite-difference time-domain, FDTD）模拟进行验证与量化分析。

Result: PINEM 显示，与 NbS3 接触的 Au 颗粒相较同等尺寸孤立颗粒具有更强、范围更大的近场，且接触处热点仅在入射偏振平行于颗粒-NbS3 轴时被激发；FDTD 模拟重现了上述观测，并量化了耦合对颗粒-NbS3 间隙、NbS3 厚度及 NbS3 结构尺寸比的依赖。NbS3 纳米线上的尺寸特异性 Au 纳米颗粒使罗丹明 6G（rhodamine 6G）的拉曼散射（Raman scattering）较单独 Au 颗粒增强 1.8–4.2 倍，检测限为 10^-7 M。

Conclusion: 研究将等离激元金属/vdW 杂化体系拓展至一维 vdW 晶体，并建立了将金属/vdW 界面原子结构与纳米尺度等离激元响应相关联的多模态（multimodal）研究路径。

Abstract: Hybrids of plasmonic metals and van der Waals (vdW) materials combine electromagnetic field enhancement with the electronic properties of the vdW component, but the studies reported so far concern two-dimensional layers, and the near field at the metal/vdW interface is generally inferred from far-field measurements. Here we report the plasmonic coupling between Au nanoparticles and a one-dimensional (1D) vdW material, NbS3, and measure the near field at the Au-NbS3 contact directly. NbS3 nanowires and nanoribbons were grown by salt-assisted vapor-liquid-solid growth on graphene-covered TEM grids, and atomic-resolution electron microscopy shows atomically sharp Au/NbS3 interfaces. Photon-induced near-field electron microscopy at 1030 nm shows that an Au particle in contact with NbS3 produces a near field of higher intensity and larger extent than an isolated particle of similar size, and that the hot spot at a contact is excited only when the incident polarization is parallel to the particle-NbS3 axis. Finite-difference time-domain simulations reproduce these observations and quantify the dependence of the coupling on the particle-NbS3 gap, the NbS3 thickness, and the dimension ratio of the NbS3 structure. Size-specific Au nanoparticles on NbS3 nanowires enhance the Raman scattering of rhodamine 6G by a factor of 1.8-4.2 relative to Au nanoparticles alone, with a detection limit of 10^-7 M. These results extend plasmonic metal/vdW hybrids to 1D vdW crystals and establish a multimodal approach that correlates the atomic structure of a metal/vdW interface with its nanoscale plasmonic response.

</details>


### [368] [Humidity-modulated Ostwald Step Rule in $\rm FAPbI_3$ Martensitic Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16147)
*Ping Tuo,Ju Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用从头算机器学习势（ab initio machine-learned potential）驱动的大规模原子尺度模拟，揭示了水汽（moisture）在 $\rm FAPbI_3$ 相变中的双重作用机制，并再现了湿度升高时 $\delta\to\alpha$ 转变温度上升的实验现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验已观察到水汽的双重作用：高温制备时湿度改善 $\alpha$ 相薄膜质量，而低温服役时湿度加速光活性 $\alpha$ 相向非光活性六方 $\delta$ 相退化；但水汽调控相稳定性与相变路径的微观机制尚不清楚。

Method: 采用由从头算机器学习势（ab initio machine-learned potential）支持的大规模原子尺度模拟（large-scale atomistic simulations），研究水汽对 $\rm FAPbI_3$ 相变路径与动力学的影响。

Result: 模拟再现了实验观测到的湿度增加下 $\delta\to\alpha$ 转变温度升高现象，并揭示沿 Burgers 转变路径存在一个对水具有强亲和力的层状亚稳中间相（metastable intermediate）；该中间相与 $\delta$ 相对水的竞争性吸收（competing water uptake）决定了相变动力学的湿度依赖性。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了水汽在钙钛矿相变中的双重作用，表明环境组分可稳定亚稳中间相并重塑相变路径，为 Ostwald 阶梯规则（Ostwald's step rule）提供了依赖环境的视角。

Abstract: Experimental observations reveal a dual role of moisture in $\rm FAPbI_3$ phase transitions. During fabrication at high temperatures, humidity improves the quality of the perovskite ($α$ phase) films, whereas at low service temperatures, humidity accelerates degradation of the photoactive $α$ phase into the photo-inactive hexagonal $δ$ phase. Despite extensive experimental evidence, the microscopic mechanisms by which moisture modulates phase stability and transition pathways remain unknown. Here, using large-scale atomistic simulations powered by an ab initio machine-learned potential, we reproduce the experimentally observed increase in the $δ$-to-$α$ transition temperature under increased humidity. Our simulations reveal a layered metastable intermediate along the Burgers transition pathway that exhibits a strong affinity for water. The competing water uptake of this intermediate and the $δ$ phase governs the humidity dependence of transition kinetics. These findings elucidate the dual role of moisture in perovskite phase transitions and demonstrate how environmental species can stabilize metastable intermediates and reshape transition pathways, providing an environment-dependent perspective on Ostwald's step rule.

</details>


### [369] [Studying temperature-induced decrease of spontaneous polarization in PbTiO$_3$ from vibrational amplitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16266)
*Zhikai Gao,Yuanji Xu,Fuyang Tian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作基于第一性原理分子动力学（$ab$ $initio$ molecular dynamics）模拟，提出以振动振幅投影（projection of vibrational amplitude）定量描述PbTiO$_3$中原子的各向异性热畸变，并阐明TiO$_6$八面体转动模式与负热膨胀（negative thermal expansion, NTE）及自发极化衰减之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PbTiO$_3$是典型的铁电极化诱导负热膨胀材料，TiO$_6$八面体常被用于研究极化与NTE，但缺乏对其定量表征，本文旨在弥补这一不足。

Method: 采用第一性原理分子动力学模拟，提出简洁的振动振幅投影以刻画原子随温度的各向异性畸变，并结合势能面（potential energy surface）、投影振幅与单原子受力进行分析。

Result: 巨大的负模式格林艾森系数（mode Grüneisen coefficient）主要源于四方相中沿$c$方向两个边界对称点上TiO$_6$八面体转动的不同振动模式；振幅相关的势能面表明$c$轴压缩促进八面体转动。

Conclusion: 围绕负膨胀轴的TiO$_6$八面体转动振动模式可随温度升高促进自发极化（spontaneous polarization）降低，从而驱动PbTiO$_3$由四方相向立方相转变。

Abstract: PbTiO$_3$ is a typical ferroelectric polarization induced negative thermal expansion (NTE) material at a wide range of temperature. The elegant TiO$_6$ octahedron is used to study the polarization and NTE, whereas lacking its quantitative characterization. In this work, we propose a concise projection of vibrational amplitude to describe anisotropic distortion of atoms with temperature based on $ab$ $initio$ molecular dynamics simulations. We find that the huge negative mode Grüneisen coefficient is mainly from the different vibrational modes of the TiO$_6$ octahedral rotation at two boundary symmetry points in tetragonal phase along $c$ direction. The potential energy surface as a function of amplitude suggests the $c$-axis compression facilitates the TiO$_6$ octahedral rotation. We show that the vibrational mode of TiO$_6$ octahedral rotation around negative-expansion axis can promote spontaneous polarization reduction with increasing temperature, making transformation of PbTiO$_3$ from tetragonal to cubic phase occur, via the projected amplitude, potential energy surface, and forces of individual atoms.

</details>


### [370] [Optically-detected room-temperature spin-coherence from the GaV$^-$ in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16281)
*Adam D. Dodson,Tiamike Dudley,Luca Basso,Leopold Diaz,Jesse J. Lutz,Raj Patel,Matthew Markham,Jacob Henshaw,Andrew M. Mounce*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作报告了金刚石中新发现的带负电镓-空位色心（GaV⁻）的物理性质，其兼具NV⁻与G4V色心的优势：室温自旋相干、高德拜-沃勒因子、短辐射寿命、强零场分裂温度依赖及抗电荷噪声的D3d对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态量子色心（quantum defects）是量子信息科学的重要平台，但NV⁻易受电荷噪声（charge noise）影响且零声子线（zero-phonon line）发射弱，G4V色心则需低温工作，亟需兼具二者优点的新型色心。

Method: 通过聚焦离子束（FIB）注入与高温高压退火（high-pressure high-temperature annealing）制备GaV⁻色心，并表征其光学与自旋相关物理性质。

Result: GaV⁻表现出室温可光学探测的自旋相干（T₂(293K)=1.06 μs）、约0.4的大德拜-沃勒因子（Debye-Waller factor）、约3.83 ns的短辐射寿命、为NV⁻约16倍的零场分裂（zero-field splitting）温度依赖，以及抗电荷噪声的D3d对称性，低温下性能进一步改善。

Conclusion: GaV⁻因上述特性被视为量子传感（quantum sensing）、量子网络（quantum networking）与量子计算（quantum computation）的关键候选资源。

Abstract: Solid-state quantum defects (point crystal imperfections with optically addressable spins) are among the most promising platforms for quantum information science due to their long-lived electron and nuclear spin lifetimes. The negatively-charged nitrogen-vacancy (NV$^-$) and negatively-charged group IV vacancies (G4V's) in diamond are some the most promising quantum defects but, the NV$^-$ is highly susceptible to charge noise and has low emission from its zero-phonon line while the G4V's require low operating temperatures. Here we report the physical properties of the newly discovered negatively-charged gallium-vacancy in diamond, GaV$^-$. We find that the GaV$^-$, created through FIB implantation and high-pressure high-temperature annealing, exhibits a combination of the best properties of both the NV$^-$ and G4V's: optically-detectable spin coherence at room temperature ($T_2(293K) = 1.06 μ$s), a large Debye-Waller factor $\sim$0.4, a short radiative lifetime ($\sim$3.83 ns), strong temperature dependence of the zero-field splitting (16x that of the NV$^-$), and charge-noise-protected D$_{3d}$ symmetry. These features (along with improved performance at low temperature) position the newly discovered GaV$^-$ as a key resource for quantum sensing, networking, and computation.

</details>


### [371] [ORCHID-SAGE: Fully Reproducible CALPHAD Assessment from Evidence to Phase Diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16308)
*Raymundo Arróyave*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文提出将CALPHAD数据库构建中隐含的专家判断编码为形式化协议（formal protocols），并推出ORCHID-SAGE标准，使数据库评估过程可复现、可自动化、可记录。在Cr-Ni体系上验证了该记录可由脚本、AI代理（MCP）及公开数据重拟合在另一台机器上重现同一预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相稳定性（phase stability）知识是材料设计的前提，自驱动实验室（self-driving laboratories, SDLs）需预测新材料可合成的条件。但CALPHAD数据库构建依赖多步专家判断，且这些决策很少被记录，导致他人只能使用数据库而无法重建或更新它，阻碍自动化与可复现性。

Method: 提出ORCHID-SAGE标准，将评估背后的专家知识编码为形式化协议，使评估过程产出对构建过程的记录（record of its construction）而非仅参数。在Cr-Ni体系上进行首次尝试，通过脚本在第二台机器上、仅经模型上下文协议（Model Context Protocol, MCP）工作的AI代理、以及仅用公开来源的重拟合三种方式重建数据库。

Result: 三种重建路径——脚本跨机器重生成、AI代理经MCP操作、以及基于公开来源的重拟合——均给出与原始一致的预测结果，表明该记录具备可复现性与可自动化性。

Conclusion: 若以这种方式记录，数据库不必在发表时固化，而可在SDLs及其他来源提供新证据时由AI代理更新；同时判断权仍归人类并保留在记录中，实现可复现自动化与人类主导判断的结合。

Abstract: Knowledge of phase stability is the first requirement for materials design, and predicting the conditions under which new materials can be synthesized has become a key capability of self-driving laboratories (SDLs). For inorganic materials, notably alloys, these predictions come from thermodynamic databases built with the CALPHAD method. Building a database, however, requires expert judgment at many steps, and because these decisions are rarely recorded, others can use a database but cannot regenerate or update it. Machines can already collect the data, build a first model with machine-learned interatomic potentials and refine it against experiments. The assessment itself, the sequence of judgments that turns these parts into a reliable database, is still carried out by human experts. We argue that it can be automated reproducibly only if the expert knowledge behind an assessment is encoded in formal protocols, so that an assessment delivers a record of its construction and not only its parameters. We introduce ORCHID-SAGE, a standard that organizes this record into reproducible and automatable protocols. In this first attempt, applied to Cr-Ni, the database was regenerated from its record by a script on a second machine, by an AI agent working only through the Model Context Protocol (MCP) and by a refit from public sources only, with the same predictions. Recorded in this way, a database need no longer be fixed at publication but can be updated by AI agents as SDLs and other sources provide new evidence, while the judgment remains with people and on the record.

</details>


### [372] [Dynamic Tunneling a Prior Phenomenon to Intermediate Resistive States in Defect Engineered Mn-SrTiO3 RRAM Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16380)
*Gowtham Velpandi,Ishaan Tushir,Durairaj Murugan,Thanumalayaperumal Thirunavukarasu,Ranjith Ramadurai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过PLD制备2%Mn掺杂SrTiO3阻变器件，利用Mn3+负电荷缺陷与氧空位正电荷缺陷的协同作用，实现无forming的双极性阻变，ON/OFF比超过10²、保持时间超过10³秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻变存储器（resistive random-access memory, RRAM）依赖外加电场下由高阻态向低阻态的阻变行为，本文旨在通过缺陷工程调控导电细丝（conducting filament）的形成，改善阻变特性并实现无forming切换。

Method: 采用脉冲激光沉积（PLD）制备2%Mn掺杂SrTiO3器件，设计两类带相反电荷的缺陷：Mn3+掺杂产生的带负电Mn与Ti缺陷，以及单/双正电荷氧空位；并通过切换前后器件的XPS表征观察偏压电极周围缺陷型细丝的形成。

Result: 器件表现出稳定的双极性阻变（bipolar resistive switching），在0.1V低读取电压下ON/OFF比超过10²、存储保持（memory retention）超过10³秒；SET区细丝形成前及RESET区突变后均观察到动态隧穿（dynamic tunnelling）中间过程，伴随电流尖峰并出现在中间阻态。

Conclusion: Mn-STO器件在不同厚度下均展现出良好的保持特性，缺陷工程可对单态和/或多态存储（multi-state memory）实现调控；动态隧穿对应的中间阻态具备多态应用潜力。

Abstract: Resistive random-access memory devices work under the principle of resistive switching from high resistive state to low resistive state under applied electric field. In this work, 2%Mn doped SrTiO3, devices were fabricated using PLD in which, the defects are engineered such that the conducting filament formation is expected to have two oppositely charged defects: a) presence of Mn3+ dopants, giving rise to a negatively charged Mn and Ti and b) single and double positive oxygen vacancies. This process of two filaments emanating from both the electrodes has shown enhancement in the resistive switching characteristics aiding in forming-free switching that exhibits over 102 as ON/OFF ratio with a memory retention beyond 103 seconds at a low read voltage of 0.1V. All devices demonstrated a stable bipolar resistive switching dominated by conduction filament formation resulting from a synergic effect of oxygen vacancies and the varying oxidation states of Mn based negative defects that propagates toward each other under bias. The formation of defect-based filaments around the bias electrode was evidently observed using XPS of the devices before and after switching. A dynamic tunnelling process is observed as an intermediate process before the conduction filament forms in the SET region and following the snapping in the RESET region. Conduction filaments being heavily doped localizes conduction pathways with a small finite tunnelling barrier gap that are instantaneously formed and ruptures during switching cycles. The dynamic tunnelling behavior gives rise to a tiny spike in current and is observed at the intermediate resistive states which are potential for multi-state usage of these devices. Overall, Mn-STO devices of varying thicknesses show promising retention features and defect engineering offers control over single and/or multi-states memory.

</details>


### [373] [Multi-reference variational states resolve stripe selection and local $d$-wave pairing in the Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16430)
*Honghui Shang,Jinlong Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文用多参考神经变分辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（MR-nVAFQMC）在二维Hubbard模型中叠加条纹（stripe）构型，发现半满反相畴壁（antiphase domain wall）上存在内禀局域d波配对（d-wave pairing），而静电荷调制并非配对来源；跨越条纹周期的长程配对相关性未能分辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条纹（stripe）与d波超导（d-wave superconductivity）在二维Hubbard模型中究竟是竞争还是通过涨落促进配对尚无定论，需要高精度变分方法在较大晶格上厘清二者的关系。

Method: 采用多参考神经变分辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（multi-reference neural variational auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo, MR-nVAFQMC），在U/t=8下跨条纹周期与畴壁位置叠加未配对Slater行列式，检验周期晶格至32×32格点，并针对t'/t=-0.2的铜氧化物参数与1/8空穴掺杂开展分析，包括壁位移相干离域与参数迁移测试。

Result: 对角跳跃（diagonal hopping）保留电子掺杂反铁磁性（antiferromagnetism），但动力学上抑制空穴形成Yamada条纹，并给出短程连通d波配对穹顶；1/8掺杂时空穴跨反相自旋边界离域胜过节内排斥，纯模型的满条纹分裂为具强局域配对的半满壁。相干离域位移壁强烈压制静态电荷调制（static charge modulation）而短程配对不变；但跨反铁磁畴在一个条纹周期及以上配对关联不可分辨，基准检查点仅容许可分辨度内的弱超导。

Conclusion: 局域d波配对源于半满反相畴壁而非条纹涨落；仅压制静态条纹序并不能在所研究态中产生跨条纹的可分辨相干性。

Abstract: Whether stripes compete with $d$-wave superconductivity or promote pairing through fluctuations remains unsettled in the two-dimensional Hubbard model. Using multi-reference neural variational auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (MR-nVAFQMC) at $U/t=8$, we superpose unpaired Slater determinants across stripe periods and domain-wall positions, reaching variational energies competitive with leading benchmarks on periodic lattices up to $32\times32$ sites. For cuprate next-nearest-neighbor hopping ($t'/t=-0.2$), diagonal hopping preserves electron-doped antiferromagnetism while kinetically frustrating hole motion into Yamada stripes with a dome of short-range connected $d$-wave pairing. At $1/8$ hole doping, hole delocalization across antiphase spin boundaries outweighs on-site repulsion, splitting the filled stripes of the pure model into half-filled walls with strong local pairing. Coherently delocalizing shifted walls strongly suppresses static charge modulation yet leaves short-range pairing unchanged, showing that local pairing is intrinsic to half-filled walls rather than driven by stripe fluctuations. Across intervening antiferromagnetic domains, however, pairing correlations become unresolved at one stripe period and beyond. Both parameter transfer to larger lattices without reoptimization and deep benchmark checkpoints retain stripe spin order without resolving long-range pairing, within uncertainties allowing weak superconductivity. Half-filled antiphase walls thus generate local $d$-wave pairs, whereas suppressing static stripe order alone yields no resolved coherence across stripes in the states studied.

</details>


### [374] [Edge-scaling softening of a universal machine-learned potential: point-defect formation energies in a coronene graphene quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16528)
*Andrii Tuzhykov,Vladimir Soloviev,Arnold Kiv,Andrii Bielinskyi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以晕苯（coronene, C24H12）为基准，将通用机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）SevenNet与自旋极化PBE DFT对比，发现SevenNet系统性低估空缺缺陷形成能，且低估幅度随配位不足程度增大，但在Stone-Wales缺陷与对称等价位点上表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势（universal MLIPs）被广泛用作密度泛函理论（DFT）的高通量替代方案，但已有报道指出训练数据对高能、远离平衡构型覆盖不足时会出现系统性“软化”（softening）。本文旨在考察该行为在石墨烯量子点（GQDs）配位不足边缘位点的表现，此处空位可能呈现明显的分子自由基（molecular-radical）特征。

Method: 采用具有D6h对称性与三类碳位点的六方石墨烯量子点晕苯（coronene, C24H12），将SevenNet（7net-omni-i12）与自旋极化PBE DFT（GPAW）在单空位、双空位和Stone-Wales缺陷上进行基准对比；除全位点与键分辨势能面外，还进行了受控的匹配重构比较，以自洽化学势储层（self-consistent chemical-potential reservoirs）对28个空位与双空位构型进行评分。

Result: SevenNet低估了全部28个空位与双空位形成能，平均亏损随配位不足程度增大：单空位为0.30–0.74 eV，双空位为0.65–1.11 eV（随断裂边缘C-H键数增加）；而Stone-Wales能量平均差异仅0.013 eV。SevenNet还保持了三个氢依赖重构盆地的排序，并将D6h等价位点还原至数meV以内。

Conclusion: 在本晕苯基准下，结果支持基于MLIP的结构筛选与初步排序，但绝对空位能量与多盆地边缘构型仍需DFT验证；类内对称性离散度（within-class symmetry spread）可作为盆地敏感弛豫的实用诊断指标。

Abstract: Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are increasingly used as computationally efficient surrogates for density functional theory (DFT) in high-throughput nanomaterials screening. Systematic softening has been reported for several universal MLIPs when high-energy and far-from-equilibrium configurations are underrepresented in training data. We examine how this behavior appears at under-coordinated edge sites in graphene quantum dots (GQDs), where a vacancy can have pronounced molecular-radical character. Using coronene (C$_{24}$H$_{12}$), a hexagonal GQD with $D_{6h}$ symmetry and three carbon classes, we benchmark SevenNet (7net-omni-i12) against spin-polarized PBE DFT (GPAW) for monovacancy, divacancy, and Stone-Wales defects. Beyond the full site- and bond-resolved landscapes, we perform a controlled matched-reconstruction comparison in which 28 vacancy and divacancy configurations are scored by both engines using self-consistent chemical-potential reservoirs. In this dataset, SevenNet underestimates all 28 vacancy and divacancy formation energies, and the mean deficit increases with under-coordination within each defect family: 0.30-0.74 eV for monovacancies and 0.65-1.11 eV for divacancies as the number of broken edge C-H bonds increases. By contrast, the mean Stone-Wales energies differ by only 0.013 eV. SevenNet also preserves the ordering of three hydrogen-dependent reconstruction basins and reproduces $D_{6h}$-equivalent sites to within a few meV. The within-class symmetry spread therefore provides a practical diagnostic of basin-sensitive relaxations. Within the present coronene benchmark, these results support MLIP-based structure screening and preliminary ranking, while absolute vacancy energies and multi-basin edge configurations still require DFT validation.

</details>


### [375] [Bulk CMSX-4 Single-Crystal Builds via Laser-Directed Energy Deposition: Epitaxial Stability and Microstructure Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16535)
*Divya Nalajala,Swapnil Bhure,R. Ajithkannan,Shubhang Galagali,Abhik Choudhury*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合原位红外热反馈驱动的激光功率动态调节与仿真引导的外部冷却系统，在激光定向能量沉积（L-DED）中实现镍基高温合金长圆柱棒的单晶连续外延生长，并优化热处理以缩短固溶时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光定向能量沉积（laser-directed energy deposition, L-DED）制备镍基高温合金单晶时，多工艺参数耦合作用使单晶生长极难维持，需要发展可行的工艺控制策略。

Method: 将基于原位红外热反馈（in-situ infrared thermal feedback）的激光功率动态调节，与仿真引导（simulation-guided）的外部冷却系统相结合，以优化增材制造过程中的热量提取，并在凝固界面维持近稳态条件。

Result: 该组合策略使连续外延生长高度提升至仅靠工艺控制时的近两倍；电子背散射衍射（electron backscatter diffraction, EBSD）验证了连续单晶生长，近恒定的一次枝晶臂间距表明凝固处于稳态。热处理固溶时间缩短47%，共晶溶解更快，时效后析出物分布与经典布里奇曼（Bridgman）法样品相近。

Conclusion: 热反馈功率调节与外部冷却的协同控制可有效维持L-DED单晶生长并提升成形高度，同时经优化的热处理可获得与Bridgman工艺相当的时效组织。

Abstract: Single-crystal processing of Ni-based superalloys using laser-directed energy deposition (L-DED) is extremely challenging, given the interplay of a number of processing parameters. In this work, we develop strategies to maintain single-crystal growth in long cylindrical rod geometries by combining dynamic adjustment of laser power, informed by in-situ infrared thermal feedback, with a simulation-guided external cooling system. This combined approach ensures optimal heat extraction during additive manufacturing, maintaining near-steady-state conditions at the solidification interface and extending continuous epitaxial growth to nearly twice the build height achievable through process control alone. The developed strategy results in continuous single-crystal growth, as validated by electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD). The steady-state nature of solidification is revealed through the near-constant primary dendritic arm spacing. Further, the heat-treatment is optimized by reducing the solutionizing time (by 47\%), resulting in faster eutectic dissolution. Comparison of the aged microstructures reveals a similar distribution of precipitates between the classical Bridgman- and L-DED-processed samples.

</details>


### [376] [Dislocation Arrays Hosting Topological Bound States in Epitaxial Tetragonal FeSe Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16681)
*Huimin Zhang,Basu Oli,Zhengfei Wang,Michael Weinert,Lian Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该摘要指出实空间拓扑缺陷（topological defects）可承载稳健的局域量子态（localized quantum states），可用于量子信息处理与传感，但在晶态材料中确定性控制其形成仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于拓扑缺陷所承载的局域量子态具备稳健性，对量子信息处理（quantum information processing）与量子传感（quantum sensing）有应用价值；然而缺陷在晶态材料（crystalline materials）中的形成尚缺乏确定性调控手段。

Method: 摘要未提及具体研究方法或技术路线，仅陈述了问题背景与研究缺口。

Result: 摘要未给出实验结果或定量结论；仅以“仍具挑战”概括当前对缺陷形成控制的现状。

Conclusion: 可推断该工作旨在解决晶态材料中拓扑缺陷形成的确定性控制问题，但摘要未披露具体结论与验证方式，需查阅正文确认。

Abstract: Topological defects in real space can host robust, localized quantum states for quantum information processing and sensing, yet deterministic control over their formation in crystalline materials remain challenging.

</details>


### [377] [Metric random tilings: exact entropy and the diffraction of counted disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16730)
*Xinyan Wu,Qingqing Ji*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文提出“度规随机镶嵌（metric random tilings）”——一种拓扑锁定但边长在严格面闭合约束下波动的周期网络，使几何本身成为可计数对象；通过对Kepler十一种均匀网络的枚举，区分出七种零熵与四种正度规熵载体，并在衍射中给出度规无序的可辨识特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镶嵌数学曾两度成为物质状态的语言（晶体与准晶），但其计数变量是排布方式而非几何，而度规恰是化学设计与衍射测量所设定的量；本文旨在让几何本身进入精确计数范式。

Method: 引入度规随机镶嵌：拓扑锁定的周期网络，其边长在精确面闭合（exact face closure）条件下涨落；用专门构建的计数引擎（counting engines）枚举Kepler的十一种均匀网络并分类，每种正熵载体均以显式构造加以认证；再结合衍射分析，检验熵与Bragg强度相干分数随值域的变化。

Result: 十一种均匀网络被分为七种零熵与四种正度规熵载体；在蜂窝网络（honeycomb）上，所考虑的所有边长值域均具广延熵，算术类达每格 s = 0.544 k_B（拟合区间0.53–0.58），另两类约0.2；衍射中值域的平移式增大使熵精确不变，而固定斑块尺寸下Bragg强度相干分数从9×10⁻⁴变至0.58，遵循振幅流的Debye–Waller定律。

Conclusion: 度规无序由此进入精确计数范式，并可通过被度规晕（metric halos）环绕的Bragg斑点在实际材料中被辨识。

Abstract: Tiling mathematics has twice become the language of a state of matter: crystals, then quasicrystals, whose thermodynamics are described by the random tiling of fixed shapes. There the counted variable is the arrangement rather than the geometry, although the metric is what chemical design sets and diffraction measures. Here we introduce metric random tilings, periodic networks of locked topology whose edge lengths fluctuate under exact face closure, so that the geometry itself is countable. Purpose-built counting engines enumerate Kepler's eleven uniform networks and divide them into seven zero-entropy ones and four carriers with positive metric entropy, each certified by an explicit construction. On the honeycomb, the densest carrier, all edge-length value domains considered carry extensive entropy, with the arithmetic class reaching $s = 0.544\,k_B$ per tile (fit band 0.53--0.58), the other two $\approx 0.2$. In diffraction, translational increase of the value domain leaves the entropy exactly invariant while the coherent fraction of the Bragg intensity at fixed patch size varies from $9\times10^{-4}$ to 0.58, following the Debye--Waller law of the amplitude flow. Metric disorder thus enters the exact-counting paradigm, and is identifiable in real materials by Bragg spots wrapped in metric halos.

</details>


### [378] [Tailored magnetic domain-wall ratchet effects through density gradients in triangular-hole arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16735)
*Julia Herrero-Albillos,María Ángeles Laguna-Marco,María Vélez,Xabier Zubizarreta-Iriarte,Daniel Arechavaleta,Aurelio Hierro-Rodriguez,Carlos Quirós,Michael Foerster,Cristina Bran,Arantxa Fraile-Rodriguez,José Ignacio Martín*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在密度梯度三角孔阵列的非晶Co0.86Zr0.14薄膜中，孔取向、密度梯度与磁静相互作用共同调控畴壁（domain wall, DW）传播，梯度可显著降低脱钉场随机性并实现确定性调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究工程化密度梯度孔阵列如何影响非晶磁性薄膜中畴壁的传播与形貌，并寻求对DW运动的确定性控制途径。

Method: 采用Kerr显微镜与高分辨XMCD-PEEM成像观测DW动力学与形貌；比较"协同（cooperative）"与"竞争（competing）"两种孔取向—梯度构型；并用刚性DW模型（rigid DW model）与含孔间距及阵列周期性的经验标度律描述脱钉场。

Result: 协同构型中密度梯度强化几何棘轮效应（geometric ratchet effect），竞争构型中两机制相互对抗形成反向棘轮并实现后退DW传播的实验观测；密度梯度阵列较周期阵列大幅降低脱钉场（depinning field）的随机波动，DW运动高度可重复。

Conclusion: 工程化密度梯度为DW传播的确定性控制提供了有效途径，在自旋电子学（spintronics）与神经形态器件（neuromorphic devices）中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: We investigate domain wall (DW) dynamics and morphology in amorphous Co$_{0.86}$Zr$_{0.14}$ thin films patterned with engineered, density-graded triangular-hole arrays. Using Kerr microscopy and high-resolution XMCD-PEEM imaging, we demonstrate that DW propagation is governed by the interplay between hole orientation, density gradients, and local magnetostatic interactions. In cooperative configurations, the density gradient reinforces the geometric ratchet effect, whereas in competing configurations, the two mechanisms oppose each other, leading to an inverse ratchet and enabling experimental access to backward DW propagation. Remarkably, density-graded arrays substantially reduce the stochastic variability of depinning fields observed in periodic arrays, resulting in highly reproducible DW motion. The depinning fields are described by a rigid DW model and an empirical scaling law incorporating both inter-hole spacing and array periodicity. These results demonstrate that engineered density gradients provide an effective route to deterministic control of DW propagation, with potential applications in spintronic and neuromorphic devices.

</details>


### [379] [Large Pockels Effect in Multiaxial Ferroelectrics Induced by Polarization Reorientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16773)
*Zhijun Jiang,Jinyang Ni,Hong Jian Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出通过多轴铁电体（multiaxial ferroelectrics）中的极化重取向（polarization reorientation）引入结构软化，从而获得大线性电光（Pockels）效应，并用唯象理论与第一性原理计算在单层SnSe与块体KNbO₃中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大Pockels效应材料对低能耗光子器件（photonic devices）具有重要意义，但以往策略主要依赖相界、畴壁或应变诱导相变等结构软化（structural softness）机制，亟需新的设计思路。

Method: 发展唯象理论（phenomenological theory）分析多轴铁电体中极化方位角（azimuth angle）与电场的滞回关系及线性电光系数可能的发散，并对多种多轴铁电体开展第一性原理（first-principles）模拟。

Result: 理论表明在合适极化方位角处线性电光系数可能出现发散；计算预测极化重取向可显著增强单层SnSe与块体KNbO₃的电光系数。

Conclusion: 极化重取向是多轴铁电体中实现大Pockels效应的全新工程策略，多轴铁电体可作为相关应用的 promising 平台。

Abstract: Materials with large linear electro-optic (Pockels) effect have significant potential for fabricating energy-efficient photonic devices. Previous strategies have mostly relied on structural softness---associated with phase boundary, domain wall, or strain induced phase transition---to create large Pockels effect in ferroelectrics. In this Letter, we show that polarization reorientation in multiaxial ferroelectrics may create structural softness and thus induce large Pockels effect. By developing a phenomenological theory, we find that multiaxial ferroelectrics exhibit a hysteretic relationship between the azimuth angles of the polarization and the electric field, and a possible divergence of the linear electro-optic coefficient may occur at an appropriate polarization azimuth angle. This suggests a brand-new strategy for engineering large Pockels effect in multiaxial ferroelectrics. We perform first-principles simulations on several multiaxial ferroelectrics and predict that polarization reorientation can significantly enhance the electro-optic coefficients in monolayer SnSe and bulk KNbO$_3$. This validates the soundness of our strategy and emphasizes multiaxial ferroelectrics as promising platforms toward large Pockels effect.

</details>


### [380] [Systematic Evaluation of Training Data Generation Strategies for Machine Learning-Based Molecular and Materials Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16792)
*Minh Nghia Nguyen,Beate Paulus,Akhil S. Nair*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较了分子与材料性质预测中多种训练数据采集策略（从随机采样到各类主动学习），发现策略有效性高度依赖模型、数据集与预测任务的相互作用，但基于不确定性的采样总体表现最优，并据此提出在给定标注预算下选择采集策略的指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习（machine learning, ML）性质预测模型常基于任意或通用采样策略构建的大数据集训练，易导致对预测任务最关键化学与材料空间区域（chemical and materials space）的过度或不足表征；核心挑战在于如何高效筛选训练数据，在最小化所需标注样本数的同时最大化预测性能。

Method: 系统比较分子与材料性质预测的训练数据采集策略（training data acquisition），涵盖随机采样（random sampling）与多种主动学习（active learning）方法，并在不同模型类别、表征（representation）、数据集与目标性质上评估其表现。

Result: 数据采集策略的有效性强烈依赖于模型、数据集与预测任务之间的相互作用，但基于不确定性的采样（uncertainty-based sampling）总体优于其他策略。

Conclusion: 基于上述发现，研究给出了在给定标注预算（labelling budget）条件下选择数据采集策略的指导原则。

Abstract: Machine-learning (ML) models for molecular and materials property prediction are often trained on large datasets curated through arbitrary or generic sampling strategies. Such approaches can lead to the over- or under-representation of regions of chemical and materials space that are most relevant to the prediction task. An important challenge is therefore how to efficiently curate training data to maximize predictive performance while minimising the number of labelled examples required. Here, we systematically compare training data acquisition strategies for molecular and materials property prediction, ranging from random sampling to various active learning approaches. We evaluate their performance across different model classes, representations, datasets, and target properties. Our results show that the effectiveness of data acquisition strategies depend strongly on the interplay between the model, dataset, and prediction task, although uncertainty-based sampling generally outperforms alternative strategies. Based on these findings, we derive guidelines for selecting data acquisition strategies under a given labelling budget.

</details>


### [381] [Circular phonons generate negligible magnetization in insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17222)
*C-C. Wu,M. Buzzi,A. Iudica,R. Romero,G. De Vecchi,R. Merlin,A. Cavalleri*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过同时测量驱动晶体内部的偏振旋转与外部杂散磁场，澄清了圆偏振声子（circular phonons）是否伴随"真实"磁化（true magnetization）的争议：SiC中观测到的巨大偏振旋转并不对应可观的磁化，实际磁化可忽略（μ0M < 0.001 T）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆偏振声子的光学激发催生了合成磁场、拓扑序调控、铁轴态（ferroaxial states）操控等非平衡现象，但关于其是否伴随可观"真实"磁化存在重大争议。既有实验依赖探测驱动材料中的偏振旋转，受非平衡法拉第效应（Faraday effect）解读与泵浦-探测中寄生非线性光学贡献等混杂因素干扰，难以定论。

Method: 同时测量驱动材料内部的偏振旋转与其外部产生的杂散磁场（stray magnetic field），后者由邻近的法拉第探测器（Faraday detector）采集；驱动源为5 MV/cm的中红外脉冲，样品为SiC晶体。

Result: 在5 MV/cm中红外驱动下，SiC晶体内部出现巨大的偏振旋转，与先前报道一致；依不同假设，该旋转可对应高达μ0M = 0.3 T或低至4 μT的磁化。但外部杂散磁场测量明确表明SiC中的磁化可忽略不计（μ0M < 0.001 T）。

Conclusion: 内部偏振旋转不足以作为磁化的可靠判据，非平衡法拉第效应或寄生非线性光学贡献可造成巨大表观旋转；结合外部杂散磁场测量可消除歧义，确立圆偏振声子驱动下SiC磁化可忽略的结论。

Abstract: The optical excitation of circular phonons has opened up non-equilibrium phenomena with no equilibrium counterparts, ranging from the generation of synthetic magnetic fields to the control of topological order or the manipulation of ferroaxial states. In this area, significant controversy has arisen on whether sizeable "true" magnetizations accompany circular phonons. Several confounding factors cloud the interpretation of the experiments, which are based on probing polarization rotation in the driven material. These range from the interpretation of the Faraday effect in non-equilibrium conditions to spurious nonlinear optical contributions in pump probe experiments. Here we resolve these ambiguities by measuring both polarization rotation inside the driven material and the stray magnetic field generated outside it with a proximate Faraday detector. For a 5 MV/cm mid-infrared drive, we observe a colossal polarization rotation inside a driven SiC crystal, similar to what was reported in previous work. Depending on the assumptions made, this could correspond to magnetizations as high as $μ_0 M = 0.3 T$ or as low as 4 $μT$. However, when measuring the magnetic stray field outside the driven sample we unambiguously determine that the magnetization in SiC is negligibly small ($μ_0 M \lt 0.001 T$).

</details>


### [382] [SiC-BN composites with extreme thermal and environmental stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17283)
*Abhijit Biswas,Eugenio Garcia-Granados,Saman Zare,Mozhdeh Mirakhory,JungHun Park,Shuvodeep De,Jessica Reyes,Hunter B. Schonfeld,Noah A. Burke,William Riffe,Michael S. Lafferty,Sudaice Kazibwe,Maryam Amiri,Shreyasi Chattopadhyay,Mingrui Xu,Gelu Costin,Tanguy Terlier,Jianhua Li,Ching-Wu Chu,Liangzi Deng,Bharat K. Jasthi,Manoj Tripathi,Venkataramana Gadhamshetty,Gi-Dong Sim,Kathy Lu,Adri C. T. van Duin,Patrick E. Hopkins,Sanjay Sampath,Pulickel M. Ajayan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 等质量比的6H-SiC与h-BN复合陶瓷经放电等离子烧结后，在超高温氧化与热循环条件下表现出优异稳定性及温度无关的稳定热导率，具备极端环境结构材料潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端条件下需保持结构完整性并抗热震的陶瓷材料对诸多应用至关重要，而纯6H-SiC与h-BN在高速氧燃料火焰暴露下会迅速断裂或侵蚀。

Method: 采用放电等离子烧结（spark plasma sintering）制备含等质量分数六方碳化硅（6H-SiC）与六方氮化硼（h-BN）的复合材料，并结合高速氧燃料火焰（high-velocity oxygen-fuel torch）考核、微观结构表征、有限元分析（finite-element analysis）热流图与ReaxFF分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）进行研究。

Result: 复合材料在空气中经火焰暴露、表面温度超过1500 °C的长时间加热与热循环下仍保持稳定；表面形成多相二氧化硅层增强抗氧化性，本征孔隙缓解热应力，界面重构形成富Si-N/C-B键合网络提升高温界面结合力；热导率在宽温区内呈温度无关的稳定特性。

Conclusion: 该轻质、易加工的陶瓷复合材料可作为极端环境应用中有前景的结构材料。

Abstract: Ceramic materials that maintain structural integrity and resist thermal-shock under extreme conditions are critical for many applications. Here we show that spark plasma sintered composites containing equal weight fractions of hexagonal silicon carbide (6H-SiC) and boron nitride (h-BN) exhibit exceptional stability under extreme high-temperature oxidizing conditions and unusually stable temperature-independent thermal conductivity across a broad temperature range. Under high-velocity oxygen-fuel torch exposure in air, it withstands prolonged heating and thermal-cycling at surface temperatures reaching >1500 °C, whereas pure 6H-SiC and h-BN rapidly fractures or erodes. Microstructural evaluation shows the formation of a multi-phase surface silica layer that further protects the material from oxidation. Finite-element analysis heat-flux map shows that intrinsic porosity mitigates thermal stress, while ReaxFF molecular dynamics simulations reveal thermally driven interfacial reconstruction and the formation of a Si-N/C-B-rich bonding network, enhancing the interfacial cohesion at elevated temperatures. This lightweight, easily machinable ceramic composite will be a promising structural material in extreme environment applications.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [383] [Critical schemes, jet syzygies, and twisted periods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14631)
*Monica Jinwoo Kang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文研究非既约临界概型（nonreduced critical schemes），证明其结构由首阶射流方程中一个特定的极小不变 syzygy（minimal invariant syzygy）所唯一确定，并在幂零轨道闭包与 Kleinian 奇点两类情形下给出显式多项式刻画，进而统一地将 Argyres-Douglas 理论的 Schur/Macdonald 指标解释为弧空间（arc spaces）的 Hilbert 级数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既约临界轨迹（reduced critical locus）在给定支撑下不足以唯一确定概型结构，尤其在 D 型情形仅由 Betti 数无法决定概型；需寻找额外的代数数据来固定非既约结构，并与 Dubrovin 及 Feigin–Silantyev 的扭曲周期方程建立联系。

Method: 对 $\mathfrak{sl}_N$、$\mathfrak{so}_{2N}$（Coxeter 数 $h$，$p\ge2$ 与 $h$ 互素）取 $\det(1-sM)^{p/h}$ 中 $s^{p+1}$ 的系数作为选取多项式，以指定极小不变 syzygy 固定概型结构；在 $\mathbb C^2/\mathbb Z_n$ 上则用截断超几何级数（terminating hypergeometric series）给出选取多项式，并考察 Slodowy 切片限制与临界概型及其幂零根（nilradical）构造的相容性。

Result: 选取 syzygy 等价于 Dubrovin 与 Feigin–Silantyev 的扭曲周期方程（twisted period equation）；该次数的极小不变 syzygies 对应最低非二次扭曲周期，在 $\mathfrak{so}_8$ 情形构成 triality 的标准表示；限制到 Slodowy 切片与临界概型及幂零根的构造可交换。

Conclusion: 结果以与秩和次数无关的统一方式确定了相应的环与幂零根滤过（nilradical filtrations），并据此提出相应 Argyres-Douglas 理论的 Schur 与 Macdonald 指标可作为弧空间 Hilbert 级数出现。

Abstract: We study nonreduced critical schemes with prescribed support on nilpotent orbit closures and on the Kleinian singularity $\mathbb C^2/\mathbb Z_n$, and show that one specified minimal invariant syzygy among the first jet equations fixes their scheme structure. For $\mathfrak g=\mathfrak{sl}_N$ or $\mathfrak{so}_{2N}$ with Coxeter number $h$ and $p\ge2$ coprime to $h$, the selected polynomial is the coefficient of $s^{p+1}$ in $\det(1-sM)^{p/h}$, with reduced critical locus $\{M^p=0\}$, and the selecting syzygy is equivalent to the twisted period equation of Dubrovin and Feigin-Silantyev. The minimal invariant syzygies of that degree correspond to the lowest nonquadratic twisted periods, which, for $\mathfrak{so}_8$, form the standard representation of triality; in type D the Betti number alone does not determine the scheme. On $\mathbb C^2/\mathbb Z_n$, the selected polynomial is given by a terminating hypergeometric series. Restriction to Slodowy slices commutes with forming critical schemes and their nilradicals. These results determine, uniformly in rank and degree, the rings and nilradical filtrations through which the Schur and Macdonald indices of the corresponding Argyres-Douglas theories are proposed to arise as Hilbert series of arc spaces.

</details>


### [384] [Critical L-values and genus-zero Gromov--Witten invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15696)
*Wu-yen Chuang,Yi-Jing Tseng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文在有理数域上光滑射影三维簇（smooth projective threefold）中，为类型 $(3,0)+(0,3)$ 的二维有理 Hodge 分量（rational Hodge summand）建立了周期比较（period comparison），并在 Fermat 六次超曲面（Fermat sextic）上具体构造对应（correspondence）并计算其 Betti 与 de Rham 作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是建立几何周期（geometric periods）与模形式临界 $L$-值（critical modular $L$-values）之间的精确关系，并将该比较推广到具体的 Calabi–Yau 型三维簇实例上，以获得临界值求值与镜像（mirror）意义下的 Gromov–Witten 不变量结论。

Method: 给定代数投影子（algebraic projector）与一个对应（correspondence），其在有理权四新形式（weight-four newform）动机上的作用经投影后非零；限制到全纯 de Rham 线（holomorphic de Rham lines）后，动机态射（motivic morphisms）被等同于这些线之间的映射，固定有理生成元即唯一确定同调动机（homological motives）的同构。对 Fermat 六次超曲面在 $\mathbb Q$ 上构造该对应，计算其 Betti 与 de Rham 作用，并由代数迹（algebraic traces）与杯积配对（cup-product pairings）确定尺度。

Result: Betti 实现（Betti realization）给出几何周期与两个临界模 $L$-值之间的精确关系；对 Fermat 六次超曲面得到对应的显式计算，并由此推出临界值求值（critical-value evaluations）。

Conclusion: 在镜像比较（mirror comparison）与显式延拓假设（explicit continuation hypotheses）下，可进一步得到亏格为零的延续 Gromov–Witten 分拆恒等式（continued genus-zero Gromov–Witten partition identity）作为推论。

Abstract: We establish a period comparison for a two-dimensional rational Hodge summand of type $(3,0)+(0,3)$ in a smooth projective threefold over $\mathbb Q$ with $h^{3,0}=1$. Given an algebraic projector onto the summand and a correspondence whose action on the motive of a rational weight-four newform is nonzero after projection to the summand, restriction to the holomorphic de Rham lines identifies motivic morphisms with maps between these lines. Fixing rational generators of the holomorphic de Rham lines determines a unique isomorphism of homological motives. Its Betti realization then yields an exact relation between geometric periods and two critical modular $L$-values. For the Fermat sextic, we construct the correspondence over $\mathbb Q$ and compute its Betti and de Rham actions, determining the scale from algebraic traces and cup-product pairings. Critical-value evaluations and, under mirror comparison and explicit continuation hypotheses, a continued genus-zero Gromov--Witten partition identity follow as corollaries.

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [385] [Amplitude analysis of the $Λ_c^+\to n K^0_{\mathrm{S}} π^+$ decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14932)

Zheng,Y. H. Zheng,B. Zhong,C. Zhong,X. Zhong,H. Zhou,J. Q. Zhou,S. Zhou,X. Zhou,X. K. Zhou,X. R. Zhou,X. X. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,Y. X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,J. J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次对粲重子强子衰变 $\Lambda_c^+\to nK^0_S\pi^+$ 进行振幅分析，基于 BESIII 探测器 4.5 fb$^{-1}$ 的 $e^+e^-$ 湮灭数据，识别出四个中间共振态并测量其分支比乘积与衰变不对称参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粲重子（charmed baryon）强子衰变的内部共振结构此前缺乏振幅层面的实验刻画，该工作旨在通过振幅分析（amplitude analysis）分辨 $\Lambda_c^+$ 衰变至 $nK^0_S\pi^+$ 末态所经过的中间共振态并定量刻画其衰变性质。

Method: 利用 BESIII 谱仪（BESIII spectrometer）在质心能量（center-of-mass energy）4.600–4.699 GeV 采集的 $e^+e^-$ 湮灭数据，总积分亮度（integrated luminosity）为 4.5 fb$^{-1}$，对 $\Lambda_c^+\to nK^0_S\pi^+$ 进行振幅分析。

Result: 识别出四个中间共振态 $\Lambda(1830)^0$、$\Lambda(2000)^0$、$N(1440)^+$、$N(1650)^+$；测得各自分支比乘积（branching fraction product）依次约为 $(2.4\pm0.7\pm0.5\pm0.1)$、$(5.4\pm1.7\pm1.1\pm0.3)$、$(4.0\pm2.0\pm1.5\pm0.2)$、$(5.2\pm1.1\pm0.8\pm0.3)\times10^{-3}$，并给出相应的衰变不对称参数（decay asymmetry parameter）$\alpha$，其中前三项不确定度分别为统计、系统与归一化分支比贡献。

Conclusion: 该分析首次给出 $\Lambda_c^+\to nK^0_S\pi^+$ 的共振结构与分支比、不对称参数测量结果，为粲重子强子衰变机制及共振态性质研究提供实验约束；不对称参数的不确定度仍较大，需更高统计量数据加以精确检验。

Abstract: We perform the first amplitude analysis of the charmed baryon hadronic decay $Λ_c^+\to n K^0_{\mathrm{S}} π^+$. The analysis uses $e^+ e^-$ annihilation data corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of 4.5~$\rm{fb}^{-1}$ collected with the BESIII spectrometer at center-of-mass energies between 4.600 GeV and 4.699~GeV. The amplitude analysis identifies four intermediate resonances $Λ(1830)^{0}$, $Λ(2000)^{0}$, $N(1440)^{+}$ and $N(1650)^{+}$. We measure the products of branching fractions: $$\mathcal{B}(Λ_{c}^{+} \rightarrow Λ(1830)^{0}\pip)\cdot \mathcal{B}(Λ(1830)^{0} \rightarrow n \Ks) = (2.4\pm0.7\pm0.5\pm0.1)\times 10^{-3}, \mathcal{B}(Λ_{c}^{+} \rightarrow Λ(2000)^{0}\pip)\cdot \mathcal{B}(Λ(2000)^{0} \rightarrow n \Ks) = (5.4\pm1.7\pm1.1\pm0.3)\times 10^{-3}, \mathcal{B}(Λ_{c}^{+} \rightarrow N(1440)^{+}\Ks)\cdot \mathcal{B}(N(1440)^{+} \rightarrow n \pip) = (4.0\pm2.0\pm1.5\pm0.2)\times 10^{-3}, \mathcal{B}(Λ_{c}^{+} \rightarrow N(1650)^{+}\Ks)\cdot \mathcal{B}(N(1650)^{+} \rightarrow n \pip) = (5.2\pm1.1\pm0.8\pm0.3)\times 10^{-3},$$ where the first uncertainties are statistical, the second are systematic, and the third are related to the normalizing branching fraction $\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+\to n K^0_{\mathrm{S}} π^+)$. In addition, we measure the decay asymmetry parameters: $$α_{Λ(1830)^{0}\pip} = \phantom{-}0.93^{+0.07}_{-0.72}\pm0.07,α_{Λ(2000)^{0}\pip} = -0.91^{+0.56}_{-0.09}\pm0.14,α_{N(1440)^{+}\Ks} = -0.13\pm0.43\pm0.35,α_{N(1650)^{+}\Ks} = -0.42\pm0.45\pm0.29.$$

</details>


### [386] [Benchmarking Machine-Learning Top Taggers from JetClass to a Full-Simulation $Z'\to t\bar{t}$ Search: Transfer, Extrapolation, Sensitivity and Inference Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15274)
*Manisha Kumari,Amal Sarkar*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究在JetClass上以相同条件训练七种顶夸克标记器（top tagger），并将其零重训迁移至含真实堆积（pileup）的\(Z'\to t\bar{t}\)搜寻中，发现ROC曲线下面积（AUC）相近的标记器在喷注级发现显著性（discovery significance）上可相差达三倍，粒子变换器（particle transformer）可将质量可达范围延伸约400 GeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习顶夸克标记器多在不含堆积（pileup）的快速模拟样本、窄喷注横动量（\(p_T\)）窗口下比较，基准排名难以反映其对真实物理搜寻的实际增益，因此需要以端到端方式评估标记器对搜寻灵敏度的影响。

Method: 在JetClass上以完全相同的条件训练七种标记器（提升决策树（boosted decision tree）、两种图像型网络、ParticleNet及ParT、UParT、GloParT三种粒子变换器），随后不重训直接应用于基于2016年CMS开放数据（Geant4模拟、含真实堆积）的\(Z'\to t\bar{t}\)搜寻，共振质量\(m_{Z'}\)取1.2–2.5 TeV。

Result: 标记器的整体基准排序得以保持，但AUC相近者在喷注级发现显著性上差异可达三倍；粒子变换器将搜寻质量可达范围扩展400 GeV，且优势随共振质量增大，该增益出现在完全并合（fully merged）的顶夸克喷注中，并与训练范围以上喷注\(p_T\)处的稳定表现相关；堆积影响较小，小型卷积网络以显著更低算力取得有竞争力的判别能力。

Conclusion: 标记器组间差距在重新训练后及统计不确定度内依然成立；标记器的选择应基于其运动学覆盖（kinematic coverage）、分析工作点（working point）、发现灵敏度与计算成本，而不能仅凭基准分数。

Abstract: Searches for heavy resonances decaying to \(t\bar{t}\) test physics beyond the Standard Model at the TeV scale, and their reach depends on how well boosted top jets are identified. Machine-learning top taggers are usually compared on fast-simulation samples without pileup and in a narrow jet-\(p_T\) window, so a benchmark ranking does not directly show how much each tagger adds to a real search. This study trains seven taggers under identical conditions on JetClass. They are a boosted decision tree, two image-based networks, ParticleNet, and three particle transformers (ParT, UParT, and GloParT). The taggers are then applied without retraining to a \(Z'\to t\bar{t}\) search with \(m_{Z'}\) between 1.2 and 2.5~TeV, using 2016 CMS Open Data simulated with Geant4 and realistic pileup. The overall benchmark order of the taggers is preserved. However, taggers with similar area under the ROC curve (AUC) differ by up to a factor of three in jet-level discovery significance. The particle transformers extend the mass reach of the search by \(400~\mathrm{GeV}\), and their lead grows with resonance mass. This gain is seen in fully merged top jets and is associated with stable performance at jet \(p_T\) above the training range. Pileup has only a minor effect on all taggers. A small convolutional network achieves competitive discrimination at a much lower computational cost. The gaps between tagger groups hold under retraining and within statistical uncertainties. The results show that a tagger must be chosen on its kinematic coverage, the working point of the analysis, its discovery sensitivity and its cost, and not on its benchmark score alone.

</details>


### [387] [Adaptive Matrix-Based Denoising Algorithm Using Local Spatial Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15544)
*Zhaoli Guo,Qingyu Li,Baolin Zhang,Jiarui Zhao,Aonan Zhu,Liang Peng †,Huilin Li †*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 面向高亮度对撞机设施中高事例率、高本底条件下高层次触发（HLT）的在线数据处理需求，提出一种基于局部空间支撑的自适应矩阵去噪算法，并在超级陶粲装置（STCF）主漂移室（MDC）中实现与测试。测试表明算法在离散点簇本底抑制与真击中保留上均达到HLT上游去噪模块的严格要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亮度对撞机设施带来高事例率与高本底，对高层次触发（high-level trigger, HLT）的在线数据处理能力构成挑战，亟需在前端提供高效、可预测的在线去噪环节。

Method: 提出基于局部空间支撑（local spatial support）的自适应矩阵（adaptive matrix）去噪算法，在超级陶粲装置（Super Tau-Charm Facility, STCF）主漂移室（main drift chamber, MDC）中实现，并使用OSCAR软件模拟的五个带电末态物理通道进行测试验证。

Result: 在低至中等本底水平下，离散点簇本底（discrete point-cluster background）的剔除率超过90%，真击中保留率（true-hit retention ratio）保持在98%以上；即使探测器效率降至90%，真击中保留率仍高于98%。

Conclusion: 算法满足HLT上游去噪模块对平均处理时间低且可预测、高精度与高吞吐量的严格要求；由于滤波判据源自探测器击中点间的局部空间关系而非预定义径迹参数或特定物理过程模型，该算法为规则结构探测器中的在线去噪提供了通用研究思路。

Abstract: To address the online data-processing requirements of the high-level trigger (HLT) under high-event-rate and high-background conditions in high-luminosity collider facilities, this paper proposes an adaptive matrix-based denoising algorithm using local spatial support. The algorithm is implemented and tested in the main drift chamber (MDC) of the Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF). Based on tests with five charged-final-state physics channels simulated using the Offline Software of the Super Tau Charm Facility (OSCAR), the rejection rate for discrete point-cluster backgrounds exceeds 90% at low to moderate background levels, while the true-hit retention ratio remains above 98%. Even when the detector efficiency is reduced to 90%, the true-hit retention ratio remains above 98%. These results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm satisfies the stringent online-processing constraints of an HLT upstream denoising module, including low and predictable average processing time, high precision, and high throughput. Because its filtering criterion is derived from local spatial relationships among detector hits rather than predefined track parameters or a model of a specific physical process, the proposed algorithm provides a general research approach for online denoising in regularly structured detectors used in high-luminosity collider facilities.

</details>


### [388] [Observation of the $η$ $\to$ $μ^+μ^-π^+π^-$ decay and an improved measurement of the $η$ $\to$ $μ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-$ branching fraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15973)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验利用13.6 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据首次观测到稀有衰变η→μ⁺μ⁻π⁺π⁻，并高精度测量了两个η衰变分支比比值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有η介子衰变（rare η decay）的分支比测量可用于检验强相互作用与电磁相互作用的理论预言，但此前实验数据有限，尤其是η→μ⁺μ⁻π⁺π⁻尚未被观测到。

Method: 基于CERN LHC上CMS探测器（CMS experiment）于2022—2023年采集的质心能量（center-of-mass energy）13.6 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据，对应积分亮度（integrated luminosity）62.6 fb⁻¹，分析并测量相关衰变的分支比比值（branching fraction ratio）。

Result: 测得ℬ(η→μ⁺μ⁻π⁺π⁻)/ℬ(η→μ⁺μ⁻) = [2.89 ± 0.48 (stat) ₋₀.₂₉⁺⁰.⁴² (syst)]×10⁻³，ℬ(η→μ⁺μ⁻μ⁺μ⁻)/ℬ(η→μ⁺μ⁻) = [0.86 ± 0.06 (stat) ₋₀.₀₈⁺⁰.⁰⁹ (syst)]×10⁻³；后者精度较此前测量提升44%。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了对稀有衰变η→μ⁺μ⁻π⁺π⁻的观测，并给出精度显著提高的四μ子衰变分支比比值，为η介子衰变性质的进一步理论与实验研究提供了更精确的约束。

Abstract: An observation of the rare decay $η$ $\to$ $μ^+μ^-π^+π^-$ is reported from proton-proton collision data at a center-of-mass energy of 13.6 TeV, collected by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC in 2022 and 2023, and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 62.6 fb$^{-1}$. In addition, the branching fraction ratio of $η$ $\to$ $μ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-$ relative to $η$ $\to$ $μ^+μ^-$ is measured with a precision improved by 44% compared with the previous measurement. The measured branching fraction ratios are $\mathcal{B}(η\toμ^+μ^-π^+π^-)/\mathcal{B}(η\toμ^+μ^-)$ = [2.89 $\pm$ 0.48 (stat) $^{+0.42}_{-0.29}$ (syst)]$\times$ 10$^{-3}$ and $\mathcal{B}(η\toμ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-)/\mathcal{B}(η\toμ^+μ^-)$ = [0.86 $\pm$ 0.06 (stat) $^{+0.09}_{-0.08}$ (syst)]$\times$10$^{-3}$.

</details>


### [389] [Multi-approach Standard Model Effective Field Theory analyses of top-quark associated production in leptonic final states at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16347)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS利用LHC Run 2的140 fb⁻¹质子-质子碰撞数据，对顶夸克关联产生过程进行标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）联合解释，同时拟合33个六维Wilson系数，结果与标准模型一致并给出95%置信区间限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在用统一框架对多个顶夸克关联产生过程开展SMEFT解释，并系统比较不同分析策略对Wilson系数敏感度的影响，为日益复杂的SMEFT解释设计提供依据。

Method: 对t tbar γ、t tbar Z、t tbar W、t tbar H、t tbar t tbar、tZq、tWZ、tHq和tHW等末态进行同时似然拟合（simultaneous likelihood fit），统一重建末态对象并一致处理探测器层次的SMEFT效应；比较两种策略：基于重建对象计数的通用事件分类，以及针对单个过程优化的正交区域组合方法。

Result: 获得影响顶夸克相互作用的六维SMEFT算符Wilson系数限制，涵盖顶-玻色子、四夸克和顶-轻子算符及复系数贡献；结果未显示相对标准模型的显著偏离，并在同时拟合中给出33个Wilson系数的95%置信区间上限。

Conclusion: 两种策略比较为SMEFT解释设计提供新见解；针对单个过程的更复杂方法对多数Wilson系数给出最佳敏感度。

Abstract: Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) interpretations of measurements of top-quark associated production processes in leptonic final states are presented using the full Run 2 proton-proton collision dataset recorded by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. A simultaneous likelihood fit is performed across final states sensitive to $t\bar{t}γ$, $t\bar{t}Z$, $t\bar{t}W$, $t\bar{t}H$, $t\bar{t}t\bar{t}$, $tZq$, $tWZ$, $tHq$, and $tHW$ production, with harmonised reconstructed final-state objects and a coherent treatment of detector-level SMEFT effects across all contributing processes. Two complementary analysis strategies are investigated: an approach employing a more generic event categorisation based on counting reconstructed objects, and a more complex approach combining orthogonal regions optimised to specifically identify individual processes, as employed by previous dedicated measurements. Constraints are obtained on the Wilson coefficients of dimension-six SMEFT operators affecting top-quark interactions, including top-boson, four-quark and top-lepton operators, together with complex operator contributions. The results do not show a significant deviation from the Standard Model expectation and 95% confidence interval limits are placed in a simultaneous fit to 33 SMEFT Wilson coefficients. The comparison of the two analysis strategies provides new insight into the design of increasingly complex SMEFT interpretations. The second, more complex, approach targeting individual processes results in the best sensitivity to the majority of Wilson coefficients.

</details>


### [390] [Light-Meson Spectroscopy at COMPASS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16397)
*David Spülbeck*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本报告基于衍射产生的 $\omega\pi^0\pi^-$、$K_S^0K^-$、$\pi^-\pi^+\pi^-\eta$ 末态，提取共振参数，重点报道最轻的混杂介子（hybrid meson）候选态 $\pi_1(1600)$（$J^{PC}=1^{-+}$）在多个衰变道的信号，以及奇异数扇区 $J^P=0^-$ 中超出夸克模型预言的额外态，并首次实现基于事例的双雷吉（double-Regge）模型似然拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于系统确定衍射产生末态对应的共振参数，特别是寻找自旋奇异（spin-exotic）量子数 $J^{PC}=1^{-+}$ 的最轻混杂介子候选态，并检验夸克模型（quark model）在奇异数扇区的预言是否完备，同时加深对非共振产生机制的理解。

Method: 采用对衍射产生末态的分波/共振参数分析，在 $b_1(1235)\pi$、$f_1(1285)\pi$、$\eta^\prime\pi$ 等衰变道中寻找 $\pi_1(1600)$ 信号；并对高质量区 $\eta^{(\prime)}\pi$ 数据中的非共振双雷吉交换（double-Regge exchange）过程进行基于事例的似然拟合（event-based likelihood fit）。

Result: 在多个衰变道中观测到 $\pi_1(1600)$ 的信号；在 $J^P=0^-$ 的奇异数扇区发现一个相对于夸克模型预言属于“超额”的态（supernumerary state）；双雷吉模型的首次基于事例似然拟合给出了对非共振产生机制的改进描述。

Conclusion: 结果为最轻混杂介子候选态提供了多通道证据，并提示奇异数扇区 $0^-$ 态谱可能超出朴素夸克模型预期；所发展的基于事例的双雷吉拟合方法有助于厘清轻介子谱学（light-meson spectroscopy）中的非共振产生机制。

Abstract: We report on our latest findings from studying the diffractively produced $ωπ^0π^-$, $K_S^0K^-$, and $π^-π^+π^-η$ final states and determining the associated resonance parameters. A particular focus is on the signal of the lightest hybrid meson candidate with spin-exotic quantum numbers $J^{PC}=1^{-+}$, i.e. the $π_1(1600)$, which we observe in different decay channels including $b_1(1235)π$, $f_1(1285)π$, and $η^\primeπ$. Furthermore, we highlight the observation of a supernumerary state with respect to predictions of quark models in the strange sector with $J^P=0^-$. In addition, the analysis of the non-resonant double-Regge exchange process using $η^{(\prime)}π$ data in the high-mass region is discussed. It is the first event-based likelihood fit of a double-Regge model and improves our understanding of non-resonant production mechanisms in light-meson spectroscopy.

</details>


### [391] [Performance of the ATLAS tau trigger in Run 3 and future prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16454)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍ATLAS实验触发层面用于筛选强子衰变τ轻子（hadronically decaying tau leptons）事件的算法与选择策略，并基于大型强子对撞机（LHC）Run 3 阶段 √s = 13.6 TeV 的质子-质子对撞数据评估强子τ触发（hadronic tau trigger）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高事例率条件下需在触发层面高效筛选强子衰变τ末态，因此须明确触发算法与选择策略，并量化其效率及蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）模拟修正，以保证物理分析的可靠性。

Method: 描述ATLAS触发层面筛选强子衰变τ事例的算法与选择；利用Run 3（√s = 13.6 TeV）质子-质子对撞数据测量触发效率，并用2022与2023年富含强子τ末态的数据推导模拟触发效率修正因子（correction factors）；同时借助2026年数据在部分τ触发测试链（test chains）中评估改进方案。

Result: 单一τ触发效率曲线平台区（plateau region）效率为90–98%；模拟触发效率修正因子在0.95–1.05之间，在较宽横向动量（transverse momentum）范围内不确定度为2–3%；并对触发层强子τ候选鉴别（identification）的潜在改进及其性能进行了讨论。

Conclusion: 强子τ触发在Run 3条件下表现出高效率与可控的系统不确定度，所导出的修正因子可用于模拟数据的校准；相关鉴别改进已进入测试阶段，有望进一步提升触发层性能。

Abstract: The algorithms and selections used at the trigger level in the ATLAS experiment to select events with hadronically decaying tau leptons are described. The performance of the hadronic tau trigger is presented using proton-proton collision data recorded by the ATLAS experiment in Run 3 at $\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV at the Large Hadron Collider. In the plateau region of the efficiency curve for a given single tau trigger, efficiency varies between 90-98%. Correction factors for the simulated tau trigger efficiency in Monte Carlo samples were derived using 2022 and 2023 data enriched with hadronically decaying tau lepton final states, and these range from 0.95-1.05 with an uncertainty of 2-3% over a wide range of the transverse momentum of the hadronic tau candidates. In addition, potential improvements and their performance for the identification of hadronic tau candidates at trigger level are discussed using data recorded in 2026, having been implemented in selected tau trigger test chains.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: NA62实验基于2023–2024年新数据给出\(K^+\to\pi^+\nu\bar\nu\)衰变分支比的新测量，数据集翻倍后对标准模型预期过程达到5\(\sigma\)灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该衰变的分支比在标准模型中可被极精确预言（小于\(10^{-10}\)），是探测最高质量标度新物理间接效应的最佳候选之一，因而需要实验精确测量其分支比。

Method: 由位于CERN SPS的NA62实验专门测量\(K^+\to\pi^+\nu\bar\nu\)的分支比，本工作基于2023–2024年数据集给出新的分支比测量结果。

Result: 新数据集使总数据量翻倍，对标准模型（Standard Model）预期过程实现5\(\sigma\)的预期灵敏度（expected sensitivity）。

Conclusion: 数据集规模的扩大显著提升了实验灵敏度，使NA62对\(K^+\to\pi^+\nu\bar\nu\)标准模型过程的观测达到5\(\sigma\)预期灵敏度水平，为检验新物理间接效应提供了更强约束。

Abstract: The decay $K^+\rightarrowπ^+ν\barν$ with a very precisely predicted branching ratio of less than $10^{-10}$ is one of the best candidates to reveal indirect effects of new physics at the highest mass scales. The NA62 experiment at the CERN SPS is designed to measure the branching ratio of the $K^+\rightarrowπ^+ν\barν$ decay. A new measurement of its branching ratio based on the 20230-2024 dataset is presented. This doubles the total dataset, leading to a 5$σ$ expected sensitivity for the Standard Model process.

</details>

### [393] [Resolving Radiogenic Heterogeneity in the Earth's Mantle with Multi-Site Geo-neutrino Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16715)
*Michael Leyton,Stephen T. Dye*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 全球拟合四个地下站点的地球中微子（geo-neutrino）数据，得到均匀地幔放射性产热约18.5±5.6 TW；但江门与神冈的推断地幔信号存在张力，且六类非均匀地幔模型均无法解释该差异，提示其为局域效应或统计涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在利用多地点的地球中微子测量约束地球地幔（mantle）中放射性元素的总量与空间分布，并检验不同站点推断的地幔信号之间是否存在统计上显著的差异。

Method: 联合拟合Kamioka、Gran Sasso、Sudbury、Jiangmen四个站点的信号率与站点相关岩石圈（lithosphere）贡献预测；构建六类含异常体或储库的非均匀地幔模型（以亏损地幔DM为背景，总量由全硅酸盐地球质量平衡与Th/U地球化学约束确定），共定义并测试968,274种模型配置，进行约三百万次拟合。

Result: 在允许岩石圈预测在其不确定度内变化时，均匀地幔信号率为S_mantle=14.8±4.5 NIU，对应放射成因产热18.5±5.6 TW，以约3.2σ显著度被分辨；但江门与神冈推断的地幔信号之间存在明显张力，而所有统一适用于四个站点的合理幔/岩石圈模型均无法再现该差异。

Conclusion: 仅依赖站点局域的特定设定才能达到观测到的差异，说明神冈—江门张力源于局域效应或统计散差，而非大尺度地幔结构；该结果对利用地球中微子反演地幔放射性分布的大尺度模型构成约束。

Abstract: We present a global fit to geo-neutrino measurements at four underground sites (Kamioka, Gran Sasso, Sudbury, Jiangmen), combining signal rates with site-specific predictions of the lithospheric contribution to constrain the quantity and distribution of radioactivity in the Earth's mantle. Assuming a uniform distribution of radioactivity in the mantle, we find a geo-neutrino rate of $S_\text{mantle} = 14.8 \pm 4.5$ NIU when the lithospheric predictions are allowed to vary within their uncertainties. This uniform mantle signal rate corresponds to radiogenic heating of $18.5 \pm 5.6$ TW.
  Although the uniform-mantle signal is resolved at $\sim3.2σ$, there is noticeable tension between the inferred mantle signals at Jiangmen and Kamioka, which may indicate non-uniform geo-neutrino production in the mantle. We explore six families of models to describe the heterogeneous spatial distribution of radiogenic elements in the Earth's mantle. Each model contains anomalous bodies or reservoirs carrying an excess of heat-producing elements over a depleted mantle (DM) background, with total inventory set by bulk-silicate-Earth mass balance and by a geochemical Th/U constraint. For each model, we compute the geo-response at each detector site and fit the resulting signals to the four measurements. A total of 968,274 individual model configurations are defined and tested. The results of $\sim$3 million distinct fits show that no plausible mantle or lithosphere model applied uniformly to all four sites reproduces the difference between the inferred mantle signals at the Jiangmen and Kamioka sites. Only local, site-specific prescriptions are able to reach the observed difference, suggesting that the Kamioka-Jiangmen tension is due to a local effect or statistical scatter, rather than large-scale mantle structure.

</details>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [394] [Structure-Preserving Integrators for Locally Conformally Symplectic Systems: Variational Methods and Globalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17256)
*Ayten Gezici,Melvin Leok,Cristina Sardón*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文为局部共形辛（locally conformally symplectic, LCS）流形上的结构保持数值积分建立了Hamilton变分框架，给出局部离散右Hamilton量在坐标图重叠处的相容变换律，并证明其等价于局部离散Hamilton映射的等变性，从而保证全局化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LCS结构虽局部共形于普通辛结构，但局部辛描述未必来自单一整体辛形式，因此局部辛离散化须在图重叠处相容才能定义整体数值动力学，形成几何数值积分（geometric numerical integration）中的局部到整体问题。

Method: 先在适应Type II Hamilton变分原理（Type II Hamiltonian variational principle）的形式下回顾连续理论；再为局部辛代表引入精确与近似的离散右Hamilton量，并识别其在重叠处必须满足的变换律；由此导出相容性条件及全局化充分条件。

Result: 相容性条件推出局部离散Hamilton映射的等变性，构成全局化的充分条件；所得全局离散映射以由局部共形势决定的离散共形因子共形保持LCS二维形式。框架适用于Type II Hamilton变分积分器，并在余切型情形下适用于辛Runge–Kutta方法，Gauss–Legendre方法与隐式中点法则给出全局相容的LCS结构保持格式；在适当正则性假设下经离散Legendre变换可恢复相应Lagrange表述。

Conclusion: 数值算例说明了重叠相容性、坐标图切换、收敛性与离散共形保持；该工作为LCS系统提供了一类可全局化的结构保持积分器构造途径。

Abstract: Locally conformally symplectic (LCS) manifolds are characterized by a nondegenerate two-form $Ω$ satisfying $\mathrm{d}Ω=\varphi\wedgeΩ$, where $\varphi$ is a closed one-form, called the Lee form. Although an LCS structure is locally conformal to an ordinary symplectic structure, the local symplectic descriptions need not arise from a single global symplectic form. This creates a natural local-to-global problem for geometric numerical integration: local symplectic discretizations must be compatible on chart overlaps in order to determine a globally defined numerical dynamics. We develop a Hamiltonian variational framework for the construction and globalization of structure-preserving integrators for LCS systems. The continuous theory is first recalled in a form adapted to a Type~II Hamiltonian variational principle. We then introduce exact and approximate discrete right Hamiltonians for the local symplectic representatives and identify the transformation law that they must satisfy on overlaps. This compatibility condition implies equivariance of the associated local discrete Hamiltonian maps and therefore provides a sufficient condition for their globalization. The resulting global discrete map preserves the LCS two-form conformally, with a discrete conformal factor determined by the local conformal potentials. The framework applies naturally to Type~II Hamiltonian variational integrators and, in the cotangent-type setting considered here, to symplectic Runge--Kutta methods. In particular, Gauss--Legendre methods and the implicit midpoint rule yield globally compatible LCS structure-preserving schemes. Under suitable regularity assumptions, the corresponding Lagrangian formulation is recovered through discrete Legendre transformations. Numerical examples illustrate overlap compatibility, chart switching, convergence, and discrete conformal preservation.

</details>


<div id='math.CT'></div>

# math.CT [[Back]](#toc)

### [395] [Higher Categorical Bistellar Moves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17244)
*Aleksandar Ivanov*

Main category: math.CT

TL;DR: 本文对任意维数 $n$ 构造了以单形为生成元、以双星变换（bistellar moves）为关系的 $n$-范畴（$n$-category）$\mathscr{P}_n$，并论证其到理论 $n$-范畴的函子（functor）为 TQFT 的 orbifold 构造提供正确输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 TQFT orbifold 构造的相关例子均局限于 $n \leq 3$ 维，缺乏统一的高维框架，本文旨在给出适用于任意维数的构造。

Method: 以单形（simplices）为生成元、单形间的双星变换（bistellar moves）为关系，构造 $n$-范畴 $\mathscr{P}_n$；并通过研究从 $\mathscr{P}_n$ 到理论 $n$-范畴的函子（functor）来刻画 orbifold 构造所需的输入。

Result: 从 $\mathscr{P}_n$ 到理论 $n$-范畴的函子给出了 TQFT orbifold 构造的正确输入，并涵盖此前所有已知例子（均限于 $n \leq 3$）；在 3 维情形下，该构造给出某类非单位、非余单位的 Frobenius 代数（Frobenius algebra）的高度非平凡范畴化（categorification）。

Conclusion: 该构造将 TQFT orbifold 构造的范畴输入统一推广到任意维数，并在 3 维给出新的非平凡范畴化结果。

Abstract: We construct $n$-categories $\mathscr{P}_n$ with generators given by simplices and relations given by bistellar moves between them, in any dimension $n$. We show that functors out of $\mathscr{P}_n$ into an $n$-category of theories, give the correct input for the orbifold construction for TQFTs, and subsume all previously known examples, which are all in dimensions $n \leq 3$. In particular, in dimension 3 the construction gives a highly nontrivial categorification of a kind of nonunital, noncounital, Frobenius algebra.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [396] [Controlling avalanche sizes in the Abelian sandpile model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15549)
*Maike C. de Jongh,Antal A. Járai,Richard J. Boucherie,M. N. M. van Lieshout*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文将阿贝尔沙堆模型（Abelian sandpile model）中通过定向移除沙粒抑制雪崩的问题建模为平均代价马尔可夫决策过程（average-cost Markov decision process），并对局部干预策略给出结构性质与最优性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿贝尔沙堆模型是研究森林火灾、地震等大规模级联事件（cascading events）的经典框架，如何通过定向移除沙粒来控制此类雪崩（avalanches）尚缺乏系统的优化理论分析。

Method: 在每次加沙之前允许控制器从选定顶点移除一粒沙，构成序贯控制问题并表述为平均代价马尔可夫决策过程（average-cost Markov decision process）；分析“只要可能就从指定顶点集移除沙粒”的局部干预策略（local intervention policies），研究诱导马尔可夫链的常返类（recurrent classes）与平稳分布（stationary distributions）；在干预集满足 topple-freeness 性质时推导 gain 与 bias 函数及一般有限图上的最优性充要条件。

Result: 给出局部干预策略下常返类与平稳分布的结构性结果，并在 topple-freeness 条件下得到 gain 与 bias 函数的表达式以及一般有限图（finite graphs）上最优性的充要条件；对一维格点（one-dimensional lattice）、完全图（complete graph）与星图（star graph），在任意干预代价取值下完整刻画了最优策略。

Conclusion: 研究表明平均代价马尔可夫决策过程框架可有效刻画沙堆雪崩的定向控制，并在若干具体图类上实现最优策略的完全解析刻画。

Abstract: The Abelian sandpile model is a classical framework for studying large cascading events, such as those arising in models of forest fires and earthquakes. This work studies the control of such avalanches through the targeted removal of sand grains. We formulate this sequential control problem as an average-cost Markov decision process in which, before each grain addition, a controller may remove a sand grain from a selected vertex. The objective is to balance the long-run expected avalanche size against the cost of intervention. We analyze local intervention policies, which remove grains from a prescribed set of vertices whenever possible, and establish structural results for the recurrent classes and stationary distributions of the induced Markov chains. When the intervention set satisfies a property called topple-freeness, we sharpen these results, derive expressions for the gain and bias functions, and give necessary and sufficient conditions for optimality on general finite graphs. Finally, we apply the general theory to several specific graph classes and obtain a complete characterization of optimal policies on the one-dimensional lattice, the complete graph and the star graph for every value of the intervention cost.

</details>


<div id='nlin.CD'></div>

# nlin.CD [[Back]](#toc)

### [397] [Tendex Lines and Short-Time Deformation: The Geometry of Lunar Tides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16083)
*F. J. Beron-Vera*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 该文通过短时粒子变形分析，统一了Lagrangian相干结构理论中tendex线与stretchline/squeezeline的几何构造与动力学解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tendex lines与Lagrangian coherent structure（LCS）理论中的拉伸线/挤压线均来自对称张量本征向量场的积分，但其动力学含义的联系尚未明确，本文旨在建立二者的对应关系。

Method: 考虑自主引力场中相对自由落体参考系以零初速释放的粒子，分析其短时变形；利用Cauchy–Green张量对单位阵的偏离（与潮汐张量成正比、时间二次方标度），并构造以潮汐张量为度量的Lorentzian度规及其零测地线。

Result: 潮汐张量线给出极限主拉伸与挤压方向；在平面月球场中为径向线与同心圆。该Lorentzian度规的零方向对应线元长度无二次变化，其零测地线为对数螺旋线，刻画有限时间单位拉伸几何的短时极限。地球附近，主张量线澄清两种向外潮汐趋势，零方向则区分主导伸长消失与相对加速度消失。

Conclusion: 潮汐张量的本征线与零结构为tendex线及LCS拉伸/挤压几何提供了统一的短时动力学解释。

Abstract: Tendex lines and the stretchlines and squeezelines of Lagrangian coherent structure theory share a geometric construction: they integrate eigenvector fields of symmetric tensors. We connect their dynamical interpretations through the short-time deformation of particles released with zero initial velocity relative to a freely falling reference in an autonomous gravitational field. The leading departure of the Cauchy--Green tensor from the identity is quadratic in time and equals the tidal tensor multiplied by the squared observation time. The tidal tensorlines therefore give the limiting principal stretching and squeezing directions. For the planar lunar field, these tensorlines are radial lines and concentric circles centered on the Moon. The same tidal tensor defines a Lorentzian metric whose null directions have no quadratic change in line-element length. Its null geodesics are logarithmic spirals, describing the short-time limit of finite-time unit-stretch geometry. Near Earth, the principal tensorlines clarify the two outward tidal tendencies, while the null directions distinguish vanishing leading extension from vanishing relative acceleration.

</details>


<div id='physics.bio-ph'></div>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [398] [Spontaneous mechanical patterning in active elastic tissues with stress feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14943)
*Henrik Weyer,Divyoj Singh,M. Cristina Marchetti*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个极简模型，说明生物组织（biological tissues）中纤维（fiber）组织受局部主动应力（active stress）与弹性应力（elastic stress）反馈调控，可产生对外部应变的双稳态响应与正交纤维畴的自发分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物组织的主动力学（active mechanics）常由嵌入的主动收缩纤维（active contractile fibers）响应力学反馈所主导，但缺乏描述该反馈如何决定纤维组织的最小力学机制。

Method: 构建类固体组织（solid-like tissue）的极简模型（minimal model），其中纤维组织由局部主动应力与弹性应力对其施加的反馈所控制。

Result: 所得组织普遍表现出对外部应变（external strain）的双稳态响应（bistable response），并自发分离为纤维正交排列（orthogonally aligned fibers）的畴（domains）。

Conclusion: 该理论提供了一个最小力学反馈机制，可解释生物组织中普遍存在的正交结构排列（orthogonal structural alignment）。

Abstract: The active mechanics of biological tissues is often governed by embedded active contractile fibers responding to mechanical feedback. We introduce a minimal model of a solid-like tissue where the mechanics is controlled by feedback of local active and elastic stresses on fiber organization. The resulting tissue generically exhibits a bistable response to external strain and spontaneously segregates into domains of orthogonally aligned fibers. Our theory thus provides a minimal mechanical feedback mechanism generating orthogonal structural alignment ubiquitous in biological tissues.

</details>


### [399] [Feedback-induced mechanical bistability and perpendicular-domain formation in active elastic tissues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14964)
*Divyoj Singh,Henrik Weyer,Harry Kroft,M. Cristina Marchetti*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究表明，仅凭机械应力反馈即可在肌肉组织中自发产生纤维排列及正交结构域。收缩纤维沿局部应力方向排列的反馈具有自增强特性，结合边界条件与组织几何可形成双稳态及正交排列的结构域（domain）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌肉组织中的收缩纤维（contractile fibers）在跨越多个细胞的尺度上高度排列，围绕含内腔器官的肌肉层常呈正交方向分布。已有认识缺乏对结构序及其正交结构域图案化（patterning）如何仅由力学机制产生的最小解释，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将肌肉组织建模为具有应力反馈（stress feedback）的活性向列相固体（active nematic solid）：弹性介质中嵌入收缩纤维，纤维沿局部组织应力方向被增强。反馈源自纤维在张力下成熟的假设，分别考察收缩纤维应力反馈与弹性应力反馈（elastic stress feedback）的作用。

Result: 收缩纤维应力反馈具有自增强性并驱动自发纤维排列（spontaneous fiber alignment）；弹性应力反馈将排列与力学边界条件及组织几何耦合。依边界条件不同，弹性应力反馈稳定两种正交排列的组织状态，形成结构与力学双稳态（bistability）并表现出滞回（hysteretic）应力—应变响应；在空间延展组织中产生由畴壁（domain wall）分隔的正交结构域。

Conclusion: 在环绕受压内腔的环形体（annulus）中，径向排列内域与周向排列外域稳定共存，畴壁钉扎于使纤维承载腔内压力的位置。结果表明应力反馈可通过将自发排列与组织几何耦合来实现结构序的图案化，提供了肌肉组织中正交纤维图案化的纯力学路径（purely mechanical route）。

Abstract: Contractile fibers in muscle tissues are highly aligned over the scale of many cells. Muscle layers surrounding organs with an inner lumen are often arranged in orthogonal directions. Here, we study a minimal mechanism of how structural order and its patterning into orthogonally aligned domains can emerge from mechanical feedback alone. The central idea is that contractile fibers align in the direction of the local active and elastic stresses, a feedback motivated by the maturation of muscle fibers under tension. We thus model muscle tissue as an active nematic solid with stress feedback: an elastic medium with embedded contractile fibers that are reinforced along the directions of the local tissue stresses. We find that the feedback from the contractile fiber stress is self-amplifying and drives spontaneous fiber alignment. Feedback from the elastic stress couples the alignment to the mechanical boundary conditions and tissue geometry. Depending on the mechanical boundary conditions, the elastic-stress feedback stabilizes two orthogonally aligned tissue states, resulting in a structurally and mechanically bistable tissue that shows a hysteretic stress-strain response. In spatially extended tissues, this bistability gives rise to orthogonally aligned domains separated by domain walls. In an annulus surrounding a pressurized lumen, a radially aligned inner domain and a circumferentially aligned outer domain stably coexist because the domain wall is pinned at a position that results in the fibers carrying the lumen pressure. Our results show that stress feedback can pattern structural order by coupling spontaneous alignment to the tissue geometry. Our mechanism thus provides a purely mechanical route to orthogonal fiber patterning in muscle tissues.

</details>


### [400] [Proliferating against the current: how multistep branching nucleation can sort microtubule polarity in the spindle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16200)
*Ido Lavi,Sebastian Fürthauer,Daniel J. Needleman,Michael J. Shelley*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一个动力学模型（kinetic model），解释大卵母细胞纺锤体（meiotic spindle）中微管（microtubule）正端朝外、负端聚集于极部的悖论性排布：若分支复合物（branching complexes）以足够延迟（多步限速步骤）组装，则群体会向负端漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纺锤体是细胞骨架自组织（cytoskeletal self-organization）的范例，但其微管取向存在悖论：微管在染色体附近成核且正端生长远快于马达蛋白（motor）介导的后向滑动（backward sliding），按理应正端朝外而非负端朝外，该现象此前缺乏解释。

Method: 构建整合核心丝级过程（filament-level processes）的动力学模型，将分支、生长、滑动与更新纳入统一框架；并证明丝长构型变量可被精确消去，得到有限时空层级（finite space-time hierarchy），导出描述群体漂移（population drift）方向的一般判据。

Result: 当分支以足够延迟组装（经多个序贯限速步骤）时，分支复合物积聚在母微管后向滑动负端附近的老晶格位点，使每一代微管生成位置更靠后，群体遂向负端漂移；针对非洲爪蟾卵提取物（Xenopus egg extract）纺锤体实测速度，多步机制至少需四个限速步骤（rate-limiting steps）。

Conclusion: 群体漂移方向可由比较分支位点的平均晶格年龄（mean lattice age）与正端前进速度对滑动速度之比这一通用判据确定，从而为纺锤体微管极性的自组织提供了一种动力学解释。

Abstract: The spindle, which segregates chromosomes during cell division, is a paradigm of cytoskeletal self-organization: a highly ordered structure that persists for hours is assembled from dynamic microtubules that branch, grow, slide, and turn over within tens of seconds. Its microtubules point inward, with minus ends clustered at the poles and plus ends extending toward the chromosomes at the center of the structure. In large meiotic spindles this arrangement is puzzling, since microtubules nucleate near the chromosomes and grow at their plus ends much faster than motors slide them backward. One would therefore expect the plus ends, rather than the minus ends, to radiate outward. Here, we propose a resolution to this seeming paradox through a kinetic model that integrates the core filament-level processes. When branching complexes assemble with a sufficient delay, for instance through several sequential rate-limiting steps, they accumulate on old microtubule lattice sites near the backward-sliding minus ends of mother microtubules. Each generation is then born farther back than the last, so the self-renewing population drifts toward the minus ends even though each filament's center advances toward its plus end. For the sliding and growth velocities measured in Xenopus egg extract spindles, a multistep mechanism requires at least four rate-limiting steps. The kinetic theory has a surprisingly amenable structure: the filament length configuration variable can be eliminated exactly, yielding a finite space-time hierarchy, and the direction of population drift follows from a general criterion comparing the mean lattice age at branching sites with the ratio of plus-end advance to sliding speed.

</details>


### [401] [Stretched bilayer-cytoskeleton links hold echinocytes in a model splenic slit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16241)
*Zhaojie Chai,Jianlu Zheng,He Li,Ming Dao,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究借双组分力学模型模拟贮存红细胞（stored red blood cells）通过脾脏狭缝（splenic slit），发现棘状突起（spicules）本身即可显著阻碍通过，且仅贮存诱导的微小红细胞（microerythrocytes）需高压差，提示棘突与膜丢失共同决定脾脏对贮存细胞的清除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贮存红细胞会变成棘红细胞（echinocytes）并最终失去膜，脾脏负责清除由此形成的小球形细胞；但棘突单独是否妨碍脾脏通过此前未知，且形状与面积变化通常同时发生、难以分离。

Method: 采用双组分模型（two-component model），其中细胞骨架（cytoskeleton）收缩驱动棘突生成；在面积与体积相等的条件下，使盘状细胞（discocyte）与I至III期棘红细胞通过模型脾脏狭缝，比较所需压差（pressure drop），并与毛细血管尺寸通道中的结果对照。同时考察双层-骨架连接（bilayer-cytoskeleton links）近全伸展时的力学作用。

Result: 通过狭缝所需压差随棘红细胞分期上升，III期棘红细胞所需压差为盘状细胞的37至50倍；近全伸展的双层-骨架连接固定住细胞，将其延长约两倍可释放。毛细血管尺寸通道对各期区分远弱。在所考察的贮存血液形状类别中，仅贮存诱导的微小红细胞在高压差下被模型滞留，而该类细胞恰是灌注人脾脏清除最多的。

Conclusion: 棘突除导致膜丢失外，本身也可能是决定脾脏保留哪些贮存细胞的因素；脾脏滞留与灌注清除结果在贮存诱导微小红细胞上一致，支持形状（棘突）在清除机制中的作用。

Abstract: Stored red blood cells become echinocytes, growing spicules before losing membrane; the spleen clears the resulting small, spherical cells. Whether spicules alone hinder splenic passage is unknown. Shape and area change together. In a two-component model whose shrinking cytoskeleton raises spicules, we drove a discocyte and stage I to III echinocytes of equal area and volume through a model splenic slit. The pressure drop needed to pass rose with stage; stage III echinocytes were held at 37 to 50 times the pressure drop that passed the discocyte. Bilayer-cytoskeleton links stretched near full extension held these cells; lengthening them about twofold released a held cell. Capillary-sized channels, as in our device, separated the stages far less. Of the shape classes in stored blood, the model held at high pressure drops only storage-induced microerythrocytes, which perfused human spleens clear most. Spicules, besides membrane loss, may decide which stored cells the spleen retains.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [402] [Quantum Hall Fabry-Pérot Interferometry in Monolayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14633)
*Himadri Chakraborti,Charles L. Tschirhart,James Fang,Saisab Bhowmik,Chengrui Yu,Shujian Cao,William Caro,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jie Shan,Kin Fai Mak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在单层WSe$_2$中首次实现栅定义量子霍尔法布里-珀罗干涉仪，观测到稳健的Aharonov-Bohm干涉，并测得边缘速度约$4.9\times10^4\,\mathrm{m\,s^{-1}}$，确立其作为无简并、分辨味自由度的量子霍尔干涉平台。


<details>
