  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有范德华材料平台中多重自旋与谷自由度使量子霍尔边缘干涉的解释复杂化；单层WSe$_2$因强自旋-轨道耦合与自旋-谷锁定可解除这些简并，产生本质单分量朗道能级，因而提供了理想的研究契机。

Method: 在单层WSe$_2$中制备首个栅定义的量子霍尔法布里-珀罗干涉仪（Fabry-Pérot interferometer），通过连续调节单一器件的腔体尺寸，并开展温度与偏压依赖测量。

Result: 观测到量子霍尔边缘通道的稳健Aharonov-Bohm（AB）干涉，AB区域的占优与静电学估算的弱体-边耦合（$Δ\approx 0.034$）一致；温度与偏压测量一致给出一致的边缘速度$v \approx (4.9 \pm 0.2) \times 10^4\,\mathrm{m\,s^{-1}}$。

Conclusion: 单层WSe$_2$可作为一种洁净的、味分辨（flavor-resolved）的量子霍尔干涉平台，并为后续研究单分量分数量子霍尔态及过渡金属硫化物中的任意子（anyonic）统计干涉探针奠定基础。

Abstract: Electronic Fabry-Pérot interferometers provide a powerful probe of coherent quantum Hall edge transport, yet the presence of multiple spin and valley degrees of freedom in existing van der Waals material platforms can complicate their interpretation. Monolayer WSe$_2$ offers a distinct opportunity because strong spin-orbit coupling and spin-valley locking lift these degeneracies, producing intrinsically single-component Landau levels. Here we realize the first gate-defined quantum Hall Fabry-Pérot interferometer in monolayer WSe$_2$. By continuously tuning the cavity size in a single device, we observe robust Aharonov-Bohm (AB) interference of quantum Hall edge channels across the interferometer; the observed predominance in the AB regime is consistent with a weak bulk-edge coupling ($Δ\approx 0.034$) estimated by electrostatics. Moreover, temperature and bias dependence measurements consistently yield an edge velocity $v \approx (4.9 \pm 0.2) \times 10^4\,\mathrm{m\,s^{-1}}$. Our results establish monolayer WSe$_2$ as a clean, flavor-resolved platform for quantum Hall interferometry and provide a foundation for future studies of single-component fractional quantum Hall states and interferometric probes of anyonic statistics in transition-metal dichalcogenides.

</details>


### [403] [A discrete realization of the boundary sine-Gordon model and the Schmid transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14637)
*Nicolas Paris,Oscar Bouverot-Dupuis,Christophe Mora*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出一个可精确对应边界正弦-戈尔登模型（boundary sine-Gordon model）的离散模型，借助团簇蒙特卡洛算法（cluster Monte Carlo algorithms）计算了弱隧穿与强隧穿区间之间的完整普适渡越行为，并确证了施密德转变（Schmid transition）的存在与性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界正弦-戈尔登模型（等价于电阻分流约瑟夫森结）的相图近期存在争议，需要一种可高效数值模拟且能覆盖大参数范围的方法来裁决分歧。

Method: 构建一个精确复现边界正弦-戈尔登模型性质的离散模型，利用团簇蒙特卡洛算法（cluster Monte Carlo algorithms）进行高效模拟，从而探索广泛参数空间并计算完整的普适渡越（universal crossover）。

Result: 确立了模型的自对偶性（self-duality），明确识别出施密德转变所特有的竖直临界不动点线（vertical line of critical fixed points），并以高精度重现了沿该线的共形场论（conformal field theory）预言；同时厘清了施密德转变与别列津斯基-科斯特利茨-索利斯转变（Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transitions）的本质区别。

Conclusion: 结果决定性地确立了施密德转变的存在及其性质，并验证了施密德-布尔加达耶夫情景（Schmid-Bulgadaev scenario）的有效性。

Abstract: The phase diagram of the boundary sine-Gordon model (bsG), or equivalently of the resistively shunted Josephson junction, has been the subject of recent debate. We introduce a discrete model that reproduces exactly the properties of the bsG model and can be simulated extremely efficiently using cluster Monte Carlo algorithms. This enables us to explore a broad range of parameters and compute the complete universal crossover between the weak- and strong-tunneling regimes. We establish the self-duality of the model and unambiguously identify the vertical line of critical fixed points characteristic of the Schmid transition, recovering the conformal field theory predictions along this line with high accuracy. We further clarify the essential distinctions between the Schmid and Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transitions. Our results definitively establish the existence and nature of the Schmid transition and demonstrate the validity of the Schmid-Bulgadaev scenario.

</details>


### [404] [Controlling the Vacuum: Tunable Magnonic Casimir Effect in Magnetic Moiré Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14809)
*Alejandro Roldan,Dante Navarrete,Mario A. Castro,Sebastián Allende,Roberto E. Troncoso,Alvaro S. Nunez*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文证明莫尔结构（moiré structures）磁自由度的量子涨落可通过调节莫尔晶格加以调控，并据此探讨其对磁振子真空能（magnonic vacuum energy）与磁振子卡西米尔力（magnonic Casimir force）的影响，进而提出一种以真空涨落为工作介质的原型引擎与新型磁-机效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索莫尔晶格参数能否作为调控磁自由度量子涨落的自由度，并将磁振子真空涨落用于实现功的输出（磁-机效应）。

Method: 采用 Bistritzer-MacDonald 方法，计算莫尔结构的磁振子真空能与磁振子卡西米尔力随莫尔图案（moiré pattern）参数的变化，并以莫尔梯（moiré Ladder）作为具体算例。

Result: 磁振子真空能与磁振子卡西米尔力成为莫尔图案参数的函数，从而可驱动一个以真空涨落为工作介质的原型引擎，产生新型磁-机效应（magneto-mechanical effect）。

Conclusion: 计算结果虽以莫尔梯为例，但可径直推广至任意莫尔结构；作者预期该性质属于莫尔结构的普适特征，与所涉激发（excitations）的物理本质无关。

Abstract: We prove that the quantum fluctuations of the magnetic degrees of freedom of moiré structures can be tuned by adjusting the moiré lattice. Using the Bistritzer-MacDonald approach, we explore the consequences of this fact for the magnonic vacuum energy and the magnonic Casimir force, which become functions of the moiré pattern parameters. This allows a prototypical engine to be run with the vacuum fluctuations as a working agent and, in that way, to furnish a novel type of magneto-mechanical effect. Our calculations focus on the moiré Ladder as an example but can be generalized to any moiré structure in a straightforward fashion. We anticipate that our results belong to a generic property of moiré structures, regardless of the physical nature of the excitations at hand.

</details>


### [405] [Interplay of the Fano-Andreev Effect and Nonlinear Screening in Electric and Thermoelectric Transport through a T-Shaped Double Quantum Dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14991)
*Sachin Verma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论上研究T形双量子点与正常/超导电极耦合体系中的弱非线性电输运与热电输运。采用特征势形式主义处理非线性屏蔽，揭示Fano-Andreev效应与屏蔽对输运的联合修正，并预测偏压不对称的Andreev微分电导及由两种贡献竞争决定符号的亚带隙交叉热电响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱非线性区中，非平衡电荷再分布引起的电平重整化与Fano-Andreev效应如何共同影响电输运和热电输运，尚缺乏系统分析，尤其是交叉热电响应（cross thermoelectric response）的符号来源需要澄清。

Method: 基于特征势形式主义（characteristic-potential formalism）处理非线性屏蔽，以内部静电位描述与电极耦合的中心量子点上的非平衡电荷再分布；体系为T形双量子点，与正常电极和超导电极耦合，侧耦合量子点引入Fano-Andreev效应。

Result: 电压偏置下，屏蔽诱导的电平重整化（level renormalization）产生明显的Andreev微分电导偏压不对称；纯热偏置下，增大点间耦合削弱电平重整化并抑制准粒子热电响应，使热电压与非线性Seebeck系数降低；在电压与热偏置同时施加时，线性区之外出现有限的亚带隙交叉热电响应。

Conclusion: 该交叉热电响应的符号由两项竞争决定：受Fano-Andreev效应修正的能量依赖输运贡献，与非线性屏蔽贡献；该竞争最终决定热偏置是增强还是抑制低偏置Andreev电流。

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of weakly nonlinear electric and thermoelectric transport through a T-shaped double quantum dot coupled to normal and superconducting leads. Nonlinear screening is treated within the characteristic-potential formalism, in which an internal electrostatic potential accounts for nonequilibrium charge redistribution on the lead-coupled central dot. The side-coupled dot induces the Fano-Andreev effect, which, together with nonlinear screening, significantly modifies the transport properties. Under voltage bias, the screening-induced level renormalization produces a pronounced bias asymmetry in the Andreev differential conductance. In response to a pure thermal bias, increasing the interdot coupling weakens the level renormalization and suppresses the quasiparticle thermoelectric response, leading to reduced thermovoltage and nonlinear Seebeck coefficient. When voltage and thermal biases are applied simultaneously, a finite subgap cross thermoelectric response emerges beyond the linear regime. The sign of this response is governed by the competition between the energy-dependent transport contribution, modified by the Fano-Andreev effect, and the nonlinear screening contribution, thereby determining whether a thermal bias enhances or suppresses the low-bias Andreev current.

</details>


### [406] [Momentum-Space Mismatch Enables Non-Hermitian Valley Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15036)
*Kai Shao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 论文提出以体系带接触节点（band-touching nodes）与耗散库（dissipative reservoir）之间的动量空间失配（momentum-space mismatch）作为实现节点选择性非厄米趋肤效应（non-Hermitian skin effect, NHSE）的普适微观机制，并据此实现近100%的谷极化（valley polarization）输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谷过滤器（valley filter）多针对特定材料或晶格几何设计，缺乏普适性，因此需要寻找一种与具体体系无关的共同微观要素来实现谷选择性输运（valley-selective transport）。

Method: 将节点与耗散库耦合，使动量失配编码进推迟自能（retarded self-energy），产生依赖节点的耗散与对比鲜明的准粒子寿命；有效非厄米哈密顿量（non-Hermitian Hamiltonian）在两个节点处呈现相反的点能隙谱绕数（point-gap spectral winding）。在最小一维二聚体链（dimer chain）及含真实狄拉克谷（Dirac valleys）的二维体系（π通量矩形与蜂窝晶格）中验证。

Result: 输运计算在所有情形下给出近100%的谷极化率，一个节点快速衰减入库而另一个在器件中存活，且该效应保持栅极可调（gate-tunable），并对无序与边缘缺陷具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 动量空间失配可作为一种普适途径实现非厄米谷过滤（non-Hermitian valley filtering），为谷电子学（valleytronics）中的节点选择性输运提供了不依赖特定材料或晶格几何的机制。

Abstract: Valley-selective transport is central to valleytronics, but existing valley filters are largely tailored to specific materials or lattice geometries, motivating the search for a common microscopic ingredient. Here we identify the momentum-space mismatch between the band-touching nodes of a system and its dissipative reservoir as a route to node-selective non-Hermitian skin effects (NHSE). When the two are coupled, the mismatch is encoded in the retarded self-energy, producing node-dependent dissipation and contrasting quasiparticle lifetimes. The resulting effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian exhibits opposite point-gap spectral winding at the two nodes, giving rise to a node-resolved NHSE with strongly asymmetric dissipation rates: one node decays rapidly into the reservoir while the other survives over the device, yielding a nearly valley-polarized outgoing current. We demonstrate this mechanism in a minimal one-dimensional dimer chain and in two-dimensional systems hosting genuine Dirac valleys, including $π$-flux rectangular and honeycomb lattices. In all cases, transport calculations yield nearly 100\% valley polarization, while remaining gate-tunable and robust against disorder and edge imperfections. Our results show that momentum-space mismatch provides a route to non-Hermitian valley filtering.

</details>


### [407] [Magnon Chirality Selection at Equal Linewidths in Open Collinear Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15113)
*Dinghui Wang,Lulu Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出一种被动共线模式网络，利用空间关联的损耗与相干交换，使不同角动量（磁振子手性，magnon chirality）具有不同存活概率，从而在共振线宽相同的条件下实现手性选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常手性选择依赖共振线宽差异，而文中关注的是即使所有共振具有相同线宽（linewidth）时，损耗（loss）是否仍能筛选单一磁振子手性，并厘清这类选择究竟约束了极点（poles）还是制备（preparations）、本征矢（eigenvectors）与物理库（physical reservoirs）。

Method: 构建被动共线模式网络（passive collinear mode network），其中空间关联的损耗与相干交换对不同角动量赋予不同存活概率；并利用非厄米共线自旋—空间小群（non-Hermitian collinear spin-space little group）对易手性与复频率的作用来组织允许的极点配对（pole pairings），另给出一种将共轭手性扇区分离到相对对角边缘的第二实现。

Result: 初始无偏振混合物达到26.9\%偏振度，同时保留29.9\%的布居；等谱的正常对照（isospectral normal control）则无偏振。组合的自旋与晶体旋转可反转信号，并交换在更强损耗下到达的奇异轮廓（exceptional contours）。第二实现尽管总绕数为零，仍将对角边缘处的共轭手性扇区分开；两种读出可区分对极点与对制备、本征矢及物理库的约束。

Conclusion: 相同线宽下损耗亦可实现手性选择，非厄米共线自旋—空间小群为极点配对的分类提供了组织框架，且所提出的两类读出方式能把极点约束与制备、本征矢及物理库约束区分开来。

Abstract: Loss can select one magnon chirality even when all resonances have the same linewidth. We construct a passive collinear mode network in which spatially correlated loss and coherent exchange give opposite angular momenta different survival probabilities. An initially unpolarized mixture reaches 26.9\% polarization with 29.9\% of the population remaining; an isospectral normal control gives none. A combined spin and crystal rotation reverses the signal and exchanges the exceptional contours reached at stronger loss. We organize the allowed pole pairings by the action of a non-Hermitian collinear spin-space little group on chirality and complex frequency. A second realization separates conjugate chirality sectors at opposite diagonal edges despite zero total winding. The two readouts distinguish constraints on poles from constraints on preparations, eigenvectors, and physical reservoirs.

</details>


### [408] [Magnetically Compensated Spin-Polarized Edge States in Coplanar Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15134)
*Dinghui Wang,Lulu Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出共面交换磁体（coplanar exchange magnets）中边界释放自旋极化电子态的对称性判据：允许的偶宇称边缘自旋空间等于允许的均匀磁化空间，对称性保护的磁补偿因此筛选出垂直于磁矩面的奇自旋织构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁补偿且自旋简并的层通常不表现净自旋极化，但边界可能释放自旋极化电子态；其成立条件与对称性选择规则此前缺乏统一判据，故需建立边界对称性框架。

Method: 在共面交换磁体中建立对称性判据，将偶宇称边缘自旋空间与均匀磁化空间对应；以六个最大扇区（maximal sectors）组织可能的边缘织构，并用一个交换模型的正交切割实现奇、偶扇区，结合 FeSe 自旋涡旋与 NiI₂ 周期四螺旋的 Wannier 模型作材料实例，考察半无限边界的电学响应。

Result: 奇扇区边界呈现垂直自旋积累（perpendicular spin accumulation），偶扇区边界呈现面内自旋流响应（in-plane spin-current response），二者构成互补的电学响应；模型与材料实例均验证奇、偶扇区的实现。

Conclusion: 边界对称性将磁补偿与平衡态自旋织构及电学自旋读出（electrical spin readout）联系起来，为磁补偿体系中探测与利用边缘自旋极化提供对称性指导原则。

Abstract: A boundary can release spin-polarized electronic states from a magnetically compensated, spin-degenerate layer. We establish the symmetry criterion governing this release in coplanar exchange magnets: the allowed even-parity edge-spin space equals the allowed uniform-magnetization space. Symmetry-protected compensation therefore selects an odd spin texture perpendicular to the moment plane. Six maximal sectors organize the possible edge textures. Orthogonal cuts of one exchange model realize odd and even sectors, while FeSe spin-vortex and NiI$_2$ period-four spiral Wannier models provide material realizations. Their semi-infinite boundaries exhibit complementary electrical responses: perpendicular spin accumulation at the odd edge and in-plane spin-current response at the even edge. Boundary symmetry thus connects magnetic compensation to equilibrium spin textures and electrical spin readout.

</details>


### [409] [Quantum-Metric-Induced Distinct Optoelectronic Responses in Triple $M$ Valleys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15145)
*Yongliang Song,Shuxian Sun,Zhichao Zhou,Xiao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作发现三种 $M$ 谷（$M$ valleys）可通过量子几何度规（quantum metric）诱导的光电响应加以区分，并在反铁磁 $\mathrm{MoI}_3$ 双层中验证了可测量的非线性谷霍尔效应（nonlinear valley Hall effect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谷电子学（valleytronics）研究长期集中于性质互补的 $K_{\pm}$ 谷，而 $M$ 谷缺乏可靠操控手段，导致大量 $M$ 谷材料未被利用。

Method: 采用紧束缚计算（tight-binding calculations）调节电场取向，选取任意一对 $M$ 谷产生相反的非线性霍尔电流；结合线偏振光（linearly polarized light）实现单个 $M$ 谷的优先激发；并通过第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）在反铁磁 $\mathrm{MoI}_3$ 双层中进行验证。

Result: 任意一对 $M$ 谷均可产生方向相反的非线性霍尔电流以实现非线性谷霍尔效应；线偏振光可优先激发单个 $M$ 谷并驱动光激发谷极化霍尔输运（photoexcited valley-polarized Hall transport）；$\mathrm{MoI}_3$ 双层表现出显著的非线性谷霍尔电导（nonlinear valley Hall conductivity），适于输运测量。

Conclusion: 研究将谷电子学的适用范围从传统二元 $K_{\pm}$ 谷拓展至三元 $M$ 谷，为设计具有多量子态、高可调性的先进光电器件提供了途径。

Abstract: While $K_{\pm}$ valleys with contrasting optoelectronic properties have been the main focus of valleytronics, the lack of reliable manipulation methods for $M$ valleys leaves numerous $M$-valley materials untapped. Here, we discover that three flavors of $M$ valleys become distinguishable via quantum-metric-induced optoelectronic responses. The tight-binding calculations demonstrate that by tuning the electric-field orientation, opposite nonlinear Hall currents from any pair of $M$ valleys can be chosen to realize the nonlinear valley Hall effect. Moreover, linearly polarized light allows for preferential excitation of individual $M$ valleys, driving photoexcited valley-polarized Hall transport. Using first-principles calculations, we further validate the quantum-metric-induced optoelectronic responses in antiferromagnetic $\mathrm{MoI}_3$ bilayers, which exhibit a significant nonlinear valley Hall conductivity amenable to transport measurements. Our findings extend the scope of valleytronics from conventional binary $K_{\pm}$ valleys to triple $M$ valleys and pave the way for designing advanced optoelectronic devices with multiple quantum states of high tunability.

</details>


### [410] [Interface-Driven Remote-Surface States in Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15192)
*Pierre A. Pantaleon,Alejandro Jimeno-Pozo,Francisco Guinea*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该摘要提出三层石墨烯（rhombohedral multilayer graphene）中外表面低能态沿层间逐层传播的机制，可解释远程表面调制现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三层石墨烯的低能态局域于两个外表面，但此前缺乏对低能态如何在内层与对侧表面之间建立联系的单粒子层面解释。

Method: 通过识别逐层（layer-by-layer）传播机制，分析外层空位、圆形空洞与锯齿形终止所束缚的亚晶格极化零模，以及周期性莫尔界面（moire interface）上各动量谐波的层极化行为。

Result: 外层空位波函数在相继层上空间扩展，随体系尺寸增大归一化权重向对侧表面转移；同一关系支配零模的延伸，并在莫尔界面处使低能带的层极化依赖布洛赫动量（Bloch momentum）与调制周期。

Conclusion: 该机制为近期多层石墨烯实验中观测到的远程表面调制提供了内禀的单粒子（single-particle）解释。

Abstract: Rhombohedral multilayer graphene hosts low-energy states on its two outer surfaces. We identify a layer-by-layer mechanism through which a low-energy state generated at one surface acquires weight on successive layers up to the opposite surface. For an outer-layer vacancy, the wavefunction spreads over a larger area on successive layers, shifting normalized weight toward the remote surface as the system size increases. The same relation governs the extension of sublattice-polarized zero modes bound to circular voids and straight zigzag terminations. At periodic moire interfaces, it acts separately on each momentum harmonic, making the layer polarization of the low-energy bands depend on Bloch momentum and modulation period. This provides an intrinsic single-particle explanation for the remote-surface modulations observed in recent multilayer graphene experiments.

</details>


### [411] [Chirality-Odd Readout of Coherence-Mediated Magnon Spin Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15214)
*Yanmeng Lei,Ji Zou,Sanchar Sharma,Tao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文预测铁磁与反铁磁螺旋磁体在电学自旋注入-探测配置下的手性响应，指出螺旋对称性使磁振子自旋输运完全由模式叠加介导，手性反转不改变注入自旋流，但通过探测界面散射振幅反号使读出电压反号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构手性（structural chirality）在磁体中普遍存在，但其对绝缘体自旋输运的影响仍基本未被探索，仅有少数近期实验提供了初步线索，因此需要理论预测手性在自旋注入-探测中的角色。

Method: 针对铁磁与反铁磁螺旋（ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic spirals）中的电学自旋注入-探测配置进行理论预测，基于螺旋的螺旋对称性（screw symmetry）分析磁振子自旋矩阵（magnon spin matrix）与本征模的偏振性质，并考察注入与探测两个界面的电子—磁振子散射（electron--magnon scattering）。

Result: 螺旋的螺旋对称性禁止磁振子自旋矩阵的任何非零对角元，每个本征模为线偏振且不携带自旋，自旋输运完全由不同模式间的叠加介导；反转手性会同步翻转沿螺旋轴的磁振子自旋与注入的量子相干（quantum coherence），但注入自旋流保持不变；手性作用体现在探测界面，使散射振幅反号，从而将磁振子积累读出为相反符号的电压。

Conclusion: 该界面读出机制与多铁性 BiFeO3 中电场切换下反号的一阶谐波电压（first-harmonic voltage）一致，从而将结构手性确立为调控磁振子相干及其电学读出的内禀控制参数。

Abstract: Structural chirality is ubiquitous in magnets, yet its impact on spin transport in insulators remains largely unexplored, with only a few recent experiments offering the first glimpses. Here we predict the chiral response in the electric spin injection-detection configuration in both ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic spirals. The screw symmetry of the spiral forbids any nonzero diagonal element of the magnon spin matrix: every eigenmode is linearly polarized and carries no spin, so spin transport is mediated entirely by superposition between distinct modes. Reversing chirality flips the magnon spin along the spiral axis and the injected quantum coherence in lockstep, leaving the associated injected spin current invariant. Spiral chirality acts instead at the detection interface, where electron--magnon scattering amplitude is reversed and causes the magnon accumulation to be read out as a voltage of opposite sign. This interfacial readout is consistent with reversed first-harmonic voltage under electric-field switching in multiferroic BiFeO$_3$, establishing structural chirality as an intrinsic control parameter for magnon coherence and its electrical readout.

</details>


### [412] [Steering Skyrmion Diffusion with Chiral Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15253)
*Palina Shaban,Ulrich Pototschnig,Gulnaz Rakhmanova,Mikhail Lemeshko,Ragheed Alhyder*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出理论模型：吸附于磁性薄膜上的手性分子通过其手性（handedness）改变界面磁相互作用，从而调控磁性斯格明子（magnetic skyrmion）的热运动；空间均匀分子层不影响其位置，而边界与覆盖梯度会施加横向力，且该力在不同对映体（enantiomer）间反向，可重塑长时扩散行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性分子与磁化强度的耦合可改变斯格明子的热运动，但缺乏描述该手性相互作用的明确理论框架及其对扩散行为影响的定量预测。

Method: 构建理论模型，令取向一致的倾斜分子层依据手性（handedness）修正界面磁相互作用；结合随机微磁模拟（stochastic micromagnetic simulations）与集体坐标计算（collective-coordinate calculations），并对一维势场景观给出精确解析。

Result: 斯格明子仅响应耦合的空间变化：均匀层不改变其位置，边界与覆盖梯度则产生力；在手性区域附近，该力使其横向偏转，方向在对映体（enantiomer）间反转。在周期图案中，扩散沿势变化方向被抑制、垂直方向被增强，净效应由陀螺响应（gyrotropic response）与耗散响应（dissipative response）的平衡决定。

Conclusion: 分子取向、空间图案化与斯格明子性质共同决定其运动；手性诱导的横向偏转可作为手性相互作用的直接特征信号，并可用于调控斯格明子的扩散输运。

Abstract: Chiral molecules adsorbed on a magnetic film interact with its magnetization and can thereby alter the thermal motion of magnetic skyrmions. Here we develop a theoretical model in which an aligned layer of tilted molecules modifies the interfacial magnetic interactions according to its handedness. Within this model a skyrmion responds only to spatial variations of the coupling, so a uniform layer leaves its position unaffected, whereas boundaries and coverage gradients exert forces on it. Near a chiral region, these forces deflect the skyrmion transversely in a direction that reverses between enantiomers, which provides a direct signature of the chiral interaction. In extended periodic patterns, the same forces reshape the long-time diffusion, suppressing it in the direction in which the potential varies and enhancing it in the perpendicular direction. The net effect on the mean diffusion is set by the balance between the gyrotropic and dissipative responses of the skyrmion, as we show exactly for one-dimensional landscapes. Stochastic micromagnetic simulations and collective-coordinate calculations confirm these predictions and show how molecular orientation, spatial patterning, and skyrmion properties together determine the motion.

</details>


### [413] [Directed Domain Wall Motion by Radio-Frequency Currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15305)
*Lukas Fischer,Rouven Dreyer,Simon Arnold,Jae-Chun Jeon,Georg Woltersdorf,Stuart S. P. Parkin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作证明射频电流（RF current）可高效激发远低于铁磁共振频率的低频磁畴壁（domain wall, DW）振荡，并可通过横向磁场或初始电流脉冲打破对称性，将其整流为定向运动乃至辅助磁化翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋轨道矩（spin-orbit torque）驱动的直流电流畴壁运动已较为成熟，但射频电流与畴壁的耦合机制、以及在对称周期驱动下能否产生定向运动尚不清楚。

Method: 采用时间分辨与静态磁光克尔显微术（magneto-optical Kerr microscopy），在射频自旋轨道矩激励下观测畴壁动力学；分别引入横向磁场与初始电流脉冲作为对称性破缺手段，并考察脉冲诱导边缘畴在均匀磁态中的扩展行为。

Result: 射频自旋轨道矩可有效激发低于铁磁共振频率的低频畴壁振荡；无微扰时振荡保持对称，横向磁场下表现为自推进（self-propulsion），初始电流脉冲下则形成持续畴壁运动；均匀磁态中脉冲诱导边缘畴的射频驱动扩展最终实现高效的射频辅助磁化翻转（RF-assisted magnetic switching）。

Conclusion: 研究建立了射频驱动畴壁振荡经对称性破缺整流为定向运动的通用框架，为畴壁的高频调控及自旋电子学存储、逻辑与神经形态计算应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: Magnetic domain walls (DWs) are central to emerging spintronic technologies for memory, logic and neuromorphic computing, where their controlled displacement is a key functionality. While current-induced DW motion driven by spin-orbit torques is well established under direct currents, it remains unclear how radio-frequency (RF) currents couple to DWs and whether they can generate directed motion under a symmetric periodic drive. Here we show that RF currents drive DW oscillations, which can be rectified into directed motion through symmetry breaking. Using time-resolved and static magneto-optical Kerr microscopy, we demonstrate that RF spin-orbit torques efficiently excite low-frequency DW oscillations well below the ferromagnetic resonance frequency. Whereas these oscillations remain symmetric in the absence of perturbations, symmetry breaking by a transverse magnetic field produces self-propulsion, while symmetry breaking by an initial current pulse gives rise to sustained DW motion. We further show that in initially uniform magnetic states, RF-driven expansion of a pulse-generated edge domain ultimately results in efficient RF-assisted magnetic switching. Together, these results establish a general framework for understanding how RF-driven DW oscillations are rectified into directed motion, providing new opportunities for their high-frequency control.

</details>


### [414] [Matrix-aware 2D field-effect biosensing of cardiac troponin I using complementary ion-sensitive transduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15347)
*Subhabrata Das,Bharti,Babulu Pradhan,Abhishek R Nath,Kashish Sapra,Jaydeep sharma,Vijayender Bhalla,Satyaprakash Sahoo,Manish Mathew,Md. Ehesan Ali,Kaushik Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作构建了适配体功能化二硫化钼场效应晶体管（MoS2 2D-FET）与石墨烯离子敏场效应晶体管（ISFET）组成的双传感器平台，用于复杂生物基质中心肌肌钙蛋白I（Trop-I）的检测，并借助机器学习补偿血清高离子强度引起的信号畸变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病（cardiovascular diseases）为全球主要死因，亟需在即时检验（point of care）场景下实现快速、灵敏、可靠的心肌损伤检测；而血清高离子强度会显著削弱FET传感响应，构成复杂基质下的检测障碍。

Method: 采用原子级厚度MoS2沟道的适配体功能化FET实现Trop-I识别与高灵敏检测，并以石墨烯基ISFET作为辅助传感器表征样品的离子环境；进一步通过机器学习辅助的方式整合两类传感器响应，以补偿血清诱导的信号失真。

Result: MoS2 2D-FET对Trop-I的检出限（limit of detection）达0.001 ng mL-1；但血清高离子强度使场效应响应衰减约80%。机器学习辅助整合后，可恢复从2D-FET转移特性中提取的关键电学品质因数（figures of merit），包括最大开态电流（Ids_max）、亚阈值摆幅（SS）与跨导（gm）。

Conclusion: 该双传感器架构融合分子识别、高分析灵敏度与基质感知的电信号校正，为复杂生物流体中更可靠的FET生物标志物检测提供潜在路径；通过引入靶标特异性适配体，还有望实现多种心脏生物标志物的多重检测（multiplexed detection），用于更广泛的心脏应激与心肌损伤风险评估。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases remain a leading cause of mortality worldwide, highlighting the need for rapid, sensitive and reliable detection of cardiac injury at the point of care. Here, we develop a dual-sensor platform that combines an aptamer-functionalized molybdenum disulfide (MoS2) field-effect transistor (2D-FET) with a graphene-based ion-sensitive field-effect transistor (ISFET) for the detection of cardiac troponin I (Trop-I) in complex biological matrices. The atomically thin MoS2 channel enables highly sensitive detection of Trop-I, achieving a limit of detection of 0.001 ng mL-1. However, the high ionic strength of serum substantially attenuates the field-effect response, resulting in approximately 80% degradation of the sensor response. To address this matrix effect, the ISFET is employed as a secondary sensor to characterize the ionic environment of the sample. Machine-learning-assisted integration of the 2D-FET and ISFET responses is investigated as a strategy for compensating serum-induced signal distortion and recovering key electrical figures of merit extracted from the 2D-FET transfer characteristics, including maximum on-state current (Ids_max), subthreshold slope (SS) and transconductance (gm). This dual-sensor architecture combines molecular recognition, high analytical sensitivity and matrix-aware electrical signal correction, providing a potential pathway towards more reliable FET-based biomarker detection in complex biological fluids. The approach may further enable multiplexed detection of cardiac biomarkers through incorporation of target-specific aptamers, offering potential for broader assessment of cardiac stress and myocardial injury risk.

</details>


### [415] [Nonlocal multipolar spin accumulation in altermagnets beyond d-wave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15438)
*J. W. Gonzalez*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在自旋守恒的 g 波与 i 波交错磁体（altermagnet）中，均匀纵向电导不含自旋奇分量，但点注入器可通过非局域自旋积累（nonlocal spin accumulation）探测费米速度（Fermi velocity）的高阶矩。非局域信号的角度谐波阶数由首个非零自旋奇速度矩的秩决定，并伴随可预测的径向幂律与弛豫时间标度，量子输运模拟与玻尔兹曼理论在径向上一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀纵向电导在自旋守恒的 g/i 波交错磁体中缺乏自旋奇分量，常规输运测量手段因此受限。作者试图借助点注入器的非局域自旋积累，从高阶速度矩中获得可辨识的交错磁序对称性信号。

Method: 采用玻尔兹曼输运理论（Boltzmann theory）推导首个非零自旋奇速度矩的秩 m 与主导角谐波、渐近径向依赖 r^{2-m} 及固定距离与注入电流下的振幅标度 τ^{m-3} 之间的关系；并以量子输运模拟（quantum transport simulations）在有限几何下检验径向剖面，另在双接触纳米带中开展无序实现统计检验。

Result: 玻尔兹曼理论预测 m=2,4,6 分别对应不同的主导角谐波与径向幂律；对 g 波序，四阶矩给出四次角谐波并带 r^{-2} 渐近尾部。量子模拟在无需振幅拟合的条件下复现有限几何玻尔兹曼理论给出的径向剖面；但三种对称性下量子振幅超出基于玻恩散射时间（Born scattering time）的玻尔兹曼预测 15–65%。在双接触纳米带中，以预设径向窗口在 1800 个独立无序实现上以 6.8σ 显著性探测到 g 波四次谐波，且其振幅随交错磁劈裂（altermagnetic splitting）减小而下降。

Conclusion: 非局域自旋积累可作为探测交错磁体高阶速度矩与角谐波的选择性探针，其径向形式与对称性依赖可由玻尔兹曼理论定量描述。量子振幅相对玻尔兹曼预测的系统性偏高提示需超越玻恩散射时间近似的振幅描述；g 波四次谐波的高显著性探测及其对交错磁劈裂的依赖，为实验识别 g 波交错磁序提供了可行途径。

Abstract: In spin-conserving models of $g$- and $i$-wave altermagnets, the uniform longitudinal conductivity has no spin-odd component. A point injector reveals higher moments of the Fermi velocity through the nonlocal spin accumulation. Boltzmann theory predicts that the rank $m=2,4,6$ of the first nonzero spin-odd velocity moment sets the leading angular harmonic, its asymptotic radial dependence $r^{2-m}$, and its amplitude scaling $τ^{m-3}$ at fixed distance and injected current. For $g$-wave order, the fourth moment produces a fourth angular harmonic with an asymptotic $r^{-2}$ tail. Quantum transport simulations support the radial profiles predicted by finite-geometry Boltzmann theory without an amplitude fit. Across the three symmetries, the quantum amplitudes exceed the Boltzmann predictions based on the Born scattering time by $15-65$\% in the windows analyzed. In a two-contact ribbon, we detect the $g$-wave fourth harmonic at $6.8\,σ$ in an independent set of 1800 disorder realizations using a preselected radial window. Its amplitude decreases when the altermagnetic splitting is reduced.

</details>


### [416] [Symmetry-Enabled Ferroelectric Valley Transduction in Rhombohedral Bilayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15494)
*Katsunori Wakabayashi,Souren Adhikary,Tomoaki Kameda,Kazuhito Tsukagoshi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该摘要研究了菱面体堆叠双层 WSe2 中铁电极化（ferroelectric polarization）与层自由度（layer degree of freedom）的耦合如何转化为谷选择性（valley-selective）能带响应，并预言差分跨导（differential transconductance）等可测输运特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电极化虽与双层的层自由度耦合，但要使谷选择性响应成立，两个谷必须具有不同的层间特征（interlayer character），这一条件此前缺乏具体材料与定量论证。

Method: 利用菱面体双层 WSe2 的对称性：三重旋转（three-fold rotation）禁止 K 谷的层间隧穿（interlayer tunneling），而 Γ 处仍保持强杂化（hybridization）。结合有限场拟合与谷依赖屏蔽（valley-dependent screening）计算，提取 K 边位移并分析差分跨导随位移场（displacement field）的行为。

Result: 不施加该规则的有限场拟合给出 K 耦合与零一致，K 边位移达到 \polshiftdft~meV，即不对称性对单谷所能给出的最大值；极化反转使谷景观在位移场中镜像，使两种配准（registry）在零场下具有等大反号的差分跨导 g_d；理想对称极限下各配准补偿场处存在 g_d 零点，谷依赖屏蔽仅使该零点在读出点偏移 \gdnulllow\%。

Conclusion: 结果将已知的堆叠选择定则（stacking selection rule）与铁电谷输运（ferroelectric valley transduction）联系起来，给出定量的谷位移与可直接检验的输运信号，其精度受器件不对称性限制。

Abstract: Ferroelectric polarization couples to the layer degree of freedom in a bilayer, but a valley-selective energy response requires the valleys to carry different interlayer character. Rhombohedral bilayer WSe$_2$ meets that condition by symmetry: three-fold rotation forbids interlayer tunneling at the $K$ valley, while strong hybridization remains at $Γ$. The resulting contrast converts the band-effective ferroelectric layer asymmetry into a shift of the $K$ edge, and so displaces the $K$--$Γ$ landscape: a finite-field fit that does not impose the rule leaves the $K$ coupling consistent with zero, and the shift reaches \polshiftdft~meV, the most that asymmetry can give a single valley. Reversing the polarization mirrors the valley landscape in displacement field, so the two registries have equal and opposite differential transconductance $g_d$ at zero field. In the ideal symmetric limit a second parity holds about each registry's own compensation field and places a zero of $g_d$ there. The valley-dependent screening calculated here displaces that zero by only \gdnulllow\% at the read point, so device asymmetry limits the accuracy. The result links a known stacking selection rule to ferroelectric valley transduction, with a quantitative valley shift and a directly testable transport signature.

</details>


### [417] [Filling and Sealing Carbon Nanotubes by Local Anodic Oxidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15567)
*Ruiqing Zang,Jianan Xiao,Jacob Bair,Ian Wang,Chuyue Peng,Matthias Kuehne*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究表明，原子力显微镜中的电容耦合局部阳极氧化（local anodic oxidation, LAO）切割碳纳米管（carbon nanotubes, CNTs）时，会意外地一步完成"切割—填充—密封"：切开的管段被水填充并在干燥氩气中保持密封数周。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数合成路线得到的碳纳米管两端封闭，需开口与填充才能研究其内部流体与离子输运；而对管内物质的研究又常需密封以防在干燥或真空环境中逸出。LAO 已被用于在预定位置切割单根碳纳米管，但切割究竟导致开口、填充还是密封此前并不清楚。

Method: 采用空间分辨的显微拉曼光谱（micro-Raman spectroscopy）表征 LAO 切割后的碳纳米管段，分析最内壁径向呼吸类模式（radial breathing-like mode）的蓝移与展宽，并结合干燥氩气环境存放与 140 °C 高真空退火实验检验其密封性与内容物释放行为。

Result: LAO 切割意外产生被填充的碳纳米管段，且在干燥氩气气氛下密封状态可维持数周。径向呼吸类模式的蓝移与展宽幅度与水填充相符，提示填充物来源于切割过程中 AFM 针尖与基底之间凝聚的水弯月面（water meniscus）。140 °C 高真空退火可使部分（而非全部）管段释放封装物，表明存在热可逾越的势垒，而非端帽重新形成。

Conclusion: LAO 具备一步实现切割—填充—密封（cut-fill-seal）的能力，为按直径分辨研究纳米限域水（nanoconfined water）及水溶液提供了途径，也可用于向真空基分析技术进行仄升（zeptoliter）量级的样品递送。

Abstract: Carbon nanotubes (CNTs) are prime nanofluidic conduits and nanopores for studying matter under extreme confinement. Since most synthesis routes yield CNTs with closed ends, opening and filling strategies are required to access their interior for fluid and ion transport, whereas studies of the encapsulated species themselves may additionally require sealing to prevent their escape in dry or vacuum environments. Capacitively coupled local anodic oxidation (LAO) in an atomic force microscope (AFM) has been shown to cut individual CNTs at predefined positions, but whether cutting induces opening, filling, or sealing has remained unclear. Using spatially resolved micro-Raman spectroscopy, we find that LAO cutting surprisingly produces filled CNT segments that remain sealed under dry Ar atmosphere for weeks. The magnitudes of the blueshift and broadening of the innermost-wall radial breathing-like mode are consistent with water filling, pointing to the water meniscus condensed between the AFM tip and the substrate as the source of CNT filling during cutting. High-vacuum annealing at 140 °C releases the encapsulated species from some, but not all, segments, indicating a thermally surmountable barrier rather than re-formed end caps. This one-step cut-fill-seal capability offers a route to diameter-resolved studies of nanoconfined water and aqueous solutions, and to zeptoliter-scale sample delivery for vacuum-based analytical techniques.

</details>


### [418] [Single Spin Hall Conversion in the Hall Response of Magnetic-Insulator/Heavy-Metal Bilayers Identified by Odd Spin-Hall-Angle Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15571)
*Akashdeep Akashdeep,Ewiese Mohammad Ababneh,Botros Gerges Mikhail Fenhas,Duc Minh Tran,Mathias Kläui,Steven S. -L. Zhang,Vivek Amin,Gerhard Jakob,Timo Kuschel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过磁场窗口分离YIG/Ta与YIG/Pt双层霍尔电压的三种贡献，发现磁场驱动分量在Ta与Pt间反号，表明霍尔响应可对自旋霍尔角呈奇对称；漂移-扩散模型将之归因于界面产生自旋流主导的单次自旋霍尔转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁绝缘体/重金属双层（magnetic-insulator/heavy-metal bilayers）的霍尔响应通常被归因于两次连续的自旋霍尔转换（spin Hall conversion），因而预期对自旋霍尔角（spin Hall angle）呈偶函数依赖；该文旨在检验这一既有图像是否完备。

Method: 选取自旋霍尔角相反的Ta与Pt作为重金属层，利用不同磁场窗口将钇铁石榴石（yttrium iron garnet, YIG）双层的霍尔电压分离为磁化驱动进动、磁场驱动进动与普通霍尔效应（ordinary Hall effect）三类贡献，并用含界面产生自旋流（interface-generated spin currents）的漂移-扩散模型（drift-diffusion model）进行描述。

Result: 磁场驱动分量在Ta与Pt之间反号，揭示出对自旋霍尔角呈奇对称（odd-in-spin-Hall-angle）的霍尔响应；磁化驱动分量在Ta与Pt中同样反号，但最薄重金属层情形除外。模型表明磁场驱动分量主要由单次自旋霍尔转换承载，磁化驱动分量亦与之相符。

Conclusion: 基于有限虚自旋混合电导（imaginary spin-mixing conductance）的替代解释需要异常大的电导值，因而可能性较低；该结果推动了磁绝缘体/重金属双层霍尔响应向更一般描述的理解。

Abstract: The Hall response of magnetic-insulator/heavy-metal bilayers is usually ascribed to two successive spin Hall conversions and is therefore expected to be even in the spin Hall angle. Using distinct magnetic-field windows, we separate the Hall voltage of yttrium iron garnet bilayers with Ta and Pt of opposite spin Hall angle into contributions from magnetization- and magnetic-field-driven precession as well as from ordinary Hall effect. The field-driven component reverses sign between Ta and Pt, revealing a Hall response odd-in-spin-Hall-angle. The magnetization-driven component also has opposite signs for Ta and Pt, except for the thinnest heavy-metal layers. A drift-diffusion model with interface-generated spin currents describes this behavior: the field-driven component is predominantly carried by a single spin Hall conversion, and the magnetization-driven component is consistent with one as well. Other potential explanations, based on a description with a finite imaginary spin-mixing conductance rather than interface-generated spin currents, require unusually large values of this conductance, making this mechanism unlikely. Our findings advance the understanding of Hall responses in magnetic-insulator/heavy-metal bilayers toward a more general description.

</details>


### [419] [Exact characterization of three-terminal fermion counting under one-body scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17122)
*Zhanning Wang,Yongtao Li,Rubén Seoane Souto,José C. Abadillo-Uriel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将无相互作用费米子出射统计的广义二项分布判据从两端情形推广至三端（三通道）散射区域，给出精确统计界：对两费米子、三端情形，六个可测概率满足的四个不等式可判定观测统计是否可由无相互作用散射体解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两端体系中无相互作用费米子的出射统计服从由泡利不相容原理（Pauli exclusion principle）决定的广义二项分布（generalized binomial distribution），偏离该分布被视为相互作用（interaction）的特征。将该判据推广到多端情形，可为识别相互作用提供更一般的检验工具。

Method: 将两端结果推广至与三个出射端（通道）耦合的散射区域（scattering region），推导出射费米子统计的精确界（exact bounds）。具体针对两费米子、三端情形，构造涉及六个可测概率（measured probabilities）的四个不等式，作为判定是否存在无相互作用散射体（non-interacting scatterer）的依据。

Result: 所得四个不等式构成判定观测统计能否由无相互作用散射体解释的充要式检验。将其应用于近期冷原子（cold atoms）散射实验数据，识别出相互作用对出射粒子有显著贡献的参数区域。

Conclusion: 该工作将费米子统计判据拓展至三端几何，为在介观导体（mesoscopic conductors）到冷原子光陷阱（optical traps）等纳米尺度系统中识别费米子相互作用提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Quantum scattering is a fundamental probe of the scattering region and the quantum dynamics of outgoing particles. In two terminals, the statistics of non-interacting fermions is described by a generalized binomial distribution, that arises from the Pauli exclusion principle, while deviations are a signature of interactions. In this work, we extend this result to a scattering region coupled to three outgoing terminals (or channels), obtaining exact bounds for the statistics of the outgoing fermions. In particular, for two fermions and three terminals, four inequalities involving six measured probabilities determine whether a non-interacting scatterer can explain the observed statistics. Applying these inequalities to recent scattering experiments in cold atoms, we are able to identify a regime where interactions have a significant contribution on the outgoing particles. Therefore, our work opens up the possibility of identifying fermion interactions at the nanoscale in systems that range from mesoscopic conductors to optical traps in cold atoms.

</details>


### [420] [Non-Bloch Topology of Frustrated Non-Hermitian Flat Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15863)
*Fan Yang,Jiong-Hao Wang,Hui Liu,Emil J. Bergholtz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作构建了一类二维阻挫非厄米晶格模型，在非布洛赫能带理论框架下揭示了平带因其他色散带诱导而获得与基无关的趋肤效应，并给出平带边界趋肤模的几何构造与拓扑起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维阻挫晶格的严格平带与紧致局域态是研究平带拓扑的理想平台，但非厄米趋肤效应（non-Hermitian skin effect）会剧烈改变能带结构，且一维以上的情形理解不足，因此需要可解析处理的二维非厄米平带模型。

Method: 构造一大类具有严格平带的二维阻挫非厄米晶格模型，并以非布洛赫能带理论（non-Bloch band theory）描述；以非厄米呼吸Kagome模型（breathing Kagome model）与棋盘模型（checkerboard model）为例进行分析，提出基于零能角模双正交极化（biorthogonal polarization）的不变量来刻画体-边对应。

Result: 平带可获得由其他能带诱导的、与基无关的趋肤效应；在呼吸Kagome模型中，该效应恰在平带进入色散带周期性边界谱所张区域时出现。平带在带交叉处本征矢出现不可移动间断时变为奇异，该奇异性对边界条件敏感且不受平带平坦性保护。体-边对应表现为体内奇异能带接触与边界稳健平带趋肤模，后者可由紧致局域趋肤模的宏观求和构造，其拓扑起源可追溯至非布洛赫环面（non-Bloch torus）上两个线性无关的不可收缩环态。破坏严格平坦性后可实现近平坦非布洛赫陈带（non-Bloch Chern bands）。

Conclusion: 研究将平带物理从厄米推广至非厄米二维体系，表明非厄米平带的奇异性与趋肤效应具有新的边界敏感性与拓扑内涵，并可通过双正交极化不变量刻画其体-边对应。

Abstract: Frustrated lattices with exactly flat bands and compact localized states provide an ideal setting to uncover the topology of flat bands, leading to intriguing physics in two dimensions. Extending from Hermitian to non-Hermitian systems, however, band structures are drastically changed by the non-Hermitian skin effect, which is less well understood beyond one dimension. Here, we construct a broad class of two-dimensional frustrated non-Hermitian lattice models with exactly flat bands which can be naturally described in a non-Bloch band theory. Using non-Hermitian breathing Kagome and checkerboard models as illustrations, we reveal how flat bands acquire a basis-independent skin effect induced by the other bands; in the breathing Kagome model it appears exactly when the flat band enters the area spanned by the periodic-boundary spectra of the dispersive bands. A flat band becomes singular when its eigenvectors hold immovable discontinuities at band crossings. Such singularity in non-Hermitian systems is sensitive to boundary conditions and unprotected by band flatness. Non-Bloch topology underlying is uncovered in a bulk-boundary correspondence with the formation of a singular band touching in the bulk and a robust flat band skin mode on the boundary. We present a geometric approach to construct this boundary skin mode from a macroscopic sum of compact localized skin modes and trace its topological origin to two linearly independent noncontractible loop states on a non-Bloch torus. To characterize the correspondence, an invariant is proposed based on biorthogonal polarization of the zero-energy corner mode. Breaking exact flatness, we further realize nearly flat non-Bloch Chern bands.

</details>


### [421] [Emergence of Phonon Hydrodynamics as a Non-Hermitian Symmetry-Breaking Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16161)
*Xin Qian,Guanda Quan,Ning Wang,Yaoming Chu,Run Hu,Te-Huan Liu,Xiangfan Xu,Jianming Cai,Ronggui Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作将声子流体动力学（phonon hydrodynamics）的起始条件表述为非厄米动力学相变（non-Hermitian dynamical transition），用一对奇异点（exceptional points）定量界定流体动力学窗口的上下边界，并给出基于材料输运性质的可预测判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子流体动力学可实现超越经典扩散极限的集体热流，但如何定量判定该流体动力学态何时出现及其边界所在，是凝聚态物理中长期未解的难题；既有的Guyer-Krumhansl判据仅为定性的散射率不等式。

Method: 从Peierls-Boltzmann输运方程（Peierls-Boltzmann transport equation）出发，为温度场与声子漂移场的耦合动力学推导等效薛定谔方程（effective Schrodinger equation），使流体动力学区域可直接由材料输运性质识别，并据此解析推导流体动力学输运存在的判据。

Result: 一对奇异点定量给出流体动力学窗口的上下边界，构成超越定性散射率不等式的关键设计准则；解析判据表明，只有当有效热扩散率（effective thermal diffusivity）超过声子动量扩散率（phononic momentum diffusivity）时才能存在有限的流体动力学窗口。以第一性原理（ab initio）声子性质为输入，理论准确复现了实验观测到的、依赖温度与长度的超快第二声（second sound）动力学。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了理解集体能量输运的对称性框架，并为在低维材料与功能器件中识别支持波状热输运的材料、温度及特征长度尺度提供定量设计原则。

Abstract: Phonon hydrodynamics enables collective heat flow beyond the classical diffusive limit and offers a promising route to controlling thermal transport in low-dimensional materials and nanoscale devices. However, quantitative criteria for determining when this hydrodynamic state emerges and where its boundaries lie remain a longstanding challenge in condensed matter physics. Here, we show that the onset of phonon hydrodynamics can be formulated as a non-Hermitian dynamical transition that converts microscopic phonon scattering properties into predictive boundaries for wavelike heat transport. Starting from the Peierls-Boltzmann transport equation, we derive an effective Schrodinger equation for the coupled dynamics of temperature and phonon drift fields, allowing the hydrodynamic regime to be identified directly from material transport properties. Within this framework, a pair of exceptional points quantitatively define the lower and upper boundaries of the hydrodynamic window, providing a critical design criterion beyond the qualitative scattering-rate inequalities proposed by Guyer and Krumhansl six decades ago. Furthermore, we derive an analytical criterion for the existence of hydrodynamic transport, showing that a finite hydrodynamic window can be supported only when the effective thermal diffusivity exceeds the phononic momentum diffusivity. Using ab initio phonon properties as inputs, our theory accurately captures the experimentally observed temperature- and length-dependent ultrafast second-sound dynamics. These results establish a symmetry-based framework for understanding collective energy transport and provide quantitative design principles for identifying the materials, temperatures, and characteristic length scales that support wavelike heat transport in low-dimensional materials and functional devices.

</details>


### [422] [Controlled Growth and Disorder Mitigation of GaAs1-xSbx/InAs Core-Shell Quantum Wires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16203)
*Ran Zhuo,Huading Song,Yanlin Wu,Wenlong Yang,Haiyan Shi,Xiyu Hou,Sijun Chen,Zhichuan Wang,Donghai Li,Jinjin Bai,Runan Shang,Hao Zhang,Dong Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过向GaAs核中引入Sb形成GaAs1-xSbx/InAs核壳纳米线，显著降低核壳晶格失配，从而减少缺陷并提升器件性能，获得低无序核壳量子器件平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规GaAs-InAs核壳体系存在较大的晶格失配（lattice mismatch），被视为纳米线中无序的重要来源；研究旨在通过成分调控降低失配、改善晶体质量与器件性能。

Method: 生长GaAs1-xSbx/InAs核壳纳米线（core-shell nanowires），系统比较不同Sb组分下的晶体质量与输运性质，并开展磁电导（magneto-conductance）测量。

Result: x由0.15增至0.35时，InAs壳中层错（stacking faults）等晶体缺陷密度减半，场效应迁移率（field-effect mobility）由3850 cm2/Vs升至4800 cm2/Vs；纳米线更易被栅压夹断（pinch off），且磁电导呈现类Aharonov-Bohm振荡，表明InAs壳具有相位相干的管状输运。

Conclusion: 晶格失配是核壳纳米线中的关键无序来源，Sb引入为低无序核壳量子器件提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We report the growth and transport properties of GaAs1-xSbx/InAs core-shell nanowires. Compared with the well-studied GaAs-InAs system, incorporating Sb substantially reduces the lattice mismatch between the core and shell. Accordingly, both the nanowire crystal quality and device performance improved significantly. Increasing x from 0.15 to 0.35, the density of crystalline defects, such as stacking faults in the InAs shell, decreases by half. Moreover, the field-effect mobility increases from 3850 cm2/Vs to 4800 cm2/Vs, consistent with the reduction of the lattice mismatch. The incorporation of Sb makes the nanowire easier to pinch off via the gate voltage, enhancing the electric field tunability over the nanowire. Magneto-conductance measurements reveal Aharonov-Bohm-like oscillations, suggesting a phase-coherent tubular InAs shell. Our results identify lattice mismatch as a critical disorder source in core-shell nanowires and provide a practical route toward low-disorder core-shell quantum devices.

</details>


### [423] [High-Quality InSb Nanowires Grown by Molecular-Beam Epitaxy via Precise Catalyst Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16234)
*Wenlong Yang,Jiaye Xu,Shuai Yang,Wenyu Song,Haiyan Shi,Donghai Li,Xiyu Hou,Sijun Chen,Ke He,Hao Zhang,Dong Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作报告了无外来催化剂的自催化分子束外延（MBE）生长InSb纳米线方法：先生长GaAs纳米线茎，再原位将Ga液滴转化为In液滴，实现InSb轴向异质外延生长，并获得高质量、尺寸可控的纳米线及优异量子输运性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: InSb纳米线是拓扑量子计算（topological quantum computation）的重要候选材料，但现有器件多用金属有机化学气相沉积（MOCVD）生长，与超导体原位生长不兼容，难以实现低无序器件；MBE生长虽可解决此问题，却受限于极窄参数窗口与可重复性，且外来催化剂会引入深能级杂质。

Method: 采用自催化（self-catalyzed）MBE生长：先制备GaAs纳米线茎，原位将Ga催化剂液滴转化为In液滴，借助GaAs茎顶部的In液滴实现InSb轴向异质外延（axial heteroepitaxial）生长；并利用低通量抑制径向生长以调控长度。

Result: 低通量可抑制径向生长，使长度随生长时间在0.8至2.6微米线性可调而直径基本不变；纳米线为无孪晶、无层错的纯闪锌矿（zinc-blende）结构，电子迁移率约19000 cm²V⁻¹s⁻¹，并观察到准弹道输运（quasi-ballistic transport）、量子点（quantum dots）与近藤关联（Kondo correlations）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条不依赖衬底（substrate-independent）、自催化的高质量InSb纳米线可控合成路线，为高性能InSb基电子与量子器件建立了稳健的材料平台。

Abstract: Semiconductor InSb nanowires are a major material candidate for topological quantum computation. The nanowires in current devices were mostly grown using metal organic chemical vapor deposition, incompatible with in situ growth of superconductor, a crucial requirement for low-disorder devices. Growth of InSb nanowires using molecular-beam epitaxy (MBE) can address this issue. So far, studies on MBE-grown InSb remain limited due to an extremely narrow parameter window for the growth and reproducibility. Moreover, the use of foreign catalysts can introduce deep-level impurities. Here, we report a self-catalyzed MBE growth of InSb nanowires without those foreign catalysts. We first grow GaAs nanowire stems, and then convert Ga catalyst droplets into In droplets in situ. Axial heteroepitaxial growth of InSb can be realized through the In droplets on top of GaAs stems. We further find that a low flux suppresses radial growth, enabling linear length tuning from 0.8 to 2.6 micrometer with growth time, while diameter stays nearly constant. These InSb nanowires have a pure zinc-blende structure free of twins, stacking faults. Transport measurement reveals an electron mobility near 19000 cm2 V-1s-1. Moreover, the observation of quasi-ballistic transport, quantum dots, and Kondo correlations demonstrates the coherent quantum transport capability of the MBE-grown InSb nanowires. Our work provides a substrate-independent, self-catalyzed route for the controllable synthesis of high-quality InSb nanowires, establishing a robust material platform for high-performance InSb-based electronic and quantum devices.

</details>


### [424] [Ferroelectric control of exciton photoluminescence in rhombohedrally stacked bilayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16475)
*Wenlong Zhong,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Xiaoxiang Xi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用近零扭转菱面堆垛（rhombohedral stacking）的人工组装WSe₂双层，通过激子光致发光（photoluminescence）实现对滑动铁电性（sliding ferroelectricity）的可逆、迟滞电学翻转，并解析出由两次能带重排与一次铁电翻转事件分隔的四阶段序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动铁电性为实现电可编程的光电功能提供了非易失途径，但激子发光的确定性调控常被莫尔重构（moiré reconstruction）与畴平均效应所掩盖，故需构建可控体系以实现确定性的激子读出。

Method: 研究近零扭转、菱面堆垛的人工组装WSe₂双层，在面外电场下测量动量间接QK激子发射的斯塔克斜率反转（Stark-slope reversals）与能量跃变，并结合共振拉曼散射（resonant Raman scattering）作为互补读出手段。

Result: 观测到可逆的迟滞电学翻转，并区分出四阶段序列（由两次能带重排和一次铁电翻转分隔）；极化反转还可选择与层内激子杂化的动量直接KK层间激子分支，产生依赖历史的光致发光，拉曼强度亦呈现与极化相关层间场对应的迟滞行为。

Conclusion: 结果建立了基于激子的铁电WSe₂电子结构定量表征方法，并为电可编程光电子功能开辟了途径。

Abstract: Sliding ferroelectricity offers a non-volatile route to electrically programmable electronic and optical functionality, but deterministic control of excitonic photoluminescence is often obscured by moiré reconstruction and domain averaging. Here we study an artificially assembled WSe$_2$ bilayer with near-zero twist in rhombohedral stacking and observe reversible, hysteretic electrical switching through excitonic photoluminescence. Under an out-of-plane electric field, momentum-indirect QK exciton emission exhibits Stark-slope reversals and abrupt energy jumps, resolving a four-stage sequence separated by two band realignments and one ferroelectric switching event. Polarization reversal also selects the momentum-direct KK interlayer-exciton branch that hybridizes with an intralayer exciton, producing history-dependent photoluminescence. Ferroelectric modulation of resonant Raman scattering provides a complementary readout, with hysteretic intensities correlated with the polarization-dependent interlayer field. These results establish an exciton-based quantification of the electronic structure in ferroelectric WSe$_2$ and open a route to electrically programmable optoelectronic functionality.

</details>


### [425] [Micromagnetic modelling and simulation of inertial magnetization dynamics in ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16567)
*Massimiliano d'Aquino*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文是一篇专题综述（topical review），系统梳理铁磁纳米结构中惯性磁化动力学（inertial magnetization dynamics）的理论建模与微磁学模拟，涵盖惯性 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 方程、线性自旋波谱、大波数极限及频域/时域数值积分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚皮秒时间尺度的磁化动力学可表现出超出传统 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 描述的惯性效应（inertial effects），并在太赫兹（terahertz）频率产生章动运动（nutational motion）；自理论预言与实验观测以来，磁惯性（magnetic inertia）已成为理解与调控超快自旋动力学的重要课题，亟需对其建模范式与模拟方法进行综述。

Method: 从惯性 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 方程的力学类比出发，引入其空间非均匀的微磁学表述，并讨论磁化长度守恒（magnetization-length conservation）、角动量弛豫（angular-momentum relaxation）与广义自由能平衡等性质；进而综述惯性自旋波（inertial spin waves）的线性动力学，以及用于空间非均匀惯性动力学的频域与时域微磁学模拟技术。

Result: 线性分析揭示 GHz 进动支（precessional branch）与 THz 章动支（nutational branch）的共存、进动模的惯性红移（inertial redshift）、谱线宽，以及交换作用、静磁相互作用、受限、曲率与扭率的影响；在大波数区，磁性惯性导致有限的自旋波传播速度上限。

Conclusion: 综述强调几何中点积分器（geometric midpoint integrators）等数值方案可保持连续动力学的基本约束与能量性质，并通过超短章动自旋波的激发与传播、超快惯性磁化翻转（inertial magnetization switching）等应用，说明微磁学建模在连接惯性 LLG 理论与可实验观测的纳米尺度磁化动力学之间的桥梁作用。

Abstract: Magnetization dynamics at sub-picosecond time scales can exhibit inertial effects beyond the conventional Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert description, giving rise to nutational motion at terahertz frequencies. Following its theoretical prediction and experimental observation in ferromagnets, magnetic inertia has attracted increasing interest for understanding and controlling ultrafast spin dynamics. In this topical review, we discuss the theoretical modelling and micromagnetic simulation of inertial magnetization dynamics in ferromagnetic nanostructures. Starting from the mechanical analogy underlying the inertial Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation, we introduce its spatially inhomogeneous micromagnetic formulation and discuss its main properties, including magnetization-length conservation, angular-momentum relaxation, and the generalized free-energy balance. We then review the linear dynamics of inertial spin waves, highlighting the coexistence of GHz precessional and THz nutational branches, the inertial redshift of precessional modes, spectral linewidths, and the effects of exchange, magnetostatic interactions, confinement, curvature and torsion. Particular attention is devoted to the large-wavenumber regime, where magnetic inertia leads to a finite limiting spin-wave propagation velocity. Finally, we address frequency- and time-domain micromagnetic simulation techniques for spatially nonuniform inertial dynamics, including geometric midpoint integrators preserving the fundamental constraints and energy properties of the continuous dynamics. Applications to the excitation and propagation of ultrashort nutational spin waves and ultrafast inertial magnetization switching illustrate the role of micromagnetic modelling in connecting inertial Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert theory with experimentally accessible nanoscale magnetization dynamics.

</details>


### [426] [A superconducting quantum point contact in Ge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16705)
*Anna Tacchini,Inga Seidler,Isabell Grandt-Ionita,Thibault Thuegaz,Stephen W. Bedell,Fabrizio Nichele*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作基于Ge/SiGe材料实现栅极可调的超导量子点接触（SQPC），并将其嵌入高度不对称超导量子干涉仪（SQUID），从而获得电流-相位关系（CPR）与开关电流，实现少模超导输运的相位敏感研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合超导-半导体电路为介观弱连接中的相干输运提供了通用平台，而超导量子点接触（SQPC）中由离散数目弹道输运模式承载超电流的特性具有特殊研究价值，需要一种能对少模超导输运进行相位敏感探测的手段。

Method: 在Ge/SiGe中制备栅极可调的超导量子点接触，并将其集成到高度不对称的超导量子干涉仪（SQUID）中，以此测量其电流-相位关系（CPR）与开关电流，并在微波辐照下考察其输运响应。

Result: 观察到电导量子化与开关电流量子化之间的直接对应关系，将正常态输运模式与超导响应联系起来；在首个电导平台起始附近展示了有效透射率的强栅极可调性；微波辐照下，低透射率时出现光子辅助多重Andreev反射（multiple Andreev reflections），高透射少模区则出现Shapiro复制峰（Shapiro replicas）。

Conclusion: 栅极可调的Ge/SiGe超导量子点接触结合不对称SQUID结构，可有效实现少模超导输运的相位敏感表征，并揭示正常态电导量子化与超导开关电流量子化的对应关系及微波下的透射率依赖行为。

Abstract: Hybrid superconducting-semiconducting circuits provide a versatile platform for investigating coherent transport in mesoscopic weak links. Superconducting quantum point contacts (SQPCs) are particularly interesting in this context, as their supercurrent is carried by a discrete number of ballistic transport modes. Here, we investigate a gate-tunable superconducting quantum point contact realized in Ge/SiGe. By incorporating the QPC into a highly asymmetric superconducting quantum interference device (SQUID), we gain access to its current-phase relation (CPR) and switching current, enabling phase-sensitive studies of few-mode superconducting transport. We observe a direct correspondence between conductance quantization and switching-current quantization, linking the normal-state transport modes to the superconducting response. In addition, we demonstrate strong gate tunability of the effective transmission near the onset of the first conductance plateau. Under microwave irradiation, the QPC exhibits photon-assisted multiple Andreev reflections at lower transparencies and Shapiro replicas in the high-transparency, few-mode regime.

</details>


### [427] [Interfacial Effects of Thermal Reorganization on Charge and Spin Transport in Organic Spin Valves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16852)
*Zihao Liu,Yunzhe Ke,Hajar Komekoz,Shuai Gu,Rico Ehrler,Chen Wang,Olav Hellwig,Frank Ortmann,Dietrich R. T. Zahn,Carsten Deibel,Michael Sommer,Shuaishuai Ding,Georgeta Salvan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作研究原位热退火（in situ thermal annealing）对基于NDI的完整有机自旋阀（OSV）电学与磁输运性质的影响，对比直接蒸镀Co/Au与转移石墨烯（Gr）/Co/Au两种顶电极构型，发现退火虽提升体相有序度与有效垂直电导，却无法获得可重复的磁电阻（MR）响应，界面因素主导器件表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机半导体因弱自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）、小超精细相互作用、机械柔性与可调电子性质而适合自旋电子学应用，但有机自旋阀的磁电阻响应对铁磁电极/有机半导体界面（spinterface）高度敏感，界面形貌、化学相互作用、能级排列与自旋极化（spin polarization）的差异使器件性能难以重复，因此需明确界面与热处理的作用。

Method: 制备完整的NDI基有机自旋阀，比较两种顶电极构型：直接蒸镀Co/Au与转移石墨烯（graphene）/Co/Au；对器件实施原位热退火，表征其电学与磁输运性质，并结合伴随薄膜的结构表征与电子单载流子器件（electron-only devices）提取的有效垂直电导进行评估。

Result: 两类未退火（as-cast）器件均表现出清晰的磁电阻响应，转移电极器件的绝对响应更强。原位退火后，两类器件电阻显著增大，且未能解析出可重复的MR响应；与此同时，伴随薄膜的结构有序度提高，电子单载流子器件提取的有效垂直电导约提升36倍。

Conclusion: 体相有序度的改善本身不足以保障自旋阀性能提升；退火引起的界面接触、接触均匀性与局域电流分布变化可在完整器件响应中占据主导。结果凸显有机自旋阀中界面稳定性（spinterface stability）与界面工程对实现可靠、耐热运行的关键作用。

Abstract: Organic semiconductors are attractive for spintronic applications owing to their weak spin-orbit coupling, small hyperfine interactions, mechanical flexibility, and tunable electronic properties. Organic spin valves (OSVs) provide a representative platform for investigating spin-dependent transport. However, the magnetoresistance (MR) response is highly sensitive to the ferromagnetic electrode/organic semiconductor interface (spinterface). Variations in interfacial morphology, chemical interaction, energy-level alignment, and spin polarization can strongly affect spin injection, transport, and detection, making reproducible OSV performance difficult to achieve. Here, we investigate how in situ thermal annealing affects the electrical and magnetotransport properties of complete NDI-based OSVs. Two top-electrode configurations are compared: directly evaporated Co/Au and transferred Gr (graphene)/Co/Au. Both as-cast devices exhibit clear MR responses, with a stronger absolute response observed for the transferred-electrode device. After in situ annealing, the resistance of both device types increases substantially, and no reproducible MR response is resolved, despite the enhanced structural order observed in companion films and an approximately 36-fold increase in the effective vertical conductivity extracted from electron-only devices. This contrasting behavior indicates that improved bulk order alone is insufficient to ensure enhanced spin-valve performance. Instead, annealing-induced changes in interfacial contact, contact homogeneity, and local current distribution can become dominant in determining the complete-device response. These results emphasize the critical role of spinterface stability and interface engineering in achieving reliable and thermally robust OSV operation.

</details>


### [428] [Squeezed terahertz light from nonlinear diamagnetism in ultrastrongly coupled two-dimensional electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16924)
*Dasom Kim,Junichiro Kono,Stephen Hughes*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文指出，太赫兹腔中二维电子气的本征能带非抛物性（band nonparabolicity）使其成为可压缩太赫兹光的量子克尔振子（quantum Kerr oscillator）；作者预测现有GaAs样品在弱受限腔（品质因子~10²）下可实现1.8–2.4 dB输出压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类平台虽为超强光-物质耦合（ultrastrong light–matter coupling）的成熟体系，但其集体响应仅由线性Hopfield模型描述，该模型具有精确的经典对应，因而缺乏量子非线性效应。作者希望借助新近由非线性抗磁性（nonlinear diamagnetism）揭示的能带非抛物性，把体系推向量子非线性区域。

Method: 采用非旋转波理论（non-rotating-wave theory），并配套与超强耦合自洽的输入-输出（input–output）处理、从头算的腔浴（ab initio cavity bath）以及电子加热（electron heating）的自洽模型进行理论计算与预测。

Result: 预测在现有GaAs样品、弱受限腔（品质因子为10²量级）下输出压缩为1.8–2.4 dB；若迁移率（mobility）或腔频率有适度改善，可达约9 dB。载流子加热（carrier heating）通过一个材料品质因数（figure of merit）设置限制。

Conclusion: 由材料品质因数导出简单设计规则：最优腔品质因子随超强耦合强度增大而反比下降。

Abstract: Two-dimensional electron gases in terahertz cavities are among the most mature platforms for ultrastrong light--matter coupling, yet their collective response is captured by the linear Hopfield model, which has an exact classical correspondence. We show that the intrinsic band nonparabolicity of the electrons, recently revealed through nonlinear diamagnetism, turns this system into a quantum Kerr oscillator that squeezes terahertz light. Using a non-rotating-wave theory with ultrastrong-coupling-consistent input--output, an ab initio cavity bath, and a self-consistent model of electron heating, we predict output squeezing of 1.8--2.4\,dB with present GaAs samples in weakly confined cavities with quality factors of order $10^2$, and up to $\approx9$\,dB with modest improvements in mobility or cavity frequency. Carrier heating sets limits through a material figure of merit, which yields a simple design rule that the optimal cavity quality factor scales inversely with the ultrastrong-coupling strength.

</details>


### [429] [Achiral-Chiral Dissociation and Structural Zero of the Current in the Spinterface Model of CISS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16998)
*Seif Alwan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: We study the transport sector of the spinterface model of chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) under spin-resolved dissipation, extending the quantum non-equilibrium Green's function formulation of Ref.~\cite{Alwan2026QSpinterface} to spin-resolved lead broadenings. The spin current decomposes into an achiral part, driven by the interfacial moment, and a chiral part, driven by the Rashba spin flip. Under increasing lead spin asymmetry, the achiral part grows linearly while the chiral part follows the geometric-mean suppression, so the two sectors dissociate; they also peak at different coupling scales along the lead broadening. The coupling and bias optima are operating points of the reference configuration, not universal scaling laws. A structural zero appears at $λ_R\sin(φ/2)=t_{\mathrm{mol}}$ in the collinear-lead configuration, where the chiral signal collapses to machine precision universally across chain lengths, frequency resolutions, and twist angles; the zero originates from the rank-one singular bond matrix, whose null eigenvector traps an electron in a spin-superposition dark state that does not hop. With non-collinear ferromagnetic leads, the zero is broken in a controlled way: the residual is generically linear in the transverse polarization, arising from a single coherent spin flip at a boundary, and becomes quadratic at a tuned cancellation angle where two boundary contributions interfere destructively, with the scaling exponent jumping sharply from one to two. These results establish the spinterface model as a transport platform with a two-axis dissociation structure, non-scaling operating optima, and an exact structural zero whose non-collinear deformation is a clean linear-plus-quadratic hierarchy.

</details>


### [430] [Reflection Zeros Set the Scattering Invariant of Finite Majorana Wires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17001)
*Alex Krasnok*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文阐明零能电子反射符号 $Q$ 在有限器件中计数的是“无反射态”（reflectionless states），从而给出输运实验可以直接读取的拓扑不变量判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马约拉纳模式（Majorana modes）的输运证据（零偏压电导峰）可被普通束缚态模仿，其是否被观测到仍有争议；长导线中标志拓扑相的反射符号 $Q$ 在有限器件中究竟认证什么并不清楚。

Method: 通过理论分析建立 $Q$ 与无反射态数目的对应：$Q=-1$ 当且仅当复能量平面下半平面中存在奇数个无反射态；并分析等耦合接触下 $Q$ 变号点与例外点（exceptional point）及透射平坦性的关系。

Result: 在有限导线中，$Q=-1$ 意味着两个马约拉纳模式的分裂小于它们与接触的耦合；等接触时 $Q$ 的每次变号（包括体拓扑转变）都是两个无反射态合并的例外点，此时透射最大程度平坦；零温下 $Q=-1$ 恰对应接触处测得零偏压电导超过 $e^2/h$。

Conclusion: 结果为输运实验能对马约拉纳器件认证什么提供了明确答案，并给出从电导读数提取该不变量的方法。

Abstract: Majorana modes, zero-energy excitations of superconducting wires that are their own antiparticles, could store quantum information protected from local noise. In transport experiments, however, ordinary bound states mimic their signature, a conductance peak at zero voltage, and whether Majorana modes have been observed remains debated. A sign, $Q$, of the electron reflection at zero energy marks the topological phase of a long wire, but what $Q$ certifies in a finite device has been unclear. Here we show for the first time that $Q$ counts reflectionless states, waves that enter through one contact and leave entirely through the other: $Q=-1$ when an odd number of them lie in the lower half of the complex energy plane. In a finite wire this means that the two Majorana modes are split by less than their coupling to the contacts. With equal contacts, every sign change of $Q$, including the bulk topological transition, is an exceptional point at which two such states merge and transmission is maximally flat. At zero temperature, $Q=-1$ exactly when the zero-bias conductance measured at a contact exceeds $e^2/h$. These results show what transport can certify about a Majorana device and how to read the invariant from conductance.

</details>


### [431] [Acoustically driven motion of single molecules at hBN surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17112)
*Hande N. Açıkgöz,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Peter G. Steeneken,Sabina Caneva*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种声表面波（surface acoustic wave, SAW）与六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride, hBN）集成的平台，可在hBN表面实现DNA分子的无接触、可调控定向输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单分子成像与传感技术已能直接观测界面处生物分子动力学，但将主动表面输运、定位与分子运动控制集成仍具挑战，限制了单分子器件的实现。

Method: 构建SAW-hBN集成平台：hBN提供原子级平整表面以限域DNA并保持其面内迁移性，SAW提供无需依赖分子电学、介电或磁学特性的主动引导输运；采用基于轨迹与跳跃（trajectory- and jump-based）的分析方法。

Result: 提高SAW功率会增大沿流方向（streaming direction）的位移偏置，使输运速度提高、轨迹愈发线性化，表明SAW可诱导DNA分子的可调定向输运。

Conclusion: 该工作为纳流控系统（nanofluidic systems）中生物分子的片上操控提供新途径，对先进单分子测序平台及生物分子操控与分析技术具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Developments in single-molecule imaging and sensing techniques have enabled direct observation of biomolecular dynamics at interfaces, which has important implications for the fields of membrane biophysics, nanofluidic sensing and transport. However, integrating active surface transport, localization and control over molecule motion remains challenging, limiting further progress in the realization of single-molecule devices. Here, we introduce an integrated surface acoustic wave (SAW)-hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) platform as a contactless actuation platform for directional transport of biomolecules over hBN surfaces. hBN provides an atomically flat surface on which DNA can be confined and still retain in-plane mobility, whereas SAW actuation enables active and guided transport without requiring specific electrical, dielectric or magnetic properties of the biomolecules. Trajectory- and jump-based analyses show that increasing the SAW power leads to higher displacement bias in the streaming direction, resulting in transport velocities along increasingly more linearized trajectories. We thus demonstrate that SAWs can induce tunable directional transport of DNA molecules confined on hBN surfaces, providing a new approach for the on-chip manipulation of biomolecules in nanofluidic systems. Our findings have important implications for the development of advanced single-molecule sequencing platforms and other technologies for biomolecular manipulation and analysis.

</details>


<div id='physics.atom-ph'></div>

# physics.atom-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [432] [Rotor-driven piecewise cross polarization of wide quadrupolar lines: bath-contact pumping of vector and tensor order in dynamically polarized solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14575)
*D. Keller,M. F. Hossain*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出将 Hartmann–Hahn 交叉极化（cross polarization, CP）理论推广到自旋-1 四极核（quadrupolar spin-1）单个跃迁，并配合慢速样品旋转，用于在动态极化固体中把质子极化转移给 $^{14}$N 与氘核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在核自旋温度共同的动态极化固体中，$^{14}$N 与氘核的极化远低于质子（$P_N\simeq0.14$、$P_D\simeq0.29$，质子为 0.90）；虽可用 CP 从质子提升，但一阶四极相互作用（first-order quadrupole interaction）使 $^{14}$N 谱线在 5 T 下展宽约 5 MHz，远超任何单一接触带宽。

Method: 建立 CP 进入四极自旋-1 单一跃迁的理论，以慢速样品旋转让每个微晶依次通过固定频率接触窗口；将浴接触视为被接触跃迁布居上的幂等映射（idempotent map），并在小储库极限与连续旋转条件下分析稳态极化与可达比例。

Result: 因接触映射幂等，重复或连续接触可累积而非抵消，脉冲反转所需的“只中一次”与宇称约束（hit-once and parity constraints）不再出现。小储库极限下跃迁布居比为 $(1+P_H)/(1-P_H)$，$P_H=0.9$ 时一次接触得 $\Pzz=+0.88$、两次得 $\Pzz\to-1.8$，超出幺正界（unitary bound）$[-0.85,+0.65]$；连续旋转下 Pake 角窗可在垂直场轴时覆盖所有键，而洁净基座区窗口在魔角 $54.74\degree$ 处覆盖比例 $\sin2\theta_c=0.943$。立方极限下一次接触即令 $\Pzz=+0.85$，但负张量序（tensor order）仅在四极劈裂可分辨时出现。

Conclusion: 稳态序由接触速率与旋转诱导的跨支弛豫（cross-branch relaxation）竞争决定，对 $^{14}$N 后者可忽略；限制性资源是质子自旋锁（spin lock），其强度须远大于约 28 kHz 局域场且接触时间短于 $T_{1\rho}$，而后者在 1 K 下尚未测量。文中给出 NH$_3$ 中 $^{14}$N 与 NH$_2$D 中氘核的实验方案。

Abstract: In a dynamically polarized solid with a common nuclear spin temperature, spin-1 $^{14}$N in ammonia sits at $P_N\simeq0.14$ when the protons are at $0.90$, and a deuteron in a proton-rich matrix at $P_D\simeq0.29$. Hartmann--Hahn cross polarization (CP) from the protons could raise either toward $P_H$, but the first-order quadrupole interaction spreads the $^{14}$N line over $5$~MHz at $5$~T, far beyond any single contact bandwidth. We develop the theory of CP into one transition of a quadrupolar spin-1, with slow sample rotation carrying each crystallite through a fixed-frequency contact window. A bath contact is an idempotent map on the populations of the contacted transition, so repeated or continuous contacts accumulate rather than cancel, and the hit-once and parity constraints that force stepped rotation for pulsed inversions do not arise. In the small-reservoir limit the population ratio across the contacted transition becomes $(1+P_H)/(1-P_H)$, giving $\Pzz=+0.88$ in one contact and $\Pzz\to-1.8$ in two from $P_H=0.9$, beyond the unitary bound $[-0.85,+0.65]$. Under continuous rotation, a window at the Pake horn reaches every bond for an axis perpendicular to the field, while a window in the clean pedestal region, needed for a definite sign of tensor order, reaches a fraction $\sin2θ_c=0.943$ at the magic angle $54.74\degree$. Steady-state order is set by the contact rate against rotation-driven cross-branch relaxation, negligible for $^{14}$N. In the cubic limit one contact places the spin-1 at the proton temperature ($\Pzz=+0.85$), but only a resolved quadrupole splitting permits negative tensor order. The limiting resource is the proton spin lock, which must far exceed the $\simeq28$~kHz local field for a contact short compared with $T_{1ρ}$, unmeasured at $1$~K. Protocols are given for $^{14}$N in NH$_3$ and deuterons in NH$_2$D.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [433] [Quivers with potential for quantum affine superalgebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14550)
*Andrei Neguţ*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文构造了从特定带势箭图的K-理论Hall代数到（半）量子仿射及环面正交辛超代数的满代数同态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确说明研究动机，仅提出构造满代数同态这一目标，联系K-理论Hall代数与量子仿射/环面正交辛超代数两类结构。

Method: 方法为构造（construct）一个从某些带势箭图（quivers with potential）的K-理论Hall代数（K-theoretic Hall algebras）出发的满代数同态（surjective algebra homomorphism）。

Result: 所得的满代数同态以（半）量子仿射与环面正交辛超代数（(half) quantum affine and toroidal orthosymplectic superalgebras）为目标。

Conclusion: 摘要未给出进一步结论；可确认的是在K-理论Hall代数与正交辛型量子仿射/环面超代数之间建立了满同态联系。

Abstract: We construct a surjective algebra homomorphism from the $K$-theoretic Hall algebras of certain quivers with potential to the (half) quantum affine and toroidal orthosymplectic superalgebras.

</details>


### [434] [Affine Vertex Operator Algebras and Correlation Functions on Elliptic Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16453)
*Linfang Hou,Si Li,Yongchang Zhu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文对任意水平的仿射顶点代数（affine vertex algebra）模，给出亏格一仿射流迹的显式公式，并证明其收敛性与亚纯延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作缺乏对仿射顶点代数任意模在亏格一（genus-one）上仿射流迹（affine current traces）的显式统一表达与收敛性保证，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将每条亏格一仿射流迹写成关于循环分割（cycle partitions）的有限和，证明所有固定零模（zero-mode）迹级数收敛，从而得到流迹及其在椭圆构型空间（elliptic configuration space）上的亚纯延拓（meromorphic continuation）；并推广至任意仿射态插入（affine-state insertions）。

Result: 获得仿射流迹的显式循环分割公式、绝对局部一致收敛性及亚纯延拓；对至多四条流的情形和节点极限（nodal limit）q→0 完成显式计算，其中第一类与第二类椭圆循环系数（type-1 and type-2 elliptic cycle coefficients）退化为有理亏格零循环系数，模迹约化为基态空间（ground-state space）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了亏格一仿射流迹的有限求和表示与解析性质，并揭示其在节点极限下向亏格零有理结构与基态空间的自然退化。

Abstract: Let $V^{\Bbbk}(\mathfrak{g})$ be the universal affine vertex algebra attached to a finite-dimensional complex simple Lie algebra at arbitrary level, and let $M=\bigoplus_{N=0}^{\infty}M_N$ be a $V^{\Bbbk}(\mathfrak{g})$ -module. We give an explicit formula for every genus-one trace of affine currents as a finite sum over cycle partitions. We then show the convergence of all fixed zero-mode trace series, hence absolute locally uniform convergence of the current traces and their meromorphic continuation on the elliptic configuration space. The same conclusion is obtained for arbitrary affine-state insertions. Finally, we compute the cases of at most four currents and the nodal limit $q\to 0$, in which the type-1 and type-2 elliptic cycle coefficients degenerate to rational genus-zero cycle coefficients and the module trace reduces to the ground-state space.

</details>
